āḍñūṣṭ ṇūṃBEṛīṇṅ FṛOṃ āFṭEṛ 232 

1a] oṃ namo mahābhairavāya digdevatādibhyo gurubhyaḥ ||


jyotiḥsthaśikhare ramye nānādhātuvicitrite |
nānādrumalatākīrṇe nānāṛṣisamākule || 1-1 ||
siddhavidyādharākīrṇe gaṇapramathasevite |
brahmāviṣṇusurādhyakṣyāḥ sarve tatra samāgatāḥ || 1-2 ||
muditaṃ bhairavaṃ dṛṣṭvā pratyudyayanti cāmbikā |
tvaṃ mātā jagataḥ hyasya tvameva śaraṇaṃ tu me || 1-3 ||
saṃsārabhayabhītānāṃ tārakā tvaṃ mahāyaśe |
ṛṣayaścaiva gāndharvā gaṇavidyādharādayaḥ || 1-4 ||
trastāḥ saṃsāracakre'smin jarāmaraṇabhīravāḥ | 
duḥkhitāḥ prāṇino'nye'pi tvameva śaraṇaṃ gatāḥ || 1-5 ||
pṛccha tvaṃ devadeveśaṃ śaṃkaraṃ lokaśaṃkaram |
sadbhāvajñānasarvasvaṃ bhuktimuktiphalārpaṇam || 1-6 ||
yena jñānena mucyante siddhyante tuṣṭitena ca |
teṣāṃ tu vacanaṃ śrutvā devī vacanamabravīt || 1-7 ||
stutvā devaṃ maheśānaṃ stutibhirvasturūpakam | 
yā sṛṣṭirjananī vāmā jyeṣṭhā śaktistathāmbikā || 1-8 ||
raudrī śaktirjagaddhātrī tāsāṃ kāryaṃ ca yādṛśam |
tattvarūpā tu yā stutyā stutibhiḥ parameśvarī || 1-9 ||
vāmā jyeṣṭhāṃvikārau prathitaguṇamayaṃ kāryama * rya vīryaṃ yasya 
sthānā * * sthaviṣṭhaṃ viracitasakalaṃ tattvasargādisaṃgham |
1b kh] taṃ devaṃ niṣkalāṃśaṃ hatasakalaguṇaṃ sarvalakṣmīśanāthaṃ 
śrīkaṇṭhaṃ sarvabhāvād triguṇamatamasaṃ nīrajaskaṃ susattvam || 1-10 ||
śruta,m mayā rudra rahasyaṃ gūḍhagocaram |
vāmadakṣiṇamārgāśca yāmalāstu anekadhā || 1-11 ||
siddhāntāśca sureśāna daśāṣṭādaśabhedataḥ |
lakṣakoṭivibhāgena koṭayastu anekadhā || 1-12 ||
siddhayogeśvarītantre śatakoṭipravistare |
mūlatantre mahāsūtre sūtradvayavinirgatam || 1-13 ||
tatraikaṃ tu mayā jñātaṃ yonyārṇavasamudbhavam |
na śrutaṃ śrotumicchāmi tantrasadbhāvamuttamam || 1-14 ||
tantracaryā kriyā jñānaṃ yogaścāpi anekadhā |
sabījadhāraṇābhyāṃ tu nirbījantu pṛthak prabho || 1-15 ||
anekopāyasaṃyogād yajanaṃ cātmanaṃ prati |
nādasthaṃ bindusaṃsthaṃ ca cārasthaṃ cādhvaṣaṭkagam || 1-16 ||
śaktyuccāragataṃ deva kāraṇatyāgakālagam |
1b g] śūnyabhāvagataṃ cānyaṃ śaktisthaṃ hṛdi saṃsthitaṃ || 1-17 ||
dvādaśāntasthitaṃ caiva adhvasthaṃ adhvavarjitam |
visargasthaṃ bhruvontasthaṃ brahmanāḍyāṃ vyavasthitam || 1-18 ||
turyātītaṃ paraṃ devaṃ brahmadvārāntaraṃ punaḥ |
udbhavasthaṃ tathā yogaṃ viśleṣasthaṃ ca śūline || 1-19 ||
2a kh] layasthaṃ ca layātītametadicchāmi veditum |
punareva tu te sarve ekabhāvagatāḥ prabho || 1-20 ||
lolībhūtā yathā santi tathā tvaṃ kathayasva mām |
mantrāṇāṃ ca sureśāna yad vīryaṃ siddhikāraṇam || 1-21 ||
tat kimarthaṃ punardeva gopitaṃ surasattama |
ye tvayā kathitā mantrāḥ pūrvaṃ syuḥ kāmasiddhidāḥ || 1-22 ||
saptakoṭirasaṃkhyātāḥ paśūnāṃ nugrahaṃ prati |
kimarthaṃ te na siddhyanti kalpānte'pi kṛte sati || 1-23 ||
kliśyanti manujātyarthaṃ muktihetorjagatpate |
kathaṃ mokṣo'tra siddhiśca mantrahīne sureśvara || 1-24 ||
mantrāṇāṃ jananī yā ca vāmākhyā śaktiravyayā |
1b] tayā guptāstu deveśa varṇāḥ kaivalyatāṃ yayuḥ || 1-25 ||
kathaṃ sā jñāyate sūkṣmā tejorūpā parā kalā |
yathāgnerdāhikāśaktirbhātyevaṃ hi gabhastayaḥ || 1-26 ||
tadvadeva śivasyāpi dharmatvenopacaryate |
avinābhāvayogena sarvatraivopacaryate || 1-27 ||
na śivād rahitā śaktirna śaktirahitaḥ śivaḥ |
viyogo naiva dṛśyeta pavanāmvarayoriva || 1-28 ||
śaktyā karaṇarūpayā śivakṛtyaṃ pravartate |
nānyo'tra kāraṇaṃ tasya yena kṛtyaṃ prakurvati || 1-29 ||
2b kh] mantraḥ śivaḥ samuddiṣṭo varṇāstasyaiva vācakāḥ |
vācyavācakasaṃyogācchivastena prapadyate || 1-30 ||
tatra deva kutaḥ śaktirgatā tyaktvā maheśvaram |
yena te niṣphalā jātā mantrāścāmitatejasaḥ || 1-31 ||
mātṛkā tu kathaṃ jātā mantrāṇāṃ mātarī tu yā |
viśvasya jananī yā ca mantrarūpā kathaṃ tu sā || 1-32 ||
khecarīṇāṃ ca sarvāsāṃ yā yoniḥ kathayasva mām |

śrībhairava uvāca 

sādhu sādhu mahābhāge yat tvayā pṛcchito hyaham || 1-33 ||
tat sarvaṃ kathayiṣyāmi śṛṇusvāyatalocane |
ye mayā kathitāḥ tantrā vāmadakṣiṇayāmalāḥ || 1-34 ||
rudrabhedāstathā devi śivabhedāstathaiva ca |
tatra caryā kriyā yogo jñānaṃ ca suranāyike || 1-35 ||
tatra te bhrāmitāḥ sarve ye yasmin yatra pṛcchakāḥ |
2a g] na teṣāṃ kathitaṃ jñānaṃ cittavṛtterapekṣayā || 1-36 ||
yenāsya cārthitaṃ devi tat tathā kathitaṃ mayā |
mudrāmaṇḍalamantraiśca kaṣṭayogaistathāparaiḥ || 1-37 ||
recakaiḥ pūrakairdhyānaiḥ sopāyairbahubhiḥ priye |
bhrāmitāḥ karmavistārairna jñātaṃ kathitaṃ mayā || 1-38 ||
taistu jñānāvalepena tacca pṛṣṭamavajñayā |
brahmā viṣṇustathā rudra indraścandraḥ prajāpatiḥ || 1-39 ||
skandanandigaṇāḥ sarve śukrādyā ye ca yoginaḥ |
***********
3a kh] kṛtakṛtyāstu te sarve yaiśca taccāvadhāritam || 1-40 ||
taṃ tathaivaṃ varārohe gṛhītaṃ mandabuddhibhiḥ |
sarvopāyakaraṃ jñānaṃ sadyaḥpratyayasaṃyutam || 1-41 ||
yoginīhṛdayānandaṃ pāramparyakramāgatam |
kathayāmi samāsena tvatprītyā suranāyike || 1-42 ||
mukhyā nāḍyastrayo jantoḥ suṣumṇā piṅgalā iḍā |
adhordhvagā tataḥ sā tu jīvayuktasamīraṇā || 1-43 ||
tāmādhastād bhaved granthiḥ kukkuṭāṇḍasamaprabhā | 
tasyānālaṃ gatordhvaṃ tu vibhātiryacca saṃmitam || 1-44 ||
kanyasāṅgulavat sthūlaṃ stanamadhye vyavasthitam |
tasyopari bhavet padmaṃ aṣṭapatraṃ sitāruṇam || 1-45 ||
caturaṅgulavistīrṇaṃ keśaraiḥ ṣoḍaśāvṛtam |
ekāṅgulaparīṇāhaṃ karṇikā tadvadeva hi || 1-46 ||
adhaḥsthā caturasreṇa pṛthutvena ca sā bhavet |
mallakākārarūpeṇa meruvacca prakīrtitā || 1-47 ||
tasyā madhye sthito jīvaḥ śivavacca vaset sukham |
bālāgraśatabhāgaḥ tu aṇurūpo'tinirmalaḥ || 1-48 ||
vidyudvad bhāti tejena bhramate sūryabimbavat |
paramātmā sa dehastho yogibhistu upāsyate || 1-49 ||
sa eva bindurityukto vyāpayanto vyavasthitaḥ |
avyāpī paśubhāvasthaḥ saṃsāre saṃsaratyasau || 1-50 ||
2a] 3bkh] anādimalamāyātmā vimuktastu sadāśivaḥ |
saṃsṛjed vividhān bandhān bhāvābhāvātmakān guṇān || 1-51 ||
te guṇā bandhakatvena adhoddhṛtya vyavasthitāḥ |
tasmādevamabhāveṣu binduryonau svarūpake || 1-52 ||
bindurātmā samākhyātaḥ ātmā binduriti smṛtaḥ |
vyāpakaḥ parato bindurlayātīte vyavasthitaḥ || 1-53 ||
bindudvayaṃ tato jñātvā sa saṃsārād vimucyate |
catuṣkalasamopeto hutāśakaṇikākṛtiḥ || 1-54 ||
2b g] tiṣṭhate bhagavānīśo hṛtpadme suranāyike |
māyodaragatastiṣṭhed vidyota iva varcasā || 1-55 ||
māyāprasuptanāgākhyā saṃsthitā kuṭilākṛtiḥ |
kuṇḍalī tena sā proktā cidrūpā hyātmanasya tu || 1-56 ||
tayā nīyatyasau jīva adhaścordhve ca bhāmini |
sā tu māyā samākhyātā vāmāvartā tu kuṇḍalī || 1-57 ||
jyeṣṭhā nāma dvitīyā tu ṛjurekhā tu sā smṛtā |
padmatantunibhākārā sṛṣṭimārgapravartanī || 1-58 ||
raudrī śṛṅgāṭakākārā tṛtīyā sā prakīrtitā |
śaśāṅkasakalākārā nirodhī sā caturthikā || 1-59 ||
icchā jñānakriyā viśvī catuṣkaṃ iti paṭhayate |
4a kh] pañcamī śivaśaktistu yā karoti gamāgamam || 1-60 ||
icchā utpādayed buddhiṃ saṃkalpaṃ yā prakurvati |
sā tu tribhedato jñeyā sattvarājasatāmasā || 1-61 ||
jñānaśaktistathā jñānaṃ tadaivāṣṭaprakārataḥ |
dharmo jñānaṃ ca vairāgyamaiśvaryaṃ ca catuṣṭayam || 1-62 || 1-
adharmaśca tathājñānamavairāgyamanaiśvaryam |
jñānaṃ caivāṣṭadhābhinnaṃ karoti vikaroti ca || 1-63 ||
saṃkalpaśca vikalpaśca vyavahārārthakarmaṇi |
kriyā tu kurute sarvaṃ dharmādharmātmakaṃ paśoḥ || 1-64 ||
viśvī tu bodhanī jñeyā cicchaktipratibodhanī |
pañcamī yā smṛtā śaktistadādhāro vyavasthitā || 1-65 ||
ekā eva parā śaktiḥ kriyābhede anantadhā |
dharmārthakāmamokṣaṃ ca bhuñjate tadvaśāt priye || 1-66 ||
etacca mānasaṃ sargaṃ sattvarājasatāmasaḥ |
dharmādi sātvikaṃ sargamadharmādi ca tāmasam || 1-67 ||
rajaḥ kṣobhakṛdubhayostenaliṃgata sūyate |
śabdaḥ sparśaṃ ca rūpaṃ ca raso gandhaśca pañcamam || 1-68 ||
buddhirmanastvahaṃkāraḥ puryaṣṭaka iti smṛtaḥ |
prāṇo'pānaḥ samānaśca udāno vyānameva ca || 1-69 ||
nāgaḥ kūrmo'tha kṛkaro devadatto dhanañjayaḥ |
prāṇādhāraḥ smṛto vāyustadādhārāstu nāḍayaḥ || 1-70 ||
4b kh] iḍā ca piṅgalā caiva suṣumṇā ca tṛtīyakā |
gāndhārī hastijihvā ca pūṣā caiva yathā tathā || 1-71 ||
alambuṣā kuhū nāma śaṅkhinī daśamī smṛtā |
etaiḥ saha mahābhāge hṛdbindustiṣṭhate sadā || 1-72 ||
brahmādidevatāstasya akārākṣaravācakaḥ |
dvidhā bhūta akārastu tatra kṛtyaṃ prakurvati || 1-73 ||
icchā yāti layaṃ jñāne jñānaṃ ca kriyayā punaḥ |
kriyā yāti layaṃ bindvyāṃ bindvī yāti śivātmakā || 1-74 ||
3a g] sve sve sthāne layaṃ yānti brahmabhāgairadhiṣṭhitāḥ |
brahmā api svakāryāṇi upasaṃhṛtya gacchati || 1-75 ||
ikārāntaṃ paraṃ sthānaṃ yatra viṣṇuḥ saṃtiṣṭhati |
tatra śabdaḥ pralīyeta svakārya upasaṃharaḥ || 1-76 ||
2b] brahmā kaṇṭhe pralīnastu tu pūrvādhāre vyavasthitaḥ |
viṣṇostu tat paraṃ sthānaṃ tatra ikāraḥ saṃsthitaḥ || 1-77 ||
viṣṇuryātyapi rudrāntaṃ ukāro yatra tiṣṭhati |
tālvantaṃ tad vijānīyāt sarvajantusamāgamam || 1-78 ||
rudro'pi caiśvaraṃ yāti makārastatra tiṣṭhati |%%%%nirodhā sā samuddiṣṭā lambakasyordhvasaṃsthitā || 1-79 ||
urdhvamārganirodhinye raudrī tena prapadyate |
tasyordhve nādajaṃ lakṣaṃ makārastatra gacchati || 1-80 ||
5a kh] śivasthānaṃ tu taṃ proktaṃ yatra nādasamudbhavaḥ |
lambakasyordhvataścaiva dvau bindvau yatra te priye || 1-81 ||
visargāntaṃ tu taṃ proktaṃ dvādaśāntaṃ ca bhāmini |
kalāntaṃ ca svarāntaśca ṣoḍaśāntaṃ ca taṃ smṛtam || 1-82 ||
tatra cotpadyate śabdo raudrī bhinnā yadā bhavet |
nādo'pi gacchate devi yatra kuṇḍalikā sthitā || 1-83 ||
nādāntaṃ tad vijānīyād visargādūrdhvataḥ priye |
tasyordhve jñānaśaktistu ūrdhvasrotā manonmanī || 1-84 ||
vyāpinyāntaṃ tu deveśe tatra līnā manonmanī |
sphoṭamutpadyate tatra guruvaktrādadhaḥ priye || 1-85 ||
tasyordhve guruvaktraṃ tu brahmasthānaṃ tu taṃ smṛtam | 
śaktisthānaṃ smṛtaṃ tacca layāntaṃ taṃ taducyate || 1-86 ||
tasyānte tu layātītaṃ kathitaṃ tu na saṃśayaḥ |
eṣa bindostu saṃcāraṃ prabuddhānāṃ varānane || 1-87 ||
aprabuddhāstu ye devi nirodhinyā nirodhitāḥ |
teṣāṃ tiryaggatiḥ proktā saṃsārabhavabandhanī || 1-88 ||
eṣa cāraḥ samākhyātaḥ pravṛtta iti sarvadā |
grahaṇaṃ tu punarvakṣye yena tyāgaṃ bhaviṣyati || 1-89 ||
pañcakāraṇakaṃ tyaktvā svacchandagatimāpnuyāt |
eṣa vindvātmako yogo yadā tyaktuṃ samīhate || 1-90 ||
5b kh] tadā tasya bhave devi aṇimādiguṇāṣṭakam |
brahmasthānagatastyāgo yadā bhavati śobhane || 1-91 ||
tadā brahmāsahaikatvaṃ śabdasparśaṃ ca gacchati |
tau tyaktvā ūrdhvato yāti padmajasya tu kāraṇam || 1-92 ||
kāryarūpī vrajed brahmā svakāryamupasaṃhṛtaḥ |
viṣṇutatve layaṃ yāti svakāryakaraṇaiḥ saha || 1-93 ||
keśavaḥ paramaḥ tiṣṭhet so'pi tyājyo janārdanaḥ |
rasendriyagatairbhāvaistyaktaistyakto na saṃśayaḥ || 1-94 ||
keśavo'pi layaṃ rudre vyāpārastyaktacetasaḥ |
adhikāranivṛtto'sau keśavo guhyavedakaḥ || 1-95 ||
3b g] rudro'tra paramastiṣṭhed vyāpāraguṇasaṃyutaḥ |
rudro'pi yāti deveśa aiśvare tejarūpiṇe || 1-96 ||
bindusthānaṃ tu tat proktaṃ tejorāśi vinirdiśet |
tejātmakavibhāgaṃ tu prāṇavyāpārameva ca || 1-97 ||
etau tyaktavā vrajedūrdhvaṃ rudrasya guṇavṛttayaḥ |
īśvaro'pi tathā nāde śabdākhye tu sadāśive || 1-98 ||
buddhyādhikaraṇe yuktai ahaṃkāraguṇānvitaḥ |
gacchate ca tathā īśaḥ svavyāpārasusaṃhṛtaḥ || 1-99 ||
śivavidyāguṇādhāro yatra devaḥ sadāśivaḥ |
tataiṣaḥ paramastiṣṭhed īśvaro jñānadhārakaḥ || 1-100 ||
6a kh] svakṛtyamupasaṃhṛtya gacchate tu layaṃ svakam |
layātītaṃ paraṃ sthānaṃ kramāt tyaktvā tu gacchati || 1-101 ||
brahmā viṣṇustathā rudra aiśvaro'tha caturthakaḥ |
kriyārūpadharāḥ sarve svaguṇaṃ gṛhya gacchati || 1-102 ||
śive līnāstu te sarve svaśaktyā hṛtavigrahāḥ |
3a] icchā jñānaṃ kriyā teṣāṃ sarveṣāṃ karaṇāni tu || 1-103 ||
pūrvavat pratisaṃcāraḥ kathito'tra varānane |
udbhave ca tathā śleṣe laye caiva pṛthak pṛthak || 1-104 ||
layātītā parā śaktiryā sā viśvī śivātmikā |
tatrādhārā sureśāni saṃcaranti kramāt kramam || 1-105 ||
sadāśivo'pi saṃhāraṃ kurute svecchayā prabhuḥ |
manobindurvarārohe yo'sau cetā mahādhvani || 1-106 ||
tena saṃhṛtamātreṇa kāṣṭhavat tiṣṭhate tanuḥ |
dharmādharmātmako bandhastadā naṣṭo na saṃśayaḥ || 1-107 ||
tena naṣṭena suśroṇi gacchate paramaṃ padam |
yāvadetairmahābandhairna mucyate varānane || 1-108 ||
tāvat tasya kuto mokṣaḥ ṣaḍvidhārthe'pi śodhite |
evaṃ tyāgo mayā devi khyāpito vidyayā tava || 1-109 ||
śive'pi gacchate devi samastaguṇasaṃyutaḥ |
yatra sā kuṇḍalī śaktiramanaskā manonmanī || 1-110 ||
6b kh] śaktitrayasamopetā bodhanī ca nirodhanī |
adhogā rodhanī jñeyā ūrdhvagā ca prabodhanī || 1-111 ||
dvidhā bhāvagatā hyekā kṛtyabhedairanantatā |
sṛjate sā jagat kṛtsnamābrahmabhuvanāntagam || 1-112 ||
saṃharecca na sandehaḥ kṛtyabhedena pārvati |
vāmārūpā sṛjet pūrvaṃ jyeṣṭhādhārā prapālane || 1-113 ||
saṃhāro raudrarūpeṇa ekā eva tridhā priye |
sā tu pañcavidhā proktā aṣṭadhā navadhā tathā || 1-114 ||
pañcāśadbhedasaṃbhinnā anantānantatāṃ gatā |
4a g] sā eva pararūpeṇa jñātavyā vīranāyike || 1-115 ||
tayā vyāptamimaṃ śeṣaṃ yat kiñcid vāṅmayaṃ priye |
tena māyā samākhyātā jñeyarūpā tu kuṇḍalī || 1-116 ||
tatra prāpto bhaved devi unmano manavarjitaḥ |
guṇāṣṭakasamopetaḥ sarvajñatvādisaṃyutaḥ || 1-117 ||
ye dharmāḥ kāraṇe devi taiḥ saṃyukto bhavatyasau |
so'pi yāti layaṃ randhre śūnye padmātmake priye || 1-118 ||
manātītaṃ tu taṃ sthānaṃ suṣiraṃ padmajanmanaḥ |
tasmin yukto vimuktena svakṛtyamupasaṃhṛtaḥ || 1-119 ||
tasyordhve tu smṛto vyāpī nirguṇo guṇasaṃbhavaḥ |
acetanaḥ sucetā ca guṇātīto mumukṣavaḥ || 1-120 ||
sa śivaḥ paramo mokṣo vikalpātītagaḥ paraḥ |
naiva puṇyamapuṇyaṃ vā sarvānandakaraḥ paraḥ || 1-121 ||
7a kh] anena kramayogena yogabindvātmakaḥ priye |
jñātavyastu vipaścidbhiḥ samāsāt tadudīritaḥ || 1-122 ||
ataḥ paraṃ pravakṣyāmi nādasthaṃ tu śṛṇu priye |
nādakuṇḍalinī jñeyā nābhimadhye vyavasthitā || 1-123 ||
nirgatā brahmarandhreṇa tyaktvā tu pañcakāraṇam |
brahmādyaṃ śivaparyantaṃ bhitvā ūrdhvaṃ gatā tu sā || 1-124 ||
jñānaṃ kriyā tathā cecchā brahmādīnāṃ varānane |
sve sve sthāne tu yat kṛtyaṃ taṃ tu tyaktvā gatā tu sā || 1-125 ||
manobinduṃ gṛhītvā tu nirgatā vyomapañcake |
śaradabhraśaśiprakhyā taḍitkoṭisamujjvalā || 1-126 ||
uditārkakarābhāsā pravālāṅkurasannibhā |
ūrṇātantunibhā jñeyā padmasūtranibhāthavā || 1-127 ||
tuhinendukarā caiva sthiradīpaśikhopamā |
tasyāgre tu tato bindurhutāśakaṇikākṛtiḥ || 1-128 ||
nalinīva dale devi tuṣārakaṇikākṛtiḥ |
evaṃ vidhaṃ gṛhītvā tu śeṣān bandhān paricchinet || 1-129 ||
visargeṇa tato hṛtsthamākṛṣya ca śanaiḥ śanaiḥ |
padmanālagataṃ toyamambarastho vikarṣayet || 1-130 ||
tadvad yogī śarīrasthaḥ hṛdvāyumapakarṣayet |
yathā dīpo nivātastho avicchinnagatiḥ priye || 1-131 ||
tadvad devi manaḥ kāryaḥ śaktyādhārastu ekataḥ |
7b kh] tyaktvā tu kāraṇān sarvān yadā rudragatirbhavet || 1-132 ||
tadā tu kuñcitau dvau tu bhruvau caiva śubhekṣaṇe |
vikṣepastūrdhvataḥ kāryaḥ kuñcayeta puṭadvayam || 1-133 ||
triśūlaṃ khecarasthaṃ tu kartavyaṃ suranāyike |
karābhyāṃ pīḍanaṃ kāryamupadvāragatasya tu || 1-134 ||
ūrdhve tu gacchate śīghraṃ yatra randhraṃ tu padmajam |
nāḍyāvastho bhaved devo nānyathā vīranāyikā || 1-135 ||
3b g] nāḍyāvastho yadā jātastadā kāraṇakān tyajet |
recakaiḥ pūrakairyogaiḥ kumbhakaiḥ sopapattikaiḥ || 1-136 ||
evaṃ kāraṇakān tyaktvā yadā śaktisamo bhavet |
tadā tasya bhaviṣyeta aṇimādiguṇāṣṭakam || 1-137 ||
atītānāgatārthaṃ ca vartamānaṃ tathaiva ca |
vindate nātra sandeho nāḍyādhāro maheśvaraḥ || 1-138 ||
atītaṃ ceti nābhisthaṃ vartamānaṃ hṛdi sthitam |
lambakasthaṃ bhaviṣyaṃ ca yadā śaktisamo bhavet || 1-139 ||
jñānavijñānasaṃpannaḥ praviśecchāśvatīṃ tanum |
nādasthaṃ kathitaṃ devi bindusthaṃ pūrvameva hi || 1-140 ||
sāṃprataṃ cārasaṃsthaṃ tu yathā bhavati tacchṛṇu |
tadvibhāgaṃ mahādevi yathā jñāyati tattvataḥ || 1-141 ||
ṣaṭtṛṃśadaṅgulaṃ cāraṃ hṛtpadmād yāvat tatpadaṃ | (tasa 1/142ab = svata 4/234cd)
truṭyādikramayogeṇa ṣoḍaśāvayavena tu || 1-142 ||kālena lakṣitavyaṃ hi prāṇāntaṃ tu dinaṃ bhavet |
etat kalanarūpaṃ tu kālena kalitaṃ priye || 1-143 || (tasa 1/142cd-143 = svata 235ab tuṭiṣoḍaśamānena kālena kalitaḥ priye |)
saṃcareta vibhāgena sthānāt sthānāntaraṃ yathā | (tasa 1/144ab = svata 4/235cd saṃcarantaṃ vibhāgena yathāvattaṃ śṛṇuṣva me |)
abuddhānāṃ prabuddhānāṃ yugmasandehanirṇayam || 1-144 ||
hṛtpadmād yāvad āyātaḥ bhāgaikaṃ tyajate tu saḥ |
nāsikāgre dvitīyaṃ tu tṛtīyaṃ śaktinā yutam || 1-145 || (svata: nāsikāgre dvitīyaṃ tu śaktyante tu tṛtīyakam |)
tatrastho vinivarteta yāvat tattvaṃ na vindati |
vidite tu pare śānte tatrastho'pi na bādhyate || 1-146 ||
śaktyādhastād yadā gacched abuddhaḥ sa tadā bhavet | (svata: śaktyā cādho yadā gacchedabudhastu tadā bhavet |)
hṛdgataś ca punaḥ tiṣṭhed buddhyamānaḥ sa ucyate || 1-147 ||
śaktiṃ prāpya buddho jñeyo vyāpinyaṃśe prabuddhyate |
atīte suprabuddhaś ca unmanatvaṃ tadā bhavet || 1-148 ||
na kālaṃ na kalācāraṃ na tattvaṃ na ca kāraṇān |
sunirvāṇaṃ paraṃ śuddhaṃ gurupādaṃ kramāgatam || 1-149 ||
taṃ viditvā vimucyeta gatvā bhūyo na jāyate | (tasa 1/145-150ab = svata 4/236-241ab)
adhvaṣaṭkaṃ yathā prāṇaṃ saṃsthitaṃ kathayāmi te || 1-150 ||
āpādād yāva mūrdhāntaṃ citiḥ saṃvedanaṃ hi yaḥ | (svata: āpādānmūrdhaparyantaṃ citteḥ saṃvedanaṃ hi yat |)
bhuvanādhvā sa vijñeyas tattvādhvānas tathaiva ca || 1-151 || (tasa 1/150cd-151 = svata 4/241cd-242)
kalākalitasantānaṃ prāṇaṃ yac carate sadā |
nivṛttiś ca pratiṣṭhā ca adhobhāge prakīrtite || 1-152 ||
vidyā śāntis tathā cordhve śāntātītā - adhiṣṭhite |
tasyātītaṃ paraṃ bhāvaṃ tadūrdhve padam avyayam || 1-153 ||
evaṃ bindukalā jñeyā nādaśaktyātmikā tu yā |
vyāpinyāś ca kalāyāś ca vyāptivyāpakabhedataḥ || 1-154 || (svata: vyāpinyādyātmikā yāśca vyāpyavyāpakabhedataḥ |)
prāṇaike saṃsthitāḥ sarvāḥ prāṇānte tu layaṃ smṛtam | (svata: prāṇaikasaṃsthitāḥ sarvāḥ ṣaṭtyāgāt saptame layaḥ | tasa 1/152-155ab = svata 4/243-246ab) 
kalādhvā evam ākhyāto varṇādhvānaṃ nibodhataḥ || 1-155 ||
varṇāḥ śabdātmakāḥ sarve jagaty asmin carācare |
sthitāḥ pañcāśabhedena śāstreṣv anantakoṭiṣu || 1-156 ||
śabdaḥ prāṇaḥ samākhyātas tasmād varṇās tu prāṇataḥ |
utpadyanti layaṃ yānti yatra śabdo layaṃ gataḥ || 1-157 ||
śabdātīto varārohe tattvena saha yujyate |
muktaḥ sarvagato devi dharmādharmavivarjitaḥ || 1-158 ||
nādho nirīkṣate bhūyaḥ śivatattve gato yadā |
adho yāti adharmeṇa dharmeṇordhvo vrajet punaḥ || 1-159 ||
vijñānena dvayaṃ tyaktvā sarvagatvaṃ bhaved iha | (tasa 1/155cd-160ab = svata 4/246cd-251ab)
varṇādhvānaṃ samākhyātaṃ pādādhvānam ihocyate || 1-160 ||
ekāśītiḥ padā ye tu vidyārāje vyavasthitāḥ |
padā varṇātmikās te'pi varṇāḥ prāṇātmakāḥ smṛtāḥ || 1-161 || (svata: varṇātmakāni tānyatra varṇāḥ prāṇātmakāḥ sthitāḥ |)
tasmād evaṃ padāḥ sarve sthitāḥ prāṇakrameṇa tu |
padādhvā evam ākhyāto mantrādhvānaṃ nibodha me || 1-162 || (tasa 1/160cd-162 = svata 4/251cd-253)
mantraikādaśikā yā ca sā ca śaktyā vyavasthitā |
sa caikādaśatattvānte prāṇe carati nityaśaḥ || 1-163 ||
akāraś ca ukāraś ca makāro bindur eva ca |
ardhacandro nirodhī ca nādaṃ nādāntam eva ca || 1-164 ||
śaktiś ca vyāpinī caiva samanaikādaśa smṛtaḥ |
unmanā tu tadātītā tasyātītaṃ nirāmayam || 1-165 ||
mantrā evaṃ sthitāḥ prāṇe śaktyoccāram athocyate | (tasa 1/163-166ab = svata 4/254-257ab)
hakāras tu smṛtaḥ prāṇaḥ supravṛtto halākṛtiḥ || 1-166 ||
akāreṇa śiroyukto varṇoccāraṃ bhavet sphuṭam | (svata: akāreṇa yadā yukta ukāracaraṇena tu | makāramātrayā yukto varṇoccāro bhavet sphuṭaḥ | tasa 1/167ab = svata 4/258)
binduśiraḥsamāyogāt svasvaratvaṃ prapadyate || 1-167 ||
nādasya vadanaṃ proktaṃ nādaḥ śabdam udīrayet |
anenaiva tu yogena ātmā sa puruṣocyate || 1-168 ||
brahmaviṣvīśamārgeṇa carate sarvajantuṣu |śaktitattve layaṃ yāti vijñānenordhvato vrajet || 1-169 ||

śaktyoccāraḥ samākhyātaḥ kāraṇatyāgam ucyate | (tasa 1/166cd-171ab = svata 4/257cd-262)
hakāraḥ prāṇaśaktyātmā akāro brahmavācakaḥ || 1-171 ||
hṛdi tyāgaḥ bhavet tasya ukāro viṣṇuvācakaḥ |
kaṇṭhatyāgaṃ bhavet tasya makāro rudravācakaḥ || 1-172 ||
tālumadhye tyajet so'pi viṣṇuś caiva svaraḥ svayam |
tyāgaṃ tasya bhruvor madhye nādavācyaḥ sadāśivaḥ || 1-173 ||
lalāṭād yāva mūrddhāntaṃtyāgaṃ tasya vidhīyate | 
śaktis tu vyāpinī samanā tāsāṃ vācyaḥ śivo bhavet || 1-174 ||
mūrdhni madhye tyajec chaktiṃ tadūrdhve vyāpinīṃ tyajet |
samanāṃ unmanāṃ tyajya tataś caiva layaḥ smṛtaḥ || 1-175 ||

pañcabhūtātmabhuvanāḥ kāraṇaiḥ samadhiṣṭhitāḥ |

samanādhyānayogena yogī sarvagato vrajet | (tasa 1/181cd-185ab = svata 4/273-276)

yaiḥ susaṃsthitakāleṣu sarvaṃ carati vāṅmayam |

muhurtaś ca ahorātraṃ pakṣo māsa ṛtus tathā |
ayanaṃ vatsaraṃ caiva yugaṃ manvantaraṃ tathā || 1-192 ||

vyāpinyaṃśe paraṃ kālaṃ taṃ tattvāṃśe parityajet || 1-193 ||
sa saptadaśamo jñeyaḥ parārdhaḥ parataḥ sthitaḥ |

sarvakālas tu kālasya vyāpakaḥ param avyayaḥ |
unmanyante parā yojyā kālas tatra na vidyate || 1-195 ||

tac ca nityoditaṃ prāpya tatsamo jāyate sadā || 1-196 ||
kālatyāgaṃ bhaved eva śūnyabhāvam athocyate | (tasa 1/195-197ab = svata 4/286cd-288)
ūrdhvaśūnyam adhaḥ śūnyaṃ madhyaśūnyaṃ tṛtīyakam || 1-197 || 
śūnyatrayāvalambena adhordhvamadhyataḥ punaḥ | vyāpinīśūnyaṃ samanā cātra pañcamam || 1-198 ||
unmanyāyās tathā ṣaṣṭhaṃ ṣaḍ ete saṃvyavasthitāḥ |
tattvenādhiṣṭhitāḥ sarve sā māyāpi phalapradā || 1-199 ||
ṣaṭśūnye tāni saṃtyajya saptame tu layaṃ kuru | 
tac chūnyaṃ tu paraṃ sūkṣmaṃ sarvāvasthavivarjitam || 1-200 ||
aśūnyaṃ śūnyam ity uktaṃ śūnyaṃ cābhāvam ucyate |
abhāvaṃ tat samuddiṣṭaṃ yatra bhāvāḥ kṣayaṃ gatāḥ || 1-201 || 
sattāmātraṃ paraṃ śāntaṃ tat kathaṃ kim avasthitam | (tasa 1/197-202ab = svata 4/289-293)
yatra yatra na bhāvas tu sthūlānyo'pi vyavasthitaḥ || 1-202 ||

sa eva vyāpya sthūlāni sthūlopādhiśca sa bhavet || 1-203 ||

taṃ prāpya tatsamatvaṃ ca bhavanā nātra saṃśayaḥ || 1-204 || 
śūnyavādaṃ samākhyātaṃ tvatpriyārthaṃ surārcite | (tasa 1/202cd-205ab = svata4/294-296)
11a kh] sāṃprataṃ śaktisaṃsthaṃ tu yathā bhavati tacchṛṇu || 1-205 ||
avarṇā varṇayogena yathā sā saṃsthitānaghe |
tathāhaṃ kathayiṣyāmi niścayaṃ tu sulocane || 1-206 ||
pūrvaṃ ye kathitā mantrā saptakoṭirasaṃkhyayā |
gopitāste purā devi varṇarūpāvatāritāḥ || 1-207 ||
tena te na prasiddhyanti japtvā koṭiśatairapi |
5a] mantrāṇāṃ jīvabhūtā tu yā sthitā śaktiravyayā || 1-208 ||
tayā hīnā varārohe niṣphalā śaradāmbudāḥ |
devānāṃ ca ṛṣīṇāṃ ca yakṣagandharvakinnarām || 1-209 ||
siddhānāṃ nāgasaṃghānāṃ eteṣāṃ yogināṃ nṛṇām |
teṣāṃ tat gopitaṃ bhadre bhaktihīnā narādhamāḥ || 1-210 ||
na jānanti guruṃ devaṃ śāstroktasamayāstathā |
dambhakauṭilyaniratā lolārthe kriyavarjitāḥ || 1-211 ||
anena kāraṇārthena mayā vīryaṃ pragopitam |
tena guptena deveśe śeṣā varṇāstu kevalāḥ || 1-212 ||
yā sā tu mātṛkā devi parā tejasamanvitā |
tayā vyāptaṃ jagat kṛtsnaṃ ābrahmabhuvanāntikam || 1-213 ||
6b g] tatrasthaṃ tu yadā deva vyāpitvena surārcite |
avarṇastho yathā varṇo sthitaḥ sarvagataḥ prabhuḥ || 1-214 ||
tathā te kathayiṣyāmi nirṇayārthaṃ surārcite |
yā sā śaktiḥ parā sūkṣmā nirācāreti kīrtitā || 1-215 ||
11b kh] hṛdbinduṃ veṣṭayitvā tu prasuptabhujagākṛtiḥ |
tatra suptā mahābhāge na kiñcinmanyate ume || 1-216 ||
candrāgniravinakṣatrairbhuvanāni caturdaśaḥ |
udare kṣipya sā devi viṣamūrccheva yā gatā || 1-217 ||
prabuddhā sā ninādena pareṇa jñānarūpiṇā |
mathitā codarasthena bindunā varavarṇini || 1-218 ||
tāvaddhi yatra vegena mathanaṃ śaktivigrahe |
bhede tu prathamotpattau binduvaite pravarcasaḥ || 1-219 ||
tena bindormathitvā tu kalā sūkṣmā tu kuṇḍalī |
catuṣkalamayo binduḥ śaktyodaragataḥ prabhuḥ || 1-220 ||
madhyamasthānayogena ṛjutvaṃ jāyate guruḥ |
jyeṣṭhā śaktiḥ smṛtā sā tu bindudvayasumadhyagā || 1-221 ||
bindunā kṣobhamāyātā rekhaivāmṛtakuṇḍalī |
rekhinī nāma sā jñeyā umau bindu vimadhyagā || 1-222 ||
tripathā sā samākhyātā raudrī nāmena gīyate |
nirodhī sā samuddiṣṭā mokṣamārganirodhinī || 1-223 ||
śaśāṅkaśakalākārā ambikā cārdhacandrikā |
ekā eva parā śaktistridhā sā tu prajāyate || 1-224 ||
ebhyo yuktaviyuktebhyaḥ saṃjātā navavargajāḥ |
navadhā tu smṛtā sā tu navavargopalakṣitā || 1-225 ||
12a kh] pañcamantragatā devi sadyādibhiryathākramam |
tena pañcavidhā proktā jñātavyā suranāyike || 1-226 ||
svaradvādaśagā devi dvādaśasthā udāhṛtā |
akārādikṣakārāntā sthitā pañcāśabhedataḥ || 1-227 ||
ekāṇavā hṛddeśasthā kaṇṭhe jñeyā dvirāṇavā |
trirāṇavā tu vijñeyā jihvāmūle yadā sthitā || 1-228 ||
jihvāgre varṇaniṣpattirbhavati tu na saṃśayaḥ |
evaṃ śabdasya niṣpattiḥ śabdavyāptaṃ carācaram || 1-229 ||
śire raudrīṃ nyaset pūrvaṃ vaktre vāmā prakīrtitā |
ambikā bāhurityuktā jyeṣṭhā caivāyudhaṃ smṛtam || 1-230 ||
śrīkaṇṭho hi samākhyātaḥ vyāpakaḥ parameśvaraḥ |
yathā hyekaṃ tathā sarve ṣoḍaśaiva mahānilāḥ || 1-231 ||
7a g] pañcaviṃśat tataḥ paścāt tasyordhve tu nava smṛtāḥ |
anena kramayogeṇa pañcāśānāṃ samudbhavaḥ || 1-232 ||
kathitā devadevena kāryakāraṇabhedataḥ |
evaṃ dvādaśasāhasre pṛthagdhāraprakīrtitam || 1-233 ||
nāmāni rudrasaṃghasya sūcitānīha pārvati |
ete'pi vyāpakā nityāḥ śabdākārā guṇānvitāḥ || 1-234 ||
vācyavācakabhedo'yaṃ na cetyaḥ parataḥ sthitaḥ |
vyavahārārthe mayā sṛṣṭā śabdaviśleṣaṇaṃ prati || 1-235 ||
5b] na ca rudrairvinā śabdo nārtho nāpi ca tairgatiḥ |
12b kh] śarīraṃ śabdarāśaistu pañcāśāṃśaiḥ samujjvalam || 1-236 ||
rudrapañcāśikā hyeṣā mātṛkā ca prapadyate | 
tasmād devi vijānīyāt sarvaṃ taṃ mātṛkodbhavam || 1-237 ||
na mātṛkāt parā saṃjñā na mantro mātṛkāt param | 
sthūlasūkṣmavibhāgena mātṛkādehasaṃbhavam || 1-238 ||
yatra cotpadyate śabdo layaṃ tatraiva gacchati | 
jalamadhye yathā bindurutpannaśca vinaśyati || 1-239 ||
tadvadeva hi śabdātmā manobindau vilīyate |
śire raudrīṃ parityajya jyeṣṭhāmāyudhamutsṛjet || 1-240 ||
ambikā bāhudeśe tu sarvaṃ tyaktavā vyavasthitā |
kevalā kuṇḍalī jñeyā nānyaṃ kiñcit pradṛśyate || 1-241 ||
evaṃ tu bhavate nāśamutpattiśca tathaiva hi | 
utpattiṃ ca vināśaṃ tu yo jñāyati varānane || 1-242 ||
sa bhavecchaktimān vīro yathā tvaṃ ca priyā mama |
bhavate sādhakendrastu pūjyaḥ pūjyatamo bhavet || 1-243 ||
vāmā brahmā smṛto bhadre jyeṣṭhā viṣṇuḥ prakīrtitaḥ |
raudrī rudro varārohe aṃvikā īśvaro viduḥ || 1-244 ||
saṃyogaḥ śabdarūpastu śivo nādātmakaḥ paraḥ |
pañcakāraṇako devi vyāpakatve vyavasthitaḥ || 1-245 ||
varṇe varṇe pradṛśyante śaktayaśca tathaiva hi |
icchā raudrī samākhyātā jñānaṃ vāmā prakīrtitā || 1-246 ||
kriyā jyeṣṭhā samuddiṣṭā jñeyā ambā śubhekṣaṇe |
13a kh] udayo vāmayā jñeyo jyeṣṭhā madhyandinaṃ smṛtam || 1-247 ||
sandhyā raudrī tu vijñeyā tadaivāstamanaṃ bhavet |
recako vāmayā proktaḥ pūrako jyeṣṭhayā viduḥ || 1-248 ||
kumbhako raudrayā jñeyaḥ svabhāvasthā ca ambikā |
udbhave kuṇḍalī jñeyā vāmā sā samudāhṛtā || 1-249 ||
viśleṣasthā tathā jyeṣṭhā laye raudrī nigadyate |
layātītā tathā ambā jñātavyā suranāyike || 1-250 ||
7b g] nābhisthā kuṇḍalī jñeyā prasuptabhujagākṛtiḥ |
kaṇṭhasthānordhvato raudrī yatra rudro jagatpatiḥ || 1-251 ||
pravāhe jyeṣṭhagā madhye vindudvayavimadhyagā |
ambikā brahmarandhrasthā jñātavyā tattvavedine || 1-252 ||
samanā kuṇḍalī jñeyā jāgrāvasthā prakīrtitā |
jyeṣṭhā svapnagatā caiva raudrī conmanakārikā || 1-253 ||
suṣuptākhyaṃ padaṃ devi unmanaṃ tu taducyate |
ambikā tu manātītā turyasthānagatā śubhā || 1-254 ||
kuṇḍalī prathamā jñeyā dvitīyā tu manonmanī |
vindvīnāma tṛtīyā tu triśaktikalitaṃ jagat || 1-255 ||
udbhave caiva viśleṣe laye caiva tṛtīyakā |
layātītaṃ paraṃ śāntaṃ sarvagaṃ parameśvaram || 1-256 ||
13b kh] kriyākāraṇanirmuktaṃ heyopādeyavarjitam |
jñātvā caiva varārohe na bhūyo janmamāpnuyāt || 1-257 ||
grahaṇaṃ kuṇḍalisthasya jñātavyā tu vipaścitaiḥ |
amiṣaṃ tu yathā khasthaḥ paśyate śakuniḥ priye || 1-258 ||
kṣipramākarṣayed tadvad vegena mahatena tu |
tadvadeva hi yogīndro manobinduṃ prakarṣayet || 1-259 ||
yathā śaro nalīnastho yantreṇātāḍya ] dhāvati |
tathā bindurvarārohe uccāreṇānudhāvati || 1-260 ||
khasthaṃ khakaraṇaṃ kṛtvā khamukhaṃ yojya khaḥ sthiraḥ |
kūrmaṃ tu maṅgale yojyaṃ svarāntaśca dvibhiryutam || 1-261 ||
preraṇaṃ tu udānena yāvat tat khamukhaṃ gataḥ |
maṅgalaṃ kūrmasaṃyuktaṃ śarāstreṇaiva śodhanam || 1-262 ||
śuddhyate tu na sandeho dvijamārge varānane |
śuddhenaiva tu mārgeṇa grahaṇaṃ caiva lakṣayet || 1-263 ||
manasā grahaṇaṃ kāryaṃ śaktyādhāragatasya tu |
6a] pratyayo tu bhavet tasya grahaṇasya varānane || 1-264 ||
nābhisthaṃ kampanaṃ jñeyaṃ jṛmbhaṇaṃ mukhamākulam |
aśrupātaṃ ca bhrūbhaṅgaṃ cittavyākulatā tathā || 1-265 ||
ete tu pratyayā nābhau kaṇṭhasthasyādhunā śṛṇu |
kaṇṭhastho dhunute gātraṃ nādaṃ muñcatyanekadhā || 1-266 ||
śiraḥkampaḥ pralāpaśca hūṃkāraśca mahāsvanaḥ |
14a kh] tatordhvaṃ tu yadātītaṃ rudrasthānaṃ varāmbike || 1-267 ||
tadā tu pratyayāstasya te śṛṇuṣva samāhitā |
rudrastho vedate śāstraṃ mudrāmantrāṇyanekadhā || 1-268 ||
udgāthayati śāstrāṇi aśrutānyapi sādhakaḥ |
tasyordhve tu mahādevi visargākhyaṃ padaṃ bhavet || 1-269 ||
visargasthaṃ yadā devi tadā śabdaṃ śṛṇoti sa |
sparśaṃ ca śītalaṃ vetti himavacchikharā iva || 1-270 ||
śaktisthānaṃ tato devi sādākhyenaikatāṃ gatam |
8a g] śaktisthamunmanatvaṃ hi viṣayāṇāṃ varānane || 1-271 ||
aṇimādiguṇāvāptirbhavate ca na saṃśayaḥ |
tallayo yadi tatraiva sthirībhavati śobhane || 1-272 ||
tadā cotpatate śīghraṃ satyameva na saṃśayaḥ |
randhrasthaṃ sarvagaṃ jñeyaṃ tadātītaṃ tu vyāpakam || 1-273 ||
ghaṭamadhye yathākāśaṃ paricchinnaṃ pravartate |
evaṃ śarīravicchinnaṃ vyāpakastvanubhāsate || 1-274 ||
ghaṭe bhinne yathā devi ekabhāvaṃ tu gacchati |
bahirantaryasadbhāva eka evaṃ prabhāsate || 1-275 ||
tadvadeva hi bhūtātmā pāśamuktaḥ prabhāsate |
yathā dīpo ghaṭastho hi ekadeśaprakāśakaḥ || 1-276 ||
tathā cātmā tu liṅgastho manavyāpārapeśalaḥ |
ghaṭe bhinne yathā devi sarvatraiva prakāśakaḥ || 1-277 ||
tathā pāśairmano mukto vyāpako bhavate priye |
14b kh] evaṃ vyāptirmayā khyātā tava devi sureśvari || 1-278 ||
ātmavyāptirbhavet teṣāṃ śivavyāptirataḥ param |
śeṣabhāvena bandhānāṃ sarvopādhivivarjitaḥ || 1-279 ||
aviditvā paraṃ tattvaṃ śivatvaṃ kalpitaṃ tu yaiḥ |
te nātmāśāsakāḥ śaivāḥ na te yānti śivaṃ padam || 1-280 ||
ātmatattvagatiṃ yānti ātmatattvātmarañjitāḥ |
ye sākārānyupāsanti kalpayitvā tu devatāḥ || 1-281 ||
tāsāṃ padaṃ labhantyete na muktaḥ paramārthataḥ |
tasmāt sarvaṃ parityajya svabhāvasthamupāsayet || 1-282 ||
svabhāvasthaṃ punarvakṣye yathā bhavati tacchṛṇu |
na nirodho na coccāro na lakṣyo naiva yojanā || 1-283 ||
svarūpasthaṃ tato jñātvā sa mukto nātra saṃśayaḥ |
saṃkalpastu mahābandho ucchedo bhāvakāraṇam || 1-284 ||
taṃ tyaktvā mucyate yogī savikalpastu badhyate |
na mano nāpi mantavyo mantā ca na vibhāvyate || 1-285 ||
yāvat saṃkalpayed deva tāvad bandhaḥ pravartate |
na bindurnaiva nādaśca na cāraścādhvaṣaṭkagaḥ || 1-286 ||
na śaktyuccāramārgasya kāraṇatyāgakālataḥ |
na śūnyabhāvanābhāvaṃ na śaktiḥ śiva eva ca || 1-287 ||
avidyāvāsanā hyeṣā saṃsārabhayabandhanī |
yannāsti tatra santoṣaḥ prāyaḥ kaścit kariṣyati || 1-288 ||
15a kh] taṃ tyaktvā tyajyate sarvaṃ āśāpāśamaśeṣataḥ |
āśā eva mahābandho yayā vyāpto'khilaṃ jagat || 1-289 ||
yāvanna tyajyate sā vai tāvacchāntirna vidyate |
kā sāśā mokṣavādīnāṃ yatra sarvaṃ kṣayaṃ gatam || 1-290 ||
mokṣe'pi yasya notkaṇṭhaḥ sa mokṣamadhigacchati |
6b] 8b g] na mokṣasya bhavet sthānaṃ na dhyānaṃ na ca kalpanā || 1-291 ||
sarvatra vitatā dṛṣṭiḥ sa mokṣo mokṣavādinām |
mokṣo nāmamanirdeśyaṃ ūhāpohavikalpanā || 1-292 ||
sa vidyāpanāyeta avidyāṃ tu parityajet |
tadākāraṃ jagat sarvaṃ tasyākāraṃ na kiñcana || 1-293 ||
nirākārastu bhāvasthaḥ kartavyo'tra manīṣibhiḥ |
tattvabhāvaḥ sa bhūtākhyaḥ sargo yatra pravatate || 1-294 ||
ānandaṃ brahmaṇo rūpaṃ na vibheti kadācana |
sarvaṃ sarvātmakaṃ jñātvā kartavyaṃ nāsti kiñcana || 1-295 ||
astitvamiti ced bhāvastadā bandho na saṃśayaḥ |
svayamevātmanātmānaṃ kośakāraḥ kṛmiryathā || 1-296 ||
badhnāti unnato bhāvaḥ tathā caiva tu bhāvanaiḥ |
sa vimucyastayorbaddhaḥ kimabaddhasya mokṣaṇam || 1-297 ||
sahajāgantukā bandhā dvaitabhāvenādhiṣṭhitāḥ |
kalpitā svayamevātra manaḥ saṃkalpalakṣaṇam || 1-298 ||
15b kh] mānasaṃ tu parityajya yat kiñcid vāṅmayaṃ priye |

śuddhāśuddheṣu bhāveṣu mano yatra pravartate |
varṇāvarṇavikalpeṣu bhakṣyābhakṣye tathaiva ca || 1-300 ||

tatkṣaṇāt pratyayaṃ yānti tadvacchūnyo mano viduḥ |
śaṅkhaśabdo yadā devi śrūyate vipulasvanaḥ || 1-308 ||
tatkṣaṇāt kṣīyate taṃ tu tadvacchūnyo mano viduḥ |
arthavairāgyamāśṛtya kaṣṭaiścāndrāyaṇādibhiḥ || 1-309 ||
16a kh] kṣapayet taṃ tu mokṣāya saindhave conmanātmani |
bahubhirgṛhya vairāgyamātmānaṃ kṣapitaṃ tu yaiḥ || 1-310 ||
9a g] teṣāmeko'pi nāstyatra gato nirupapattikaṃ |
brahmacaryaṃ taponiṣṭhā śaucādyā ye ca saṃyamāḥ || 1-311 ||
na taistu siddhyate mokṣo'bhāva evātra kāraṇam |
agnidarbhāśca mantrāśca hotā hotavyameva ca || 1-312 ||
praṇītā viṣṭarāścaiva dīkṣā dhyānaprakalpanā |

alakṣasya kuto lakṣo amanasya kuto manaḥ || 1-314 ||

7a] yāvad bhāvayate jñānaṃ vijñānādhārakevalam |

16b kh] ghaṭavad bhaṃgurākāraṃ vidyuddarśanasannibham || 1-320 ||

devyuvāca 

mahat kautūhalaṃ deva mamotpannaṃ mahāprabho || 1-321 ||
yannāsti tatra ko lakṣaḥ alakṣasya kuto gatiḥ |
dhyānaṃ pūjā japo homaṃ nānārūpā tu bhāvanā || 1-322 ||
kimarthaṃ sā tvayā deva kathitā parameśvara |

śrībhairava uvāca 

nānārūpāṇi varṇāni lakṣāṇi vividhāni ca || 1-323 ||
manaḥprasādhanārthāya sarvametat prakāśitam |
mano hi cañcalo nityaṃ nirāśrayamatīndriyam || 1-324 ||
sa kṛtaṃ śakyate dhartuṃ yasya vāyvādhikā gatiḥ |
yāvad bhāvayate ekaṃ tāvadanyamupasthitam || 1-325 ||
na dhyānaṃ na ca vā lakṣaṃ niṣpadyeta kadācana |
anena kāraṇārthena sarvaṃ tyajyamaśeṣataḥ || 1-326 || 
manodbhavaṃ tu yallakṣaṃ dhyānaṃ vā kalpanātmakam |

nāhamasmi na cānyo'sti advaitakriyayā rataḥ |
yāvanna vindate hyevaṃ tāvat tasya na kiñcana || 1-328 ||
ahamiti stuyo arthaḥ sa ca baddho guṇatraye |
yāvanna tyajate devi sa niruddho bhaviṣyati || 1-329 ||
9b g] 17a kh] icchā dveṣaḥ sukhaṃ duḥkhaṃ virāgo mānavā tathā |

19a kh] parastato prasiddhatvādanitya iti iṣyate || 1-370 ||
8a] anekayonisambandhastatprapañcāt pravatate |
kathaṃ prapañcahīno'pi gamyate tattvadarśibhiḥ || 1-371 ||
acintyaścāprameyaśca cintāhīnastathā paraḥ |
prahīṇāśeṣadoṣātmā kathaṃ buddhvā prabodhakaḥ || 1-372 ||
ākāśasya guṇaḥ śabdastasya sotprekṣikā śrutiḥ |
yadyeva paratattvaṃ tu grāhyānandaḥ kathaṃ bhavet || 1-373 ||
ajño nityaḥ paśuścātmā śivo'nyaḥ smṛtigocaraḥ |
kathaṃ ca jñāyate tena nirupāyo nirañjanaḥ || 1-374 ||
āgamaścedupāyo'sti nirupāyakṣatirbhavet |
saupāyaśca kathaṃ devo vāgakṣātītagocaraḥ || 1-375 ||
upāyaḥ sa manāgamyo manaścendriyasaṃjñakaḥ |
indriyātītatā tasmin kathaṃ saṃbhavate prabhoḥ || 1-376 ||
karaṇairyaḥ paricchedyaḥ sthūlaḥ sa ca vinaśvaraḥ |
yo'vināśī kathaṃ devaḥ paramātmā bhaviṣyati || 1-377 ||
saṃvittiścet pare tattve saṃvittiḥ savikalpakā | 
nirvikalpe vikalpasya praveśaḥ kiṃ kṛto bhavet || 1-378 ||
buddho jinaḥ śivo hyātmā viṣṇurbrahmeti vācakaḥ |
nāmni vipratipattistu upāsye'jñaḥ kathaṃ bhavet || 1-379 ||
19b kh] viṣeṇa mūrcchito yastu nirvikalpastathā bhave |
garbhavāse susuptaśca bhidyamānaśca pādapaḥ || 1-380 ||
muktirādau bhavet teṣāmitareṣāṃ tu saṃśayaḥ |
ko jānāti vikalpasya vināśo nāsti vā na vā || 1-381 ||
kasmāt kathaṃ kadā kasya kena kaḥ kva pratiṣṭhitaḥ |
iti śaktyaḥ na yad vaktuṃ śaśaśṛṅgastu kevalam || 1-382 ||
śṛṅgo'pyasti śaśo'pyasti pṛthaganyatra darśanāt |
saṃbandho naiva śṛṅgeṇa abhāvācchaśamastake || 1-383 ||
indriyāṇāṃ ca cittasya parasparavirodhinā |
kadācinna kvacid dṛṣṭaḥ saṃbandhaḥ paramātmanā || 1-384 ||
durvijñeyastadāsau tu yadi jñātaḥ kadācana |
calatvānna sthiraṃ cittaṃ tatra muktiḥ kathaṃ bhavet || 1-385 ||
jñānaṃ mānaṃ tathā sadyaṃ trividhaṃ paripaśyate |
sarvajñasya jagat sarvaṃ jñānenaitat prasūyate || 1-386 ||
atīndriyamacintyaṃ ca niṣprapañcātmalakṣaṇam |
susūkṣmaśivanāthasya jñānātmavapuriṣyate || 1-387 ||
yenātmā jātadehastu balavān priyadarśanaḥ |
darśayitvātmano dharmaṃ svecchayā yāti tatpadam || 1-388 ||
11a g] kṣaṇāt sa dṛśyate rūpaṃ kṣaṇājjñāne pralīyate |
yātīpsitaṃ prakṛtyaiva tena sadyaḥ prakīrtitaḥ || 1-389 ||
chitvā bhittvāpi mūlāni na tatra phaladarśanam |
akasmād dṛśyate tacca parecchātatsamudbhavaḥ || 1-390 ||
bījād vṛkṣastato bījā vṛkṣādanyonyasaṃbhavaḥ |
svabhāvenaiva cotpattiranādinidhanātmikā || 1-391 ||
phalāntaṃ cūtavṛkṣasya bhramarāṇāṃ pradarśanam |
āmalā nānyavṛkṣeṣu nāsti bījaparamparā || 1-392 ||
bhūmau jāle tathākāśe virodhotpattidarśanāt |
niyatotpattirapyatra śūnyāt sūtiḥ prasādhayet || 1-393 ||
svena svena hi rūpeṇa viparīte na saṃbhavaḥ |
bhuvaneśena cotpadyastenaiva niyamaḥ kṛtaḥ || 1-394 ||
jñātvā nirviṣayo yastu nirvikalpo vidyādhinā |
mūrcchito'jñaḥ kathaṃ muktaḥ saṃsārī saṃsaratyasau || 1-395 ||
gṛhasaṃsāravāsasya vikalpaḥ savikalpakaḥ |
gṛhasthaḥ saṃbhavatyātmā kuṭumbī tadanantaraḥ || 1-396 ||
manobuddhirahaṃkāracittānāṃ yatra saṃkṣayaḥ |
8b] taṃ tattvaṃ sarvasiddhīnāṃ nilayaṃ na vinaśvaram || 1-397 ||
nirasto bhāvanāsaṅgaḥ saṃsārocchittikārakaḥ |
yasya bhāvasvabhāvena nīyate tatpadasya tu || 1-398 ||
ahaṃkāro vibuddho tu buddhirmanasi tanmayaḥ |
citrācitraṃ layaṃ yāvat tāvat tat paramaṃ padam || 1-399 ||
saṃsāraḥ savituryāgaścandrayāgastathāparaḥ |
ubhau yasmāt tatastasyāviṣṭiḥ vijñānasāgare || 1-400 ||
na saṃsāro na nirvāṇaṃ manyate tattvadarśinaḥ |
kathaṃ teṣāṃ manaḥ śīghraṃ calitvā yāti tatpadam || 1-401 ||
vāyuryathā calitvā tu muktvākāśaṃ sa gacchati |
tathaiva yogināṃ cittaṃ muktvā tattvaṃ tu gacchati || 1-402 ||
manaśca calanātmā tu calanaṃ tasya tena ca |
tasya nāśaḥ kathaṃ hyātmācalo bhavati niścalaḥ || 1-403 ||
vañcitā svena cittena karmayogaratā narāḥ |
nirbuddhiyogatātparyānmucyante nātra saṃśayaḥ || 1-404 ||
ālambena gataṃ jñeyaṃ niyatārthāspadaṃ bhavet |
sarvālambanasaṃsthaṃ tu tadvivekaṃ ca sarvadā || 1-405 ||
paramārthaparijñānaṃ grahaṇāsaktacetasām | 
ālambanagataṃ jñānaṃ bālānāṃ tadudāhṛtam || 1-406 ||
ālambanakṣayaṃ yāvad bhūyaḥ kālāvaśeṣikān |
jñānaṃ vive jitendriyaḥ? samyak sarvasiddhipadaṃ bhavet || 1-407 || 
paramārthaparijñānaṃ grahaṇānandacetasaḥ |
yoginaḥ paramārthena sarvajñatve udāhṛtāḥ || 1-408 ||
11b g] avibhāvitasaṃbaddhā hyavasthā sā parā smṛtā |
pareṇaiveha saṃbaddhā muktireṣāṃ tathāpi sā || 1-409 ||
sambandhaḥ sukhaduḥkhātmā dravyendriyasamudbhavaḥ |
21a kh] saṃsṛtiḥ sukhaduḥkhatvādābhyāṃ tyaktaḥ sa muktavān || 1-410 ||
nirmalo'pi layāsaktaḥ salayaḥ samalaḥ smṛtaḥ |
asvasthaḥ saṃsaratyātmā svastho muktaḥ prakīrtitaḥ || 1-411 ||
sanimittaṃ ca vijñānaṃ anityaṃ cānyabādhanāt |
nirnimittaṃ ca nityaṃ ca vijñānaṃ muktireva sā || 1-412 ||
taṃ jñānaṃ sā parāvasthā sā muktiḥ sā ca saṃgatiḥ |
nirnimittamidaṃ jñānaṃ sarvamasmāt pravartate || 1-413 ||
nirnimittatvamasyaiva tasmāt sṛṣṭipravartanam |
na nirodhaḥ sadā yasmāt pratyakṣeṇa tu bhūyate || 1-414 ||
yadīndriyaparicchinnaṃ tattvaṃ yadi bhaviṣyati |
sarve tattvavido jātā guruṇānyena kiṃ tadā || 1-415 ||
indriyairupalabdhaṃ yat tat tattvaṃ na bhaved yadi |
tadāstisvasya vaktavyaṃ pramāṇaṃ kimataḥ param || 1-416 ||
cakṣurādiparicchinnaṃ yat sthūlaṃ tad vinaśvaram |
tena vijñātamātreṇa kathaṃ tattvavido bhavet || 417 ||
sthūlasya yā parā koṭiḥ koṭimuktā ca sā parā |
anayorantaraṃ kiñcit susūkṣmamapi vidyate || 1-418 ||
nāndhaḥ paśyati rūpāṇi anyacittastathaiva ca |
smaritavyābhicāritvāt samudāyo'pi nāstyataḥ || 1-419 ||
na cakṣuḥ paśyate rūpaṃ na manaḥ samudāyakaḥ |
etaddhi paramaṃ guhaṃ yatra līno na gāhate || 1-420 ||
kiñcit prakṣipate tasmin nāpareyamataḥ param |
21b kh] viparītaṃ tu taṃ paśyed viparītamaninditam || 1-421 ||
sarvakārakabhedena sthito hyeko maheśvaraḥ |
naiva kārakabhedena bhinnarūpatvamāpnuyāt || 1-422 ||
yena rūpeṇa saṃgrāhastenaiva paramārthataḥ |
grāhakatvamavāpnoti kulajā gatirīdṛśī || 1-423 ||
advayo'tha parijñānāt pradhvastāśeṣakalmaṣaḥ |
nirmalaṃ niścalaṃ caiva nirvātagṛhadīpavat || 1-424 ||
9a] prāktanecchāvaśāt samyag gatā bhogena saṃpadā |
bhunakti sarvasiddhīni pararūpo'pi sarvagaḥ || 1-425 ||
na sthitaṃ na mṛtaṃ tasmān na jātaṃ na tiraskṛtam |
vastu vaktavyarūpeṇa paramārthena saṃsthitaḥ || 1-426 ||
yadā jātaṃ kathaṃ nāsti yadi nāsti kathaṃ sthitiḥ |
asthitasya kathaṃ nāśaṃ parasparavirodhitaḥ || 1-427 ||
yannaṣṭaṃ tasya cotpattiryasyotpattiḥ sthitiḥ sthitā |
12a g] asthitistasya nāśoktā nātra kiñcid virodhanam || 1-428 ||
na kāryaṃ kasya vijñānaṃ kāraṇaṃ vāpi kasyacit |
ubhayorekavadbhāvo na kāryaṃ naiva kāraṇam || 1-429 ||
vyavahāro'tha yatraiva jñānaṃ jñeyaṃ svabhāvataḥ |
sāpekṣatvāt tato naṣṭa avācyatvena saṃsthitaḥ || 1-430 ||
laukiko vyavahāro'yaṃ sadā vācyasya gocare | 
niṣprapañce kathaṃ cātra vāṅmano na pravartate || 1-431 ||
22a kh] yoginyo vyavahāro'yamatra saṃgīyate punaḥ |
nādhikāro'tra lokasya rathyāmadhye pravartate || 1-432 ||
brahmā viṣṇau tathā viṣṇu rudre rudro'pi ceśvare |
īśvaro'pi śive so'pi paramātmani saṃsthitaḥ || 1-433 ||
tadā tat paramaṃ guhyaṃ tadā tat paramaṃ padam | 
prāmāṇikavacoyuktyā yatra bādho na vidyate || 1-434 ||
indriyārthotthitaṃ jñānaṃ pratyakṣamiti gamyate |
anumānaṃ ca tatpūrvaṃ kathaṃ tatra tayorgatiḥ || 1-435 ||
grāmād grāmāntaraṃ gacchet madhyadeśaṃ yathā tyajet |
tathaiva devatātyāgāt kramāt tatpadamāpnuyāt || 1-436 || 
upāyaṃ yadgurorvākyaṃ tatpadāvāptilakṣaṇam | 
pramāṇaṃ yogināmeva cakṣuṣādyutthitaṃ na hi || 1-437 || 
evaṃ te kathitaṃ devi rahasyaṃ paramādbhutam |
nāsya parataraṃ kiñcinmokṣasyaiva tu sādhanam || 1-438 ||

bindusthaṃ caiva nādasthaṃ cārasthaṃ cādhvaṣaṭkagam |
śaktyuccāragataṃ cānyaṃ kāraṇatyāgakālagam || 1-439 || 
śūnyabhāvagataṃ śaṃbho śaktisthaṃ hṛdi saṃsthitam | 
svarūpasthaṃ viśeṣeṇa tat sarvamavatāritam || 1-440 || 
sāṃprataṃ varṇasaṃsthaṃ tu yathā bhavati tat katham | 

bhairava uvāca

varṇasthaṃ kathayiṣyāmi śṛṇuṣvāyatalocane || 1-441 ||
22b kh] mātṛkā trayacakrasthā jñātavyā tu vipaścitaiḥ |
ete svarāḥ samākhyātāḥ ṣoḍaśaite mahānilāḥ || 1-442 ||
taṃ cakraṃ ṣoḍaśāraṃ tu agnivātena dīpitam | 
karṇikāyāṃ sthitāścetāḥ śaktiyuktāstu suvrate || 1-443 || 
śaraddīpaśikhākārā nirgatā vyomapañcake | 
dvādaśānte punardevi jvalan taṃ bhāskaraṃ yathā || 1-444 ||
tatra līnā tu sā śaktiramanaskā manonmanī |
nimiṣaṃ yāvad deveśi tiṣṭhate sādhakottamaḥ || 1-445 ||
tāvat stobho bhavet kṣipraṃ mudrābandhaṃ tvanekadhā |
bhāṣā ca vividhākārā dharmaśāstrāṇyanekadhā || 1-446 ||
vedādisarvaśāstrāṇi udgāhayati tatkṣaṇāt |
12b g] atītānāgataṃ caiva pṛṣṭo'sau kathayatyapi || 1-447 ||
utpatet gaganābhogāṃ vidyādharapatirbhavet |
napuṃsakagaṇaṃ tyaktvā dvādaśāramudāhṛtam || 1-448 || 
dvātriṃśadvarṇasaṃyogāt mahācakraṃ prakīrtitam |
evaṃ tu mātṛkāḥ sarvāḥ cakratrayamupāgatāḥ || 1-449 ||
dvādaśāraṃ hṛdi madhye tatra śūryo vyavasthitaḥ |
tāluke ṣoḍaśāraṃ tu yatra sā amṛtāvahā || 1-450 || 
mastake sarvavarṇātmā dvātriṃśaddalasaṃyutaḥ |
9b] hṛccakre udbhavo jñeyo viśleṣo vedhacakragaḥ || 1-451 ||
layastu śiracakrastho jñātavyo viditātmabhiḥ |
grahaṇaṃ nābhideśasthaṃ sattvānāṃ kaṇṭhamāśritam || 1-452 ||
23a kh] yogastatra punardevi laye caiva nirāmaye |
evaṃ jñātvā varārohe yojayecchāśvate pade || 1-453 ||
ātmatattvaṃ hṛdisthaṃ tu vidyā lambakamāśritā | 
śivatattvaṃ layasthaṃ tu brahmarandhragataṃ priye || 1-454 ||
śaktisthaṃ kathitaṃ pūrvaṃ adhvāne tu yathā sthitam |
sāṃprataṃ bhuvanīśastho yathā te kathayāmyaham || 1-455 ||
ekaikaṃ tu yathā varṇaṃ śaktisthaṃ bhavate priye |
cakramālāgataṃ tūrṇaṃ mantrasiddhirjigīṣiṇām || 1-456 ||
tat tathā kathayiṣyāmi bhuvanīśā yathā sthitāḥ | 
sve sve varṇādhikāreṇa vyāptisteṣāṃ yathārthataḥ || 1-457 ||
vyāpakatvaṃ ca deveśi saṃcāraṃ ca ataḥ śṛṇu |
ātmavidyā śivaścaiva tattvatrayavibhāgaśaḥ || 1-458 ||
kāraṇānāṃ tathā tyāgaṃ varṇe varṇe yathā sthitam |
agnisūryākhyacandrākhyā ye cakrāḥ prāk prakīrtitāḥ || 1-459 ||
yoginyo vyāpakāsteṣāṃ pṛthak saṃsthā yathā śṛṇu | 
jvalinī jvālinī jvālā tejasā tejavarcasā || 1-460 || 
tejorminyā tathā tejā tejovatyā tamopahā |
tejonidhiḥ samākhyātā yoginyastvagnicakragāḥ || 1-461 ||
saṃharanti jagatsarvaṃ kālāgnivapuṣodbhavāḥ |
sūryacakrasamudbhūtā yoginyaḥ sāṃprataṃ śṛṇu || 1-462 ||
23b kh] sūryā sūryavatā kāntā svadhā svastiḥ sudhā tathā |
sumanā conmanā kāntā śāśvatī ca tathāparā || 1-463 ||
saṃvartā saṃharī caiva yoginyaḥ sūryacakragāḥ |
dvitīyāvaraṇe jñeyā tṛtīye kathayāmi te || 1-464 ||
śaśinī śāśvatī śāntā sevanī ca śaśiprabhā |
śītā śītavahā kāntā pṛthvī ca priyakārikā || 1-465 ||
pṛthivī pārthivī prītā kṣobhaṇī kṣubhitākṣayā | 
somacakre samākhyātāḥ sṛṣṭestu sthitikārikāḥ || 1-466 ||
13a g] cakre cakre tu draṣṭavyā bhuvaneśapade sthitāḥ |
krīḍanti bhuvaneśānāṃ yogibhiḥ saha cācyutāḥ || 1-467 ||
rudraśaktiḥ varārohe ye rudrā yogibhiḥ saha |
teṣāṃ saṃkhyā na vidyeta bhedena suranāyike || 1-468 ||
somasūryavibhāgena koṭikoṭivibhāgaśaḥ |
krīḍanti tatra te sarve utpattisthitikārakāḥ || 1-469 ||
ekaikā tu yathā khyātā cakre cakre yathā sthitā |
vyāptisteṣāṃ mayā proktā kaulikī tu samāsataḥ || 1-470 ||
akārādi smṛtāścakrāḥ kṣakārāntāvasānikāḥ |
pañcāśacakrā evoktā yairvyāptamakhilaṃ jagat || 1-471 ||
sarve te śaktimadhyasthāḥ jñātavyāstu varānane |
24a kh] na tairvinā bhavenmantro dṛṣṭādṛṣṭārthasādhane || 1-472 ||
ekaikaṃ varṇamuccārya svarairdvādaśabheditam |
anilānalasaṃyuktaṃ bindunā samalaṃkṛtam || 1-473 ||
ātmaśaktiśivān tatra tejorūpān vicintayet |
lolībhūtamidaṃ sarvaṃ śaktisthāne layaṃ gatam || 1-474 ||
sphuratkiraṇasaṃkāśaṃ vidyutkoṭisamujjvalam |
uditārkakarābhāsaṃ kṣayākṣayavivarjitam || 1-475 ||
yadā tallayatāṃ yāti kālajñānaṃ tadā bhavet |
sadāśivapadāvasthaṃ paśyate divyacakṣuṣā || 1-476 ||
ṣaḍvidhādhvā mahādevi bhuvanādyaṃ tathā priye |
10a] adhaḥsthaḥ paśyate sarvamasya cakraprabhāvataḥ || 1-477 ||
ṣaṇmāsādutpatet kṣipraṃ satyameva na saṃśayaḥ |
etaccakraṃ mahāgauri nākhyātaṃ kasyacinmayā || 1-478 ||
tava devi samākhyātaṃ satyaṃ satyaṃ na saṃśayaḥ |
guhyād guhyataraṃ guhyaṃ gopitavyaṃ varānane || 1-479 ||
nāśiṣyāya pradātavyaṃ nāputrāya kadācana |
anyaśāstraratā ye ca kapaṭavratadhāriṇaḥ || 1-480 ||
anyadevatābhaktā ye śaṭhakriyāstathābhūtāḥ |
ḍambhakauṭilyaniratā advaitācāradūṣakāḥ || 1-481 ||
etaiścaiva mahādoṣairye yuktā varavarṇini |
na teṣāṃ dāpayejjñānaṃ kaulikaṃ guṇavattaram || 1-482 ||
24b kh] pramādād dadate yastu tantrāmnāyaṃ priyavrate |
yoginīgaṇamadhyasthaḥ paśurevātra sādhakaḥ || 1-483 ||
upāsannaṃ yadā śiṣyaṃ bhaktiyuktaṃ dṛḍhavratam |
gurudevāgnibhaktaśca śucivrataparāyaṇam || 1-484 ||
evaṃ jñātvā varārohe śiṣyaṃ caiva sulakṣaṇam |
dīkṣākāle parīkṣeta pāśastobhādikaiḥ kramāt || 1-485 ||
sa ca pātaviśeṣeṇa dvividhaḥ parikīrtitaḥ |
purato varṇayiṣye'hamuttamo madhyamottamaḥ || 1-486 ||
parīkṣite tataḥ śiṣye gurudevāgnisannidhau |
13b g] samayān śrāvayet taṃ ca ye ca śāstre pracoditāḥ || 1-487 ||
abhiṣekaṃ tato datvā upadeśaṃ samarpayet |
yathā ekaṃ tathā sarve cakrāḥ pañcāśa suvrate || 1-488 ||
analānilasaṃyuktaṃ astavyastakṛtānaghe |
sarvakarmakarāḥ proktā yadā yonisamīkṛtā || 1-489 ||
mātṛkā pūjitā yena tridhā siddhirlabhatyasau |

iti bhairavaśrotasi mahātantre vidyāpīṭhe saptakoṭipramāṇe śrītantrasadbhāve praśnayogādhikāro nāma prathamaḥ paṭalaḥ || 1 ||



devyuvāca

adya me sapalaṃ janma adya me saphalaṃ tapaḥ |
yena tvaṃ varado mahyaṃ kathitaṃ jñānamuttamam || 2-1 ||
adyāpi saṃśayo nātha mātṛkāyāstu pūjane |
pṛthak cakravibhedena niḥśaṃśayaḥ karaṃ vada || 2-2 ||

25a kh] bhairava uvāca

śṛṇu devi pravakṣyāmi mātṛkāyāstu pūjanam |
śrīkaṇṭha prathamaṃ cakraṃ bheditaṃ dvādaśasvaraiḥ || 2-3 ||
anulomavilomena śaktibījaṃ tu vinyaset |
rañjayet padmagarbheṇa śaraddīpaśikhopamam || 2-4 ||
cintayed hṛdaye devi karṇikāntargatamume |
kadambagolakākāraṃ tu sāramivaśīkaram || 2-5 || 
taṃ dṛṣṭvā tu paraṃ tejaṃ kālajñānaṃ pravartate |
aṇimādiguṇaiśvaryaṃ jāyate hyavicārataḥ || 2-6 ||
anantaṃ tad dvitīya syāt svarairdvādaśabheditam |
śaktibījena taṃ dīpyaṃ dhyānaṃ kṛtvā tu pūrvavat || 2-7 ||
sūkṣmeṇa tu tṛtīyā ca trimūrttiṃ tu caturthakam |
careṣaṃ pañcamaṃ jñeyaṃ ū ṣaṣṭhaṃ tu nigadyate || 2-8 ||
ṛkāreṇa tu vijñeyaḥ saptamaṃ parikīrtitam |

14a g] ete cakrā mahādevi pañcaviṃśa udāhṛtāḥ |

26a kh] bhakāreṇa samākhyātaṃ caturviṃśa tu śobhane || 2-23 ||

evaṃ devi samākhyāto homadravyavidhiḥ śubhaḥ || 2-134 ||
mṛgīṃ vadhvā tilān juhyāt padmavilvānakhaṇḍitān |
hotavyāḥ satataṃ devi homāt siddhipradā nṛṇām || 2-135 ||
kāmyākāmyavibhāgaṃ tu jñātvā karma samārabhet |
anuṣṭhānaṃ tataḥ kuryājjñātvā cakrāṇyanukramāt || 2-136 ||
yathā caikaṃ tathā sarve varṇāḥ pañcāśa saṃbhavāḥ |
31b kh] dvādaśaiva tu yoginyaḥ pañcāśad vyāpakāḥ smṛtāḥ || 2-137 ||
tena te cakrapañcāśad varṇādhvani surārcite |
adhvastho varṇamālāsthaḥ śaktisthastrividhaḥ priye || 2-138 ||
mayā te kathito yogaḥ sabījaṃ dhāraṇaṃ śṛṇu |
pārthivī pañcadhā jñeyā āpyā jñeyā caturvidhā || 2-139 ||
tejasī trividhā vṛddhiḥ dvidhā tu pavanātmikā |
ekadhā suṣirākhyā tu lakṣaṇaṃ tu pṛthak pṛthak || 2-140 ||
indrāyudhanibho bindurvajramaṇḍalalāñchitaḥ |
tena krānto ripuḥ devi indrākṣaraniyogataḥ || 2-141 ||
stambhaneṣu praśasyeta yadā yoniṃ tu vindati |
śuddhasphaṭikasaṃkāśo vajramaṇḍalamadhyagaḥ || 2-142 ||
āpyāyane praśasyeta vapuḥ stambheta śobhane |
sphuradvahniśikhākāro vajramaṇḍalagaḥ sadā || 2-143 ||
aṇimādiguṇānāṃ ca bodhako bhavate priye |
athavā mārayet krurddho rakārāntargataṃ ripum || 2-144 ||
13a] dāghayatyādayed vātha śoṣaṃ vā cittabhedataḥ |
nalinīdalamadhyastho yathā binduḥ sucañcalaḥ || 2-145 ||
dhīvare vāyunā tūrṇaṃ prerito manasi sthitaḥ |
vajramaṇḍalamadhyastho vācāsiddhikaraḥ priye || 2-146 ||
uccāṭane'pi śeṣeṇa cittavṛtterapekṣayā |
32a kh] kurute vāyusaṃstho hi eko bindurmahāvalaḥ || 2-147 ||
śūnyabhāvagato binduḥ suśire śaktimadhyagaḥ |
purandarapadastho hi sarvajñatvādi sādhayet || 2-148 ||
17b g] sūkṣmaṃ ca kurute dehaṃ eko bindurhṛdi priye |
pārthivī hṛdi saṃsthā tu āpyā kaṇṭhe prakīrtitā || 2-149 ||
tejasī tālumadhyasthā vāyavī nādagā priye |
ākāśā vācakāsaṃsthā brahmanāḍyāṃ vyavasthitā || 2-150 ||
pārthivī cakrasaṃsthā tu āpyā dhanyā nibodhataḥ |
tejasī cāgnimadhye tu vāyavī bindumiśritā || 2-151 ||
suśirā śaktimadhyasthā sthānānyetāni sūcitāḥ |
ekaikā nyūnatā jñeyā sthānasaṃkhyā pṛthak pṛthak || 2-152 ||
pārthivaṃ maṇḍalaṃ khyātā mantrekarmanilānilam |
nābhagaṃ pañcamaṃ jñeyaṃ etaccakraṃ mahodayam || 2-153 ||
brahmasthānagataṃ dhyāyet karṇikāśaktimantragam |
satatābhyāsayogena nityayuktasya yoginaḥ || 2-154 ||
tallayatvaṃ yadā yāti tadā yogaṃ pravartate | 
vyādhi yogena jāyante kṣuttṛṣādyaiḥ sa muhyate || 2-155 ||
sthiratvaṃ prajñavān yogī sarvaśāstrārthavid bhavet |
svayaṃprabhuryathādevaḥ kartā hartā tathā bhavet || 2-156 ||
icchāyā sthūlatā yāti parvate vākṛtirbhavet |
jalabhāvā prajāyeta agnijvālāvalīdharaḥ || 2-157 ||
32b kh] pūrṇaṃ ca kurute sarvaṃ vātāntargatadiṅmukham |
kharūpaṃ rūpavad yogī sarge syāt piṇḍasaṃgrahaḥ || 2-158 ||
svarūpaṃ vā virūpaṃ vā vyādhyārtaḥ kāṇakuṇṭhakam |
icchayā kurute rūpaṃ ekadhānekasaṃkhyayā || 2-159 ||
nagarāḥ pattanāgrāmāḥ gajavājisamākulāḥ |
darśayed bhūtale yogī svecchayā copasaṃharet || 2-160 ||
eke tu pārthive cakre dhāraṇāphalakīrtitam |
yogasiddhiriyaṃ bhadre apratighnaphalodayā || 2-161 ||
mantrairvārādhitaḥ samyagaṣṭadhā siddhibhāgbhavet |
sve sve sthāne phalāvāptirdvārāṇānāṃ prakāśitāḥ || 2-162 ||
tripañcasaṃkhyayā devi dhāraṇāḥ kathitāḥ sphuṭam |

iti śrībhairavaśrotasi mahātantre vidyāpīṭhe saptakoṭipramāṇe śrītantrasadbhāve mātṛkāpūjādhikāro nāma dvitīyaḥ paṭalaḥ || 2 ||





devyuvāca

tṛptāhaṃ devadeveśa bījamantrairanekaśaḥ |
cakraiśca vividhākāraiḥ sadyaḥpratyayakārakaiḥ || 3-1 ||
adyāpi saṃśayo nātha mantrāṇāṃ nirṇayaṃ prati |
pūrvaṃ ye kathitā mantrāḥ saptakoṭirasaṃkhyayā || 3-2 ||
sādhanāni punasteṣāṃ sadyaḥpratyayasaṃyutāḥ |
33a kh] kliśyanti manujātyantaṃ japahomaparāyaṇāḥ || 3-3 ||
18a g] vrataiśca vividhākāraiḥ kaṣṭaiścāndrāyaṇādikaiḥ | 
kimarthaṃ te na siddhyanti japyaiḥ koṭiśatairapi || 3-4 ||
tvaṃ tu devaḥ vibhuḥ kartā tvayoktaṃ satyamucyate |
avicāreṇa tu tad grāhyaṃ mithyaṃ kiṃ deva bhāṣitam || 3-5 ||
yācitastu tvayā loko māyārūpeṇa bhairava |

bhairava uvāca

sādhu sādhu mahāprājñe vastucodyavikalpini || 3-6 ||
13b] ye mayā kathitā mantrāḥ pūrvaṃ vai kāmasiddhidāḥ | 
te gopitāḥ punardevi varṇarūpāḥ prakāśitāḥ || 3-7 ||
tena tena prasidhyanti sthitāḥ pañcatayai rapi |
oṃkāreṇa tu te guptā namaskāreṇa bhāmini || 3-8 ||
tena guptena guptāste śeṣāḥ varṇāstu kevalā |
ekākṣarā dvākṣarāśca catuṣpañcanavākṣarāḥ || 3-9 ||
kūṭamantrāśca ye kecit piṇḍamantrāstathaiva ca |
ekāśītipadāḥ cānye sahasrārṇaśatadvikāḥ || 3-10 || 
sarve te niṣphalāḥ proktāḥ kintu jīvavivarjitāḥ |
loke prasiddhamevaṃ hi jīvahīno mṛtaḥ kila || 3-11 ||
mṛtasya upacāreṇa kiṃ tasya jīvitaṃ bhavet |
eva mantrā varārohe akṣarārthe vyavasthitāḥ || 3-12 ||
vratacaryairna siddhyanti satyamevamudāhṛtam | 
siddhyante jīvarūpāstu kimatra pravicāryate || 3-13 || 
anyonyavalitāścaiva bheditairdvādaśaḥ svaraiḥ | 
33b kh] rañjitāḥ śaktibījena siddhyante varavarṇini || 3-14 || 
uktāḥ kāmapradāḥ sarve sarve vāmaughaśaktayaḥ |
śivavaktrodbhavāḥ sarve mananatrāṇadharmiṇaḥ || 3-15 ||
trāṇaṃ tu rakṣaṇaṃ proktaṃ tacca varṇavivarjitam |
śuddhasphaṭikasaṃkāśaṃ cāroccāravivarjitam || 3-16 || 
jvalantaṃ svena tejena hṛtpadme ca vyavasthitam | 
bhāvayecchūnyamātmānamekībhūtaṃ tayā saha || 3-17 || 
suṣumṇoccārayogena udantaṃ ravivimbavat |
brahmasthānaṃ tato bhittvā viṣṇū rudrapade sthitaḥ || 3-18 ||
tāvat kanyetvasau yogī stobhamāyāti tatkṣaṇāt |
mudrā mantrāstathā bhāṣāḥ sarvaṃ jānāti tatvataḥ || 3-19 ||
īśvaraṃ tu padaṃ prāptaḥ sarve viśvaṃ tu jāyate |
iṣanmātraṃ tu jānāti suptāvastho vyavasthitaḥ || 3-20 || 
śivāṃtantu yadā prāptaṃ kāṣṭhavat tiṣṭhate tadā | 
bherīmṛdaṅgaśabdāni gītavādyānyanekadhā || 3-21 ||
na śṛṇoti na paśyeta na cānyaṃ manyate prabhum |
khaḍgacakrādiśastraistu cchidyamāno na jāyaṃte || 3-22 ||
iṣanmātraṃ tu jānanti śaktyāvastho varānane |
18b g] śaktyāṃ * tu yadā prāptastadā cotpatate kṣaṇāt || 3-23 || 
evaṃ krameṇa deveśi śakticāraṃ samabhyaset |
satatābhyāsayogena amaratvaṃ tasya jāyate || 3-24 ||
māsamekaṃ yadābhyastaṃ kāvyakartā na saṃśayaḥ |
dvibhirmāsairvapuṣmantaḥ kṣuttṛṣādyairna bādhyate || 3-25 ||
34a kh] vicaredakhilān bhogān yāvadābhūtasaṃplavaḥ |
evametanmayā khyāto mantrāṇāṃ vidhiruttamaḥ || 3-26 ||

devyuvāca 

varṇamantraiḥ kalāmantraiḥ padamantraistathā punaḥ | 
piṇḍamantraistvaṣṭapuṣpīṃ vibhinnāṃ brūhi bhairava || 3-27 || 
eko varṇa padaṃ vāpi kalaikā rocate tu yā |
piṇḍamantrasya tasyaiva kathaṃ pūjā pratiṣṭhitā || 3-28 ||
brāhmaṇi tasya kāni syuḥ śivāṃgāni ca yāni vai |
āsanasya ca ke mantrā jñātumicchāmi tatvataḥ || 3-29 ||

bhairava uvāca

akārastu bhavenmadya ikāro vāmamiṣyate |
ukāro bahurūpastu ekāraḥ puruṣaḥ smṛtaḥ || 3-30 || 
okārastu tathaiśānaṃ pañcabrahmāṇya mūrddhniṣu |
śivāṃgāni ca kathyante vidyāṃgāni ca yāni ca || 3-31 ||
ākāraṃ hṛdayaṃ proktamīkāraṃ tu śiraḥ smṛtaḥ |
ukārastu śikhā caiva aikāre kavacaṃ smṛtam || 3-32 ||
akāraṃ mantramuddiṣṭaṃ plutākāraṃ tu locanam || 3-33 ||
14a] brahmāṇyaṅgāni caitāni vindunā mūrcchitāni tu |
aṃkāraḥ śivagāyatrī sāvitrī bindunā hataḥ || 3-34 || 
śivāṅgānāṃ svanāye tu niṣṭhāntāste prakīrtitāḥ | 
kramād brahmāstrapūrvāṇi vidyāṅgāni svarūpataḥ || 3-35 ||
okārastu mahāpadma aukāraścaiva karṇikā |
34b kh] e-ai-o-augātrakāstu prāgārabhya pradakṣiṇam || 3-36 ||
amadhaschadanaṃ syātāṃ asyaivamuttaraṃ cchadam |
ṛkāro dharmamityāhu ṝkāro jñānameva ca || 3-37 ||
lṛkāraścaiva vairāgyaṃ aiśvaryaṃ lṝ prakīrtitam | 
amanantaśca kathitaṃ prabhuśaktistathā smṛtā || 3-38 ||
vidyādhipaśca kathito vidyāṅgānāṃ ca madhyataḥ |
iṣṭo varṇaḥ śivatvena yojyaṃ brahmādikāraṇam || 3-39 ||
ikāro jñānaśaktiśca īkāraśca kriyā smṛtā |
ukāraścaiva cācchaktirū ? ca vāgīśvarī smṛtā || 3-40 ||
ekārastu smṛtā jvālā ai vāmā saṃprakīrtitā | 
ukāraśca bhavejjyeṣṭhā au raudrī samudāhṛtā || 3-41 ||
kalāpiṇḍapadānāṃ tu kramādante niyojayet |
piṇḍamantrādiko bhedaścaturdhā yaḥ prakīrtitaḥ || 3-42 ||
tasyāṣṭapuṣpikābhedaḥ samāsāt samudāhṛtaḥ |
piṇḍamantrairamībhiśca sakalīkaraṇaṃ bhavet || 3-43 ||
nyāset gāyatrīṃ sāvitrīṃ dehārdhe vāmadakṣiṇe |
19a g] mantragrāmasya sarvasya hṛdayādimanukramāt || 3-44 ||
svasaṃkhyātaḥ samudbhūtāḥ svarāḥ kalpyāstathā kramāt |
svarayoniprabhedena saṃsthito mātṛkāśivaḥ || 3-45 ||
akārādikṣakārāntā varṇāḥ pañcāśa saṃsthitāḥ |
svabrahmāṅgasamāyogā jñeyā tasyāṣṭapuṣpikā || 3-46 ||
35a kh] dvādaśaiḥ ṣoḍaśaiścaiva samastavyastayogataḥ |
akṣarārthe ca ye mantrāsteṣāmeva vidhiḥ sphuṭā || 3-47 ||


devyuvāca

akṣarārthopadeśaśca saṃpradāyaśca kaulikaḥ |
yathā taṃ jñāyate deva prasādaṃ kuru me tathā || 3-48 ||

bhairava uvāca

śṛṇu devi pravakṣyāmi mantrāṇāṃ nirṇayaṃ sphuṭam |
prastāraḥ pratimāyāṃ tu digvargakoṣṭhakaistathā || 3-49 ||
nārācyādiranekaiśca ye mantrāścodgatāḥ priye |
akṣarārthe tu te jñeyāḥ khaṇḍamantrāḥ śivoditāḥ || 3-50 ||
rañjakena samāyuktā upadeśāḥ surārcite |
saṃpradāyo bhaved devi so'pi ṣaḍbhiḥ prakārataḥ || 3-51 ||
pallavo yogarodhaśca saṃpuṭaṃ grathanastathā |
vidarbhaśca ṣaḍetāni upāyāḥ kulamārgagāḥ || 3-52 ||
mālinīśabdarāśistu kauliko vidhiruttamaḥ |
mantro jñeyo varārohe bhedaiḥ pañcāśa suvrate || 3-53 ||
kulastu ṣaḍvidho jñeyo tasya vākṣyāmi lakṣaṇam |
paraṃ bījaṃ tathā mūlamāgame vidhireva ca || 3-54 ||
varṇarāśisamāyuktaḥ ṣaḍvidhastu kulakramaḥ |
sakalo niṣkalaścaiva tathā sakalaniṣkalaḥ || 3-55 ||
sūkṣmo bhinnakalaścaiva kalātīto varānane |
ṣaṭprakāro bhavenmantro jñātavya siddhimicchataḥ || 3-56 ||
35b kh] śuddhadvandvajasaṃkīrṇa upadeśa tribhedataḥ |
devyuvāca
sūcitā mantramārge tu ye mantrā lakṣaṇotthitāḥ || 3-57 ||
te jñātāstatprasādena dhyānadhāraṇayogataḥ |
kulamārgagatā deva yathā bhavati tatkathaṃ || 3-58 ||
kathaṃ tu pallavo yoga ādau ṣaḍbhiḥ prakārakaiḥ |
kaulikaṃ ṣaḍvidhaṃ kiṃ tu mantrāṇāṃ ṣaḍvidhā gatiḥ || 3-59 ||
tridhā caivopadeśastu etadicchāmi veditum |

14b] bhairava uvāca

pallava ādideśe tu yogo madhye vijānataḥ || 3-60 ||
rodhastu cādimadhyānte saṃpuṭāścādirantarāḥ |
grathanamantare jñeyamakṣarākṣarayogataḥ || 3-61 ||
vidarbho mantra ādau tu mantrānte vīranāyike |
mālāgrathanametaddhi jñātavyaṃ mantravādinām || 3-62 ||
kasya ko viṣayo devi vibhāgaṃ tvadhunā śṛṇu |
pallavo mantrabodhe tu yogo jñeyastu sarvadā || 3-63 ||
19b g] amalīkaraṇe caiva sandhāne ca vidhau priye |
yogastu kathito hyevaṃ rodha ityanukīrtyate || 3-64 ||
tīvre mantre padastambhe vākstambhe sainyastambhane |
hastyādisakaṭayantrā bhāvāntā ye ca kīrtitāḥ || 3-65 ||
teṣu rodhaḥ praśasyeta paśūnāmutkrameṣu ca |
saṃpuṭaṃ mantrarakṣāśca vaśyārthe caiva yojayet || 3-66 ||
amṛtīkaraṇe caiva vidhe sthāvarajaṅgame |
36a kh] śāntikādiṣu kāryeṣu saṃpuṭastu praśasyate || 3-67 ||
grathanaṃ japakarmeṣu ākṛṣṭyādiṣu sarvasu |
sandhāne ca varārohe grathanaṃ samudāhṛtam || 3-68 ||
vidarbhaḥ sarvakāryeṣu uktānakteṣu karmasu |
kartavyaṃ satataṃ mantrī yadicchet siddhimuttamām || 3-69 ||
etad devi samākhyātaḥ saṃpradāyavidhiḥ śubhaḥ |
na mayā kasyacit khyātaṃ satyaṃ satyaṃ gaṇāmbike || 3-70 ||
sāṃprataṃ kulamārgaṃ tu yathā bhavati tacchṛṇu |
paro vinduḥ samākhyāto hṛtpadme suranāyike || 3-71 ||
grahaṇaistasya yopāyastaṃ jñeyaṃ kaulikaṃ param |
bījakuṇḍalinī śaktiḥ yā karoti gamāgamam || 3-72 ||
tasyāntaṃ tu tato jñātvā bījaṃ kaulikamucyate |
mūlaśaktistathā viśvī jagataḥ kāraṇātmikā || 3-73 ||
yasyā jātamaśeṣaṃ tu ābrahmabhuvanāntikam |
sṛjate yena suśroṇi kāryakāraṇayogataḥ || 3-74 ||
jñeyo dharmiṇi dharmitve yathosmākṛṣṇadharmiṇaḥ |
etat kaulikamākhyātaṃ mūlasaṃjñaṃ varānane || 3-75 ||
āgamastava sūtrārthe vidhistatraiva coditaḥ |
varṇarāśikramo jñeyo nādiphalantasvarūpataḥ || 3-76 ||
ādikṣāntastu deveśi varṇarāśikrameṇa tu |
etat kaulikamākhyātaṃ ṣaṭprakāraṃ varānane || 3-77 ||
36b kh] sakalādikrameṇaiva vakṣyamāṇaṃ nibodha me |
brahmasthaḥ sakalo mantra aṣṭatriṃśakalairyutam || 3-78 ||
puryāṣṭakasamopeta udbhavastho vijānataḥ |
kaṇṭhastho niṣkalo devi kalākalavivarjitaḥ || 3-79 ||
rudrasthānagato rudre mantro bhāvadvayānvitaḥ |
sthūlajālakale mukto bindvādibhiśca bandhanaiḥ || 3-80 ||
sūkṣmādhāraḥ smṛto hyekaḥ sparśākhyo mantravedakaḥ |
sakalaṃ niṣkaścāsau mantro jñeyastu suvrate || 3-81 ||
nilīne vindudeve tu jyotyākāre svarūpataḥ |
20a g] śabdasparśavinirmukto mantro jñeyastu niṣkalaḥ || 3-82 ||
sūkṣmasūkṣmataro devi sa ca sūkṣmo nigadyate |
kalārūpaḥ smṛto bindustaṃ bhittvā vrajate yadā || 3-83 ||
ūrdhvapadapravṛttaśca suṣumṇādhāragaḥ paraḥ |
pralīnaḥ śabdadeve tu cicchaktipratibodhitaḥ || 3-84 ||
bhinnakalaḥ smṛto hyevaṃ kalātītastu mokṣadaḥ |
na dhyānaṃ na japaṃ caiva na pūjā havanaṃ tathā || 3-85 ||
kriyākāraṇanirmuktaḥ kalātītastu sa smṛtaḥ |
ṣaḍavasthāḥ samākhyātā mantrāṇāmamitaujasām || 3-86 ||
15a] yāvadevaṃ na vindanti tāvat siddhiḥ kuto bhavet |
hṛtkaṇṭhatālujihvoṣṭhaudantanāsodbhavākṣarāḥ || 3-87 ||
kṣaṇapradhvaṃsinau devi yathotpannāstathā kṣayaḥ |
kṛtakāḥ sacetanāḥ śūnyā anityā jalpakārakāḥ || 3-88 ||
37a kh] ṣaḍavasthā prabhinnasya tadā mantratvamiṣyate |
evaṃ mantragatiṃ jñātvā siddhyante līlayā nṛṇām || 3-89 ||
udbhave śuddhirityukto viśleṣe dvandvajaḥ smṛtaḥ |
saṃkīṇo layasaṃstho hi upadeśastridhā smṛtaḥ || 3-90 ||
sparśanaṃ cāvalokaśca saṃbhāṣatvātmadarśanaṃ |
svayamāviṣaṇaṃ caiva saṃkrāntiḥ pañcalakṣaṇā || 3-91 ||
sparśanaṃ hṛdisaṃsthaṃ tu ālokaṃ kaṇṭhadeśataḥ |
rudrasthāne tu saṃbhāṣaṃ darśanaṃ īśvare sthitam || 3-92 ||
svayamāviṣaṇaṃ devi śivasthāne bhaved dhruvam |
sparśane kampane jñeyamāloke dhunanaṃ bhavet || 3-93 ||
saṃbhāṣe tu bhavet stobhaṃ śāstrārthaṃ caiva manyate |
darśanena guṇāvāptiraṇimādiguṇāṣṭakam || 3-94 ||
svayamāviṣaṇe devi utpatet tu na saṃśayaḥ |
evaṃ mantragatiṃ jñātvā siddhyate tu na saṃśayaḥ || 3-95 ||

devyuvāca

yo'yaṃ mantrasya saṃskāro akṣarārthādi kīrtitaḥ |
saṃskārādatakaraṇaṃ kriyate kasya vā vibho || 3-96 ||
tasmānmantrāḥ samācakṣva kulavaineyamārgagāḥ |
brahmāṅgamūlamantrotthā yenāyaṃ saphalo bhavet || 3-97 ||

bhairava uvāca

ataḥ paraṃ pravakṣyāmi mantroddhāraṃ varānane |
kulavaineyikaṃ devi vidhānaṃ tu suvistaram || 3-98 ||
37b kh] caturasraṃ puraṃ likhya saptabhāgavikalpitam |
punaḥ saptaguṇaṃ kṛtvā koṣṭhakān kārayet priye || 3-99 ||
pañcāśadrunamekena kartavyāṃ tu yathāvidhiḥ |
īśānyādikrameṇaiva abhyaset svaramaṇḍalam || 3-100 ||
tasyānte tu tataḥ sparśo yāvanmadhyamupāgatāḥ |
brahmasthānagataṃ devaṃ haṃsākhyaṃ tu mahātmanaḥ || 3-101 ||
kaṣākhyaṃ mantrarājānaṃ saṃyogena tu jāyate | 
evaṃ nyāse kṛte devi uddharet mālinīṃ śubhām || 3-102 ||
nādiphāntasvarūpeṇa yathā bhavati tacchṛṇu |
aidamadhyaṃ śiro devyāḥ kārayecchubhalakṣaṇam || 3-103 ||
padamadhyaṃ śikhā caiva adhaḥ śiro vyavasthitaḥ |
epūrvārṇacatuṣkaṃ tu śiromālyanigadyate || 3-104 ||
tṛtīyaṃ nayanaṃ kāryaṃ dahamadhyagatam priye |
nadamadhyagataṃ jñeyaṃ dvidhā bhūtaṃ varānane || 3-105 ||
nayane te smṛtā devyāḥ kramād dakṣiṇavāmage |
ṭapūrvaṃ nāsikā jñeyā saṃśliṣṭā tu dhamadhyagāṃ || 3-106 ||
ṭatamadhyegataṃ gūhya dvirabhyāsapaderitam |
ṭhaḍapūrve yutaudhastā bhūṣaṇau karṇayoḥ smṛtau || 3-107 ||
38a kh] vāmadakṣiṇamārgeṇa yathodbhūtau sthitāviha |
sacamadhyagataṃ cakraṃ devyāyā vīranāyike || 3-108 || 
visargakhaśca madhyasthaṃ kagamadhyayutaṃ punaḥ | 
ya paścimaṃ samuddiṣṭaṃ yasya cottarameva ca || 3-109 ||
ghacasyāntarasaṃsthaṃ tu uddharedakṣaraṃ śubham |
ete pañca smṛtā varṇā devyādarśanakalpane || 3-110 ||
ñapūrvaṃ rasanā proktā japūrveṇa sarasvatī |
śatamadhyasthitaṃ kaṇṭho masthamadhyagatoddharet || 3-111 ||
rasamadhyagatastadva dakṣarau dvau śubhātmakau |
śikharau tu smṛtau bhadre vāmadakṣiṇagau śubhau || 3-112 ||
15b] raṭhadakṣiṇagau dvau tu bāhugau vāmadakṣiṇau |
ra-umadhyagataṃ caiva dvidhābhūtaṃ prakalpayet || 3-113 ||
karatalasthau smṛtau devi savyāsavyaṃ vijānataḥ |
bhamapūrvau tathāṃgulyau vāmadakṣiṇagau śubhāḥ || 3-114 ||
ñaṭhamadhyagataṃ gṛhya vāmahaste prakalpayet | 
ūrddhvavaktraṃ kapālaṃ tu amṛtākhyena pūritam || 3-115 ||
dakṣiṇe tu kare jñeyaṃ paḍhamadhyaṃ tu daṇḍakam |
38b kh] śūlasya kathitaṃ bhadre uddhāreṇa samuddhṛtam || 3-116 ||
achamadhyagataṃ śūlamuttānamūrddhvavaktragam hṛcca devyāyāḥ sarvakāmikam | 
nasamadhyagamudaraṃ cajamadhyagataḥ priye || 3-118 ||
ravasandhigataṃ tadvadakṣarau dvau stanātmakau |
jhapūrvaṃ tu yayo jñeyamamṛtaṃ samudāhṛtam || 3-119 ||
yasamadhyagataṃ prāṇaṃ devyāyā vīranāyike |
maṣamadhyagataṃ devi ātmā bījāmṛtātmakam || 3-120 ||
visargasahitaṃ rudre uddhṛtaṃ mantramuttamam |
kaṣākhyaṃ mantrarājānaṃ nābhidevyā prakalpayet || 3-121 ||
21a g] bhayamadhyā smṛtaṃ devi nitambaṃ sakalātmakam |
vaśamadhyagataṃ gṛhyaṃ dapaścimasamanvitam || 3-122 ||
ūrvākārau bhaved bījaṃ ṇaṣamadhyagatonaghe |
tathadakṣiṇagau bījau jānukau dvau suśobhanau || 3-123 ||
savyāsavyagatau jñeyau krameṇaiva śubhekṣaṇe |
aiṣapaścimagau dvau tu jaṃghe dve vāmadakṣiṇe || 3-124 ||
ṣadhamadhyagataṃ devi ṛṣamadhyaṃ tathaiva ca |
dvau bījāvuddhṛtau bhadre pādau jñeyau vipaścite || 3-125 ||
vāmadakṣiṇagau proktau lakṣaṇena vilakṣitau |
evaṃ samyagvidhānena uddhṛtā mālinī priye || 3-126 ||
39a kh] saptakoṭyastu vidyānāṃ mantrāṇāmamitaujasām |
teṣāmekā parā yoniḥ mālinī sarvakāmadā || 3-127 ||
mālayitvā sthitā yena teneyaṃ mālinī smṛtāḥ |
ye jātā ye bhaviṣyanti aprameyā varānane || 3-128 ||
rudrāṇāṃ yoginīnāṃ ca sā māteva nigadyate |
avarṇāvarṇasaṃyogā jñātavyā tu varānane || 3-129 ||
sarve varṇātmakā mantrā varṇāḥ śaktyātmakāḥ smṛtāḥ |
śaktistu mātṛkā proktā sā jñeyā tu śivātmikā || 3-130 ||
etanmantrapramāṇaṃ tu kathitaṃ tava śobhane |
etadādyaṃ samākhyātaṃ gopanīyaṃ prayatnataḥ || 3-131 ||
ekavīrapradhānaṃ tu prāguktamanyamāgame |
sāṃpratamaṅgamuddhāraṃ kathayāmi tava priye || 3-132 ||
yakāraṃ hṛdayaṃ jñeyaṃ thakārastu śiraḥ smṛtaḥ |
takāraṃ tu śikhā jñeyā hakāraṃ kavacaṃ smṛtam || 3-133 ||
cakāraṃ netramityuktaṃ jakāramastragocare |
evaṃ samyagvidhānena mālinyaṅgāḥ samuddhṛtāḥ || 3-134 ||
sāṃprataṃ tu mahādevi parāparamahābalā |
lakṣaṇamaṅgamuddhāramaparā ca parā tathā || 3-135 ||
vidyātrayaṃ samuddhṛtya hṛdayaṃ mātṛjaṃ tathā |
yoginī hṛdayaṃ cānyaṃ bhairavaṃ hṛdayaṃ tathā || 3-136 ||
śākinīhṛdayaṃ devi vidhānaṃ tu suvistaram |
sādhanaṃ samudāyaṃ ca samastavyasta eva ca || 3-137 ||
39b kh] eteṣāṃ viniyogaṃ tu śṛṇuṣva vahitā priye |
vidvau tu dakṣiṇā jaṃghā tato vāk parikalpayet || 3-138 ||
caturthaṃ daśanaṃ mantrī dakṣajaṃghāsamanvitam |
śūladaṇḍaṃ samuddhṛtya jānunā tu samanvitam || 3-139 ||
16a] dakṣiṇāyā varārohe prāṇaṃ caiva svarūpataḥ |
śūladaṇḍāsanāsīnaṃ nāsākrāntaṃ visargiṇam || 3-140 ||
hṛdayaṃ kevalaṃ caiva śūladaṇḍaṃ tathaiva ca |
nitambaṃ kevalaṃ gṛhya caturthaṃ daśanaṃ punaḥ || 3-141 ||
21b g] dakṣajaṃghāsamopetaṃ śūladaṇḍaṃ punar nyaset |
dakṣiṇāyā varārohe jānunā bheditaṃ tataḥ || 3-142 ||
prāṇabhūṣaṇasaṃyutaṃ guhyasthe nāvalaṃkṛtaṃ |
dakṣiṇena varārohe caturthaṃ daśanaṃ punaḥ || 3-143 ||
dakṣajaṃghāsamopetaṃ śūladaṇḍaṃ svarūpiṇam | 
punareva tato gṛhya śūladaṇḍaṃ vicakṣaṇaḥ || 3-144 || 
vāmabhūṣaṇasaṃyuktaṃ hṛdayaṃ jānunā yutam | 
dakṣiṇena varārohe prāṇaṃ caiva visargiṇam || 3-145 ||
punareva dvijaṃ gṛhya caturthaṃ jaṃghamāyutam |
śūladaṇḍaṃ punar nyasya nitambaṃ ca svarūpiṇam || 3-146 ||
karṇabhūṣaṇapūrveṇa bhūṣitaṃ ca dvitīyakam |
jihvāyuktaṃ ca kartavyaṃ daśanaṃ ca dvitīyakam || 3-147 ||
śikharaṃ nāsikākrāntaṃ dakṣiṇaṃ tadanantaram |
nitambaṃ kevalaṃ gṛhya tathaiva śikharaṃ punaḥ || 3-148 ||
udaraṃ kevalaṃ nyasya karṇaṃ caiva tathā punaḥ |
40a kh] dakṣajānuyutaṃ corddhve kaṇṭhaṃ caiva svarūpataḥ || 3-149 ||
nitambaṃ kevalaṃ dadyād hṛdayaṃ jihvayā yutam |
vaktraṃ kevalamuddhṛtya prāṇaṃ jānuyutaṃ punaḥ || 3-150 ||
dakṣiṇena varārohe śūladaṇḍaṃ dvidhā punaḥ |
karṇabhūṣaṇapūrveṇa bheditaṃ dvitayaṃ priye || 3-151 ||
punareva tato daṇḍaṃ dvidhābhūtaṃ prakalpayet |
vāmapādaṃ tato nyasya kapālaṃ patitaṃ punaḥ || 3-152 ||
karṇabhūṣaṇasaṃyuktaṃ prāṇaṃ binduyutaṃ punaḥ |
savisargaṃ tataḥ prāṇaṃ vāmapādāntato dharet || 3-153 ||
kapālaṃ patitaṃ nyastvā tasyānte yatra dīpakam |
parāparā samākhyātā triṃśadvarṇāṣṭakā śubhā || 3-154 ||
arddhākṣaradvayopetā lakṣaṇena vilakṣitā |
aparā tryakṣarā caiva arddhakṣaraparānvitā || 3-155 ||
prāṇaṃ nāsāyutaṃ kṛtvā daṇḍāsīnaṃ sabindukam |
punareva nyaset prāṇaṃ vāmabhūṣaṇasaṃyutam || 3-156 ||
binduyuktaṃ tu kartavyaṃ vāmapādaṃ tataḥ punaḥ |
kapālaṃ patitaṃ nyastvā samyajjñātvā vicakṣaṇaḥ || 3-157 ||
aparā tu samākhyātā parāyāḥ sāmprataṃ śṛṇu |
parā tvakākṣarā jñeyā sadyaḥpratyayakārakā || 3-158 ||
nābhaktāya pradātavyā na māyākarmakāriṇe |
na gurudveṣiṇe caiva śaṭhe klīve tathānṛte || 3-159 ||
dambhakauṭilyanirate na ca vāmoktinindake |
40b kh] tatvadveṣi na dātavyaṃ kapaṭavratadhāriṇe || 3-160 ||
anyaśāstre ratā ye ca teṣāṃ caiva na dāpayet |
muhūrtaṃ smarate yastu śucirbhavati mānuṣaḥ || 3-161 ||
22a g] sa yātyābhimataṃ sthānaṃ gatvāsau na nivartate |
vihārapādacāraṃ ca sarvamasmāt pravartate || 3-162 ||
kvacit pralambakeśitvaṃ śirasāgamanaṃ bhavet |
kvaciddarduraplutyā ca pṛṣṭhataścāvasarpaṇam || 3-163 ||
kvacinnirmālitākṣasya ūrddhvadṛṣṭirprajāyate | 
karaṇā corddhvamūlena viyati sthitireva ca || 3-164 ||
darśanaṃ devataiḥ sārddhaṃ saṃcāraṃ merumūrdhani |
sadyastanmukhatāmeti svadehāveśalakṣaṇam || 3-165 ||
udgrāhayati cāvaśyaṃ śāstrasadbhāvamuttamam |
atītānāgatāṃ caiva pṛṣṭo'sau kathayatyapi || 3-166 ||
vāmajaṃghāsamopetāṃ sātmā deyaḥ sadaiva tu |
pareyaṃ paramā siddhā iha śāstrairvicāritā || 3-167 ||
calantaṃ mātṛjaṃ cānyaṃ taṃ pravakṣyāmi vistarāt |
vāmapādaṃ samuddhṛtya bhinnaṃ dakṣiṇajānunā || 3-168 ||
bindunā samalaṃkṛtya amṛtārthe prayojayet |
16b] cavargasya caturthaṃ tu ārūḍhaṃ kaṣasaṃjñake || 3-169 ||
tasyādhastād nyaset prāṇaṃ vāmabhūṣaṇasaṃyutam |
vāmajaṃghāsamopetaṃ ardhacandraśikhānvitam || 3-170 ||
41a kh] nādaśaktisamopetaṃ bhairavaṃ hṛdayaṃ param |
yoginīhṛdayaṃ cānyaṃ nitambaṃ daṇḍadīpitam || 3-171 ||
punardaṇḍaṃ nitambaṃ tu aparasparasaṃyutam |
daṇḍasaṃpuṭamadhyasthaṃ yoginīhṛdayaṃ param || 3-172 ||
vāmaśikharamārūḍhaṃ bhūṣitaṃ bhūṣaṇena tu |
vāmakarṇasya suśroṇi bindunādaśikhānvitam || 3-173 ||
arddhacandrakṛtoṣṇīṣametaddhi hṛdayaṃ param | 
rudraśaktisamāveśo tatra nityaṃ pratiṣṭhitaḥ || 3-174 ||
sākinīhṛdayaṃ caiva pravakṣyāmi tapodhane |
ṭavargasya tṛtīyaṃ tu nitambāsanasaṃsthitam || 3-175 ||
so'pi daṇḍāsanāsīnaṃ dviguṇaṃ daṇḍadīpitam |
āsīnāṃ bāhuśikhare vāme vāmena bhūṣitam || 3-176 ||
bhūṣaṇena varārohe guhā caiva? khilī? kṛtam |
śākinīhṛdayaṃ proktaṃ noccaren mantravit kvacit || 3-177 ||
dharmañca jīvitaṃ caiva vīryaṃ tāsāṃ śubhekṣaṇe |
klidyanti vaśamāyānti rakṣayet pāṇivadyathā || 3-178 ||
vidyāṅgapañcakaṃ devi te vakṣyāmyanuśeṣataḥ |
yena rakṣito mantrī vighnaiśairnābhibhūyate || 3-179 ||
praṇavaṃ pañcadhoccārya hṛdayā patati padām |
hṛdayaṃ kathitaṃ rudre sarvasandohalakṣaṇam || 3-180 ||
yoginyetatpadaṃ pūrvamamṛteti dvitīyakam |
22b g] tejo mālini cānyaṃ syācchiraṃ ceti padaṃ tataḥ || 3-181 ||
41b kh] svāhākārāntasaṃyuktaṃ śiro vai sarvakāmadam |
omīti prathamaṃ veda veda nityamataḥ param || 3-182 ||
hūṃ phaḍantaṃ śikhāyeti vauṣaḍantā śikhā matā |
oṃ vajriṇe padaṃ pūrvaṃ tathā vajradharāya ca || 3-183 ||
kavacāyāntahuṃyuktaṃ kavacaṃ sarvakāmadam |
oṃ harī paśuśabdaṃ caiva hu/ phaḍantāstu muttamam || 3-184 || 
ataḥ paraṃ pravakṣyāmi yajanaṃ syāmudāyikam |
āsāṃ saṃsmaraṇāccaiva phalaṃ prāpnoti cottamam || 3-185 ||
bhairavādhiṣṭhitā devyo yathāyojyāstathā śṛṇu |
bhūmibhāge same śuddhe gomayenopalepite || 3-186 ||
puṣpaprakarasaṃyukte gandhadhūpasamanvite |
tatra maṇḍalamālikhya trihastaṃ rajasānvitam || 3-187 ||
dvādaśāṅgulamānena arakatrayasaṃyutam |
aṣṭāraṃ tat samaṃ kuryāt karṇikākeśarānvitam || 3-188 ||
nacaiva puṣkarāḥ kāryāḥ keśarāścaiva tatsamāḥ |
dalāḥ śuklāstu kartavyā antaraṃ raktavarṇakam || 3-189 ||
karṇikā pītikā kāryā puṣkarā haritāḥ smṛtāḥ |
keśarā raktapītāstu śuklāścaiva yathākramam || 3-190 ||
sitā nepāstu kartavyā antaraṃ raktavarṇakam |
dvādaśāraṃ tato likhya dalāruṇasamanvitam || 3-191 ||
dvātriṃśaṃ tasya bāhye tu śuklavarṇaṃ samujjvalam |
tasyānte bhrāmaṇī kāryā caturasraṃ tu bāhyataḥ || 3-192 ||
vīthikā hasta ekena kartavyā śubhalakṣaṇāḥ |
42a kh] dvārāṇi caturaḥ kāryā hastamātrā samaṃtataḥ || 3-193 ||
yathā sobhā tu karttavyā varṇakaiścāvaśeṣakaiḥ |
śaṃkhavalyādi patraiśca padmaiḥ svastikalāñchanaiḥ || 3-194 ||
evaṃ lekhya prayatnena ghaṃṭā pāṇḍuradarpaṇaiḥ |
toraṇaiḥ sapātakaiśca vitānadhvajamākulam || 3-195 ||
evaṃ vibhavataḥ kṛtvā tato yajanamārabhet |
mantrī yaśca kṛtāṭopaḥ sādhakaiśca svakaiḥ saha || 3-196 ||
bhairavaṃ pūjayet tatra pṛthak yaśaḥ ? śarīriṇāṃ |
tacchūnyaṃ gaganā devī nādiphāntasvarūpiṇī || 3-197 ||
vidyāṅgaiśca samopetāṃ sarvālaṃkārabhūṣitām |
dvibhujapadmahastantu sitapadmāsanasthitām || 3-198 ||
17a] varṇākhyāścāṣṭabhiḥ pattairyādihānta krameṇa tu |
 aghorānte kavarṇaṃ tu pūjāyetannivedyayet || 3-199 ||
dvātriṃśavarṇavinyāsaṃ dvātriṃśe viniyojayet |
parāṃ dvādaśabhedena dvādaśārthaṃ prayojayet || 3-200 ||
23a g] catuṣke tu tataścakre samyak pūjā yathāvidhiḥ |
aparāṃ pūjyaṃ pūjyante na vidhidṛṣṭena karmaṇā || 3-201 ||
keśarai śaktaya pūjyā yakārādi yathākramam |
gandhadhūpopahārādyai bhairavehṛditāhṛdi || 3-202 ||
tatvaṃ garbha yogena yatkiṃcid vidhicoditaṃ |
prājñena mātṛjenaiva mātarastu prapūjayet || 3-203 ||
yoginīnāṃ tu kartavyaṃ gandhadhūpopahārakam |
yoginīhṛdaye prītaistāḥ sidhyanti balotkaṭṭāḥ || 3-204 ||
calena mātaraḥ sarvāḥ sidhyantyevanna saṃśayaḥ |
bhairave hṛdaye siddhe sarvametanna saṃśayaḥ || 3-205 ||
sidhyante nātra saṃdeho yathā bhairavamantravit |
lakṣāṇāṃ daśakaṃ japtvā lakṣahomāt purasya tu || 3-206 ||
tyaktāstu tilaṃ juhuyāt padmavilvānakhaṇḍitān |
vāruṇyāṃ kārayet kuṇḍaṃ kuṇḍaṃ vai sarvatomukham || 3-207 ||
śāntipuṣṭimavāpnoti sadyametanna saṃśayaḥ |
japahomārcanāsaktaḥ siddhyante hyavicārataḥ || 3-208 ||
pātālaṃ khecaratvaṃ ca pādalepaṃ parāyaṇam |
rasaṃ vai svayaṃ nādaṃ ca svadvai?ddhāra meva ca || 3-209 ||
amṛtākṛṣṭivijñānaṃ svapnakhecarako'thavā |
rūpādiparivartanaṃ kṣiprameva tu sādhayet || 3-210 ||
maṇḍalīkaraṇendrāṇāṃ kiṃkurvāṇavidheyatā |
samastajanavācchalyaṃ nigrahānugrahaṃ tathā || 3-211 ||
na tadvastvantaraṃ kiṃcijjapādyaṃ naiva sādhayet |
vratacaryāsamopeto homāt samayapālakaḥ || 3-212 ||
bhaviṣye te na saṃdeho yat kiñcinmanasepsitam |
ataḥ paraṃ pravakṣyāmi cakrasthā yāśca devatāḥ || 3-213 ||
tāsāṃ nāmāni vakṣyāmi śṛṇu tatvena pārvati |
tryarake tu tataścakre sthitāḥ sarvagatāḥ śubhāḥ || 3-214 ||
pūrve tu mānasī proktā cakravegā tathāpare |
43a kh] kauverye mohanī jñeyā mahābalaparākramā || 3-215 ||
aṣṭāre tu pravakṣyāmi brahmaṇyādi yathākramam |
aghoryaṃ cāvasāne tu tathā mātṛgaṇaṃ priye || 3-216 ||
arakāntai karṇāstu aghoryādyaṣṭakaṃ tathā |
svacchandā caṇḍarūpā ca krodhanonmādanī tathā || 3-217 ||
asitāṅgī rurutī ca ṛṅkarī ca kapālikā |
nāmabhirmudritāstvetā dvādaśāre tathā śṛṇu || 3-218 ||
siddhibuddhistathā lakṣmī dīptimālā śivāśivā |
23b g] sumukhī vāmanī nandā harikeśī hayānanā || 3-219 ||
etāsāṃ viniyogaṃ tu purvavat kathitaṃ priye |
sumukhī gomukhī nandā havīkeśī mahābalāḥ || 3-220 ||
cakravegā suvegā ca mohanī daṃṣṭrinī calā |
piṅgalā kālarātrī ca vāmanī harṣaṇī dhruvā || 3-221 ||
vamanī pivanī durgā viśveśī surapūjitā |
vāyuvegā suvegā ca manovegā manovatī || 3-222 ||
jayā ca vijayā caiva jayantī cāparājitā |
śaṅkarī bhadrakālī ca karṇamoṭī mahāravā || 3-223 ||
etāvantya devyaḥ dvātriṃśāre vyavasthitāḥ |
ya idaṃ pūjayet cakraṃ vīro vā yadi vābalaḥ || 3-224 ||
vatsarād yoginī bhūtvā praviśecchāśvatāṃ tanum |
bhīrurvīratvamāyāti vīrabhāvairadhiṣṭhitaḥ || 3-225 ||
42b kh] iti bhairavasrotasi mahātantre vidyāpīṭhe saptakoṭipramāṇe śrītantrasadbhāve mantranirṇayādhikārastṛtīyaḥ paṭalaḥ || 3 || 



bhairava uvāca 

ataḥ paraṃ pravakṣyāmi vyastasādhanakarmaṇi |
aparā yā varārohe sādhanaṃ yad vyavasthitam || 4-1 ||
tryacakre tu tataścakre nābhinemisamanvite |
javākusumasaṃkāśaṃ dāḍimīkusumārciṣi || 4-2 ||
tatrasthaṃ pūjayed devaṃ sarvavarṇadharaṃ haram |
tadutthāṃ gaganāṃ devīṃ trivarṇāmaparāṃ śubhām || 4-3 ||
prahasantīṃ manonmattāṃ madabhrāntalocanām |
taḍitsahasrasaṃkāśāṃ sarvālaṃkārabhūṣitām || 4-4 ||
itthaṃrūpeṇa sā devi pūjanīyā samādhinā |
hṛccakre pūjayed devaṃ vahiḥsthāśca prapūjayet || 4-5 ||
mānasī cakravegā ca mohanyā parivāritā |
śakrādyān lokapālāṃśca vahisthāśca prapūjayet || 4-6 ||
aindrādi kramayogena pūjanīyāḥ prayatnataḥ |
māṃsasthaṃ caiva raktasthaṃ nitambasthaṃ ca prāṇagam || 4-7 ||
kaṇṭhasthaṃ tvacasaṃsthaṃ ca śukrasthaṃ guhyasaṃsthitam |
nāsasthaṃ ca payasthaṃ ca ete mantrāḥ prakīrtitāḥ || 4-8 ||
svanāmapadavinyastā namo'ntāḥ praṇavādikāḥ | 
evaṃ pūjya prayatnena gandhadhūpapavitrakaiḥ || 4-9 ||
naivedyaiḥ pānabhakṣaiśca surāsavasamanvitaiḥ |
43a kh] āmiṣairvividhākāraiḥ tarpayed guhyakāṃ priye || 4-10 ||
japaṃ cātra prakurvīta lakṣāṇāṃ navakaṃ priye |
lakṣahomaṃ tu kartavyaṃ nṛmāṃsaṃ ghṛtasaṃyutam || 4-11 ||
śākinyo vaśanāstasya trailokye yā vyavasthitāḥ | 
śmaśāne kānanodyāne giriśṛṅge catuṣpathe || 4-12 ||
24a g] eṣu deśeṣu japtavyā vidyā caivāparā śubhā |
adhamāṃ madhyamāṃ siddhiruttamā vātha sādhayet || 4-13 ||
dviguṇaṃ triguṇaṃ kāryaṃ kalpoktaṃ yad vyavasthitam |
vidhānavihitaṃ tantai yadicchet siddhimuttamām || 4-14 ||
ataḥ paraṃ pravakṣyāmi vidyāṃ caiva parāparām |
ekavīravidhānena bhairavārddhaśarīragām || 4-15 ||
karṇikasthāṃ nyased devī catvāriṃśākṣarāṃ śubhām |
patrāṣṭake tvaghoryādyāḥ padabhede yajet priye || 4-16 ||
praharṣantīṃ madonmattā daṃṣṭrotkaṭā bhayāvahām |
vikarālā mahābhīmā śavamālāvibhūṣitām || 4-17 ||
gonāsairvṛścikaiścaiva sarpairābharaṇaistathā |
mahāśavakarāṃbhujāṃ cārukarṇāvataṃśikāṃ || 4-18 ||
pralayāmbuda nirghoṣāmaṣṭahastāṃ subhīṣaṇām |
tryakṣāñca mahādaṃṣṭrāmudgirantīmivānalām || 4-19 ||
icchārūpadharīṃ devīṃ praṇatārtivināśanīm |
ya idaṃ pūjayed yāgaṃ dhanadena samo bhavet || 4-20 ||
43b kh] duṣkṛtairāpadai rogairmuhuḥ sa parimucyate |
japaṃ cātra prakartavyaṃ lakṣākṣaraṃ vibhāgaśaḥ || 4-21 ||
naivedyaṃ vidhivat datvā gandhadhūpasamanvitam |
homaṃ caiva daśāṃśena śrīvatsāṃ ghṛtasaṃyutām || 4-22 ||
bilvaṃ vātha padmaṃ vā yadicchecchreyamātmanaḥ |
astavyastakṛtā vidyā calena sahitā priye || 4-23 ||
dvādaśasvarasaṃbhinnā jātiṣaṭkāsamanvitāḥ |
hṛdayādi krameṇaiva padabhedena pūjayet || 4-24 ||
navanābhe mahāpadme ekāśītipadānvite |
toraṇaiḥ sapatākaiśca caturdvāropaśobhite || 4-25 ||
tatra madhye yajed devīṃ navaiścaiva krameṇa tu |
pūrvoktena vidhānena sādhayet sacarācaram || 4-26 ||
ghorikāṃ pūjitābhiśca laḍukāśokavarttibhiḥ |
surā tu trividhā deyā paiṣṭī mādhvī ca gauḍikā || 4-27 ||
anyāpi śubhasatvānā yatkāmā pisitāni tu |
evaṃ samyagvidhānena pūjayed yatnataḥ priye || 4-28 ||
vīrabhojyaṃ tu kartavyaṃ śaktīnāṃ bhojanaṃ tataḥ |
18a] kanyāṃstu bhojayed devi veśyā vā antyajāpi vā || 4-29 ||
samadṛṣṭistu kartavyā yathā mātṛgaṇaṃ priye |
evaṃ kṛte bhavecchāntiḥ khecaratvaṃ ca gacchati || 4-30 ||
ataḥ paraṃ pravakṣyāmi parāyā vidhimuttamam |
svābhāvikaṃ mahācakraṃ dvitīyaṃ tu calaṃ smṛtam || 4-31 ||
tṛtīyaṃ dīpamityāhuḥ caturthaṃ tu sthiraṃ bhavet |
44a kh] pañcamaṃ saṃjñañca ṣaṣṭhaṃ caiva nabhoyutam || 4-32 ||
24b g] saptamamamṛtaṃ proktaṃ aṣṭamaṃ khecarīpadam | 
navamaṃ śaktinā bhinnaṃ daśamaṃ vrahmasaṃyutam || 4-33 ||
daśaikaṃ vāmapādasthaṃ dvādaśasthordhvadīpitam |
ete cakrā mahādevi dvādaśaiva prakīrtitāḥ || 4-34 ||
vibhāgaṃ teṣu vakṣyāmi yathāvadanupūrvaśaḥ |
svarairdvādaśasaṃbhinnaṃ svābhāvikamudāhṛtam || 4-35 ||
calavāyusamāyuktaṃ dīptaṃ rakārabheditam |
sthiraṃ lakārasaṃyuktaṃ dravaṃ varuṇabheditam || 4-36 || 
vindumastakasaṃyuktaṃ nabhaḥ saṃjñantu tad viduḥ |
amṛtaṃ iva madhyasthaṃ khecarī māyayā saha || 4-37 ||
śaktiyuktaṃ varārohe brahmābhinnamataḥ param |
vāmapādayutaṃ cānyamadhordhvaṃ caiva dīpitam || 4-38 ||
taccakraṃ mahadannāma mahāścaryapradākam |
dvādaśasvarasaṃbhinnāḥ sarvacakrā na saṃśayaḥ || 4-39 ||
karma teṣāṃ pravakṣyāmi yathāvat taṃ nibodhataḥ |
svābhāvikaṃ mahācakraṃ śuklavarṇaṃ vicintayet || 4-40 ||
sadyaḥsiddhikaraṃ proktaṃ satyametadudāhṛtam |
calaṃ sūryasamaprakhyaṃ bhāṣāsiddhikaraṃ nṛṇām || 4-41 ||
tasya madhye nyaset sādhyaṃ vāyunā preritaṃ priye |
saptāhaṃ devadevaṃ tu tāvad uccāṭayed ripum || 4-42 ||
dīptayuktaṃ tu yat proktaṃ jvālanena samanvitam |
44b kh] stobhādisarvakāryeṣu yuktaṃ kāryaprasādhane || 4-43 ||
asya madhye nyaset sādhyaṃ jvalantaṃ dīptatejasam |
gṛhītaṃ hastapādaistu nirbhinnaṃ tu triśūlike || 4-44 ||
praharārddhena deveśi yāti sādhyo yamālayam |
stambhane bhedane mohe vākustambhe sainyastambhane || 4-45 ||
hastyādiśakaṭā mantrā svabhāvāntā ye prakīrtitāḥ |
sarveṣāṃ stambhakāryāṇāṃ sthiracakraṃ praśasyate || 4-46 ||
āpyāyanakaraṃ caiva śāntiḥ puṣṭikaraṃ tathā |
jvaragrahaṃ tathā lūtā sarpadaṃṣṭraistathāvidhaiḥ || 4-47 ||
mantramāpyāyane caiva chinne bhinne'tha kīlane |
traste vināśite dagdhe draveṇaiva tu kārayet || 4-48 ||
evamāpyāyanaṃ proktaṃ sarvāsu mantrajātiṣu |
khecaratvaṃ yadā cecchennabhaścakraṃ vicintayet || 4-49 ||
vapuḥ stambhe ca deveśi amṛtīkaraṇeṣu ca |
rasāyanāśca ye kecidamṛtatvaṃ praśasyate || 4-50 ||
khecarīṇāṃ tu sarveṣāṃ melakaṃ tvicchate yadā |
pādapracārikā siddhiraṇimādiguṇāṣṭakam || 4-51 ||
atītānāgatārthaṃ ca khecaraṃ tu tadābhyaset |
śakticakraṃ smṛtaṃ gauri śṛṇu tasyaiva kautukam || 4-52 ||
uccāre tu kṛte tasya pratyayaścopajāyate |
25a g] udghātaiḥ pañcabhiścaiva svayaṃ jānāti tatkṣaṇāt || 4-53 || 
45a kh] mantramudrāgaṇaṃ caiva nātra kuryād vicāraṇāt |
śarīre stobhamāyāti drutaṃ cotpatate kṣaṇāt || 4-54 ||
udgrāhayati śāstrāṇi aśrutānyapi sādhakaḥ |
ṛgvedaṃ ca yajurvedaṃ sāmavedamatharvaṇam || 4-55 ||
saṣaḍaṅgaṃ purāṇaṃ ca mīmāṃsā nyāyavistaraḥ |
dharmaśāstrasamopetaṃ vidyāsthānāścaturdaśaḥ || 4-56 ||
evaṃvidhāni śāstrāṇi granthārthena tu jānāti |
yathā jalaughasaṃghasya nimnāmbukaprayāsataḥ || 4-57 ||
na dhartuṃ śakyate kaścit tadvadvāgeva sādhakaḥ |
18b] avyāhatapado dhyātā kriyākāraṇamālinī || 4-58 ||
vāgīśo bhavate sākṣācchakticakravyavasthitaḥ |
vicaredakhilān bhogān kāmarūpo mahābalaḥ || 4-59 ||
yān yān cintayate kāmān tān tān labhati sādhakaḥ | 
cintāmaṇiryathā loke cintitārthaphalapradaḥ || 4-60 ||
tathā śakti varārohe cintitā jñānarūpiṇī |
icchāsiddhipradā sā tu nātra kāryā vicāraṇā || 4-61 ||
saptakoṭyastu mantrāṇāmaprameyāśca te smṛtāḥ |
teṣāṃ caiva varārohe vīryarūpā prakāśitā || 4-62 ||
tayā hīnā varārohe bālāmṛd vṛṣabhairiva |
krīḍanti cājñārūpāstu dṛṣṭakaivalyakāṃkṣiṇaḥ || 4-63 || 
brahmabhinna yadā cakraṃ abhyaset sādhakottamaḥ |
45b kh] vedādisarvaśāstrāṇi aśrutānyapi pāragaḥ || 4-64 ||
vāmapādena saṃbhinnaṃ yadā japati sādhakaḥ |
