tadā kṣobhaṃ karotyāśu divyādivyataraṃ priye || 4-65 ||
yakṣagāndharvakanyāstu nāginyo baladarpitāḥ |
sarvāstā kṣobhayatyāśu vāmapādaprabhāvataḥ || 4-66 || 
tatkṣaṇāt pṛthivīṃ sarvāṃ sameruvanakānanām | 
samudradvīpasaṃyuktāṃ śatakoṭipravistarām || 4-67 ||
paryaṭennātra sandehaḥ satyaṃ satyaṃ na saṃśayaḥ |
viśiṣṭāḥ pratyayā ye tu śāstroktāḥ kṣobhanādayaḥ || 4-68 ||
māraṇoccāṭanādyāstu mantracchedāstathaiva ca |
sarvetāṃ kurute mantrī adhordhvaṃ caiva dīpitaḥ || 4-69 || 
parācakrāḥ samākhyātāḥ sādhanāni pṛthak pṛthak | 
kimanyat pṛcchase devi tat sarvaṃ kathayāmi te || 4-70 ||

iti bhairavasrotasi mahātantre vidyāpīṭhe saptakoṭipramāṇe śrītantrasadbhāve vyastasādhanacaturthaḥ paṭalaḥ || 4 ||



25b g] śrībhairava uvāca 

ataḥ paraṃ pravakṣyāmi sarvātmācakramuttamām | 
ṣaṭkasaptati bhedena yugmādhikena bhairavi || 5-1 ||
yathā te kathayiṣyāmi tacchṛṇuṣva śubhekṣaṇe |
samasūtraṃ puraṃ kṛtvā caturbhāgavibhājitam || 5-2 ||
46a kh] caturbhāgacaturdvāraṃ vīthīhīnaṃ tu madhyataḥ |
prathamaṃ yad bhaved bhāgaṃ caturbhāgān prakalpayet || 5-3 ||
prathame bhāge tatraiva karṇikāṃ tu prakalpayet | 
dvitīye kesarā kāryā dalasandhiṃ tṛtīyake || 5-4 ||
dalāgrāṇi caturthe tu tasyānte bhrāmaṇī smṛtā |
sādhayet pūrvataḥ sūtraṃ dakṣiṇamuttaraṃ tathā || 5-5 ||
paścimamevamevaṃ tu samantāt samavartulam |
caturdalasamopetaṃ karṇikākesarairyutam || 5-6 ||
tasyāpi bāhyato devi aṣṭapatra kuśeśayam | 
dvādaśāraṃ tato bāhye tasyo'pyante tu ṣoḍaśam || 5-7 ||
ṣoḍaśānte punardevi dvātriṃśadalasaṃyutam |
evaṃ cakrāṇi pañcaite cakrānte tu nigadyate || 5-8 ||
bāhyato hasta ekena vīthikāṃ parikalpayet |
dvārāṇi tatra catvāri kartavyā padmamānataḥ || 5-9 ||
sitavarṇojjvalā śubhrā kartavyā śubhalakṣaṇā | 
athavā bhāvamāpanno hṛtpadme parikalpayet || 5-10 ||
mantranyāsaṃ tataḥ kuryād bījairdvādaśabhedataḥ |
sve sve varṇādhikāreṇa parāyāstu yathākramam || 5-11 ||
dīrghāstu yoginī jñeyā hrasvā rudrāḥ prakalpayet |
dale dale tu dātavyo rudraṣaṭko varānane || 5-12 || 
taducchagagatā devya ṣaṭkā yuktāni yogataḥ | 
46b kh] ṣaḍ mudrāḥ ṣaṭyoginyastenāyaṃ dvādaśo gaṇāḥ || 5-13 ||
19a] dvisaptatiḥ samākhyātāḥ ṣaṭkaścaiva surārcite |
nyāsaṃ teṣāṃ pravakṣyāmi śrūyatāṃ vidhiniścayaḥ || 5-14 ||
catuṣke tu nyased devi napuṃsakagaṇaṃ śubham |
aṣṭāre tu yakārādyāḥ prāṇāntā yāvadeva hi || 5-15 ||
26a g] dvādaśāre kalāḥ nyasya dvādaśaḥ parisaṃkhyayā |
ṣoḍaśairvā yathā nyasya nādyādhārāḥ śubhekṣaṇe || 5-16 ||
sparśākhyaṃ tu tato dadyād dvātriṃśaddalamadhyataḥ |
evaṃ nyāsastu kartavyo bāhyācaraṇagaḥ priye || 5-17 ||
karṇikāyāṃ nyased devaṃ ruruṃ sarvātmakaṃ śubham |
tryakṣaṃ daśabhujaṃ bhīmaṃ padmahastaṃ trilocanam || 5-18 ||
jaṭājūṭadharaṃ devaṃ śaśāṅkaṃ kṛtamūrddhvajam |
prottuṃgena lalāṭena nāsāvaṃśena śobhitā || 5-19 ||
itthaṃrūpaṃ sadā dhyeyaṃ karṇikāntargataṃ vibhum |
tasyordhvagagatā devī nādiphāntasvarūpiṇī || 5-20 ||
sarvāvayavasaṃyuktā krīḍayantī mahādyutiḥ |
dale dale tu yugmaṃ tu krīḍābhinayatatparāḥ || 5-21 ||
teṣāṃ nāmāni vakṣyāmi vīrāṇāṃ yogibhiḥ saha |
harirvāmanakaḥ siṃho mahākālo'tha bhairavaḥ || 5-22 ||
jayā ca vijayā caiva gokarṇī ca mahābalāḥ |
catuṣke vinyased devi yogeśvaryo mahābalaḥ || 5-23 ||
47a kh] baliśca balinandaśca daśagrīvo haro hayaḥ |
mādhavaśca samākhyāto mahābalaparākramaḥ || 5-24 ||
viśvā viśveśvarī caiva hāraudrī vīranāyike |
ambā pūrveti nāmāni yoginyastvambharodbhavā || 5-25 ||
pūjyāḥ ṣaṭke mahāgauri vāñchitārthaphalapradāḥ |
nandiścaiva mahākālo vṛṣabho bhṛṅgireva ca || 5-26 ||
sameśvarastathā śarmaḥ ajeśaścaturānanaḥ |
jambhanī mohanī vāmā andhanī kartṛreva ca || 5-27 ||
kalanī damanī caiva tathā pramathanīti ca |
aṣṭāre pūjayedetā yogeśvaryo mahābalāḥ || 5-28 ||
dakṣaścaṇḍo haraḥ śauṇḍī pramatho bhīmamanmathaḥ |
śakuniḥ samatirnando gopālo'tha pitāmahaḥ || 5-29 ||
vāmanī jaṭilā rambhā siṃhī haṃsā gajānanā |
bhīṣaṇī piṅgalā śūrā gajakarṇiḥ pinākinī || 5-30 ||
śikhidhvajāśca vikhyātā dvādaśāre vyavasthitāḥ |
nabhau bhānuranantaśca helārājo mahābalaḥ || 5-31 ||
bhīmārjuno tathā droṇo mahārājo'tha sundaraḥ |
bhasmarājo'tha kalyaṇo madhuḥ sindūrakastathā || 5-32 ||
caturo vāmanaścāpi vīrā hyevaṃ prapūjayet |
śivā hariprabhā durgā bhadrakālī mano'nugā || 5-33 ||
kauśikī kāli viśveśī jayā ca vijayā tathā |
47b kh] 26a g] raktā raktapriyā caiva karālā kekarā tathā || 5-34 ||
mahocchuṣmā ca yoginyaḥ ṣoḍaśāre vyavasthitāḥ |
saṃvarto lakulīśaśca bhṛguścetastathaiva ca || 5-35 ||
vakaḥ khaḍgī pinākī ca bhujaṅgo vālireva ca |
mahākālo durāṇḍaśca cchagalaṇḍaḥ śikhistathā || 5-36 ||
lohito meṣamīnau ca tridaṇḍī ca tathā ṣaḍhiḥ |
umākāntorddhanārīśo dāruko lāṅgalī tathā || 5-37 ||
someśvaro'tha śarmāṇaścaturvaktraikanetrakaḥ |
kūrmaścaivaikarudraśca śivottamastathā paraḥ || 5-38 ||
pañcāntakaścaṇḍakrodhī saṃpūjyāścakre tu śobhane |
bhagayogī tu citrākṣī viśālā ca tathāparā || 5-39 ||
dāmoṭī caiva kalhoṭī daṃṣṭriṇī piṅgalā tathā |
mūlā mūlatarā caiva yatrā vai mūlaghātikā || 5-40 ||
mūlakārī tathānyā ca vijñeyā prāṇahārikā |
chuchundarī ca ratnākṣī rākṣasī ca tathāparā || 5-41 ||
ghorā ca śūkarī caiva tathānyā ca valotkaṭā |
piśācānyatyanāpīḍā vyāghrī haṃsī halāyudhā || 5-42 ||
19b] sahanī grasanī caiva mohanī priyadarśanā |
trāsanī ca samākhyātā dvātriṃśāre prapūjayet || 5-43 ||
eṣāṃ pūjāṃ tataḥ kṛtvā sugandhaiḥ kusumojjvalaiḥ |
48a kh] naivedyaiḥ pānabhakṣaiśca gandhadhūpapavitrakaiḥ || 5-44 ||
atha hṛdāmbujāvasthaṃ etaccakraṃ samādhinā |
māṃsaiḥ kusumaiḥ śubhairdivyayogacidāmvaraiḥ || 5-45 ||
eṣa sarvodayo yāgaḥ khecaratvaprasādhakaḥ |
padmāvaraṇasaṃsthāstu yoginyastu mahābalāḥ || 5-46 ||
atra sannihitā devi bhavante parameśvari |
nyāsamātreṇa cakrasya ṣaṇmāsād vidito bhavet || 5-47 ||
sevanād devi siddhyeta nātra kāryā vicāraṇā |
kaulikaṃ tu idaṃ cakraṃ nākhyeyaṃ kasyacit priye || 5-48 ||
pramādād jñāpayed yastu cakraṃ jagati viśrutam |
nāhamanugṛhāmi pāpācāraṃ durātmakam || 5-49 ||
upekṣayāmi taṃ nityaṃ caurāgnyupadravādikam |
yoginyaiḥ pūtanaiḥ śyāmairbhakṣyamāṇaṃ narādhamam || 5-50 ||
yastu pālayate guptaṃ tantrācāraṃ sudurlabham |
sa siddhyati na sandeho yathā bhairavamavravīt || 5-51 ||
yāmalākhyamidaṃ rudre pūjanīyaṃ yugatraye |
kalau yuge mahāghore alpavīryāstu sādhakāḥ || 5-52 ||
teṣāṃ tu kathayiṣyāmi ekavīraṃ sudurlabham |
cakraṃ rudrātmakaṃ nāma tacchṛṇuṣvamṛtāṃ girām || 5-53 ||
27a g] aṣṭāraṃ cakramālikhya keśarālaṃ sakarṇikam |
ṣoḍaśāraṃ punaḥ paścād dvātriṃśat tasya bāhyataḥ || 5-54 ||
aṣṭārāgre kalāḥ nyasya dve dve jīvavidarbhite |
48b kh] aṣṭavargāḥ smṛtā ye tu dalamadhye niyojayet || 5-55 ||
vāmanāḍīgatāḥ sarve otaprotamidaṃ priye |
keśaraistu kalā yojyāḥ sumanādiśivāntagāḥ || 5-56 ||
dvātriṃśad yad dalā devi varṇāstatraiva vinyaset |
kakārādihakārāntā nyastavyāḥ parameśvari || 5-57 ||
yosau makāra ityukto vindurūpo maheśvaraḥ |
sarveṣāṃ caiva varṇānāṃ śirasthaḥ karmasaṃsthitaḥ || 5-58 ||
kṣakāro bhairavaḥ sākṣāt puruṣārūḍha īśvaraḥ |
daṇḍena dīpitāścaiva vāyunā preritaḥ punaḥ || 5-59 ||
arddhacandrakṛtoṣṇīṣo bindunādaśikhānvitaḥ |
śuddhasphaṭikasaṃkāśaṃ sphurantaṃ dīptatejasām || 5-60 ||
marud vyomni śikhākāraṃ taḍitkoṭisamujjvalam |
karṇikoparidīpyantaṃ bhairavaṃ tatra cintayet || 5-61 ||
hṛdaye vinyaseccakraṃ sarvātmā cātrasaṃsthitaḥ |
anena vidhinā devi ṣaṇmāsaṃ yāvadabhyaset || 5-62 ||
tāvat siddhyatyasau yogī nātra kāryā vicāraṇā |
śukladhyānaprayogeṇa śāntiḥ puṣṭimvāpnuyāt || 5-63 ||
vaśīkaraṇa cākarṣe raktavarṇaṃ vicintayet |
stambhane bhedane dhvaṃse indracāpasamaprabham || 5-64 ||
māraṇe kṛṣṇavarṇābhaṃ vidveṣe dhūmravarṇakam |
evaṃ dhyānaviśeṣeṇa yad yatkāmaṃ samīhate || 5-65 ||
tat sarvaṃ jāyate tasya śaktiyuktasya suvrate |
49a kh] bhramantaṃ cintayeccakraṃ vāyvagnipuramadhyagam || 5-66 ||
kaulikāni tu bhāṣāṇi vadate sādhakottamaḥ |
mudrābandhānyanekāni anyaśāstroditāni tu || 5-67 ||
udgahayati cāvaśyaṃ śāstrasadbhāvameva ca |
atītānāgatārthaṃ ca pṛṣṭo'sau kathayiṣyati || 5-68 ||
aṇimādiguṇāvāptirjāyate hyavicārataḥ |
utpatennātra sandeho yadā tallayatāṃ gataḥ || 5-69 ||
yāḥ kalāḥ parvamuddiṣṭāḥ keśarāgre pratiṣṭhitāḥ |
20a] tāsānnāmāni vakṣyāmi yathāvadanupūrvaśaḥ || 5-70 ||
sumanā spṛhanā durgā devakī sumanoharī |
gaurī gaṅgā tvatharvāṇā suśivā navamī smṛtā || 5-71 ||
nava śaktyāḥ smṛtā hyetāḥ keśareṣu vyavasthitāḥ |
27b g] pūrvādikramayogena nyasedīśānamantagāḥ || 5-72 ||
suśivā madhyadeśe tu dātavyā tu varānane |
etaccakraṃ samākhyātaṃ na deyaṃ kasyacit priye || 5-73 ||
pramādād vadate yastu prāyaścittī sudurmatiḥ |
prāyaścittaṃ japellakṣaṃ sarvātmanasya suvrate || 5-74 ||
preritaṃ śaktinā pūrvaṃ śuddho janmaśatairapi |
tasya devi pradātavyaṃ satyaṃ satyaṃ na saṃśayaḥ || 5-75 ||

devyuvāca

adbhutaṃ kathitaṃ deva surāṇāṃ yanna gocaram |
kathaṃ jānanti martye'smiṃ puruṣā hyalpabuddhayaḥ || 5-76 ||
49b kh] durvijñeyaikajanmā tu śatajanmā kathaṃ viduḥ |

bhairava uvāca
śṛṇu devi pravakṣyāmi janmaṃ yenātra vindate || 5-77 ||
jīvavahnisamārūḍhaṃ pavanena tu dīpitam |
yonimadhye nyasitvā tu śrīkhaṇḍena vilepayet || 5-78 ||
dhūpayenmṛṣṭadhūpena pūjayet kusumaiḥ śubhaiḥ |
taṃ nirīkṣāpayed yavācchaktivedhaṃ tu kārayet || 5-79 ||
ātmānaṃ cintayet tatra yonimadhye surārcite |
muhūrtaṃ cintayed yāvat tāvat stobho na saṃśayaḥ || 5-80 ||
athaitat stobhakaṃ piṇḍaṃ mātṛkākṣaradarbhitam |
nirvāhaṃ yāvadāyātaṃ tāvadeva samālikhet || 5-81 ||
vāmāvartavidhānena pūjayed gandhapuṣpakaiḥ |
sudhūpairdhūpyatāṃ paścāt tarpayedarpayet tathā || 5-82 ||
taṃ nirīkṣāpayecchiṣyaṃ bhramadhyāyet suvegavān |
stutyate nātra saṃdeho yadi dhyānātrantamuhyate || 5-83 ||
atha caiva * piṇḍaṃ kuṇḍalyāyāḥ praveśayet |
karṇikāntargataṃ likhya paścāt padmaṃ samālikhet || 5-84 ||
ṣoḍaśārānvitaṃ śubhaṃ catustriṃśadalaṃ punaḥ |
ṣoḍaśāre svarān nyasya agnivātasamīritān || 5-85 ||
sparśāñcaiva vidhānena yāvadāntāntamāgatāḥ |
pūjayed vidhivat pūrvaṃ yathā stobhaṃ prajāyate || 5-86 ||
50a kh] stabdho vadatyasau bhadre yatra jātaḥ sa pūrvakaḥ |
tāṃ yoniṃ vadate kṣipraṃ śatajanmodbhavāṃ priye || 5-87 ||
sukṛtaṃ duṣkṛtaṃ caiva tat sarvaṃ jñāyate dhruvam |
evaṃ parīkṣite śiṣye upadeśaṃ samarpayet || 5-88 ||


iti bhairavasrotasi mahāatantre vidyāpīṭhe saptakoṭipramāṇe śrītantrasadbhāve sarvātmacakrādhikāro nāma pañcamaḥ paṭalaḥ || 5 ||



bhairava uvāca

ataḥ paraṃ mahāguhyaṃ girirājākhyate tava |
umāmāheśvaraṃ cakraṃ kathayāmi suniścayāt || 6-1 ||
28a g] padaṃ ca padabhedaṃ ca yo jānāti sa siddhyati |
oṃ namo bhagavato rudrāya padaṃ hyekam mahādhipe || 6-2 ||
niścayena varārohe kathitaṃ tu daśākṣaram |
namaścāmuṇḍe dvitīyaṃ pañcākṣaramudāhṛtam || 6-3 ||
tamaścākāśa mātṛṇāṃ padamanyat tṛtīyakam |
aṣṭākṣaraṃ samākhyātaṃ lakṣaṇena vilakṣitam || 6-4 || 6- 
sarvakāmārthasādhanīnāṃ padaṃ caiva caturthakam |
navākṣaraṃ sa evātra padaṃ yat samudāhṛtam || 6-5 ||
ajarāmarīṇāṃ cātra padaṃ paṃcaguṇāvaham |
akṣarāṇāṃ samāsena ṣaṭsaṃkhyātaṃ varānane || 6-6 ||
sarvatrāpratihatagatīnāṃ padaṃ ṣaṣṭhamudāhṛtam |
50b kh] daśārṇaṃ tu samākhyātaṃ saṃkhyāyāstu varānane || 6-7 ||
20b] svarupapararūpāparivartanīnāṃ padaṃ saptamaṃ bhavet | 
akṣarāṇāṃ samāsena daśatritayamuttamam || 6-8 ||
sarvasattvavaśīkaraṇocchedanonmūlasamastakarmapravṛttānāṃ padaścāṣṭamakaṃ bhavet |
akṣarāṇāṃ samāsena viṃśaccatvārisaṃkhyayā || 6-9 ||
sarvamātṛguhyahṛdayaṃ parasiddhaṃ padaṃ taṃ navamaṃ bhavet |
caturdaśākṣaropetaṃ kathitaṃ vīravandite || 6-10 ||
parakarmacchedanakaraṃ satsiddhikaraṃ padaṃ caiva dvipañcakam |
akṣarāṇāṃ tathā saṃkhyā ekatra samudāhṛtā || 6-11 ||
dvisaptaparimāṇena sphuṭāmetanmaheśvari |
aṣṭākṣaraṃ samākhyātaṃ mātṝṇāṃ vacanaṃ śubham || 6-12 || 
ekādaśamametaddhi padaṃ sarvaguṇāvaham |
tad yatheti samāyuktaṃ mātṝṇāṃ hṛdayaṃ matam || 6-13 ||
akṣarāṇāṃ śataikena viṃśaccatvārimūddhṛtam |
padaikādaśabhiḥ proktaṃ nādhikaṃ vidyate'tra tu || 6-14 ||
nyūnaṃ vā padavarṇābhyāṃ dvitīyaṃ śobhanaṃ priye |
oṃ cāmuṇḍeti saṃyuktaṃ brahmāṇī prathamaṃ padam || 6-15 ||
māheśvarī dvitīyaṃ syāt kaumāryāyāstṛtīyakam |
51a kh] vaiṣṇavyāyāścaturthaṃ tu vārāhyāḥ pañcamaṃ tataḥ || 6-16 ||
aindryāḥ ṣaṣṭhaṃ vijānīyādaiśānyāḥ saptamaṃ bhavet |
āgneyāścāṣṭamaṃ jñeyaṃ kathitaṃ tava śobhane || 6-17 ||
evamādyā smṛtā devyā kathitā tava śobhane |
brahmāṇyādipradā ye tu natyantāḥ praṇavādikāḥ || 6-18 ||
ekatra piṇḍitāṣṭaiva aṣṭaṣaṣṭyādhikākṣaraiḥ |
cāmuṇḍāpadasaṃyuktaṃ mātṛkhaṇḍaṃ prakīrtitam || 6-19 ||
sarvamātṛnikāyasya vācakaṃ siddhikārakam |
 oṃ namaścāmuṇḍe padaṃ ṣaḍvarṇaṃ prathamaṃ bhavet || 6-20 ||
ūrddhvakeśī caturvarṇaṃ dvitīyaṃ jvalitaśśikhe |
28b g] pañcavarṇaṃ tṛtīyaṃ hi vidyujjihve caturthakam || 6-21 ||
pañcamaṃ tārakākṣī tu dve pade caturakṣare |
piṅgalabhruve paṣṭhaṃ vikṛtadaṃṣṭre saptamam || 6-22 ||
dve pade pañcavarṇe tu kruddhe? varṇaṃ tathāṣṭamam |
māṃsaśoṇitasaṃyuktaṃ surāsavapriye padam || 6-23 ||
navamavarṇadaśaikaṃ tu daśamaṃ tu hasadvayam |
nṛtya dvayadaśaikaṃ ca caturvarṇau padāvimau || 6-24 ||
vipramāpa dvayaṃ syād dvādaśaṃ tu trayodaśam |
māyā trailokyarūpeti saptārṇaṃ samudāhṛtam || 6-25 ||
aṣṭā * ?daśaṃ turyuktaṃ sahasraparivartani |
nudayugmaṃ tripañcaiva padaṃ ṣoḍaśamaṃ sphuṭam || 6-26 ||
51b kh] ciri saptadaśaṃ viddhi hiryaṣṭādaśamaṃ padam |
niryekonaviṃśatimaṃ pañcaiva caturakṣarāḥ || 6-27 ||
tryaśadvidvitayaṃ viṃśadekottara bhrāmaṇīdvayam |
dvau padau ṣaṭkavarṇauktau vidrāvaṇiyugaṃ tataḥ || 6-28 ||
dvāviṃśati * * ṣṭhārṇaṃ kṣobhaṇidvitayaṃ tathā |
triviṃśamaṃ padaṃ hyetanmāraṇi turyaviṃśamam || 6-29 ||
dvāvimau tu padau proktau ravarṇaṃ ca pramāṇakau |
saṃjīvanidvirabhyastaṃ ca varṇaṃ pañcaviṃśamam || 6-30 ||
heriyugmaṃ ṣaḍviṃśaṃ geri vai saptaviṃśamam |
ghuryāṣṭaviṃśamaṃ proktaṃ trayete turyavarṇakāḥ || 6-31 ||
ghurilayugmekonatriṃśatistriṃśamaṃ padam |
namo mātṛgaṇāyeti saptārṇaṃ saṃprakīrtitam || 6-32 ||
namo namastathā vicce svāhāntaṃ aṣṭavarṇakam |
ekatriṃśatimametat sarvāvāptikaraṃ param || 6-33 ||
ekatriṃśatpadairdevi guṇasaṃkīrtanaṃ sphuṭam |
sāṣṭaṣaṣṭiśataikena varṇānāṃ saṃprabhāvitam || 6-34 ||
varṇānāṃ tu śate dve tu dvānavatyā varānane |
adhikaḥ kathitastatra mātṝṇāṃ nāmavarjitām || 6-35 ||
21a] trikhaṇḍā sahitā hyeṣā agraṇīḥ smaraṇād bhṛśam |
ekatra padasaṃyogāt parimāṇāṃ śatārddhataḥ || 6-36 ||
varṇānāṃ tu śatāstrīṇi saṣaṣṭyā tu varānane |
adhikāḥ kathitāstantre samayākhyā padātmikā || 6-37 ||
52a kh] padabhedena vidyeyaṃ jñātavyā sādhakena tu |
prayatnena varārohe tantrāmnāyaprapālanāt || 6-38 ||
sakṛduccāritā vidyā samayajño bhavatya sau |
pratyūṣeṇa tatoccārya śucirbhūtvā samāhitaḥ || 6-39 ||
yad dṛṣṭamaśubhaṃ kiñcit svapnānte tad vyapohate |
29a g] agamyāgamanaṃ vāpi prāṇāyāmaṃ trayaṃ caret || 6-40 || 
āvartairekaviṃśadbhirakāmād yat kṛtaṃ kṣapet | 
kāmato dviguṇaṃ kuryājjapaṃ yaḥ sadvidhotthitaḥ || 6-41 ||
madhyāhne tu śuciḥ snātvā prāṅmukho vā udaṅmukhaḥ |
nābhimātrāmbu madhyasthaṃ japedaṣṭottaraṃ śatam || 6-42 ||
śodhayet kilviṣaṃ bāhyaṃ māsamātreṇa sādhakaḥ |
sāyāhne samaye japtvā śatamekaṃ samāhitaḥ || 6-43 ||
yadvilomaṃ dinaudbhūtaṃ nāśayennātra saṃśayaḥ | 
atha bhaktiḥ samāviṣṭaḥ samayācārarakṣakaḥ || 6-44 || 
sevanājjapahomād vā dhyānāccakrasamo bhavet | 
ṣaṇmāsādutyate devi satyametadudāhṛtam || 6-45 ||
kṛtvā saṃcārajaṃ doṣānuccārāt kalmaṣāpahā |
devadrohe gurudrohe trilakṣaṃ kurute priye || 6-46 ||
chedane puṣpapatrāṇāṃ stuvatāṃ śuṣyeta tu saḥ |
sandhyālope kṛte devi trayāvartena śuddhyati || 6-47 || 
āhnikacchedasaṃjāte śatamekamudīrayet | 
52b kh] laṃghane samayānāṃ ca abhakṣasya ca bhakṣaṇe || 6-48 ||
avācyavācite devi sahasrācchuddhiriṣyate |
cāvakākakapotānāṃ pakṣīṇāṃ ghātane kṛte || 6-49 ||
śaptena śuddhyate devi satyaṃ satyaṃ na saṃśayaḥ |
chāgameṣastathānye ca mṛgajaṃ pūtarikṣakāḥ || 6-50 ||
sahasrācchuddhiruddiṣṭā yathā bhairavamavravīt | 
sarpāntakarahantāro ḍuṇḍagomatsyaghātakaḥ || 6-51 || 
śatena śuddhyate kṣipraṃ iti śāstre pracoditam |
śvānamārjāranakulāḥ mūṣako vātha godhikā || 6-52 ||
pañcāśācchuddhirityeṣāṃ japāt pāpakṣayo bhavet |
gavāṃ hantā praśuddhyeta japasāhasrasaṃkhyayā || 6-53 ||
vratinaṃ tu yadā devi pramādād ghātitaṃ vudhaiḥ | 
śravaṇakaṃ na cānya tu lakṣaikāt sa viśuddhyati || 6-54 || 
mahāvratadharaṃ hatvā trikoṭyo japamāpnuyāt | 
pāśupato dvikoṭayastu buduścāyutasaṃkhyayā || 6-55 ||
anye'pi vratadhārī syādayutā siddhiriṣyate |
striyo ghāto durātmāno daśakoṭijapācchuciḥ || 6-56 ||
anyeṣāṃ varṇajātīnāṃ adhamottamamadhyamām |
sahasraistu bhavecchuddhistriṃśaścatvārisaṃkhyayā || 6-57 || 
ekānekena kartavyā varṇāścaiva krameṇa tu |
brāhmaṇāṃ tu yadā hatvā lakṣaiḥ pañcāśa suvrate || 6-58 ||
53a kh] śuddhyate tu na sandeha iti śāstrasya niścayaḥ |
nindate yoginī yastu śivabhaktaśca ? nindyate || 6-59 ||
śāstrāṇi dūṣayatyevaṃ striyāmākoṭiyeti ca |
29b g] krośeta kanyakāṃ yastu sahasrācchuddhiriṣyate || 6-60 || 
krodhenāpi yadā devi soccaśabda pralāpitam | 
kṛtvā cāvartayed vidyāṃ śāntimāśu prayacchati || 6-61 ||
kṛte karmaṇi vālānāṃ lūtāpiṭakagaṇḍakaiḥ |
grahajvaraviṣādibhya auṣadhyā yaugakhyāpane || 6-62 ||
sahasrācchuddhiruddiṣṭā vaitaṇḍāt śaivaśāsane |
deśikaṃ sādhakaṃ vāpi putrakaṃ samayajñakam || 6-63 ||
pramādānninditā hyete dvisahasrācchuddhiriṣyate | 
21b] aliṃ jugupsayed yastu phalguṣaṃ ca tathā priye || 6-64 || 
ekoccāreṇa śuddhyeta anyad vā yajjugupsitam |
kandukaṃ mallakausalyaṃ śilpaka., carmakārakam || 6-65 ||
dhvajaṃ śūnyakaraṃ vāpi matsyaghātaṃ ca lubdhakam |
gathodhikaṃ ca gauḍaṃ ca naṭacchatruṃ ca chatrikā || 6-66 ||
chāyākrīḍaṃ ca māhendraṃ laṇḍikā indrajālikam | 
aṃkelakaṃ ca kathakaṃ indrākhyaṃ bhavanaṃ tathā || 6-67 || 
vandinaṃ bhājanaṃ bhāṇḍaṃ gāyakavādapeśalam |
evaṃvidhaviśiṣṭānyat kālā kālāntarā bhavet || 6-68 ||
raṃgopajīvinaṃ devi khasrakairātavarvaram |
parāsikaṃ mahādevi putrikāṃ bandhukārikam || 6-69 ||
53b kh] mātaṅgayājanaṃ ḍomvaṃ hantikaṃ śavaraṃ tathā |
kāpotakaṃ kulatthaṃ ca kuṭṭakaṃ goṇḍukaṃ tathā || 6-70 || 
siṃhalaṃ draviḍaṃ karṇāṭaṃ gotumaśca pulindakam |
varṇaṭāṃ bhaṇḍakākāraṃ mahārāṣṭraṃ ca snāpitam || 6-71 ||
koṅkaṇaṃ cīnaṃ vāhlīkaṃ bāṅgalaṃ kāmarūpakam |
māgadhaṃ saiṃdhavaṃ vātha gorjunaṃ lāṭasaṃjñakam || 6-72 ||
kulutaṃ kaśmīraṃ ca jaṭṭatu hāsavyāṭakaṃ |
lampākaṃ śvānavaktraṃ ca kīraṇṭākaṃ trimālavam || 6-73 ||
anye'pi deśamadhyasthā vane vā sāntyajā yataḥ | 
veśyādikramaśaḥ sarve nindanā śuddhiriṣyate || 6-74 ||
trayāvartena deveśi akāmāt kāmato'pi vā |
kāmato dviguṇaṃ jāpyaṃ kartavyaṃ siddhimicchataḥ || 6-75 ||
ete nirodharūpāstu sādhakānāṃ prakāśitāḥ |
tadarthe kathitā vidyā yena śuddhyanti mānavāḥ || 6-76 ||
śreyārthināṃ mayākhyātā madbhaktikṛtaniścayāḥ |
anyeṣāṃ na kadācit syāllaulyārthe ye sthitānaghe || 6-77 ||
eṣā vidyā samākhyātā upayogā varānane |
sāṃprataṃ padabhedena yathā yojyā tu bhairavi || 6-78 ||
tathā te kathayiṣyāmi tacchṛṇuṣva samāsataḥ |
yā vidyā kathitā pūrvā nādiphāntakrameṇa tu || 6-79 ||
54a kh] taccharīragatā varṇā bhedaye jānunā priye |
vāmāgrā yonirūpā yā varṇāmekaikasaṃkhyayā || 6-80 ||
bhairavaḥ śabdarāśistu ādikṣāntasvarūpataḥ |
varṇāste śaktibījena saṃpuṭitvā pṛthak pṛthak || 6-81 ||
ādikṣāntakrameṇaiva niyojanamudāhṛtam |
dvābhyāṃ ca grathanaṃ kāryaṃ samastābhyāṃ varānane || 6-82 ||
ādau varṇacaturmadhye vidyāyāstu padaṃ dadet |
ante caiva catuṣkaṃ syāt tasyānte tu padaṃ punaḥ || 6-83 ||
catuṣkaṃ tu punardevi padaṃ ca tadanantaram |
anena kramayogena nirvāheṇa tapodhane || 6-84 ||
kṣāntaṃ vai yāvad deveśi tāvadeva niyojayet |
padasaṃkhyā samastasya nirvāhobhayadīpitaiḥ || 6-85 ||
mālinī dvādaśairdevi śabdarāśistu ṣoḍaśaiḥ |
anena kramaśaḥ sarve varṇāścaiva pṛthak pṛthak || 6-86 ||
calacakravibhāgena padavidyā yadā yajet |
tadā kṣobhaṃ karotyāśu divyādivyatareṣvapi || 6-87 ||
yonayaḥ pañcadhā yāstu sarvāḥ klidyanti nānyathā |
drāvaṇaṃ kṣobhaṇaṃ mohaṃ jṛmbhaṇaṃ śoṣaṇaṃ tathā || 6-88 ||
sarvaṃ taṃ kurute devi sadā śaktisamo bhavet |
prasuptabhujagākārā dvādaśānte varānane || 6-89 ||
nābhisthānastathāpyevaṃ draṣṭavyā parameśvarī |
dṛśyate dehamadhye tu vyomānte ca parāpare || 6-90 ||
54b kh] tasyāgre tu tato mantro hutāśakarṇikākṛtiḥ |
22a] uccareta tato mantraṃ śabdarūpaṃ hṛdi priye || 6-91 ||
śabdānte śaktimuccārya iṣanmadhyagamārutā |
padmasūtranibhākārā uccāryā sā sunāsikā || 6-92 ||
uccārāntāvasāne tu jñātavyālātacakravat |
tatra madhyagataṃ devi caitanyaṃ mantrasaṃyutam || 6-93 ||
prasphuratkiraṇānekaiḥ koṭiśo digvyavasthitaiḥ |
tasyāpyante tato devi śaktirādyā manonmanī || 6-94 ||
atītā tu yadā sā vai tadā viśvī udāhṛtā |
viśvānte vyāpako devo māyātīto nirāmayaḥ || 6-95 ||
sa śivo bhāvanātīto nirguṇo guṇasaṃbhavaḥ |
avikārī sarvakārī śaktyātīto mahāprabhuḥ || 6-96 ||
anena kramaśo devi japaḥ kāryaḥ suniścitaiḥ |
ātmā manaśca mantraśca śivaśaktistathaiva ca || 6-97 ||
ekībhāvagatāḥ sarve jñātavyāḥ siddhimicchataḥ |
tasmāt prāṇasamaṃ jāpyaṃ mantrānte nādagocaram || 6-98 ||
nādāntaṃ tu tato jñātvā etat smaraṇamucyate |
30b g] smaraṇaṃ śaktiruddiṣṭā yā karoti gamāgamam || 6-99 ||
tasyānte tu parā sūkṣmā sā kalā tvamṛtātmakā |
layātītā tvarūpā tu svasaṃvedyā vicārataḥ || 6-100 ||
na tasyā lakṣaṇaṃ devi na lakṣo naiva yojanā |
55a kh] na kṣayo naiva vṛddhistu śuklakṛṣṇena caiva tu || 6-101 ||
na rātrirna na dinaṃ caiva na sandhyā tvayanaṃ na ca |
viṣuvaṃ naiva deveśi saṃkrāntirnaiva vidyate || 6-102 ||
sarvāvasthāgatiṃ jñātvā vijñānamupajāyate |
etat kaulikamākhyātamumāmaheśvare priye || 6-103 ||
sāṃprataṃ ca punardevi helāmārgagataṃ yathā |
nirujasya yuvānasya svasthasya taruṇasya ca || 6-104 ||
sakrāntiviṣuvasthasya dinayāmagṛheṣu ca |
vāmadakṣiṇagasthasya nāḍikākalanasya ca || 6-105 ||
uttarāyaṇasaṃsthasya dakṣiṇāyanameva ca |
sauraṃ candramasaṃ jñātvā grahaṇaṃ śaktigocaram || 6-106 ||
tadahaṃ saṃpravakṣyāmi śṛṇuṣvāyatalocane |
dve yute ca sahasraikaṃ ṣaṭśatāni tathaiva ca || 6-107 ||
ahorātreṇa deveśi etaccāramudāhṛtam |
etat samvatsaraṃ proktaṃ māsaṃ ca divasaṃ tathā || 6-108 ||
saṃkrāntirlabhyate devi vibhaktā dvādaśaiḥ kramāt |
śataiścāṣṭādaśaiḥ prāṇaiḥ saṃkrāntirbhavate priye || 6-109 ||
ghaṭikāḥ pañca vijñeyāḥ svapravṛttasya yoginaḥ |
eṣā sthūlagatirjñātvā sarveṣāṃ prāṇināṃ gatāḥ || 6-110 ||
bāhyato ghaṭikāpyekā samvatsaramiheṣyate |
ūrddhvaprāṇo ahaḥ prokto apāno gatireva ca || 6-111 ||
prāṇāpānagatiṃ jñātvā ahorātraṃ taducyate |
55b kh] triṃśat prāṇairbhavenmāsaḥ saṃkrāntiḥ sā vidhīyate || 6-112 ||
pṛthivyāpastathā tejo vāyurākāśameva ca |
pañcaite maṇḍalāḥ proktā yaiśca saṃcarate'nilaḥ || 6-113 ||
antare yanmuhūrtaṃ tu pañcānāṃ caiva śobhane |
ṣaṣṭaṃ ca tat samuddiṣṭaṃ maṇḍalāḥ ṣaḍudāhṛtāḥ || 6-114 ||
ekaike tu puṭe devi ṣaṭ saṃkāntyotyudāhṛtā |
meṣādikramayogena jñātavyā vīranāyike || 6-115 ||
dakṣiṇāyanaje kāle saṃkrāntyaḥ ṣaṭ prakīrtitāḥ |
punareva tataḥ ṣaṭkamuttarāyaṇaje smṛtam || 6-116 ||
samvatsaraṃ samākhyātaṃ puṭe caike prakīrttitam |
evaṃ samvatsarāḥ pañca bṛhaspatirahoyugam || 6-117 ||
punaḥ saṃkramate corddhe vāmadakṣiṇayogataḥ |
saṃkrāntiḥ kramaśo devi savyāsavyavijānataḥ || 6- 118 ||
yathā vāme purā proktā dakṣiṇena tathaiva ca |
31a g] jñātavyaṃ tu vipaścidbhiḥ samāsāt samudīritam || 6-119 ||
22b] meṣasthe viṣuvaṃ pūrvaṃ tulasthe tu dvitīyakam |
ādityaṃ grahaṇaṃ bāhye saumyamante prakīrtitam || 6-120 ||
saura candramasaṃ jñātvā tato mucyati vandhanāt |
sūkṣmaḥ kālaḥ samākhyāto adhyātmikamataḥ śṛṇu || 6-121 ||
ekaikasmiṃ tatasthāne kalanaṃ daśadhā smṛtam |
56a kh] daśakaṃ triguṇīkṛtvā brahmādīnāṃ varānane || 6-122 ||
guruvaktrāntāvasānaṃ hi sucāraḥ kramaśaḥ kramaḥ |
dvādaśaiva tu saṃkrāntyastatvasthāstu varānane || 6-123 ||
jñātavyāstu vipaścidbhistatvasaṃkrāntilakṣaṇam |
puṭasaṃkrāntiranyā ca nāḍīsaṃkrāntireva ca || 6-124 ||
śaśisaṃkramaṇaṃ jñātvā śubhāśubhavikalpanā |
āgame tu śubhaṃ proktaṃ nirgame vāśubhaṃ smṛtam || 6-125 ||
samaśūnye bhaved vaktraṃ nātra kāryavicāraṇāt |
āgama gamamadhyaṃ yat sā sandhyā samudāhṛtā || 6-126 ||
bāhyasandhyāntyasandhyā ca sandhyādvayamudāhṛtam madhyāhnaṃ nāyanaṃ viṣuvaṃ tathā || 6-128 ||
na kālaṃ na kalācāraṃ na tatvaṃ na ca devatā |
sunirvāṇaṃ paraṃ tatvaṃ guruvaktre pratiṣṭhitam || 6-129 ||
adhaḥsthaṃ ca japaṃ kuryāt sarvaśaṃkāvivarjitaḥ |
na mano naiva mantavyo noccāro'lamvanā na ca || 6-130 ||
nirlakṣastu manaḥ kṛtvā bhāvābhāvavivarjitaḥ |
tallayastu japaḥ kuryād yuktaścottamasiddhiṣu || 6-131 ||
manaścānyatra nikṣiptaṃ dṛṣṭiranyatra pātitā |
tathāpi yogināṃ yogo japametat pravartate || 6-132 ||
56b kh] svayaṃ japati cātmānaṃ svayaṃ jānāti cātmani |
svayaṃ kartā svayaṃ bhoktā nāhaṃ nāhaṃ na saṃnamaḥ || 6-133 ||
na saṃkhyā lakṣaṇaṃ tasya japasya suranāyike |
muktyārthe tu japaṃ kuryāt tyaktvā caivākṣamālikām || 6-134 ||
gamāgamasya jāpyena sarvapāpakṣayo bhavet |
tanmayastallayo bhūtvā rūpaṃ kuryāt sadā budhaḥ || 6-135 ||
aṇimādiguṇāvāptiraṅgaṣaṭkaṃ maheśvaryam |
pātālottiṣṭhakhaḍgādi antardhyānarasāyanam || 6-136 ||
rasaṃ vaikharyanādaṃ ca sarvamasmāt pravartate |
aihikāmuṣmikān bhogān sarvamasmāt prasādhyati || 6-137 ||
31b g] yat kiñcid draviṇaṃ loke sādhanaṃ yat samīhitam |
tatsarvamāpnuyānmantrī gamāgamajapāt priye || 6-138 ||
ātmācāragatiṃ jñātvā saṃkrāntyādipratiṣṭhitam |
karkaṭaḥ siṃhaḥ kanyā ca tulo vṛścikameva ca || 6-139 ||
dhanuṣī ca samākhyātā dakṣiṇaṃ ṣaṭkamucyate |
kālo dakṣiṇatvayanaṃ ṛtutrayasamanvitam || 6-140 ||
makaraśca tathā kumbhaṃ mīno meṣo vṛṣastathā |
mithunena samāyuktamuttarāyaṇajaṃ priye || 6-141 ||
ṣaḍ ṛtustu samākhyātā ayanadvayasaṃyutāḥ |
nāḍīkramavibhāgaṃ tu jñātavyaṃ surapūjite || 6-142 ||
57a kh] nāḍīsaṃkrāntivibhāgaṃ kathitaṃ sarvakāmadam |
puṭe puṭe tu yaścāra prāṇānāṃ kathito'naghe || 6-143 ||
saṃkrāntiḥ sā samuddiṣṭā puṭe maṇḍalakātmikā |
tatve tatve tu yo devi saṃkrāmaśca parasparam || 6-144 ||
tatvasaṃkramaṇaṃ nāma tṛtīyaṃ parikīrtitam |
caturthaṃ caiva sūkṣmaṃ tu anurūpaṃ hṛdi priye || 6-145 ||
na tasya lakṣaṇaṃ proktaṃ sūkṣmarūpaṃ yataḥ punaḥ |
tantrasthaṃ tu vijānīyājjāpaṃ vai sarvakāmadam || 6-146 ||
varṇoccāravibhāgena yathā te kathayāmyaham |
śatabhedavibhāgena sahasraṃ lakṣameva ca || 6-147 ||
23a] koṭibhedena deveśi siddhyante nātra saṃśayaḥ |
śatabhedena śuddhistu bhavate saptajanmikā || 6-148 ||
sahasreṇa prasiddhyante siddhya yogī prakīrtitāḥ |
lakṣeṇa lākṣikā siddhirbhavate nātra saṃśayaḥ || 6-149 ||
koṭistu japate yastu brahmaghno'pi hi śuddhyati |
siddhyate tu na sandehaḥ satyaṃ satyaṃ na saṃśayaḥ || 6-150 ||
etat tadgrahamākhyātaṃ gopitaṃ na prakāśitam |
tava eva mayākhyātaṃ satyaṃ satyaṃ gaṇāmbike || 6-151 ||

devyuvāca

japasya lakṣaṇaṃ deva pūrvaṃ hi kathitaṃ tvayā |
ekoccāraśataṃ jñeya sahasraṃ lakṣameva ca || 6-152 ||
koṭiścaiva tathā śaṃbho pṛthaglakṣaṇalakṣitam |
na me jñātuṃ pramāṇaṃ ca japasya suranāyike || 6-153 ||
57b kh] tadahaṃ śrotumicchāmi mandabuddhyalpamedhasām |

bhairava uvāca

yā sā kuṇḍalinī śakti cidrūpā ca parā kalā || 6-154 ||
ādyā śakti maheśasya aṇumātrā hṛdi sthitā |
saivāṇuḥ kathitastaṃtre dvāvaṇa truṭi smṛtā || 6-154 ||
truṭibhūtā tu sā devi japtavyā tu parāparā |
varṇahīnā parā proktā arāvarṇa rūpiṇī || 6-155 ||
32a g] evaṃ jñātvā varārohe japaḥ kāryaḥ sadā budhaiḥ |
truṭirūpā tu yā devī tadādhāro vyavasthitaḥ || 6-156 ||
kalate prāṇago nityaṃ daśasthānairanukramāt |
brahmaviṣṇustathā rudra īśvaro'tha sadāśivaḥ || 6-157 ||
kuṇḍalī vyāpinī caiva prāṇāntaṃ viṣuvaṃ tathā |
śaktisthānaṃ tu deveśi ete sthānā daśaḥ smṛtāḥ || 6-158 ||
yeṣu saṃcarate devi śivaḥ paramakāraṇaḥ |
ūrddhve vā yadi vā tirye svasthāne vā sureśvari || 6-159 ||
prasphureta kalairyukto daśabhistu mahābalaiḥ |
daśadhā kalanaṃ tena kathitaṃ tava śobhane || 6-160 ||
prayatnena kṛtoccāro yāvacchaktirlayaṃ gatā |
tāvad devi śataṃ proktaṃ varṇoccāre na saṃśayaḥ || 6-161 ||
svabhāvastho na coccāryaḥ sūkṣmādhāro jagatpatiḥ |
58a kh] śatadhā kalpanaṃ tasya truṭirūpasya yogine || 6-162 ||
sahasraṃ tu samuddiṣṭaṃ śatadhā parameśvari |
ubhayasya parityāgād balādhāraḥ sadāśivaḥ || 6-163 ||
dṛkkriyājñānanirmuktaḥ kalate tu sahasradhā |
lakṣabhedaḥ samuddiṣṭa iti śāstre na saṃśayaḥ || 6-164 ||
japameva vinirdiṣṭaṃ yogināṃ tatvavedinām |
lakṣātīto manātīto nirmuktastatvavandanaiḥ || 6-165 ||
unmantve sadā līno aṇurūpo na kiñcanaḥ |
sattāmātre sthito dehī guṇānāṃ pratibodhakaḥ || 6-166 ||
viṣayādhāranirmuktaḥ kalate lakṣadhā priye |
koṭistu bhavate hyevaṃ jñātavyaṃ mantravādibhiḥ || 6-167 ||
soccāropāṃśubhūtastu mānaso manavarjitaḥ |
japaḥ pūrvaṃ mayākhyātaḥ śāstre śāstre surārcite || 6-168 ||
saśabdoccārayogena śuddhyarthe kathitaṃ sphuṭam |
siddhyarthaṃ yat samuddiṣṭaḥ svavṛttau hṛdi saṃsthitaḥ || 6-169 ||
mānaso yogahetvarthe ubhayatra vivarjitaḥ |
manātīto mahādevi mokṣadastu na saṃśayaḥ || 6-170 ||
evaṃ devi samākhyāto japaḥ prāṇasamastavaḥ |
japaḥ prāṇasamaḥ kāryo dṛṣṭādṛṣṭaphalārthinā || 6-171 ||
avarṇo varṇayogena mayā te samudāhṛtaḥ |
kathitaṃ tu punaścānyaṃ mantrasadbhāvamuttamam || 6-172 ||
58b kh] lakṣaṇaṃ paramaṃ devyā yoginīhṛdayaṃ tathā |
pāraṃparyakramāyātaṃ na ca likhyati pustake || 6-173 ||
kulaṃ tu kaulikaṃ devi kulāmnāyādihāgatam |
23b] na ca labhyati gūḍhārthaṃ kulaṃ devi ca kaulikam || 6-174 ||
tadaṃśānāṃ tu vīrāṇāṃ prāptaṃ kulajakaulikam |
32b g] sa ca prāptiḥ prabhedena bhidyate tu tridhā punaḥ || 6-175 ||
upadeśaṃ ca prathamaṃ saṃpradāyaṃ dvitīyakam |
kaulikaṃ ca tṛtīyaṃ syāt kasyāyātaṃ nibodhataḥ || 6-176 ||
ye yoginīgṛhe labdhvā upadeśā varānane |
yoginīdevatairlabdhāḥ saṃpradāyāḥ śivoditāḥ || 6-177 ||
khecarairyoginīdattāḥ kaulikāste na saṃśayaḥ |
ityeṣa kathito bhedaścalabījaniṣevake || 6-178 ||
utpate tu na sandeho lakṣajāpācca labhyatu |
calaśaktiḥ samākhyātā lakṣaṇena udāhṛtā || 6-179 ||
avarṇavarṇasaṃyogāt mālinī samudāhṛtā |
padabhedagatāśca yā asaṃkyātā varānane || 6-180 ||
etat tadgrahamākhyātaṃ sadyaḥ pratyayakārakam |
na kasyacinmayākhyātamumāmāheśvaraṃ priye || 6-181 ||
satataṃ japate yastu yoginīvallabho bhavet |
ṣaṇmāsācchuddhyate devi brahmaghnopi na saṃśayaḥ || 6-182 ||
siddhyate nātra sandeho yadā cakrasya madhyataḥ |
59a kh] yasyate virajaṃ śāntaṃ jyotirūpa mahadyutim || 6-183 ||
ekoccāragatānte tu parāvasthā tu gīyate |
śatabhedaḥ samākhyātastadguṇā daśameva tu || 6-184 ||
sahasrabheda ityukto lakṣācchataguṇaḥ smṛtaḥ |
koṭibhedaśatānāṃ tu lakṣaṇaṃ varavarṇini || 6-185 ||
etajjapavidhānaṃ tu kathitaṃ tava śobhane |
muktakādi prabhedānyā japasaṃkhyā śṛṇu ṣvame || 6-186 ||
muktako yuktakaścaiva calacakrastathā paraḥ |
calacakrapadākhyasya japaḥ proktaścaturvidhaḥ || 6-187 ||
muktakaḥ śatabhedena yuktakaṃ daśaguṇaṃ smṛtam |
calacakravibhāgena lakṣabhedamudāhṛtam || 6-188 ||
calacakrapadākhyena koṭibhedo varānane |
bāhyataḥ kathito bhedo adhyātmikamataḥ śṛṇu || 6-189 ||
udbhave śatabhedastu sahasraḥ śeṣako viduḥ |
laye tu lakṣabhedo vai layātīte tu koṭivān || 6-190 ||
vāmā jyeṣṭhā tathā raudrī viśvī ca samudāhṛtāḥ |
icchā jñāna kriyā śāntā krameṇaiva surārcite || 6-191 ||
ātmacāragatiṃ jñātvā japaḥ kāryaḥ sadā budhaiḥ |
anena bāhyasūtreṇa lakṣalakṣaṇalakṣite || 6-192 ||
bhuvanākhye varārohe śaktyākhye ca tathaiva ca |
cāroccāravibhāgena japaḥ śreṣṭha udāhṛtaḥ || 6-193 || 
33a g] mālā pañcāśikā proktā sūtraṃ śaktiḥ śivātmikā |
grathanaṃ kuṇḍalī devī layānte meru saṃsthitaḥ || 6-194 ||
etadguptataraṃ devi akṣasūtraṃ śivātmakam |
prakaṭaṃ naiva kartavyaṃ na meruṃ laṃghayet kvacit || 6-195 ||
śaṃkhasphaṭikarudrākṣā putraṃjīvakariṣṭakā |
evamādyā smṛtā yāstu maṇimālā varānane || 6-196 ||
na tatra vidyate devo na mantro naiva cetanā |
yatra tatra sthitā mālā na doṣo vidyate priye || 6-197 ||
mantranyāse sthite devi kila gopyaṃ tu kārayet |
śarīraṃ kutra gopyaṃ tu kārayet tu varānane || 6-198 ||
mantranyāsakṛte hyātmā sakalīkṛtavigrahaḥ |
yathā gupto na yujyeta tadvadakṣaramālikā || 6-199 ||
akṣaṇīndriyāṇīyuktaṃ sūtraṃ kuṇḍalinī smṛtā |
lakṣaṃ tu sā parā sūkṣmā kalā yāmṛtavāhinī || 6-200 ||
saṃyogakāriṇī vyomni tena sūtreti kīrtitā |
24a] saṃkhyāgrahaṇakārye tu yā coktā cākṣamālikā || 6-201 ||
sā kasmiṃstu samudbhūtā katidhā saṃprakāśyate |
śaṃkhajā prathamā proktā sphaṭikā ca dvitīyakā || 6-202 ||
padmākṣajā tṛtīyā sā yā rudrākṣākhyā caturthikā |
jīvajā pañcamī jñeyā ṣaṣṭhī ca vaidrumā bhavet || 6-203 ||
mauktikā saptamī viddhi vaidūryā cāṣṭamī smṛtāḥ |
60a kh] indranīlā tu navamī mahānīlā dvipañcamī || 6-204 ||
daśaikā padmarāgākhyāriṣṭajā dvādaśī matā |
asthijā ca trayodaśyā nāgajā tu caturdaśī || 6-205 ||
vaṃgajā pañcadaśyā tu lohajā ṣoḍaśī priye |
sauvarṇā saptadaśyā ca kāmabhede'dhunocyate || 6-206 ||
yasya kāmasya yā mālā japakarmaṇi śasyate |
śaṃkhajā tu śriyāḥ kāmā sphaṭikā muktihetave || 6-207 ||
padmākṣā padmajā proktā śriyāpuṣṭikarā priye |
rudrākṣaiḥ siddhimāpnoti yaccānyat khecarī padam || 6-208 ||
jīvakā sarvadā jñeyā gopatyā prathitānaghe |
putrajīvakasaṃjñā tu tena tatsamudāhṛtā || 6-209 ||
vaidrumā vaśyakārye tu mauktikā sarvakāmadā |
vaiḍūryā aindranīlākhyā mahānīlasamudbhavā || 6-210 ||
padmarāgodbhavā mālā trividhā siddhidāyikā |
anyāni tu smṛtā yāstu ratnajāḥ parameśvari || 6-211 ||
sarvadā tāḥ samuddiṣṭā nātra kāryā vicāraṇāt |
riṣṭakākhyātmikā mālābhicāre sā praśasyate || 6-212 ||
33b g] uccāṭe cāsthijā mālā mahāsiddhāvabhīpsuke |
nāgajā nāgasiddhyarthaṃ vaṃgajā krūrasādhane || 6-213 ||
lohajā ca nidhānārthe sauvarṇānalasādhane |
etā mālāstu te proktā miśrānyāpi ca sā smṛtā || 6-214 ||
60b kh] māraṇe sā praśasyeta stambhane mohane tathā |
kampane dhvaṃsane devi kartavyā cābhicārake || 6-215 ||
evamanyāpi yā coktā tāsāṃ śreṣṭhā tu śaṃkhajā |
praśastā sarvakārye tu japakarmaṇi śasyate || 6-216 ||
ete cāṣṭādaśaproktā akṣasūtrā bahisthitāḥ |
adhyātme ca gatāstvete sāṃprataṃ tānnibodha me || 6-217 ||
adhyātma bāhyato dṛṣṭvā bāhyamadhyātmasaṃsthitam |
yo jānāti sa siddhyeta tadbhavabhāvabhāvitaḥ || 6-218 ||
ekaivādhyātmagaṃ sūtraṃ prāgeva kathitaṃ mayā |
saṃkhyāvartā tu yā nāḍī śikhānte tu vyavasthitā || 6-219 ||
tena śaṃkhamayī proktā akṣasūtraṃ varānane |
sphuṭate mastake yā sā dvidhā caiva visarpiṇī || 6-220 ||
sphaṭikaṃ te samuddiṣṭaṃ guruvaktre pratiṣṭhitam |
hṛtpadme saṃsthito nityaṃ akṣāṇāṃ pratibodhakaḥ || 6-221 ||
padmākṣamakṣasūtraṃ ca śāstre śāstre prakāśitam |
raudrībhāvaḥ smṛto rudrastālvante ca vyavasthitaḥ || 6-222 ||
śabdasparśarasorūpagandhatanmātrasaṃyutaḥ |
vikāratve pravarteta nirodhalakṣaṇātmakaḥ || 6-223 ||
tena rudrākṣamālāyā jāpaṃ śreṣṭhamudāhṛtam |
putravadudare kṛtvā prasuptāmṛtakuṇḍalī || 6-224 ||
61a kh] tayā nīyatyasau jīvo adhaścordhvaṃ ca bhāmini |
vaihāyasapadādūrdhvaṃ yāvattallayagocaram || 6-225 ||
bhavitābhābhibhāsantā svapadānandavigrahā |
ūrṇatantunibhākārā sindūrāruṇasaprabhā || 6-226 ||
praviṣṭā brahmarandhreṇa brahmasthaṃ citsvarūpakam |
bhāvayitvaikabhāvena gāḍhaṃ gūhyaḥ vrajet tataḥ || 6-227 ||
svabrahmarandhramārgeṇa viśedādhāramaṇḍalam |
tadātmani gataṃ bhāvyaṃ yāvat tallayatāṃ gatam || 6-228 ||
24b] satatābhyāsayogena cidākṛṣṭirbhaved dhruvam |
tena devi samākhyātā mālākṛṣṭau ca vai drumā || 6-229 ||
maunasthā manate sarvaṃ muktā vāgjālasaṃcayam |
tena te mauktijā mālā sarvāvāptikarā parā || 6-230 ||
siddhaṃ brahmātmakaṃ cakraṃ kṣiptvātra vairi pañcakam |
tena vahirgatārjitya vaiḍūryāmālayā japet || 6-231 ||
indriyāṇi suduṣṭāni calāni lolupāni ca |
svaviṣaye tu * raktāni kārayet kāraṇālaye || 6-232 ||
mahāratnaṃ bhavedātmā rañjito'sitakarmaṇā |
34a g] anādibhavikeneha śarīre vastutāṃ yayuḥ || 6-233 ||
sa eva padmarāgeṇa rañjitonmatavat priye |
bhramate cāṣṭapatreṣu svabhāvenaiva lakṣayet || 6-234 ||
aindrai caindragatāṃ bhāvaṃ bhogaiśvaryasamutsukam |
61b kh] haṭhapāśca rathabhṛtyāśca vasudhādyāṃ samīhate || 6-235 ||
agniyatrakṛte dāghaṃ śoṣamūrcchājvarāturam |
athavā ceṣṭadharmeṣu matitvasya pravartate || 6-236 ||
yāmye yāmyaṃ bhaved rāvaṃ krodhāhaṃkāraniṣṭhuram |
hanadvandvacchinnabhindamadharmapriyavādinam || 6-237 ||
nairṛte rākṣasaṃ bhāvaṃ kravyamāsavalolupam |
sarvabhakṣasthalāghātī ādye hṛṣṭaḥ sadā sukhī || 6-238 ||
priyavāsanavācchalyaṃ sarvabhūteṣu pālakī |
vāyupatre sadodvegaṃ deśāntaragamotsukam nirīkṣyate? bhūyo bhayoddvignaṃ yathāmṛtam |
yākṣe dravyāpti yo nityaṃ dharmaśīlo dṛḍhavrataḥ || 6-240 ||
pūrtadharmapriyo bhogī kāminījanasaṃkathā |
iśe cīśagataṃ bhāvaṃ puṇyatīrtha kathā priye || 6-241 ||
seved devāgniguravaṃ pūjākarmasadotsukam |
niḥspṛhaḥ kāmalobheṣu yadā cese samāśritaḥ || 6-242 ||
karṇikāntasthito jñānaṃ sarvato dik pravartate |
tenāsau buddhyate mānaṃ karaṇā mārjanāṃjitaḥ sve sve bhāvā pradāpayet |
mahānīlapadmarāgotthaṃ akṣasūtraṃ surārthadam || 6-244 ||
ariṣṭāni tvanekāni sukhaduḥkhātmakāni tu |
bhuñjate satataṃ devi svārjitaṃ yat purātanam || 6-245 ||
62a kh] riṣṭakākhyā smṛtā mālā aprameyā bhavāntare |
asthīnāṃ ca śatāstrīṇi ṣaṣṭyādhikyāḥ pṛthak sthitāḥ || 6-246 ||
sūtratā śaktisūtreṇa cetā vairvanditā satī |
brahmasthāne sthitiṃ kṛtvā labhet tadguṇagocaram || 6-247 || 
nāgavalanayogena gatā corddhvaṃ layātmakaiḥ |
tena nāgātmikā mālā nāgasiddhipradāyikā || 6-248 ||
vyaṃganāmayasauḥ karma tad vicchitvā samānatā |
labhate kāraṇaikyatvaṃ vaṃgamālā kṛtaḥ paśuḥ || 6-249 ||
lolībhūtā tu yā śaktirgatā hatvāghasantatiḥ |
tena lohātmikā mālā praśastā vidhisiddhaye || 6-250 ||
yo sā cātmā malagrastastāṃ sraṃkālikayā tathā | 
bhāvitvā ṣaḍrasaiḥ paścādvamayedagnicakragaḥ || 6-251 ||
yathā na calate'nyatra calitaḥ sāharet manaḥ |
tadgatastallayo bhūtvā suvarṇaṃ bhāti tadyathā || 6-252 ||
kāraṇaikyatvamadhyastha sadā dhyāyet suniścalaḥ |
miśramālāprabhāveṇa tadguṇatvamupāsate || 6-253 ||
34b g] evamādyā smṛtā ye tu paryāyāścākṣamālayā |
te sarve cātmanasyaiva kathitāḥ parameśvari || 6-254 ||
akṣasūtravidhiḥ khyātaḥ samyak tantrārthavedinām |
nyāyaṃ kṛtvā yathoddiṣṭaṃ tato yajanamārabhet || 6-255 ||
62b kh] sāṃprataṃ nyāsamākhyāmi śṛṇu tatvena śobhane |
25a] svābhāvikaṃ calaṃ dīptaṃ sthiraṃ drava * * * * ? || 6-256 ||
nyāsamātraṃ samākhyātaṃ ṣoḍhā dvādaśabhedataḥ |
ṣoḍhā śaktiḥ samākhyātā parā tvekākṣarā śubhā || 6-257 || 
kuṇḍalī nābhideśasthā parā sā vyomarūpiṇī |
ekātvena parā sūkṣmā akṣayā tejarūpiṇī || 6-258 ||
parā caisā tu boddhavyā ṣaḍvarṇarahitā kalā |
brahmasthānagatā suptā svābhāvikamudāhṛtam || 6-259 ||
viṣṇusthāne calā proktā dīptā rudrapadāśritā |
īśvare sthirasaṃjñā tu nādākhye dravasaṃbhavā || 6-260 ||
śaktisthā nabharūpā tu jñātavyā tatvavedibhiḥ | 
aṇimādiguṇādhārā ṣaḍguṇā guṇabodhanī || 6-261 ||
vyāpinī vyomarūpā tu anantānātha nāśritā |
saṃyoktri ca viyoktrī ca ṣaḍbhavaguṇasaṃsthitā || 6-262 ||
ekā eva parā śaktiḥ saṃsthitā kṛtyabhedataḥ |
kṛtyabhedena bhedaḥ syānna bhedaḥ paramārthataḥ || 6-263 ||
evaṃ nyāsaḥ smṛto devi antaraṅge pravartate |
bahiraṅge varṇarūpā ekā caiva tvanekadhā || 6-264 ||
ṣoḍhā dvādaśabhedena nyāsaḥ prokto gamāgame |

iti bhairavasrotasi mahātantre vidyāpīṭhe saptakoṭipramāṇe śrītantrasadbhāve samayākhyoddhāraḥ ṣaṣṭhaḥ paṭalaḥ || 6 ||



63a kh] devyuvāca

umāmāheśvaraṃ cakraṃ śrotumicchāmi tatvataḥ |
vidyāyāḥ sādhanaṃ deva ekavīrāvidhānataḥ || 7-1 || 
svakīyāṃgasamopetā mātṛbhiḥ parivāritā |
yathā yojyā mayā deva tathā caiva vadasva me || 7-2 ||

śrībhairava uvāca 

śṛṇu devi pravakṣyāmi sāraṃ yadviśvatomukham |
tadahaṃ saṃpravakṣyāmi śṛṇuṣvāyatalocane || 7-3 ||
aṣṭāraṃ cakramālikhya karṇikākeśarānvitam |
dvādaśāraṃ punaḥ paścāccaturviṃśadalaṃ tataḥ || 7-4 ||
karṇikāyāṃ nyased devaṃ śabdarāśiṃ tu bhairava |
nādiphāntasvarūpeṇa umā dehārddhadhāriṇī || 7-5 ||
arddhanārīśvaraṃ devaṃ tryakṣaṃ ca padmadhṛkkaram |
sarvālaṃkārasaṃpannaṃ jaṭāmukuṭamaṇḍitam || 7-6 ||
itthaṃrūpadharī devī praṇatārtivināśanī |
khānapānaratā dhyeyā hārakepūrabhūṣitā || 7-7 ||
35a g] evaṃvidhena rūpeṇa nyasenmārtaṇḍabhairavam |
patrāṣṭake nyased devi bhairavāṣṭakasaṃyutam || 7-8 ||
yakārādi samārabhya hakāraṃ yāvadeva hi |
mahātejena saṃyuktā nādādhārāntasaṃyutāḥ || 7-9 ||
śaktiyuktāstu kartavyā aukārasvarabhūṣitāḥ |
63b kh] nādaśaktisamopetā arddhendukalayānvitāḥ || 7-10 ||
bhairavāste mayākhyātā graharūpā vijānataḥ |
somādikramayogena ketvantā ye vyavasthitāḥ || 7-11 ||
dakṣaścaṇḍo haraḥ śauṇḍī pramatho bhīma manmathaḥ |
śakuniḥ sumatirnando gopālo'tha pitāmahaḥ || 7-12 ||
dvādaśaite mahāvīrā dvādaśāre niyojayet |
napuṃsakagaṇaṃ tyaktvā svarai hyete vyavasthitāḥ || 7-13 ||
meṣādikramayogena rāśayo yā vyavasthitāḥ |
udayaṃ teṣu kurvanti māsi māsi taducyate || 7-14 ||
caturviṃśatibhiḥ patraiḥ kādibhāntakrameṇa tu |
pūrvādikramayogena nyasedīśāna antagāḥ || 7-15 ||
piṅgalā caiva gokarṇī lolupā haraṇī tathā |
kekarā caiva deveśi bhaṭṭā yogeśvarī tathā || 7-16 ||
suprabhā vīramātā ca jambukī kroṣṭukī tathā |
25b] bībhatsā vānarī caiva yogyastridaśapūjitā || 7-17 ||
devavratā kumārī ca nandā nandapriyā tathā |
hayagrīvā surūpā ca virūpā ca kalāvatī || 7-18 ||
vāmanī devamātā ca viśveśī samudāhṛtā |
etad viṃśacatuṣkaṃ tu patrānte saṃvyavasthitāḥ || 7-19 ||
māsi māsi mahādevi dve dve caiva krameṇa tu |
64a kh] udayantāḥ prakurvanti matsamīpe vyavasthitāḥ || 7-20 ||
caitrādikramayogena nyastavyāstu prayatnataḥ |
yasya rudrasya yo māsastamādāṃvudayād dadet || 7-21 ||
taṃ tu pūjya prayatnena ādau kṛtvā vidhānataḥ |
pūrvapatrāt samārabhya śeṣā dikṣu yathāvidhiḥ || 7-22 ||
evaṃ samyag vidhānena cakrasamvatsaropamam |
yathā cābhyasate devi tathā tat pravadāmi te || 7-23 ||
karṇikāyāṃ sthitaḥ sūryastrīṇi patrāvalokayet |
jvalinī caiva dhūmā ca aṅgārā ca tṛtīyakā || 7-24 ||
yatra sthitaḥ pravarteta jvālinī sā udāhṛtā |
muktāgāriṇi vijñeyā purastād dhūminī smṛtā || 7-25 ||
yo grahaḥ pīḍayedyasya tanmadhye tu prapūjayet |
śreyā dikṣu sthitāḥ pūjyā gandhadhūpādyanukramāt || 7-26 ||
so graho varadastasya na tu pīḍākaro bhavet |
svamantraistarpaṇaṃ proktaṃ japahomabalikriyā || 7-27 ||
35b g] evaṃ śāntimavāpnoti bhairavājñāvidhāyinaḥ |
udayaṃ teṣu vakṣyāmi ahorātrakrameṇa tu || 7-28 ||
dinādhipaṃ purastasya tamādāvudayo hi saḥ |
catvāri ghaṭikāḥ khyātāḥ pādonāḥ tu prakīrtitāḥ || 7-29 ||
udayaṃ te samākhyātaṃ dinarātrau tapodhane |
64b kh] dakṣādi ye smṛtā rudrā meṣādiścaiva rāśayaḥ || 7-30 ||
teṣāṃ saṃcārayogaṃ ca udayaṃ tu yathākramam |
yasmin rāśau sthitaḥ sūryaḥ udayādipratiṣṭhitaḥ || 7-31 ||
ghaṭikāḥ pañca vijñeyā ekaike tu yathākramam |
udayāḥ ṣaṭ samākhyātāḥ yāvadastaṃ gato raviḥ || 7-32 ||
raviḥ sarvaryā yevamevaṃ tu nirdiṣṭaṃ śāstravādinām |
ahorātreṇa deveśi samvatsaramihocyate || 7-33 ||
dakṣanāmādi yo rudro meṣo rāśirbhunaktyasau |
tamādau pūjayed devi yogibhiḥ saha cācyutam || 7-34 ||
dve dve tāstu samākhyātāḥ piṅgalādyāḥ yathākramam |
yo rudrastena saṃyukto yoginyaścaiva pūjayet || 7-35 ||
dvādaśasvarabhedena bheditā mālinī priye |
ṣoḍaśaistu punardevaḥ śabdarāśistu bhairavaḥ || 7-36 ||
dvādaśaiḥ ṣoḍaśaiścaiva devo devī tathaiva ca |
dvādaśaiḥ sūryacakraṃ tu somacakraṃ tu ṣoḍaśaiḥ || 7-37 ||
sūryasomavibhāgaṃ tu jñātvā karma smārabhet |
ṛtavaḥ ṣaṭ samākhyātāḥ śiśirādiranukramāt || 7-38 ||
dvau dvau teṣu samākhyātau śṛṇuṣvāyatalocane |
śaradavasantagrīṣmāmbudayanteṣu yadbhavet || 7-39 ||
dvau dvau rudrau tu vijñeyau yoginyaścaturaścatuḥ |
kathitāstu samāsena udayānti k, g: udāyānti] yathā kramam || 7-40 ||
evaṃ ṣaḍvibhāgena ṛtucakraṃ prakīrtitam |
ṛtubhedena yaṣṭavyaṃ cakraṃ samvatsaropamam || 7-41 ||
śāntikādīni kāryāṇi śaśisthena tu kārayet |
vidveṣoccāṭanādīni homādyanalavāhake || 7-42 ||
dīptāṅgārāṃ ca dhūmrāṃ ca varjayet puṣṭikāmyayā |
śāntyādīni viśeṣeṇa jñātvā karma samārabhet || 7-43 ||
śāntogramiśrakarmāṇi śubhāśubhavibhājataḥ |
kartavyaṃ sādhanaṃ devi cittavṛtterapakṣayā || 7-44 ||
26a] śukladhyānādbhavecchāntiḥ puṣṭiśca bhavate priye |
raktaṃ caiva vaśīkarma ākarṣe caiva kṣobhaṇe || 7-45 ||
drāvaṇe caiva saṃmohe paracittopahāriṇī |
vidveṣoccāṭane mohe jambhane kīlane tathā || 7-46 ||
viprītikaraṇe caiva dhūmavarṇaṃ vicintayet |
māraṇe kṛṣṇavarṇābhaṃ daṃṣṭrotkaṭabhayāvaham || 7-47 ||
36a g] vṛddharūpaṃ mahograṃ tu rātrau caiva vicintayet |
pratyūṣe caiva bālaṃ tu śāntyarthe padmahastakam || 7-48 ||
madhyāhne tu vaśīkaraṇamākarṣe yovanodvaham |
pāśāṃkuśadharaṃ śāntaṃ staṃbhane pītavarṇakam || 7-49 ||
aparāhne miśrarūpaṃ madhyamaṃ parikīrtitam |
bālarūpaṃ tu yat proktamupadeśena kīrtitam || 7-50 ||
65b kh] utsaṃgaṃ saṃpradāye tu yaścārūḍhaṃ tu suvrate |
kulamārge samuddiṣṭe murddhanārī tu kaulike || 7-51 ||
na dehe cintayenmantrī devatā ugrarūpiṇaḥ |
padmahastāstu saṃcintya khānapānaratāḥ priye || 7-52 ||
kṛtyarūpā tu yā devī? asthimālairvibhūṣitā |
sarpairābharaṇairdīptā dānavānāṃ vadhārthikā || 7-53 ||
evaṃ dhyānaṃ viśeṣeṇa devatārūpakaṃ smṛtam |
na dhyāyedātmakaṃ piṇḍaṃ dahyamānaṃ kadācana || 7-54 ||
noplaveta varārohe na punaḥ plāvayet priye |
nityamevaṃ sadā śāntiḥ cintayenmanasātmani || 7-55 ||
umāmāheśvaraṃ cakraṃ kathitaṃ tu tapodhane |
calabījasamopetaṃ siddhyate nātra saṃśayaḥ || 7-56 ||
sāṃprataṃ kathayiṣyāmi yajanaṃ padavidyayā |
ekavīravidhānena kathayāmi tapodhane || 7-57 ||
aṣṭāre pujayet madhye rudrādikramayogataḥ |
vicce svahāvasānāṃ tu pūjanīyā manīṣibhiḥ || 7-58 ||
patrāṣṭake tato mantrya pūjanīyā subhaktitaḥ |
savāhanāstrakairyuktāḥ sarvālaṃkārabhūṣitāḥ || 7-59 ||
brahmāṇyādikrameṇaiva aiśānyantā prapūjayet |
oṃkārādi namo'ntaṃ tu namaścāntaṃ varānane || 7-60 ||
66a kh] viśvāntaṃ tu padaṃ cānyat svāhāntakṛtalakṣaṇaḥ |
pūjayet tadvarārohe yadicchecchreyamātmanaḥ || 7-61 ||
atha caivāṅgabhedena jātiṣaṭkamanukramāt |
hṛdayādikrameṇaiva nyastavyā parameśvarī || 7-62 ||
saṃjīvanīti hṛdayaṃ dvirabhyāsapaderitam |
oṃkārādisamāyuktaṃ namaścāntaṃ varānane || 7-63 ||
ūrddhvakeśīti yat proktaḥ śiraḥ svāhāntalakṣaṇaḥ |
jvalitaśikheti vikhyātā vauṣaḍantā śikhā smṛtā || 7-64 ||
māyā trailokyarūpeti sahasraparivartanī |
kavacaṃ tu samuddiṣṭaṃ huṃjātipadayojitam || 7-65 ||
tārakākṣi tathā netraṃ vaṣaṭjātisamanvitam |
māraṇīti dvirabhyāsāt phaḍantenāyudhaṃ smṛtam || 7-66 ||
aṅgāni kathitā hyevaṃ vaktrāṇi tvamataḥ śṛṇu |
oṃ ruḥ pūrvaktrāya svāhā ||
oṃ rukmiṇi dakṣiṇavaktrāya svāhā ||
36b g] oṃ ruhā paścimavaktrāya svāhā || 
oṃ ruñca muttaravaktrāya svāhā ||
devyā vaktrāḥ samākhyātāḥ yathā yojyā varānane || 7-67 ||
athavā varṇabhedena ekaikaṃ varṇakaṃ hi tat |
saṃvatsaravibhāgena aṅgavaktraiḥ samanvitā || 7-68 ||
yaṣṭavyā tu mahādevi samayākhyā parā priye |
66b kh] evaṃ samyag vidhānena vidyā bhedena yojayet || 7-69 ||
tasya vai cintitaṃ kāmamacirāt tu bhaviṣyati |
varṇasaṃkhyā samuddiṣṭā vidyāyāśca parā priye || 7-70 ||
śatatrayaṃ tu varṇānāṃ ṣaṣṭyādhikyaṃ varānane |
26b] lakṣaṇaṃ pūrvamākhyātaṃ calena sahitaṃ priye || 7-71 ||
japeta prayato nityaṃ sarvakāmānavāpnuyāt |

devyuvāca 

calaṃ deva tvayā proktaṃ cakre cakreśvareśvara || 7-72 ||
yenābhinandanaṃ deva calavīrasya kathyate |

bhairava uvāca

calanāma samākhyātaṃ sarvamantramayaṃ priye || 7-73 ||
jīvaṃ ca sarvamantrāṇāṃ yoginīhṛdayaṃ param |
tena siddhena siddhyanti aśeṣā mantranāyakāḥ || 7-74 ||
na ca labhyati gūḍhārthaṃ guptaṃ taṃ sarvatomukham |
upadeśena devīnāṃ prāpyate vīranāyike || 7-75 ||
calamamṛtarūpaṃ tu calāmānandakārakam |
calaṃ sarvagataṃ śāntaṃ nirvāṇaṃ paramaṃ padam || 7-76 ||
calamātmā calaṃ śaktiścalaṃ prāṇaḥ prakīrtitaḥ |
calasya tu purākalpaḥ kartavyo mantravādinā || 7-77 ||
lakṣaṃ ca navadhā japtvā raktāśī phalguśāsanaḥ |
taṃ dhyāyeta tato mantrī lākṣārasasamaprabham || 7-78 ||
67a kh] yaduddiśya japenmantraṃ tamāścākarṣayed drutam |
raktaṃ tasya raśīrasthaṃ prāṇaṃ vā māṃsameva vā || 7-79 ||
medaṃ vātha varārohe karṣayeta yadicchitaḥ |
amṛtaṃ vānyamevaṃ vā yadbhāvyaṃ manasepsitam || 7-80 ||
kroṣṭukasya tvayāmantrī raktena plāvya yatnataḥ |
rātrau pitṛvanasthastu taṃ prāvṛtya japedyadi || 7-81 ||
lakṣamekaṃ varārohe paśustu patate'grataḥ |
taṃ hutvā prāśayitvā tu kaulikīsiddhimāpnuyāt || 7-82 ||
paracittagatiṃ jñātvā rūpādiparivartanam |
antardhānaṃ ca deveśi sarvadeheṣu saṃkramam || 7-83 ||
anāgatavidhāyitvaṃ vartamānārthabodhanam |
atītārthasya bodhitvaṃ sarvaṃ jānāti tatvataḥ || 7-84 ||
evaṃ vai devadeveśi kartavyaṃ tu yathāvidhiḥ |
yena yena tu raktena bhāvitaṃ tu paṭaṃ priye || 7-85 ||
taṃ prāvṛtya japenmantrī śmaśānastho digambaraḥ |
tattadrūpī varārohe paśavaścopatiṣṭhati || 7-86 ||
pretapaṭasya deveśi sādhanaṃ tava kīrtitam | 
37a g] calena sahitā vidyā parāparamahābalāḥ || 7-87 ||
japeta prayato dhīraḥ sarvakāmānavāpnuyāt |
athavā samayākhyā tu vidyā sarvaguṇojjvalā || 7-88 ||
japeta manasā rātrau māsādantarhito bhavet |
nābhimātrajalāntastho nāśayet sarvakilviṣam || 7-89 ||
sādhayeta tathā tūrṇaṃ yatkiñcin manasepsitam |
mālinī śabdarāśistu calena sahitaṃ japet || 7-90 || 
sarvakāmānavāpnoti nātra kāryā vicāraṇā | 
śabdarāśirmahādevi mālinī ca tataḥ punaḥ || 7-91 ||
vidyātrayaniyogena hṛdayaṃ pañcakānvitam |
śatārddhaśatamekaṃ tu japeta prayataḥ sudhīḥ || 7-92 ||
sarvakāmānavāpnoti yoginyo'sāvupāsyate |
athavā śabdarāśistu mālinī ca trikānvitam || 7-93 ||
hṛdayapañcakaṃ devi samayākhyā kṛtaikataḥ | 
varṇānāṃ tu pramāṇaṃ syādekatra samudāhṛtam || 7-94 || 
śatāni pañca deveśi daśaiścaivottarāṇi tu | 
calabījasamopetaṃ mantragrāmaṃ susiddhidam || 7-95 ||
bhavate nātra sandeho vidhinārādhitaṃ yadi |
sarvakāmikamatyantaṃ balaṃ tu samudāhṛtam || 7-96 ||
nātaḥ parataraṃ devi sādhanaṃ sāmudāyikam | 
ekavīravidhānena viśeṣāt parameśvari || 7-97 || 
japataḥ prayato mantrī śuddhirāśuḥ prajāyate | 
devyā śuddhasya suśroṇi yacchanti paśumuttamam || 7-98 || 
27a] saptajanmodbhavaṃ caiva tajjuṣṭacaruko'thavā |
68a kh] tena jugdhena ? deveśi tattulyastu prajāyate || 7-99 ||
vicared devataiḥ sārddhaṃ kṣaṇād gaganago bhavet |

devyuvāca

yadyeva syānmahāghore tvayā prokto vilakṣaṇaḥ || 7-100 ||
kena karma vipākena paśutvaṃ jāyate vibho | 
kiṃ tasya lakṣaṇaṃ proktaṃ bāhyamadhyātmakaṃ ca yat || 7-101 || 

bhairava uvāca 

yat tvayā kathitaṃ pūrvaṃ tat sarvaṃ kathitaṃ mayā |
sāṃprataṃ paśumākhyāmi lakṣaṇaṃ siddhimicchataḥ || 7-102 ||
mantrasaṃskṛtadehastu śivabhakto dṛḍhavrataḥ |
sādhane siddhikāme ca yasya cittaṃ parāṅmukham || 7-103 ||
gurudrohī sa vijñeyo durātmā pāpakarmaṇaḥ | 
na tasya bhavate muktirniruddhaḥ saṃbhavannapi || 7-104 || 
janme janme punarbhakto dīkṣāmantrārthasevanāt | 
udyukto jāyate so hi punaścaiva parityajet || 7-105 || 
mantrasaṃskārajaṃ yaddhi phalaṃ syād balavān yadi |
nāpa kṛtya yajyakarmeṇa gurudrohādṛte sati || 7-106 ||
37b g] ekajanmā dvijanmā ca trijanmā caturo'thavā | 
pañcaṣaṭsaptajanmā ca paśavastu na saṃśayaḥ || 7-107 || 
tena jastena? siddhyeta khecaratvaṃ varānane | 
antarddhānaṃ bilottiṣṭhaṃ rūpādiparivartanam || 7-108 ||
cakrasāmānyamevaṃ ca tataḥ prabhṛti jāyate |
yasya sugandhinī vāṇī aṇimi yā pravartate || 7-109 ||
68b kh] netrayoḥ pratigrāhye ca tilakaṃ yasya dṛśyate |
hastapādanakhe devi romā yasyaiva dṛśyate || 7-110 || 
vindate cātmano janmaṃ śivabhaktaṃ tvamatsarī | 
devabrāhmaṇabhaktaśca japate śaucatatparaḥ || 7-111 || 
evaṃ lakṣaṇamākhyātaṃ vīrācāraratātmanām |
na jñāyate varārohe vinā tāsāmanugrahāt || 7-112 ||
saṃkīrṇalakṣaṇā devyaḥ paśuścaivaṃvidhā priye |
prayacchanti mahābhāgā japavrataratātmanām || 7-113 || 
samayācārayuktānāṃ mantrārādhanatatparām | 
dadanti bhāvitātmāno devyaḥ sadbhāvamuttamam || 7-114 || 
prāpnuvanti narā devi siddhayo vividhā bhuvi |
evaṃ prāptaṃ yadā devi pāśavaṃ lakṣaṇānvitam || 7-115 ||
hotavyamathavā bhaktyaṃ yadiṣṭaṃ vātha kārayet |
nāpaśuṃ bhakṣayen mantrī janmahīnaṃ na homayet || 7-116 ||
sākinīnāṃ priyaṃ bhadre yoginīnāṃ priyaṃ na hi | 
na bhakṣayed varārohe homayennaiva caiva hi || 7-117 || 
ādiṣṭaṃ divyayogībhiḥ kartavyamavicārataḥ |
anyathā na kvacinmantrī vadhejantu svalīlayā || 7-118 || 
svalīlā nihitannārau jihvārthe cātmapuṣṭaye |
yastu bhakṣayate māṃsaṃ pāpātmāsau narādhamaḥ || 7-119 ||
69a kh] bhakṣako hārako ghātī saṃskartā visuvikrayī | 
saravāyaścāntamantā ca aṣṭau vetretra pātakī || 7-120 ||
nayo anayarūpeṇa gṛhīto mandabuddhibhiḥ |
nayaḥ sarveṣu bhūteṣu kāruṇyaṃ saumyabhāvanā || 7-121 || 
evaṃ śāstrakramaṃ jñātvā vīrācāraṃ priyaṃ vratam |
pālanīyaṃ vipaścidbhiryathā śāstrapracoditam || 7-122 ||
dhyānaṃ pūjā japo homo bhaktipūrvāṇyanukramāt | 
sādhanaṃ sarvatantreṣu pañcadhā parikīrtitam || 7-123 ||
evaṃ pūjā tataḥ kṛtvā gandhadhūpapavitrakaiḥ |
naivedyaiścaiva pānaiśca vijñāpya parameśvaram || 7-124 ||
putrakaiḥ sādhakaiḥ sārddhaṃ carubhakṣed dinakṣaye |
nīcāsana pṛthaksaṃsthairgandhapuṣpasudhūpitaiḥ || 7-125 ||
27b] durāsthitaiḥ pṛthakpatrairvāgviruddhairna colvaṇaiḥ |
38a g] kartavyaṃ bhairave tantre śaucācāraratātmanaiḥ || 7-126 ||

devyuvāca

ekapātraṃ tu kiṃ deva pṛthakpātraṃ tathaiva ca |
sadṛśārthā viśuddhānāṃ vikalpaṃ kriyate katham || 7-127 ||

bhairava uvāca

dhyānapūjājape yukto anyasamayalopakaḥ |
tena kāryeṇa deveśi pṛthak pātraṃ tu kārayet || 7-128 ||
vajralepo yathā devi samayālopastathaiva ca |
brahmahatyādikaṃ pāpaṃ dīkṣā caiva viśodhayet || 7-129 ||
69b kh] samayalopo na śuddhyeta tena devi udāhṛtam |
mantravādanibhaṃ kṛtvā sarvāvasthāstu lolupāḥ || 7-130 ||
dūtīnāṃ saṃkare līnāḥ laulyarthe kriyavarjitāḥ |
rājasāstāmasāścaiva hāhābhūtamacetanāḥ || 7-131 ||
khānapānaratā nityaṃ jihvopastharatāśca ye |
na te vīrā varohe teṣāṃ saṃgaṃ na kārayet || 7-132 ||
anena kāraṇād devi pṛthakpātraṃ tu kārayet |

devyuvāca

rājasāstāmasā deva śrotumicchāmi tatvataḥ || 7-133 ||

bhairava uvāca

tāmaso vratinaḥ prokto jaṭājūṭo vibhūṣitaḥ |
bhasmoddhūlitagātrastu mantraviplavakārakāḥ || 7-134 ||
mudrālaṃkṛtadehastu maithunāśaktacetasaḥ |
dūtīparigrahāśaktastāmaso'sāvudāhṛtaḥ || 7-135 ||
rājaso ūrdhvaliṅgī tu kopanī valayānvitaḥ |
atha caivānyaveśena jaṭābhasmavivarjitaḥ || 7-136 ||
avyaktāḥ sātvikā jñeyā vratacaryāvivarjitāḥ |
te vīrāḥ śubhapanthānaḥ śāstrārthakriyayānvitaḥ || 7-137 ||
samayācāranirato devāgnigurupūjakaḥ |
vratastho'pyavratastho vā śa yatiḥ sātvikaḥ smṛtaḥ || 7-138 ||
sarvāvastho'pyavratasthastāmaso rājasaśca saḥ |
70a kh] ye punarvratadhārī syāḥ prakaṭārthopajīvakāḥ || 7-139 ||
te vīrā varjanīyāstu apā jñeyā durātmanaḥ |
na taistu saha saṃparkaḥ kartavyaḥ śāstratatparaiḥ || 7-140 ||
yastu mohāt pramādena maithunaṃ yadi sevate |
vratacaryārato devi vetālo jāyate mahā || 7-141 ||
ayonau ca viyonau ca yastu retaṃ vinikṣipet |
vetālaḥ so'pi jāyeta skandhe vahati mohajam || 7-142 ||
yo ruhet tāpasī pāpī rākṣasaḥ sa tu jāyate | 
bhairavavratadhāriṇyāṃ yaḥ kāmayati durmatiḥ || 7-143 ||
kūṣmāṇḍādhipatitvaṃ tu prāptabhogo yo bhujyati |
ekamaṇḍaladīkṣā tu ekatra carubhakṣaṇam || 7-144 ||
ye'tra santaḥ prabhuñjanti piśācāste tu dāruṇaḥ |
samayinī makṣayegho mātā vā bhaginīpi vā || 7-145 ||
sa rājā vaitaraṇyāyāṃ tatra bādhā vivarjitaḥ |
38b g] adatvā madyamāṃsāni yo bhakṣayati durmatiḥ || 7-146 || 
pūtivaktro mahākāyo rākṣasastu prajāyate |
anivedyastu bhuñcāno jīvārthe svātmapoṣakaḥ || 7-147 ||
so'pi sūcīmukhaḥ preto mahākāyo sudāruṇaḥ |
ye tu samyag varārohe vīrabhojanakarmaṇi || 7-148 ||
samadṛṣṭyā na yekṣanti tatvena tamasāvṛtaḥ |
70b kh] gacchanti suciraṃ kālaṃ varṇāvarṇau vikalpakāḥ || 7-149 ||
gurūṇāṃ ca yadā jñātaṃ na karoti narādhamaḥ | 
sa gacchennarakaṃ ghoraṃ dustaraṃ parameśvari || 7-150 ||
āsanaṃ śayanaṃ pānaṃ bhojanaṃ chādanaṃ tathā || 7-151 || ?
pāduko pā na hau * * * * * * ? | ? 
28a] * * * *? na vidyeta majjyamāne bhavārṇave || 7-152 || 
yāvat tiṣṭhet samīpasthaḥ svaguroragrato budhaḥ | 
tāvadviḍālavad devi grāsārthī bakavadyathā || 7-153 || 
yastu sarvāṇi kāryāṇi svagurośca upekṣakaḥ |
tamahaṃ nānugṛhṇāmi pāpācāraṃ narādhamam || 7-154 ||
tasmāt sarvaprayatnena preṣaṇaṃ guravasya tu |
kartavyaṃ satataṃ devi ātmanā tu dhanena vā || 7-155 ||
yāvajjīved varārohe dāsavat tiṣṭhayennaraḥ |
gurumukhena gṛhṇīyād guruduḥkhena duḥkhitaḥ || 7-156 ||
tāni bhogāni deveśi ye guru bhuñjate'tra tu |
te deśā yatra tiṣṭheta te lokā ye prapaśyati || 7-157 ||
yo'pyanena tu deveśi vartate sādhakottamaḥ |
sa patiḥ sarvasiddhīnāṃ bhairavācārasaṃyutaḥ || 7-158 ||
asadguṇātha santā ye khyāpyā vai guravasya tu |
guṇaistu khyāpitairdevi aśvamedhaphalaṃ labhet || 7-159 ||
71a kh] sadā rogyaṃ śriyā śāntirdīrghāyurvairivarjitaḥ |
acintitaphalāvāptirguroḥ kīrtyati yo guṇān || 7-160 ||
mantra tantra vitarṇāṃ tu na kuryād guruṇā saha |
na vadedīdṛśaṃ vākyaṃ bhrānti yadyupajāyate || 7-161 ||
yena kruddhena kupyanti devatāḥ saptadhā sthitāḥ |
tuṣyanti tena tuṣṭena tasmāt kopaṃ na kārayet || 7-162 ||
upahāsyamavākyaśca iti hāsyamanṛtyatā |
asadvādaṃ na kurvīta guruṇā saha bhairavi || 7-163 ||
riktapāṇiṃ na paśyeta deṃvāgniguravaṃ kvacit |
evaṃ pālayate yastu bhairavyasau na saṃśayaḥ || 7-164 ||
sāmānyaṃ sarvaśāstreṣu ācārapratipālanam |
kartavyaṃ tu varārohe ātmanaḥ śreyamicchataḥ || 7-165 ||
anenācārahīnā ye rājasāstāmasāstu te |
teṣāṃ pāpāvasāne tu mokṣaścaiva kramād bhavet || 7-166 || 
39a g] mātṛputratvamāpannāḥ kulakāpālikāśca ye |
te'pi muktiṃ gamiṣyanti kramād devi na saṃśayaḥ || 7-167 ||
ye punarbhairavenuktā dīkṣājñānavidhau sthitāḥ |
sarve te patirākhyātāḥ patijñānena saṃskṛtāḥ || 7-7-168 ||
te sarve pūjanīyāstu yathā devi ahaṃ tava |
aprasveyamidaṃ devi te hāna * parīkṣite || 7-169 || 
71b kh] jñānagarbho vasannaiva kubuddhe cakraśīline |
calacitte pramāde ca dattaṃ naiva tu siddhidam || 7-170 ||
kathitaṃ sarahasyaṃ tu calabījaniṣevanam |
surabhedena deveśi calacakraṃ prakīrtitam || 7-171 ||
mudrāmantragaṇopetaṃ nyāsacakravibhāgavit |
siddhyate acirād devi mudrāmaṇḍalabhāgavit || 7-172 ||
mudrā khageśvarī nāma sarvasaṃdohalakṣaṇā |
saptāhāt samajñāsya jāyate devi niścayaḥ || 7-173 || 
anāmā pṛṣṭasaṃlagnā sve yantraṃ caiva pīḍayet |
pīḍanaṃ tu varārohe khagatiśca na saṃśayaḥ || 7-174 ||
khaṃ khe ṣaṭkaṃ khasaṃsthaṃ tu kartavyaṃ tu khamadhyagam |
khamukhaṃ khagateryojya khaṃ bhāvaṃ bhāvayet priye || 7-175 ||
khagatistu bhaved devaṃ smaraṇācca na saṃśayaḥ |
evaṃ devi samākhyātaṃ rahasyaṃ mantravādinām || 7-176 || 
aprakāśyaṃ sugopyaṃ ca rakṣayet prāṇavad yathā | 

iti bhairavasrotasi mahātantre vidyāpīṭhe saptakoṭipramāṇe śrītantrasadbhāve umāmāheśvaracakrādhikāro nāma saptamaḥ paṭalaḥ || 7 ||



devyuvāca

mudrā tu sūcitā nātha na me jñātā mahāprabho |
mudrāṃ pīṭhānusāreṇa kathayasva jagannidhe || 8-1 ||
72a kh] triśikhī padmamudrā ca yonimudrā tṛtīyakā |
lakṣaṇaṃ tāsu vyākhyāhi yā noktā alpamedhayā || 8-2 || 

28b] bhairava uvāca 

vidyākhyā yā parā śakti yasyodbhūtaṃ? mahādbhutam |
mudrāṇāṃ lakṣaṇaṃ devi kathayāmi samāsataḥ || 8-3 ||
hastābhyāṃ kārayedādau saṃpuṭaṃ ūrdhvadiṅmukham |
aṅgulyo grathayet sarvāṃ saṃkliṣṭā ubhayeṣvapi || 8-4 ||
tarjanyānāmike kuṃcya saṃśdhiṣṭo madhyata ubhau |
tābhyāṃ mūle mukhaṃ kāryaṃ kaniṣṭhāṃ madhye yojayet || 8-5 || 
puṭākārau karau kṛtvā aṅguṣṭhau madhyasaṃsthitau | 
nisṛtāṃ vāmahastasya aṅgulyāṃ tu karīyasī || 8-6 ||
yonimudrā smṛtā bhadre sarvāsāṃ kṣobhakārikā |
mudrātrikamidaṃ bhadre sādhāraṇamudāhṛtam || 8-7 ||
vacyā sādhāraṇā mudrā yogīvīreśatuṣṭaye |
smṛtāṃgulyaḥ prajāyeta dakṣiṇasya karasya ca || 8-8 ||
aṅguṣṭhaṃ kārayed devi tarjanyā mūlamāśritā |
39b g] skandāgrataḥ pradadyāṃtu abhayeyaṃ pinākine || 8-9 ||
athavā trastabhītānāṃ sukhasvīkaraṇāya ca |
prasatīva karaṃ vāmaṃ adhovaktraṃ pracālayet || 8-10 ||
aṅgulāgraṃ varaṃ devi? vāñchitārthaphalapradā |
kaniṣṭhānāmike dve ca hastayorubhayorapi || 8-11 ||
vaktraṃ visphārayenmantrī madhyame nāsikāpuṭe |
72b kh] tarjanye bhrūyugaṃ nāḍya cālayeṅgunṣṭhakau bahiḥ || 8-12 ||
hāhākāratrayaṃ kuryāt krūradṛṣṭavyā sthitaḥ sudhīḥ |
vikarālavido mudrā bhairave tuṣṭidāyikā || 8-13 ||
anayā baddhayā devi yoginīvallabho bhavet |
vighneśairnābhibhūyeta grahairyasto vimokṣayet || 8-14 ||
mahāghorapraveśeṣu nirbhayatvaṃ dadāti hi |
pūrvāgraṃ vāmahastaṃ ca tiryak kṛtvātha dakṣiṇam || 8-15 ||
svastikeyaṃ mahāmudrā jayā sānaidhyakārikā |
kaniṣṭāṅguṣṭha saṃgūhya tanmadhyonyāṃ punaḥ kuru || 8-16 ||
śeṣāṃgulyastu saṃkucya śṛṃkhalā vijayā matā |
vāmahastasya tarjanyā madhye vaktrā tu kārayet || 8-18 ||
śeṣāstu *? kuṃcayeṃgulyo vaktrāṃguṣṭhaṃ tu kārayet |
tanmadhye tarjanī cānyā rijubhūtā niveśayet || 8-19 || 
kālakarṇā iyaṃ mudrā lāṅgalākhyā tu karṣaṇe |
tarjanyāṃguṣṭhakau caiva vartulīkṛtya hastayoḥ || 8-20 ||
adhordhvasaṃsthitau dvau tu pāśametanmahābalā | 
hastābhyāṃ saṃpuṭaṃ kāryaṃ kanyase tiryage priye || 8-21 ||
darśayeta prayatnena kamaṇḍaluriti smṛtaḥ |
brahmaṇyāyā pradātavyaṃ sarvaśuddhikaraṃ param || 8-22 ||
muṣṭiṃ badhvā tu vāmena tarjanyā cocchṛtāgrataḥ |
73a kh] vāmaskandhe niveśitvā khaḍgāgaṃ tu pradarśayet || 8-23 ||
maheśvaryā mahādevi sarvasānaidhyakārakā |
veṇibandhena vanditvā karau dvau tu varānane || 8-24 ||
dakṣahastena muṣṭiṃ tu bandhayitvā tu bhrāmayet |
ākāśe ca kumāryāyāḥ sainyabhakṣe ca pitthakam ? || 8-25 ||
tarjanīṃ vāmahastasya kṛtvā vai vartulākṛtim |
madhyamāṅguṣṭhakaṃ lagnamagraparvād varānane || 8-26 ||
saṃkhyākhyā tu iyaṃ mudrā vaiṣṇavyāyādbhutārthadā |
tarjanyāmucchritaṃ kṛtvā śeṣā muṣṭyā tu cākramet || 8-27 ||
daṇḍamudrā samākhyātā vārāhyā tuṣṭikārikā |
nābhimātrau karau tiryag maṇibandhena granthayet || 8-28 ||
kanyasāṃguṣṭhakāvagre śeṣāśritān prakārayet |
vajramudrā pradātavyā vāsavyā tuṣṭikārikā || 8-29 ||
stambhane tu pradātavyā śatrūṇāṃ dhvaṃsane tathā |
29a] kaniṣṭhāmākramed devi aṅguṣṭhena prayatnataḥ || 8-30 ||
trayaṃ prasārayed devi triśūlatridaśārcitam | 
aiśānyāyāṃ pradātavyaṃ mālinyāyāṃ varānane || 8-31 ||
40a g] ūrddhvavaktrau karau kṛtvā suśliṣṭe madhyame tathā |
tarjanye'nāmike dve ca kaniṣṭhā ca prayatnataḥ || 8-32 ||
karapṛṣṭhe niviṣṭhaitāḥ śaktimudrā smṛtā tava |
āgneyāyāṃ pradātavyā sannidhānārthaṃ varānane || 8-33 ||
73b kh] savyena bandhayetmuṣṭimaṅguṣṭhamucchritopari | 
liṅgākhyā tu iyaṃ mudrā sthāne devasya dāpayet || 8-34 || 
hastayoḥ kārayettālpā nayedūrddhva śiro'vadhi |
śanairadhomukhottārya mocayet pādayorapi || 8-35 ||
arghākhyā tu iyaṃ mudrā sarvatuṣṭikarā parā |
tarjanyādistadākuṃcya śeṣāṅguṣṭhena saṃcaret || 8-36 ||
aṃkuśākhyā iyaṃ mudrā sarvotkṛṣṭā udāhṛtā | 
adhomukhaṃ tu aṅguṣṭhaṃ kṛtvā bandhena muṣṭinā || 8-37 || 
nirodhākhyā tviyaṃ mudrā devadevena kīrtitā |
culukeva karaṃ kṛtvā tarjanyaṅguṣṭharodhitā || 8-38 ||
mocayekaikatogre tu saṃhatā madhyaparvaṇi |
amṛtākhyā iyaṃ mudrā ajinamaṅgulitrayam || 8-39 ||
saṃśliṣṭaṃ tiryagaṃ kṛtvā skandhe caiva niveśayet |
aṅguṣṭhenākrameṅgulya suṣirākāramadhyataḥ || 8-40 ||
