cālanaṃ mūlamantreṇa ghaḍharantena kārayet || 9-57 ||
svinnocoṣṇābhidhānaṃ tu kartavyaṃ mūlavidyayā |
śīte śītābhidhāraṃ tu kartavyamavatāraṇam || 9-58 ||
prokṣaṇaṃ kṣālanaṃ caiva mūlamantreṇa kārayet |
saṃpāta hṛdgataṃ kṛtvā caturbhāgaṃ tu kārayet || 9-59 ||
prathamaṃ sthaṇḍile deyaṃ dvitīyaṃ kalaśasya tu |
tṛtīyaṃ balivahnibhyāṃ caturthamātmanasya tu || 9-60 ||
naivedyaṃ tu tribhiḥ sthānairbaliṃ dadyāt tu vāhyataḥ |
bālānāṃ mātarāṇāṃ tu kṣetrabhūtaprapālakān || 9-61 ||
evaṃ saṃtarpayitvā tu paścādācamya yatnataḥ |
praṇipatya tato devaṃ visarjyā susamāhitaḥ || 9-62 ||
sādhakaiḥ putrakaiḥ sārddhaṃ upasannaistathā priye |
pañcagavyaṃ tataḥ pītvā yathānukramayogataḥ || 9-63 ||
caruṃ prāśya vidhānena ācamya susamāhitaḥ |
dantakāṣṭhaṃ tato datvā sakṣīraṃ graṃthivarjitam || 9-64 ||
tālamekāyataṃ kāryaṃ ṛjusnigdhaṃ samaṃ śubham |
kanīyasī parīṇāhamaudumvaryaṃ varānane || 9-65 ||
80b kh] athavānye'pi ye proktāḥ kṣīrādyāḥ puṣpasaṃyutāḥ |
patanaṃ tasya lakṣeta śobhanaṃ vāpyaśobhanam || 9-66 ||
sanmukhaṃ patanaṃ śreṣṭhaṃ pūrvaṃ uttaradikṣu ca |
vilomā itarā proktā lakṣayate prayatnavān || 9-67 ||
śubhaiḥ siddhimavāpnoti aśubhaistu viparyayaḥ |
svapeta rajanīṃ mantrī sakhāyaiḥ sahitaṃ priye || 9-68 ||
pratyūṣe vimale gatvā sandhyopāsanapūrvakam |
devān pitṝnnamaskṛtya āgacchedgurusannidhau || 9-69 ||
svapnaṃ nivedayed devi śubhaṃ vā yadi vāśubham |
śubhaiḥ siddhiḥ samādeśyā aśubhairhomamācaret || 9-70 ||
evaṃ kṛtvā bhavecchāntirvighnopasamanaṃ bhavet |
kāryādau lakṣayet svapne kāryānte ca śubhāśubham || 9-71 ||
śubhān svapnān pravakṣyāmi aśubhāni varānane |
svapne ca madirāpānaṃ matsyamāṃsaprabhakṣaṇam || 9-72 ||
kṛmiviṣṭānulepaṃ ca rudhireṇābhiṣecanam |
bhakṣaṇaṃ dadhibhaktasya śvetavastrānulepanam || 9-73 ||
44a g] śvetātapatramūrdhnisthaṃ śvetasragdāmabhūṣaṇam |
siṃhāsanaṃ rathaṃ yānaṃ dhvajaṃ rājābhiṣecanam || 9-74 ||
ratnāṃgābharaṇaṃ dīptaṃ tāmbūlaphalameva ca |
darśanaṃ śrīsarasvatyāḥ śubhanāryāvagūhanam || 9-75 ||
narendraṛṣidevaiśca siddhavidyādharairgaṇaiḥ |
81a kh] ācāryaiḥ saha saṃvādaṃ kṛtvā svapne prasiddhyati || 9-76 ||
nadīsamudrataraṇaṃ ākāśagamanaṃ tathā | 
mṛtaṃ ca rodanaṃ caiva prajvalantaṃ hutāśanam || 9-77 ||
grahanakṣatratārāṇāṃ candrasūryasya darśanam |
harmyamārohaṇaṃ caiva prāsādaśikhare'pi vā || 9-78 ||
gajāśvavṛṣayāneṣu taruśilāgrarohaṇam |
vimānagamanaṃ caiva siddhamanuṣyadarśanam || 9-79 ||
32a] lābhasiddhacareścaiva devīnāṃ caiva darśanam |
guḍikā daṇḍakāṣṭhaṃ ca khaḍgapādukarocanā || 9-80 ||
upavīto'ñjanaṃ caiva amṛtaṃ pāradoṣadhīḥ |
śaktiḥ kamaṇḍaluḥ padmamakṣamālā manaḥśilā || 9-81 ||
prajvālaṃ siddhadravyāṇi gairikāntani yāni ca |
dṛṣṭyā siddhyati svapnānte bhūlābhaṃ bheṣajaṃ ca yat || 9-82 ||
kṣatajārṇavasaṃgrāme taraṇaṃ vijayaṃ raṇe |
jvalanpitṛvane ramye vīravīreśibhirvṛte || 9-83 ||
vīravetālasiddhāśca mahāmāṃsasya vikrayam |
mahāpaśorvibhāgaṃ ca labdhvā devibhirādarāt || 9-84 ||
ātmanaḥ pūjayaṃ deva japaṃ dhyāyaṃ stavaṃ api |
juhvānte vānale dīpte pūjitaṃ vā prapaśyati || 9-85 ||
haṃsasārasacakrāhvamayūraśavarohaṇam |
mātṛbhirbhairavairaṣṭairmantrarudragaṇaiḥ saha || 9-86 ||
bhairavīṃ bhairavaṃ dṛṣṭvā siddhyate nātra saṃśayaḥ |
81b kh] śubhāḥ svapnāḥ samākhyātā aśubhāśca nibodha me || 9-87 ||
tailābhyaṅgaṃ tathā pānaṃ viśanaṃ ca rasātale |
andhakūpe ca patanaṃ atha paṅke nimajjanam || 9-88 ||
ṛkṣavānarayāneṣu patanaṃ vṛkṣaparvatāt |
kṛntanaṃ nāsikā karṇamathavā hastapādayoḥ || 9-89 ||
patanaṃ dantakeśānāṃ ṛkṣavānaradarśanam |
darśanaṃ krūrasattvānāṃ vetālāsitapuruṣayoḥ || 9-90 ||
kṛṣṇāṃ kucailamalināṃ kṛṣṇamālyāmbarāvṛtām |
raktākṣī ca striyāṃ svapne puruṣo vāvagūhane || 9-91 ||
mriyate nātra sandeho yadi śāntiṃ ca kārayet |
gṛhaprāsādabhedaṃ ca śayyāyāsanachatrayoḥ || 9-92 ||
ātmano vibhavaṃ paśyedātmadravyāpahāraṇam |
kharoṣṭrāśvaśṛgālānāṃ kaṃkagṛddhavakeṣu ca || 9-93 || 9-
44b g] vāyasolūkamahiṣārohaṇaṃ ca punarbhavam |
bhakṣaṇaṃ pakṣimāṃsasya raktamālyānulepanam || 9-94 ||
raktakṛṣṇāni vastrāṇi kṛtātmānaṃ ca paśyati |
hasanaṃ valganaṃ svapne mlānasragdāmadhāraṇam || 9-95 ||
svamāṃsotkṛtyanodvandhaṃ kṛṣṇasarpeṇa bhakṣaṇam |
udvāhaṃ ca tathā svapne dṛṣṭyā hyevaṃ na siddhyati || 9-96 ||
aśubhā hyevaṃ samākhyātā vijñeyā deśikena tu |
śubhāni tvanumodyāni aśubhe śataṃ homayet || 9-97 ||
82a kh] aṣṭotkṛṣṭamaghoreṇa prāyaścittādvimucyate |
śuṣkagovarikā datvā trivārāṃ saṃyatendriyaḥ || 9-98 ||
unmattaṃ patraṃ valyādyai gharṣayet tāṃ prayatnataḥ |
karaṇī trivṛtāṃ gṛhya karpāsikasamudbhavām || 9-99 ||
khaṭikāṃ nāgarāṃ śuklāṃ ānayet yatnataḥ śuciḥ |
brahmasthānagataṃ sūtraṃ sphālayet pūrvato diśi || 9-100 ||
citrāṃ vābhyantare prācīmaparāṃ ca iheṣyate |
uttarā mīnayogena yāmye caivāpyayaṃ vidhiḥ || 9-101 ||
caturbhāgaṃ tataḥ kṛtvā pūrvasūtrasamāśritam |
caturthaṃ yad bhaved bhāgaṃ tena matsyadvayaṃ priye || 9-102 ||
sādhayed vigate sūtre pūrvapaścimayāsu ca |
dakṣiṇottaramevaṃ syāt paścātkoṇāṃstu sādhayet || 9-103 ||
īśānyāgneyanairṛtyāṃ vāyavyāṃstu yathākramam |
evaṃ tu sādhite kṣetre sādhayeccaturasrakam || 9-104 ||
caturasrīkṛte kṣetre paścāt padmaṃ prakalpayet |
caturviṃśāṅgulāyāmamālikhet padmamuttamam || 9-105 ||
aṣṭapatraṃ samaṃ kāryaṃ karṇikākeśarolvaṇam |
prathame karṇikābhāgaṃ dvitīyaṃ keśarāṇi ca || 9-106 ||
tṛtīye dalasandhīni dalāgrāṇi caturthake |
prativāraṇarekhāṃ tu dadyāt padmasya bāhyataḥ || 9-107 ||
32b] gātrakā bāhyataḥ kāryāścaturaṅgulamānataḥ |
tasyāpi bāhyato vīthī hastamātrapramāṇataḥ || 9-108 ||
82b kh] padmamānāḥ smṛtā dvārāstadarddhenāthavā priye |
evaṃ vai sūtrayitvā tu rajāṃśi vinipātayet || 9-109 ||
raktam pītaṃ tathā śuklaṃ rajastrayamudāhṛtam |
samā rekhāḥ prayātavyā avicchinnāḥ suśobhanāḥ || 9-110 ||
rekhānāmantaraṃ pūrvaṃ yavamātraṃ tu kārayet |
karṇikā pītikā kāryā puṣkarā raktavarṇakāḥ || 9-111 ||
keśarā raktapītāḍhyā śuklān caiva tu kārayet |
dalāḥ śuklāḥ samākhyātāḥ sarvataḥ paritā ume || 9-112 ||
dalāntarāṇi sarvāṇi raktapītāni kārayet |
bhrāmaṇī pītikā kāryā gātrakā raktapītakāḥ || 9-113 || 
45a g] yathā śobhā tu kartavyā maṇḍale sarvatobhadrake |
patravallī tathā saṃkhyā latā svastikameva ca || 9-114 ||
upaśobhā tu sarvatra kārayed varṇakaiḥ śubhaiḥ |
aparāhṇe tato mantrī snātamuddhūlyameva vā || 9-115 ||
sitavastradharo yogī sitamālyānulepitaḥ | 
praviśya yāgabhramiṃ tu puṣpahastaḥ suyantritaḥ || 9-116 || 
pūjayeta tato devaṃ vyomavyāpi parāparam |
sarvavarṇadharaṃ śāntaṃ nava ca gopalakṣitam || 9-117 ||
padmahastaṃ samakuṭaṃ candrārddhakṛtaśekharam |
evaṃ saṃpūjya yatnena arghapādyādikaiḥ kramāt || 9-118 ||
bhogasthāne ] tu vargākhyā pūjayed yatnataḥ priye |
83a kh] gandhapuṣpaiḥ sanaivedyairbhakṣairnānāvidhaiḥ śubhaiḥ || 9-119 || 
pānaiśca vividhākārairmāṃsaiḥ pakṣiṇayāsavaiḥ |
evaṃ saṃpūjayitvā tu sthaṇḍile kalaśe tathā || 9-120 ||
kuṇḍe cātmaśarīre ca pūjayet parameśvaram |
praṇipatya tato bhaktyā śiṣyamāhūya yatnataḥ || 9-121 ||
adhvānaṃ kalpayet tasya śivādyavanimāvadhim |
pūjayed gandhapuṣpaistu netrabandhaṃ tu kārayet || 9-122 || 
dukūlapaṭṭavastreṇa kārpāsena navena vā | 
sadṛśena tu śuklena netravandhaṃ tu kārayet || 9-123 || 
pāṇau puṣpaṃ tato datvā praveśed yāgamaṇḍale |
devasya dakṣiṇe bhāge puṣpaṃ mocāpayet priye || 9-124 ||
yasya mantrasya puṣpāṇi dṛśyante patitāni tu |
tasya tatpūrvakaṃ nāma śaktyāntaṃ parikalpayet || 9-125 ||
sa mantrastasya dātavyo dattaścaiva tu siddhyati | 
netrodghāṭaṃ tataḥ kṛtvā praṇipatya subhaktitaḥ || 9-126 || 
kalaśe tu tato hyevaṃ namaskāraṃ tataḥ priye |
nītvā cāgnisamīpe tu dakṣiṇāsyāṃ vyavasthitaḥ || 9-127 ||
darbhahastaṃ tataḥ kṛtvā vicchitvāgre suśobhanam |
ālabhenmantrakośena pūjayeta tato guruḥ || 9-128 ||
adhvānaṃ manasā dhyātvā ācāryastatvapāragaḥ | 
saṃhārakramayogena paśūnāṃ tu yathecchayā || 9-129 || 
83b kh] yuktyārthe sṛṣṭimārgeṇa saṃhāro muktimicchatām |
evaṃ cittagatiṃ jñātvā upasthānaṃ tu kārayet || 9-130 || 
ādikṣāntastu sṛṣṭiḥ syāt saṃhāraṃ kṣādikalpane |
kṣakāraṃ pūrvato nyasya akāraṃ yāvadeva hi || 9-131 ||
sandhānaṃ kuṇḍamadhye tu kārayet tatvavit sadā | 
45b g] nāḍīsandhānapūrvaṃ tu tatvasandhānamantarāt || 9-132 ||
paścāddhomaṃ prakartavyaṃ śāstradṛṣṭena karmaṇā |
ekaikaṃ varṇamuccārya namaḥ praṇavavarjitam || 9-133 ||
ṣaṭkajātivinirmuktaṃ śiraḥ śikhāvivarjitam |
bhedayet padmagarbheṇa kuṇḍalyākhyāṃ tu dīpayet || 9-134 ||
naivāsya dīpako yojyā kuṇḍalyākhye tu dīpite |
nābhisthaṃ grahaṇaṃ kāryaṃ visargeṇa pareṇa tu || 9-135 ||
33a] ūrṇātantunibhākārā caitanyaṃ tasya cāgrataḥ |
gṛhītvā śaktinā gāḍhamarkatulāmivāmvare || 9-136 ||
brahmasthaṃ viṣṇusaṃsthaṃ hi rudrasthamīśvare sthitam |
nādasthaṃ śaktisaṃsthaṃ tu svaiḥ svairbhāvaiḥ prapādayet || 9-137 ||
sthāne janme tadaiśvaryaṃ tadbhogāpādanaṃ tathā |
saṃyogaśca viyogaśca tatve tatve tu kārayet || 9-138 ||
nīyate dvādaśānte tu varṇaṃ bhitvā yathākramam |
tatvaṃ vā padasaṃjñaṃ vā kalāmantrātmako'pi vā || 9-139 ||
bhuvanaṃ vā varārohe varṇādhvamathavā priye |
kartavyo bodhabhā ca stu tyāgasandhānayojanā || 9-140 ||
84a kh] śuddhādhvānamaśeṣasya yojanā tu pare śive |
nirlakṣe nirguṇe śānte śuddhe cātyantanirmale || 9-141 ||
sarvage guṇadhātāre vyāpake manavarjite |
acetane sucetajñe śabdasparśe vivarjite || 9-142 ||
abodhe bodharūpe ca aguṇe guṇasaṃbhave |
arūpe sarvarūpe ca atṛpte tṛptilakṣaṇe || 9-143 ||
anāśraye mahāyoge sarvāśraye guṇālaye |
apade padabhāvasthe arūpe sarvarūpake || 9-144 ||
ānande ca nirānande sakale niṣkale kale |
abhāve bhāvamāpanne yogaṃ kṛtvā nirāśraye || 9-145 ||
bhrūkṣepeṇa varārohe viṣuvasthasya sādhakaḥ |
tasmin yukto mahādevi na bhūyo janmamāpnuyāt || 9-146 ||
tāvad bhramati saṃsāre yāvadaikyaṃ na vindati |
ekībhāvagate citte na bandho na ca bandhakaḥ || 9-147 ||
yathā tāmrarasaspṛṣṭaṃ suvarṇatvamupāgatam |
evaṃ yuktaḥ pare tatve na bhūyaḥ paśutāṃ vrajet || 9-148 ||
adhvānaiva mahābandhaḥ ṣoḍaśaṃ parikīrtitam |
tasyaiva kāraṇaṃ śaktirmātṛkākhyā parāparā || 9-149 ||
parā tejātmikā proktā svarṇaprotā parā matā |
sā caiva varṇa-adhvānaṃ jantūnāṃ bandhakāraṇam || 9-150 ||
varṇādhvānaṃ samāsena kathayāmi tava priye |
84b kh] kṣakāraṃ tu mahātejaḥ kālāgnidīptatejasaḥ || 9-151 || 
46a g] sarvasaṃhārakartāraṃ nyaset pādatalaṃ hyadhaḥ |
hakāramuparistasya pādādyaṃguṣṭhataḥ sthitam || 9-152 ||
śakāraṃ gulphayordadyāt ṣakāraṃ jānunī nyaset |
sakāraṃ talake dve tu vakāraṃ ghoṣavāhake || 9-153 ||
lakāramasthisaṃghāte rakāraṃ raktamucyate |
yakāraṃ tvacamityāhurmakāraṃ puruṣaṃ viduḥ || 9-154 ||
bhakāraṃ jānusandhīṣu bakāraṃ kīlake tathā |
phakāraṃ jānunī jñeyaṃ upariṣṭāt samāśritam || 9-155 ||
pakāramūruke yojyaṃ nakāraṃ kaṭisandhiṣu |
dhakāraṃ vṛṣaṇotyāhurdakārāmitare smṛtaḥ || 9-156 ||
thakāraṃ liṅgamadhyasthaṃ takāraṃ śukrameva ca |
ṇakāraṃ guhyadeśe tu ḍhakāraṃ nābhimaṇḍale || 9-157 ||
ḍakāraṃ nābhimadhyasthaṃ ṭhakāramudare tathā |
ṭakāraṃ hṛdaye yojyaṃ ñakāraṃ tanumadhyataḥ || 9-158 ||
jhakāraṃ tu dvitīye tu jakāraṃ śikharaṃ smṛtam |
vāmaṃ tu dakṣiṇaṃ cānyacchakāraṃ varavarṇini || 9-159 ||
cakāraṃ maṇibandhe tu ṅakāraṃ tu dvitīyake |
ghakāraṃ hastasandhīṣu gakāraṃ tu ataḥ param || 9-160 ||
khakāraṃ hastamadhye tu kakāraṃ vāmataḥ priye |
akāraṃ galake yojyaṃ aṃ dadyāt tālumadhyataḥ || 9-161 ||
85a kh] au tu vinyasya jihvāgre o dadyād dantapaṅktiṣu |
aikāraṃ vāmato dadyāde tu dakṣiṇanāsike || 9-162 ||
lṝkāramakṣivāme tu lṛkāraṃ dakṣiṇe tathā |
ṝkāraṃ śravaṇe vāme ṛ ca dakṣiṇage tathā || 9-163 ||
ūkāraṃ bhrūvayordadyādukāraṃ bhrūvamadhyataḥ |
īkāraṃ tu lalāṭe tu ikāraṃ bindurūpiṇam || 9-164 ||
ākāraṃ mastake dadyādakāraṃ tatvagocare |
evaṃ vinyasya cādhvānaṃ granthibhedaṃ tu kārayet || 9-165 ||
33b] anena kramayogena śodhyādhvānaṃ varānane |
tatve tatve layaṃ bhogaṃ viyogaṃ yojanaṃ tathā || 9-166 ||
saṃśodhya kramaśaḥ sarvaṃ yojayecchāścate pade |

devyuvāca

varṇadīkṣā śrutā deva sārāt sāratarā parā || 9-167 ||
sāṃprataṃ tatvadīkṣāṃ tu śrotumicchāmi tatvataḥ |

bhairava uvāca 

tatvadīkṣā mahādevi śārāyā viśvatomukhā || 9-168 ||
tāmahaṃ saṃpravakṣyāmi śṛṇuṣvāyatalocane |
pṛthivyāpastathā tejo vāyurākāśameva ca || 9-169 ||
etaiḥ śuddhaistu śuddhyeta ṣaṭprakāro varānane |
pañca karmendriyāḥ proktā buddhīndriyāḥ tataḥ param || 9-170 ||
tanmātrāḥ pañca vijñeyāḥ lakṣaṇaṃ tu ataḥ śṛṇu |
śabdaḥ sparśaḥ raso rūpaṃ gandhaṃ tanmātrapañcakam || 9-171 ||
śrotraṃ tvak cakṣuṣī jihvā ghrāṇaṃ buddhīndriyāṇi tu | 
85b kh] kathitāni mayā pañca śrūyatāmeṣa niścayaḥ || 9-172 || 
vācā pāṇī tathā pādau pāyūpasthaśca kīrtitaḥ |
karmākṣapañcakaṃ hyetat kathitaṃ tu varānane || 9-173 ||
buddhirmanastva haṃkāraḥ prakṛtyādi yathākramam |
sattvaṃ rajastamo jñeyaṃ pradhānaguṇasaṃbhavāḥ || 9-174 ||
avyaktaṃ tu samākhyātaṃ pañcaviṃśadudāhṛtam |
puruṣaṃ caiva ṣaḍviṃśat sukhāsukhanivedakam || 9-175 ||
kṣetrajaṃ sukhavedajñaṃ duḥkhaṃ vā yat purārjitam |
tat karmavāsanāśeṣaṃ bhogyaṃ tat samudāhṛtam || 9-176 ||
tena śuddhena śuddhyeta bhavabījamanekadhā |
saṃśodhya kramaśo devi adhvāne tu yathāvidhiḥ || 9-177 ||
atorddhe niyatirākhyātā niyamatve vyavasthitā |
kālasaṃkhyāprakartārau yat kṛtaṃ tasya śobhane || 9-178 ||
kalayā vyāpitaṃ tatvaṃ kalitaṃ tu yataḥ sthitam |
teneyantu ? kalā proktā rāgād dalitacetasaḥ || 9-179 ||
rāgācca rañjito devi māyayā balavān priye |
vidyā vedanikā jñeyā vidyā vetti śubhāśubham || 9-180 ||
parāvidyārthavettāraḥ śuddhyāmārgāśritaḥ pumān |
īśvarādhiṣṭhito bhuyo gacchateśvabhrameva vā || 9-181 ||
mokṣaṃ vā yadi vānyaṃ vā īśvarāt saṃpracoditam |
sadāśivastu vettā vai paśūnāṃ mocakaḥ priye || 9-182 ||
86a kh] māyābījaparityakto bhogaviplutacetasām |
śaktistu vyāpinī sūkṣmā śivadharmānuvartanī || 9-183 ||
śivastu sarvagaḥ proktaḥ ṣaḍtriṃśānte vyavasthitaḥ |
evaṃ śodhya kramād devi adhvānaṃ tatvasaṃkhyayā || 9-184 ||
ṣaṭtriṃśakapade devi śodhanīyaṃ manīṣibhiḥ |
navatatvasvarūpeṇa prakṛtyādi yathākramam || 9-185 ||
śodhanīyaṃ varārohe aṇūnāṃ citrabhogadam |
ātmavidyāśivākhyaṃ tu tatvatrayamudāhṛtam || 9-186 || 
mukhyāstvete samākhyātāḥ śodhanīyāḥ prayatnataḥ |
etaiḥ śuddhaistu śuddhyeta ṣaṭprakāro varānane || 9-187 ||
tatprabhāvitāḥ sarve draṣṭavyāḥ parameśvari |
sāṃprataṃ tu kalādhvānaṃ vakṣyāmi tava sundari || 9-188 ||
kalābhivyāpitaṃ tatvaṃ tatvaṃ vai kālarūpiṇam |
kalārūpo mahādevaḥ sarvajñaḥ parameśvaraḥ || 9-189 ||
nivṛttiśca pratiṣṭhā ca vidyā śāntistathaiva ca |
47a g] śāntyātītā parā jñeyā pṛthivyādi sakalā smṛtā || 9-190 ||
indikā dīpikā caiva rocikā mocikā tathā |
ūrddhvagāmī parā jñeyā sarvāsāṃ pūraṇī smṛtā || 9-191 ||
sūkṣmā caiva susūkṣmā ca tathā vai cāmṛtā smṛtā |
śakteścātmakalā proktā vyāpinyāyāḥ śivasya tu || 9-192 ||
86b kh] vyāpinī vyomarūpā ca anantānātha nāśritā ? |
34a] kalādhvānaṃ samāsena kathitaṃ tava sundari || 9-193 ||
eteṣvekatamaṃ śodhyaṃ deśikena mahātmanā |
mantrādhvānaṃ tu saṃśodhya vidyayā tu parāparā ? || 9-194 ||
pādaiḥ padādhvāvid dīkṣā kartavyā mama vidyayā |
bhuvanādyā varārohe kartavyā śāstravittamaiḥ || 9-195 ||
kālāgnimāditaṃ kṛtvā śivāntaṃ yāvadeva hi |
ṣaṭprakāro vidhistveṣa varṇādhvāne yathākramam || 9-196 ||
grahaṇaṃ yojanaṃ caiva viyogañca yathākramam |
kartavyaṃ tu vipaścidbhiḥ śāstradṛṣṭena karmaṇā || 9-197 ||
eṣāmekatamaṃ devi śodhanīyaṃ prayatnataḥ |
saṃskāraṃ vidhivat kṛtvā guruṇādhvānavedinā || 9-198 ||
kartavyo'nugraho devi paśūnāṃ ca yathākramam |
sṛṣṭisaṃhārabhedena jñātvā kāmyānurūpataḥ || 9-199 ||
tadā mukto varārohe pāśastobho tadā bhavet |
pāśastobho yadā jāto nānyathā vīranayike || 9-200 ||

devyuvāca

śrutā dīkṣā mayā nātha ṣaḍvidhādhve yathā sthitā |
sāṃprata pāśasaithilyaṃ śrotumicchāmi tatvataḥ || 9-201 ||
pāśāḥ sūkṣmāstu ye proktā māyotthā māyavarjitāḥ |
acetanā arūpāstu teṣāṃ stobho na vidyate || 9-202 ||
paśoryat kriyate stobhā paśustobha iti smṛtaḥ |
caitanyaḥ pāśitaḥ pāśairanyonyāvyāpi saṃsthitaḥ || 9-203 ||
dvābhyāṃ kasya bhavet stobhaḥ paśupāśātmakau prabho |
mado mohaśca rāgaśca viṣādaḥ śokameva ca || 9-204 ||
vaicintyo harṣa ākhyā ca saptaite sahajā malāḥ |
dharmādharmātmako bandhastadātmā prakṛtisthitaḥ || 9-205 ||
sūkṣmatvaṃ ca arūpatvaṃ cetanārahitā yataḥ | 
sarveṣāṃ vidyate stobho muktvā kṣetrī sucetanaḥ || 9-206 || 
tasmāt stobho na vidyeta nirguṇe tu acetane |

bhairava uvāca 

ātmā nityo amūrtaśca guṇahīnaśca niṣkriyaḥ || 9-207 ||
vyāghātabhājano jñeyaḥ śodhyo bodhyaḥ svabhāvataḥ |
akalpaḥ paśuruktastu dharmādharmairadhiṣṭhitaḥ || 9-208 ||
pūrvaṃ māyātmakairbaddhaiḥ pāśitaḥ pāśapañjaraiḥ | 
47b g] yato'nādimalastveko bījabhūto vyavasthitaḥ || 9-209 ||
prarohatvamiti dharmaḥ kṣetramāśritya suvrate |
kṣetraṃ māyā bhavet tasya sahajasya paśormalaḥ || 9-210 ||
yathāśvatthakaṇaḥ kṣetre patitotthaḥ prarohati |
śākhāskandhasamākīrṇaḥ kṣetramāśrayayogataḥ || 9-211 ||
evaṃ māyāśrito devi nityaṃ māṇavako malaḥ |
87b kh] māyāpi saṃnimittatvāt pravarteta aṇorhi sā || 9-212 ||
animittasya deveśi naiva māyā pravartate |
kadācid bhavate'pyevaṃ śuddhādhvānapravartanam || 9-213 ||
na śivo vidyate devi māyā hyekā bhavet tadā |
sā tu bandhasvabhāvotthā na mokṣastatra kasyacit || 9-214 ||
ayathā yathāyogo'yaṃ pravarteta surairapi |
vidyeśādi smṛtā ye tu mokṣagāḥ parameśvari || 9-215 ||
na teṣāṃ bhavate mokṣo animitto yadā bhavet |
yathā mokṣo na vidyeta vidyamāne'pi satpathe || 9-216 ||
avicāritaśāstrārthe māyākhyā pāśapaddhatiḥ |
yathā rājña svadoṣe tu badhavandhe pravartate || 9-217 ||
nirdoṣe bandhanaṃ naiva tadvanmāyā prasahyate |
āśrayaḥ sahajaḥ puṃsāṃ malo hyekaḥ prabandhakaḥ || 9-218 ||
tadāśritya pravarteta māyā santānabodhanī |
bodhitaṃ māyayā tatvaṃ viśleṣaṃ caiva gacchati || 9-219 ||
kalodvalitacaitanyaṃ vidyādarśitagocaram |
34b] rāgeṇa rañjitaścāsau buddhyādikaraṇairyutaḥ || 9-220 ||
evaṃ māyātmako bandhaḥ prasajyedanusantatau || 9-221 ||
tadāśrayaguṇo dharmastvadharmaśca samāsataḥ |
tadātmakāḥ smṛtāḥ pāśāḥ paśito taistu tiṣṭhati || 9-222 ||
88a kh] evaṃ jñātvā tu viśleṣamātmamāyāśivasya tu |
pāśasaithilyakaṃ paścād yathā bhavati tacchṛṇu || 9-223 ||
acetano yathā devi sarpadaṃṣṭro gatāyuṣaḥ |
calate spandate caiva tīrthaṃ vai preṣayanti hi || 9-224 ||
dṛśyate mantrasāmarthyāt kimatra praticānyate |
śilānāṃ kampanaṃ stobhaṃ sphoṭanaṃ gatirāgatiḥ || 9-225 ||
mṛtadehapraveśaṃ ca rūpādiparivartanam |
hrasvatvaṃ ca kṛṣatvaṃ ca nirgamaṃ jālakāntare || 9-226 ||
evaṃvidhāni siddhāni acintyāni tu suvrate |
mantrasāmarthyabhāvena kathaṃ carcā śivāgame || 9-227 ||
pramāṇāni yānyuktāni pratyakṣāni tu śaṃbhunā |
teṣāṃ tu pravaraṃ hyetat pratyakṣaṃ tu yaśasvini || 9-228 ||
akṣaistu gṛhyate yastu tatrānyā kalpanā katham |
dharmādharmātmako bandho ātmano yad varānane || 9-229 ||
tasya caiva kṣayaḥ kāryo dīkṣayā parameśvari |
48a g] kṛtāni yāni karmāṇi idānīṃ yāni karoti ca || 9-230 ||
dīkṣayā hi kṣayasteṣāṃ piṇḍapātācchivaṃ vrajet |
kṣīṇairdaśātmakaiḥ pāśairadharme ye ca saṃsthitāḥ || 9-231 ||
ādhāro nāsti deveśi yenāsau tiṣṭhate'dhvani |
pāśamukto yadā hyātmā nirādhāraḥ prakīrtitaḥ || 9-232 ||
patate kampate caiva cchinnamūlopa * tpriye | 
88b kh] yathā vṛkṣo bahirbhāvaiśchinnaḥ kampati bhūtale || 9-233 ||
patate ca na sandehastadvadātmā prakīrtitaḥ |
####viyogaścaiva pāśānāṃ kartavyo dīkṣayā budhaiḥ || 9-234 ||
viyogastu yadā jātastadā stobho na saṃśayaḥ |
stobho nāma samuddiṣṭaḥ kampanaṃ patanaṃ tathā || 9-235 ||
kāṃsyapītalasaṃsthasya yogaścaiva nirāmaye |
pāśastobho bhavatyevaṃ viyogaṃ bandhanaiḥ saha || 9-236 || 
yathā baddhaḥ kvacid vṛkṣo mūlaiḥ śākhāntagaiḥ priye |
taiśchinnaiḥ patanaṃ yadvadādhārarahitasya ca || 9-237 ||
tadvad dehī patatyevaṃ yukto dharmādibandhanaiḥ |
mokṣaṃ tu bhavate tasya iti śāstre pracoditam || 9-238 ||
pāśastobhāt kṣayaḥ siddhaḥ saṃsiddhaiḥ so'pi saṃvaraiḥ |
saṃvarāṇāmacintyatvāt yathāmūrtaviṣakṣayaḥ || 9-239 ||
tasmānmuktiḥ sphuṭāpyevaṃ yadā pāśā nikṛntitāḥ |
kṛntanaṃ stobhanaṃ proktaṃ na cānyā kalpanā smṛtā || 9-240 || 
dagdhabījāstu te devi yeṣāṃ dīkṣā tu pāśavī |
stabdhāḥ pāśā yadā tasya tadā mokṣo na saṃśayaḥ || 9-241 ||
śāsanānāṃ tu sarveṣāṃ dīkṣāṃ mokṣaṃ vadanti ca |
sa ca mokṣastvadṛṣṭastu sādhyate dṛṣṭahetunā || 9-242 ||
adṛṣṭā ye pratyayā proktā adṛṣṭasya tu sādhane |
śuṣkatarkaistu deveśi na caivāgamakoṭibhiḥ || 9-243 || 
89a kh] sādhyate dīkṣayā muktiryadā stobhaḥ prajāyate | 
stobhahīnā na muktiḥ syāt satyaṃ satyaṃ na saṃśayaḥ || 9-244 ||

devyuvāca

dharmādharmātmakāstasya pāśā ādhārarūpakāḥ |
cchinnamūlā yadā te vai tadātmā caiva gacchati || 9-245 ||
mokṣaṃ tu śāśvataṃ nityaṃ ānandaṃ sarvatomukham |
bhogyenaiva sa tiṣṭheta yato bhuktaṃ kṣayīkṛtam || 9-246 ||
na cātmā tiṣṭhate tasmin bhukte karmātmake vibho | 
bhogyaṃ nāma paśoḥ karma tadutthaṃ bandhanaṃ smṛtam || 9-247 ||
tanniṣkṛtya kuto bandhastanmuktyāmṛtamaśrute |
prāptaḥ pañcatva mātaṃ tu ādhārasyāpyabhāvataḥ || 9-248 ||
35a] śarīrenaiva tiṣṭheta yatastasya kṣayīkṛtaḥ |

bhairava uvāca 

paśostu bandhakā śaktirjagatkāraṇātmikā || 9-249 ||
48b g] tayā baddhaṃ jagat kṛtsnaṃ muktaṃ caiva tayā priye | 
bandhanī ajñarūpāṇāṃ mocanī viditātmanām || 9-250 ||
ubhayārthasādhanī hyeṣāṃ sā śaktirupacaryate |
tadādhāraḥ pravarteta puṇyapāpakṣayeṣvapi || 9-251 ||
bahiraṅgagatā bhāvād rūpasyeva nikṛntanī |
patanaṃ naiva vidyeta guṇenaikena dhāryate || 9-252 ||
tadvadātmā śarīrasthaḥ śaktyādhārastu tiṣṭhati | 
patanaṃ tu na vidyeta śaktyādhārastu pudgalaḥ || 9-253 || 
yathāmṛtaśarīre tu praviśetāparaṃ puram |
pañcabhūtasamāyuktaṃ karaṇaistu trayodaśaiḥ || 9-254 ||
89 b kh] kurute yoginastūrṇaṃ sajīvaṃ tu gatāyuṣam |
calate dhāvate caiva vadate bhuñjate svayam || 9-255 ||
svaśarīre'pi cāspanda ekaivātmā ubheṣvapi |
caitanyadāyako devi śaktyādhārastu pudgalaḥ || 9-256 || 
avicchinnamanā yā tu vyāpinī sā parā kalā |
yathā supto brajed devi grāmād grāmāntaraṃ tathā || 9-257 ||
saptadvīpān samudrāñca aṭate caiva dehinaḥ |
svaśarīre'pi paśyeta yāti śṛṇvati bhuñjati || 9-258 ||
tad bhavati śūnyaṃ hi yaṃ tyaktagaticetanaḥ |
na kaścid gacchate devi nānyo mantā tu vidyate mantā prabhurvettā śaktyādhārāṃ prapaśyati |
yatra śaktirmanastatra manastvanilasaṃsthitaḥ || 9-260 ||
nilasthaścāmahaṃkāro? hyahaṃkāre pratiṣṭhitā |
trividhā sā tu vijñeyā satvarājasatāmasā || 9-261 ||
tāmasā tiryalokānāṃ narāṇāṃ cāpi rājasā |
satvotkaṭā tu devīnāṃ bhavate varavarṇini || 9-262 ||
tatprabhāvāt prapaśyanti yānti śṛṇvanti caiva hi |
kāmikaṃ cāpi gṛhanti manasā yatra tatra gam || 9-263 ||
evaṃ śaktirmayākhyātā savyāpārā śivātmikā |
tasyā cchedo yadā devi tadā nirvāṇakaṃ padam || 9-264 ||
bhavate'tra na sandeho yathā śāstre pracoditam |
sāvalambe bhavet siddhiraṇimādiguṇāṣṭakam || 9-265 ||
90a kh] śaktikāryastu suśroṇi bhuñjate paramaṃ padam |
vidyātmakamidaṃ proktaṃ devadevena śaṃbhunā || 9-266 ||
sāṃprataṃ grahaṇaṃ brūmi yathā te niścalaṃ bhavet |
śaktyāyā grahaṇaṃ kāryaṃ mantrātītasya suvrate || 9-267 ||
kriyājñānaparityāgād rudrasthāne tu bhāvanā |
nirodhastatra kartavyo manākhyā parameśvari || 9-268 ||
anilānalayogena stutpate tu na saṃśayaḥ |
grahaṇaṃ nābhideśe tu kadambagolakākṛtiḥ || 9-269 ||
49a g] śaktyāgre tu tato bhāvyaṃ jvālāmālāvalīdharam |
vāyvagnipuramadhyasthaḥ patate nātra saṃśayaḥ || 9-270 ||
nābhisthaṃ bhramaṇaṃ dhyāyed durlakṣo'lātacakravat |
grahaṇaṃ sūkṣmabhāvasthaṃ bhavate nātra saṃśayaḥ || 9-271 ||
caladrūpā manākhyā tu bhāvayed yatra tatragā |
nāgodvalanayogena patate'tra na saṃśayaḥ || 9-272 ||
yadrūpaṃ bhāvayitvā tu vedhayed yoganāyakaḥ |
tadvikāro bhavatyāśu jñātvā śaktiṃ parāṃ priye || 9-273 ||
tatvasya yādṛśaṃ rūpaṃ vedhaṃ kṛtvā tu tādṛśam |
tadātmako bhavet stobho vikārāstādṛgeva hi || 9-274 ||
evaṃ jñātvā varārohe mantrātītasya suvrate |

sāṃprataṃ mantrayogena yathā bhavati tacchṛṇu |
90b kh] haraśca na yutaṃ hyetacchaktibījamudāhṛtam || 9-276 ||

35b] hṛccakre vinyasenmantraṃ dvādaśasvarabhūṣitam || 9-277 ||
javākusumasaṃkāśaṃ caitanyaṃ tasya madhyataḥ |
vāyunā preritaṃ cakraṃ vahninā caiva dīpitaṃ || 9-278 ||
taṃ dhyāyeta japenmantraṃ nāmāntaritayogataḥ |

anena kramayogena sarvādhveṣu sa paśyati |

91a kh] bhūṣaṇena tu vāmena trailokyaṃ stobhayet priye |

prathamaṃ tu tato datvā daśanaṃ rephasaṃyutam || 9-313 ||
nābhistu tena saṃyuktaḥ prathamaṃ tu punardvijam |
yathā pūrvaṃ tathā kāryaṃ lakṣaṇaṃ samudāhṛtam || 9-314 ||
kathitā tu mayā bhadre kṣurikā marmachedanī |
chedayeta tato marmaṃ yojayet parame pade || 9-315 ||
sāṃpratamastrarājānaṃ kathayāmi samāsataḥ |
nābhibījaṃ samuddhārya daṇḍākrāntaṃ tu kārayet || 9-316 ||
nitambaṃ tadadhastāṃ tu punardaṇḍaṃ tu kārayet |
śikharaṃ tu tato yojyaṃ bhūṣaṇaṃ tadanantaram || 9-317 ||
vāmakarṇasya suśroṇi kalākhyaṃ mastake nyaset |
92b kh] evaṃ mayā samuddiṣṭaṃ astrarājaṃ sudurlabham || 9-318 ||
āpādatalamūrdhānāntaṃ smared devaṃ tu vyāpakam |
uccāro hyastrarājasya kartavyaḥ śaktisaṃyutaḥ || 9-319 ||
jvalatpāvakasaṃkāśo dhyāyamāno japet sadā |
kuñcanaṃ aṅgulīnāṃ tu kartavyaṃ codanaṃ tataḥ || 9-320 ||
jānuṃ cākuñcayet paścādūrakau tadanantarāt |
kaṭiguhyaṃ tato kuñcya nābhisthagrahaṇaṃ tataḥ || 9-321 ||
brahmasthaṃ caiva viṣṇusthaṃ rudrasthaṃ bindumadhyagam |
nādasthaṃ caiva śaktisthaṃ vyāpinīsthaṃ ca sarvataḥ || 9-322 ||
viṣuvasthaṃ ca jñātavyaṃ visargasthamanantaram |
guruvaktragataṃ jñātvā mucyate mocayati ca || 9-323 ||
evaṃ tyāgagatiṃ jñātvā śaktyādhārasya suvrate |
śaktiḥ sūkṣmaparā jyotsnā paradharmaprabodhanī || 9-324 ||
taṃ jñānaṃ paramaṃ devi pāramparyakramāgatam |
kathitaṃ sarahasyaṃ tu sadyonirvāṇadaṃ padam || 9-325 ||
utkrāntiḥ kathitā bhadre ātmano vā parasya vā |
kartavyā jñānavijñānairyathā śāstre pradarśitāḥ || 9-326 ||
athānyaṃ saṃpravakṣyāmi abhiṣekavidhiṃ śubham |
sāṃpradāyikametat tu nābhāgyāḥ prāpnuvanti hi || 9-327 ||
gandhadignau karau kṛtvā śeṣyasya susamāhitaḥ |
śaktyāsanaṃ nyaset tatra gandhāmbupūritaṃ tataḥ || 9-328 ||
93a kh] kalaśaṃ vāthavā saṃkhaṃ sahiraṇyaṃ tu vinyaset |
somaṃ prapūjayet tasmin aṣṭavargasamanvitam || 9-329 ||
vartulīkṛtya śaktiṃ tu uccaret tat punaḥ punaḥ |
tataḥ stutya hyasau hastaṃ kalaśaṃ caiva kampati || 9-330 ||
50b g] dhārāsaṃpātanirghoṣaḥ patate śiṣyamūrddhani |
dhārāsaṃpātayogena brahmahatyāṃ vyapohati || 9-331 ||
etat pratyayasaṃvedyaṃ dṛśyate yasya kasyacit |
sa eva pūjayed devi siddhavidyādharādibhiḥ || 9-332 ||

devyuvāca

asyaiva rūpakaṃ kintu yasya varṇo na vidyate |
varṇahinā yadā deva kautārtha ? pratipadyate || 9-333 ||
yathā taṃ jñāyate vīryaṃ ko vīryasya ca bhājanaḥ |

36b] bhairava uvāca 

likhed bhūmyāṃ tu tāṃ śaktiṃ kuṭilākārarūpiṇīm || 9-334 ||
nirīkṣaṇamiṣad dṛṣṭvā upasannastu suvrate |
bhavitātmā yadā paśyecchaktirvai kuṭilākṛtiḥ || 9-335 ||
śatena patate yastu tīvrapātaḥ prakīrtitaḥ | evaṃ sahasramekena athavaivāyutena ca || 9-336 ||
yasya pāto bhaved devi madhyamaḥ parikīrtitaḥ |
dvayayutaistryayutairvāpi tathā pañcadaśaiḥ priye || 9-337 ||
patate vartanairyastu so'dhamaśca tviti smṛtaḥ |
pātānāṃ ca vibhāgo'tra vivṛṇvedaṃ śakānvaye || 9-338 ||
patitaṃ dīkṣayed devi pātahīnaṃ tu varjayet |
93b kh] pātahīno durātmāno na dīkṣāphalamarhati || 9-339 ||
pūrvajātiḥ smṛtā mantrāstatvaṃ bhāvanti suvrate |
bhāvitānāṃ tu cidbhedaṃ calate kampate dhunet || 9-340 ||
pāśacchede tu saṃjāte patate kāṃsyapītale |
sanmukhaṃ patate yastu cchinnapāśo na saṃśayaḥ || 9-341 ||
uttamo'sau samuddiṣṭa uttāno madhyamo mataḥ |
nirpakṣāto'dhamaḥ prokto devadevena śambhunā || 9-342 ||
sūkṣmaḥ pāśo varārohe kartaryā naiva cchidyate |
śuddhadvandvattamiśrāśca yadā devi vinaśyate || 9-343 ||
tadā mukto bhavatyāśu pāśajālena suvrate |
kāṣṭhavat tiṣṭhate yastu pāṣāṇo vā sureśvari || 9-344 ||
tasya dīkṣā na kartavyā yadi nirmānuṣī matā |
tīvreṇa khecaraṃ yāti pātena varavarṇini || 9-345 ||
madhyamena tu pātālaṃ adhame sukha jīvati |
evaṃ pratyayamākhyātaṃ pāśastobhaṃ karoti yaḥ || 9-346 ||
na jñānena vinā stobho na vīryeṇa sureśvari |
siddhayogī karotyevaṃ saṃpradāyena saṃyutam || 9-347 ||
saṃpradāyavihīnastu yo dīkṣāṃ kartumicchati |
niṣphalaṃ pariśramaṃ tasya na dīkṣā narakaṃ vrajet || 9-348 ||
vratinastu punardevi kṣetrapālā bhavanti hi |
yadi dīkṣā bhavenmuktiḥ sarveṣu cāgameṣu ca || 9-349 ||
51a g] tasmād dīkṣā tu boddhavyā pāśastobho yadā bhavet |
dharmā dharmanibaddhastu piṇḍo tatra patet priye || 9-350 ||
94a kh] tadā nirvāṇadā dīkṣā yaḥ karoti sa deśikaḥ |
yenaivālabdhamātrasya kṣubhyate pāśapañjaram || 9-351 ||
sa gurustu samākhyātaḥ saṃsārārṇavatārakaḥ |
tasya pādarajo devi śirasā dhārayed yadi || 9-352 ||
tatkṣaṇādeva mucyeta sarpasya kavacaṃ yathā |
sācāryaḥ śaktimantastu śivasadbhāvabhāvitaḥ || 9-353 ||
36b] tatvavit sa samākhyātaḥ stobhayet pāśapañjaram |
sa gurustu samākhyāto dhamārthakāmamokṣadaḥ || 9-354 ||
sāṃprataṃ tu punarvakṣye tulāyā vidhimuttamam |
tulā paṭṭamayī kāryā lakṣaṇaṃ tasya kathyate || 9-355 || 
dīrghatvaṃ śākhayoḥ kāryaṃ caturhastapramāṇataḥ | 
vistāre trīṇi hastāni madhye hastadvayārddhataḥ || 9-356 ||
caturasrau samau kāryau tritoraṇasamanvitau |
madhye tu śṛṃkhalā kāryā ? kaṭakatrayasaṃyutā || 9-357 ||
karṇau tu sudṛḍhau kāryau sthūlau sandhitasandhinau |
evaṃ tataḥ prakalpyādau tulā vai maṇḍapāgrataḥ || 9-358 ||
adhivāsanaṃ tataḥ kṛtvā gandhapuṣpapapavitrakaiḥ | 
naivedyairbahubhirdhūpairmantranyāsaṃ tu kārayet || 9-359 || 
dvitīye'hani śiṣyaṃ ca taulyamānaṃ samārabhet |
samasūtraṃ tu taṃ taulyaṃ sikatāyāḥ sahaikataḥ || 9-360 ||
samamānaṃ tato jñātvā dāpayedagnipārśvataḥ |
37a] grahaṇaṃ śaktinā kāryaṃ sandhānaṃ śaktinā punaḥ || 9-361 ||
94b kh] saṃśodhya kramaśaḥ sarvaṃ adhvareṣu yathākramam |
yojayeta tato mantrīṃ sakale niṣkale'pi vā || 9-362 || 
sarvaśudhinivṛtyarthaṃ bahurūpeṇa dāpayet |
pūrṇā tu pūrṇatāṃ kuryād vidhiracchidratāṃ bhavet || 9-363 ||
ārohayet tato mantrī sādhakaṃ śaktivigraham |
uccārya śaktinā tūrṇaṃ prāṇaṃ jīvasamāśritam || 9-364 ||
kaṇṭhayuktaṃ sabinduṃ ca yakāreṇā pradīpitam |
bhūṣaṇairyāmakarṇasya bhūṣitaṃ tu yathepsayā || 9-365 ||
laghutvaṃ bhavate devi gacchate corddhvataḥ svayam | 
śuddhaḥ pāpavinirmukto paraṃpāraṃ ca gacchati || 9-366 ||
śuddhirevaṃ samākhyātaṃ brahmaghnasyāpi śaṃbhunā | 
gāva prasyāpi ? kartavyā tulāśuddhirmanīṣiṇā || 9-367 ||
evaṃ tulāviśuddhasya mokṣa ityabhiśabditaḥ |
mṛteṣā dvaraṇaṃ proktaṃ prasannā yā varānane || 9-368 ||
darpaṇaṃ cādhivāsyādau śiṣyaṃ kanyakayā saha | 
dvitīye'hani dīkṣā tu kartavyā śiva adhvare || 9-369 ||
grahaṇaṃ hṛdayasthasya kartavyaṃ paśorātmanaḥ |
tatvasthaṃ yojayet paścācchuddhyarthaṃ bandhanāya tu || 9-370 || 
darpaṇasthāṃ prapaśyanti kanyakāṃ divyacakṣuṣā |
yat tatra sthitaṃ sūkṣme śarīre bhuvanādhvani || 9-371 ||
grahaṇaṃ yojayed devi tatve tatve na saṃśayaḥ |
yāvad yuktaḥ pare tatve tāvadevaṃ prapaśyati || 9-372 ||
śaktinā labdhadṛṣṭā tu netrabījena suvrate | 
95a kh] abhimantrya tato devi udakaṃ tu śatārddhataḥ || 9-373 ||
kṣālayennayane tāsāṃ darpaṇaṃ tu pradarśayet |
paśayanti tatra tāḥ kanyāḥ śubhāśubhaṃ phalāphalam || 9-374 ||
athānyaṃ saṃpravakṣyāmi prasannā vidhimuttamam |
oṃ namaścaṇḍikāyai yogavāhini pravarta pravarta mahāmohaya mohaya yogamukhi yogeśvari mahāmāyādhāriṇi hiri 2 bhūtapriye svakāyaṃ paśyāmi bāḍhaṃ śṛṇomi sarvalokāni paśyāmi turu 2 sādhaya 2 svāhā |
rudrasthāne śucirbhūtvā sahasrā daśa yojayet || 9-375 ||
siddhā bhavati sā vidyā daśa karmāṇi kārayet |
candrasūrye'thavā khaḍge darpaṇe vātha dīpake || 9-376 ||
aṅguṣṭhe vā ghaṭe vāpi dārikāṃ vātha dārakām |
paśyā peyakṣasandehāt tilāṃ vā taṇḍulādataḥ || 9-377 ||
bhūtaṃ bhavyaṃ bhaviṣyaṃ ca pṛcchate kathayanti hi |
atha vidyāṃ samāvartya rajānyāṃ svapayecchuciḥ || 9-378 ||
svayameva prapaśyeta svapnānte yacchubhāśubham |
oṃ rakte raktāṅguṣṭhe ucchuṣme avatara avatara piśācini kathaya kathaya kathāpaya kathāpaya svāhā |
khaḍgamādarśake vātha aṅguṣṭhe vā varānane || 9-379 ||
paśyanti kanyakāḥ sarvaṃ śubhāśubhaṃ phalāphalam |
95b kh] oṃ piṅgali pāśupati mahāvidye svāhā |
eṣā vidyā mahādevi karma kurvanti saptadhā || 9-380 ||
oṃ rakte virakte avatara 2 mātaṅgini svāhā |
navavidhaṃ syād yat karma eṣā vidyā karoti hi | 
saptabhirmantritaṃ hastaṃ kṛtvā svaurasi vinyaset || 9-381 ||
svayameva hi jānāti mantrasyāsya prabhāvataḥ |
oṃ namaścaṇḍikāyai avatara 2 turu 2 svāhā |
sopavāsaḥ śucirbhūtvā aṣṭautkṛṣṭaśataṃ japet || 9-382 ||
rātrau svapne tvavatīrya kathayed yacchubhāśubham |
sarvāsāṃ caiva vidyānāṃ caṇḍikāgṛhamāśritaḥ || 9-383 || 
daśasāhasrikaṃ jāpya tataḥ karmaṇi kārayet |
37b] evaṃ parīkṣya hetvarthe jijñāsārthaṃ svayaṃbhunā || 9-384 ||
kathitaṃ sarahasyaṃ tu dīkṣākarmasuvistaram |
dīkṣitāya praśāntāya abhiṣiktāya śobhane || 9-385 ||
samayīputrake vāpi sādhake ca varānane |
nākhyeyaṃ nādhikāriṇye satyamīśena bhāṣitam || 9-386 ||
cumbake śrāvaṇīyaṃ tu vidhidṛṣṭena karmaṇā |

devyuvāca

cumbakaṃ svādhikārī syāt sarvasya parameśvaraḥ || 9-387 ||
tasyābhiṣecanaṃ śambhoḥ kalaśādeva taiḥ saha |
kuṇḍaṃ ca mekhalāṃ caiva stucyā vā śruvameva ca || 9-388 ||
jihvādidevatāvaktraṃ pramāṇaṃ śabdasaṃyutam |
jvālānāṃ gandhavarṇaṃ ca pūrṇastucyā grahet tathā || 9-389 ||
96a kh] etanme saṃśayo nātha yaṃ noktaṃ tad brabīhi me |

bhairava uvāca 

aṣṭau kalaśāḥ samāhṛtyāḥ sauvarṇā rājatāpi vā || 9-390 ||
tāmramṛṇmayataḥ kāryāḥ sudṛḍhā nirvraṇāḥ śubhāḥ |
kambugrīvā bṛhakukṣā akṛṣṇā pravalaṃviṣā || 9-391 ||
gandhodakena pūryādau cūtapallavaśobhitāḥ |
sitavastrāvṛtāḥ kāryāḥ sitacandanacarcitāḥ || 9-392 ||
śuklamālyairalaṃkṛtya ratnagarbhāḥ samodarāḥ |
kṣārādiṃ vinyaset teṣu samudrākalaśeṣu vai || 9-393 ||
garbhadattāḥ smṛtāḥ cāṣṭau vidyeśā lokapālakāḥ |
vargāṣṭakaṃ samanyastvā abhiṣekaṃ tu kārayet || 9-394 ||
athavaikena kartavyā kalpanā sakalā priye |
kalaśaikena vā kārya abhiṣeko varānane || 9-395 ||
samastakṛtasūtārtho yathā śāstre pracoditaḥ |
tat tathā tu prakartavyamanyathā doṣabhājanam || 9-396 ||
abhiṣiktastato mantrī sitacandanacarcitaḥ |
sitavastradharaḥ sragvī ānayed devasannidhau || 9-397 ||
namaskāraṃ tataḥ kṛtvā nipated daṇḍavad bhuvi |
nītvā cāgnisamīpe tu homaṃ kuryāt sadā budhaḥ || 9-398 ||
ratnimātraṃ ca deveśi athavā muṣṭimānataḥ |
kuṇḍaṃ ca hastamātraṃ syāccaturhastaṃ trimekhalam || 9-399 ||
vṛttaṃ vā kārayenmantrī svecchayā digvyavasthitam |
kuṇḍeṣu hīnamāneṣu mekhalārahiteṣu ca || 9-400 ||
96b kh] khaṇḍasphuṭitavaktreṣu vilome homamārabhet |
dvāṅguṣṭhaparvavaipulyā prathamā mekhalā bhavet || 9-401 ||
dvitīyā ca tribhirjñeyā caturbhiścopari sthitā |
yonirnavāṅgulā sapta - athavā - dvādaśāṅgulā || 9-402 ||
gajoṣṭhasadṛśādhastādoṣṭhaṃ tasyāṅgulāyatam |
trikoṇaṃ dīrghavaktraṃ ca māraṇoccāṭane hitam || 9-403 ||
vidveṣaṇe ca kartavyaṃ lakṣaṇena vivarjitam || 9-403 ||
ekakarmānusāreṇa kuṇḍaṃ kuryāt susiddhidam || 9-404 ||
52b g] nityanaimittike home śavike vā varānane |
hastamātrapramāṇena kuṇḍaṃ syāt phaladāyakam || 9-405 ||
dvikaraṃ daśasāhasre lakṣahome catuṣkaram |
ṣaḍhastaṃ prayatākhye tu home ca varavarṇiṇi || 9-406 ||
koṭihome ca kartavyaṃ aṣṭahastaṃ suśobhanam |
hastamātrasya kuṇḍasya mekhalā yāḥ prakīrtitāḥ || 9-407 ||
tāścaiva dviguṇā vṛddhyā aṣṭahastaṃ tu kārayet |
vāhumātrakarottu tu sruksruvau ca śubhau matau || 9-408 ||
vaikaṅkataśamī garbha palāśakhadirodbhavau |
anyeṣu ca śubhaikeṣu lakṣaṇena vilakṣitam || 9-409 ||
kuryācchubheṣu homeṣu viparīteṣu nānyathā |
vibhītaka mahāvṛkṣatinduśālmalisaṃbhavau || 9-410 ||
malakāṣṭhodbhavau vātha gardabhāsthinarāsthijau |

97a kh] mānahīnau padāsau ca vaktrau ca sphuṭitāvubhau || 9-411 ||
38a] sarvathā sarvayatnena śobhanārthau vipasvinau |
vakra sphuṭitahīnau ca śubhavṛkṣamayāvapi || 9-412 ||
varjitavyau prayatnena sruksruvau ca śubhau yadi |
sruvaṃ ca karmikādhāraṃ srucyā catuṣpatyārcitā? || 9-413 ||
dvādaśāṃgulavistīrṇa itaraṃ syādaṃguladvayam |
madhyamāṅgulavaipulyaṃ caturviṃśāṅgulāyatam || 9-414 ||
ṣaḍaṅgulaparīṇāhā śruciḥ padmavilāñchitāḥ |
śaṃkhasvastikavajrāṃka matha cakravilāñchitam || 9-415 ||
kṛtā hyevaṃvidhāṃ mantrī alābhe nāthapāṇinā |
homayed bhairavāgrau tu mantravit susamāhitaḥ || 9-416 ||
agnikāryaṃ prakurvīta jihvālakṣaṇatatvavit |
siddhyasiddhividhānajño varṇarūpahutāśane || 9-417 ||

tasya mantrātmikā mūrti rājyaṃ vai somasaṃbhavam || 9-418 ||
agnestu devatā sarvā mukhe vai saṃpratiṣṭhitāḥ |

97b kh] aṅguṣṭhaparvavistīrṇa vahurujva? late śikhā |
ekībhūtāstu tiṣṭhanti svalpahavyendhanena tu || 9-422 ||
agnikuṇḍapramāṇena mukhametahutāśane |
53a g] jvālāmālāsahasraistu pātālavadanaṃ tu tat || 9-423 ||
agraiḥ prabodhanaṃ kṛtvā jihvā lakṣedvidhānataḥ |
devatārūpakaṃ caiva phalaṃ yasyaiva yādṛśam || 9-424 ||
pūrvapaścimato jihvāgrahāstatra pratiṣṭhitā |
homakāle prayatnena dve jihve caiva varjayet || 9-425 ||
prathamā dharmajihvā tu nīlavarṇā tu sā smṛtāḥ |
dvitīyā yakṣajihvā tu tābhrātmakā rasaprabhā || 9-426 ||
tṛtīyā saumyadaivatyā śuklavarṇāmṛtopamā |
yamajihvā caturthī ca kṛṣṇavarṇā tu sā smṛtā || 9-427 ||
pañcamī viṣṇudaivatyā atasīpuṣpasannibhā |
ṣaṣṭhī tu sūryadaivatyā śuklavarṇā tu sā smṛtāḥ || 9-428 ||
saptamī sarvadaivatyā sarvarūpaistu dīpyate |
prathamā niṣphalā jihvā dvitīyā dhanadāyikā || 9-429 ||
tṛtīyā prīṇayet sarvaṃ jagat sthāvarajañgamam |
caturthī ca kṣayaṃ gotre pañcamī kulavarddhanī || 9-430 ||
ṣaṣṭhyāṃ caiva hutaṃ devi ārogyaṃ saṃprayacchati |
saptamī dharmakāmārthaṃ dadāti vidhipūjitā || 9-431 ||
siddhiścaiva viśeṣeṇa rudraśaktistu homitā |
brāhmī māheśvarī caiva kaumārī vaiṣṇavī tathā || 9-432 ||
98a kh] vārāhī caindrikā devī cāmuṇḍā saptamī matā |
jihvārūpā stutā jñeyā sādhakānāṃ tu siddhidā || 9-433 ||
tasmāt sarvaprayatnena madhye homaṃ praśasyate |
hūyamāne yadāśabdaṃ śaṃkhakā halataṃtu?jam || 9-434 ||
śrūyantevaṃ savādyāni tadā siddhestulastuṇam |
kharoṣṭrakākakrauṃcaṃ ca gṛddholūkakharothavā || 9-435 ||
śabdebhicārasiddhyarthaṃ mahādhūmolvaṇopi vā |
vyālapakṣimṛgākārā vitruḍanti śikhā yadi || 9-436 ||
vilomaṃ sādhayantyāśu astrākārāstathaiva ca | 
chatrākārā dhvajākārā vitānasadṛśāśca yā || 9-437 || 
kumudotpalamākārā dṛśyanti ca nabhastale |
vajra śaṃkhaṃ ca śrīvatsaṃ girirūpārciṣo yadi || 9-438 ||
dakṣiṇāvartakopetāḥ śubhakarmaprasādhikāḥ |
candanośīrakarpūrakuṃkumāgarugandhikāḥ || 9-439 ||
38b] anyairvā susugandheṣu jātyādyeṣu śrameṣvapi |
anulome śubhau gandhaḥ kṣipraṃ siddhikaraṃ smṛtam || 9-440 || 
māṃsāsthimṛgaromeṣu dahya?māneṣu yādṛśam | 
gandho vāti virupo vā ki?ṣṭasūtrasamopi vā || 9-441 || 
śubheṣvapi viloma syā jñeyaṃ mantravicakṣaṇaiḥ |
hūyamāne virūpepi yadi gandhaṃ suśobhanam || 9-442 ||
siddhiliṅgaṃ tu taṃ jñeyaṃ vāñchitārthapradāyikam |
śubhāśubheṣu dravyeṣu ājyagandho yadā bhavet || 9-443 || 
98b kh] sopi veṣṭaphale dattaṃ śubhāśubhavimiśrakam |
53b g] ya evaṃ jñāya mantrajño yo japate jahutepi ca || 9-444 ||
kriyā kālāṃ sa tatvajñaḥ sasiddhiḥ phalabhāgbhavet |
anyathā vidhihīnasya vṛthā caiva vilomakṛt || 9-445 ||
saṃskṛtepi kriyāhīna ehikāma prasādhayet |
āgne varṇā daśa proktaḥ sarvakarmajayāvahā || 9-446 ||
ādityodayavarṇābhaṃ snigdhavaidūryasannibhaḥ | 
ghṛtavarṇanibhaścaiva lākṣāvarṇastathaiva ca || 9-447 || 
taptāyasasuvarṇābhaḥ kusuṃbharasasannibhaḥ | 
haritālanibhaścaiva dhūmavarṇastathaiva ca || 9-448 ||
bahuhavyendhane vahnau susamiddhe tathaiva ca |
vidhūme lelihāne ca hotavyaṃ karmasiddhaye || 9-449 ||
arciṣmāṃ piṇḍitaśikhaḥ sarpiḥ kāñcanasannibhaḥ |
snigdhaḥ pradakṣiṇāvartaḥ sarvakāmaphalapradaḥ || 9-450 ||
evaṃ vijñāya vidhivad guruṇā sādhakasya tu |
saṃskārayoga kartavyaṃ yathāvantaṃ nibodhataḥ || 9-451 ||
kṛtvā sūtramayaṃ pāśā navātmā cābhi mantritā |
uccārya navatatvāni ākāreṇa tu bheditāḥ || 9-452 ||
mūrddhni kaṇṭhe hṛdiścaiva nābhe ūrū kaṭistathā |
jaṃghā gulphau tathā pādau sarvatra viniyojayet || 9-453 ||
vāhubhyāṃ hastayoścaiva sarvatra viniveśayet |
99a kh] navātmājyāhutiśataṃ svāhākāreṇa homayet || 9-454 ||
pūrṇāhutyai kapālaṃ vai paśu yojayate pare |
pūrṇāhutyā prayogaṃ tu adhunā kathayāmyaham || 9-455 ||
ūrddhakāyo ṛjugrīvaḥ samapādavyavasthitaḥ |
nābhisthāne sruvāmūlaṃ uttānāgramukhā sama || 9-456 ||
srucopari sruvaṃ devi kṛtvā caivāvadhomukham |
puṣpaṃ datvā srucāgre tu darbheṇa sahitau karau || 9-457 ||
muṣṭinā caiva hastābhyāṃ gṛhītvā yatnataḥ priye |
agrato dakṣiṇaṃ hastaṃ vāmaṃ vai pṛṣṭhataḥ śubhaiḥ || 9-458 ||
muṣṭibhyāṃ saṃgṛhītvā tu uttānakarayogataḥ |
tato ghṛtena saṃpūrya ahīnā na ca kārayet || 9-459 ||
ahameva paraṃ tatvaṃ parāparavibhāgataḥ |
tatvamekaṃ hi sarvatra nānyabhāvaṃ tu bhāvayet || 9-460 ||
yatkumbhe nyastamadhvānaṃ ṣaṭprakāraṃ varānane |
maṇḍalāgnau śivāntasthaṃ sādhāraṇavikalpitam || 9-461 ||
srucau tadadhvamāropya prāṇasthaṃ nāḍimadhyagam |
prāṇādhāre samai kṛtvā srucādhārāṃ vinikṣipet || 9-462 ||
vasorddhāre prayogena prakṣiyejjātavedasi |
nābhisthāne sruvāmūlaṃ tapedāsāntagocare || 9-463 ||
yathā yathā kṣipeddhārā tathā prāṇaṃ samuccaret |
54a g] prāṇastu varṇatāṃ yāti ṣaḍvidhādhvāna yastu saḥ || 9-464 ||
ṣaḍvidhādhvānato naiva prameyaṃ vidyate kvacit |
99b kh] tasmānmantrastha sarve te heyo pādeyataḥ sthitāḥ || 9-465 ||
varṇe kāraṇaṣaṭkaṃ tu ṣaṭtyāgāt saptame layaḥ |
akāraśca ukāraśca makāro bindureva ca || 9-466 ||
arddhacandranirodhī ca nādaścaivorddhvagāminī |
śaktiśca vyāpinī devī samanā ca ata param || 9-467 ||
39a] samanāntaṃ varārohe pāśajālamanantakam |
kāraṇaiḥ ṣaḍbhirāṃkāntaṃ tatrasthaṃ heya kārayet || 9-468 ||

śivatatvaguṇāmoda śivadharmāvalokanam || 9-469 ||

100a kh] anyathāṃ na bhaveddevi nadīvegamivārṇave |

trīṇi pañcadaśaikā vā tilenātha ghṛtena vā |

54b g] paraṃ śaktyāmṛtaṃ kṣomya śiṣyamūrddhnau nidhāpayet |

100b kh] śarīra suṣita taistu tadarddhaṃ cābhiṣecanam || 9-486 ||

darbha muñcāpayitvā tu śivāgnau kalaśe guroḥ || 9-487 ||
pradakṣiṇatrayaṃ kṛtvā daṇḍavannipategrataḥ |

protphullanayanaṃ śāntaṃ paritṛptātma bhāvayet |
nirvāṇakī tviyaṃ dīkṣā nirvījā vāsaṃ bījikā || 9-489 ||
yeṣāṃ sabījakā dīkṣā teṣāṃ kuryābhiṣecanam |
śrutiśīlasamācāro deśikatve niyojayet || 9-490 ||

śitapadmamukhāṅgāracūtapallavasaṃyutaiḥ |

ekaike kalaśe nyāsamanantādiśivāntagam || 9-494 ||
pūjayed bhairavaṃ deva sarvasaṃhāranukramāt |

39b] aṣṭottaraśatenaiva paratatvamanusmaret || 9-496 ||

101a kh] saṃpūjyaivaṃ vidhānena abhiṣekaṃ samārabhet || 9-497 ||

55a g] abhiṣiktonyavāsaṃ tu paridhāpyācamettataḥ |

101b kh] adyaprabhṛti kartavyamadhikāraṃ śivājñayā |

teṣāṃ kuryābhiṣekaṃ tu sādhakaṃ *? niyojayet || 9-518 ||
102a kh] sādhakasyābhiṣekoyaṃ vidyādīkṣādanantaram |

55b g] pūrṇāhuti prayogeṇa aṇimādi guṇairyutam || 9-523 ||

āhuti hṛṣṭakaṃ hutvā abhiṣiñcetmāsādhrakaṃ || 9-524 ||

śāntyātītā pañcame tu śānti paścā caturthake || 9-525 ||
evaṃ śānti puṭitvā tu pṛthivyādīni pañca su |

102b kh] tṛvṛtyādiḥ tṛbhiḥ kumbhaisnā payet pūrvadidmukham || 9-529 ||
śāntyātītaṃ ghaṭa grāhya paścācchiṣyaṃ prasevayet |

sādhakasyābhiṣekoyaṃ vilome cānulomataḥ |

103a kh] dīkṣāvasāne deveśi śrāvaṇīyā vipaściteḥ |

anujñāto'bhiṣiktaśca samayāṃ śrāvayed gurum |####anivedya na bhoktavyaṃ khādennāvidhināmiṣam || 9-541 ||

56a g] śākinīti na vaktavya dhapyaṭiṃ varavarṇini |

103b kh] cchāgī mṛgī tathā meṣī sunakhī ca śṛgālikā |

104a kh] saṃmājitañca nāvācayā ca pūjā na hi kṛtā || 9-561 ||

56b g] gaṇḍāsakaṃ kuṭhāraṃ caramyanīvāsi vedanam || 9-565 ||

vāvallaka tathāsthoḍruṃ sillañca ghātanantathā || 9-567 ||
daṃhikā karṇikaṃ ṛvakaṃ kuntañca vasunandakam |
kartavyā kurtarī caiva kartikā pāda cellakaṃ || 9-568 ||
pharakaṣvāpi khaḍgañcā anyavāpyā yudhaṃ priye |
pādenaiva spṛśet mantrī na tu laṃghat kadācanaḥ || 9-569 ||
vaṭāścasthakaṃ ṣā tu na bhuñjīta kadācanaḥ |
na mantrī bhakṣayed bhakṣā vāma hasta sthitānaghe || 9-570 ||
vāma haste na kadā tavyaṃ dakṣiṇena tu |
gṛhnīyā paradattantu vāma haste sadā priye || 9-571 ||
nadadyādauṣa dharmantrī rakṣārtha naiva kārayet |
104b kh] na jalpaṃ śivaśāstreṣu kartavyantu parasparam || 9-572 ||
avājyannaiva vaktavyaṃ adhikāraṃ vidhau sthite |
vīragoṣṭhī na bhagna dhyānopekṣet viḍamvanaṃ || 9-573 ||
parairevātmanāpirvāri taṇḍānaiva kārayet |
rudrasthānāni sarvāṇi śrīpūrvāṇyābhi bhāṣayet || 9-574 ||
gurossaṃjñāna gṛhīyātmahimā saṃsthita sudhīḥ |
śayyāsana vimuktvā tu puṣpaṣaṇaurvigṛhya ca || 9-575 ||
praṇamya daṇḍa vadbhūmau śrīkārādyaṃ samuccaret |
sthāna nāmanato te tu dervottathā evadā || 9-576 ||
tathaivare rūparvasya siddhā cārya maheśvari |
evaṃ devi samākhyātā samayāssamāyārthinā || 9-577 ||
śrāvayed guru revantu śāstra dṛṣṭena karmaṇā |
traikkālyampūjayeddevaṃ dhyāna dṛṣṭena karmaṇā || 9-578 ||

iti bhairava srotasi mahātantre vidyāpīṭhe saptakoṭi pramāṇe śrītantrasadbhāve samayadīkṣādhikāro navamaḥ paṭalaḥ || 9 || 


 
41a] devyuvāca

adhvānaṃ tu mahādeva sūcitaṃ na tu varṇitam |
kathayatva prasādena sādhakānāṃ hitāya vai || 10-1 ||

bhairava uvāca

adhvānaṃ saṃpravakṣyāmi samāsena varānane |
adhvakālāgni rudrādhaḥ kaṭāhaṃ saṃvyavasthitam || 10-2 ||
105a kh] koṭiyojanasthauvyena tasyorddhe bhuvanāni tu |

prāsādotuṅgaśikharaiścandrātapasamaprabhaiḥ | 
rathayāmārgavanārāmaiḥ sadāpuṣpaphalānvitaiḥ || 10-122 ||
kokilārāvamadhurairmayūraṣaṭpadākulaiḥ |
haṃsakāraṇḍavākīrṇaiścakravākopaśobhitaiḥ || 10-123 ||
sārasāravasaṃghuṣṭaiḥ padminīṣaṇḍamaṇḍitaiḥ |
taḍāgaiḥ svacchatoyādyairdīrghikāvāpibhiḥ śubhaiḥ || 10-124 ||
puruṣaiśca mahākāyairmahāvalaparākramaiḥ |
sarvaiśvaryasurūpādyaiḥ sarvalakṣaṇasaṃyutaiḥ || 10-125 ||
divyavastrasutāmbūlairdivyagandhānulepanaiḥ |
divyābharaṇakusumairmakuṭai ratnamaṇḍitaiḥ || 10-126 ||
śivārādhanabhaktā ye matprasādhakatatparāḥ |
60a g] te viśanti mahādevi pātālaṃ siddhasevitam || 10-127 ||
rasaṃ rasāyanaṃ divyaṃ siddhadravyaṃ labhanti te |
110b kh] krīḍayanti tathā cānye sārddhaṃ divyavarāṃganaiḥ || 10-128 ||
kāminyaiḥ kāmarūpaistu mattamātaṅgagāmibhiḥ |
sarvābharaṇasaṃyuktaiḥ kāmaśāstrasupeśalaiḥ || 10-129 ||
divyavastraparīdhānai stanabhārabharonnataiḥ |
madhyakṣamaiḥ prasannāsyaistaralāyatalocanaiḥ || 10-130 ||
sukiṅkiṇīnitambasthairhārakeyūrabhūṣitaiḥ |
sugandhigandhaliptāṅgaiḥ kācinmekhalamaṇḍitaiḥ || 10-131 ||
savibhramāloka? hāsyalāsyaisma saṃbhramālisita bhāvabhāvaiḥ |
sasādarādgṛhyabhujaiḥ svakāntaṃ ramanti nāryasmaracāturaṃgyaḥ || 10-132 ||
evaṃ te kathitā devi bhuvanāntaravāsinaḥ |
trayo surāstathā nāgā rākṣasāśca vijānatā || 10-133 ||
ekaike caiva pātāle ete jñeyā varānane |
pātālasaptake jñeyāstathānye bhuvanādhipāḥ || 10-134 ||
balo hyativalaścaiva valavānvalavikramaḥ |
valeso valavantaśca valādhyakṣa prakīrtitaḥ || 10-135 ||
etaiḥ śuddhairime śuddhāḥ saptapātāla vāsinaḥ |
puro dvai caiva sauvarṇaṃ pātālaṃ parikīrtitam || 10-136 ||
yatrāsau tiṣṭhate devo hāṭakaḥ parameśvaraḥ |
purakoṭisahasraistu samantāt parivāritam || 10-137 ||
siddharudragaṇairdivyairbhaginyairmātṛbhiryutam |
yoginyairyogakanyābhi rudrakanyābhi rudrakaiḥ || 10-138 ||
111a kh] siddhadravyarasairmantraiścintāmaṇirasāyanaiḥ |
siddhavidyā samṛddhaiśca hāṭakeśasya mandiram || 10-139 ||
haṭhātpraveśayellokāṃ tadbhāvagatacetasām |
tenāsau hāṭakaḥ prokto devadevo maheśvaraḥ || 10-140 ||
evamekatra saṃyogāt pātālānāṃ ṣṭake pathi |
samasrāṇi samākhyātā navādiparisaṃkhyayā || 10-141 ||
tasyorddhve tu sahasraikaṃ yojanānāṃ pramāṇataḥ |
bhūkaṭāhaṃ samuddiṣṭaṃ samāsāṃ tuṃ varānane || 10-142 ||
tatorddhveṃ tu bhavet pṛthvī nānājanapadākulā |
tasya madhye mahāmeruḥ sauvarṇastu varānane || 10-143 ||
tasyācalasya vistāramūrddhvataḥ kathayāmi te |
yojanānāṃ sahasrāṇi caturāśīti cocchrayām || 10-144 ||
43b] ṣoḍaśaiva sahasrāṇi adhobhāge praropitam |
tānyeva mūlavistāraṃ dviguṇaṃ mūrdhni vistaram || 10-145 ||
tasyorddhe tu sabhāvedyā nāmnā caiva manovatī |
caturdaśasahasrāṇi yojanānāṃ pramāṇataḥ || 10-146 ||
sarvaratnasuśobhāḍhyā strīsahasrasamanvitāḥ |
60b g] sarvabhogaguṇo petā brahmāṇasya mahātmanaḥ || 10-147 ||
siddhavidyādharākīrṇā ṛṣibhiḥparivāritāḥ |
tasyeśānadiśābhāge jyotiṣkaśikharaṃ smṛtam || 10-148 ||
sūryakoṭi pratīkāśaṃ gaṇaprathamasevitam |
sarvatra kusumopetaṃ devagandharvasevitam || 10-149 ||
strī sahasrasamākīrṇaṃ sarvaiśvaryasamanvitam |
tatrāste bhagavāndevastryamvakaḥ parameśvaraḥ || 10-150 ||
111b kh] lokapālādhipaḥ so hi brahmaviṣṇvindranāyakaḥ |
samāṃsastadvijānīyāt surasiddhanamaskṛtaḥ || 10-151 ||
adhikāraṃ prakurute parecchāsaṃpracoditaḥ |
sabhā yā brahmaṇodhastād yojanānāṃ caturdaśaḥ || 10-152 ||
sahasrāṇi samuddiṣṭaṃ cakra vāṭaṃ samaṃtataḥ |
svargāṣṭakasamuddiṣṭaṃ yatra tiṣṭhanti lokapāḥ || 10-153 ||
pūrveṇendrasya vikhyātā purīnāmnāmarāvatī |
tejovatī tathāgneyā citrabhāno prakīrtitā || 10-154 ||
dakṣiṇe yamarājasya nāmnā saṃyamanī purī |
kṛṣṇāṃganā tu naiṛtyāṃ rākṣasasya prakīrtitāḥ || 10-155 ||
paścimena jaleśasya purī śuddhavatī smṛtāḥ |
vāyavyāṃ ca purī vāyornāmnā gandhavatī priye || 10-156 ||
uttareṇāpi somasya purī nāmnā mahodayā |
īśānyāmīśarājasya purī nāmnāṃ yaśovatī || 10-157 ||
eteṣā mantare devi ṣaḍviṃśānyā purī purī ? śṛṇu |
dakṣiṇenāmarāvatyā kāmavatyāpsarā purī || 10-158 ||
sauvarṇasiddhasaṃghānāṃ tasya vai dakṣiṇe na tu |
tasyaiva dakṣiṇe cānyā padmarāgopaśobhitā || 10-159 ||
ādityānāṃ purī khyātāṃ nāmnāṃ cāṃśumatī śubhā |
siddhānāṃ rājatī divyā khyātā vai kusumāvatī || 10-160 ||
vahneḥ paścimadigbhāge viśveśā revatī purī |
112b kh] tasyāpi paścime devi divyo vai viśvakarmaṇaḥ || 10-161 ||
paścime dharmarājasya mātṛnandā purī smṛtā |
krīḍanti mātarāstatra madhupāna vighūrmitā || 10-162 ||
paścime tasya rudrāṇāṃ rohitā nāma kāñcanī |
tatra śūladharā rudrā yamasya paricārakāḥ || 10-163 ||
tasya paścimato jñeyā nāmnā guṇaṃvatī purī |
ekādaśānāṃ rudrāṇāṃ vajraprakāratoraṇā || 10-164 ||
naiṛte pūrva bhāge tu piṅgalā nāma vai purī |
sukarma nāmadeveśi piśācāstatra saṃsthitāḥ || 10-165 ||
naiṛtyuttarasāmīpye kṛṣṇavatyā purī smṛtā |
nirmāsā nāma tatraiva vasante rākṣasāḥ sadā || 10-166 ||
tasyāpyuttaradigbhāge purī hemā sukhāvatī |
mitro vasati tatraiva vahubhṛtya janāvṛtaḥ || 10-167 ||
tasyapyuntarato hemād gāndhārī nāma viśrutā |
vasanti tatra gāndharvā divyakanyāsamāvṛtā || 10-168 ||
daśakoṭisahasrāṇi teṣāṃ saṃkhyā prakīrtitāḥ |
bhūtānāṃ siddhasenā tu varuṇasyaiva dakṣiṇe || 10-169 ||
61a g] hemasaṃjñā vasūnāṃ ca varuṇasya tu cottare |
tasyaicottarabhāge tu nāmnā siddhavatī purī || 10-170 ||
sarvavidyādharāṇāṃ tu sā purī parikīrtitam |
vāyo dakṣiṇato devi siddhā nāma purī smṛtāḥ || 10-171 ||
vasanti kinnarāstatra purairhemārkasaprabhaiḥ |
vāyoḥ pūrveṇa gāndhārī hemā citra rathasya tu || 10-172 ||
112b kh] gandharvarājamakhyasya divyagandharvavādināḥ |
tatra bhagavatī sākṣāt saptasvaravibhūṣitāḥ || 10-173 ||
44a] grāmatrayaparīdhānā jātimekhalamaṇḍitā |
mūrcchanottaracitrāṃgī nānābharaṇakalodayā || 10-174 ||
lakṣaṇairvyañjanopetā madhyamairavaguṇṭhitā |
gandharvairgītamānā sā tatra devī sarasvatī || 10-175 ||
nāradādyaiśca ṛṣibhirnāgakinnarasevitāḥ |
tatra pūrveṇa citrā vai tumvuro nāradasya tu || 10-176 ||
somapaścime pramādā guhyakānāṃ prakīrtitā |
pūrveṇaiva tu somasya nāmnā vaitravatī purī || 10-177 ||
sarvadhātu mayā citrā kuverasya mahātmanaḥ |
ṣaḍviṃśatisahasraistu koṭibhiḥ parivāritā || 10-178 ||
yakṣāṇāmīśvaraḥ śrīmāṃ āste bhogairanuttamaiḥ |
tasyāḥ pūrve śubhā nāma jambūnadamayī purī || 10-179 ||
tatra vai karmadevāstu devatvaṃ karmaṇā gatāḥ |
paścime vasarājasya viṣṇoḥ śriyavatī purī || 10-180 ||
tatrāste śrīpati śrīmāṃ atasīpuṣpasannibhaḥ |
śaṃkhacakragadāpāṇi pītavāso janārdanaḥ || 10-181 ||
īśasya dakṣiṇe bhāge nāmnā padmavatī purī |
mahāpadmopaviṣṭasya padmamālādharasya tu || 10-182 ||
padmapatrāyatākṣasya brahmaṇaḥ padmajātmanaḥ |
tasya dakṣiṇato devi nāmnā kāmamukhā purī || 10-183 ||
aśvinau tatra deveśi tathā dhanvantari sthitaḥ |
113a kh] uttare cāmarāvatyāṃ mahāmegheti viśrutā || 10-184 ||
vināyakānāṃ ca purī vasati saṃprakalpitāḥ |
daśakoṭisahasrāṇi teṣāṃ vīryavatāṃ śubhām || 10-185 ||
vināyakānāṃ dīptānāṃ agnijvalitatejasām |
asurāṇāṃ vadhārthārya svāṃguṣṭhā nirmitā mayā || 10-186 ||
evaṃvidhairadhorddhaṃ vai meruḥ puravarairvṛtaḥ |
puryaśca yā mayākhyātā meroścaiva samaṃtataḥ || 10-187 ||
purakoṭisahasraistu sarvāstāṃ saṃbhṛtā priye |
sarvaiśvaryasusaṃpūrṇā sarvaratnasamujjvalāḥ || 10-188 ||
divyastriyaiḥ samākīrṇerdivyadevaiḥ samākulāḥ |
ānandaṃ satataṃ devi devānāṃ ca pure pure || 10-189 ||
vimāne nagarā rāmaiścatvarodyānamaṇḍapaiḥ |
61b g] chatradhvajapatākaiśca gajavājisamākulaiḥ || 10-190 ||
dundubhirnandiśabdaiśca śaṃkhakāhalanisvanaiḥ |
nṛtyagītaravākīrṇairdevānāṃ mandire sadā || 10-191 ||
iṣṭāpūrtaratā devi ye narā puṇyabhārate |
tryamvakaśca sadā pūjyā meruṃ gacchanti te narāḥ || 10-192 ||
gaṅgātoyena saṃsiktā krīḍanti surasattamāḥ |

devyuvāca

kathaṃ gaṅgā samutpannā surasiddhanamaskṛtā || 10-193 ||
kathayasva prasādena samāsātsurasattama |

bhairava uvāca

gaṅgāyāśca samutpattiṃ kathayāmi sulocane || 10-194 ||
jaganmātā mahādevi mama patnī parā hi ṣā |
113b kh] mama netrodakaṃ hyetat pavitraṃ kāraṇe ratām || 10-195 ||
purādāsīn mahādevi airiṣyā saṃsthitā karaiḥ |
devā sadiṣu saṃsthasya netraumetchāditatvayā || 10-196 ||
punarudghāṭitau netro jaganmāte tvayā purā |
mama netra śravattoyaṃ tvayīyāṃgulibhirdaśaiḥ || 10-197 ||
daśaiva nisṛtā gaṅgā kapālāvaraṇe mama |
saptaiva saṃsthitāstatra ekā viṣṇupure sthitā || 10-198 ||
dvitīyā brahmaloke tu tṛtīyā saptalokagā |
svarge caiva punaḥ sā vai saṃsthitā somamaṇḍale || 10-199 ||
somāścaiva vinisṛtya punākāśe vyavasthitāḥ |
tato haṃ saṃstuto devairbrahmaviṣṇupuraḥ sarau || 10-200 ||
gaṅgā nadī mahāpuṇyā martyānāṃ hitakāmyayā |
avatārya mahādeva martyaloke visarjaya || 10-201 ||
tato mayā sureśāni uktāsatvaparājitā |
lokānāṃ ca hitārthāya āgaccha surasundari || 10-202 ||
44b] āgatya mama mūrdhne tu merumūrdhni purāgatāḥ |
tasmānnirgatya deveśi caturdikṣu divaṃ gatāḥ || 10-203 ||
pūrve śvetā samuddiṣṭā suvaṃkṣā dakṣiṇena tu |
sunandā paścime bhāge bhadra somā tu cottare || 10-204 ||
mandaraṃ tu mahādevi gandhamādanasaṃjñakam |
vipulaṃ ca svapārśvaṃ ca pūrvādyāścottararāntikāḥ || 10-205 || viṣkāṃbhā ca samākhyātā varṇāścaiva nibodha me |
śita caiva haridrābhaṃ nīlaṃ dāḍimasaprabham || 10-206 ||
114a kh] prāgviṣkāṃbhāt samīpe tu nāmnā caitra? rathaṃ vanam |
tatrāruṇodakaṃ nāma taḍāgaṃ padmamaṇḍitam || 10-207 ||
gandhamādanasāmīpye nandanaṃ tu mahāvanam |
tasya madhyāmbuje cchannaṃ mānasaṃ tu sarottamām || 10-208 ||
vipulasya samīpe tu vaibhājaṃ tu mahāvanam |
śitodaṃ tasya madhye tu taḍāgaṃ vipulodakam || 10-209 ||
vanaṃ pitṛvanaṃ nāma supārśvasya samīpataḥ |
tatrānte tu mahābhadraṃ taḍāgaṃ sumanoramam || 10-210 ||
62a g] kalpadrumāśca catvāraḥ kathayāmi nibodhataḥ |
mandare ca kadambaṃ syānmastake tu vyavasthitaḥ || 10-211 ||
sahasrayojanāyāmaṃ śākhāpañca śatohabhavam? |
puṣpaiḥ kumbha pramāṇaistu bhrājate tat supuṣpitam || 10-212 ||
tatpramāṇā smṛtā jambū gandhamādanamūrddhani |
tasyāḥ phalasamūhotthaṃ rasaṃ yadamṛtopamam || 10-213 ||
tena jambū nadī jātāpriye vegavatī bhṛṣam |
meruṃ pradakṣiṇīkṛtya jambūmūle viśeṣataḥ || 10-214 ||
tatsaṃparkāt samutpanna kanakaṃ devabhū * *? |
tena jambūnadaṃ śubhraṃ jāyate bhūdharīttamam || 10-215 ||
tatra vṛkṣā latā gulmāḥ pakṣiṇaścā padādayaḥ |
jambūnadamayā sarve ye cānye tatra vāsinaḥ || 10-216 ||
vipule tu mahāśvattha ketumāleti viśrutaḥ |
indreṇepyatra surāṃjitvā ketumālāvalaṃvitāḥ || 10-217 ||
tenāsau ketu māleti khyātaḥ siddhaniṣevitaḥ |
nyagrodhastu su *?pāra śvetat tulyaḥ parikīrtitaḥ || 10-218 ||
114b kh] anekaguṇasaṃpanno meruḥ khyātaḥ samāsataḥ |
pāraścatastathā deśāḥ kathayāmi samāsataḥ || 10-219 ||
merumadhyā caturdikṣu lakṣārddhaṃ tu samāsataḥ |
lavaṇodadhiparyantaṃ jambūdvīpā prakīrtitam || 10-220 ||
parvatāntaritāstatra nava bhedā bhavanti hi |
dakṣiṇe caiva digbhāge trayo jñeyā mahidharāḥ || 10-221 ||
niṣadho hemakūṭaśca himavāniti te trayaḥ |
uttareṇāpi merostu nīlaḥ śvetastriśṛṅgavān || 10-222 ||
pūrvapaścāyatāstvete ṣaḍeva tu mahīdharāḥ |
nīlaśca niṣadhaścaiva lakṣāyāmau prakīrtitau || 10-223 ||
śvetaśca hemakūṭaśca sahasranavati smṛtau |
himavāñchṛgavāṃścaiva sahasrāśītimānataḥ || 10-224 ||
lavaṇodadhiparyantāḥ sahasradvayavistarāḥ |
kailāsayukto himavānṛtriśṛṅgāśca sagārudhiḥ || 10-225 ||
śikhicandrikasaṃkāśaśitaśvete virājite |
nīlaratna mayo nīlo niṣadhaḥ padmarāgajaḥ || 10-226 ||
sauvarṇo hemakūṭaśca himarājo himavāniti |
pūrveṇa mālyavān meroḥ pūrvatastu vijānataḥ || 10-227 ||
catustriṃśasahasrāṇi yojanā surasundari |
yāmyottarāyanaṃ bhāti sahasraṃ tasya vistaram || 10-228 || 
tathaivāparadigbhāge taṃtulyā gandhamādanaḥ |
catustriṃśatsahasrāṇi yojanānā samucchrayaḥ || 10-229 ||
115a kh] caturdikṣugatā mero dvau dvau maryādaparvatau |
45a] jaṭharo devakūṭaśca pūrvabhāge vijānatā || 10-230 ||
kailāso himavāṃścaiva dakṣabhāge vijānataḥ |
niṣadhaḥ pāriyātraśca apareṇa vijānatā || 10-231 ||
62b g] jārudhiḥ śṛṃgavāṃścaiva uttareṇa dvayaṃ sthitam | 
meroḥ samaṃ tato ramyaṃ ilāvṛttaṃ prakīrtitam || 10-232 || 
adhastāśccakravāṃ daṇḍa navasāhasra vistṛtam | 
yojanānāṃ caturdikṣu caturasraṃ samaṃ tataḥ || 10-233 || 
nātapo bhānujastatra naiva somasya raśmayaḥ |
prabhavanti hi lokānāṃ mero bhāsāt prabhāṣitam || 10-234 ||
pratyagnāmbujapatrābhā janāścātīvakomalāḥ |
jambūphalarasāhārā jarāmṛtyuvivarjitāḥ || 10-235 ||
trayodaśābdasahasrāṇi teṣāmāyuḥ prakīrtitaḥ |
devagandharvasiddhāśca ṛṣayotha vināyakāḥ || 10-236 ||
gaṇamātṛbhaginyaśca vetālo rākṣasādayaḥ |
evamādirasaṃkhyātai vṛttaṃ caiva ilāvṛtam || 10-237 ||
gandhamādana vāruṇyā samudrasya ca pūrvataḥ |
ketumāleti vikhyātaṃ varṣaṃ sarvaguṇottamam || 10-238 ||
nīlotpaladalaśyāmā janāstatra suśobhanāḥ | 
panasasya rasaṃ pītvā jīvatyayutasaṃkhyayā || 10-239 ||
jāyanto varddhamānaśca aśoko haritaparvataḥ |
viśālakambalaḥ kṛṣṇastatra sapta kulādayaḥ || 10-240 ||
115b kh] mālyavattasya pūrveṇa samudrasyāpareṇa tu |
bhadrāśvaṃ nāmato varṣaṃ tatrāpyevāyutāyuṣaḥ || 10-241 ||
janāścandrapratīkāśā kadambaphalabhojanāḥ |
kaurañjaśvetaparṇaśca nīlomālāgrakastathā || 10-242 ||
padmaṃ caiva samākhyātaṃ tatra pañca kulādayaḥ |
dvātriṃśatisahasrāṇi pūrvapaścāyatau smṛtau || 10-243 ||
catustriṃśatsahasrāṇi dakṣiṇottaramāyatau |
varṣau dvau tu samākhyātau kathyante cottarasthitāḥ || 10-244 ||
kuravo nāmalokāstu nāmavṛkṣaphalāsinaḥ |
tridaśābdasahasrāṇi jīvante sthirayauvanaḥ || 10-245 ||
yugmaṃ yugmaṃ prasūyante viyogabhayavarjitāḥ |
śyāmā puṣpanibhā śnigdhāḥ surūpāḥ purusastriyaḥ || 10-246 ||
tatra ratnamayīṃ bhūmi hemavālukayācitaḥ |
navayojanasāhasraṃ dhanvākāraṃ prakīrtitam || 10-247 ||
sūryakāntendukānte ca dvau tatra kulaparvatau |
kalpavṛkṣaḥ kururnāma tatraiva kusumojjvalaḥ || 10-248 ||
tasyanāmnā tu taṃ jñeyaṃ kuruvarṣa suśobhanam |
tasya cottaradigbhāge praviśya lavaṇodadhiḥ || 10-249 ||
pañcayojanasāhasraṃ candradvīpaṃ prakīrtitam |
yojanānāṃ pramāṇaṃ tu sahasraṃ tasya vistaram || 10-250 ||
tathā vāyavyadigbhāge praviśya lavaṇodadhim |
yojanānāṃ sahasrāṇi catvāryevaṃ varānane || 10-251 ||
daśayojanasāhasraṃ dvīpaṃ tatra prakīrtitam |
116a kh] bhadrākaramiti jñeyaṃ sarvakāmasamanvitam || 10-252 ||
63a g] ayutāyuṣo janāstatra divyāmṛtaphalāśinaḥ |
candrākhyepyayutaṃ cāyuḥ jīvante phalabhojanāḥ || 10-253 ||
śuklapītakavarṇābhāva virujā sthirayauvanā |
 paścime tu diśābhāge sahasrāṇi daśaiva tu || 10-254 ||
praviśya yojanānāṃ tu dvīpaṃ kumudasaṃjñakam |

nārikerarasaṃ pītvā jantavo muditocchavāḥ || 10-256 ||

tatrāyutāyuṣo lokāḥ kharjūraphalabhojanāḥ |
kṛṣṇāñjananibhā snigdhāḥ sarvabhogasamanvitāḥ || 10-258 ||
dakṣiṇe tu diśābhāge praviśaivāyutaṃ jalam |
45b] pañcayojanasāhasrāṃ siṃhadvīpaprakīrtitām || 10-259 ||

116b kh] janā kuṃkumasaṃkāśā viyogabhayavarjitāḥ || 10-263 ||

63b g] varṣaṃ kiṃpuruṣaṃ nāma tatra hemanibhā janāḥ || 10-273 ||

117a kh] navaiva tu sahasrāṇi vistāraṃ tasya kīrtitam || 10-274 ||

64a g] aṃgadvīpo yamākhyaśca malayaḥ śaṃkhasaṃjñakaḥ |

64b g] bhadrāścaṃ ketumālaṃ ca ramyakastu hiraṇmayaḥ || 10-314 ||

46b] nābheḥ putro mahāvīryo ṛṣabho dharmatatparaḥ || 10-316 ||

122a kh] caturdaśa samāsena pravakṣyāmyanupūrvaśaḥ |

saṃhārakramayogena śodhanīyā śivādhvare || 10-387 ||
paśu pakṣi mṛgaścaiva tathā jñeyā śarīsṛpāḥ |
sthāvaraṃ pañcamaṃ caiva ṣaṣṭhaṃ mānuṣayonijam || 10-388 ||
tiryakpañcavidhaṃ khyātaṃ etat saṃsāramaṇḍalam |
caturdaśavidhaṃ caiva bhūrloke tu viśodhayet || 10-389 ||
ātmā saṃsarate hyatra māyādyavanigocare |
saṃsāraḥ procyate tasmāt paryaṭeta itistvitaḥ ? || 10-390 ||
sukha duḥkhaṃ tathā mohaṃ bhukte caivādhvamadhyagaḥ |
vandhatrayasamākrānto vāmaśaktyā tvadhiṣṭhitaḥ || 10-391 ||
īśvareṇa nimittena sṛṣṭisaṃhāravartmani |
atha madhye punaścātmā yojayeta śubhāśubham || 10-392 ||
adhva madhye tathā pāśā jñeyāścānantakoṭayaḥ |
pradhānaguṇabhedena yāvaścānāśritaṃ padam || 10-393 ||
tasmād devi vijānīyādadhvavandhasya kāraṇam |
caturdaśavidhaṃ yacca proktaṃ saṃsāramaṇḍalam || 10-394 ||
66b g] tasya bhedāṃ hyanantā ca bhidyante karmabhedataḥ |
karmavalā hyanantāśca karmī cānādikārakaḥ || 10-395 ||
ātmānā vandhatetmānaṃ mocedātmānamātmani |
kośakāro yathā kīṭa ātmānaṃ veṣṭayeddṛḍham || 10-396 ||
na co veṣṭayituṃ śaktyo ātmānaṃ ca punaryathā |
122b kh] tathā saṃsāriṇaḥ sarve vaddhā saṃsāravandhanaiḥ || 10-397 ||
na ca mocayituṃ śaktyāḥ paśavaḥ pāśavandhanāt |
svayamevātmanātmānaṃ evaṃ nekṣayate śivaḥ || 10-398 ||
śivaśaktinipātācca mucyate pāśabandhanāt |
anyathā naiva prānāti svarūpaṃ yatsunirmalam || 10-399 ||
yattat svabhijanaṃ śubhaṃ anaupamyamanāmayam |
mohitā malamohena vaddhāḥ karmakalādinā || 10-400 ||
nirgūḍhāstatra tiṣṭhante kāṣṭhe vahniryathā tathā |
uddhatastu?yathāvahnirmanthakavaśagā sphuṭam || 10-401 ||
48a] satsvarūpaṃ vijānāti śāśvataṃ yatsunirmalam |
anyeṣāmapi jantūnāṃ timiropahate kṣaṇām || 10-402 ||
prakāśayati dravyāṇi hatvā timira raśmibhiḥ |
tathātmā tu vijānāti yatsurūpamanādimam || 10-403 ||
manthakasya vadhād devi nānyathā tu kathañcana |
manthakastu yadā devi svayameva sadāśivaḥ || 10-404 ||
ācāryatanumāsthāya sadā cānugrahe sthitaḥ |
netrā manthānavajjñeyā adhvāna maraṇī yathā || 10-405 ||
vāgīśī yonisaṃsthā tu dhūmo jñeyo malādivat |
ātmā vai vahnivatprokta udbodhita paraḥ śivaḥ || 10-406 ||
udbodhito yathā vahni nirmalotīva bhāsvaraḥ |
na bhūyo viṣate kāṣṭhe tathātmā dhvāna uddhṛtaḥ || 10-407 ||
malakarma kalādyaiśca nirmukto vigataklamaḥ |
123a kh] tatrasthopi na vadhyeta patro vahni sunirmalaḥ || 10-408 ||
rasavahni samāyogāt tāmrakālikavadyathā |
viśliṣṭā caiva tajjñaistu hematvaṃ ca tathā bhavet || 10-409 ||
na bhūyastāmratāṃ yāti kalpakoṭi śatairapi |
rasavanmantraśaktistu kriyā jñeyā tu vahnivat || 10-410 ||
tajjñeścaiva śivojñeye ācāryatanuvigrahaḥ |
ātmānaṃ hemavajjñeyo malo jñeyastu kālikā || 10-411 ||
mantradravyakriyā yogād vatyādi?karaṇai priye |
guruṇā * dvidātmā vai nirmalīkṛta suvrate || 10-412 ||
na bhūyaḥ samalo yāti śivatvaṃ yāti nirmalam |
evaṃ jñātvā varārohe dīkṣā kāryā yathā purā || 10-413 ||
śodhayenmukhyapāśāni yānyuktāni purā mayā |
guṇabhūtāstu ye pāśāstepi śuddhyanti suvrate || 10-414 ||
caturdaśavidhāścaiva yaduktaṃ tu mayā purā |
saṃsāramaṇḍalāṃ devi śodhayedaraṇītale || 10-415 ||
67a g] tadvakṣyāmi kramāt sarvaṃ yathā śodhya śivādhvare |
brāhmyādi yāva brāhmantaṃ paripāṭyā viśodhayet || 10-416 ||
vahyaṃ praśodhya saumyaṃ ca vendra gāndharvayakṣarāṭ |
paiśācaṃ kramaśaḥ śodhyaṃ tiryatvaṃ caiva śodhayat || 10-417 ||
saptachadasthā varāṇāṃ vāsukiṃ ca sarīsṛpāṃ |
pakṣiṇā garuḍaṃ śodhya mṛgāṇāṃ siṃhameva ca || 10-418 ||
paśūnāṃ caiva goyoniṃ mānuṣaṃ caiva śodhayet |
antyajaṃ śūdraviṭ kṣatrabrāhmaṇaṃ tamapaścimam || 10-419 ||
123b kh] bījeśe na tu deveśe adhikārāṇi śodhayet |
daśāhutiprayogena antyajā brāhmaṇāvadhiḥ || 10-420 ||
brāhmaṇasyādhikārāṇi catvāriṃśāṣṭa homayet |
garbhaṃ puṃsavanaṃ caiva sīmantaṃ jātakarma ca || 10-421 ||
nāma niṣkramaṇaṃ caiva annaprāśana cūḍakam |
ete caiva varārohe śodhyāstvaṣṭau prakīrtitāḥ || 10-422 ||
etairnivartitairdevi tataḥ saṃjāyate dvijaḥ |
navamaṃ vratavandhastu tadyāgā parikīrtitam || 10-423 ||
aṅgāni saṃpravakṣyāmi yathāvadanupūrvaśaḥ |
mekhalā daṇḍa kāṣṭhaṃ ca ajinaṃ tryāyuṣaṃ tathā || 10-424 ||
saṃdhyā caivāgnikāryaṃ ca bhikṣā vai saptamī viduḥ |
mekhalā vrata saptaite brahmacārī samācaret || 10-425 ||
niryantakāśca voddhavyā dīkṣākāle varānane |
bhūteṣāṃ pāśupatyaṃ ca gaṇaṃ caiva gaṇeśvaram || 10-426 ||
unmattakāsidhāraṃ ca ghṛtesaṃ saptama smṛtam |
saptaite tu samākhyātā vratā vai brahmacāriṇe || 10-427 ||
caryāvratāstu boddhavyā aṅgabhūtā prakīrtitāḥ |
etaiśca sahitaṃ hyekaṃ navamaṃ vratavandhanam || 10-428 ||
tasyāntarbhūtaboddhavyāḥ kathitāstava suvrate |
48b] tato vedavratāṃ guhya catvāryeva na saṃśayaḥ || 10-429 ||
iṣṭikā parvaṇī caiva bhautikā saumikā tathā |
vratacarāstu catvārī brahmacāri niyanti yā || 10-430 ||
catvāryevaṃ vratā hyevaṃ hutvaitaistu varānane |
124a kh] trayodaśaṃ tadvijānīyāt tato vai vedabhājanaḥ || 10-431 ||
ato bhavati godānaṃ taccaturdaśamaṃ priye |
snātakodvāhayedbhāryāṃ jñānasiddhā kumārikām || 10-432 ||
kṛtvā darbhamayī patnī tayā saha yajet kratum |
jñeyaṃ taṃpaṃ ca daśamaṃ tataḥ pākamakhāni * || 10-433 ||
naimittikāni yānyāhuḥ pravakṣyāmi samāsataḥ |
aṣṭakā parvaṇī sārddhaṃ śrāvaṇyāgrāyaṇī tathā || 10-434 ||
67b g] caitrī cāśvayujī caiva sapta pākamakhā smṛtāḥ |
etaiḥ saha vijānīyādvāviṃśatparisaṃkhyayā || 10-435 ||
ādheyaṃ cāgni hotraṃ ca daśaṃ caiva mataḥ param |
paurṇamāsī tato jñeyā cāturmāsyāṃ tathaiva ca || 10-436 ||
paśuvandhaṃ samuddiṣṭaṃ sautrāmaṇimataḥ param |
haviryajñāḥ samākhyātāḥ saptaivaṃ pavanāni tu || 10-437 ||
etaiḥ saha vijānīyāḥ saṃsāraikonaviṃśati |
agniṣṭomotyagniṣṭoma uktā ṣoḍaśa eva ca || 10-438 ||
vājapeyotirātraśca āptuyāmaśca saptamaḥ |
somasaṃsthā samākhyātā ṣaṭtriṃśatsamudāhṛtāḥ || 10-439 ||
hiraṇyapādaprathamoraṇyaguhyo hiraṇyaghaḥ |
hiraṇyamedro hiraṇyābhirhiraṇyagarbha eva ca || 10-440 ||
hiraṇyasrota hiraṇyatvaṃ hiraṇyākṣastathaiva ca |
hiraṇyajihvo vikhyāto daśa yajña prakīrtitāḥ || 10-441 ||
śatena tu vṛtā hyete ekaikastu vijānataḥ |
124b kh] etaiḥ sarvai sahasropi śuddhate saptaviṃśamam || 10-442 ||
tatośvamedhavikhyāta etai sarvairgṛhī bhavet |
aṣṭatriṃśatu mantrastu vānaṃ pradhva?mato bhavet || 10-443 ||
paribhrājyaṃ tatonteṣṭiṃ kṛtvā vrāhmyā padopnuyāt |
catvāriṃśati saṃskārā etaiḥ saha vijānatā || 10-444 ||
āṣṭātmaguṇānyeva pravakṣyāmi samāsataḥ |
dayā ca sarvabhūteṣu kṣāntiścaivānusūyatā || 10-445 ||
śaucaṃ caivamanāyāsaṃ maṅgalaṃ ca ataḥ param |
akāryaṇyamaspṛheti aṣṭau cātmaguṇā smṛtāḥ || 10-446 ||
catvāriṃśāṣṭasaṃskārāḥ kathitāstu samāsataḥ |
etaiḥ śuddhaistu śuddhyante asaṃkhyāyepi suvrate || 10-447 ||
atonteṣṭi gaṇitvā tu guṇāmāpādayecchiśoḥ |
pañcapañcāhutībhiśca brāhmairaṃgairanukramāt || 10-448 ||
tilairghṛtena hutvā vai ūrddhaṃ kṛtvā niyojayet |
ūrddhaśabdena cāśuddhaṃ yatkarma parikīrtitam || 10-449 ||
tasminsaṃyojanaṃ kāryaṃ na cānyatra vidhīyate |
tasmāccoddharaṇaṃ kāryaṃ na cāpi vapanaṃ kvacit || 10-450 ||
yadatra paripāṭyā tu karme tatra niyojayet |
tatoṇimādi māyādya ādikṣāntairanuranukramāt || 10-451 ||
hutvā tilaprayogeṇa bhogārthaṃ naiva mātmanaḥ |
ūrddhaśabdena taṃ jñeyaṃ yadbhūloke samāsṛtam || 10-452 ||
tasminyuktasya sarvatvaṃ na cānyasmiṃ kadācana |
anitṛpte kathaṃ yogo yāvatkarma na bhujyate || 10-453 ||
125a kh] tasmāttayogaśabdena tasminkarmaikacintatā |
nirvartite mahādevi tato nivṛti homayet || 10-454 ||
68a g] śivenāṣṭahutaṃ hutvā tataśca bhuvanādhipāḥ |
bhuvanāntarivāsāśca bhuvanānāṃ yathā kramam || 10-455 ||
homaṃ caiva prakartavyaṃ tato viśleṣacchedanam |
pūrṇā caiva tatoddhāraṃ tatsthaṃ ca tadanukramāt || 10-456 ||
49a] prāyaścittaṃ tato hutvā nyūnā jñānārthahetave |
evamādikrameṇaiva śivāntaṃ pariśodhayet || 10-457 ||
bhūrlokaṃ tu samākhyātaṃ bhuvarlokaṃ nibodha me |
bhūpṛṣṭādyāvadāditya lakṣāmekaṃ pramāṇataḥ || 10-458 ||
daśa vāyu yathā madhye ayutāyutamantarāḥ |
prathame vāyupathe meghāḥ kathayāmi yathā sthitāḥ || 10-459 ||
pañcāśadyojanādūrddhaṃ ritad vināmamārutaḥ |
ye vivarddhayate puṣṭimauṣadhīnāṃ valaṃ tathā || 10-460 ||
vahante ca mahī sarvāmāpyāyati sa cāvaham |
tasmādūrddhvaśataṃ sārddhaṃ yojanānāṃ parisphuṭam || 10-461 ||
vivahatsaṃsavai vāyuṃ manujānāṃ sukhāvahaḥ |
haratīti sa vai gandhaṃ tena gandhāpaha smṛtaḥ || 10-462 ||
śataikaṃ yojanānāṃ ca praceto nāma rūpataḥ |
sa vai nāśayate vṛkṣāḥ kadācit saṃpravartate || 10-463 ||
agni pracetaso nāma tenaiva hasatiṣṭhati |
yadā dahati vesmādi tadāsau saṃpravartate || 10-464 ||
sukhī vasati sāmudre udakenopaśāmyate |
125b kh] yojanānāṃ śatādūrddhvaṃ senavidvāyurucyate || 10-465 ||
vidyunmattāmukhā meghā vasantyasmiṃstu mārute |
te mahyā kośamātreṇa tiṣṭhanti varṣayanti ca || 10-466 ||
tugho nāma mahāvāyurasmād gṛhya pramāṇataḥ |
yojanānāṃ śatādūrddhva meghāḥ satvavahānatra tu || 10-466 ||
matsya maṇḍūka kūrmā ca vaṣanti durdinaṃ ca te |
pañcāśayojanādūrddhvaṃ vāyuroga prakīrtitaḥ || 10-468 ||
tasmin roga gadā nāma varṣante ca viṣodakam |
tenopasargā bhavanti marakaḥ sarvadehinām || 10-469 ||
vālāṃ vasanti vai dīpyāḥ piśācā skandhadehajāḥ |
viṃśatkoṭisahasrāṇi skandasyānucarā smṛtāḥ || 10-470 ||
te caiva divyakusumairarcayanti bhavātmajam |
amoghaninadā ghorā mahādevasamudbhavāḥ || 10-471 ||
triṃśatkoṭisahasrāṇi tasminvāyoḥ pratiṣṭhitāḥ |
haranti ye kṛtaṃ karma narāṇāmakṛtātmanām || 10-472 ||
vajrāṅgo nāma vai vāyuḥ pañcāśadyojane sthitaḥ |
tasminnupalakā nāma meghāścopalavarṣiṇaḥ || 10-473 ||
tatra raudrā mahāghorā mātaṅgā krūrakarmiṇaḥ |
bhinnāñjananibhāghorāstāpanā nāma viśrutāḥ || 10-474 ||
vidyādharāṇāmadhamā manaḥ pavanaveginaḥ |
avidyāḥ puruṣā ye tu śmaśānavetālasādhanaiḥ || 10-475 ||
sādhayitvā tataste vai tasminvāyoḥ pratiṣṭhitāḥ |
126a kh] pañcāśadyojanaiścaivaṃ tato vai vaidyutonilaḥ || 10-476 ||
meghāśca vaidyutā tasmin nivasanti tu vidyute |
asanirvāyu saṃkṣobhāt teṣṭaugha jāyate mahān || 10-477 ||
sasiddhāpsarasastasminvāsavena suprayojitāḥ |
tiṣṭhante ca sadā tasmin pṛthivī puramālinīm || 10-478 ||
tridhāgnau tu jale vātha saṃgrāme vā nivartakāḥ | 
gograhe vandimokṣe vā mriyante puruṣottamāḥ || 10-479 ||
vrajanti te tataścorddhaṃ vimānairmaṇicitritaiḥ |
patākādīpikākīrṇe divyaghaṇṭānināditaiḥ || 10-480 ||
strīsahasrasamākīrṇairvimānaistaṃ nayanti tāḥ |
tatorddhayojanānāṃ tu pañcāśairvahatenilaḥ || 10-481 ||
tasmin puṣṭi vahāmeghāḥ puṣṭiṃ varṣanti dehinām |
tatraiva ca mahādevi siddhā vai saṃpratiṣṭhitāḥ || 10-482 ||
gorocanāṃjanādīni pādukāntāni yāni ca |
sādhayitvā mahātmāna siddhā ye kāmarūpiṇaḥ || 10-483 ||
te vasanti mahātmāno divyasiddhā vyavasthitāḥ |
samvarte rogadā meghā rogodakapravarṣiṇaḥ || 10-484 ||
pañcāśadyojanaiste vai vidyādharagaṇā sthitāḥ |
49b] daśatriṃśacca koṭyā vai divyābharaṇabhūṣitāḥ || 10-485 ||
divyagandhānuliptāste divyasragdāmabhūṣitāḥ |
visaṃvartonilo nāma pañcāśadyojanopari || 10-486 ||
tasmiṃ krodhodakā nāma meghā vai saṃpratiṣṭhitāḥ |
126b kh] krodharāvahatāsteṣāṃ saṃgrāmavahulāśca te || 10-487 ||
rājñaḥ kṣayakaraṃ caiva prajāyā kṣayameva ca |
varṣamāṇāḥ prakurvanti yadā varṣanti te ghanāḥ || 10-488 ||
khaḍgaguḍikāsiddhā ye vasanti subhoginaḥ |
āgneyā dhūmajā meghāḥśītādurdinajā smṛtāḥ || 10-489 ||
samāvahaṃ vāyurete āśritā śatayojane |
tatra gāndharvakuśalā gāndharvasahadharmiṇaḥ || 10-490 ||
vatsavīṇā vidhijñasya pakṣiṇaḥkāmarūpiṇaḥ |
brahmajā nāma vai meghā brahmaniśvāsasaṃbhavāḥ || 10-491 ||
upariṣṭādyojanaśatādurja yasyopari sthitaḥ |
tatra vai durjayā nāma indrasya parira kṣiṇaḥ? || 10-492 ||
mahāvīryavalopetā daśakoṭyaḥ prakīrtitāḥ |
durjayasyaśatenorddha yojanānāṃ priyaṃ vrate || 10-493 ||
parāvahamato vāyu meghāsyuḥ padmasaṃbhavāḥ |
śakreṇa pakṣāyecchinnāḥ parvatānāṃ mahātmanām || 10-494 ||
parāvahastāṃ vahate devājñānuvidhāyinaḥ |
tasmin vā yugatānāma gandharvā gaganālayāḥ || 10-495 ||
69a g] ekādaśaśca koṭyā vai teṣāṃ tu samudāhṛtāḥ |
parā brahmasahasrāṇāṃ tṛtayena tatopari || 10-496 ||
athānyamāvahaṃ vāyuṃ jīmūtā nāma te ghanāḥ |
tasmiñjīmūtakā nāma vidyādharagaṇā daśaḥ || 10-497 ||
devā vai dhvani mārgeṇa sahasra kṛtayena tu |
127a kh] āvāhaṃ tu tato vāyuṃ yacadroṇāḥsamāśritāḥ || 10-498 ||
tasmindroṇaḥ samākhyātā meghānāṃ parirakṣakāḥ |
hitārthaṃ tu prajānāṃ ca nirmitāstu mayā purā || 10-499 ||
tadevādhvānamārgeṇa yojanānāṃ varānane |
upariṣṭāt kalāpotthā samvartā nāma vai ghanāḥ || 10-500 ||
mahāparivaho nāma vāyusteṣāṃ samāśrayaḥ |
prathame vāyupathevaṃ meghā vai vāyavaiḥ saha || 10-501 ||
siddhāstvadhipatiścaiva kathitā meghacāriṇaḥ |
dvitīye ca pathe jñeyā agnikanyāśca mātaraḥ || 10-502 ||
tā vasanti guṇopetā rudraśaktyā tvadhiṣṭhitāḥ |
tṛtīye tu pathe caiva vasante siddhacāraṇāḥ || 10-503 ||
svakarmabhogasaṃsiddhāḥ sarvasiddhairadhiṣṭhitāḥ |
caturthe tu pathe caiva vasante devatāyudhāḥ || 10-504 ||
nānāprakārāstānyeva pañcayonigatāni ca |
pañcame tu pathe devi vasantyairāvatādayaḥ || 10-505 ||
airāvato janaścaiva vāmanaśca mahāgajaḥ |
apratīka karīndraśca puṣpadantastathaiva ca || 10-506 ||
kumudaḥpuṇḍarīkasya sarvabhautikacāṣṭamaḥ |
diggaja iti vikhyāto sudiśā suvyavasthitāḥ || 10-507 ||
ṣaṣṭhe vāyupathe devi pakṣirājā mahāvalaḥ |
garuḍeti sa vikhyāto durjayotīvavīryavān || 10-508 ||
saptame vyomagaṅgā tu nānājalacarākulāḥ |
127b kh] divyāmṛtajalā puṇyā tridhā sā pathisaṃsthitāḥ || 10-509 ||
bhrāntā sa nabhaso madhye samantātparimaṇḍalāḥ |
ākāśagaṅgā prathitā devānāṃ śatatotmayā || 10-510 ||
puṣpamāle ca sā bhāti nabho saiva śirastathā |
tatra siddhairmahābhāgairvidyādharagaṇaistathā || 10-511 ||
gandharvāpsarasobhiśca sādhyaiścaiva marudgaṇaiḥ |
rudrairvasubhirādityaiḥ pitṛdevamahāṛṣaiḥ || 10-512 ||
rakṣobhiguhyakaiścaiva divyastutiparāyaṇaiḥ | 
50a] yogibhiśca japadbhiśca gāyabhiśca mahātmabhiḥ || 10-513 ||
nṛtyadbhirvatmamānaiśca divyadundubhinisvanaiḥ |
bherī mṛdaṅgaghoṣaiśca vallakīnāṃthanisvanaiḥ || 10-514 ||
vatsavāditratādaiśca manovāyu samīritaiḥ |
vaiḍūryanālakamalairhemapatraiḥ sugandhibhiḥ || 10-515 ||
keśarau padmarāgotthairmahācakrapramāṇibhiḥ |
69b g] nṛtyantī vasaricchreṣṭhā vimānaśatamaṇḍitāḥ || 10-516 ||
maṇḍitā ca vanairdivyairdharmadhārā mahānadī |
mama netrādviniṣkrāntā kriyāśaktiparā hi sā || 10-517 ||
mahāmandākinīdevī tridaśaiḥ paryupāsitā |
mahāvimānakoṭībhinirantaramavasthitāḥ || 10-518 ||
