52b] sarvo rudrastathā bhīmo bhavaścāgrastathaiva ca |
mahādevastatheśāno rudralokādhipāstvime || 10-650 ||
brahmaloke tathā brahmā viṣṇurvai vaiṣṇave pure |
rudraloke sthitā rudraḥ sarveṣāṃ nāyaka smṛtāḥ || 10-651 ||
kālāgnerdaṇḍaparyantamaṣṭānavatikoṭayaḥ |
yojanānāṃ varārohe etadhvāna varṇitam || 10-652 ||
kaṭāhaṃ tu tataścordhve brahmāṇḍasya varānane |
koṭiyojanamānena ghaṭākāreṇa saṃsthitaḥ || 10-653 ||
pañcāśatkoṭayordhvaṃ tu bhū pṛṣṭvā tu varānane |
pañcāśañca adho bhāge yojanānāṃ samaṃtataḥ || 10-654 ||
73a g] evaṃ koṭiśataṃ devi pārthivaṃ tatvamucyate |
śatarudrāvadhi jñeyaṃ sauvarṇavartulaṃ samam || 10-655 ||
vajrasārādapi sāraṃ durbhedyaṃ tridaśairapi |
huṃphaḍāntaprayogeṇa bhedayeta varānane || 10-656 ||
śatarudrā mato vakṣye samāsena kṛṣodari |
134a kh] daśadaśakrameṇaiva daśa dikṣu samaṃtataḥ || 10-657 ||
pūrvādikramayogena kathayāmyanupūrvaśaḥ |
kapālīśo hyajo buddho vajradeha pramardanaḥ || 10-658 ||
vibhūtiravyayaḥ śāstā pinākī tridaśādhipaḥ |
indrasya valamākramya prabhuśaktisamanvitāḥ || 10-659 ||
vivicaranti mahādevā indrarājā supūjitāḥ |
agnirudro hutāśī ca piṅgalaḥ khādako haraḥ || 10-670 ||
jvalano dahano vabhrurbhasmāntaka kṣayāntakaḥ |
agnestu balamākramya prabhuśaktisamanvitāḥ || 10-671 ||
vicaranti mahādevā agnirājā supūjitāḥ |
yāmyo mṛtyu haro dhātā vidhātā kartṛkālinaḥ || 10-672 ||
saṃyoktā ca viyoktā ca dharmādharmapratistathā |
yamasya balamākramya prabhuśaktisamanvitāḥ || 10-673 ||
vicaranti mahādevā yamarājā supūjitāḥ |
naiṛto māraṇo hantā krūradṛṣṭi bhayānakāḥ || 10-674 ||
ūrddhvasepho virūpākṣo dhūmro lohitadaṃṣṭravān |
naiṛtervalamākramya prabhuśaktisamanvitāḥ || 10-675 ||
vicaranti mahādevā naiṛtena supūjitāḥ |
valo hyatibalaścaiva pāśahasto mahāvalaḥ || 10-676 ||
śvetotha jayabhadraśca dīrghavāhu jalāntagaḥ |
meghanādī sunādī ca samāsāt parikīrtitāḥ || 10-677 ||
vāruṇyā valamākramyaprabhuśaktisamanvitāḥ |
vicaranti mahādevā varuṇena supūjitāḥ || 10-678 ||
134b kh] śīghro laghurvāyuvegasūkṣmastīkṣṇa kṣayāntakaḥ |
pañcāntakaḥ pañcaśikhaḥ kapardī meghavāhanaḥ || 10-679 ||
vāyostu valamākramya prabhuśaktisamanvitāḥ |
vicaranti mahādevā vāyurājā supūjitāḥ || 10-680 ||
nidhīśo rūpavāndhanyaḥ saumyadeho jaṭādharaḥ |
lakṣmīratnadharaḥ kāmī prasādaśca prakāśakaḥ || 10-681 ||
somasya valamākramya prabhuśaktisamanvitāḥ |
vicaranti mahādevāḥ somarājā supūjitāḥ || 10-682 ||
vidyādhipotha sarvajña jñānabhug vedapāragaḥ |
sureśaḥ sarvajyeṣṭhaśca bhūtapālo valipriyaḥ || 10-683 ||
73b g] īśānānumatā deva īśaśaktisamanvitāḥ |
vicaranti mahādevā īśaśaktyāstvadhiṣṭhitāḥ || 10-684 ||
vṛṣo vṛṣadharonanta krodhino mārutāsanaḥ |
grasano durvaseraśca phaṇīndra vajradaṃṣṭravān || 10-685 ||
viṣṇostu valamākramya prabhuśaktisamanvitāḥ |
vicaranti mahādevā anantena supūjitāḥ || 10-686 ||
śaṃbhurvibhugaṇādhyakṣa rūpākṣaśca tridaśeśvaraḥ |
53a] saṃvāhaśca vivāhaśca nabholipsustrilocanaḥ || 10-687 ||
brahmaṇo palamākramya prabhuśaktisamanvitāḥ | 
vicaranti mahādevā brahmaṇā nu supūjitāḥ || 10-688 ||
ekaikasya sahasraṃ tu parivāre vidhīyate |
śatarudrā iti khyātā brahmāṇḍaṃ vyāpya saṃsthitā || 10-689 ||
135a kh] asaṃkhyātā sahasrāṇi ye tvadhorddhvadiśi sthitāḥ |
svacchanda viśvagā devāḥ kalpe manvantareṣvapi || 10-690 ||
ekaikamadhipaṃ caiva kathayāmi varānane |
sthito vai pūrvatoṇḍasya śveto vai nāma nāmataḥ || 10-691 ||
rudrāṇāṃ tu daśairyukto mahāvīryaparākramaiḥ |
dīptibhiśca mahātīvrairmayūkhairiva bhāskaraḥ || 10-692 || 
āgneyyāmapi saṃkāśo vaidyuto nāma viśrutaḥ |
sopi vindyācchatai rudraiḥ samantāt parivāritaḥ || 10-693 ||
yāmyeṇḍasya mahākālo yugāntānalasaṃprabhāḥ |
śatarudrairvṛto devi tiṣṭhatemitavikramaiḥ || 10-694 ||
naiṛtyāṃ vikaṭo nāma śatena parivāritaḥ |
tiṣṭhate ca mahātejodvitīya iva bhāskaraḥ || 10-695 ||
paścimeṇḍasya yo rudro mahāvīryeti viśrutaḥ |
śatarudrairvṛtaḥ sopi tiṣṭhate mitavikramaiḥ || 10-696 ||
vāyavyāṃ ca digaṇḍasya vāyuvego mahābalaḥ |
śatena so vṛtaḥ śrīmāntiṣṭhate ca mahābalaḥ || 10-697 ||
subhadranāmottarataḥ śatena parivāritaḥ |
mahāyogabalopetastatra tiṣṭhati vīryavān || 10-698 ||
vidyāmayo nāmarudra īśānyāṃ vai pratiṣṭhitaḥ |
śatarudrairvṛtaścaiva upariṣṭhāttu vāhurāṭ || 10-699 ||
mahāyogavalopetastiṣṭhate tatra vikramaiḥ |
atha kālāgnirudronya sthitastatra dvitīyakaḥ || 10-700 ||
samāvṛto rudraśatai sthitastatra varānane |
śatena so vṛto rudro mayukhairiva bhāskaraḥ || 10-701 ||
135b kh] upariṣṭhādvīrabhadro vṛto rudrairmahātmabhiḥ |
ekādaśairmahākāyai rudrakrodhasamudbhavaiḥ || 10-702 ||
evaṃ caiva mahātmāna ekaikena śatena tu |
daśaite veṣṭhitā devi śatarudraistu suvrate || 10-703 ||
74a g] eṣāmapi asaṃkhyeyāḥ parivārā mahātmanaḥ |
āvṛtyāṇḍaṃ sthitā hyete madhuṃ madhu karā iva || 10-704 ||
kadambakusumaṃ yadvatkeśaraiḥ parivāritam |
parivāritaṃ tathā hyaṇḍaṃ rudrairamitavikramaiḥ || 10-705 ||
bhuvanaistoraṇāddālairnānāratna vicitritaiḥ |
jāmbūnadamaiścitraiḥ samantat samalaṃkṛtam || 10-706 ||
divyanārībhirākīrṇaṃ sarvakāmasamanvitam |
brahmāṇḍaisa samākhyātā pāśajālaṃ balānvitam || 10-707 ||
janmavyādhi jarāmṛtyumahodadhipariplutaṃ |
guṇatrayamalacchannaṃ nānāmatisamākulam || 10-708 ||
paśujñānaparibhrāntaṃ gatitrayasamākulam |
anyanta eva gatayaḥ sarveṣāmeva vādinām || 10-709 ||
parāpara vibhāgaṃ tu naiva jānanti mohitāḥ |
haimāṇḍaṃ tu purā sṛṣṭaṃ kṣayātmā bhuvanākṛti || 10-710 ||
īśamāyāsamāviṣṭamātmavargasya bhūtaye |
eta daṇḍaṃ samākhyātaṃ samāsānna tu vistarāt || 10-711 ||
vimānasaṃkhyāsaṃyuktaṃ nānābhogaphalodayam |
atopariṣṭāt tatvāni āpādyādiśivāntikāḥ || 10-712 ||
uttarottarayogena daśadhā saṃsthitāni tu |
136a kh] prakṛtyantāni tatvāni śatadhā ca tatopari || 10-713 ||
māyāntāni vijānīyādupariṣṭhāt sahasradhā |
tasyorddhve lakṣadhā jñeyā tatvam īśvarasaṃjñakam || 10-714 ||
53b] sādākhyaṃ koṭiparyantaṃ vindunādāntamūrddhataḥ |
yojanānāṃ tu vṛndaṃ vai śaktiṃ vyāpya vyavasthitā || 10-715 ||
vyāpinī sarvamadhvānāṃ vyāpayitvā vyavasthitāḥ |
ameyaṃ paratastasyā sūkṣmaṃ sarvagataṃ śivam || 10-716 ||
bhuvanānāṃ saṃpravakṣyāmi āpatatvādyanukramāt |
ākāraṃ bhuvanaṃ caiva bhuvanānāṃ ca vistaram || 10-717 ||
yanna dṛṣṭaṃ paśujñānaiḥ kupathebhrānti dṛṣṭibhiḥ |
yatra sākṣādgatairdṛṣṭaṃ na ca vai pañcarātrakaiḥ || 10-718 ||
svabhāvacintakairvāpi na caiva karmavādibhiḥ |
nāpi saṃśayavādaiśca na syādvādasumohitaiḥ || 10-719 ||
na bhūtavādibhiścaiva nāpi syāllaukikairapi | 
na cātmacintakairvāpi na ca tarkapravādibhiḥ || 10-720 || 
na ca vaiśeṣikairvāpi ṣaṭpadārthaparāyaṇaiḥ |
na cāpi nyāyavādaiśca hetudṛṣṭāntavādibhiḥ || 10-721 ||
nāpyekajanmavādairvā na cāpyanekavādibhiḥ |
na mūrtavādairlokairvā aparijñānamaiśvaram || 10-722 ||
ityevamādayonyepi vādināṃ tu śatatrayam |
74b g] triṣaṣṭiradhikā cānye vādināṃ bhrāntacetasam || 10-723 ||
ajñānatimirāndhānāṃ unmīlanakarottamam |
saṃsārapaṅka magnānāṃ naurivottaraṇaṃ param || 10-724 ||
136b kh] mahātimira andhānāṃ tamonudamidaṃ param |
parameśvaramukhādgīrṇaṃ parāyā prāptamadbhutam || 10-725 ||
jñānāmṛtamidaṃ divyaṃ nānābhuvanavistaram |
śṛ.uṣvekamanā devi vicitrākāramadbhutam || 10-726 || 
anantaṃ bhuvanasaṃhātamavyucchedād vyavasthitam | 
madhukośajālavacca tathā bhūnicayākṛtiḥ || 10-727 || 
mīnaśaṃkhakulākāraṃ dāḍimībījavatsthitam | 
kadambakeśarākāraṃ pūraṇaṃ tu samuhavat || 10-728 ||
mahāsenāvāsanibhaṃ vane tarusamūhavat |
nirantaramanantāni bhuvanāni varānane || 10-729 ||
nānākārāṇi citrāṇi sarvaratnamayāni ca |
parimaṇḍalāni dīrghāṇi arddhacandrākṛtīni ca || 10-730 || 
dhūmākṛtāni cānyāni nandāvartakṛtāni | 
padmākṛtīni cānyāni śaradabhrākṛtīni ca || 10-731 || 
parvatākṛtirūpāṇi gajayūthākṛtīni ca |
śirākṛtīni cānyāni jvālārūpākṛtīni ca || 10-732 || 
mahāvimānarūpāṇi śūlikākṛtivanti ca |
murajākṛti cānyāni tryasākṛti purāṇi ca || 10-733 ||
mahāpuruṣarūpāṇi śataśṛṅgānvitāni ca |
sahasraśṛṅgāvartāni tathānyāni varānane || 10-734 ||
koṭiśṛṅgāṇi cānyāni asaṃkhyaśikharāṇi ca |
vṛtāni caturasrāṇi trikoṇyāni parāṇi ca || 10-735 ||
divyacitrapatākāni divyaghaṇṭadhvajāni ca |
137a kh] bherīnādasvarādyāni divyagītadhvajāni ca || 10-736 ||
divyadundubhinādyāni mahāvīṇāsvanāni ca |
nānāvāditraghoṣāṇi bhuvanāni ca sarvadā || 10-737 ||
śuktāni sphaṭikābhāni padmarāgacchatrīṇi ca |
candrakāntasavarṇāni muktādāmanibhāni ca || 10-738 ||
lākṣārasasavarṇāni kānicid varavarṇini |
inndragopakavarṇāni indranīlamayāni ca || 10-739 ||
bālādityeva varṇāni padmagarbhanibhāni ca |
candraprabhāni cānyāni candrakoṭinibhāni ca || 10-740 ||
madhyāhnārddha savarṇāni sūryakoṭinibhāni ca | 
aśokastavakābhāni haritālanibhāni ca || 10-741 || 
nīlotpalasavarṇāni vidyutpuñjanibhāni ca | 
śakracāpasavarṇāni gokṣīradhavalāni ca || 10-742 || 
sindūrakaṃ kamā?bhāni gorocana nibhāni ca | 
54a] taptahemasarṇāni nirdhūmāgni nibhāni ca || 10-743 || 
śaṃkhapāṇḍuravarṇānikānicid bhuvanāni ca | 
75a g] nānāvarṇāni cānyāni nānārupākṛtīni ca || 10-744 ||
ityeṣāṃ parato devi vyāpakaṃ paramaṃ padam |
aprameyamasaṅkhyeyamagamyaṃ sarvavādinām || 10-745 ||
vinā prasādādīśasya jñānametanna labhyate |
nāpi bhāvanā bhavati dīkṣyāmaprāpya dehinaḥ || 10-746 ||
137b kh] yadā tu kāraṇācchakti bhavennirvāṇakārikā |
śivecchā tatpatatyāsau dīkṣānamayī śubhāḥ || 10-747 ||
mantrayogātmikā divyā tadā mokṣaṃ vrajet paśuḥ |
nānyathā mokṣamāyānti jñānairjñānaśatairapi || 10-748 ||
yasya prakāśitaḥ sarva śivenānantarūpiṇām |
sa eva mokṣa brajate śivaḥśarvo maheśvaraḥ || 10-749 ||
tenedaṃ jñāna mukhyaṃ tu purā proktaṃ mayā tava |
saṃsārapaṅkamagnānāṃ naurivottaraṇaṃ param || 10-750 ||
mahāmāyā janātītamajñāna paśugocaram |
anantāt paramaṃ kṣobhyaṃ subodhaṃ parameśvaram || 10-751 ||
parameśamukhodgīrṇa yanmayā prāptamadbhutam |
vakṣye jñānāmṛtaṃ divyaṃ śṛṇuṣvekamanā priye || 10-752 ||
ūrddhvaṃ vai brahmaṇāṇḍasya bhuvanaikādaśa smṛtāḥ |
ekādaśānā rudrāṇāṃ yugāntāgnisamatviṣāṃ || 10-753 ||
atorddhvaṃ tu paraṃ devyāḥ kathayāmi varānane |
indranīlamayaṃ divyaṃ samantātparimaṇḍalam || 10-754 ||
tasminbhagavatī devī bhadrakālī vyavasthitāḥ |
vasatendīvaraśyāmā snigdhakaṃ kuṣṭhasaprabhā || 10-755 ||
sūryamaṇḍalarūpābhyāṃ maṇḍalābhyāṃ malaṃkṛtā |
paurṇamāsyāṃ yathā sandhyā candrikābhyāṃ virājate || 10-756 ||
rājate ca tathā hārastanābhyāmantare sthitaḥ |
asitāñjananā ābhyāṃ madhye srotavahā iva || 10-757 ||
caturbhiśca dhṛte pīṭhe siṃhe ramitavikramaiḥ |
138a kh] sarvavajramayā divye divyaratnavibhūṣite || 10-758 ||
āsane suprabhe devī jātyaṃjana samaprabhā |
śukle himavataḥ śṛṅge nīlameghe ca saṃsthitāḥ || 10-759 ||

pitamalyāṃ śubhavatī śarvarī vāruṇodaye || 10-760 || 
tṛtīyaṃ nayanaṃ tasya lalāṭasthaṃ virājate |
udayantamivādityo raśmijvālā vibhūṣitaḥ || 10-761 ||
ucchatenātapatreṇa sāśvetena virājate |
kṛṣṇameghoparisthena candreṇaiva tu śarvarī || 10-762 ||
koṭikoṭisahasreṇa strīṇāṃ tu parivāritāḥ |
75b g] āvṛtā candra lekhetra nakṣatraistu nabhasthale || 10-763 || 
kumudotpalavarṇāśca hemaśyāmāśca yoṣitaḥ | 
priyaṅgukalikā śyāmāścandragauryaḥ suyauvanāḥ || 10-764 ||
padmāvadātarūpiṇyā pīnaśroṇīpayayodharāḥ |
bhāvābhāvavidhijñāśca gītanṛtyaviśāradāḥ || 10-765 ||
veṇuvīṇāmṛdaṅgādyaiḥ kāṃsavāditranisvanaiḥ |
upāsya mānāstaṃ devīṃ ramante tatra yoṣitaḥ || 10-766 ||
evaṃ tu vijayaṃ nāma bhuvanaṃ tu varānane |
yā durgeti smṛtā loke brahmāṇḍodaravartinām || 10-767 ||
yā vijñeyāstapasā pūrvamāsādya parameśvaram |
avatāritā vadhārthāya mahiṣasya mahātmanaḥ || 10-768 ||
yena caikena śṛṅgeṇa bhagavān himavān giriḥ |
138b kh] śuṣkapatramiva kṣipto bhagavatyā vināśitaḥ || 10-769 ||
sā taṃ vināya devī tamaḥ sūrya ivotthitā |
54b] sā devī sarvadevānāṃ nāmai rūpaistu tiṣṭhati || 10-770 ||
yogamātā pratisvecchā kumārī lokabhāvanī |
acintyā vāprameyā ca anyatra paripaṭhyate || 10-771 ||
visṇunā sahitā devī kalpe kalpe punaḥ punaḥ |
bhaginitvena sāyāti nāmarūpaviparyaye || 10-772 ||
manvantare manvantare tathaiva ca yuge yuge |
rakṣaṇārthaṃ tu lokānāṃ māteva hitakāriṇī || 10-773 ||
ityākhyā tu bhuvanaṃ vijayaṃ nāmanāmataḥ |
tadbhaktāstatra gacchanti tasya maṇḍaladīkṣitāḥ || 10-774 ||
na caitat tapasā prāpyaṃ na yajñairbhūridakṣiṇaiḥ |
na dānairvividhairvāpi śakyaṃ prāptuṃ varānane || 10-775 ||
pramādādevadevasya śaśāṅkāṅkita maulinaḥ |
dīkṣā prāpyā tu prāpyeta maṇḍalaṃ cakravartinām || 10-776 ||
nirbījadīkṣā nirvāṇaṃ dadāti khalu devinām |
yā muktidīkṣā paramā vidhivat parikalpitāḥ || 10-777 ||
vidyeśāvaraṇād dīkṣā pāvatī kriyate nṛṇām |
tāvatī gatimāpnoti bhuvaneṣu varānane || 10-778 ||
bhuvaneśāśva sthānāśca saṃsthitāstu yathākramam |
kathayiṣyāmi vaḥ sarvaṃ śṛṇuṣveka manā priye || 10-779 ||
bhadrakālyā parā devo rudrakrodhasamudbhavaḥ |
139a kh] koṭimātreṇa vīreśe yugāntāgnisamaprabhaḥ || 10-780 ||
yugāntāmvudavṛndasya garjitadhvaninisvanaḥ |
śatavāhu mahātejo divyābharaṇabhūṣitaḥ || 10-781 ||
śaradendīvaraśyāmā nīlāñjanasamadyuti |
76a g] śikhikaṇṭhanibhaḥ kiñcit kiñcidāpāṇḍulohitaḥ || 10-782 ||
vāsajīmūtavarṇābha atasīpuṣpasaprabhaḥ |
indranīlanibhaṃ kiñcit kiñcidbhṛṅganibhākṛtiḥ || 10-783 ||
jātyāñjananibhākārai rudraikādaśasaṃyutaḥ |
yutaḥ koṭisahasreṇa rudrāṇāṃ ca mahātmanāṃ || 10-784 ||
bhuvanaṃ tasya devasya vijayaṃ nāma viśrutā |
indranīlanibhaṃ divyaṃ sarvavajramayaṃ mahat || 10-785 ||
daśakoṭisahasrāṇi rudrāṇāṃ varavarṇini |
antarbhuvanasaṃghātai ranyaiśca parivāritaḥ || 10-786 ||
nīlotpaladalaḥśyāmaiḥ śikhikaṇṭhanibhaistathā |
rudrairdivyairmahāvīryaiḥ samantāt parivāritam || 10-787 ||
stutibhirmaṅgalairgītairnṛtyavāditravāditaiḥ |
praṇavairveṇuvīṇābhirbherī jhillīrigomukhaiḥ || 10-788 ||
paṭalaiḥ kāhalaiścaiva śaṅkhadundubhipīlukaiḥ |
mardalaistad darībhiśca tālakairmurajaistathā || 10-789 ||
mondakāhalaṭaṃgaiśca ] timilādraghaṭādibhiḥ |
vāditrairvallitaistālai rauṭalairmukhamardalaiḥ || 10-790 ||
ucchuṣṭairaṭṭahāsaiśca narditaiḥ kurditaistathā |

bhūtairbhūtagaṇai rudrairjalpanaiḥ paṭhanaistathā || 10-791 ||
dhyāyadbhiśca japadbhiśca dhāvadbhirviṣṭitaistathā | mayūrakokilārāvairmuñcadbhiśca tathā paraiḥ || 10-792 ||
nānārutavikāraiśca vikurvadbhirmahātmabhiḥ |
āvṛtaistairmahātejairmayūkhairiva bhāskaraḥ || 10-793 ||
gajavaktrairaśvavaktraiḥ siṃhavaktraiḥ śukānanaiḥ |
gokarṇairgomukhaiścānyairdvīpi ṛkṣamukhaistathā || 10-794 ||
vyāghravānaravaktraiśca bhagavānparyupāsyate |
vīrabhadro mahātejo yugāntāgnisamaprabhaḥ || 10-795 ||
āsanaṃ tasya devasya sarvavajramayaṃ mahat |
śatasahasravistīrṇaṃ caturasrānalaprabhām || 10-796 ||
rājate cāpi siṃhaiśca vṛtaṃ bhīmaparākramaiḥ |
tatra te puṇyakarmāṇo ye smaranti maheśvaram || 10-797 ||
55a] jale maruṣu cāgnau vā śiraścchedena vā punaḥ |
ye yānti ceśvaraṃ bodhaṃ vīrabhadrapuraṃ yayuḥ || 10-798 ||
bhuvanasyāsya deveśi uparyāvaraṇaṃ mahat |
āpomayṃ ghanamayaṃ śakracāpamiva sthitaḥ || 10-790 ||
tatrāsane mahātmāno aṅguṣṭhāgrapramāṇataḥ |
76b g] tatra yojanakoṭirvai viṣkambhādūrddhva mucyate || 10-799 ||
tiryak triguṇavistāre āpyaprāvaraṇo priye |
āvṛtaṃ tena tatsarvaṃ mahāmbuda visarpiṇā || 10-800 ||
rudrāṇḍamiti tatkhyātaṃ rudraloketi cānyataḥ |
vīrabhadraniketeti bhadrakālpālayeti ca || 10-801 ||
trayodaśabhiranyaiśca bhuvanai rūpaśobhitam |
nānārudragaṇairdivyairnirantaramalaṅkṛtam || 10-802 ||
ayaṃ vai vīrabhadrasya brahmāṇḍasadṛśaṃ priye |
ataḥ paraṃ pravakṣyāmi dharitryā bhuvanaṃ mahat || 10-803 ||
dhātrī yasminbhagavatī dharāloka sanātanā |
hairaṇyamatulaṃ prāptā ārādhayati saṃsthitāḥ || 10-804 ||
cakravartitvamānaiśca vahubhiḥ parivāritam |
āvṛtaṃ bhūtasaṃghaiśca ātmajaistatparāyaṇaiḥ || 10-805 ||
divyagītaninādādyairvāditraśatanisvanaiḥ |
anantabhuvanasaṅghātai rudrāṇāṃ parivāritaiḥ || 10-806 ||
bhuvanasyāsya madhye tu udayādityasannibham |
raktotpalanibhaṃ divyaṃ aśokastavakacchavi || 10-807 ||
padmarāgamayairdivyaiḥ prāsādāneka bhūmikaiḥ |
tasya madhye bhagavatī dharitrī lokadhāriṇī || 10-808 ||
raktasrajasya mālā yā lamvayā nityabhūṣitāḥ |
candrārkamaṇḍalābhe ca kapolatalamaṇḍitāḥ || 10-809 ||
pītahaimāṃśukacchavyā mahāhāravibhūṣitāḥ |
sahasrayojanāyāme kūrmapṛṣṭhe vyavasthitāḥ || 10-810 ||
caturvaktrā sāṣṭabhujā divyābharaṇabhūṣitāḥ |
rūpayauvanasaṃpannā nṛtyagītaviśāradā || 10-811 ||
parivārya upāsanti divyā vai mānasāstriyaḥ |
triṃśatkoṭyastu tā sā vairdivyābharaṇabhūṣitāḥ || 10-812 ||
utpāditāstāḥ sarveṇa tasyārthe hitamicchatāḥ |
taptajāmbūnadanibhā divyābharaṇabhūṣitāḥ || 10-813 ||
ucchṛtenātapatreṇa dhriyamāṇena mūrddhani |
sthitaḥ pūrve mahātejā meru yosau mahāgiriḥ || 10-814 ||
upāsyamānastāṃ devīṃ tatrāste tu nagādhipaḥ |
nīlotpaladalaḥ śyāmo nīlajīmūtasannibhaḥ || 10-815 ||
nīlonāmamahāśailaḥ pītavāso mahādyutiḥ |
atikrāntena rūpeṇa kaiṭabhāririvāparaḥ || 10-816 ||
upāsyamāno divyābhirnāgarībhirnagādhipaḥ |
77a g] tasyottare candranibho mukuṭālaṅkārabhūṣitaḥ || 10-817 ||
 śvetātapatri rūpasvī śveto nāma mahāgiriḥ |
tasyottareṇa sūryābho mukuṭālaṅkārabhuṣitāḥ || 10-818 ||
pītāmbaradharaḥ śrīmānkusumāṃstra ivā paraḥ |
dakṣiṇenāpi vakṣyāmi śṛṇuṣvevahitā priye || 10-819 ||
candrāvadā ta dīptaujo divyābharaṇabhūṣitaḥ |
śuklāmbaradharaḥ śrīmān pitāmaha ivāparaḥ || 10-820 ||
taptahemapratīkāśo divyābharaṇabhīṣitaḥ |
pītāmbaradharaḥ śrīmān pītamālyānulepanaḥ || 10-821 ||
hemakūṭo mahātejā tejasā iva śaṅkaraḥ |
rarājabhagavāñcchaila sandhyāvṛta ivāṃ śumān || 10-822 ||
pāṇḍurābhapratīkāśaḥ śaṅkhagokṣīrapāṇḍuraḥ |
śuklāmbaradharaḥ śrīmān divyakuṇḍalabhūṣitaḥ || 10-823 ||
ātapatreṇa mahata priyamāṇena mūrddhani |
rājā uttarataścaiva dvitīyamiva devarāṭ || 10-824 ||
indragopakasaṃkāśaḥ paścime gandhamādanaḥ |
55b] raktāmvara dharaḥ śrīmānnyastādṛtha ivāṃ śumān || 10-825 ||
śuddhasphaṭikasaṃkāśaṃ śuklāmbaradharaḥ srajaḥ |
kirīṭī kuṇḍalī śrīmān mālyavānpūrvataḥ sthitaḥ || 10-826 ||
ityevamādibhiścānyaiḥ parvataiḥ parivāritāḥ |
lokālokavasānaiśca tathā vai kulaparvataiḥ || 10-827 ||
divyarūpadharā devī tanurvai pārameśvarī |
dhāraṇā gandhamātreṇa prāṇaṃ tyaktvā tu yoginaḥ || 10-828 ||
sayāti tāvatī mūrtirdharitryā paramā tanum |
ataḥ parataraṃ devi sāmudraṃ bhuvanaṃ mahat || 10-829 ||
sarvavajramayaṃ divyaṃ divyāścaryaśatairyutam |
meghotpalasamacchāyaṃ sarvataḥ parimaṇḍalam || 10-830 ||
madhye tu bhuvanasyāsya maṇḍalaṃ candrasannibham |
śatayojanasāhasraṃ samantāt parimaṇḍalam || 10-831 ||
tasya madhye tu puruṣo rukmavarṇo mahādyutiḥ |
kirīṭī kuṇḍalī sragvī divyābharaṇabhūṣitaḥ || 10-832 ||
āpayonistu bhagavān vāruṇīṃ paramāṃ tanum |
tato devaṃ mahātmānaṃ parivārya samaṃ tataḥ || 10-833 ||
rūpayauvanasaṃpannā paryupāsanti nityaśaḥ |
śuklāmbaradharā devī śuklagandhātulepanā || 10-834 ||
śuklayajñopavītāṅgī śuklahāropaśobhitā |
77b g] śukle naivātapatreṇa dhriyamāṇena mūrddhani || 10-835 ||
gaṅgā cottaratastasya sthitā vai paramāṃ tanum |
nīlāmbaradharā devī nīlagandhānulepanā || 10-836 ||
nīlasragdāma kaṇṭheca yamunā tasya dakṣiṇe |
evamādyā mahānadyaḥ parivārya mahādyutiḥ || 10-837 ||
samudrāṣṭaka deveśi svanadībhirvarāṅganaiḥ |
upāsyate sadā bhaktyā vāruṇīṃ paramāṃ tanum || 10-838 ||
nānāsarāṃśi tīrthāni tadbhaktā ye ca devatāḥ |
satanmātre tu deveśai kṛtvā caiva tu dhāraṇām || 10-839 ||
āpayoniparā mātrā vāruṇīṃ paramāṃ tanum |
ataḥ paraṃ pravakṣyāmi bhuvanaṃ varavarṇini || 10-840 ||
śrīniketana deveśi padmagarbheti viśrutaḥ |
vimānaśatasaṅghātairnirantaramavasthitaiḥ || 10-841 ||
śobhitaṃ bbuvanīśaiśca rudrai rudragaṇaistathā |
sarobhirmānasairdivyairdīrghikābhiśca śobhitam || 10-842 ||
rathacakrapramāṇaiśca maṇikāñcanamaṇḍitaiḥ |
vaiḍūryanālakamalairdivyagandhasugandhibhiḥ || 10-843 ||
mṛdubhiḥ kāntimadbhiśca candramaṇḍalasannibhaiḥ |
upaśobhita vicitraiśca vikacai vajrakeśaraiḥ || 10-844 ||
udyānairvividhaiścāpi nānāvihagapuritaiḥ |
nānākāmapadairvṛkṣaiḥ sarvatra samalaṅkṛtam || 10-845 ||
nānāmaṇimayaiścitrai krīḍā śailaiśca mānasaiḥ |
mānasābhiśca nārībhi rūpayauvanaśālibhiḥ || 10-846 ||
bhāvābhāvavidhijñābhirdivyastrībhiralaṅkṛtam |
vicitrairmaṇipadmaiśca nānākāntisamaprabhaiḥ || 10-847 ||
upaśobhita rājendraiśca stutimaṅgalavādibhiḥ |
gāyadbhirnṛtyamānaiśca divyastrībhiḥ samākulam || 10-848 ||
tasmiṃstu bhuvane ramye padmagarbhaṃsamaprabhaiḥ |
śaradendunibhaṃ divyaṃ maṇḍalaṃ raśmisaṃkulam || 10-849 ||
tasya madhye bhagavatī śrī svayaṃ lokabhāvinā |
catuṣkoṭisahasrāṇi yā kāntiḥ parivartyate || 10-850 ||
ekatra yugavat tejā tejasā tu vibhāvyate |
nirvāṇamivākāntā sarvānandamanoharāḥ || 10-851 ||
rūpiṇī paramā devī mūrtiravyabhicāriṇī |
śatayojanavistīrṇe uditādityasaprabhaiḥ || 10-852 ||
candrakāntimaye padme vajrakeśarakarṇike |
56a] koṭipatre mahādivye gandhasparśaguṇānvite || 10-853 ||
padmāsane bhagavatī padmagarbhasamaprabhā |
upariṣṭāṃ tu sā nityaṃ vibhūtyā paramāparāḥ || 10-854 ||
78a g] mahāratnaiśca sragdāmaṃ pralamvasurasā śubham |
sphuranmayūkhavalane kapolatalamaṇḍale || 10-855 ||
sūryamaṇḍalasaṃkāśe dhārayantī ca kuṇḍale |
sphuranmukhasaṃghātāṃ rasānāṃ sā tu bibhratī || 10-856 ||
hemā bhāpītavasanā mahāhāravibhūṣitāḥ |
candrābhenātapatreṇa dhriyamāṇena mūrddhani || 10-857 ||
upagīyamānagandharvairmāna vai rudrasaṃbhavaiḥ |
parivāritā bhagavatī sātaṃtuḥ parameśvarī || 10-858 || 
yā prāptā tapasārādhya viṣṇunā prabhaviṣṇunā |
dattā ce śvetapītena viyogārura sivāsinī || 10-859 || 
arddhena sā bhagavatī viṣṇoraṅgepratiṣṭhitāḥ |
pādenendrasya devasya pādārddhena divi sthitā || 10-860 || 
tadarddhena punardevi pārthive supratiṣṭhitāḥ |
tadarddhena manuṣyā vai yā sthitā vyāpya mūrtibhiḥ || 10-861 ||
surūpā ca arūpā ca dvidhā sā parikīrtitāḥ |
acalā sā tanu sūkṣmā akṣobhyā tatra tiṣṭhati || 10-862 ||
rudrakrīḍāvatāreṣu prayāgādiṣu suvrate |
śrīgirau ca viśeṣeṇa mṛtadabhuvanaṃ vrajet || 10-863 ||
itarevyā satrabhoge iyaṃ sā gaditā priyā |
prāpyatāmīdṛśīṃ devī naiśvaryamaṇimādikam || 10-864 ||
bhūtvā tu sāṣṭadhā divyā deveṣvapi ca tiṣṭhati |
siddhānāmapi sā devī uttamā siddhirucyate || 10-865 ||
yasyāste tārakādyaistu saṃgrāmastridaśeśvaraiḥ |
kutaṃ ghoramasaṃkhyeyaṃ tāṃ śriyāmabhikāṅkṣibhiḥ || 10-866 ||
asaṃkhyeyāśca saṃgrāmāṃ kṛtā vai cakravartibhiḥ |
sāmvandhaścaiva muktānāmabuddhānāṃ parā smṛtāḥ || 10-867 ||
ata ūrddhaṃ pravakṣyāmi bhuvanaṃ tu nibodhataḥ |
sārasvatamiti khyātaṃ gāndharvamiti ca smṛtam || 10-868 ||
padmagarbhe pure vāpi koṭimātreṇa suvrate |
yojanānāṃ samākhyātaṃ pramāṇena samaṃtataḥ || 10-869 ||
sarvaratnamayaṃ divyaṃ sarvaiśvaryasamanvitam |
vimānairvividhākārairnānāratnamayaiḥ śubhaiḥ || 10-870 ||
gandharvairmānuṣaiścaiva gāyadbhiścāpyanekadhā |
nṛtyamānaistathānyaiśca gaṇaistu pārśvagaistathā || 10-871 ||
strībhiḥ surūpiṇībhiśca gandharvaiśca samākulam |
tasya madhye tu deveśi śaraccandranibhaṃ śubham || 10-872 ||
raśmimālākulaṃ dīptaṃ maṇḍalaṃ parimaṇḍalam |
tasya madhye bhagavatī sarasvatī vyavasthitā || 10-873 ||
śaraccandrasahasrasya yā kāntiriti vartate |
78b g] pītāmbaradharā devī padmapatrāyatekṣaṇā || 10-874 ||
nīlotpaladalaśyāmā divyābharaṇabhūṣitāḥ |
hemapaṭṭaparīvārā divyakuṇḍaladhāriṇī || 10-875 ||
urasā ca mahāhāramudvahantī śaśiprabham |
sphuranmayūkhasaṃghāta kuṇḍaladvayabhūṣitāḥ || 10-876 ||
grāmatrayā valī sandhyā saptasvaratanu śubhāḥ |
stanakeśaruhā devī mūrcchanāṅgaruhovahā || 10-877 ||
chandohastalatāpādā srotāvarṇaprabhāvinī |
aṅgulya sandhyayaścaiva lakṣaṇāni varānane || 10-878 ||
āsane parame divye bhūtābhūtagaṇeśvaraiḥ |
sthitā sthitirivābhāti sarvasya jagataḥ śubhāḥ || 10-879 ||
mānasābhiścanārībhirgandharvairmānasaistathā |
hā hā hū hūścitra? rathaṃ tumvarū nāradastathā || 10-880 ||
56b] viśvāvasurviśvaratho divyagītavicakṣaṇāḥ |
saṃyojyā manasātmānaṃ tyaktvā karmaphalaspṛhā || 10-881 ||
na vai śārasvataṃ sthānaṃ prāptā vai surapūjite |
ye cānye dhāraṇāṃ kṛtvā astam vāpi vimucyate || 10-882 ||
tepi śārasvataṃ sthānaṃ prāpnuvanti narottamaḥ |
sā vai sarasvatī devī mūrtirvai pārameśvarī || 10-883 ||
ya sthitā parabhāgena brahmāṇḍodaravartinām |
brahmaloke ca sā devī pādenaikena tiṣṭhati || 10-884 ||
śakre vāpi tadarddhena gandharvaistu tadarddhataḥ |
siddheṣu ca tadarddhena kinnareṣu tadarddhataḥ || 10-885 ||
tadūrdhvena ca nāgeṣu yakṣeṣvarddheṣu vai punaḥ |
piśācebhyaḥ sahasrāṃśānmānuṣeṣu ca tiṣṭhati || 10-886 ||
tāstu taptvā tapaṃ ghoraṃ ārādhya ca pinākinam |
avatāritā sā devī rūpāṇīśvarabhūṣitāḥ || 10-887 ||
svarāṃtusmaratastasyāḥ kalpādau brahmaṇā purā |
svarebhyaḥ smaryamāṇebhyaḥ satī rūpasamanvitā || 10-888 ||
udbhūtā saptasvarebhyo brahmaṇovyaktajanmanaḥ |
svarebhyastu viniṣkrāntāstena sā tu sarasvatī || 10-889 ||
sthitā sā sarvaśāstrāṇi kavīnāṃ kāvya saṃsthitāḥ |
ṛṣīṇāṃ caiva sarveṣāṃ medhābuddhivivarddhanī || 10-890 ||
sarvajñānadharī sāṃ tu sarvajñā devapūjitāḥ |
merorvāyavyabhāge tu puraṃ tasya prakīrtitam || 10-891 ||
idaṃ tu paramaṃ devyā mayā vaḥ parikīrtitam |
bhuvanaṃ śārasvataṃ tu kīrtitā paramātanuḥ || 10-892 ||
atra caivāpatatve tu śṛṇutvaṃ bhuvanottamam |
amareśvaraṃ prabhāṣaṃ ca naimiṣaṃ puṣkaraṃ tathā || 10-893 ||
79a g] āṣāḍhi diṇḍimuṇḍiṃ ca bhārabhūtiṃ malākulam |
guhyasthekamiti khyātaṃ jalāāvaraṇavāsinām || 10-894 ||
tejatatvagatorddhaṃ tu kathayāmi samāsataḥ |
agrestu bhuvanaṃ divyaṃ tannibodha varānane || 10-895 ||
aśokaṃstavakānāṃ tu sarvato dīptimudvaham |
suphullakiṃ śukacchāyāṃ javākusumasannibham || 10-896 ||
bhuvanasyāsya madhye tu uditārkasamaprabham |
parimaṇḍalamāgneyaṃ tejomaṇḍalamucyate || 10-897 ||
tasya madhye tu bhagavāñcchivāgniḥ kāraṇaḥ paraḥ |
yovatīryā tu madhye tu sthito yastu tridhā tridhā || 10-898 ||
vaktre tu dakṣiṇe tasya rudrasya paramātmanaḥ |
sthito jihvāsvarūpeṇa evaṃ bhūrnīlalohitaḥ || 10-899 ||
sa caiva tu punardevaḥ kālāgni svayam īśvaraḥ |
tasya rūpaṃ pravakṣyāmi śṛṇuṣva vahitā priye || 10-900 ||
raktapadmadalacchāyaḥ padmarāgasamadyutiḥ |
raktāmbaradharaḥ śrīmāt raktamālyānulepanaḥ || 10-901 ||
arkabhābhyāṃ kuṇḍalābhyāṃ alaṃkṛtaśubhānanaḥ |
mahāhāreṇa dīptena urasā sau virājate || 10-902 ||
kirīṭī kuṇḍalī dīpto devānana sa ucyate |
sarvavajramaye pīṭhe upariṣṭāt svayaṃ prabhuḥ || 10-903 ||
devāgniriva śailāgre veṇugharṣādivotthitaḥ |
daśakoṭisahasrāṇi āgneyānāṃ gaṇeśvarām || 10-904 ||
dakṣiṇāsyāṃ viniṣkrāntā svasatosya svayaṃbhuvaḥ |
hitāya sarvalokānāṃ rudrā vai sūryavarcasaḥ || 10-905 ||
tena cāgniṃ mahātmānaṃ paryupāsanti nityaśaḥ |
nāryaśca vividhā divyo divyagītavicakṣaṇāḥ || 10-906 ||
gaṇā rudrāśca bhūtāśca kiṃkarāśca sahasraśaḥ |
sarve śivāgniḥ paṭitaḥ sa vai homaiśvare pare || 10-907 ||
agnikāryavidhāneṣu hūyate tadvidaiḥ sadā |
57a] tamagnimīśvaraṃ yāti jñātvā jñeyaṃ tu dhāraṇā || 10-908 ||
ekadhā vahudhā so vai lokān vyāpya vyavasthitaḥ |
satejastejasāṃ yonistasmādyajño divākaraḥ || 10-909 ||
abhivyajya savahudhā kalpavanvantareṣu yaḥ |
bhinnaśca janmabhedaiśca pañcāśadbhiśca bhūtale || 10-910 ||
sa evaṃ kīrtitaṃ samyagāgneyaṃ bhuvanaṃ mahat |
bhuvanādhipāṃ * bhuvanāṃ kathayāmi ataḥ param || 10-911 ||
hariścandraṃ * śrīśailaṃ jalpamamjātikeśvaram |
79b g] mahākālavanaṃ caiva madhyamaṃ ca tathā param || 10-912 ||
kedāraṃ bhairavaṃ caiva samāsāt parikīrtitam |
atiguhyaṃ samākhyātaṃ pūrvādīśānataḥ kramāt || 10-913 ||
atorddhve vādhvāvaraṇaṃ tatrastho vāyumadhyataḥ |
prāṇeti bhuvanaṃ nāmnā vāyostu varavarṇini || 10-914 ||
śaṃkhagokṣīradhavalaṃ himakundendu samprabham |
tasmiṃstu bhuvanairdivyairdivyāścaryaśatairyute || 10-915 ||
madhye tu maṇḍalaṃ divyaṃ śatacandra samaprabham |
raśmibhiścākulaṃ divyaṃ dyotamānaṃ diśo daśaḥ || 10-916 ||
tasya madhye tu vai devo vāyośca paramā tanum |
kirīṭī kuṇḍalī dīptau hārakeyūrabhūṣitaḥ || 10-917 ||
nānābharaṇacitrāṅgaścitramālyānulepanaḥ |
citrāmvaradharaḥ śrīmānmahāhāravibhūṣitaḥ || 10-918 ||
mārutāṃ nāma vai devāṃ daśakoṭyo mahātmanām |
upāsyate mahātmānaṃ vāyumūrti mahādyutim || 10-919 ||
yo vyāpya ca śarīrāṇi ekadhā pañcadhā vibhuḥ |
saptadhā saptadhā caiva tiryagadyaguṇaṃ vibhuḥ || 10-920 ||
samaṇḍena mahādivye vibhātyekā parā tanuḥ |
tamekamekadaśadhā prāṇātmānaṃ tu yoginaḥ || 10-921 ||
dhyātvā tyaktvā tu vai prāṇāṃ kṛtvā tasmiṃstu dhāraṇam |
taṃ viśanti mahātmāno vāyubhūtāśca mūrtayaḥ || 10-922 ||
iti prāṇaṃ samākhyātaṃ bhuvanaṃ tava suvrate |
