srucyāgre tu śikhāṃ kṛtvā hutvā snānamanantaram || 10-1309 ||
sakalīkaraṇaṃ kṛtvā ācāryeṇa varānane |
śiśorapi vidhiṃ kṛtvā śivakuṃbhaṃ samarcayet || 10-1310 ||
bhairavaṃ madhyadeśasthaṃ bhairavāgni tatorcayet |
pūrṇā pūrvaṃ tato devi śivatatvamanusmaret || 10-1311 ||
pūrvoktalakṣaṇenaiva uccarantaḥ prayatnataḥ |
heyodhvā śeṣavatkṛtvā recakena varānane || 10-1312 ||
yāvat sā sumanāśaktistatorddhamunmanā smṛtāḥ |
nātra kālakalācāraṃ na tatvaṃ na ca devatāḥ || 10-1313 ||
sunirvāṇaṃ paraṃ śuddhaṃ guruvaktraṃ sa ucyate |
tasyātītaṃ varārohe paratatvaṃ nirāmayam || 10-1314 ||
guruvaktraprayogeṇa yojayecchāśvate pade |
niṣkaṃpe kāraṇātīte viraje nirmale śubhe || 10-1315 ||
sarvajñe parame tatve vyomātīte hyanāmaye |
ityādhvānamidaṃ proktaṃ samāsena mayānaghe || 10-1316 ||
jñātvā caivaṃ mahādevi prayāti paramaṃ padam |
dehe deve ca śiṣye ca kalaśe hyagnimadhyataḥ || 10-1317 ||
evaṃ jñātvā jagatsṛṣṭau mucyate mocayeti ca |

iti bhairavasrotasi mahātantre vidyāpīṭhe saptakoṭipramāṇe śrītantrasadbhāve adhvānadaśamaḥ paṭalaḥ || 10 ||



devyuvāca

adhvānaṃ ca mayā jñātaṃ tvatprasādātsurādhipa |
jagatsṛṣṭi tvayā deva sūcite na tu varṇitā || 11-1 ||
yathā sā jñāyate deva kathayastvat prasādataḥ |
kāryaṃ ca kāraṇaṃ kartā purā vai kathitā vibho || 11-2 ||

bhairava uvāca

yat tat sūkṣmaṃ paraṃ devaṃ kāraṇaṃ sarvagaṃ śivam |
nimittakāraṇaṃ so hi kathitaṃ tava suvrate || 11-3 ||
akāmā sṛjate sarvaṃ jagatsthāvarajaṅgamam |
90a g] svatejasā varārohe vyoma saṃkṣobhya līlayā || 11-4 ||
upādānaṃ tu tatproktaṃ saṃkṣubdhaṃ samayā yataḥ |
tasmānnādaṃ samutpannaṃ pūrve ca kathitaṃ tava || 11-5 ||
aṣṭadhā sa tu deveśe vyaktaḥ śabdaprabhedataḥ |
ghoṣo rāvaḥ svanaḥ śabdasphoṭākhyo dhvanireva ca || 11-6 ||
jhakāraṃ dhvaṃkṛtaṃ caiva aṣṭau śabdāḥ prakīrtitāḥ |
navamastu mahāśabdaḥ sarveṣāṃ vyāpakaḥ smṛtaḥ || 11-7 ||
nadate sa sadā yasmāt sarvabhūteṣvavasthitaḥ |
tasmāt sadāśivo devo jñeyo vai dṛkkriyātmakaḥ || 11-8 ||
nādabindusamutpannaṃ sūryakoṭisamaprabhaḥ |
sa caiva daśadhā jñeyo daśatatvaphalapradam || 11-9 ||
daśadhā varṇarūpeṇa daśadevatasaṃyutaḥ |
bindoḥ sadāśivotpanno aṣṭabhedaistu saṃyutaḥ || 11-10 ||
pañcabrahmakalābhiśca vidyāṅgaiḥ śaktibhiryutaḥ |
pañcabhiśca mahājñānairmūrtibhiśca samanvitaḥ || 11-11 ||
sa evāpararūpeṇa paramātmā śivovyayaḥ |
dvidhāvasthaḥ śivo jñeyaḥ soccārocāravarjitaḥ || 11-12 ||
mudrā mantrasvarūpeṇa sa eva ca punardvidhāḥ |
kriyājñānasvarūpeṇa icchārūpasurūpataḥ || 11-13 ||
śabdāvabodharūpeṇa vasturūpasvarūpataḥ |
sthūlasūkṣmapare caiva parātīto nirāśrayaḥ || 11-14 ||
vyomarūpasurūpeṇa samanonmana aiva ca |
unmanātīta deveśaḥ śivo jñeya śivāgame || 11-15 ||
unmanāsamanasthānaṃ śivatatvamadhiṣṭayet |
pañcakāraṇarūpeṇa tadevaṃ punareva saḥ || 11-16 ||
kāraṇaṃ pañcakaṃ devi adhastāt samadhiṣṭhayet |
vyāpinaṃ śaktimūrdhnisthaṃ biladvāre anāsṛtam || 11-17 ||
anantaṃ ca suṣumṇeśamanādhvamūrddhvagāminam |
vyomarūpaṃ mahādevi bindvīśaṃ parikīrtitam || 11-18 ||
anāsṛtaṃ svayaṃ brahmā samadhiṣṭhā vyavasthitaḥ |
anātho viṣṇurityukto ananto rudra eva ca || 11-19 ||
vyomarūpeśvaraḥ prokto vyāpinastu sadāśivaḥ |
vyāpinastu punardevi hāṭakaḥ parameśvaraḥ || 11-20 ||
vidyāmantragaṇairyuktaḥ saptapātālanāyakaḥ |
vyomarūpastu kusumāṇḍa sthito narakanāyakaḥ || 11-21 ||
anantaścaiva deveśi rudraḥ kālāgnivigrahaḥ | 
anāthonantarūpeṇa sthito adhvānadhārakaḥ || 11-22 ||
anāsṛto mahādevi sthito vai huhukaḥ prabhuḥ |
svaśaktyāśṛto bhamaśrāṃ stena gītamanāsṛtam || 11-23 ||
tasyāsṛtaṃ jagatsarvaṃ unmanantaṃ varānane |
saṃsthito aṃbhasā pūrve śaktyādhārastu hūhukaḥ || 11-24 ||
tatastu āpatatvaṃ vai āgneyaṃ tadanantaram |
90b g] vāyavyāṃ nātha saṃtraiva tanmātramindriyāṇi ca || 11-25 || 
viṣayāśca manaścaiva ahaṃkāramanukramāt |
bauddhaṃ gauṇaṃ ca deveśi prākṛtaṃ pauruṣaṃ tataḥ || 11-26 ||
niyatikālarāgaśca vidyāścaiva kalā tathā |
māyā tatvaṃ tato vidyā īśvarastu sadāśivaḥ || 11-27 ||
65a] bindumadhyendu nairodhī nādo nādīśvaraḥ param |
tadadho brahmavilaṃ vai śaktitatvamataḥ param || 11-28 || 
pañcakāraṇasaṃyuktā vyāpinī tu ataḥ param | 
samanā unmanā caiva prakriyāṇḍairyutaṃ priye || 11-29 || 
evaṃvidhā adhaścaiva ūrddhvaṃ caiva samaṃtataḥ |
yathātmā aṇavo devi asaṅkhyātā vyavasthitāḥ || 11-30 ||
evaṃ vai prakriyāṇḍāni asaṅkhyānyeva koṭiśaḥ |
ekena varṇitenaiva sarve vai varṇitā bhavet || 11-31 ||
yathā hyekaṃ tathā sarve prakriyāṇḍāsthitā tu vai |
sarveṣāṃ prakriyāṇḍānāṃ susvarūpeṇa suvrate || 11-32 ||
vyāpakastu śivaḥ sūkṣmā savāhyābhyantare sthitaḥ |
sarvātiśayasaṃyuktaḥ sarvakāraṇavarjitaḥ || 11-33 ||
sṛṣṭisaṃhāranirmuktaḥ prapañcātītagocaraḥ |
nirmalo vimalaḥ śānto adhorddhvaṃ ca vyavasthitaḥ || 11-34 ||
ākāśasya yathā norddhaṃ na madhyaṃ nāpyadhaḥ kvacit |
evaṃ sarvagato devi śivaḥ paramakāraṇaḥ || 11-35 ||
vyāpayitvā jagatsarvaṃ sthitaṃ vyomeṣu vyomavat |
evaṃ jñātvā varārohe na bhūyo janmanāṃ vrajet || 11-36 ||
kāraṇānāṃ punarvyāptiḥ kathayāmi yathā sthitaḥ |
eva pūrveva katho pūreva kathito mayā || 11-37 ||
ādityastu smṛto brahmā somo viṣṇuśca suvrate |
grahāṇāmadhipo rudro nakṣatrebhyastatheśvaraḥ || 11-38 ||
yajamānasya deveśi svayaṃ devaḥ sadāśivaḥ |
sadyojātastu vai brahmā vāmo viṣṇu prakīrtitaḥ || 11-39 ||
aghoro rudramityuktastathātatpuruṣeśvaraḥ |
aiśānastu varārohe svayaṃ deva sadāśivaḥ || 11-40 ||
sadyojātastu ṛgvedo vāmadevo yaju smṛtaḥ |
aghoraḥ sāmavedastu puruṣotharvamucyate || 11-41 ||
īśānaśca suśreṣṭhasya sarvavidyātmakaṃ smṛtaḥ |
laukikaṃ jñānadeveśi sadyojātād vinirgatam || 11-42 ||
vadikaṃ vāmadevāṃ tu aghorādhyātmanirgatam |
puruṣāccāti mārgaṃ tu nirgataṃ tu varānane || 11-43 ||
samayākhyaṃ mahājñānaṃ īśānācca vinirgatam | 
91a g] atha tatvavibhāgena punaśca śṛṇu suvrate || 11-44 ||
caturviṃśati tatvāni brahmā vyāpya vyavasthitaḥ |
pradhānādyāva deveśi pauruṣe tu janārdanaḥ || 11-45 ||
niyatyā yāva māyāntaṃ rudro vyāpa vyavasthitaḥ |
vidyāyāśce śvaraṃ tatvaṃ vyāpitaṃ tu mahātmanā || 11-46 ||
ūrddhve sadāśivo devaḥ sarva vyāpyavyavasthitaḥ |
tatvatrayavibhāgena punarvakṣyāmi sundari || 11-47 ||
ātmatatveti vai brahmā māyāntaṃ yāva saṃsthitam |
vidyātatve tathā viṣṇuryāvatsādākhyagocaram || 11-48 ||
śivatatve tathā rudro jñātavyastu varānane |
sādākhyā ūrddhvamadhvānaṃ sarva vyāpya vyavasthitam || 11-49 ||
raudryāddhiṣṭhitātmā vai sa rudrastu prajāyate |
vāmayā viṣṇuḥ mebāhurjyeṣṭhayā tu pitāmaha || 11-50 ||
jñānaśakti smṛto brahmā kriyāśakti janārdanaḥ |
icchāśaktiparorudraḥ sa śivaḥ parigīyate || 11-51 ||
viṣṇuḥ sadāśivo devo brahmāṇaśceśvaro viduḥ |
sa ca sadāśivo devi utpanna prabhumīśvaraḥ || 11-52 ||
tasmād vidyā tato māyā vidyā ca punamīśvaraḥ |
jñānaśakti karāgreṇa svecchayā parameśvaraḥ || 11-53 ||
saptakoṭyastu mantrāṇāṃ sṛjejjñānakriyātmakāḥ |
te ca sādākhyaparyante pārthivānte ca suvrate || 11-54 ||
anugrahaṃ prakurvanti dehināṃ bhuvane sthitāḥ |

65b] śivaśaktisamāviṣṭāstrinetrāścandramaulayaḥ || 11-55 ||
rudramūrtibhirekosau śivaḥ paramakāraṇaḥ |
jagad vyāpya sthito māyī śūlapāṇiranekadhā || 11-56 ||
jñānaśaktyā punaḥ sveśaḥ samālokya varānane |
icchāśaktyā samāviṣṭā kriyāśaktyā ca suvrate || 11-57 ||
māyātatvaṃ jagadbījaṃ nityaṃ ca prabhuravyayam |
tatsthaṃ tatvātmavargaṃ ca yugapatkṣobhayet prabhuḥ || 11-58 ||
helā daṇḍahatā devi vadanye vāhalā yathā |
tiryamūrddhvamadhastāntu nirgacchanti samaṃtataḥ || 11-59 ||
muktestu bhājanā yatra anudhātāḥ śivena tu |
ūrddhaṃ gacchanti te sarve śivaṃ paramanirmalam || 11-60 ||
vidyāyā bhāgino ye tu mantrarūpā bhavanti te |
saṃsārabhājanā ye tu malakarmamalānvitāḥ || 11-61 ||
te brajanti adhastāttu ghore caivātidāruṇe |
tasmātkalā samutpatrā vidyārāgastathā punaḥ || 11-62 ||
kālo niyatitatvaṃ ca puṃtatvaṃ prakṛtistathā |
satvaṃ rajastamaścaiva prakṛte ca guṇatrayam || 11-63 ||
satvaṃ prakāśajananaṃ pravṛttijanako rajaḥ |
tamo viṣṭambhakaṃ proktaṃ vijñeyaṃ hi guṇatrayam || 11-64 ||
satve brahmā rajo viṣṇustame rudraḥ prakīrtitaḥ |
brahmatve sṛjate lokānviṣṇutve sthitikārakaḥ || 11-65 ||
rudratve saṃharet sarvaṃ jagatsthāvarajaṅgamam |
jāgratsvapnasuṣuptiśca tisrovasthā maheśvaraiḥ || 11-66 ||
guṇebhyo dhiṣaṇā jātā bhavabhedaiḥ samanvitā |
brahmā tatrādhipātvena buddhitatve vyavasthitaḥ || 11-67 ||
sarvajñāḥ sa ca mevāhurbodhānāṃ paramaṃ padam |
guṇaiṣṭārurhatānāṃ ca prādhānye vedavādinām || 11-68 ||
pauruṣe caiva sāṃkhyānāṃ pañcarātravidāmapi |
ṣaḍviṃśarkaṃ ca deveśi yogaśāstrai paraṃ padam || 11-69 ||
vrate pāśupate proktaṃ aiśvaraṃ padamavyayam |
mauśale kārake caiva māyātatvaṃ prakīrtitam || 11-70 ||
kṣemīśo brahmaṇa svāmī teṣāṃ tatparamaṃ padam |
tejīśo vai malānāṃ ca pramāṇe tu dhruvaṃ padam || 11-71 ||
dīkṣājñāna viśuddhātmā dehāntaṃ yāvaparyayā |
kapālavratamāsthāya sve sve gacchanti te pade || 11-72 ||
sarvādhvānavinirmuktaṃ śaivānāṃ paramaṃ padam |
buddhitatvādahaṅkāraṃ punarjātastridhā priye || 11-73 ||
sātviko rājasaścaiva tāmasaśca prakīrtitaḥ |
bhūtādi vaikṛtaścaiva tejasaśca tridhā sthitaḥ || 11-74 ||
bhūtādeścaiva tanmātraṃ tebhyo bhūtavijānatā |
pañcakarmendriyāṇyaiva jātā vaikārikasya tu || 11-75 ||
śabda sparśañca rūpaśca raso gandhaścapañcamaḥ |
ete pañca samākhyātā tanmātrānu krameṇa tu || 11-76 ||
śabdād vyoma samutpannaṃ sparśād vāyustathā punaḥ |
rūpā tejā samutpannā rasādāpaḥ prajāyate || 11-77 ||
gandhāttu pṛthivī jātā samāsātkathitā tava |
mano buddhīndriyāṇyaiva tejasāṃ tu bhavanti hi || 11-78 ||
mano bhayātmakaḥ prokto vyāpakaḥ sarva indriyaiḥ |
ātmopacārato bhedāṃ kathayāmi daśaiva tu || 11-79 ||
ātmā caivāntarātmā ca bāhyātmā surasundari |
nirātmā paramātmā ca pañcadhā parikīrtitāḥ || 11-80 ||
arvudaścārvudaścaiva buddhyamānaṃ tathaiva ca |
prabuddhaḥ suprabuddhaśca punaśca kathayāmi te || 11-81 ||
pradhānasāmyamātyantasukhaduḥkhavivarjitaḥ |

66a] yadā tasmiṃ sthito devi tadātmā tu sa ucyate || 11-82 ||
puryaṣṭakasamāyogāt paryaṭet ūrddhva yoniṣu |
92a g] antarātmā sa vijñeyo nibaddhastu śubhāśubhaiḥ || 11-83 ||
buddhikarmendriyairyuktaḥ pañcabhūtaiḥ samāvṛtaiḥ |
bhuddhyādhā? tu tadā devi viṣayāṇi tu bhuñjati || 11-84 ||
bhūtabhāvavinirmuktastatvadharmakaloñjhitaḥ |
malakarmaikayuktātmā māyādharmatiraskṛtaḥ || 11-85 ||
nirayātmā tadā jñeya paramātmā vadāmi te |
malakarmakalādyaiśca vinirmukto yadā bhavet || 11-86 ||
sarvādhvānamatītaśca māyāmohoñjhitastathā |
nirmalatvaṃ tadā prāptipadaṃ paramamavyayam || 11-87 ||
paramātmā tadā devi procyate prabhuravyayaḥ |
arbudaśca punardevi kathayāmi samāsataḥ || 11-88 ||
tatvabhūtātmasaṃhāre kalākṣityantagocare |
māyā sāmyaniśāyāṃ vai saṃhṛtyaparameśvaraḥ || 11-89 ||
nirvyāpāro bhavetāvadyāva devodayaṃ punaḥ |
sukhaduḥkhādibhogasya ātmavargasya karmaṇaḥ || 11-90 ||
mana nidrānisūkṣmātmā ruddhacaitanyadikkriyā |
na vijānāti śabdādīnna cātmānaṃ varānane || 11-91 ||
kāraṇaṃ na vijānāti na ca sthānaṃ svakaṃ priye |
sarvametanna jānāti yato luptākṣavṛttayaḥ || 11-92 ||
abuddha tiṣṭhate tatra yāvanmāyā ahaṃmukhāḥ |
abuddhañca samākhyātaṃbuddhaścaiva nibodha me || 11-93 ||
paripākāgate karma īśvarecchākaroddhṛtaḥ |
prakāśana vapuryadvadanu gṛhṇātibhāskaraḥ || 11-94 ||
karaṇenānugṛhṇāti tatvīśaścaivamātmanām |
kalonmīlitacaitanyo vidyādarśitagocaraḥ || 11-95 ||
rāgosyarañjakatvena viṣayānandalakṣaṇaḥ |
kālo vai kalate nityaṃ tudyādi pralayāvadhiḥ || 11-96 ||
niyatiṃ niścitaṃ nityaṃ yojayeta śubhāśubhaiḥ |
paramāṇusahasrāṃśaṃ na dadāti sa cādhikam || 11-97 ||
puṃbhāvatvamanuprāpya tatve vai pauruṣau hvayet |
puraṃ pradhānamityuktaṃ prapañcānekasaṃcitam || 11-98 ||
tatpuraṃ pūrayedyasmāt tasmād vai puruṣa smṛtaḥ |
tatra śrīkaṇṭhabhagavānniyatyā karmataḥ paśuḥ || 11-99 ||
pradhānapāśajālena veṣṭayeddaśapuṃjasaḥ |
vṛddhastriguṇavandhena buddhyā vai karaṇena tu || 11-100 ||
tanmātrendriyavandhena dṛḍhabhūtaiḥ praveṣṭitaḥ |
vaddhaḥ saṃsarate hyevaṃ māyādyavanigocare || 11-101 ||
saṃsārī procyate tasmāt saṃsareta punaḥ punaḥ |
viṣayā śabdādayo yasya vidyante viṣayī tadā || 11-102 ||
92b g] viṣayāḥ pradhānetyāhurnānābhedairvisarpitāḥ |
nāmakarmavipākaiśca bhukte tadbhāvabhāvitaḥ || 11-103 ||
evaṃ bhunakti vai yasmāt tasmādbhoktā sa ucyate |
tasmiṃstajjñā varārohe kṣetre vai varṣako yathā || 11-104 ||
manobhilāsamālokya kṛṣate lobhalāṅgalaiḥ |
vāyavye mohabhāvena mano vācādi kāyikam || 11-105 ||
dharmādharmamayaṃ bījaṃ pravikīrya samaṃtataḥ |
tasmād devāṅkurotpattiṃ sukhaduḥkhaphalodayāḥ || 11-106 ||
varddhate kāmakrodhābhyāṃ saṃsiktaṃ rāgamambunā |
yasmindeśe ca pātre ca vayasā yādṛśena ca ||107 ||
uktaṃ śubhāśubhaṃ bījaṃ tasminkāle labhet phalam |
bhuñjate vividhākāraṃ pūrvakarmavaśād budhaḥ || 11-108 ||
yasmādevaṃ vijānāti tasmāt kṣetrajña ucyate |
viṣayā buddhyate yasmād buddhastasmāt prakīrtitaḥ || 11-109 ||

66b] tadevāriṣṭarūpeṇa yadā bhāvayate pumān |
pramuddyamānaśca tadā adhunā kathayāmi te || 11-110 ||
tadā jugupsayedbhogāñchubhāni aśubhāni ca |
kṛtimām yo'vamanyeta manye vairāgyamāśṛtaḥ || 11-111 ||
māyādyavaniparyantamindrajālevabuddhyati |
putramitrakalatrāṇi suhṛtsvajanabāndhavāḥ || 11-112 ||
yatrārjitaṃ mayā dravyaṃ śubhena aśubhena vā |
tadbhuṃjanti hite sarve nirviśaṃkā nirākulāḥ || 11-113 ||
ahamekākinaścaiva vayāsyāmi yamasādanam |
tasyānna śubhā hyete vai vairiṇānarthakāriṇāḥ || 11-114 ||
svātmīyamapi yaddehaṃ nityameva jugupsayet |
śukraśoṇitasaṃbhūtaṃ viṣayoragadūṣitam || 11-115 ||
nānāvyādhisamākīrṇaṃ jarāmṛtyubhayākulam |
yohamasmin samākīrṇe kathamatra ramāmyahaṃ || 11-116 ||
nityamudvignacittastu cintyamānaḥ punaḥ punaḥ | 
kathaṃ muktirbhavet tasmāt saṃsāraduratikramāt || 11-117 ||
evaṃ prabhuddhyamānastu gurvārādhana mudyataḥ |
buddhyamānaḥ samākhyātaḥ prabuddhaḥ kathayāmi te || 11-118 ||
guruprasādād deveśi dīkṣāṃprāpya śivādhvare |
yadālocayate devi śaivajñānaṃ mahodayam || 11-119 ||
karāmalakavatsarvamadhvajātaṃ svarūpataḥ |
śivādyavaniparyantamīkṣate jñānacakṣuṣā || 11-120 || 
heyaṃ kṛtvā samastaṃ tu upādeyaṃ paraṃ śivam |
unmanyāntaṃ varārohe sarvagaṃ vibhuravyayam || 11-121 ||
evaṃ prabuddhā deveśi tallayastatparāyaṇaḥ |
sarvāraṃbhavinirmuktaḥ prabuddhaścocyate tadā || 11-122 ||
prabuddhastu samākhyātaḥ suprabuddhaśca me śṛṇu |
dīkṣājñānena yogena caryāyā vātha śobhanaiḥ || 11-123 ||
93a g] yadā prāptaparaṃ sthānamadhvātītaṃ nirāmayam |
niṣkampaṃ kāraṇātītaṃ sarvajñaṃ sarvatomukham || 11-124 ||
tṛptamanāmasaṃbuddhaṃ svatantraṃ nityameva hi |
aluptasāmarthivibhavaṃ suprabuddhaṃ sanātanam || 11-125 ||
tasmin yuktastathātmā vai tadguṇaiśca samanvitaḥ |
suprabuddhaḥ sa evokto bhairavasya vaco yathā || 11-126 ||
adhunā kathayiṣyāmi bhāvabhedā varānane |
trayodaśaṃ ca karaṇaṃ kāryaṃ ca daśadhā priye || 11-127 ||
ekādaśendriyavadhādyahaṃkārastu vai tridhā |
buddhiraṣṭavidhā caiva pañcadhā ca viparyayaḥ || 11-128 ||
nāmānyeṣāṃ vibhāgena kathayāmi yathākramam |
pañcabhūtāni tanmātrā kāryaṃ ca daśadhā matam || 11-129 ||
pañcabuddhyakṣa karmākṣā daśadhā karaṇaṃ vahiḥ |
mano'haṅkāra buddhiśca tridhāntaḥ karaṇaṃ smṛtam || 11-130 ||
mūkatā kaṇṭhapaṃgutvaṃ utsargatā tathaiva ca |
nirānandaśca vijñeyo vahiratvaṃ tathaiva hi || 11-131 ||
śīrṇatā caiva gātrasya tathā cāndhatvameva hi |
anāsvādamagandhatvamanavasthā manasya ca || 11-132 ||
etendriyavadhā khyātā ekādaśa manukramāt |
ahaṃkārastridhā proktaḥ purā te varavarṇini || 11-133 ||
tamo moho mahāmohastāmisronyevaparyayaḥ |
andhatāmisramevāhurete pañcaviparyayā || 11-134 ||
aṣṭāvete parākhyātā vaddheddharmādayo guṇāḥ |
bhāvabhedā samākhyātāḥ pañcāśaite yathākramām || 11-135 ||
punaścāṣṭau ca ye vaddherbhedā dharmādaya sthitāḥ |
teṣāṃ bhedā yathā bhinnāstathāhaṃ kathayāmyaham || 11-136 ||

67a] saptadhā vandhate sā tu puṃsā saṃsāravarmiṇi |
mocayejjñānabhāvena sāṃkhyā jñānaratātmanām || 11-137 ||
jñānaṃ ca sātvikaṃ proktaṃ trayonye rājasā smṛtāḥ |
tāmasāśca adharmādyāścatvāryevaṃ varānane || 11-138 ||
dharmaṃ ca daśadhā prokto jñānaṃ caivāṣṭadhā smṛtam |
vairāgyaṃ navadhā caiva aiśvaryaṃ cāṣṭadhā viduḥ || 11-139 ||
ete caiva viparyastā adharmā sā ? prakīrtitaḥ |
akrodho guruśuśrūṣā śaucaṃ santoṣamārjavam || 11-140 ||
ahiṃsā satyamasteyaṃ brahmacaryamakalkatāḥ |
eṣa daśavidho dharmaḥ kathitastu varānane || 11-141 ||
tāraṃ sutārataraṇaṃ tārayantaṃ pramodakam |
pramuditaṃ ramyakaṃ ca sadā pramuditaṃ tathā || 11-142 ||
etajjñānaṃ samākhyātaṃ aṣṭadhā parameśvari |
aṃbhā salila-oghā ca vṛṣṭisaṃjñā tathā parāḥ || 11-143 ||
sutārā ca supārā ca sunetrā paramāmṛtāḥ |
aṣṭamātrasumārī ca uttamāṃbhasikā tathā || 11-144 || 
93b g] vairāgyaṃ navadhā caiva kathitaṃ tu mayā tava |
aṇimā laghimā caiva mahimā prāptireva ca || 11-145 ||
prākāmyaṃ ca tatheśitvaṃ vaśitvaṃ ca tathā param |
sarvakarmavaśāyitvaṃ aṣṭamaṃ parikīrtitam || 11-146 ||
aiśvaryamaṣṭadhā bhinnaṃ kathitaṃ tu mayā priye |
krodho gurorasuśrūṣā aśaucatvaṃ tathā punaḥ || 11-147 ||
asantoṣamanārjavyaṃ hiṃsā cāsatyameva ca |
steyamabrahmacaryaṃ ca tathā caiva sakalkatā || 11-148 ||
evamadharma vikhyāto daśadhā parameśvari |
sutāramasutāraṃ ca ataraṇaṃ tathā priye || 11-149 ||
atārayantaṃ deveśi caturthaṃ parikīrtitam |
apramodaṃ pramuditaṃ aramyaṃ ca tathā viduḥ || 11-150 ||
asadāpramuditaṃ vai ajñānamevamaṣṭadhā |
araṃbhā salilaṃ caiva anaughamavṛṣṭistathā || 11-151 ||
asutāramasupārasunetraṃ mataḥ param |
asumāraśca vijñeyo uttamāmbhasikastathā || 11-152 ||
avairāgyasya caivaite navabhedāḥ prakīrtitāḥ |
anaṇimāmalaghimāmamahimā tathā viduḥ || 11-153 ||
aprāptirapyakāmyaṃ ca aneśitvaṃ tathaiva ca |
avaśitvaṃ tathā caiva akāmitvamasarvathā || 11-154 ||
anaiśvaryaṃ ca evaṃ hi aṣṭadhā parikīrtitaḥ |
anaiśvaryādayo hyete paiśācādyairadhiṣṭhitāḥ || 11-155 ||
yathā krameṇa cāṣṭaiva saṃsthitāḥ kathayāmi te |
anaiśvaryaṃ ca paiśāce avairāgyaṃ ca rākṣase || 11-156 ||
yākṣe caiva tathā jñānaṃ gāndharve dharma eva ca |
dharmaścaiva tathaindre tu jñānaṃ saumye pratiṣṭhitam || 11-157 ||
prājāpatye ca vairāgyaṃ brahmaiśvaryaṃ vyavasthitam |
catuḥ ṣaṣṭigaṇaṃ tacca pade brahme prakīrtitam || 11-158 || 
ṣaṭpañcāśaguṇaiśvaryaṃ prājāpatye vyavasthitam | 
catvāriṃśaguṇo'ṣṭau ca saumyaṃ vai parikīrtitam || 11-159 || 
catvāriṃśaguṇaṃ caiva mahendraiśvaryamucyate | 
dvātriṃśadguṇa deveśi gandharvaiśvaryamucyate || 11-160 || 
caturviṃśaguṇaṃ yākṣaṃ ṣoḍaśaṃ rākṣasaṃ smṛtam | 
evaṃ sthitaṃ tu aiśvaryaṃ devayoniṣu suvrate || 11-161 ||
anyaṃ saptasvarūpeṇa saṃsthitaṃ devayoniṣu |
ete eva susaṃkīrṇā mānuṣeṣu vyvasthitāḥ || 11-162 ||
pradhānaguṇabhedena sthāvarāntavyavasthitā priye |
guṇatrayasya vyāptirvai kathayāmi ca yā sthitāḥ || 11-163 ||
satve nādhiṣṭhitā devā brahmādyā maghavāntikāḥ |
67b] gandharvā yakṣa-anujā daityāścaiva tu rākṣasāḥ || 11-164 ||
94a g] yātudhānāḥ piśācāśca tāpasāḥ parikīrtitāḥ |
rajaḥsatvotkaṭā jñeyā ṛṣayaḥ saṃsitavratāḥ || 11-165 ||
anyonyābhibhavāste ca pṛthivyā saṃvyavasthitāḥ |
atyantatamasāviṣṭā sthāvarāśca śarīsṛpāḥ || 11-166 ||
pāṇipādavihīnāśca tāmasāśca varānane |
 śarīsṛpādyā vijñeyā paśavāntāśca kīrtitāḥ || 11-167 ||
eṣāmantrabhutānye vai anantā caiva yonayaḥ |
mānuṣeṣu ca cānyantā bhedānanta vyavasthitāḥ || 11-168 ||
na śaktyāḥ kathituṃ te vai karmānantaprabhedataḥ |
guṇā tu mānuṣe loke dharmādyā eva saṃsthitāḥ || 11-169 ||
dharmādyeṣu nivaddhāni yathājñānāni suvrate |
adharmādyeṣu yoni vyāptāni? te kathayāmyaham || 11-170 ||
hetuśāstraṃ ca yalloke nityānityaviḍamvakam |
vādajalpatitaṇḍaiśca vivartitvaniścitāḥ || 11-171 ||
hetu nirvāṇavyaktāni vastuśūnyāni suvrate |
jñānayogavihīnāni devatārahitāni ca || 11-172 ||
dharmārthakāmamokṣeṣu niścayo 'tra na jāyate |
ajñānena vivaddhāste adharmeṇa nimittatā || 11-173 ||
nirayaṃ te prayacchanti ye tatrābhiratā narāḥ |
avairāgyādanaiśvaryaṃ bhuṃjanti niraye narāḥ || 11-174 ||
catvāryete varārohe duḥkhadā narake sadā |
mohakā sarvajantūnāṃ yataste tāmasā matāḥ || 11-175 ||
dharmeṇaikena deveśi nibaddhaṃ jñānalaukikam |
dharmajñānanibaddhaṃ ca pāñcarātraṃ ca vedikam || 11-176 ||
bauddhaṃ mārhantaṃ caiva vairāgyaikena suvrate |
jñānavairāgyasaṃbaddhaṃ sāṃkhyajñānaṃ ca suvrate || 11-177 ||
jñānavairāgyamaiśvarye yogajñānaṃ pratiṣṭhitam |
atītaṃ buddhibhāvānāṃ matimārga pratiṣṭhitam ? || 11-178 ||
lokātītaṃ tato jñānamatimārgeti ucyate |
lokāśca paśavaḥ proktā sṛṣṭisaṃhāravartmani || 11-179 ||
teṣāmatītāste jñeyā yetimārge vyavasthtiāḥ |
kapālapatino ye ca tathā pāśupatāśca ye || 11-180 ||
sṛṣṭiṃ na vidyate teṣāmīśvare ca dhruve gatāḥ |
tasmānmokṣaṃ gamiṣyanti apunarbhāvakāraṇam || 11-181 ||
laukikānāṃ punaḥ sṛṣṭiḥ punaḥ saṃhāra eva ca |
saṃsāracakramārūḍhaṃ bhramante ghaṭṭayaṃtravat || 11-182 ||
dharmādi-arakopetam aṣṭāraṃ cakra suvrate |
aiśvaryādhiṣṭhitaṃ devi niyatyā daṇḍa āhatam || 11-183 ||
malakarmakalāviddhaṃ bhramate kālavegavat |
laukikādyeṣu sāneṣu eteṣvabhiratā narāḥ || 11-184 ||
hetuśāstraṃ parāye ca ye cānye pāpakarmiṇaḥ |
94b g] sarve te cāsya cakrasya nāntaṃ paśyanti mohitāḥ || 11-185 ||
laukikādiṣu ye sādhyā atimārgāntagocare |
līlayā sādhayet sarve śaivajñāne mahodaye || 11-186 ||
na sarvaiḥ sādhyate taṃ vai yatotīva sunirmalam |
yato yogapate devi abhāve parame pade || 11-187 ||
abhāvaṃ bhāvanātītaṃ prapañcātītagocaram |
manobuddhyādinirmuktaṃ hetuvādavivarjitam || 11-188 ||
pratyakṣādipramāṇaiśca atītaṃ prabhuravyayam |
sarvasaṃsarvagaṃ śāntaṃ nirmalaṃ niranuplavam || 11-189 ||
sarvaśaktyātmakaṃ hyetadanādhvānantanādikam |
sarvātiśayanirmuktamanādibhavavarjitam || 11-190 ||
sarvajñānapadātītaṃ saivajñānaṃ paraṃ smṛtam |
evaṃ sṛṣṭāni tatvāni jñānāni ca varānane || 11-191 ||
68a] tatvairetairjagat sarvaṃ visṛṣṭaṃ sacarācaram |
bhuvanāni vicitrāṇi śataśotha sahasraśaḥ || 11-192 ||
tatvābhyantarasaṃsthāni śāstrāṇi vividhāni ca |
vijñānaṃ huhukaṃ śilpaṃ siddhisaṃbodhalakṣaṇam || 11-193 ||
sarvaṃ tatveṣu dṛśyeta prakriyā śivadīkṣayā |
tatvairetaistu labhyeta yatkiñcit saṃsthitaṃ priye || 11-194 ||
nāsti dīkṣāsamo mokṣo na kriyā mātṛkātparā |
prakriyā na paraṃ jñānaṃ nāsti yogamalakṣakaḥ || 11-195 ||
tatsarvaṃ kathitaṃ devi saivajñānaṃ mahodadhau |
etajjñānādbhavedyogī sṛṣṭisaṃhāravarjitaḥ || 11-196 ||

iti bhairavasrotasi mahātantre vidyāpīṭhe saptakoṭipramāṇe śrītantrasadbhāve? sṛṣṭirekādaśaḥ paṭalaḥ || 11 ||


bhairava uvāca

tatvasṛṣṭimayākhyātā vistareṇa suvistarāt |
sāṃprataṃ tu punarvakṣye saṃhāraṃ tu yathākramam || 12-1 ||
nirujasya yuvānasya svasthasya taruṇātmakaḥ |
helāmārge pravāheṇa sauraṃ caiva tu lakṣaye || 12-2 ||
saurācca paitṛkaṃ jñeyaṃ pitarā divya lakṣayet |
divyāścaiva paraṃ jñeyaṃ evamādi vijānatā || 12-3 ||
tuṭyādikramayogena yathā bhavati tacchṛṇu |
tuṭāṣaṣṭiḥ samākhyātā ḍhakameva vidhīyate || 12-4 ||
tābhiḥ ṣaṣṭi suvijñeyā lakṣitavyā prayatnataḥ |
suniṣaṣṭhi samākhyātā śvāsameva vidhīyate || 12-5 ||
śvāsaiḥ ṣaṣṭhipravāhaiśca taiḥ ṣaṣṭistu tuṭi smṛtāḥ |
dvādaśāntaṃ tataḥ prāptaḥ punarāgamanaṃ bhavet || 12-6 ||
evaṃ prāṇasya saṃcāraḥ kathitastava śobhane |
ṣaṣṭistuṭilavaḥ prokto nimeṣaścaivatatsamaḥ || 12-7 ||
nimeṣaiḥ ṣaṣṭivikhyātaḥ kāṣṭhāmeva vidhīyate |
95a g] ṣaṣṭikāṣṭhaiḥ kalākhyātā tatparastu vidhīyate || 12-8 ||
etaistu dviguṇenaiva gālavaḥ parikīrtitaḥ |
gālavaiḥ ṣaṣṭirākhyāto vipruṣaḥ parikīrtitaḥ || 12-9 ||
vipruyāṇāṃ tathā ṣaṣṭiḥ proktaścaiva pati?tpalam |
palānāṃ ṣaṣṭirevoktā kalāmekā vidhīyate || 12-10 ||
dve kale tu muhūrtā vai yāmaścaiva cayārddhataḥ |
ahayāmacatuṣkeṇa śarvaryā caiva tatsamāḥ || 12-11 ||
dinaścaiva mahādevi kathitastu na saṃśayaḥ |
daśapañcadinairpakṣo dvipakṣātmā samucyate || 12-12 ||
ṛtudvi māsikā jñeyā ayanaṃ ca ṛtutrayaiḥ |
ayanadvitayenaiva vatsaraṃ parikīrtitam || 12-13 ||
vatsarāṇāṃ samuddiṣṭaṃ lakṣāṇi daśa sapta ca |
aṣṭāviṃśatisahasrāṇi kṛtaṃ caiva prakīrtitam || 12-14 ||
tretaṃ caiva samākhyātaṃ lakṣā dvādaśakīrtitāḥ |
ṣaṇṇavatyā sahasrāṇi pramāṇaṃ tu vipaścitā || 12-15 ||
lakṣāṇyaṣṭa samākhyātāścatuḥ ṣaṣṭhisahasrakāḥ |
dvāparasya samuddiṣṭaṃ pramāṇaṃ sāmudāyikam || 12-16 ||
catvāri caiva lakṣāṇi sahasrā dvātriṃśati smṛtāḥ |
kalāvetatpramāṇaṃ tu sarvaśāstraikyatāṃ gataṃ || 12-17 ||
catvāriṃśat samākhyātā lakṣālakṣatrayaṃ punaḥ |
viṃśatiśca sahasrāṇi tajjñaiḥ sauraṃ caturyugam || 12-18 ||
caturyugaikasaptatyā proktaṃ saṃvatsaraṃ budhaiḥ |
manvantarasurendrāyuḥ kathitaṃ tu tapodhane || 12-19 ||
caturdaśasurendrāṇāṃ dinaṃ kamalajanmanaḥ |
tāvadrātrī mahākalpastrailokyasthitināśakṛt || 12-20 ||
evaṃ sauraṃ samākhyātaṃ paitṛkaṃ kathayāmyaham |
68b] śuklakṛṣṇau ca yau proktau pakṣau dvau tu śubhekṣaṇe || 12-21 ||
ahorātraṃ tu taṃ jñeyaṃ paitṛyaṃ parameśvari |
evamādi krameṇaiva pakṣānmāsa ṛtustathā || 12-22 ||
ayanādvatsaraṃ caiva caturyugavibhāgaśaḥ |
kramāṅgacchatyanenaiva pramāṇaṃ śāstravādinām || 12-23 ||
divyamayanakrameṇaiva pramāṇena śubhekṣaṇe |
divyenaiva pramāṇena sahasrāṇāṃ catuṣkṛtam || 12-24 ||
catuḥśatī bhavet sandhyā sandhyāṃśaścaiva tatsamaḥ |
ekaikāyugamātrīṇi tretādvāparayoḥ kalau || 12-25 ||
proktāni nyūnato devi pramāṇena prakīrtitāḥ |
caturyugasya tasyānte kalpameva prakīrttitāḥ || 12-26 ||
mānuṣāṇāṃ samākhyātaḥ punaścaiva tu saṃbhavaḥ |
devānāṃ ca samākhyāto dine tu brahmaṇonaghe || 12-27 ||
95b g] dinasthitiḥ samākhyātā saṃhāro rātrimeva ca |
evaṃ devi mayā proktaḥ saṃkṣepāt parameśvari || 12-28 ||
anena kramayogena mānenānena padmajaḥ |
varṣāṇāṃ tu śataṃ jīvet punaścaiva tu saṃharet || 12-29 ||
kālāgnyalantaparyante dadāhāṇḍadvikhaṇḍakṛt |
kālāgneḥ śaktayastīvrāstrailokyā saṃharet kṣamāḥ || 12-30 ||
nipatanti tathā tīvrā vrahmaviṣṇvendradharmiṣu |
dahanti śatasāhasrāḥ kālarudrātmaśaktayaḥ || 12-31 ||
natdyarkasāgarāḥ śailā janaloke janasthitiḥ |
kūṣmāṇḍapramukhā rudrāḥ praviśantyacyutaṃ padam || 12-32 ||
etadaharniśaṃ cakraṃ bhramatīti ahetukam |
mahadbhokta risaṃlīne guṇeṣu guṇavattare || 12-33 ||
dhātryaṇḍaṃ līyate toye toyamapyagnitāṃ yayuḥ |
vahni vāyau punaryāti marudgaganagocare || 12-34 ||
pralayoyaṃ pradhānāṃte jñeyā sṛṣṭivilomataḥ |
śrīkaṇṭhe vyaktamāviṣṭaḥ pracacāla punaḥ punaḥ || 12-35 ||
śrīkaṇṭhaḥ paramā yāti pradhānāttava vacchade |
anantenantatāṃ yāte māyācakramacakramat || 12-36 ||
vidyāntāni prayāntyeva parātparamasaṃbhavam |
parātparatarā bhāvā na yāti paramātparam || 12-37 ||
evaṃ kartā mayā proktaḥ śrīkaṇṭhomantra vigrahaḥ |
svādhikāraṇavṛttosau līyate śāśvate pade || 12-38 ||
śatabrahmavinaṣṭe tu tato viṣṇordinaṃ bhavet |
dinātpakṣastato māsa ṛturayana vatsaraḥ || 12-39 ||
varṣāṇāṃ tu śataṃ jīvetpunaścāsaṃsṛjāmyaham |
śataviṣṇuvināśaistu tato rudradinaṃ bhavet || 12-40 ||
śrīkaṇṭhaḥ paramo rudro adhikārapade sthitaḥ |
śatavarṣādhikāraṃ tu kurute parameśvaraḥ || 12-41 ||
* * * * * * * te? sarvaṃ tatvayāmamaśeṣakam |
māyāyādhaḥ puṭe līnaṃ yatkiñcid vāṅmayaṃ priye || 12-42 ||
eṣādhvamupasaṃhāro māyāntā ye vyavasthitāḥ |
sarve te māyayā līnā svaśaktihṛtavigrahāḥ || 12-43 ||
mahāpralayamevaṃ tu proktaṃ devena śaṃbhunā |
punarevaṃ sṛjet so vai anantaḥ parameśvaraḥ || 12-44 ||
sarvajñaḥ sarvakarteśo nigrahānugraheśvaraḥ |
kalopodvalitāḥ sarve vidyā veditamānasaḥ || 12-45 ||
rāgādirañjitātmāno buddhyādhikaraṇairyutaḥ |
kramādāgatya bhuñjanti prāgvāsana suvāsitāḥ || 12-46 ||
prāgvāsanāvaśeṣeṇa bhuñjate karmajaṃ phalam |
devāṛṣimanuṣyāśca pannagā rākṣasāstathā || 12-47 ||
96a g] asurā pitaro yakṣā bhuvanāni kalāstathā |
tatvaṃ padāśca mantrāśca yaccānyaṃ kiñci vidyate || 12-48 ||
tatsarvaṃ sṛjate devo anantaḥ parameśvaraḥ |
vidyācakrapravaktāro vidyesādibhirāvṛtaḥ || 12-49 ||

69a] anantādi śikhaṇyantā vidyeśā sūkṣmarūpiṇaḥ |
dagdhamāyed vanāḥ sarve śeṣā cakrapravartakāḥ || 12-50 ||
adhikāramalādante kramaśo yānti nirvṛtim |
māyotthānityajantyete svādhikāraṃ prakurvataḥ ? || 12-51 ||
gatāste parameśāni cakrasthāḥ paramāspadam |
saptakoṭyastu yā vidyā vṛtāstu parameṣṭhitāḥ || 12-52 ||
tāsāṃ madhye varārohe adhikāraścaivatāritāḥ |
adyuṣu koṭayo devi martye tu grahakārakāḥ || 12-53 ||
adhikārāvaśeṣeṇa kramaśo yānti nirvṛtim |
adhyuṣṭakoṭayaścānyā svatanusvāvṛtā priye || 12-54 ||
adhikāranivṛttāstā śuddhasāmarthivigrahāḥ |
tāsāṃ devi sthitiḥ proktā vidyātatva surārcite || 12-55 ||
bhuktārthe caiva rudrāṇāṃ mantrārādhanatatparām |
advaitakriyayāśaktaṃ taṃtrācāratātmanām || 12-56 ||
bhuktimārge prapannānāṃ teṣāṃ bhukti prakīrtitāḥ |
aṇimādiguṇāvāptiṃ raṅgaṣaṣṭkasamanvitāḥ || 12-57 ||
sā bhuktistatparaṃ sthānaṃ tadarthe golakādaram |
bhuktirnāmaparaṃ sthānaṃ vidyātatvaṃ mahāyaśe || 12-58 ||
vidyāyāśca abhāveṣu kuto bhukti vidhīyate |
sānandaṃ paramaṃ saukhyaṃ acyutaṃ nirahaṃkṛtam || 12-59 ||
vāgvi śuddhamanohlāda makṣayatvaṃ vyayaṃ dhruvam |
śāśvataṃ paramaṃ sthānaṃ bhogabhoktṛtvalakṣaṇam || 12-60 ||
bhogaṃ vidyāsvarūpeṇa nānyathā varavarṇini |
na bhuktirnaiva muktistu vidyāhīnairvarānane || 12-61 ||
bhogo prapādanārthāya dīkṣā proktā sabījikā |
bhogyābhāve kuto dīkṣā sabījā yā tu kīrtitāḥ || 12-62 ||
tasmāntadbhuktibhogārthe vidyātatvaṃ prakīrtitam |
parameśvareṇa devena anantīśena dhīmatāḥ || 12-63 ||
bhuktyarthe tu vṛtā vidyā jantūnāṃ bhogahetave |
vidyerājena śuddhena tāḥ siddhyanti valotkaṭāḥ || 12-64 ||

iti bhairavasrotasi mahātantre vidyāpīṭhe saptakoṭipramāṇe śrītantrasadbhāve adhvaropasaṃhāro dvādaśaḥ paṭalaḥ ? || 12 ||


devyuvāca
navanābhe mahāyāge navātmādhiṣṭhitena tu |
navatattvavibhāgena dīkṣāṃ vada mama prabho || 13-1 ||
padadīkṣāvibhāgaṃ tu padaistu guṇavattaraiḥ |
96b g] catuḥṣaṣṭiḥ samākhyātā bhairavāstu purā tvayā || 13-2 ||
padavidyā tu yā proktā rudraśaktistu yā smṛtā |
sarveṣāṃ jananīṃ brūhi dhyānasya ca vidhiṃ śubham || 13-3 ||
aṣṭāṣṭakavibhāgena nāmāni kathayasva me |

bhairava uvāca

śṛṇu devi pravakṣyāmi yat tvayā pṛcchitā hyaham || 13-4 ||
padabhedavibhāgaṃ tu navanābhe yathā sthitam |
vidyārāje tu ye varṇā navasaṃkhyopalakṣitāḥ || 13-5 ||
pṛthagbhedena teṣāṃ tu vinyāsaṃ kathayāmi te |
navanābhaṃ puraṃ kṛtvā navapadmopamakṣitam || 13-6 ||
nava sargāṇi? khekṣemaṃ? navaparvādhikaṃ budhaḥ |
saptabhāgakṛtaṃ tantu dakṣiṇottarataḥ priye || 13-7 ||
vidyāmantragaṇaiścaiva padabhedena śaṅkari |
koṣṭhakai konapañcāśatsūtreṇa tu samālikhet || 13-8 ||
madhyame koṣṭhake padmaṃ dvātriṃśāṃgulasaṃmitam |
samālikhya mahādevi caturbhāga vibhājayet || 13-9 ||
prathame karṇikābhāge dvitīye keśarāṇi tu |
tṛtīye dalasandhīni dalāgrāṇi caturthake || 13-10 ||

69b] dikṣu rekhāṣṭakaṃ likhya pratidikṣustathaiva ca |
bhrāmayeccaturo vṛttāṃścaturaṅgulasaṃmitāḥ || 13-11 ||
dvābhyāṃ prati digrekhābhyāṃ kiṃjalkasaṃ vipaścitā |
pūrvaṃ patraṃ prasādhitvā itarāṇyeva meva hi || 13-12 ||
keśarāṇi ca saṃlikhya caturviṃśatisaṃkhyayā |
patrāgrato dadad rekhā vartulāṃ tu suśobhanām || 13-13 ||
tasyānte caturasraṃ tu kartavyaṃ supramāṇataḥ |
pūrva brahmā prasādhitvā viṣuvasthena helinā || 13-14 ||
pūrvapaścāyataṃ sūtraṃ śaṃkulā sādhayet priye |
dvādaśāṅgulamānena madhyeśaṃkulya paropayet || 13-15 ||
yorisve bhrāmayed rekhā ṣoḍaśāṅgulasaṃmitām |
pūrvāhne gṛhṇatecchāyā yāmaparasthāṃ sucihnitām || 13-16 ||
aparasthena sūryeṇa śaktāyāṃ lāñchayet priye |
dhruveṇottaradikāṃsthālāñchayen matsyakena vā || 13-17 ||
tataḥ samālikhetpadmaṃ aṣṭapatraṃ sakarṇikam |
dvirkoṣṭakāstu saṃgṛhya aṣṭasaṃkhyopalakṣitām || 13-18 ||
śeṣāropya varārohe ekāntaritayogataḥ |
padmāṣṭakaṃ diśe likhya bāhye dvārāṇi cālikhet || 13-19 ||
vīśyārddhasaṃmitāṃ devi śobhā caiva prakalpayet |
ubhe śobhā ca tatprānā kapolāntaṃ samālikhet || 13-20 ||
tathā kaṇṭhaṃ ca varteta dvārametat prakīrtitam |
dvārāṣṭakavibhāgena navanābhaṃ puraṃ smṛtam || 13-21 ||
snātvā tu vidhivaddevi praviśedbhuvanaṃ tataḥ |
97a g] pūrvoktena vidhānena sakalīkaraṇādikam || 13-22 ||
tataḥ saṃpūjayed devi bhairavaṃ paramaṃ śivam |
praṇavenāsanaṃ datvā sarvāntaṃ varavarṇini || 13-23 ||
madhye saṃpūjayeddevaṃ bhairavaṃ parameśvaram |
pūrvoktena vidhānena aṅgaṣaṭkasamanvitam || 13-24 ||
patrāṣṭake nyasedvarṇaṃ pūrvādīśānataḥ kramāt |
sadāśivaṃ hakāreṇa evamādi varānane || 13-25 ||
prakṛtyantaṃ vijānīyānmadhya pīṭheśakalpanā |
dikpadmeṣu karṇikasthā aṣṭau devyaḥ prapūjayet || 13-26 ||
tatsthāne bhairavaṃ pūjya śeṣā varṇā yathākramāt |
śodhayecca prakṛtyāyāṃ śivāntaṃ surasundari || 13-27 ||
aiśānīdiśimārabhya madhyapīṭhaṃ viśodhayet |
yojayet parame tatve śive paramakāraṇe || 13-28 ||
evaṃ varṇastathā mantrā bhuvanāṃ śodhayet kramāt |
kālāgnyādi śivāntaṃ ca kalāvidhiḥ samāpayet || 13-29 ||
atha patrāṣṭake caiva bhairavāṣṭaka vinyaset |
aṣṭāṣṭakavibhāgena pūrvādīśānamantagāḥ || 13-30 ||
 madhyame tu dale devi vidyeśāstatra vinyaset |
evaṃ pūjā tu kartvyā sādhakaiḥ siddhimicchakaiḥ || 13-31 ||
puṣpadhūpādipānādyairnaivedairvividhaistathā |
pujitāḥ siddhi yacchanti sādhake bhāvitātmane || 13-32 ||
nāamasteṣāṃ pravakṣyāmi yathāvadanupūrvaśaḥ |
catuḥṣaṣṭi samākhyātā maṃtrāmnāyaprapālakāḥ || 13-33 ||
prathamomardataścaiva nandataśvīra vāhakaḥ |
śibiko yamaghaṇṭaśca kālarātrastathā paraḥ || 13-34 ||
yamadūtaścadeveśa caṃpako hariṇastathā |
kutukaścāṭṭahāsaśca caitraśca parikīrtitaḥ || 13-35 ||
jayantaścai rudraścaiva tathā cānto nigadyate | 
krodhano vidyujihvā ca ulkāpātotha garjanaḥ || 36 ||
varṣaṇaḥ sūrya candraśca nakṣatrotha grahastathā |
unmano mananāmaśca unmatta sumanistathā || 37 ||

70a] heruko ḍākinaścaiva ḍāmaro gajanāyakaḥ |
aśani tālujihvaśca harṣaṇo bhīṣaṇastathā || 13-38 ||
mārjāraścaiva siṃhaśca vānaro mūṣakastathā |
rauravaścaiva jhaṃkāraḥ kālamathana ḍohalaḥ || 13-39 || 
harikeśa piṅgalaśca tathānyo vaḍavānalaḥ |
jhaṃkāro jhaṃkino ghoraḥ sarvatokṣisukhāṇḍakaḥ] || 13-40 ||
bhairavo jharjharaścaiva picuvaktrastathā paraḥ |
bhadrako bhadrakālaśca haridrāmodarastathā || 13-41 ||
vāmacakradharaścaiva daṃṣṭrālotha surānanaḥ |
97b g] ete vīrāḥ samākhyātā mahābalabalīyasaḥ || 13-42 || 
kurvanti sādhakodeśaṃ jñānacakre prapūjitāḥ |
atha sādhayitum icched vidyāṃ kaścit subhāvitaḥ || 13-43 ||
tasyocchaṃge nyased devīṃ parāparāṃ mahābalām |
athavā sā mayākhyātā aparātha parā tathā || 13-44 ||
athavā yāmalākhyaṃ tu sādhitum icchate tu yaḥ |
bhairave tu pure devi catuḥṣaṣṭikulāhvaye || 13-45 ||
pūrvadig īśaparyantaṃ pūrvavad vinyaset kramāt | 
madhyame caiva vargākhyā vidyeśaiḥ saha saṃyutā || 13-46 || 
yadīcchet sādhituṃ devi mantraṃ vidyāsamanvitam |
vinyaset karṇikāyāṃ tu navabhis tu yathākramam || 13-47 ||
ṣaṇmāsāt siddhimāpnoti nātra kārya vicāraṇāt |
brahmaghnopi hi deveśi abdā khecaratāṃ brajet || 13-48 ||
kaulikena vidhānena pūjayetavidhānavit |
ṣoḍaśasvara ākhyātā mantraścaiva gaṇasya tu || 13-49 || 
rudraśaktivibhāgena pūjayeta gaṇaṃ gaṇam |
hrasvai rudrāstu saṃpūjyā dīrghaiścaiva tu śaktayaḥ || 13-50 ||
evaṃ pūjyā tataḥ kṛtvā śāstradṛṣṭena karmaṇā |
dhyānaṃ tatraprakartavyamarddhanārīśa kaulike || 13-51 ||
akṣarārthopadeśaṃ ca saṃpradāyaṃ ca kaulikam |
dhyānamevaṃ tu kartavyaṃ śāstradṛṣṭena karmaṇā || 13-52 ||
padmasthamakṣarārthe tu paṃkajamupadeśikam |
ucchaṃgasaṃpradāyaṃ tu arddhanārīśakaulike || 13-53 ||
etaccakra mahādevi nākhyātaṃ kasyacitpriye |
tvayāpi gopanīyaṃ tu yadicchetsiddhimātmanaḥ || 13-54 ||
asyaivasmaraṇāddevi dhyānapūjājapena ca |
jāyate tanmayo mantrī yogī vīragaṇasya tu || 13-55 ||
athavā mātṛyāgaṃ tu navanābhe yajetkramāt |
madhye tu bhairavaṃ pūjyā ādikṣāntamanukramāt || 13-56 ||
karṇikāyāṃ niveśitvā vargākhyā tu dale nyaset |
nādiphānta smṛtā yā tu utsaṅge bhairavasya tu || 13-57 ||
pūjayet tadvidhānena garbhayāge hyanukramāt |
padmāṣṭakaṃ tu yaccheyaṃ brahmaṇyādi kramādyajet || 13-58 ||
dale tu yoginī nyasya aṣṭāṣṭakavibhāgaśaḥ |
kaulikena tu dhyānena āsusiddhikaraṃ smṛtam || 13-59 ||
vīryeṇa dīpitā sarve dadante saṃpradāyakam |
vīryamantrasamāyogāt phalamāṃsavaśodikam || 13-60 ||
tvāṃ ca māṃ caiva saṃyogād yo jānāti sa siddhyati |
vīryaṃ caiva samākhyātaṃ yat tejaḥ pārameśvaram || 13-61 ||
98a g] mātṛkā tu kulaṃ proktaṃ mantramārge prakāśitāḥ |
mantramātṛkasaṃyogāt kulamārgamudāhṛtam || 13-62 ||
kulaṃ samūhamityuktaṃ devatānāṃ yaśasvini |
tāsāṃ saṃcāramārgaṃ yatkulamārgaṃ taducyate || 13-63 ||
anyārthena tathā cānyaṃ gṛhīta mandabuddhibhiḥ |
na te tatphalamaśroti samastaṃ yadvimārthitam || 13-64 ||
athātaḥ saṃpravakṣyāmi nāmāni tu samāsataḥ |
yoginīnāṃ varārohe aṣṭāṣṭakavibhāgaśaḥ || 13-65 ||
70b] mātryaṣṭakavibhedena mantre mantre visarpitāḥ |
catuḥ ṣaṣṭikulāmnāyāḥ sarve pīṭheṣu saṃsthitāḥ || 13-66 ||
anekairmantrabhedaistu anekairmantrasaṃkulaiḥ |
prasiddhāti tathānyāhurnāmāni tu nibodha me || 13-67 ||
yaistu vijñāta mātraistu sarvasiddhyaruho bhavet |
pūjognihavane caiva valikarma viśeṣataḥ || 13-68 ||
anyeṣu sarvakāryeṣu rakṣayanti na saṃśayaḥ |
pālayanti na sandehaḥ samayairluptakaṃ naram || 13-69 ||
sādhako vātha yo siddhā ācāryo vā viśeṣataḥ |
mantre? tantre tu yaḥ kaścidaniryukto varānane || 13-70 ||
tamevaṃ pālayantīha putravannātra saṃśayaḥ |
prathamārdinī caiva nandinī vīravāhinī || 13-71 ||
ciṃciṭī yamaghaṇṭā ca kālarātrī tathā parā |
yamadūtī ca deveśi catuṣpīṭhavimardinī || 13-72 ||
brahmaṇyāṃ śāstrato jñeyā yoginyo baladarpitāḥ |
campikā hariṇī caiva kulutī ca viśeṣataḥ || 13-73 ||
aṭṭahāsī ca caitrī ca jayantī cairuḍī tathā |
oḍrī ca yoginī raudrī sarvapīṭhavimardanī || 13-74 ||
māheśvarī kulodbhūtā sarvasiddhipradāyikāḥ |
kurvanti sādhakādeśaṃ tadājñāyā na saṃśayaḥ || 13-75 ||
krodhanī vidyujjihvā ca ulkāpātī ca garjanī |
varṣiṇī sūryacandrī ca nakṣatrīśagrahī tathā || 13-76 ||
etāścaivograyoginyo yoginīkulanāyikā |
kaumārīkulasaṃbhūtāḥ sarvasiddhyarthadāyikāḥ || 13-77 ||
unmanī mananābhī ca unmattā sumanī tathā |
herukī ḍākinī caiva ḍāmarī gajanāyikāḥ || 13-78 || 
vaiṣṇavīkulajā hyetāḥ sādhake siddhidāyikāḥ |
aśanī tālujihvā ca harṣaṇī bhīṣaṇī tathā || 13-79 ||
mārjārī siṃhavaktrā ca vānarī mūṣakī tathā |
vārāhyaṃsā samākhyātāḥ sarvasiddhyarthadāyikāḥ || 13-80 ||
kurvanti sādhakodeśaṃ dadanti carumuttamam |
rauravī śaṃkarī kālī mathanī ḍohalī tathā || 13-81 ||
98b g] harikeśī piṅgalā ca vaḍavānalikā parā |
aindrāṇīkulasaṃbhūtā yoginyastu mahābalāḥ || 13-82 ||
jhaṃkārī jhaṅkinī ghorā sarvatokhimukhā tathā |
bhairavī jharjharī caiva picuvaktrā tathā parāḥ || 13-83 ||
cāmuṇḍā sā smṛtā hyetāḥ sādhakādeśakārikāḥ | 
bhadrakī bhadrakālī ca haridāmodarī tathā || 13-84 ||
vāmanī cakriṇī caiva daṃṣṭrālī ca surā nanī | 
aghoryaṃ sā mahādevi yoginyo baladarpitāḥ || 13-85 ||
kurvanti sādhakādeśaṃ jñānacakre pradīpitāḥ |
catuḥ ṣaṣṭhi kulāhvayāścaruṃ ca sāṃpradāyikam || 13-86 ||
dadanti sādhakendrasya khecaratvaṃ ca sundari |
guḍikākāśagamanaṃ sarvamevaṃ prasidhhyati || 13-87 ||
aṣṭāṣṭakakrameṇaiva pūjayed vīranāyike |
rudraśaktividhānena pūjayedathavā priye || 13-88 ||
iti bhairavasrotasi mahātantre vidyāpīṭhe saptakoṭipramāṇe śrītantrasadbhāve navātmādhikārastrayodaśaḥ paṭalaḥ || 13 ||




* * * * * * bandhayed bhuvanatrayam |ākarṣet brahmaviṣṇvindrā mānuṣāṇāṃ ca kā kathā ||daṇḍasthā tu yadā devī śoṣayet sāgarāṇyapi |triśūlasthā yadā devī āsarvaṃ nāśayet śagāra |śekharasthā yadā devī śaṃ ? ||
caturdaśa paṭala śloka saṃkhyā 4 se śloka saṃkhyā 31-32 ke bīca me kahī chūṭā hai |


devyuvāca

jñātaṃ deva mayā sarvaṃ navatatvaṃ vibhāgataḥ |
yajanaṃ padabhedena navanābhe yathāsthitam || 14-1 ||
rudraśaktistvayā deva sūcitāni tu varṇitāḥ |
na jñātā śrotumicchāmi rudraśaktividhiṃ śubham || 14-2 ||
rudraśaktistu kiṃ deva nyāsaṃ ca tadanantaram |
dhyānaṃ japaṃ tathā dīkṣā karmāṇi ca vada prabho || 14-3 ||
svāṅgavidhiniyogaṃ ca yathā tasyaiva saṃsthitam |

bhairava uvāca 

71a] pūjatvaṃ kiṃ tu deveśi yenedaṃ pṛcchito hyaham || 14-4 ||
ātmīyaṃ tu saparyā? kiṃ na buddhasi pārvati |
tathāpi kathayiṣyāmi rudraśakto vidhiḥ śubham || 14-5 ||
nyāsaṃ caiva viśeṣeṇa madīyairvarṇakai śubhaiḥ |
yathā purā mayā proktaṃ nyāsāṅgavidhivat tathā || 14-6 ||
dhyānārcanavidhānaṃ hi yāgaṃ ca tadanantaram |
dīkṣā svāhāniyogena karmāṇi ca yathā śṛṇu || 14-7 ||
varṇarūpāstu pañcāśadyairnibaddhā tvadī tanu |
tvāṃ ca māṃtra * deveśi avinābhāvasaṃsthitaiḥ || 14-8 |||
yo jānāti varārohe sa siddhyanti na saṃśayaḥ |
evaṃ nyāsaṃ tataḥ kṛtvā paścād dhyānaṃ samācaret || 14-9 ||
surūpā sustanā caiva dvibhujā ca trilocanā |
sarvālaṃkārasaṃpannā jaṭāmakuṭamaṇḍitā || 14-10 ||
kapālaṃ vāmahastasthamamṛtākhyena pūritam |
99a g] khānapānaratā dhyeyā hārakeyūrabhūṣitā || 14-11 ||
itthaṃ rūpadharī devī praṇatārtivināśanī |
karṇikāyāṃ nyased devī patreṣvaṃgā niveśayet || 14-12 ||
āgneyyeśānanairṛtyāṃ vāyavyāṃ diśi saṃsthitāḥ |
netraṃ netreṣu vinyasya astraṃ digdalasaṃsthitam || 14-13 ||
evaṃ pūjāṃ tataḥ krtvā japaṃ paścāt samācaret |
mālinīṃ japamānasya siddhyante'ṣṭau ca siddhayaḥ || 14-14 ||
līlayā sādhakendrasya nātra kāryā vicāraṇāt |
lakṣamekaṃ japitvā tu daśāṃśaṃ homayet punaḥ || 14-15 ||
homam kṛtvā vidhānena paścāt karma samācaret |
śantyādīni tu karmāṇi āpyāyana viśeṣataḥ || 14-16 ||
kaṇṭhe caiva prakūrvīta tatsarvaṃ karmasiddhaye |
śikhikaṇṭhaṃ samāyuktaṃ bhrūmadhye dhyāyate yadi || 14-17 ||
naraśiramāle devyā daṇḍanyākarayedadhaḥ |
gṛhyatya yasareṇaiva śakto'pi hi varānane || 14-18 ||
karṇasthāṃ tu yadā devī pātayecchoṣaṃyet kṣaṇāt |
bhūṣaṇasthā yadā devī bhūṣaṇaṃ prekṣate tadā || 14-19 ||
nāsasthāṃ tu yadā devī ucchvāsaṃ harate kṣaṇāt |
vācasthāṃ tu yadā devī mūkatvaṃ kārayet tadā || 14-20 ||
rasanasthā yadā devī rasaṃ nāśayate kṣaṇāt |
śikharasthā yadā devī bhīṣaṇaṃ tridaśānyapi || 14-21 ||
vāmapārśvasthitā devī āśu coccāṭayed dhruvam |
marmasthā tu yadā devī sadyo melakadāyikā || 14-22 ||
tasya cānte sthitā devī śaṇṭīkaraṇamuttamam |
karasthāṃ tu yadā devī sarvāriṃ gilayet kṣaṇāt || 14-23 ||
kapāladvayasaṃyuktā pumāṃ vidveṣakārikāḥ |
dvijapañca likhen madhyenābhiyuktā triṣaṣṭhake || 14-24 ||
vāmahastena saṃyojyā lekhayecchilamadhyataḥ |
puṃstriyāmathavā vyāghramanvaṃvā duṣṭacetasam || 14-25 ||
stambhayedraṃjakasthaṃ tu udaraṃ hṛdi saṃyutāḥ |
mālinī tena yuktātu stambhaye divyapañcakam || 14-26 ||
vajrālepitalepena āśusiddhikarī nṛṇām |
dakṣiṇastanamārūḍhā haṃsamadhye niveśitā || 14-27 ||
lepitā brahmasatvena dṛṣṭivandhakarī nṛṇām |
ātmasaṃsthā yadā devī nitambena samanvitā || 14-28 ||
sādhyasūtravaco yuktāpātayet kākatuṇḍagā |
bhagavañca karotyeṣā sadyaḥ pratyayakārikā || 14-29 ||
śikharasthā mahābhāgā śikhareṇa samanvitā |
karṇabhūṣaṇa pūrveṇa likhed vai bhītaphalahake || 14-30 ||
viṣadhūpitapuṣpeṇa lepayet khadirardinā |
99b g] praharārddhagate mantrī sādhyamuccāṭayed bhṛśam || 14-31 ||
guhyasthā tu yadā devī vaktramadhye niveśitāḥ|

71b] ātmānaṃ tu yadā devī śūladaṇḍasamanvitāḥ || 14-32 ||
lekhayetkūrmapṛṣṭhe tu sādhyadehasamanvitam |
praharārdhagate mantrī nikhaned gṛhamadhyataḥ || 14-33 ||
saptāhādvekṣatāṃ yāti śakropi hi varānane |
vāmapādaṃ punarnyasya kapālaṃ patitaṃ punaḥ || 14-34 ||
śūladaṇḍakṛtādhāraṃ prāṇanāsāvibhūṣitam |
vāmapādaṃ punarnyasya kapālaṃ patitaṃ punaḥ || 14-35 ||
aṣṭamyāṃ vā caturdaśyāṃ likhetkapālasaṃpuṭe |
nisāṭāvaya sā mantrī sādhyasūtrasamanvitam || 14-36 ||
lepayeta kareṇaiva kīlayet kīlakaiḥ punaḥ |
viṣadhūpitadhūpena kīlayettridaśānapi || 14-37 ||
kapālasthā yadā devī śūlamadhye niveśitāḥ |
sādhyadehagatā devī mārayedbhuvanatrayam || 14-38 ||
ātmasthāṃ tu yadā devī kaṇṭhamadhye niveśitāḥ |
vāmahastena saṃyuktā dakṣajānuniveśitāḥ || 14-39 ||
tilakena samāyuktā trailokyaṃ vaśamānayet |
kapālaṃ patitaṃ kṛtvā vāmapādaṃ niyojayet || 14-40 ||
tṛtīyaṃ nayanaṃ caiva tilakena samanvitam |
punareva nyasetpādaṃ kapālaṃ patitaṃ punaḥ || 14-41 ||
dakṣiṇe bāhuke devi dakṣajānuniveśitaḥ |
punareva nyasetpādaṃ kapālaṃ patitaṃ punaḥ || 14-42 ||
caturthaṃ daśanaṃ caiva bhūṣaṇena vibhūṣitam |
punareva nyasetpādaṃ kapālaṃ patitaṃ punaḥ || 14-43 ||
punarevanyaset taṃ tu bhūṣaṇena vibhūṣitam |
vāmapādaṃ punarnyasya kapālaṃ patitaṃ punaḥ || 14-44 ||
vācayā daśanaṃ yuktaṃ prathamaṃ tu mahāmate |
vāmapādaṃ punarnyasya kapālaṃ patitaṃ punaḥ || 14-45 ||
kevalaṃ hṛdayaṃ mantrī vācāṃ bhinnaṃ samālikhet |
punarevaṃ nyasetpādaṃ kapālaṃ patitaṃ punaḥ || 14-46 ||
jihvāpātanasaṃyuktamakṣaraṃ tadanantaram |
punareva nyasetpādaṃ kapālaṃ patitaṃ punaḥ || 14-47 ||
śikhārūḍhā likhejjihvā vinyaset tadanantaram |
punarevaṃ nyasetpādaṃ kapālaṃ patitaṃ punaḥ || 14-48 ||
kaṇṭhādhāraṃ likhenmānaṃ vācāyuktaṃ vicakṣaṇaḥ |
punarevaṃ nyasetpādaṃ kapālaṃ patitaṃ punaḥ || 14-49 ||
kevalaṃ tu likhetprāṇaṃ vācā bhinnaṃ tu kārayet |
punareva nyasetpādaṃ kapālaṃ patitaṃ punaḥ || 14-50 ||
sādhyanāmāntasaṃyuktaṃ lekhayedbhūrjapatrakaiḥ |
vilohite lakṣakena rāsabhakṣatajena tu || 14-51 ||
100a g] sādhyapādarajorājī madanena samanvitam |
kārayetpratimāṃtena lepayed viṣalaktake || 14-52 ||
muśalolūkhalaṃ gṛhya sthāpayatnena saṃpuṭā |
śmaśāne cārddharātre ca sakhāyaiḥ parivāritaḥ || 14-53 ||
nagno muktaśikho mantrī raktasaṃkruddhalocanaḥ |
dakṣiṇāṃ diśimāśritya muśalaṃ bhrāmayettataḥ || 14-54 ||
śatamekaṃ japitvā tu kuṭṭayetkruddhacetasaḥ |
mriyate nātra sandehaḥ punarjanma na vidyate || 14-55 ||
na tithirnacanakṣatro nopavāso vidhīyate |
sarvakarmapravṛtto'pi kruddho mārayete dhruvam || 14-56 ||
kevalaṃ nayanaṃ nyasya tṛtīyaṃ vācayā yutam |
dakṣiṇe śravaṇasthaṃ tu nitamba tilakānvitam || 14-57 ||
punareva nyasedvāhuṃ bhinnaṃ caiva tu mānunā |
dvitīyaṃ daśanaṃ caiva dvidhā bhūtaṃ tu kārayet || 14-58 ||
dakṣiṇaśravaṇasthena bhūṣaṇena vibhūṣitaḥ |
tamevadaśanaṃ mantrī āhanedakṣajānunā || 14-59 ||
dakṣupāde samāsīnā jihvā yatnena cālikhet |
śikhājihvāyutā nyasya dvitīyaṃ daśanaṃ punaḥ || 14-60 ||

72a] dakṣajānusamāyuktaṃ pādaṃ jihvānvitaṃ punaḥ |
śikhāmadhomukhī nyasya jihvāyuktā samālikhet || 14-61 ||
akṣarāntaritaṃ nāma likhed yatnena karpaṭe |
goraktaṃ dīpatailaṃ tu strīrojonmamalaṃ tathā || 14-62 ||
nibārkayośca patrāṇi lāṃgalī kusumāni tu |
etaiśca saha manujo likhet kapālasaṃpuṭe || 14-63 ||
sarvatra lepayitvā tu dhūpayet phalguṣe nṛṇām |
nikhanetkākavelāyāṃ gacched vidvān makha?drumam || 14-64 ||
vaibhītake tu saṃsthāpya koṭare tu mahātmanām |
bhūyo hyadhomukhī mantrī gacchet sthānaviśeṣataḥ || 14-65 ||
paharārddhagataṃ yāvad gṛhya sādhyaṃ karāmbujaiḥ |
hasite valkate sādhyo nṛtyate bhīṣate tathā || 14-66 ||
vṛkṣā gṛhāḥ samākampyed gṛhītaṃ sarvabhūtakaiḥ |
unmattakaraṇaṃ proktaṃ sadyaḥpratyayakārakam || 14-67 ||
etatpāpakaraṃ proktaṃ mahāduṣkṛtakārakam |
gopitavyaṃ prayatnena na kathya yaṃsya kasyacit || 14-68 ||
athānyat sampravakṣyāmi vidyā caiva yathoddhṛtā |
oṃ cāmuṇḍe khukhaḍhiḍhaḍhaṃ niśācāri khakhapiñcchaṃ vāriṇivicce svāhā |
kevalaṃ mūlamālikhya lāñchitaṃ tilakena tu || 14-69 ||
punareva mahāvīraśikharaṃ dakṣiṇaṃ nyaset |
100b g] vāmaṃ ca śikharaṃ nyasya padasyānte vyavasthitāḥ || 14-70 ||
punareva nyaset sādhyaṃ bhūyo mantraṃ samālikhet |
punaḥ paścāllikhenmantrī sādhyaṃ ca tadanantaram || 14-71 ||
anyonyagranthitā bījād bhūrjapatre samālikhet |
āvṛtya bhūrjapatre tu hṛdisthaṃ kārayed budhaḥ || 14-72 ||
adhasyāt kārayet sādhyaṃ sādhakaṃ copari sthitam |
cchardirājyākṛtiṃ kṛtvā rocanāyā samālikhet || 14-73 ||
mṛṇmaye pātrake caiva sthāpya yatnena saṃpuṭe |
cchardirājyā punaścaiva paścānnicchidra kārayet || 14-74 ||
prayatnena mahāyogī jalamadhye nidhāpayet |
yadi kruddho bhavenmantrī tatkṣaṇād vaśamānayet || 14-75 ||
eṣa devi mahāyantraḥ kulamārgagato bhuvi |
rudraśaktyā samaṃ jñātvā kasya siddhirna jāyate || 14-76 ||
sarvakāmapravṛttasya siddhirbhavati śāśvatī |
māraṇākarṣaṇaṃ caiva vidveṣoccāṭanaṃ tathā || 14-77 ||
yadyatpāragate karma tattasya bhavate phalam |
athānyaṃ saṃpravakṣyāmi dīkṣā caiva samāsataḥ || 14-78 ||
yayā vijñātamātrāyā mucyate mocayeti ca |
rudraśaktiṃ yajenmantrī sthaṇḍile maṇḍalepi vā || 14-79 ||
adhivāsyatataḥ śiṣyaṃ praveśed yāgamaṇḍale |
puṣpamocāpanaṃ kṛtvā daṇḍavat pātayed bhuvi || 14-80 ||
utthāpayet tataḥ śiṣyamānayegnisamīpataḥ |
vāmajaṃghāsamāyuktaḥ ātmā daṇḍāsane sthitaḥ || 14-81 ||
vāmabhūṣaṇasaṃyuktaṃ daṇḍākrāntaṃ japed yadā |
dvādaśasvarasaṃbhinnaṃ nāntaritayogataḥ || 14-82 ||
pāśastobhaṃ bhavetkṣipraṃ nātra kāryā vicāraṇāt |
rudraśaktyā tu ye varṇā daṇḍākrāntāstadāsanāḥ || 14-83 ||
dvādaśasvarasaṃbhinnā stobhayanti carācaram |
rudraśaktistu yā devi guhyaśaktipuṭīkṛtāḥ || 14-84 ||
dātrī dīkṣā samākhyātā nirbhāgyānāṃ ca bhāgyadā |
prāṇaṃ daṇḍāsanāsīnaṃ bhūṣaṇena vibhūṣitam || 14-85 ||
vāmakarṇasthitenaiva guhyasthenāmalīkṛtaṃ |
bhruvormadhye nyased bījaṃ śikhikaṇṭhasamaprabham || 14-86 ||

72b] pātayeti sa vegena drumo vajrahato yathā |
kampanaṃ stobhanaṃ pātraṃ kurute'tra na saṃśayaḥ || 14-87 ||
prayuktaṃ sādhakendreṇa nātra kuryād vicāraṇāt |
athānyaṃ saṃpravakṣyāmi rudrāvalividhiṃ śubham || 14-88 ||
101a g] rudraśaktijapennaiva phalaṃ yasyaiva yādṛśam |
śrīkaṇṭhena tu saṃbhinnā rudraśaktiṃ japet sadā || 14-89 ||
śrīkaṇṭhapadamāpnoti prabhūtā balisaṃyutā |
evamekaikarudreṇa yadā bhinnā tu mālinī || 14-90 ||
japate sādhakendrastu tadeva padamāpnuyāt |
etadvijayamākhyātaṃ bhogyabhoktṛtvalakṣaṇam || 14-91 ||
rudraśaktivibhāgaṃ ca phalamaṃ savasāsthitam |
yasya yad bhogya muddiṣṭaṃ padaṃśānukramāgatam || 14-92 ||
brahmatvaṃ caiva viṣṇutvaṃ rudratvamīśameva vā |
śivatvaṃ caiva deveśi yakṣanāgakumārakam || 14-93 ||
āsuraṃ caiva gāndharvamendraṃ gāruḍameva vā |
yo yasya bhavate hyaṃsastattasya dadate prabhuḥ || 14-94 ||

devyuvāca

aṃśakānuvaśā prāptiḥ kathitā parameśvaraḥ |
tadvaśāṃ tu smṛtā dīkṣā mantrārādhanameva ca || 14-95 ||
sādhanāni viśeṣeṇa aṃsahīnā na siddhidā |
tasmādaṃśa parīkṣyaṃ tu kathayasva prasādataḥ || 14-96 ||

bhairava uvāca

śṛṇu devi pravakṣyāmi aṃsakānāṃ tu lakṣaṇam |
mantrāṃśaṃ caiva nāmāṃśe bhāvāṃśaśca tṛtīyakaḥ || 14-97 ||
devyāṅgaścaiva vijñeyaścaturthaṃ parameśvari |
aṃśakarmaniyogaṃ tu śṛṇuṣva kathayāmyaham || 14-98 ||
yena vīro'balā vā susiddhibhājo bhaved dhruvam |
siddhisādhyasusiddhaśca ariśceti caturthakam || 14-99 ||
mantraścaturvidho jñeyaḥ sarvatantreṣu nānyathā |
siddhaḥ siddhyati vai śīghraṃ sādhye yāgavratādibhiḥ || 14-100 ||
suddiddhastatkṣaṇādeva pāṭhamātrādadhītavān |
ariścaiva nikṛnteta prajaptasādhakena tu || 14-101 ||
ādisiddhāśca siddhānte mantraśca bhavate yadi |
acireṇaiva kālenaṃ siddhirastu bhaviṣyati || 14-102 ||
sādhyantādi samāyukto atikṛcchreṇa siddhyati |
ādāvanteṣu siddhaśca trailokye tu phalapradaḥ || 14-103 ||
prathame tu bhavecchatru rantecaiva hi tādṛśaḥ |
īdṛśaṃ lakṣaṇaṃ dṛṣṭvā dūrataḥ parivarjayet || 14-104 ||
ādisiddhāntasādhyastu mantrastu bhavate yadi |
kalpoktaṃ tu tathā karma kṛtaṃ cāpi caturguṇam || 14-105 ||
sādhyate bhaktiyuktastu agraheṇa na saṃśayaḥ |
ādisādhyantasiddhaśca pūrvoktena krameṇa tu || 14-106 ||
siddhiṃ dadātu vipulān sādhakasya varānane |
ubhayato bhavet sādhyaṃ ṣaḍguṇenaiva siddhyati || 14-107 ||
sādhyāntastu susiddhādimantreṣu bhavate yadi |
vidhānenaiva deveśi ṣaḍguṇenaiva siddhyati || 14-108 ||
101b g] susiddhādi prasiddhānto divyasiddhipradāyakaḥ |
siddhāntaśca susiddhādinayaṃ vāpi tathaiva ca || 14-109 ||
susiddhādi tu yo mantraḥ siddhāntaśca sulocane |
arīṇāṃ tu śataṃ madhye hanti caiva na saṃśayaḥ || 14-110 ||
ripurādau tathā cānte varjitavyaḥ sadā budhaiḥ |
ripuṇā dūṣito mantrairmadhyaṃ te bhavate yadi || 14-111 ||
arthavambuśarīrasya nāśaktaṃ bhavate kṣaṇāt |
tasmāt sarvaprayatnena arimantra vivarjayet || 14-112 ||
siddhānāmapi sidhyasta sasiddhena tathādhikam |
ataḥ paraṃ pravakṣyāmi mantrāṇāṃ gaṇako vidhim || 14-113 ||

73a] yena vijñātamātreṇa sādhakaḥ siddhibhāg bhavet |
ekānte ca śucau deśe puṣpaprakarasaṅkule || 14-114 ||
sugandhigandhasaṃyukte sudhūpāmodamodite |
ācāryo mantrasannaddhaḥ prastāraṃ tu samālikhet || 14-115 ||
ādihāntā samālikhya prayatnena varānane |
napṃsakagaṇaṃ tyaktvā saṃhāreṇa vivarjitāḥ || 14-116 ||
paścānmantraṃ samālikhya varṇasaṃkhyākrameṇa tu |
vibhajyeta varārohe vyaṃjayaṃti svarākṣarāḥ || 14-117 ||
mantrākṣarā pṛthak kāryā viśiṣṭaṃ suravarjitāḥ |
mantrasādhyo gataṃ nāma saṃyogākṣaravarjitam || 14-118 ||
lokaprasiddhamevoktaṃ pitṛmātṛvikhyāpitam |
likhedevaṃ varārohe yāvanmantrāntamāgatam || 14-119 ||
nāmabījānsamārabhya maṃtrabījāntamāgatam |
gaṇayitvā prayatnena yallabdhaṃ sthāpaye naghe || 14-120 ||
evaṃ savārṇekaṃ gaṇya yāvanmantrāntamāgatam |
prathamaṃ tu bhavet siddho dvitīya sādhya ucyate || 14-121 ||
susiddhaśca tṛtīyastu ariścetiścaturthakaḥ |
siddhaḥ sādhya susiddhaśca ariśceti caturthakaḥ || 14-122 ||
ekatra gaṇayet prajño yāvanmantraṃ samāpyate |
mantrānte kārayetsaṃkhyā yā tatraiva bhaviṣyati || 14-123 ||
siddhaścaiva susiddhaśca ekīkṛtvā vidhānavit |
sādhyaścaiva ariścaiva punarevaṃ daśairguṇāt || 14-124 || 
caturbhiśca haredbhāgaṃ yaccheṣamadhaśodhitam |
tejaṃśā tasya nirdiṣṭā sādhakasya mahātape || 14-125 ||
atha sadbhāva śeṣaṃ tu śeṣaṃ kiñcid bhaviṣyati |
praharāste samākhyātā nātra kāryā vicāraṇāt || 14-126 ||
evaṃ mantrātmakaṃ jñātvā tato mantraṃ samarpayet |
japaṃ homaṃ tato dhyānaṃ kārayet sādhakottamaḥ || 14-127 ||
siddhyate nātra sandeha iti satyaṃ varānane |
dhyānaṃ pūjā japo homo bhāvātmakaphalapradam || 14-128 ||
102a g] bhāvahīnaṃ na siddhyeta tasmād bhāvaṃ tu lakṣayet |
yasya puṃsasya yo bhāvo devarākṣasamānuṣaḥ || 14-129 ||
tasya tena phalāvāptirbhāvāṃśakavidasya tu |
devāṃśyo devabhaktastu bhogesveva phalotsukaḥ || 14-130 ||
asuradveṣiṇo nityaṃ sarvabhūtahite rataḥ |
daityāṃśo gārvitaḥ dhīraḥ kāmāsaktasunirghṛṇaḥ || 14-131 ||
asuroyuddhaśīlā ca deva deṣī ca nirmayaḥ | 
vivarānveṣako bhogī gandharvo gītatatparaḥ || 14-132 ||
miṣṭāśatato nityaṃ vahubhiḥ parivāritaḥ |
nāgāṃśo dīrghaśāyī ca pūtivaktro mahādyutiḥ || 14-133 ||
dīrgharogī ca suśroṇi parvatodyānatatparaḥ |
harmāśakta śuciścaiva nāma ityabhidhīyate || 14-134 ||
sarvasaṃgrahaśīlī ca dhanavān kṛpaṇo mataḥ |
grahile gahvare ramye puṣpāḍhye kandare vane || 14-135 ||
ekākī ramate nityaṃ prāsāde harmyaśobhane |
yakṣaḥ piśācaścapale ekāntecchiṣṭatatparaḥ || 14-136 ||
chāndheṣī asaṃgī ca niśauco niśi tatparaḥ |
rākṣasopiśunaḥ kṣudro māṃsāśī madyatatparaḥ || 14-137 ||
vedadviṣo guruhmaśca sarvāvasthe rataḥ sadā |
indrā * dharmavāṃ śauryo vicitravasanapriyaḥ || 14-138 ||
hastyaśvānāṃ priyo nityaṃ mantrāṇāṃ caiva tatparaḥ |
rājyārthe sādhayet mantrā strī bhogeṣu manotsukaḥ || 14-139 ||
agnyaṃśastīvrakarmāṇaḥ kuśīlo havanaḥ priyaḥ |
piṅgakeśaśca raktākṣo dharmaśīlasamanvitaḥ || 14-140 ||
śaucācārasamāyuktaḥ satyavādī dṛḍhavrataḥ |
vedavratadharo nityaṃ yukto yuktavicārakaḥ || 14-141 ||

73b] yamāṃsaḥ kṛṣṇavarṇaṃ ca raktākṣo nirghṛṇaḥ sudhīḥ |
dharmaśāstrarato nityaṃ satyavanto dṛḍhavrataḥ || 14-142 ||
yamāṃsadakathā śaktyo devabrāhmaṇapūjakaḥ |
naiṛtaḥpitṛbhaktastu rūkṣodhvāṃkṣa kṛttirnaraḥ || 14-143 ||
raktākṣaḥ piśunaḥ pretaḥ pitṛkārye ca tatparaḥ |
varuṇo rūpavān dhanya snigdhaḥ śāntavapuḥ priyaḥ || 14-144 ||
vātāṃśaścapala kṣudro rukṣo māyānvitaḥ priye |
bahvālāpī cālpasāro asadvādī ca cañcalaḥ || 14-145 ||
somāṃśaḥ kāntisaṃyukto mṛduḥ saumyākṣasaṃyutaḥ |
priyaṃvado dhanāḍhyastu śubha * priyadarśanaḥ || 14-146 ||
brahmāṃśo brahmabhaktastu vedābhyāsī dṛḍhavrataḥ |
102b g] vedadharmarato nityaṃ satyaśaucasamanvitaḥ || 14-147 ||
viṣṇvaṃ?śo viṣṇubhaktiśca samadṛṣṭi śubhānvitaḥ | 
daityadveṣī ca vijñeyo rudrāṃśo liṅgatatparaḥ || 14-148 ||
śiva bhakto mahāvīraḥ satyavanto ?]] dṛḍhavrataḥ |
adṛṣṭaphalabhogeṣu vidyāpadamanotsukaḥ || 14-149 ||
mantraṃ vā jñānayogaṃ ca dīkṣā vā mokṣa hetave |
anneṣapati sarvatra prākṛteṣu na rajyate || 14-150 ||
devānā malya bhogaṃ ca anityaṃ caiva manyate |
kāmāṃśa subhago nityaṃ kāmeśvaramivakṣitau || 14-151 || 
bhoktyā sarvatra jāyeta kāmaikagatacetasā |
evamaṃśānurūpeṇa jñātvā devi na saṃśayaḥ || 14-152 ||
kartavyo'nugrahaḥ prājñaiḥ śāstradṛṣṭena karmaṇā |
devyaṃśo'thapravakṣyāmi yathāvadanupūrvaśaḥ || 14-153 ||
śuddhāṃśaścaiva miśrāṃśo jñātvā bhāvānurūpataḥ |
brahmāṇī caiva rudrāṇī kaumārī vaiṣṇavī tathā || 14-154 ||
vārāhī ca tathendrāṇī yogeśī paramā smṛtāḥ |
ete cāṃśā smṛtāḥ śuddhā veditavyā prayatnataḥ || 14-155 ||
yasya yo bhavate bhāvastadaṃśosau vijānata |
bhinnāṃśothabhaveddevi yāmayo hṛdayena tu || 14-156 ||
ekaikāyāstu yo bhedastat pravakṣyāmyaśeṣataḥ |
brahmamāheśvarī devi brahmakaumārikā tathā || 14-157 ||
brahmavaiṣṇavikā caiva brahmavārāhikā tathā? |
brahma indrāṇikā devi brahmayogeśipūjitāḥ || 14-158 ||
yathā caikā tathā sarvā bhinnāṃśādikrameṇa tu |
ekonapañcāśatirdevyo bhinnāścaiva parasparam || 14-159 ||
bhinnāṃśasyaiva bhinnāstā bhaviṣyanti phalapradāḥ |
śuddhāṃśe caiva śuddhāṃśā veditavyā phalapradāḥ || 14-160 ||

devyuvāca

śuddhāṃśaścaiva miśrāṃśo jñātuṃ caiva na śakyate |
tasmādevaṃ prasādaṃ tu kartavyaṃ nirṇayaṃ mama || 14-161 ||

bhairava uvāca

śṛṇu devi pravakṣyāmi aṃśakānāṃ yathāvidhiḥ |
kathayāmi na sandehastvatprītyā surasundari || 14-162 ||
pāśastobhena deveśi jñāyate siddhiniścayaḥ |
arisiddhastathā sādhya sasiddhaśca caturthakaḥ || 14-163 ||
yathā niścayameteṣāṃ tathā śṛṇu maśeṣataḥ |
tiṣṭhate kampate nātha calate patatepi ca || 14-164 ||
arimādi vijānīyāt sarvāmaṃśāṃsvarūpataḥ |
yādivarṇāṣṭakaṃ devi hāntaṃ yāvatsamāsataḥ || 14-165 ||
kālarudreṇa saṃbhinnā mahātejasamanvitāḥ |
śikharasthā vāmagā sarve ṣaṭkayuktāḥ śikhānvitāḥ || 14-166 ||
103a g] arddhendukalayā śaktinādavinduvibhūṣitāḥ |
oṃkārasvarasaṃyuktā yojayet sarvakarmasu || 14-167 ||
prathame tu smṛtā vrāhmī evamādi vijānataḥ |
vargākṣarasamāyogā vāmākṣarapadānvitāḥ || 14-168 ||

74a] uccārāntāntasaṃyuktā stobhayet sacarācaram |
yasya devyāstu yo vargaḥ kūṭākṣara samanvitaḥ || 14-169 ||
tasya tena bhavet stobhastadaṃśo'sau varānane |
akṣarākṣaramekaikaṃ nirvāhaṃ kūṭabhedataḥ || 14-170 ||
patate yena bījena tadaṃśosau varānane |
evaṃ bhinnāṃśakaṃ śuddhaṃ jñātvā samyag vidhānataḥ || 14-171 ||
stobhayet prayato devi tato mantraṃ samarpayet |
samantrastasya siddhyeta vāñchitārtha phalapradaḥ || 14-172 ||
trividhaḥ sasyate stobho dīkṣā maṃśaparīkṣaṇe |
yaysa cālabdhabhāvasya stobho vāpi bhaviṣyati || 14-173 ||
śa yatiḥ sarvasiddhīnāṃ ārho bhavati śobhane |
tīvreṇa khecaraṃ yānti pātena varavarṇini || 14-174 ||
madhyamena tu pātālaṃ adhamaṃ sukhaṃ jīvati |
yāni kānyapi karmāṇi uttamasyaiva siddhyati || 14-175 ||
yasya stobho na vidyeta durātmā pāpakarmiṇaḥ |
tasya dīkṣā na kartavyā yadi nirmānuṣī prajā || 14-176 ||

devyuvāca

kecideva vadantyevaṃ mantrarūpo na stubhyati |
mantranyāsekta ve caiva parabhāvairadhiṣṭhite || 14-177 ||
stobha nasya na jāyeta evamādi vadanti te |

bhairava uvāca

pūrvajāti smarāte cārudraṃ dṛṣṭvā tu stubhyati || 14-178 ||
śuddhakāyasya deveśi mantrasaṃkramaṇasya ca |
tatva rūpasya deveśi stobho bhavati nānyathā || 14-179 ||
paśurūpo durātmāno dharmādharmasamanvitaḥ | 
na tasya stobhamāyāti nibaddhaḥ paśujātyayā || 14-180 ||
mātrāṃśena tu cāvesāṃ śarīre yasya jāyate |
samantrastasyadātavyo dantaścaiva tu siddhyati || 14-181 ||
stobhanaṃ bandhanaṃ mokṣaṃ pūrvaṃ hi kathitaṃ mayā |
sāṃprataṃ maṃśabhedaṃ tu ādimadhyaprakāśitam || 14-182 ||
stobhapātānusāreṇa kathayāmyanupūrvaśaḥ |
darśanāt sparśanāt stobhastīvrapātaḥ prakīrtitaḥ || 14-183 ||
daśapañca tathā viṃśanmadhyamaḥ parikīrtitaḥ |
triṃśaiścaiva tu deveśi catvāriṃśattathādhamaḥ pañcāśadāhutyairuttamaḥ parikīrtitaḥ || 14-185 ||
103b g] śatena madhyamo jñeyo dviśataiścādhamaḥ smṛtaḥ | 
sādhyastu triśatairdevi uttamaḥ parikīrtitaḥ || 14-186 ||
ṣaṭśatairmadhyamaṃ caiva adhamaṃ tu navaḥśataiḥ |
dvādaśairuttamaṃ jñeyaṃ ṣoḍaśairmadhyamaṃ smṛtam || 14-187 ||
viṃśateradhamaṃ proktaṃ abhibhedaṃ varānane |
evaṃ dvādaśabhedena aṃśā vai dvādaśa smṛtāḥ || 14-188 ||
niraṃśo yastu suśroṇi nāsau yogya śivādhvare | 
na tasya kriyate dīkṣā varjayet pāpakarmiṇam || 14-189 ||
luptācāro vratabhraṣṭaḥ kāṣṭapāṣāṇa eva ca |
prasādād dīkṣito vātha gurudrohī punarbhavet || 14-190 ||
laṇḍapuṇḍastu khalvāṭaṃ tathā caiva trikarṇakam |
kuṇṭhaṃ śaṇṭhaṃ khalaṃ krūraṃ badhirāndhaṃva mūkatā || 14-191 ||
vikalendriyakaṃ caiva draviḍāmbupulindakam | 
karṇāṭaṃ? mlecchajātiṃ ca tathā randhropajīvinam || 14-192 || 
jñānāvaleṣamattaṃ ca dūṣaṇaṃ ca * ? pramāṇikam |
anyaśāstrarataṃ caiva kapaṭavratadhāriṇam || 14-193 ||
pramādādīkṣitā hyetannaiva siddhestu bhājanāḥ |
ityetat kathitaṃ devi aṃśākṣara yathākramam || 14-194 ||
kathitaṃ sarahasyaṃ tu vidyāvratanarātmanām |

iti bhairavasrotasi mahātantre vidyāpīṭhe saptakoṭipramāṇe śrītantrasadbhāve rudraśaktinirṇayaścaturdaśaḥ paṭalaḥ || 14 ||



devyuvāca

anujñāto'bhiṣiktasya vīro vīratvamicchatā |
cared vidyāvrataṃ mantrī yathā tat kathayasva me || 15-1 ||

bhairava uvāca 

śṛṇu devi pravakṣyāmi vidyāyā vratamūttamam |
jaṭī muṇḍī śikhī vāpi brahmaccāryatha snātakaḥ || 15-2 ||
vratastho'pyavratastho vā sarvāvastho'pi siddhyati | 
pañcamudrādharo vāpi bhasmaniṣṭho digambaraḥ || 15-3 || 
cīravatkaladhārī vā sarvābharaṇabhūṣitaḥ |
mali?mluco'tha śuklairvā vastrāvaraṇabhūṣitaḥ || 15-4 || 
yena yena hi veśena vartate sādhakottamaḥ | 
tatva deva vrataṃ proktamitiśāstrasya niścayaḥ || 15-5 || 
yadyadābharaṇaṃ tasya yaṃ vā vadati vācayā |
sācaryā kathito mantre mantraścaiva na saṃśayaḥ || 15-6 ||
vidyā nāma parā śaktidvibhedā sā vyavasthitāḥ |
cicchaktirabhidhānasthā avarṇavarṇagā śubhā || 15-7 || 
vidyante sarvagā yena tena vidyā mayā smṛtāḥ | 
104a g] cīrṇa yathā jagatsarvaṃ varṇāvarṇe vyavasthitam || 15-8 ||
vrataṃ bhāvamiti proktaṃ tena vidyāvrataṃ priye |
ciccinoti citijñānaṃ cicchaktipratibodhakam || 15-9 || 
ārṇave dalavat piṇḍaṃ vidyāmantrātmavigrahem | 
ca?pate ca gatā śaktiścīrṇavidyaḥ sa ucyate || 15-10 || 
saptakoṭisahasrāṇāṃ vidyā nāma prameyataḥ | 
cicchakti bodhanī yasmādavarṇāvarṇatāṃ gatāḥ || 15-11 ||
ekā eva parā proktā vidyārūpā tu kuṇḍalī |
tenadevi vrataṃ proktaṃ vidyāyā caraṇaṃ śubham || 12 ||
vidyāmārge caredyastu śāstradṛṣṭena karmaṇā |
dhyānaṃ pūjā japo homaḥ samayānāṃ tu pālakam || 15-13 ||
etad vidyāvrataṃ proktaṃ nānyathā vīranāyike | 
vidyājñayā tu nābhisthā carate dvādaśāntagā || 15-14 || 
vratasthāneṣu sarveṣu tena vidyāvrata smṛtam | 
brahma viṣṇustathā rudra īśvarotha sadāśivaḥ || 15-15 ||
ete sthānā vratasyaiva yatra sā carate parā |
jñātvā taṃ paramaṃ sthānaṃ cīrṇavidyāvrato hi saḥ || 15-16 ||
pañcamudrā bhaved devi pañcakāraṇakāstu yat |
bhūṣito hṛdi tiṣṭheta pañcamudrāvibhūṣitaḥ || 15-17 ||
etaistu bhūṣito mantrairparyaṭet kṣetramāśritaḥ | 
śmaśāne kānane kūpe udyāne devakule śubhe || 15-18 ||
śūnye rājagṛhe mantrī parvatāgre catuṣpathe |
tripathe grāmarathyāsu mahodadhitaṭe tathā || 15-19 ||
nadīsaṃgamatīre vā ekavṛkṣe'thakandare |
ekaliṅge tathāṣaṇḍe kṣetre caiva.āṣṭabhiḥ kramāt || 15-20 ||
prayāgā varuṇā kolā aṭṭahāsā jayantikā |
caritre kāmbukaṃ caiva koṭivarṣaṃ tathāṣṭamam || 15-21 || 
etaistu paryaṭemantrī yoginīsiddhimicchatā | 
khaṭvāṅgadhāriṇā maunī vegāt paryaṭate sadā || 15-22 || 
arukaṃ pāśakhaṭvāṅgaṃ triśūlaṃ kheṭakaṃ tathā |
nārācaṃ kartarī cakraṃ aṃkuśaṃ musalaṃ dhanuḥ || 15-23 ||
gadā kaṭṭārikā śaktiṃ tathā daṇḍa kamaṇḍalum |
ete caivāyudhā śreṣṭhā caryākāle tu dhārayet || 15-24 || 
pañca daiva śikaṃ kārya mantrasaṃkhyā vratottamam | 
dvādaśāhaṃ carenmantrī pakṣa māsa ṛtustathā || 15-25 || 
ṣaṇmāsām athavābdaṃ ca dvirabda trīṇi eva vā |
catuṣpañca tathā ṣaṭca saptaivāṣṭamathāpi vā || 15-26 ||
navadvipañcakaṃ vātha ekādaśāthavāpi vā |
104b g] dvādaśābdaṃ cared yastu brahmaghno'pi hi sidhyati || 15-27 || 
abda ekena deveśi * * * * * * * * ? | 
* * * * * * * * * *mantre apurohitaḥ || 15-28 || 
75a] śāntaḥ puravaro rājā vaśo bhavati śobhane |
dvirabdā dakṣakanyāstu siddhyanti suranāyike || 15-29 ||
trirabdaiḥ saptapāleyāstudetyāṃ gatāḥ śubhāḥ |
vasante madamattāstu madavibhrāntalocanāḥ || 30 ||
mattamātaṅgagāminyo akṣayā yauvanodvahā | 
kṣubhyanti sādhakendrasya prāṇaṃ muṃcanti tatkṣaṇāt || 15-31 || 
catuṣpañca tathā ṣaḍ-bhirbrahmalokādi sādhayet |
saptamebde varārohe rudrāntāyā vyavasthitāḥ || 15-32 ||
siddhyanti sādhakendrasya iti śāstre pracoditam |
aṣṭame īśvarākhyaṃ tu navame tu sadāśivam || 15-33 ||
daśe vidyālayo bhūtvā krīḍante gagane mahān | 
daśamaikādaśe devi dvādaśe guṇasaṃyutaḥ || 15-34 || 
aṇimādiguṇairyukto gacchate khecarī saha |
akṣayo hyajayo yogī krīḍate sarvagaḥ śubhaiḥ || 15-35 ||
vratasya phalametaddhi kathitaṃ tu mayā priye |
sāṃprataṃ yogamārgeṇa yathā bhavati tacchṛṇu || 15-36 ||
śmaśānaṃ tu gṛhaṃ proktaṃ gṛhaṃ deha prakīrtitam |
aṭanaṃ tu aviśrāntaṃ śmaśānagata cetasa || 15-37 ||
kaṃśarīramiti proktaṃ tasyānte nayanau smṛtau |
paśyate tatra saṃstho hi vāṅmayaṃ sacarācaram || 15-38 || 
kānanaṃ te samākhyātāṃ kāyānte saṃsthitā priye | 
mana kūpasamuddiṣṭaṃ saṃkalpaṃ kurute vahūn || 15-39 || 
tatrādhāro vrajet kṣetrī tena turyeti viśrutam | 
udyato mananābhistho manate sarvajantuṣu || 15-40 || 
neti tatpada saṃprāptā udyānaṃ tena śocyate | 
tadātisatataṃ dehe amṛtaṃ tu nabhogatam || 15-41 || 
kalānte vartate yena dharmādharmātmavandhanaiḥ | 
tena devi samākhyātaṃ dehaṃ devakule priye || 15-42 || 
rājā cātmā samuddiṣṭa ṣaṭtriṃśepyathavādhvani | 
śabdādiguṇabhūyiṣṭho mama koṣṭhagataprabhum || 15-43 || 
tena tiṣṭhena teṣṭhet tenaiva saha tiṣṭhati | 
unmanatve sadā yuktāḥ śūnyo mana udāhṛtaḥ || 15-44 || 
śūnyaṃ rājagṛhaṃ tena unmanatve sadā priye | 
sarvataṃ guruvaktraṃ tu tasyāgramavalamvayet || 15-45 || 
parvatāgraṃ smṛtaṃ tantre paryāyeṇa surārcite | 
catuṣpathaṃ bhaveddevi vāmā jyeṣṭhātu raudrikā || 15-46 || 
ambikāyā samāyuktā maṭanaṃ yugalasya tu |
105a g] catuṣkaṃ tena ākhyātaṃ prathame tadudāhṛtam || 15-47 ||
pathanāḍītrayaṃ proktaṃmiḍādyaṃ yanmaheśvari |
tripathasthamaṭenityaṃ kurute gatirāgatim || 15-48 ||
tripathamekabhāvasthaṃ yaḥ karoti sa siddhyati |
yāmaṃ dehamiti proktaṃ mātmāyāmapi na smṛtaḥ || 15-49 ||
ālaya sarvatatvānāṃ sukhaduḥkhaparāparaḥ |
gacchate adha ūrddhvaṃ tu rāthyādhāro jagatpatiḥ || 15-50 ||
tenarathyā smṛtā nāḍī brahmākhyā cātmanasya tu |
taṭaṃ tīraṃ samākhyātaṃ siddhāścāraṃ nigadyate || 15-51 ||
duḥkhāntaṃ tu layātītaṃ taṭaṃ codadhisaṃjñitam |
cārānte vyāpinaṃ devaṃ śivaṃ paramakāraṇam || 15-52 ||
tiṣṭhate satataṃ mantrī tatra caryā prakāśitāḥ |
nadate aṇu ādhārā parā kuṇḍalinī tu sā || 15-53 ||
sā nadī oghabhūtā tu vyomārṇe vahate sadā |
saṃgame parayā yukta munmanāyāṃ prakāśitam || 15-54 ||
saṅgamaṃ tu samākhyātaṃ nadī tu samudāhṛtā |
tīraṃ tu samavāpinyā vindhī yā sā parā kalā || 15-55 ||
tadātīto bhaved vyāpī nadyā tīramudāhṛtam |
ekavṛkṣaṃ samākhyātaṃ ekāśakti rihocyate || 15-56 ||
vṛkāṇīndriyāṇyaivoktā vṛkaśaktiriti smṛtāḥ |
75b] kṣayaṃ gatā pare vyomni amanaske nirāmaye || 15-57 ||
tena devi mayā proktāmekavṛkṣaṃ tu paryayā |
eka eva paraṃ tatvaṃ liṅgādhāraṃ paraṃ priye || 15-58 ||
paryaṭeta divāprāya sa viśrāntaḥ punaḥ punaḥ |
ekaliṅgagati khyātā ṣaṇḍaṃ tu kathayāmi te || 15-59 ||
hṛdayaṃ tu śaraṃ proktaṃ padmaṃ tatrāṣṭapatrakam |
udānena tu deveśi vikaset ravistu saḥ || 15-60 ||
sevyate mudgalālībhiḥ sarojaṃ hṛdayātmakam |
carate tatra haṃsākhyā śaktirādyā manonmanī || 15-61 ||
ṣaṇḍaṃ taṃ kathitaṃ tantre kṣetraṃ tu kathayāmi te |
kṣetraṃ nāma paraṃ proktaṃ śarīraṃ tatvasaṃyutam || 15-62 ||
kṣetrajña svarate nityaṃ sthānāṣṭakagatisthitaḥ |
tenedaṃ kathitaṃ devi kṣetrāṣṭaka svayaṃbhunā || 15-63 ||
ye pīṭhāste bhavet kṣetrā kṣetrāḥ pīṭhā udāhṛtāḥ |
nāmaparyāyabhinnāstu śāstre śāstre pṛthak pṛthak || 15-64 ||
prayāgā nābhisaṃsthā tu varuṇā hṛtpradeśataḥ |
105b g] kolagiryāṃ tu kaṇṭhasthaṃ bhīmanādaṃ tu tāluke || 15-65 ||
vindusthāne jayantyāṃ tu nādākhye tu caritrakam |
ekāmrakaṃ śaktimadhye jñātavyaṃ viditātmanām || 15-66 ||
guruvaktragataṃ proktaṃ koṭivarṣaṃ tathāṣṭamam |
ete sthānā mayā proktā adhyātme pudgalāśrayāḥ || 15-67 ||
aṭate satataṃ yena hṛccakraśca sanātanaḥ |
sarvadevamayaṃ piṇḍaṃ sarvayogini saṃmatam || 15-68 ||
adhyuṣṭayogapīṭheṣu saṃsthitaṃ siddhikaulikam |
anyadetanmahādevi sadyaḥ pratyayakārakaḥ || 15-69 ||
kulūttamoḍḍiyānaṃ ca eruḍī pulivallabham |
tānyeva tu samantāni svadehe saṃsthitāni tu || 15-70 ||
arddhamātrā tu yā proktā sarvavīryaprakāśanī |
evaṃ kūṭasvarūpeṇa upacaryati sarvaśaḥ || 15-71 ||
tāstu kūṭaprabhāvena viceṣṭanti ca sarvadā |
svecchānuaticāriṇyaḥ samarthāḥ sarvakarmasu || 15-72 ||
yāśca devyaḥ kulodbhūtā brahmaviṭkṣetraśūdrajāḥ |
paśyate nātra sandeho vibhinnā sarvataḥ sthitā || 15-73 ||
avaśyaṃ paśyate yogī tatkūṭasya prabhāvataḥ |
kathayanti svavijñānaṃ tatkūṭenopavṛṃhite || 15-74 ||
utpated gaganāṃ bhogān khecaraiḥ sahasādhakaḥ |
tatpatitvaṃ bhaveddevi svacchandagatigocaraḥ || 15-75 ||
krīḍayed bhuvanād bhuvanaṃ tatkūṭasya prabhāvataḥ |
svacchandagatimārgeṇa vijñānena sureśvari || 15-76 ||
yāvṛdetana vindanti pīṭhāṃścā dhyātmagāṃ priye |
tāvat tasya kutaḥ siddhiraṭitepi jagattraye || 15-77 ||
bahirantarabhāvaṃ tu antaraṃ vahiraṅgayoḥ |
jñātvaivaṃ mucyate pāśaiḥ khecarīṇāṃ samo bhavet || 15-78 ||
antaraṅge sthitā devyo ta devyo bahiraṅgagāḥ |
lokapravṛttihetvarthe bahiḥ pīṭhāḥ prakāśitāḥ || 15-79 ||
antaraṃge padā śuddhaḥ paśyate na mayā priye |
tadā paśyati bāhyasthāḥ sūkṣmarūpā bhagastayaḥ || 15-80 ||
melakaṃ ca prayacchanti prarūpāyāsavaṃ priye |
saṃpradāya prayacchanti sthānaṃ vā kathayaṃtyapi || 15-81 ||
aśuddhenāntabhāvena paryaṭet pṛthivī yadi |
na tasya darśanaṃ devi dadanti manasā kvacit || 15-82 ||
paśyantapi ca deveśi paśyantopi na paśyati |
saṃkīrṇalakṣaṇā devyo miśro jñātuṃ na śakyate || 15-83 ||
prabhāvaiṣa samuddiṣṭo vinā tāsā manugrahāt |

76a] yāme grāme tathāraṇye nagare catvare pure || 15-84 || 
kheṭake caiva sandehe pīṭhe kṣetre vanethavā |
udyānopavane devi pūrvamuktaistathaiva ca || 15-85 ||
deśe deśebhijāyante jñātagarbhāgabhastayaḥ |
pārthivāvaraṇā proktā āpe tejo tathānile || 15-86 ||
ākāśe caiva suśroṇi tāsāṃ saṃkhyā na vidyate | 
pīṭhāśrayavibhāgena utpadyanti anekadhā || 15-87 ||
khānapānaratā nityaṃ krīḍante cāntyajeṣvapi |
tena devi mayā proktāḥ pīṭhā bāhyasvarūpataḥ || 15-88 ||
veśyāgṛhaṃ prayāgākhyaṃ varuṇaṃ soṇḍike viduḥ |
khaṭikī ca bhavetkolārajantyāyāṭṭahāsakam || 15-89 ||
jayantī kandukaṃ vindyāccaritraṃ cchipyarka gṛham |
ekāmbukaṃ tu kaivarttī koṭyākhyeti ca kausaṭī || 15-90 ||
jñātavyaṃ cumbakenaiva bhuktimuktiphalārthinām |
bāhyataḥ kathitā hyevaṃ pīṭhā kṣetrāstu suvrate || 15-91 ||
paryaṭe yeṣu sthāneṣu pūjanīyā sadā budhaiḥ |
bhakṣabhojyānnapānaistu tarpayenmantravit sadā || 15-92 ||
evaṃ vai saṃsthitistāsāṃ yoginyastvaprameyataḥ |
bhavantīha na sandeho varadāḥ sādhakasya tu || 15-93 ||
tarpitāḥ pūjitā devyaiḥ sādhakasya dadanti hi |
ṣaṇmāsādyuktamārgasya samayavratapālanāt || 15-94 ||
athānyaṃ saṃpravakṣyāmi paribhāṣāṃ śāstravedinām |
adhyātmakaṃ bahiścaiva yathā jñāyanti tatvataḥ || 15-95 ||
tathā te kathayiṣyāmi śṛṇuṣvāyatalocane |
adhyātmaṃ kurute bāhyaṃ vratacaryā tu sādhanam || 15-96 ||
evaṃ kṛte bhavet siddhi satyaṃ satyaṃ na saṃśayaḥ |
khaṭvāṅgaṃ kathayiṣyāmi paripāṭyā yathākramam || 15-97 || 
āpādatālumurddhāntaṃ yathā bhavati tacchṛṇu | 
śirādau sarva aṅgeṣu aṅgapratyaṅgajeṣvapi || 15-98 ||
khaṭṭate yena deveśi khaṭvāṅgī tena saṃsmṛtaḥ |
maunena vartate nityaṃ hṛdi gūḍhaṃ parāparam || 15-99 ||
tena maunīti vijñeyaṃ sarvaśāstreṣu bhāmini |
vegena paryaṭe dehe aviśrānta punaḥ punaḥ || 15-100 || 
tena vegān mayākhyāta maṭanaṃ pudgalasya tu | 
ca?rukaṃ pravakṣyāmi yathāvadanupūrvaśaḥ || 15-101 || 
amā nāma parāsūkṣmā kalāyāmṛtavāhinī |
ātmā saṃcarate tasyāṃ rāvaṃ muñcanti vinduke || 15-102 ||
visargastho mahātmāno vādayet kathayeti ca |
106b g] ḍamarukaṃ smṛtaṃ tena amanaske nirāmaye || 15-103 || 15-
tā nityā paśyatiṣṭhena? māyārūpā tu kuṇḍalī | 
pāśametadvinirdiṣṭaṃ khaḍgaṃ caivamataḥ śṛṇu || 15-104 || 
khasthasthitoti pāśāṃstu visargāstreṇa mohanī |
khaḍgavannimnagā yena khagamadhye maheśvarī || 15-105 ||
gatā sā brahmasāyojyaṃ khaḍgaṃte śaktiravyayā |
tena khaḍgamiti proktaṃ āyudhaṃ suranāyike || 15-106 || 
ekā emarā śaktistripathā vaktramaṇḍale |
vāmā jyeṣṭhā tathā raudrī icchājñānakriyātmikā || 15-107 || 
triśūlaṃ tripathaṃ khyātaṃ triśaktimanupūrvasaḥ |
kharūpā vyomagā śāntā nirmalā aṭate priye || 15-108 ||
kheṭakaṃ tena nāmaṃ tu dvādaśānte vyavasthitam |
nārācaṃ śaktiruccāraṃ karaṇātmakaṃ vyavasthitam || 15-109 ||
vedhate tu nirodhinyāṃ kāraṇā pañca eva tu |
tena nārācamākhyātaṃ paryāyeṇa varānane || 15-110 ||
kartarī jñānaśaktistu yena pāśāṃ chinatyasau |
sā kalā paramā sūkṣmā mantrāṇāṃ bodhanī parā || 15-111 ||

76b] kartarī kartarūpeṇa jñātavyā sādhakena tu |
carate dvādaśānte tu kramāt tatvāni muñcati || 15-112 ||
cakravadbhramate nityaṃ aṃkuśasthā parāparā |
aṅgapratyaṅgagā devī bahirantarasaṃsthitāḥ || 15-113 ||
kurute satataṃ ceṣṭāṃ āsane śayane tathā |
dhāvanevatsāne rodhe aṃkuśasthānamāśritāḥ || 15-114 ||
muśalatve sthito nādo rekhākārorddhvagaḥ priye |
taṃ bhitvā gamanaṃ corddhve muśalākhyaṃ sadāśivam || 15-115 ||
dhanurlakṣo anākhyaṃ tu yena vedhayate param |
āpūrya savisargeṇa pareṇa manacakṣuṣā || 15-116 ||
karaṇena ṭahāntena viparyastena suvrate |
ṛtudvayānuyuktena kānte nāntagate na tu || 15-117 ||
anena karaṇenaiva kaṭṭārikā parāparā |
prāpnoti tatvasāyujyāṃ gadā yā ca sulocane || 15-118 ||
gatā hyekā paraṃ randhraṃ daśanecāmṛtaṃ śubham |
gatā tu na nivartante ye gatā gadayā saha || 15-119 ||
śaktiśaktisthabhāvena ātmā tu nayate sadā |
tadbhāvayogasiddhistu śaktinā tu surādhipe || 15-120 ||
daṇḍavaddṛjurekhā tu gatā yā padamavyayam |
tena mārgeṇa gantavyaṃ daṇḍādhāreṇa śobhane || 15-121 ||
kaṃ śarīramiti khyātaṃ maṇḍākāraṃ vyavasthitam |
107a g] tatrādhvā tu varārohe śodhanīyo manīṣibhiḥ || 15-122 ||
kuṇḍalyāyā parā śuddhā sarvaśuddha hṛdi sthitam |
tenedaṃ kathitaṃ bhadre śubhāstraṃ tu kamaṇḍalum || 15-123 ||
ete caivāyudhā sūkṣmā paryāyaiḥ kathitā sphuṭam |
sama vāgni nītāḥ sūkṣmā kalā viśvasya nāyikāḥ || 15-124 ||
kathitāstu mayā devi parāparaṃ vibhāgaśaḥ |
dūtīhīnā na siddhyanti tasmāddūtī samāśrayet || 15-125 ||
sabāhyābhyantarā sā tu jñātavyā kaulikānvaye |
bāhyataḥ kathayiṣyāmi dūtīnāṃ lakṣaṇaṃ śubham || 15-126 ||
antaraṅge tathāpyevaṃ śrūyatāṃ tāsu niścayam |
mātā duhitā bhaginī sahajā ca tathāntyajā || 15-127 ||
rajakī carmakārī ca mātaṅgī cāgrajātmikā |
annapānaṃ tathā bhakṣamācarecchaktibhiḥ saha || 15-128 ||
antyājānā dvijānāṃ ca ekatra carubhojanam | 
kartavyaṃ sādhakenaiva yadīcchetsiddhimuttamām || 15-129 ||

devyuvāca

kutsitaṃ kathitaṃ deva ayuktaṃ śāstravādinām |
paśūnāṃ yatsamācāra ākhyātaḥ parameśvaraḥ || 15-130 ||
patīnāṃ tu yadā sohi tadāścarya mahāprabho |

bhairava uvāca

sādhu sādhu mahāprājñe praśnānetāṃ sudurlabhām || 15-131 ||
kathayāmi samāsena tvatprītyā suranāyike |
sā tveva saṃsthitā śaktirjagato yonirūpiṇī || 15-132 ||
atotpannaṃ samastaṃ hi vāṅmayaṃ sacarācaram |
tena māteti vikhyātā mayā te parameśvari || 15-133 ||
udbhavasthā duhitṛtu dūhatā jagatasya tu |
duhitṛ tu dvitīyā tu bhaginī tu tathocyate || 15-134 ||
bhagarūpā parā sūkṣmā utpannā sātmanā saha |
yathāgnestejasā nityaṃ manānātve vyavasthitāḥ || 15-135 ||
tadvadeva hi cātyāsthā bhāginī bhagarūpiṇī |

* * * * * * * * * * * ** * * *? || 15-137 ||

* * * * * * * * * * * ** * * *? || 15-138 ||

77a] rajastamo vinirmuktā mahatā rajakī ume || 15-139 ||
carate marmabhāgena svaraṅgeṇa tu rañjitā |
carmakārīti sādhyekā mātaṅgī ca athocyate? || 15-140 ||
ātmasthā satataṃ nityaṃ gītasyāntapade sthitāḥ |
mātaṅgī kathitā dūtī agrajanmī tathocyate || 15-141 ||
107b g] sarveṣāṃ caiva śāstrāṇāṃ ayojyatva varāgraṇī |
nānyantatra bhavet kiñcicchaktirādyā manonmanī || 15-142 ||
agrajanmī samākhyātā parā yāmṛtamālinī |
paryāyā kathitā devi śaktistaddharmadharmiṇī || 15-143 ||
ajñātvā dehajāṃ śaktiṃ bahi sthānagatāṃ priye |
ācaranti ca ye mūḍhāḥ paśavaste durātmanaḥ || 15-144 ||
* * * * * yā asambhāvyā śivavrataviḍmbakāḥ |
khānapānaṃ hi yatkiñcit kartavyaṃ tu tayā saha || 15-145 ||
evaṃ yuktaḥ sadā tiṣṭhenmadirānandacetasaḥ |
madīyā paramā śakti rañjitaṃ tu jagattrayam || 15-146 ||
ānandaṃ tatsamatvaṃ hi madirānandacetasaḥ |
siddhyate ca na sandeho? yathā bhairavamabravīt || 15-147 ||
jñānāmṛtena tṛptasya kṛtakṛtyasya yoginaḥ |
naivāsti kiñcitkartavyamasticenna? sa tatvavit || 15-148 ||

iti bhairavasrotasi mahātantre vidyāpīṭhe saptakoṭipramāṇe śrītantrasadbhāve caryādhikāraḥ pañcadaśaḥ paṭalaḥ || 15 ||



devyuvāca

kathitāḥ parāparā devyaḥ pīṭhasthāne yathāsthitāḥ |
pañcāvaraṇasaṃsthāstu na jñātāstatvataḥ prabho || 16-1 ||
tāsāṃ tu lakṣaṇaṃ sthānaṃ utpattiśca viśeṣataḥ |
kathayasva prasādena praṇatāyā mama priye || 16-2 ||

bhairava uvāca

śṛṇu devi pravakṣyāmi yoginīlakṣaṇaṃ śubham |
sṛṣṭiścaiva viśeṣeṇa saṃcārajagatī tale || 16-3 ||
viśvā viśveśvarī caiva hā raudrī vīranāyikāḥ |
ambā pūrveti nāmāni yoginyastvaṃ varodbhavā || 16-4 ||
vyomni brahmamaye padme ṣaṭpatre śūlasaṃsthite |
rocanābhe suvistīrṇe sutejo paramojjvale || 16-5 ||
nirmale parame padme amṛtākhye pare śive |
tatra śūlagatā devyaḥ pararūpā mahojasāḥ || 16-6 ||
anantaguṇabhūyiṣṭhā vyāpakāḥ sa tatoditāḥ |
tiṣṭhantyamṛtamadhyasthā paripūrṇā nirākulāḥ || 16-7 ||
vaindavīsṛṣṭikāriṇyaḥ paratejaḥsamanvitāḥ |
tāsāṃ bhedo na vidyeta naikarūpā gabhastayaḥ || 16-8 ||
nābhiśikhau bhaved binduḥ śaktyudaragataḥ prabhuḥ |
bhramate'lātacakre ca sṛṣṭihetoradhomukhaḥ || 16-9 ||
bindunā kṣobhitā māyā ṛjutvaṃ jñāyate kṣaṇāt |
nādākhyā sā tu vijñeyā sādākhyā tu parā kalā || 16-10 ||
108a g] sā ca dvibheditā jñeyā vāyutatvāt samāśritā |
tatra bhedā hyanekāstu nāmabhiḥ saṃprakāśyate || 16-11 ||
śabdaniṣṭhā parā mātā suśivā tu suvarcasā |
amṛtāmṛtamadhyasthā ghoṣiṇī yoganāyikāḥ || 16-12 ||
vyāpikī vyāptikāntā ca śivā jñeyāstu nāmataḥ |
dvādaśaitāḥ samākhyātāḥ śabdatatvaguṇāsritāḥ || 16-13 ||
sparśākhye tu pravakṣyāmi nāmāni tu samāsataḥ |
capalā cañcalā tīvrā vāyuvegā manonugāḥ || 16-14 ||
mṛgadhvajābhidhānā ca spṛśanī tvagratā śubhāḥ |
jīvanī sarpiṇī māyā yoginyo vāyumāśritāḥ || 16-15 ||
tatra kṛtyaṃ prakurvanti anekāni sahasraśaḥ |
77b] caturviṃśatināmāni pradhānā guṇamāśritāḥ || 16-16 ||
ekaikāyāstu bhedena koṭikoṭyastu koṭayaḥ |
pramāṇaṃ naiva vidyeta aṇurūpāstu devatāḥ || 16-17 ||
tejovaraṇivāsinyaḥ kathayāmi samāsataḥ |
tejovatī tejamātā tejinī tejarūpiṇī || 16-18 ||
tejonidhiśca tejāḍhyā tejaḥkāntā parā smṛtāḥ |
tejovṛtyā tu tejasvī tejoyoninidhistathā || 16-19 ||
tejobhayaprakāśinyo tejo dhārī tu dvādaśī |
rūpavānasamāsādya rūkmiṇī rurunāśanī || 16-20 ||
rūpasvī rūpavānyogī rūpaśīlā guṇānvitāḥ |
rūpayonistathā rūpā kāmarūpā rurupriyā || 16-21 ||
rūndhanī rūpasaṃpannā saṃparkāyākhyā tu caturdaśaḥ |
sparśākhyaṃ tu anantāśca vayostu guṇasaṃbhavāḥ || 16-22 ||
tejākhyena tu ṣaṭ-triṃśa pradhānānantatāṃ gatām |
āpyāvaraṇagākhyāmi nāmataḥ sāṃprataṃ śṛṇu || 16-23 ||
caturguṇasamopetā kathyamānā nibodha me |
varuṇā vāruṇī svacchā śivā?nī kṣālanī tathā || 16-24 ||
jalāruhā jaladhātrī aṃbhā cāṃbhavatī tathā |
aṃbhasthā cāmbhavāhā ca aṃbhiṣā salilā parā || 16-25 ||
salilā salilormiṇyā salilādravarūpiṇī |
draviṇī drāviṇī drohī varṣaṇī meghapātanī || 16-26 ||
meghadhārāstathā vṛṣṭiḥ paryamvā priyavādinī |
śasyamātā tathā dhānyā prarohī udakā parāḥ || 16-27 ||
udakṣṇatā? udadmohā udakeśvaryā parāṃbhasā |
aṃbhoruhā aṃbhavatī aṃbhasthā cāṃbhadāyikāḥ || 16-28 ||
aṃbhovidhāṃ ambhavatyā jaladāṃ jalavāhinī |
108b g] jaloruhā pravāhā ca vṛṣṭikārī supātanī || 16-29 ||
sveda * * * pravāhādyā yoginyarūcāpasaṃbhavāḥ |
catvāriṃśat tathā cāṣṭau mukhyatvena prakāśitāḥ || 16-30 ||
āsāṃ saṃkhyā na vidyeta paramāṇvākāra rūpakāḥ |
pārthivāḥ pañcabhedena kathayāmi tapodhane || 16-31 ||
visvabhāvāstu boddhavyā sthūlasūkṣmakriyārthataḥ |
sthūlā anekabhedaiśca sūkṣmāstu kathayāmi te || 16-32 ||
dharaṇī dhāraṇī raudrī rundhatī tu nirodhanī |
dhātrī dhārāvatī dhārī dhararūpā dharodvahā || 16-33 ||
dharamātā dhare śreṣṭhāvasudhaivamataḥ param |
vasudhātā vasuyoni vasudhā tu vasundharī || 16-34 ||
vasudhātā vasusvāmī vasurāḍhyā vasundharī |
vasupriyā vasorādyā vasukāntā vasauṣadhī || 16-35 || 
pārthivā pṛthivī nāma pṛthivyā pṛthivīpati | 
pṛthubhogā pṛthivyāntā pṛthurīśā pṛthūdarī || 16-36 ||
pārthivāḍhyā? yatā pṛthvī kṣoṇīkṣā'vyayā? |
kṣapaṇī kṣodarī kṣaudrī kṣubhitā u * * * * || 16-37 ||
urvī śyāmā dharaṇyā ca urvaśī ubhayā parā |
ūhanī gopanī goptā gomātā gogabhastikā || 16-38 ||
gaurī gomatikā goptā gorasā tu guṇāgraṇī |
godhānā gomavatyā ca godaṃpā gosamīkṣakāḥ || 16-39 ||
gopurā goruhā gaṃgā govatsā gosahasrikāḥ |
gaurī ca gauravatsā ca kaṭhinā kaṭhinoruhā || 16-40 ||
dṛsthā piṇḍā sugandhā ca pāvanī pavanī parā |
pārvatī himajā gauryā girijā śailajānagāḥ || 16-41 ||
kṛṣodarī śubhāsyā ca sarasvatyā sumāninī |
maṅgalā bālacandrā ca sumanā tu manovatī || 16-42 ||
vegā vegavatī śāntā kāntākāntikarā purā |
78a] kāmarāgā viramyā ca raktā raktānugā parā || 16-43 ||
śānti svasti svadhā tṛpti śriyā lakṣmī ca suvratā |
prabhujyotsnā tathā caiva aparā tu manovatī || 16-44 ||
evamādyai ranekaistu paryāyairvahubhi sthitāḥ |
ekā eva parā yonir anantānantatāṃ gatā || 16-45 ||
pṛthivyādīni tatvāni ambarāntāni tāni ca |
tāsāṃ sṛṣṭisvarūpeṇa yathāvyāptivyavasthitāḥ || 16-46 ||
teṣāṃ te kathitā devi yoginyastvaprameyataḥ |
tattvarūpāstu yoginyo jñātavyāstu varānane || 16-47 || 
śivecchānuvidhāyinyo manovegā mahābalāḥ |
109a g] vicaranti samastāśca brahmaviṣṇvindrabhūmiṣu || 16-48 ||
aparāḥ kulasaṃbhūtā yonijāḥ kaulajā priye |
pīṭhajā kṣetrajā caiva khecarāśca viśeṣataḥ || 16-49 ||
kulajā saptabhedaistu jñātavyā vīranāyikā | sandohe caiva saṃkīrṇe yonijā rūpiṇī smṛtāḥ |
tāstu bhedopabhedaistu saṃsthitāstu anekadhā || 16-51 ||
devāṃśā yoginī jñeyāṃ divyajñānapravartikāḥ |
devatā miśrāṃśāstu sandohāstu tadātmikāḥ || 16-52 ||
rūpikā yāḥ punaḥ proktā kṣudrāṃśāścaiva tā smṛtāḥ |
tāstu bhedaistathā miśraiḥ saṃsthitā kathayāmi te || 16-53 ||
brahmāṇyādi samārabhya yogeśyantāvasānataḥ |
sthitā caikonapañcāśadekaikā saptasaptasu || 16-54 ||
tāsāṃ bhedāṃ pravakṣyāmi yathāvattadanuśṛṇu |
śākinyaḥ śudraśākinyaḥ sāvyaḥ sāvarikāḥ śivā || 16-55 ||
devatā yoginīnāṃ ca bhedāḥ sapta smṛtānaghe |
yoginīpūrvasiddhāstu jñātavyā vīranāyike || 16-56 ||
devatā labdhayogāstu jñātavyā sādhakena tu |
ekā eva parā yogī sarvevarṇamayī śubhā || 16-57 ||
nādiphāntā svarūpeṇa vigrahasthā parā kalā |
sarvavarṇamaheśasya bhairavārddhaśarīriṇī || 16-58 ||
tasyāstriśūlavāsinyo bhairaveśasya caiva hi |
krīḍanti hṛdayāntasthāḥ sṛṣṭisaṃhārakārikāḥ || 16-59 ||
aparāstvadhikāriṇyasthitāḥ sarvagatāḥ śubhāḥ || 16-60 ||
tāsāṃ sthānāṃ pravakṣyāmi śṛṇuṣvāyatalocane |
kulūtāyāmaraṇyeśe sindhudeśe nageśvare || 16-61 ||
samudrakukṣyāsaurāṣṭre pretapuryāṃ himālaye |
kaṃcyolamyākaviṣaye kaliṅge kauśale sthale || 16-62 ||
triśaṃkunistathā codre kāmarūpe ca mālave |
devikoṭe sudhārāme godāvaryāṃ taṭerbude || 16-63 ||
eṣa deśeṣu yāḥ kanyā striyo vā klinnayonayaḥ |
