sarvāstāḥ kāmarūpiṇyo manovegānuvṛttayaḥ || 16-64 ||
śeṣeṣuyāḥ samutpannā śākinyo ghora mātaraḥ |
ṣaḍyoginyaḥ kulūtāyāmaraṇye śecamātarāḥ || 16-65 ||
sindhudeśe bhaginyastu narośe kulanāyikāḥ |
samudrakukṣyakāpilyaḥ saurāṣṭre gṛhadevatāḥ || 16-66 ||
pretapuryāṃ mahākālyo rūpiṇyo himavad girau |
109b g] kaṃcyāmambā samākhyātā laṃpākaviṣayeṃ mṛtāḥ || 16-67 ||
kaliṅge vratadhāriṇyā kauśale piśitāśanāḥ |
ka *?vākyāsthale proktā trisaṃnyāmarā smṛtāḥ || 16-68 ||
deśadvaye ca śākinyo nāyikā vīranāyikā |
yoginyarucaparāstābhyo divyācyārā balotkaṭā || 16-69 ||
vicaranti kṣitau bhogāṃ rājñastā lokamātarāḥ |
78b] ābhirvyāptaṃ samaṃ sarvaṃ saśailavanakānanam || 16-70 ||
divyasatvā mahātejā divyaiśvaryamahaujasā |
tāsāṃ nāmāni vakṣyāmi yathā bhavati tacchṛṇu || 16-71 ||
bhaṭṭā yogī tu citrākṣī viśālā ca tathā parā |
dāmoṭī caiva kahlāṭī daṃṣṭriṇī piṅgalā tathā || 16-72 ||
mūlāmūla tathā caiva patrā vai mūlaghātikāḥ |
mūlakārī tathānyā ca vijñeyā prāṇahārikā || 16-73 ||
dhṛtiśca cchundarī caiva raktākṣī rākṣasī tathā |
ghorā ca śūkarī caiva tathānyā ca balotkaṭāḥ || 16-74 ||
piśācā nṛtyanā pīḍā vyāghrī siṃhī halāyudhā |
haśanī grasanī caiva mohanā priyadarśanā || 16-75 ||
trāsanī ca virupākṣā vikaṭā saindhavā tathā |
sulocanā mahālakṣmī mahātejā suvarcasā || 16-76 ||
sumukhā campakā caiva vimalā ca karaṅkiṇī |
suvarṇā bhāvikambā ca viṣalākṣītiḍiṇḍimā || 16-77 ||
kampā ca nayikā caiva maṇḍanā ca trilocanā |
sukeśī lambakarṇaṃ ca utphulā diṃḍikā parā || 16-78 ||
mahāpīṭhā tathākarṣā vidhṛtiśca tathā parā |
kūṣmāṇḍī mohanī caiva mahāvaktrā sanātanā || 16-79 ||
sunāthā siddhikārī ca siṃhavaktrā tathā parā |
ānandā vāmanā proktā tathānyā tu vilamvikāḥ || 16-80 ||
svacchā vai śāśvatā jñeyā santoṣā cāmbinī tathā |
māninī cāparā jñeyā tathā citramukheti ca || 16-81 ||
samvarā ca tatodaryā subodhā padminī tathā |
vībhatsinī samākhyātā saptasaptati kīrtitāḥ || 16-82 ||
kulāmnāye samākhyātā avatāre pṛthak pṛthak |
ābhirvyāptaṃ jagatsarvaṃ saśailavanakānanām || 16-83 ||
mahāyogasusaṃpannā dīrghāpuṣpā manojavāḥ |
icchārūpadharā hyetā jarārugbhayavarjitāḥ || 16-84 ||
paśyanti pṛthivīṃ sarvān hastāmalakavatpriye |
sve sve sthāne viceṣṭante svādhikāravaśena tu || 16-85 ||
bhaṭṭā yogī samuddiṣṭā sthāne jñeyā samāsataḥ |
110a g] koṭivarṣanibhāsinyā citrākṣī cālakāpure || 16-86 ||
viśālāca jayantyā vai kasmīre daṃṣṭriṇī tathā |
vāmaliptāṃ ca dāmoṭī kahlāṭī vāraṇāpure || 16-87 ||
vasate kāmarūpe tu piṅgalā yoginī śubhāḥ |
tathā vāṭavyadeśe tu mūlā ca vasate priye || 16-88 || 
yā ca mūlatarā nāma vasate dakṣiṇāpathe |
patra vai cīnadeśe tu nagare mūlaghātikāḥ || 16-89 ||
mūlakārī cagnā vāṇī vasate vīranāyike |
prāṇahārī dhṛtiścaiva mālave saṃsthitānaghe || 16-90 ||
chucchundarī ca saurāṣṭre tathānyā ca karaṅkiṇī |
vasate tatra deveśi trāsanī śaphale tathā || 16-91 ||
ujjainyā rākṣasī caiva virūpākṣā ca saṃsthitāḥ |
kalaśāpure raktākṣī vasate ca na saṃśayaḥ || 16-92 ||
gaṃgādvāre tathā ghorā carmaraṃge balotkaṭāḥ |
śūkarī caiva caṃpāyāṃ magadhāyāṃ piśācinī || 16-93 ||
nṛtyanāvasate nityaṃ kurukṣetre varānane |
laṃkāyāṃ pīḍanā caiva vyāghrī ca druviḍe tathā || 16-94 ||
halāyudhā tathā siṃhī koṅkaṇe caiva saṃsthitāḥ |
śyāmātīre ca hasanī grasanī pārase tathā || 16-95 ||
vasate mohanā caiva darade vīravandite |
laṃpākaviṣaye nityaṃ ramate priyadarśanā || 16-96 ||
vikaṭā citrakūṭeṃ tu saindhavā kanyakubjake |
sulocanā ḍāhalāyāṃ vasate tatra bhāmini || 16-97 ||
kollagiryāṃ mahālakṣmī mahātejā ca kauśale |

79a] turuṣke sumukhe nāma oḍradeśe tu campakā || 16-98 ||
vimalā bhillamāle tu suvarṇa aṭṭahāsake |
vasate tu sadā devi vuddhamāne viyamvikā || 16-99 ||
jālandhare viśālākṣī oḍ.iyāṇe ca śiṣṭimā? |
pūrikāyā mahākāyā āyodhyāṃ vīranāyikāḥ || 16-100 ||
saṃtrāne maṇḍinā nāma kaliṃge ca trilocanā |
rāṣṭrāyāṃ ca ṣukeśī ca laṃvakarṇā tu nāsike || 16-101 ||
kha ulle caiva utphullā ca eḍikāṃḍiṇḍimā smṛtāḥ |
pratiṣṭhāne mahāpṛṣṭhā vasate tu mahābalā || 16-102 ||
elāpure tathā karṣā malaye vidhṛti smṛtā |
vatsagulme tu kūṣmāṇḍī mohanā bhogavarddhane || 16-103 ||
mahendre ca mahāvaktrā pūrṇagiryā sanātanā |
mādrīpure sunāmā ca śrīpīṭhe siddhadā matā || 16-104 ||
siṃhale siṃhavaktrā ca ānandā ca karohane |
vāmanā kośavarddhanye mātṛharme vilambikāḥ || 16-105 ||
110b g] khasthā nāgapure caiva śāśvatī ca dharāpure |
añyukyaṃ caiva saṃtoṣā mṛgukacche tathāśvinī || 16-106 || 
māninī cārbude nāma vindhyo citramukheti ca |
sahye sahyodarī nāma samavarā morakhaṇḍikā || 16-107 ||
subodhā vadarī kacche capalyāṃ padminī matā |
samudrāṃte ca vībhatsī mātryāṃ sānugatāṃ śṛṇu || 16-108 ||
saptalokanivāsinyo mātarā sarvata sthitāḥ |
bhaṭṭā ghorā piśācī ca raktākṣī rākṣasī tathā || 16-109 ||
virupākṣā ca vikaṭā saindhavā ca sulocanā | 
mahālakṣmī mahātejā sumukhā ca tathā parā || 16-110 ||
cāmuṇḍā sā smṛtā hyetā dvādaśaiva prakīrtitāḥ |
patrābalotkaṭā caiva pīḍanā trāsanī tathā || 16-111 ||
campakā vimalā caiva tathānyā ca karaṃkiṇī |
suvarṇākṣā samākhyātā tathānyā tu viḍamvikā || 16-112 ||
viśālākṣī ḍiṇḍimā ca mahācampā ca nāyikā |
trayodaśa mayā khyātā māhendryaṃśā varānane || 16-113 ||
śūkarī prāṇahārī ca tathā chucchundarī parā |
daṃṣṭriṇī maṇḍanā caiva tathānyā ca trilocanā || 16-114 ||
sukeśī lambakarṇā ca utphullā ḍiṇḍipṛṣṭhakā |
ākarṣā cāpi sāmyaṃśā ekādaśa udāhṛtāḥ || 16-115 ||
mohanā nāṃpidāmoṭī vyāghrī siṃhā halāyudhā |
dhṛti kuṣmāṇḍikā caiva mohanyānyā sanātanā || 16-116 ||
mahāvaktrā daśaite tu vaiṣṇavyaṃśā mahāvalāḥ |
hasanī grasanī caiva viśālā priyadarśinā || 16-117 ||
sunāthā siddhikārī ca ānandā siṃhavakrakā |
vāmanā ca vilamvī ca svacchāyā śāśvatāstathā || 16-118 ||
trayodaśa samākhyātāḥ kaumāryaṃśā varānane |
kahloṭīṃ nṛtyanā caiva citrākṣī piṃgalā tathā || 16-119 || 
aśvinī māninī citrā samvarā ca satodarā |
rudrāṃśā kīrtitā hyetā navaiva tu samāsataḥ || 16-120 ||
mūlā mūlatarā caiva vijñeyā mūlaghātikā |
mūlakārī ca saṃbodhā padminī ca vibhatsinī || 16-121 ||
etā sapta samākhyātā brahmāṇḍā sā varānane |
trisvabhāvā smṛtā hyetāḥ satvarājasatāmasāḥ || 16-122 || 
brahmāṇyaṃśāstathaindrāṃśā devāṃśāḥ sātvikā matāḥ | 
kaumārīvaiṣṇavīgotrā yakṣasatvā varānane || 16-123 || 
vārāhī caṇḍikā raudrīkulajā rakṣasātvikā |
111a g] divyayogabalopetā yogyastridaśabhedataḥ || 16-124 ||
nāmamantrārcitā hyetā bhavantīha phalapradāḥ |
79b] svagotrabhāvitātmānaḥ sādhakasya varānane || 16-125 ||
yāścānyāśca vinirdiṣṭā raudrā bhairavamānaraḥ |
mahāmanthānarudrastu tāsāṃ maṇḍalanāyakaḥ || 16-126 ||
daśāṣṭādaśabhedena sāṃprataṃ kathayāmi te |
vṛttibhiḥ saṃsthitā ghorā sarvāstāmiṣa laṃpaṭāḥ || 16-127 || 
etābhiḥ sadbhirevaitajjagad vyāptaṃ carācaram |
caturyugavibhāgena kathayāmi samāsataḥ || 16-128 ||
dvividhā rūpasaṃpannā kulajā devatāstathā |
mānuṣyakulajā jñeyāstāsāṃ śṛṇu kulodgatim || 16-129 ||
brahmakṣatriyavidśūdrakulajā caiva nāyikāḥ |
saptāviṃśatimād varṣādūhu? jānanti tatpadam || 16-130 ||
anyasmin kulasaṃbhūtāḥ sākinyo rudramātarāḥ |
sabdaklinnestathābhīte vyādhibhiḥ paripīḍite || 16-131 ||
praharaṃtyupamardena śoṣayanti vasāmiṣam |
rūpasya parivartārthe carantyuttamamātaraḥ || 16-132 ||
devatā pūrvamākhyātā mātṛloke pratiṣṭhitāḥ |
surasiddhaiḥ masra * * *? munibhiśca tapodhanaiḥ || 16-133 ||
pūjyante tatprabhāvena loke pūjābhilāṣiṇaḥ |
adhvānaṃ niścalaṃ tāsāṃ rūpaṃ syāccarubhakṣaṇe || 16-134 ||
sā siddhistatparaṃ sthānaṃ tadarthe golakādaram |
eteṣvevaṃ varārohe kecit kuleṣvevaṃ vidhā yadā || 16-135 ||
tadetatpadamāpnoti vīrabhāvādanantarāt |
rudragomatikā nāgī guhā gopamukhāvalā || 16-136 ||
kekarā kālarātrī ca bhaṭānāgeśvarīti ca |
kṛte yuge bhaviṣyanti yoginyaḥ surapūjitāḥ || 16-137 ||
mālā durgā śivā caṇḍā vāmanī harṣaṇī prabhāḥ |
suparṇā rukmiṇī caiva tretāyāṃ kulamātarāḥ || 16-138 ||
harṣatārā suvarṇā ca dvāpare mama yogajāḥ |
prabhāmohā tathā śāntā haraṇī pālanī tathā || 16-139 ||
badhumālī bhānumatī śrīvalā kapilaprabhā |
muktā muktāvalī caiva tayā vai bhānunī parā || 16-140 ||
kalāvetā bhaviṣyanti tathānyāstu kule kule |
haritā gomukhī godhā dhātrī vīranakhī valā || 16-141 ||
kalāvetā bhaviṣyanti caṇḍālakulajāstriyaḥ |
nāmabhirmudratāstvetāstathānyā ca śivāśivāḥ || 16-142 ||
bhaviṣyanti ca bhūtāstu tāsāṃ saṃkhyā na vidyate |
111b g] mātṛmaṇḍalasaṃbodhāt saṃskārāt tapase'thavā || 16-143 ||
prāpnuvanti narā kecit siddhimetāmanuttamām |
harirvāmanakaḥ siṃho mahākālotha bhairavaḥ || 16-144 ||
pañcaite vīrarudrāstu sarvatantrānupālakāḥ |
śakuni sumatirnando gopālotha pitāmahaḥ || 16-145 ||
kṛtasyānte bhaviṣyanti yogeśīpatayo pare |
valiśca balinandaśca darśagrīvo haro hayaḥ || 16-146 ||
tretānte patayastāsāṃ suśriyogomukhopi ca |
bhīṣmaḥ pāka sikhaṇḍī ca muniścākhaṇḍalastathā || 16-147 ||
śakrayā śānakaḥ ṣaṇḍo dvāpare mātṛjambhakāḥ |
nabho bhānu ranantaśca helārājo mahāvalaḥ || 16-148 ||
bhīmārjunastathā droṇo mahārājo'tha sundaraḥ |
bhasmarājotha kalpāṇo madhusindūrakastathā || 16-149 ||
caturo vāmanaścaiva mātṛsāyojyagāminaḥ |
caturyugasya tasyānte śatamanyaṃ prakīrtitam || 16-150 ||
devyaśca pūjyamānastu sādhakaiśāstracintakaiḥ |
kathatṛpyanti? te sarve śāstrasadbhāvamuttamam || 16-151 ||

80a] sarvāsāṃpatayonyastu caturyugavibhāgataḥ |
bhavanti vīrarājāno mātṛṇāṃ patayopare || 16-152 ||
ucchuṣmo vāmadevasya ghughuśaśca tṛtīyakaḥ |
mahākālaścaturthe tu yogivīreśa nāśakāḥ || 16-153 ||
dhyānādikusumairmāsairvidhipānādisaṃskṛtaiḥ |
nirvanteṣṭhimanudvignastatrātmā pādatandritaḥ || 16-154 ||
tasyāśu saṃprayacchanti tantrasadbhāvamuttamam |
japāgnikārya taraye atyadbhutamidaṃ mahat || 16-155 ||
paraṃ parāgataṃ puṇyaṃ prakṣyātaṃ tu mayā tava |
devatānāṃ samākhyātāṃ svarūpaṃ siddhikāraṇam || 16-156 ||
aparāṇāṃ pravakṣyāmi bhedaṃ bhinnārthasūcakam |
pañcadhā tatsamāsena śṛṇu pārvati tatvataḥ || 16-157 ||
pañcadhā tu samāsena tāsāṃ madhipati sthitaḥ |
harirvāmanakaḥ siṃho mahākālotha bhairavaḥ || 16-158 ||
pañcaite tu samākhyātā vīrā hyevaṃ kulādhipāḥ |
śākinyo rudraśākinyaḥ sādhyaḥ sāmarikāḥ śivā || 16-159 ||
yoginyaśca parāstābhyo yebhirvyāptākhilaṃ jagat |
deśe deśe tathā grāme nagare catvare gṛhe || 16-160 ||
112a g] jāyante jātamātrāstu kulasiddhisamāśrayāt |
āgantukena vidhinā yoginyo yogasaṃskṛtāḥ || 16-161 ||
samastasamayopetā saṃpūrṇajñānadehajāḥ |
aparā prāptasamayā mantravratavidhau sthitāḥ || 16-162 ||
na karoti japaṃ homaṃ pūjanaṃ vratapālanam |
chalenākṛṣya pibate kṣudraprāṇimayaḥ sadā || 16-163 ||
rūpasya parivartārthe lubdhā ghātayate paśum |
śākinī sā tu vijñeyā raudrasthānaratā sadā || 16-164 ||
paracittagatirjñā ca rūpasya parivartanam |
karotpamṛtalubdhā ca jñeyā sā rudraśākinī || 16-165 ||
sādhya caivavidhā jñeyāṃ ludhvācārāratārcane |
na ghātayanti vai sarvaṃ chidreṇāsvādayanti ca || 16-166 ||
sāmaryāstvaparā jñeyā maṃtratadgatacetasāḥ |
pañcāmṛtaṃ samaśranti mānuṣaṃ vāhayanti ca || 16-167 ||
paryaṭanti kṣaṇātpṛthivī rūpaṃ kurvantyanekadhā |
aparā yogasāmarthyāstrikālaparivedikāḥ || 16-168 ||
tathāṣṭaguṇasaṃpannā parāntargatavedikāḥ |
śivāstu tāḥ samākhyātā mantradhyānaparāyaṇāḥ || 16-169 ||
yogena tapasā vāpi svamantrasya ca darśanāt |
yānti yogīśvarīsaṃsthāṃ tathā janmapaśorvalāt || 16-170 ||
puruṣaḥ sarvamātṛjñastadeva phalamāpnuyāt |
śivānāṃ saṃpravakṣyāmi bhedaṃ saptavidhaṃ punaḥ || 16-171 ||
śākinīnāṃ varāstāstu cāturvarṇamṛte ratāḥ |
kravyādino'parā jñeyā hīnayonau vyavasthitāḥ || 16-172 ||
sthūlamātrā bhaved yā tu gaurāṃgā vartulānanā |
atītaṃ sa nirīkṣyāśuraktamāsvādayed bhṛṣam || 16-173 ||
śabdaṃ śrutvā parā raudrī āsvādayati cāmṛtam |
śuklagaurā vṛhatkāyā sā jñeyā saumyalocanā || 16-174 ||
raktagaurā ca yā nārī tathā śuṣkastanī ghanāḥ |
vastreṇāhanya pivate raktaṃ vai sārvavarṇikam || 16-175 ||
śyāmavarṇā parā nārī proccaprahasanī sadā |
hasantī pivate raktaṃ sadā prāṇivadhe ratāḥ || 16-176 ||
aparā kṛṣṇavarṇābhā raktākṣī kalahapriyā |
rudrāntyā karṣayed raktaṃ paśūṃ hanati marmasu || 16-177 ||
karkaṭānya viśālākṣī śyāmāṅgā garvitā sadā |
gāyamānā pived raktaṃmajasraṃ cchardayet punaḥ || 16-178 ||
prasuptāṃ prāśayedanyā kṣaṇānsārayate paśum |
80b] śiralā vikṛtā ghorā dhvāṃkṣakeśī praroṣaṇī || 16-179 ||
rātrau gatvā śmaśānaṃ tu rudhirākarṣayed bahum |
tayā paśugṛhītastu śīghrameva vipadyase || 16-180 ||
aśivānāṃ pravakṣyāmi bhedaṃ saptavidhaṃ punaḥ |
yaṃ viditvā bhaved vīrastāsāṃ kulaviśāradaḥ || 16-181 ||
śākinī cūṣikā caiva cumbikā patralehikā |
uccheṣī nakracūṣī ca ūrddhvaniḥśvāsikā tathā || 16-182 ||
adhoniḥśvāsikā caiva āsāṃ karma śṛṇu priye |
śākinyaścottamāstāsāṃ śeṣā ghoratarā smṛtāḥ || 16-183 ||
ajasraṃ dūṣayed yā tu raktaṃ vai sārvavarṇikam |
gacchatastiṣṭhato vātha tena sā dūṣikā smṛtā || 16-184 ||
putranaptṛ tathā bhrātṛ mṛtāṃsvādati bālakam |
cumvamānā samāśnāti vijñeyā cumbikā tu yā || 16-185 || 
patreṇa mukhamācchādya pivate cāmṛtaṃ sadā |
patralehī smṛtā sā tu durnivāryā mahābalā || 16-186 ||
rātrau bhūtvā vivastrā yā mūtrayitvā pradakṣiṇam |
kṛtvā prāśayate nityaṃ muktakeśī tvākarṣayet || 16-187 ||
ucchuṣmikā tu sā jñeyā sādhakair vīravatsalā |
nāsāgraṃ vīkṣamāṇā tu cūṣayed yāmṛtaṃ sadā || 16-188 ||
nakracūṣī tu sā jñeyā ūrdhvavaktrā tathāparā |
śvāsenaikena pivate ūrdhvaniḥśvāsikā tu sā || 16-189 || 
nagnī bhūtā tu gaccheta rātrau paragṛhaṃ sadā |
vastreṇācchādya vaktraṃ tu bhūtvā caiva adhomukhī || 16-190 ||
pivate śoṇitaṃ nityaṃ adhoniḥśvāsikā tu sā |
punastāsāṃ mahādevi bhedaṃ vakṣyāmi yadyathā || 16-191 ||
cūṣikāyā trayo bhado yathāvatpravadāmi te |
pārśvaṃ vartayate yā tu raktaṃ caivā prakarṣayet || 16-192 ||
pārśvavartī tu sā jñeyā tuṇḍā moṭī parā tathā |
vaktuṃ moṭayamānaṃ tu trayo vārāmatandritā || 16-193 ||
kṣipramākarṣayedraktaṃ sadā devi na saṃśayaḥ |
trividhā tu samākhyātā cūṣikā varavarṇini || 16-194 ||
cumbikā ca dvidhā jñeyā karālī daṃturā tathā |
karālī dantavikaṭā karṣayet tu yadṛcchayā || 16-195 ||
pakṣirūpeṇa yā ghrāti danturā sā na saṃśayaḥ |
patralehā trayo bhedā yathāvatpravadāmi te || 16-196 ||
kekarā nalikā tantrī raudryā hyetāḥ prakīrtitāḥ |
113a g] kekarārddhanimīlākṣī rudhirā karṣayet sadā || 16-197 ||
nalikāyaikapithikā ekonmīlitanetrikāḥ |
stavdhanetrā sadā bhūtvā vāmādharanipīḍitā || 16-198 ||
ākarṣayet sadā raktamathavā stambhayet paśum |
trividhaiṣā samākhyātā nakracūṣī dvidhā bhavet || 16-199 ||
nidrikā cūṣikā caiva vṛttibhiḥ kathayāmi te | 
yatheṣṭaṃ bhūṣayetsuptaṃ cūṣikā niśi dehinām || 16-200 ||
nidrikā śayanaṃ kṛtvā raktamāsvādayed bhṛṣam |
tat'ontarddhānamāpnoti rūpasya parivartanam || 16-201 ||
uccheṣikā tridhā jñeyā sādhakaiścumvukaistathā |
uccā riktā trimūrtā tu tathā ca pisitāsanāḥ || 16-202 ||
nagnībhūtā tu yā nityaṃ viṣṭāntaṃ kurute drutam |
tatraiva gandhamāghrāti kṛtvā trīṇi pradakṣiṇām || 16-203 ||
tadā bhavati vijñānaṃ nānyathā tu varānane |
tristhāne tu trimūtrādau mūtraṃ kṛtvā pradakṣiṇam || 16-204 ||
gṛhāgrato gṛhapārśve gṛhapṛṣṭhe ca nityaśaḥ |
tatontarddhānamāpnoti rūpasya parivartanam || 16-205 ||
kṛtvā pradakṣiṇaṃ cānyā gātrābhyaṅgaṃ karoti ca |
tadā siddhimavāpnoti rūpādiparivartanam || 16-206 ||
ūrddhvapādyūrdhvakeśī ca ūrddhvaniśvāsikā dvidhā |
81a] muktakeśī ca yā bhūtvā nityamākarṣayet paśum || 16-207 ||
ūrddhvakeśīti sā jñeyā samāyād vīravatsale | 
adhaḥ śirorddhapādāśca kuryāt sūtrapradakṣiṇāḥ || 16-208 || 
asaṃbhrāntatrayo vārā evamekarṣayetpaśum | 
kathitaṃ te mayā vīre hṛdayāmidamuttamam || 16-209 || 
adho niśvāsikāmāṃ tu trayo bhedā bhavanti vai | 
mūlahārī tathā vyāghrī pecikā ca tṛtīyakā || 16-210 || 
kravyādī mūlahārī ca kapālaṃ gṛhya mānuṣam |
tatra sūtraṃ sadā kṛtvā trivārāṃ jihvayā likhet || 16-211 ||
nagnī bhūtvā sadā devi nityamākarṣayet sadā |
vyāghrī mānuṣyahastaṃ tu kaṃjinyā tu vinikṣipet || 16-212 ||
taṃ tu rātrau samuddhṛtya tenākarṣayate paśum |
evaṃ tasya bhavet sidddhirnānyathā vīravandite || 16-213 ||
mānuṣya na lakṣa?sādraṃ kaṃjinyā tu vinikṣipet |
sūtrayitvā tu tristhāna tṛṣṇatvā tu pradakṣiṇam || 16-214 ||
jihvayā saptadhā lehya tataḥ siddhimavāpnuyāt |
aṣṭādaśavidho bhedaḥ kṣudrāṇāṃ kathito mayā || 16-215 ||
113b g] adhocchāyīti brahyāṃśā naktacūṣī maheśvarī |
cūṣikā tu viśākhāṃśā vaiṣṇavyāṃśā tu pārvati || 16-216 ||
patralehī samākhyātā cāmuṇḍāṃ śā tu cumvikāḥ |
ūrddhvaniśvāsikāyā tu māhendrayāśā varānane || 16-217 ||
vārāhyaṃśā tatocchuṣmā kathitā vīravandite |
samayācārahīnasya yathā nayati cāgrataḥ || 16-218 ||
mātṛkaulikamatyugraṃ rahasyamidamuttamam |
nāsau dīrghāyuṣī devi taṃcāpi sutabhāg? bhavet || 16-219 ||
mahāghorapradeśeṣu pīḍyate yogibhiḥ sadā |
tasmāt sarvasvamatyarthaṃ rakṣayet prāṇavad budhaḥ || 16-220 ||
sāmānyamaparaṃ devi śṛṇuṣva kathayāmi te |
ceṣṭitaṃ martyajānāṃ tu prākṛtānāmagocaram || 16-221 ||
yā ca hā heti hasate nirgatya svagṛhāṃganam |
mahājanād bahir yāti jñeyā sā śākinīkule || 16-222 ||
brahme nidhāya kusumaṃ pādayobhimukhā sthitāḥ |
sakṛdālokayed dṛṣṭiṃ yasyākṣṇau bhinnatārakau || 16-223 ||
tasyādyai paitṛpādeśā sā raudrī vaiṣṇavīti ca |
lalāṭaṃ vidyate yasya dṛṣṭau ca pratimādvayam || 16-224 ||
sā śākinī rarau vāpi cihneretaiḥ prakīrtitāḥ |
na cālokaṃ nayatyātmī dṛṣṭiranyatra pātayet || 16-225 ||
yā nārī puruṣo vāpi jñātavyā sā tu tatkule |
ekānte vijane yā ca sthitā hasati vismitā || 16-226 ||
gāyate rudate vātha nṛtyamārabhate'thavā | 
gātrāṇi dhunate vātha śākinī sā na saṃśayaḥ || 16-227 ||
rātrau sandhyāprabhāte ca madhyāhne satataṃ priye |
śmaśānaṃ gacchate yā tu sā jñeyā tu tadaṃśakā || 16-228 ||
samayajñaśca mantrajñaḥ svecchayā phalamāpnuyāt |
tadanujñātakarma ca vīro vīravidhau sthitaḥ || 16-229 ||
grāme vā nagare'raṇye devatāyatane'pi vā |
vidhinā yena dṛśyanti tat pravakṣyāmi tatvataḥ || 16-230 ||
ḍūṃme dūṃme mahākāli tathā saṃhāra cāparam |
viśvaśūlena saṃyojyā vīramāteti cāparam || 16-231 ||
ehyehi jṛmbhaṇapadaṃ satyena brahmaṇe tathā |
vaiṣṇave caiva rudrāya catvāriṃśālipi smṛtāḥ || 16-232 ||
sādyantau mantrarājastu paraḥ sānnaidhyakārakaḥ |
anena dikṣu sarvāsu prakṣipet kusumodakam || 16-233 ||
dhurvaṃ yasyatyanenaiva devyastrailokyasaṃsthitāḥ |
pṛthivyā bhuvane loke pāle śivasannidhau || 16-234 ||
81b] yāścaranti viyatyasmin yāśca divyairabhistutāḥ |
tābhiḥ sārdhaṃ tadā vīro vicaret sa maheśvaraḥ || 16-235 ||
114a g] rahasyaṃ guhyamātṝṇāṃ nānyo jānāti tatvataḥ |
ikāraśākinī marma nitambena samanvitam || 16-236 ||
āsīnaṃ śūladaṇḍe tu dīpitaṃ śikhareṇa tu |
śākinīhṛdayaṃ nāma marmajīvitameva ca || 16-237 ||
jṛmbhanti tena tāḥ sarvā omityādisthitena tu |
namo'ntena varārohe stobhamāyānti sarvataḥ || 16-238 ||
oṃ bhūr bhuvasvaretyade pretasāsanimadhyataḥ |
yogeśvarīṇāṃ sarvāsāmeta marmavasatkriyā || 16-239 ||
stubhyanti vaśamāyānti klidyante klinnayonayaḥ |
na kuryāt sahasā caiva tadātmānopaghātakam || 16-240 ||
na ca dadyānmahādevi jijñāmārtha mahādhiyām |
evaṃ caranti tāḥ sarvā jagacchatrograkarmabhiḥ || 16-241 ||
bhuvane bhuvane devi bhuvaneśe tathaiva ca |
lokāntareṣu sarveṣu divirbhogeṣu cāpsu ca || 16-242 ||
kartavyaṃ cānumānaṃ ca yadvṛtaṃ yena yat kṛtam |
yaducchiṣṭaṃ yadā ghrārta maraṇaṃ janmajīvitam ? || 16-243 ||
hastasthamiva tat sarvaṃ paśyantyāchannadṛṣṭayaḥ |
yat kiñci draviṇaṃ loke navaṃ cārumanoharam || 16-244 ||
tat sarvaṃ prāpnuvantyetā vidhinānena suvrate |
vīro vā bhaginī vāpi tatra dharmānuvartinī || 16-245 ||
phalaṃ prāpnotyasandehād yat tadiha visarpitam |
hīnamadhyottamaṃ caiva svakīyāṃśavaśocitam || 16-246 ||
Seven types of mortal Yoginīs related to the ṣeven ṃothers who give knowledge and caru to the vīra of their own aṃśa i.e. Kula. Each Yoginī has particular lunar days when to meet her. 
athānyat paramaṃ devi vakṣyāmi tava lakṣaṇam |
1] yā nāryā raktagauryābhā savarṇāṃ vā kṛsodarī || 16-247 ||
mukhaṃ padmopamaṃ yasya dvijasnigdhā ghanastanī |
kadalīstambhasaṃkāśā purakau sumanoramau || 16-248 ||
trivalītaraṅga madhyasthā sutalalā suvarcasā |
cihurā kuñcitā yasyā gaṇḍakūpau tu jāyate || 16-249 ||
atiśaucā suśāntā ca vaidadhvanipriyā sadā |
śuklavastrapriyā nityaṃ tathā caivottarīyakam || 16-250 ||
devabhaktā mahā devi viprāṇāṃ pūjane ratā |
amāvāsyāṃ tathā devi pratipatsu viśeṣataḥ || 16-251 ||
parvāṇyetāni devīnāṃ bhavantīha na saṃśayaḥ |
kamaṇḍaluṃ tathā daṇḍaṃ kāṣṭhamutkṛṣṭakājinam || 16-252 ||
svastikaṃ likhate haste? vedavākyāni codgiret |
etaiścihnaistu boddhavyā brāhyaṃśā devasātvikāḥ || 16-253 ||
mudrāntasyā pradarśeta tataḥ prītyā bhaviṣyati |
ṣaṇmāsād varadā devi svakīyāṃ śasya sādhake || 16-254 || 
114b g] anaṃśasyāpi cādeśaṃ vatsarāt kurute priye |
2] śuklagauryā tu yā gauryā vibhaktāṅgī ghanā ruhā || 16-255 ||
kapilā dīrghakeśī ca kapilekṣaṇarūgviṇā |
lalāṭe dṛśyate corddhe trayo rekhā triśūlagāḥ || 16-256 ||
vidhibhaktā ca boddhavyā rudrārcanaparāyaṇā |
samayikaṃ putrakaṃ vāpi ācāryaṃ sādhako'pi vā || 16-257 ||
dṛṣṭvā duṣyati yā nārī mahākaukuhalānvitā |
aṣṭamī ca tathā devi bhūtyākhyā parvaṇī yathā || 16-258 ||
evaṃ vai lakṣaṇaṃ jñātvā mudrāntasyāḥ pradarśayet |
triśūlaṃ vātha khaṭvāṅgaṃ ḍamaruko vātha govṛṣam || 16-259 ||
khaḍgaṃ va kartrikāṃ caiva sadā cchatrastu pāṇinā |
svakīyāṃśasya vīrasya saṃpradāyaṃ prayacchati || 16-260 ||
tribhirmāsairvarārohe itarasya śubhaṃ vadet |
3] yā nārī pītagaurābhā pītavastraratā sadā || 16-261 ||
82a] udyānavanasaktā tu brahmacaryaratā sadā |
mayūradhvanigaṃbhīrā priyālāpā sumālinī || 16-262 ||
ṣaṣṭhī tu parvaṇī tasyā jñātavyā vīravatsale |
evaṃ prayatnato jñātvā mudrāṃ tasyā pradarśayet || 16-263 ||
śaktighaṇṭā patākā vā vapūraṃ vāpi darśayet |
śvāsasya caiva vīrasya ṣaṇmāsā tu varapradā || 16-264 ||
itarasya varārohe sthānaṃ cādeśya vatsarāt |
4] śyāmavarṇā tu yā nārī atasīpuṣpasannibhā || 16-265 ||
pītāmbarapriyā nityaṃ mallayuddhā tu rāgiṇī |
madyāsaktā varārohe dānavānā śakapriyā || 16-266 ||
tāṃbūlapuṣpaveṣāḍhyā atikāmā gaṇapriyā |
tyaktalajjā sadā jñeyā aṭanārthe ratā sadā || 16-267 ||
evaṃ jñātvā tu mantrajño mudrāṃ tasyā pradarśayet |
śaṃkhamudgarasāraṅgacakraṃ muśalameva vā || 16-268 ||
svagotre varadā devī vigotre śubhamādiśet |
5] bhinnāṃjanapratīkāśā daṃturā pāvikadvijā || 16-269 ||
lambastanī bṛhatkāyā sthūlamātrā ghanatviṣā |
ārakte locane tasyā bhavate nirdayā sadā || 16-270 ||
nistriśā nispṛhā devi bhavate kalahapriyā |
vārāhyaṃśā samuddiṣṭā mudrāṃ tasya pradāpayet || 16-271 ||
daṇḍamudgarasāraṅgaṃ muśalaṃ parvameva vā |
svāṅgasya caiva vīrasya dadāti vidhimuttamam || 16-272 ||
itarasyāpi cādeśaṃ varṣeṇaikena sevanāt |
6] yā nārī campakābhā tu vicitravasana priyā || 16-273 ||
nṛtyagītasadāsaktyā sarvābharaṇaśīlinī |
115a g] āyudhānāṃ kathā raktā digindravāhanapriyā || 16-274 ||
indrāṇī sā tu boddhavyā lakṣaṇena vilakṣitā |
ekādaśyāṃ tu boddhavyā parvaṇī tu na saṃśayaḥ || 16-275 ||
mudrā pradarśayet tasya tataḥ prītyā bhaviṣyati |
vajraṃ śaṃkhaṃ tu kheṭaṃ vā cāmaraṃ śaktimaṃkuśam || 16-276 ||
tataḥ sā varadā devī bhavate sādhaksya tu |
anaṃśasyāpi ṣaṇmāsāt kathayeti upāsanāt || 16-277 ||
7] yā nārī śuklakaṇṭhoṣṭhā dīrghagrīvā kṛṣodarī |
koṭarākṣī pralambā ca hastau pādau sudīrghakau || 16-278 ||
lambastanī ca nirmāṃsā vahvāsī madhupiṅgalā |
rātrau paryaṭane saktā akṣobhyā satya vādinī || 16-279 ||
śmaśāne niratā nityaṃ vīrabhāvakatha priyā |
ūrddhvakeśī jvalābhānu yogī sā na saṃśayaḥ || 16-280 ||
mudrāṃ tasya pradarśyeta kapālaṃ śūlameva vā |
khaṭvāṃgāṃ ḍamarukaṃ vāpi piṃcchakaṃ vāthavā priye || 16-281 ||
svāṃśasya caiva vīrasya dadāti saṃpradāyakm |
carukaṃ vā paśuṃ vāpi vidhivā puṣkalaṃ priye || 16-282 ||
anyeṣāṃ kathayatyāśu sevanād vatsarārddhataḥ |
vijñānamudayaṃ māsāṃ kathyamānaṃ nibodha me || 16-283 ||
pīṭhajāścāṣṭabhirvarṣaiḥ kṣetrajā dvādaśābdikāḥ |
dvāre ṣoḍaśabhirdevi yonijā saptaviṃśati || 16-284 ||
tāsāṃ nāmnā tu deveśi lakṣaṇāni tathā śṛṇu |
īśānyādiṣu vargeṣu nāmānyatra prapaṭhyate || 16-285 ||
akārādyaṣṭavargāṇi aṣṭayogigatāni ca |
ekaḥ ṣoḍaśavarṇastu pañcapañcākṣarānpriye || 16-286 ||
dvau caturakṣarau devi lakṣitavyānyanukramāt |
āpā grahāstathā nāgā yoginyo bhairavāṇi ca || 16-287 ||
mantrodayāni deveśiṃ ? vargāṣṭakamudāhṛtam |
ādimadhyāvasāne ca ye varṇānāmasaṃjñitāḥ || 16-288 ||
82b] pūrvoktalakṣaṇaṃ tāsāṃ jñātavyaṃ sādhakottamaiḥ |
ādyantākṣararodhena yasyanāma pravartate || 16-289 ||
tadaṃśā sā vijānīyāt sā mudrā tasya darśayet |
svadinaṃ svodayaṃ devi svadiśāṃ svagrahodayam || 16-290 ||
tyajya yasthomakaṃ dadyād yogibhiḥ sopahāsyate |
ḷunar ḍays of the ṃothers and the Yoginīs who embody them
1] caturdaśyāṃ ca saptamyāṃ dinaisanyāḥ prakīrtitam || 16-291 ||
paurṇamāsyāmavāvāsyāṃ pratipacca caturmukhī |
2] tṛtīyā caiva ṣaṣṭhī ca kaumāryā vāsaro smṛtāḥ || 16-292 ||
3] dvādaśyāṃ ca caturthī ca vaiṣṇavyāhaḥ prakīrtitam |
4] pañcamyāṃ ca trayodaśyāṃ indrāṇyāyā dinā smṛtāḥ || 16-293 ||
5] 115b g] navamyekādaśī caiva vārāhyā tu dināstvamī |
6] aṣṭamyāṃ ca dvitīyā ca cāmuṇḍāyā dinā smṛtāḥ || 16-294 ||
7] aṣṭamyāṃ ca daśamyāṃ ca yogeśyānāṃ dināḥ priye |
8] dhvajādityastathānanta īśānyā yoginī matā || 16-295 ||
bhairavaṃ nāma sundaryaṃ svavargānmantramucyate |
etat kālaṃ samāsādya cchoṃmakaṃ tu pradāpayet || 16-296 ||
sarvasāmānyasiddhīnāṃ lakṣaṇaṃ kathitaṃ mayā |
ataḥ paraṃ pravakṣyāmi pīṭhajānāṃ tu lakṣaṇam || 16-297 ||
kṣetrajāṃ dvārapālānāṃ yonijānāṃ yathāsthitam |
cipiṭā nāsā kṛṣā caryā snāyusnigdhā pradṛśyate || 16-298 ||
na nirīkṣetyumāṃ kaściddevakarmaratā sadā |
yonijā sā tu vijñeyā cihnaṃ caiva yathā śṛṇu || 16-299 ||
uttarāvoṣṭasaṃsthā tu keśā dīrghā pradṛśyate |
etaccihnaṃ tu vijñātvā yonimudrāṃ pradarśayet || 16-300 ||
pratimudrāṃ darśayet sā liṅgākhyā sādhakasya tu |
ūrddhvakeśā mahākrūrā vikaṭā daśanā bhavet || 16-301 ||
tūryabhāṣī sthirā caiva latāhastā sadā bhavet |
animīlanākṣī kubjī dṛṣyate ca varānane || 16-302 ||
kṛṣṇarekhā bhavedgaṇḍo sabhave dvārapālikāḥ |
tāṃ tu savyaktato jñātvā diśo mudrā pradarśayet || 16-303 ||
pratimudrāṃ dadet sopi iṣaddhāsyaṃ prakurvati |
eṣā siddhi nirodhāstu sādhakānāṃ yaśasvini || 16-304 ||
dīrghaghoṇā kṛṣā caiva śuṣkagātrā ca piṅgalā |
mārjārākṣī mahāraudrī śavaṃ caiva nirīkṣaṇi || 16-305 ||
vātavegā mahācaṇḍā gaṇḍau romāṅkitau sadā |
īṣacca daṃturā caiva sā bhavet kṣetrajā priye || 16-306 ||
tāṃ ca samyaktato jñātvā adhaścaiva pradarśayet |
daśanaśabdaṃ ca kurute sā smṛtā kṣetrasaṃbhavāḥ || 16-307 ||
rudraśakti parā nityaṃ sugandhamadhupiṅgalā |
vṛhodarī sadā tuṣṭā kuṭilā caiva mūrdhvajā || 16-308 ||
ātāmranayanā sā tu mṛgacihnaṃ tanāntare |
śīghravegā na nirīkṣet sādhakānāṃ catuṣpati || 16-309 ||
bhūtaṃ bhavyaṃ bhaviṣyaṃ ca parokṣe caiva buddhyati |
tādṛśaṃ rūpakaṃ dṛṣṭvā ūrddhvadṛṣṭiṃ pradarśayet || 16-310 ||
jihvā saṃcālayet sā tu bhṛkuṭī caiva darśayet |
eṣā vai pīṭhajā jñeyā yoginīnāṃ tu saṃbhavā || 16-311 ||
gauravarṇā mahādīrghasaṃgrāmaṃ caivamicchati |
116a g] āraktastu bhavetkleśā piṅgalākṣā sugandhinī || 16-312 ||
pratyūṣai snigdhavarṇā tu madhyāhne katisnigdhatāḥ |
iṣadarśitavarṇā tu aparāhṇe ca sundari || 16-313 ||
rātrāvadṛśyatāṃ yāti dine caiva tathaiva ca |
māyārūpadharā sā tu sā bhavet siddhiyoginī || 16-314 ||
tasya mudrāṃ svakāṃ dadyāt kaniṣṭhā cūlikopari |
pratimudrāṃ dadet sāpi adhasta? vikṛtānanā || 16-315 ||

83a] etadrūpaṃ tato jñātvā varjanīyā manobhavaiḥ |
yonijā dvārapāle tu kṣetrajā pīṭhavāsinī || 16-316 ||
siddhayogeśvarī cānyā mayākhyātā varānane |
evaṃ lakṣaṇamākhyātaṃ anekaistu prakārakaiḥ || 16-317 ||
na jñāyate varārohe vinā tāsāmanugrahāt |
saṃkīrṇalakṣaṇā devyo durvijñeyā hi śaktayaḥ || 16-318 ||
viśvāsaṃ na punaryāti putrasyāpi varānane |
prāṇairapi viyujyeta na ca veṣṭāṃ prakāśati || 16-319 ||
devatā darśanenaiva siddhirbhavati tatkṣaṇāt |
yoginīdarśanenaiva māse na labhate carum || 16-320 ||
śākinīdarśane devi kiñcid kotra sādhake |
prayacchanti varārohe mantrakrīḍākaraṃ hi yat || 16-321 ||
dvārapālyastu yā proktā saṃdohe yāśca saṃsthitāḥ |
sādhake vā prayacchanti jñānaṃ caiva svakaṃ svakam || 16-322 ||
kṣudrāṇāṃ darśane devi na kiñcid vidyate priye |
asamarthā yatastāstu bandhabandheṣu cāyatāḥ || 16-323 ||
itarāsyā yathā martyā parapresāstu vartinā |
samastavratamantrāṇāṃ tarpaṇasya japasya ca || 16-324 ||
yā karoti ca yatyāṅgaṃ kṣudrāṃśā śākinī matā |
bhavanti vividhākārā martye kleśasya bhājanāḥ || 16-325 ||
pāpakṣaye punardevi bhavante maṇḍalādhipāḥ | 
evaṃ jñātvā varārohe yathāśāstraprakāśitam || 16-326 || 
tat tathā pālanīyaṃ hi yādīcchreyasamātmanaḥ |

devyuvāca

yoginīlakṣaṇaṃ deva kathitaṃ tu tvayā vibho || 16-327 ||
saṃgamaṃ tu yathātāyāṃ tathā vadamaheśvara |

bhairava uvāca

śṛṇu devi pravakṣyāmi yathā tāsāṃ tu saṃgamam || 16-328 ||
bhavate sādhakendrasya tathā vakṣyāmi tatvataḥ | 
bhairavaṃ hṛdayaṃ devi jagatsaṃhārakārakam || 16-329 || 
tena yuktā sadā devyāḥ klidyante klinnayonayaḥ |
dahyante patate śīghraṃ prāṇaṃ muṃcyanti bhūriṣaḥ || 16-330 ||
vaśamāyantyasaṃdehāt taptā bhairavavahninā |
dṛṣṭvā bālāsamūhaṃ tu hṛdayena tu bhāvayet || 16-331 ||
vahnimaṇḍalamadhyasthaṃ bhairavānalatāpitam |
116b g] samūhaṃ bhāvayedevaṃ niruddhaṃ hṛdayena tu || 16-332 || 
yāvantyastatra śākinya āgacchanti na saṃśayaḥ | 
saṃbhāvayanti taṃ vīryamakāryaṃ tu tvayā kṛtam || 16-333 || 
visarjayet tato mantrī tatkṣaṇānnaivaṃ dhārayet |
anyathā cchidrayantyevaṃ sādhakaṃ kupitātmanaḥ || 16-334 ||
grāmaṃ vā nagaraṃ vāpi dhyāyenmaṇḍalamadhyagam |
rātrau caiva śmaśānasthaḥ piṇḍojjvalitamākulam || 16-335 ||
dahyamānaṃ tu tatsthānaṃ mahābhairavavahninā | 
kṣaṇārddhaṃ yāva deveśi dhyāyamāno japettataḥ || 16-336 ||
yāvantyastatra śākinyā āgacchanti sahasraśaḥ |
tiṣṭhanti sādhakaṃ veṣṭvā kimarthaṃ trāsitāstvayā || 16-337 ||
imaṃ pāpaṃ durācāraṃ paśuṃ kṛtvā juṣusvatha |
tenāpi evaṃ vaktavyaṃ pūjārthe'vāhitā mayā || 16-338 ||	tāsāṃ tu dāpayenmantrī aliphalguṣamāṃsakam | 
yadṛcchayā varārohe anyathāhetumicchati || 16-339 || 
tṛptāstāḥ sādhakaṃ sarvāḥ śasanti niyatavratam |
dadanti kiñcit krodhaṃ yena mantro vijṛmbhati || 16-340 ||
dṛṣṭvā palasamūhaṃ tu piṇḍaṃ kulīśarūpiṇam |
ākṛṣya tena taṃ vṛndaṃ dahyamānaṃ mahātmanā || 16-341 ||
piṇḍena bhairaveṇaiva āgacchanti na saṃśayaḥ |
śākinyo yāstu tatraiva balānyā vā vīranāyike || 16-342 || 
triśūlaṃ kalpayed devi samūhasyāgrato'pi vā | 
83b] hṛdayena balenaiva samantādvahnisaprabham || 16-343 ||
yadā na laṃghayed yā tu sā jñeyā rudraśākinī |
evaṃ caiva varārohe sve sve mudrā na laṃghanāt || 16-344 ||
jñātavyā śākinī vīraiḥ kalpayedagrato yadi |
hṛdayena balenaivaṃ nānyathā vīravatsale || 16-345 ||
jñātavyāstāstu mantrajño svāṃśāyogyā na laṃghanāt | 
śākinīhṛdayaṃ devi śmaśānamāśritya coccaret || 16-346 || 
rātraiḥ sahasramekaṃ tu yāvajjapati sādhakaḥ | 
tāvat kṣobho bhavatyāśu aśeṣasya purasya tu || 16-347 ||
āgacchanti tatastāstu veṣṭvāntaṃ sādhakeśvaram |
vidravanti bhayatrastā durnivāryo mahābalāḥ || 16-348 ||
evaṃ dṛṣṭvā na bhetavyaṃ pramādānmanasā priye | 
bhairavākārarūpaṃ tu bhāvitavyaṃ yathā hyaham || 16-348 ||
paścādarghaṃ pradātavyaṃ yathā tāsāṃ priye bhavet |
visarjyeta tato devyo hṛdayena tu pūrvavat || 16-349 || 

śmaśānakapāṭaṃ gṛhya mahāmāyā parāparāḥ | 
na tu likhya tataḥ sarvā saptabhedāntagā priye || 16-350 ||
pañcadhā yā samākhyātā yonayo rūpikā kule |
117a g] śākinīhṛdayenaiva yāvad vidyā samāpyate || 16-351 ||
anulome vidarbhyeta nāmāntaritayogataḥ |
pūjayet puṣpagandhaistu ālabhed hṛdayena tu || 16-352 ||
dhvajakalpya tato mantrī śmaśānastho japet tataḥ |
āgacchanti tato devyo yāḥ kāścit puravāsinī || 16-353 ||
kṣubhyanti vaśamāyānti klidyante klinnayonayaḥ |
yathā na jñāyate kaścit saṃbhāṣayati sādhakam || 16-354 ||
cchommakaiśca vicitraiśca bhairavairaṃgajaiḥ śubhaiḥ |
sādhakena tato jñātvā hṛdayena visarjayet || 16-355 ||
anyathā kupyate devya cchidrayanti durāsadāḥ |
evaṃ devi mayākhyātā haṭhānmelāpakaṃ priye || 16-356 ||
na kartavyā varārohe pramādāt sādhakena tu |
tasmāt kiṃ bahunoktena manasāpi na cintayet || 16-357 ||
putravad rakṣayed yastu yadicchecchreyamātmanaḥ |
ataḥ paraṃ pravakṣyāmi yathā devīti saṃgamam || 16-358 ||
mitrasambandhibhāvena yathā bhavati tacchṛṇu |
kliṃkārasaṃpuṭāntasthaṃ hṛdayaṃ vidyayā saha || 16-359 ||
japennādi krameṇaiva phāntavarṇatvamāgatā |
svaradvādaśabhedena sukhayogisamāgamam || 16-360 || 
kli/ kle/ kli/ puṭitaṃ hṛdayaṃ yonyā yuktaṃ japet priye |
māṣā dvādaśa bhedena melakaṃ ca dvitīyakam || 16-361 ||
kle/ kle/ kle/ puṭitaṃ marmaṃ japedyonigataṃ priye |
melakaṃ ca tṛtīyasyāt kathitaṃ tava śobhane || 16-362 ||
kliṃ ai/ kli/ puṭitvā tu marmaṃ tu dvādaśaiḥ kramāt |
japedyoniyutaṃ bhadre melakaṃ tu caturthakam || 16-363 ||
kro/ kli/ kro/ puṭāntasthaṃ japedyoniyutaṃ priye |
dvādaśaistu surairbhinnaṃ melakaṃ pañcamaṃ smṛtam || 16-364 ||
hrī/ kli/ hrī/ puṭitaṃ marmaṃ bheditaṃ dvādaśaiḥ svaraiḥ |
japedyonigataṃ bhadre ṣaṣṭhamaṃ melakaṃ yutam || 16-365 ||
calacakravibhāgena saptamaṃ bhavatenaghe |
mālinyāyā padāyutaṃ japetmarma sa uttamam || 16-366 ||
aṣṭamaṃ tu tadā devi melakaṃ yoginīgaṇe |
padabhedavibhāgena yadā japati pārvati || 16-367 ||
tadā kṣobhaṃ jagasyaiva bhavate nātra saṃśayaḥ |
dhyānaṃ pūjā japaṃ homaṃ samayāpālakasya ca || 16-368 ||
ārādhakasya suśroṇi melakaṃ ca bhaviṣyati |
sukhopāyena bhaktyā ca ārādhyaṃ na tu kopayet || 16-369 ||
kupitāñchidraṃ vai devyo na ca śāntiṃ prayacchati |
tasmāddevi vijānīyādārādhyaṃ toṣayet sadā || 16-370 ||

84a] ārādhyenaiva kupite mantrasevā vṛthā bhavet |
mantrasevā yadā vyarthā tadā siddhiṃ na jāyate || 16-371 ||

iti bhairavasrotasi mahātantre vidyāpīṭhe saptakoṭipramāṇe śrītantrasadbhāve yoginīlakṣaṇādhikāraḥ ṣoḍaśaḥ paṭalaḥ || 16 ||





devyuvāca

vada me sisa sandohaṃ sehārīṇāṃ yathānvayam |
sādhakāṃ krīḍanārthāya mantrakośamanuttamam || 17-1 ||

bhairava uvāca

śṛṇu devi pravakṣyāmi yogisadbhāvakrīḍanam |
nigrahaṃ ca yathā tāsāṃ kathyamānaṃ nibodha me || 17-2 ||
oṃ namo virūpākṣa kirikiri rakṣarakṣa idaṃ svāhā |
anena mantreṇa mṛttikayā rakṣā kuryāt sarvabhayeṣu rakṣā bhavati |
oṃ namo rudrāya vijayarūpadharāya darśaya 2 culu 2 svāhā |
anena kāṣṭhikā saptaviṃśatyaṅgulā saptābhimantritaṃ kṛtvā gaṇikā bhavanti yatra rocate tatra gacchati |
oṃ cāmuṇḍe culu culu svāhā | 
anena pāṭalā kāṣṭhikā sapta japtā śayane sthāpayet |
strīrūpeṇa tiṣṭhati |
oṃ cāmuṇḍe parivartaya rūpaṃ muñca muñca liri liri svāhā |
anena mantreṇa hastaṃ parijāpyayaṃ spṛśati sakāṣṭhavadbhavati |
oṃ cāmuṇḍe culu culu svāhā |
anena cakṣuṃ vandhayati janasya rūpeṇa |
oṃ cāmuṇḍe matha matha dama dama hara hara svāhā |
anena tṛṇapiṇḍikānnavābhijapya sthāpayet |
mṛtakamānayati |
oṃ cāmuṇḍe kili kili darśaya satyaṃ hana hana svāhā |
dvādaśamāsaguḍikāṃ gṛhya saptajaptāṃ tu svedadāyuṃ jānāti |
ekaguḍikāyā sviṃnāyā ekamāsaṃ jīvati |
ṣaḍ bhirvarṣāyuratha sarvā dīrghāyurbhavati |
atha sarvā nosvi cā dvādaśāhaṃ jīvati |
oṃ cāmuṇḍe culu culu gṛhṇa vandite svāhā |
anena viśati sarṣapā nāmaṃ gṛhya yasya rudhireṇapra?kṣayet |
prabhāte ye cāṃkurāyante tatrya hamātraṃ jīvati |
atha sarveṇa saptāhaṃ jīvati |
oṃ cāmuṇḍe sādani sādani ādani sura sura svāhā |
anena dṛṣṭamātreṇa raktamākarṣayati mukhaṃ spṛśati raktapūrṇaṃ bhavati |
oṃ cāmuṇḍe ajagari darśaya yavāsisvāmi svāhā |
anena caityamṛttikāṃ gṛhyayet sayitā siṃcayet tṛśayanikā bhavanti |
118a g] udakena mokṣaḥ |
oṃ cāmuṇḍe ūrddhavṃ tiṣṭha 2 kiri 2 tiṣṭha 2 lulu svāhā |
anenodakaṃ saptābhimantritaṃ kṛtvā gṛhaṃ siñcayet ūrdhvanti saivabhasmanā patati |
oṃ cāmuṇḍe visphoṭaya visphoṭaya laṃghaya laṃghaya mara mara māgami sarveṣāṃ samaya mantra svāhā |
anena bhasmanā siṃcayet sarvāṅgaṃ sphuṭati tanumāṃsaṃ khādati rudhiraṃ pivati hastadarśane jīvāpayati |
oṃ cāmuṇḍe vidara vidara vācaṃ spṛśati tejāṃ vandhati ṭiṭṭi svāhā |
anena yaṃ spṛśati tasya vācāṃ harati |
ekadivasaṃ yadivā rātrau dṛṣṭvā paṭhet |
oṃ cāmuṇḍe viṣū viṣu gṛhṇa svāhā |
anena rājasarṣapāṃ abhimantryāṃgeṣu dāpayet |
* * * * ? bhavati |
oṃ cāmuṇḍe pulla pulla dhura dhura kalāpānāṃ bhavati |
anyarūpadhāraṇi dhāraya dhāraya hū/ hū/ allalla gṛhṇa svāhā |

84b] anena paśuromaṃ gṛhya saptābhimantritaṃ vandhayet tadvadrūpo bhavati apanīte mokṣaḥ |
oṃ cāmuṇḍe mahāvale devyai cuhu cuhu hū/ svāhā |
anena hastamabhimantrya mukhaṃ spṛśenmudrito bhavet |
oṃ cāmuṇḍe vikṛtānane gṛhṇa mātare dhūpaṃ mama siddhikari hā hā kiri svāhā |
anena prakṛtiṃ kṛtvā vāmapādataleṃ carmaṃ gṛhya dhūpo deyo yasya ātmā tasya kampajvaro bhavati |
kañjikena tasya dāgho bhavati naramūtreṇa mahādāho bhavati |
kṣīreṇa mokṣaḥ |
pādaranjena dhūpo deyastataḥ śūlo bhavati |
yatrāṅge dīyate tatra vyathā bhavati |
utpaladhūpena mokṣaḥ |
oṃ cāmuṇḍe ghātani dharaṇi dhāraṇi mathani dhara dhara bandhabandha svāhā |
anena paramantraṃ stambhayati dṛṣṭiṃ na paśyati |
oṃ cāmuṇḍe tiri tiri cchinda chinda vidhātre hiri hiri sphura sphura tiṣṭha tiṣṭha vividha vividha svāhā |
anena śamaśānaṃ gatvā mānuṣyakapālaṃ dhārayitvā kṛṣṇacaturdaśyāṃ paśumāṃsena tasyāgrato baliṃ datvā tāvajjaped yāvat sphuṭati |
ākāśena gacchati yatra śmaśāne tiṣṭhati tatra gṛhaṃ paśyati |
māṇikyasiṃhāsanaṃ vastrāṇi vidhāni ca mauktikamanayo bhakṣyabhojyāni yanmanasā cintayati tatsarvaṃ bhavati |
prabhāte ityāha bhagavānnīśānaḥ punarapi bhagavāñchākinīnāṃ nivartā parāṃ bhavati |
118b g] bhrātarasya siddhaye nindā gopaṃ rakṣaṇīyaṃ prayatnena dvau tāvad ātmarakṣā kartavyāḥ |
oṃ namo yogeśvarāya viśvādhipataye nila nila riti riti mama rakṣa rakṣa gaṇeśvara vidyeśvara śmaśānapriya thavi * vi hū/ hū/ ghātaya ghātaya svāhā |
anena puṣpā rocanā dūrvā pravālagranthi vā saptābhimantritaṃ kṛtvā śirasi dhārayecchikhā bandham ātmarakṣā vai sarvakarmāṇi kārayet |
oṃ namo daṃṣṭrākarālāya pratikūlajihvāparākṣasādhipataye gaṇapriyāaya dhu dhūpa smara smara hū/ hū/ rakṣa rakṣa mama svāhā |
anena śarīraṃ saptajaptena sarvatra spṛśet sarvabhayebhyo parājito bhavati |
oṃ cāmuṇḍe gaṇakālimahāvale amukaṃ vināśani pararūpadarśī na darśa darśatra tra mu mu ru ru svāhā |
anena gocano saptavārānparijāpya mukhamabhyaṅgayettato bhaginyaḥ paśyati |
yasya mastake pecikāṃ paśyati sā bhaginyā vaktavyā |
atha kathaṃ yacikāṃ na paśyati saptābhimantritaṃ mukhaṃ kṛtvā mukhaṃ prakṣyālya jānāti |
sā bhaginī samayaṃ jānet sābandhanīyā |
atha bhaginīṃ dṛṣṭvābhivādanaṃ kuryācchummakena vā svahastenāṅgulinā nāsikāṃ spṛśed bhrātarāṇāmabhivādanaṃ kṛtaṃ bhavati |
bhaginī vāmapādena pādāṃ spṛśed bhaginīpratyabhivādanaṃ kṛtaṃ bhavati |
tato dṛṣṭvā sarvaṃ karoti |
atha bhaginyātmānaṃ gṛhyati tasyāḥ * * * * * * * *? *litāsyatorayet |
85a] vastraṃ toritaṃ bhavati |
atha nāmaye nāmito bhavati |
tataḥ śākinī yācayati ahaṃ tava vadāsajā ājñā desi taṃ karomi |
evamityevaṃ cchommakena vyavahāraṃ kartavyam |
oṃ cāmuṇḍe ānaya vinayakāle sa 2 paca paca bhūta mantrite svāhā |
yena kenacid gṛhītaṃ yatkiñcit syad gṛhṇaṃ tu dadāmi vā ato bhruvante yādṛśaṃ āṇaveśīti yamicchāmiti taṃ pūrayanti kiñcit samasanti nirogī bhavanti atha na kurvate tato bhaginī sarvameva mudrayati |
āturasya homaṃ kuryād anena mantreṇa |
119a g] oṃ cāmuṇḍe bhagini bhāgaṃ samiṃsi 2 hara svāhā |
anena saṃmārjanyā saptakāṣṭhikāṃ saptābhimantritāṃ kṛtvāgnau juhyādanena bhasmanā sabhālayet tato dakuṃbhaṃ gṛhya japet |
samayet paśurogān sarvāṃstataḥ svastho bhavati |
oṃ cāmuṇḍe haraṇi kara kara karṣaṇi ca rudhana rudhana hana hana bhañja bhañja cala cala yogāṃ svāhā |
anena priyaṅgupallavaṃ gṛhyāṅge sthā?payedaśirāṃ sarvāṃ mārayet |
tanastenodakena nakhā dantāḥ keśāṃ vā malasandadet |
tataḥ svastho bhavati |
athavā mana tu āhutīnāṃ śataṃ deyaṃ tena dhūpena saṃspṛśet sa eva nirogo bhavati |
rogiṇasya rocanayā tilakaṃ kuryāt tata svastho bhavati |
oṃ cāmuṇḍe cala cāraṇī sama sama jaṃpa jaṃpa juho pātani māraṇi pūraya pūraya culu culu svāhā |
anena balividhānaṃ kṛtvā yasyāṅgāni śuṣyanti dāgho bhavati rudhiramokṛṣṭaṃ bhavati |
etenaiva kṣīrāhutīḥ sahasraṃ juhuyāt tataḥ svastho bhavati |
atha bhaginīmaṇḍale pratikṛtiṃ kṛtvā kṣīreṇa siñcayecchūlaṃ bhavati |
devadārusamidhāṃ saptajaptāṃ kṛtvā ghṛtāktāṃ sahasraṃ juhyāt sarvaśūlāṃ praṇaśyati |
athāṅgaṃ duḥkhāpayate kiñcittailaṃ parijapya deyaṃ tataḥ svastho bhavati |
atha bhaginīṃnigrahaṃ vyākhyāsyāmi māraṇaṃ siṭṭapiṭṭanaṃ bhedanī vaśīkaraṇaṃ bhavati |
oṃ namaḥ kṛtāntarūpāya vikṛtāya ajihve jihveśvarijihve mathane matha matha bhūtādhivāsinī pilebhi svāhā |
anena madanakaṇṭhakānāṃ tīṣṇa tailāktānāṃ juhyānmanasā cintya sā bhaginī saptāhena mriyati |
tasya nirvatā panaṃkṣīrāhutiṃ juhuyāt tataḥ svastho bhavati |
athabhaginyā raktasrāva kartuṃ kāmasakṣīravṛkṣe prati kīlakaṃ kṛtvā sapta japte bhage nikhātayed raktasrāvo bhavati kṣīreṇa sikto mokṣaḥ |
gandhāhutīnāṃ śataṃ juhyāt kṣayo bhavati |
ghṛtāhutiśatena mokṣaḥ |
kāṣṭhikāṃ śatajaptāṃ dantasthāne sthāpayecchūlo bhavati |
avanīte mokṣaḥ |
oṃ cāmuṇḍe bhasmakapāle lala lala dara dara hu hu vama vama hahani pavani svāhā |
119b g] anena mṛttikayā pratikṛtiṃ kṛtvā tasyopari mutrayet |
bhaginya ruvārtā bhavanti vruvanti kimājñāpayasi tatastuṣṭī bhavedyam ājñāpayasitaṃ kariṣyāmīti |
kṣīreṇa pratikṛtiṃ siñcayet svastho bhavati |
oṃ cāmuṇḍe māṃsa śoṇita khādani māraṇi daha daha vidhvaṃsaya vidhvaṃsaya yogini kili kili cili ciṃli mili mili hana hana paca paca māraya māraya svāhā |
anena tīkṣṇatailāktānāṃ lavaṇāhutīnām aṣṭasahasraṃ juhuyāddināni sapta tatastṛtīyadināt prabhṛti pañcayojanābhyantare bhaginyo dāgatāpi tāva samāgacchanti bruvanti kimājñāyasīti |

85b] tato vaktavyaṃ na kiñcin mṛgayāmīti |
tatastā bruvanti yamicchasi taṃ dāsyāmīti |
hiraṇya ratna vastraṃ vā maithunaṃ vā dine dine dināni sapta tṛtīyadivasāt prabhṛti bhaginyo vruvantiṃ karmasiddhiṃ bhakṣabhojya lehya coṣya peyaṃ ca karoti yamicchasi taṃ karomīti |
kṣīrāhutiṃ juhyācchāntirbhavati |
atha na rocyate sarvabhaginīmaṇḍalabandhaṃ kṛtvā śiṣṭāpayeti |
oṃ cāmuṇḍe vajraghoṇeśvari tiṣṭha tiṣṭha bandha bandha paca paca mahāśaile svāhā |
anena manasā japtena maṇḍalabandho bhavati |
sarvāstāsthagalikāṃkaṃ karoti cchagalikāṃ vandhaḥ |
oṃ cāmuṇḍe ccheda me darśaya rūpaṃ paca paca culu culu svāhā |
ayaṃ cchagalikāmokṣaḥ |
oṃ cāmuṇḍe saṃsāravartani vartani mokṣaṇi mokṣaṇi cala cala ghu ghu vidhvaṃsaya vidhvaṃsaya bāhukapile svāhā |
nimbakāṣṭhena bāhumātraṃ śaṃkuṃ kṛtvāṣṭasahasraṃ japed yadarthaṃ cintayet karoti |
vāmapārśve daśaśataṃ japet tato mokṣaṃ bhavati |
atha na mocayed dhruvaṃ mriyate |
oṃ cāmuṇḍe atikramaya krāma yaṃ rūpaṃ vidyataṃ caṃcale gṛhṇa gṛhṇa cira svāhā |
anena yasya sūtrakaṃ śirasi bandhayed bhaginyo rātrau kabandhaṃ paśyati tasyaiva na prabhavanti |
oṃ cāmuṇḍe bhasmāṅgāravarṇa vetāli stambhaya mocaya cala cala bhave bhave svāhā |
anena bhaginyā dṛṣṭiṃ staṃbhayati manasā uccaritena sarvaṃ karoti |
bhaginyo vismitā bhavanti |
pradakṣiṇaṃ kurvanti manasā japena mokṣaḥ |
oṃ cāmuṇḍe vicce mahābala muñca svāhā |
120a g] anena manasā japtena mokṣaḥ | 
oṃ cāmuṇḍe ghātani dara dara mālido si 2 amukasya phaḍāmuka me phaṭgātre phaṭ hṛdaye phaṭ cakṣubhyaḥ phaṭ svāhā |
anena lavaṇamayīṃ pratikṛtiṃ kṛtvāgnisamidhairagniṃ prajvālyastīkṣṇaśāstreṇa *?tvā saptajaptena bāhuṃ staṃbhayati vacaṃ ca |
saptāhutiṃ juhuyād traikālyāṃ divārātraṃ yaṃ yadaṃgaṃ juhoti tasya śūlo bhavati |
atha niravaśeṣaṃ juhoti tadā mriyate cakṣurutpāṭya juhoti adho bhavati |
śiro juhoti yadicchati tatkarma prārabhate |
evaṃ puruṣābhimataṃ kuryād atha śāntimutpādayati |
āsraṃbhasī ye vai agniṃ prajvālya kṣīrāhutiśataṃ juhyācchāntirbhavati |
oṃ cāmuṇḍe kalu kalu dhvani virutā ya vidye svāhā anena manasā bhaginīṃ dṛṣṭvā japet tāścānyonyaṃ paśyati |
saṃgṛhṇāti vā yadā tu darśanaṃ dā tu kāmo manasā japet tato mokṣaḥ |
oṃ cāmuṇḍe darśaya rūpaṃ ca jāvine svāhā |
anena dṛśyanti bhaginyaḥ pradakṣiṇīkurvanti tāsāṃ darśanena pūjyo bhavati |
avadhyaśca yathā bhagavān maheśvaraḥ |
oṃ namonantaśikhegnai suravāsini gṛhṇa gṛhṇa māraya māraya vindhyavāsini mahāsavidhanulunulu mā ciraṃ svāhā |
anena vāmahastaṃ dakṣiṇhastena pīḍayet tato pībati |
oṃ namo viśvarūpāya tuṃ tu āvisa āvisa divyarūpamahāvale divyānudhyāte kurusaṃbhrama rūpaṃ cu cu ga ga gṛhṇa gṛhṇa vāmalocane svāhā |

86a] anena gardabha go uṣṭra hastyādīnāṃ romāṇi gṛhya ekaikaṃ sūtrakaṃ veṣṭayitvā kṛṣṇāṣṭamyāṃ kṛṣṇa caturdaśyāṃ mahādevasya dakṣiṇamūrtāvaṣṭaśatābhi mantritena sūtrekeṇa yasya haste bandhayed yathoktarūpī bhavati |
apanīte mokṣaḥ |
asyā vidyayāḥ kalpasādhanaṃ vyākhyāsyāmaḥ |
ekaliṃge kṛtvā kṛṣṇāṣṭamyāṃ caturdaśyāṃ vā balividhānaṃ kṛtvā aṣṭaśatiko japo bhavati |
sarveṣāṃ mantrāṇāṃ bhaginīnāṃ māṃsabhakṣaṇe |
120b g] ityāha bhagavān maheśvareṇoktaṃ bhaginīnāṃ bhrātarāṇāṃ ca |
oṃ namo yogeśvarāya sulahi sulahi anayā sarva bhūtānāṃ rakṣāḥ |
oṃ namo bhagavate īśānāya tiri ti ri tiṣṭha tiṣṭha svāhā |
anena mṛtakaṃ staṃbhayati |
dahyamāno na dahati |
anena srotasā mṛtako gacchatau lauṣṭhakenāhata pratisrotaṃ bhitvā gacchati |
nāmaṃ gṛhya japecchoṣajvaro bhavati |
yaṃ yaṃ kālaṃ cintayati tatsarvaṃ bhavati |
oṃ cāmuṇḍe bandha bandha daha daha keli keli svāhā |
anena pratikṛtiṃ kṛtvā bhasma sarvaye śrā?bhyaṅgaṃ dadāti tasyāṃ gacchedo bhavati kṣīrāhutibhiḥ |
athānyaṃ saṃpravakṣyāmi sehārīṇāṃ matottamam |
krīḍanaṃ sarvadevīnāṃ divyamantramataḥ śṛṇu || 17-3 ||
krīḍārthaṃ sādhakendrāṇāṃ rasadravyavidhānasam |
oṃ namo asurakumārikebhyo namo suragaṇā anayā mahāvidyayā vahukarma prasādhikayā |
asurendreṇa saṃdiṣṭā varāmi pṛthivīmimāṃtīkṣṇadaṃṣṭra ghorarūpā piṅgalā vikarālikā vitrāsayantī bhūtāni sthāvarāṇi ca tadyathā |
kaṭṭobhuṭṭe mahānaṭṭe saṃcāli vicāli hili hili hili kiriṭi kiriṭe madyamūle japa karāle candrakamūle yaṃ kāmamahamicchāmi tanme kāmaṃ samṛddhyaṃ tu svāhā |
upacāraḥ |
saptapurīṣāṃ śvānoṣṭragardabhamahiṣagṛddhamārjāramānuṣaśceti |
anena mantreṇābhimantrayet cili mili vigrahakhadirakīlakamabhimantritena saṃspṛśati tatsarvamapaharati |
oṃ namo vidura vidalecili kili kumārike tiṣṭha tiṣṭha svāhā |
mṛtakavastrasūtraṃ svahaste badhvā soṇḍikagṛhe kuṃbhaṃ saṃspṛśenmadyamayahareti tasya pūrṇaṃ darśayati |
oṃ namo kumārikecchilyecchili oṃ gomiti svāhā |
kṛtānaṃ saṃkrāmayati |
pūrṇakuṃbhamabhimantrya pāṃśunā vartitaṃ sarvamapaharaṃti |
anena kṣīṇyaṃ japitvā yaṃ paśyati tatsarvamayaharati |
oṃ namo kṛṣṇadaitye makule kule svāhā |
anena vastramabhimantrya tena saṃspṛśati tatsarvamapaharati |
oṃ namo devi namo vicce svāhā |
anena sarṣapāmagnau prakṣipet sakravāmapaharati |
rocanāmabhimantrya lalāṭe tilakaṃ kṛtvā saṃspṛśati tatsarvamapaharati |
oṃ dayine svāhā |
121a g] ekaviṃśati japtvā likhya modakaṃ kuryātpiṇḍamekaṃ puttalikāṃ kārayet tatsthā gandhapuṣpabaliṃ kṛtvā dāpayet parayā bhaktyā tena prāśayed valimupahṛtya nagnau muktaśikha utkuruṭake tu bhakṣayitavyastataḥ 

86b] kuṃbhānimantrayedanena mantreṇa dhānya gorasa upacāradravyāṇāṃ madhye yamicchati taṃ spṛśati japamānaḥ tatsarvamapaharati |
tataḥ phalamātraṃ dharitavyaḥ sarvamapaharati || 17-4 ||
oṃ namo dami divyaṃ daitye hana hana sarveṣāṃ cakṣurgrahaṇaṃ karomi māpakṣya tu kaścitpaśyaṃ tu pāpakaḥ svāhā |
anena kanyākartita sūtraṃ gṛhya sapta granthayaḥ kṛtvā ekaviṃśativārāṃ japtvā grīvāyāṃ badhvā japamānonirgacched yaṃ spṛśati tat sarvamapaharati |
oṃ namo vigate jayamate svāhā |
anena sthalimagnau sthāpya mantraṃ japamānastaṃḍulāṃ prakṣiped gacchati cakṣumabhimantrya sarvamapaharati |
oṃ namo bhagavate vitale kumbhakāriṇi kumbha me prayaccha svāhā |
anena catuṣpathe gatvā dayamāharedekaṃ dravyamapaharati |
oṃ namo devi uli hili hili devikali svāhā |
ayaṃ japamāno yaṃ cintayati tat sārvamapaharati |
oṃ namo ke kiṇḍini svāhā |
anena karpāsamapaharati |
oṃ namo gigini dhrumi liṃkhu khu svāhā |
anena pañcāṅgulyobhimantrya yaṃ spṛśati tatsarvamapaharati |
atha sarveṣāmekaviṃśatiko jāpya |
atha kulamantrasya kalpo bhavati |
ekaviṃśativārāṃ jāpya hastaṃ kṛtvā yaṃ spṛśati tatsarvaṃ saṃkrāmayati |
anena yaṃ spṛśate tatsarvamapaharati |
athānenaiva sarvadravyāṇāṃ selake ekaviṃśati granthayaḥ kṛtvā gṛhadvāre sthāpayet sarvasehanikāṃ rakṣā kṛtāṃ bhavati |

iti bhairavasrotasi mahātantre vidyāpīṭhe saptakoṭipramāṇe śrītantrasadbhāve mantrakośo nāma saptadaśaḥ paṭalaḥ || 17 ||


devyuvāca

yoginīlakṣaṇaṃ deva śākinīnāṃ ca krīḍanam |
seharikānāṃ ceṣṭā vai śrutaṃ caiva viśeṣataḥ || 18-1 ||
saṅgamaṃ ca yathā tāsāṃ viśeṣādavadhāritam |
chommakāṃ śrotumicchāmi bhāṣāṃ caiva viśeṣataḥ || 18-2 ||
chommakairjñātamātraistu tāsāṃ tu saṃmato bhavet |
siddhyanti sā?dhakasyaiva mudrācchommakatatparāḥ || 18-3 ||

121b g] bhairava uvāca

athātaḥ saṃpravakṣyāmicchommakānāṃ tu lakṣaṇam |
aṃgāvayavasaṃ sparśairmantrabhāṣāviceṣṭitaiḥ || 18-4 ||
vadanti mātaro yena sādhakaistāḥ parasparam |
tadvidhānaṃ śṛṇudhvaṃ hi aṃgācchommakalakṣaṇam || 18-5 ||
hastena spṛśate hastaṃ abhivādakṛtaṃ bhavet |
anāmādarśane caiva pratyabhivādanakṛtaṃ bhavati || 18-6 ||
hastena hasta vādayate bali bhoktuṃ samīhate |
lalāṭaṃ spṛśate yā tu kutrāgacchāmi bhāṣate || 18-7 ||
nābhiṃ spṛśate yā tu bubhukṣitāsmītibhāṣate |
stanaṃ spṛśate mātaramityuktaṃ bhavati || 18-8 ||
kūrparaṃ spṛśate yātu yāsyāmīti ca jalpate |
jihvālālayamānāṃ tu mānuṣyaṃ māṃsa bhakṣayāmīti ? || 18-9 ||
akṣispṛśati dṛśyena na nivartayāmīti |
pārśvaspṛśati * * * * * * * * * * * ? yathā? pādaṃ karomīti |

87a] pādena pādaṃ spṛśati ramāmītyuktaṃ bhavati || 18-11 ||
dantanikaṭakaṭāpayati āhāraṃ dehītyuktaṃ bhavati |
ubhe dantapaṃktī jihvāyā spṛśati tadā tṛptosmīntiti || 18-12 ||
pādāgraṃ spṛśati mriyate na gantavyamiti |
niṣṭayate nāstikarmamityuktaṃ bhavati || 18-13 ||
ubhau hastau sākhyāṃ darśayed yā tu utpalaṃ tasyāḥ pradarśayet |
haste puṭāṃ prakurute hastaṃ tasya prasārayet || 18-14 ||
hastaṃ kaṇḍūyate yā tu lalāṭaṃ tasya kaṇḍūyate |
stanaṃ kaṇḍūyamānāyā haste śrūṇi nipātayet || 18-15 ||
pādāṅguṣṭhena bhūmiṃ likhedaṅguṣṭhaṃ tasyā nipātayet |
pādāvāsphālayed yā tu sphijasyai vā sphālayet || 18-16 ||
tailābhyaktā paśyate yā muktiṃ tasyāḥ pradāpayet |
nāsikāgraṃ darśayed yā jihvāṃ tasya pradarśayet || 18-17 ||
lalāṭa darśayed yā tu śrotraṃ ? tasya pradāpayet |
prottuṃgetyamivādbhutaṃ? pratyekara oṣṭhajihvāghrāṇa || 18-18 ||
* * * *? * *?nāt svāgataṃ dvābhyāṃ susvāgatam |
koṣṭhaprativimbā cāṃguṣṭhaṃ kṣemamudrā vidhīyate ||19 ||
jihvā pradarśayed yā tu dattāṃ tasya pradarśayet |
śiro darśayated yā tu lalāṭaṃ tasya darśayet || 18-20 ||
keśā darśayate yā tu śikhāṃ tasyāstu darśayet |
cibukaṃ darśayed yā tu karṇaṃ tasya pradarśayet || 18-21 ||
grīvāṃ darśayated yā tu bāhuṃ tasya pradarśayet |
vāhuṃ darśayated yā tu karau tasyāpi darśayet || 18-22 ||
netraṃ darśayated yā tu nāsyāṃ tasyāpi darśayet |
stanau ca darśayed yā tu kukṣiṃ tasyāpi darśayet || 18-23 ||
122a g] kaṭiṃ ca darśayed yā tu guhyaṃ tasyāpi darśayet |
ūruṃ ca darśayed yā tu jānuṃ tasyāpi darśayet || 18-24 ||
jānu darśayate yā tu jaṃghā tasyāpi darśayet |
jaṃghā ca darśayed yā tu pādau tasyāpi darśayet || 18-25 ||
bhṛkuṭiṃ darśayed yā tu tarjayanti vināyakāḥ |
garuḍaṃ darśayed yā tuṃ śūlaṃ tasyāpi darśayet || 18-26 ||
narthepi vādanaṃ pratyānarthe pratyabhivādanam |
evamādi mahābhāge sāmānyacchommakā priye || 18-27 ||
akṣarārthena yā proktā sā mayā tu yaśasvini |
lakṣakoṭiśatairjaptaiḥ kāyakleśaparāyaṇaiḥ || 18-28 ||
ugreṇa tapasātyantaṃ kāle naiva tu mantriṇām |
darśayanti tathātmānaṃ dadante carumuttamam || 18-29 || 
kulābhiṣekasiktānāṃ kulabhaktiparāyaṇām |
melakaṃ ca prayacchanti sukhayogisamāgamam || 18-30 ||
teṣāṃ tu kathayiṣyāmi cchommakāḥ kulasaṃsthitā |
kule sāmānyatāṃ yāti vīrovāthabalāpi vā || 18-31 ||
nyāsaṃ kṛtvā tu sakalaṃ bhairavyā bhairaveṇa tu |
praviśeddhāmaraṣyā tu yadā tu dṛśyate striyaiḥ || 18-32 ||
śikhāṃ pradarśayed yā tu śirastasyāpi darśayet |
śiraḥ pradarśayed yā tu lalāṭaṃ tasya darśayet || 18-33 ||
śiro mālā darśayed yā vaktraṃ tasyāpi darśayet |
locane darśayed yā tu locane tasya darśayet || 18-34 ||
nāsikā darśayed yā tu arghīśaṃ tasya darśayet |
tṛtīyaṃ nayanaṃ darśed bhārabhūtesya darśayet || 18-35 ||
kapālaṃ darśayed yā tu mahāsenāsya darśayet |
kaṭhaṃ darśayate yā tu trimūrtiṃ tasya darśayet || 18-36 ||
daśanaṃ darśayed yā tu bhārabhūtesya darśayet |
jihvāṃ tu darśayed yā tu visartāṃ tasya darśayet || 18-37 ||
vācāṃ darśayate yā tu lakulīśosya darśayet |
aṅgulyo darśayed yā tu ajeśaścaturānane || 18-38 ||

87b] triśūlaṃ darśayed yā tu ekanetraṃ tu darśayet |
bāhuṃ ca darśayed yā tu cchagammahākāla darśayet || 18-39 || 
udaraṃ ca darśayed yā tu ekanetraṃ tu darśayet | 
hṛdayaṃ ca darśayed yā tu meṣaṃ tasyāpi darśayet || 18-40 ||
ātmā ca darśayed yā tu śvetaṃ tasyāpi darśayet |
prāṇaṃ tu darśayed yā tu bhṛguṃ tasyāpi darśayet || 18-41 ||
stanau ca darśayed yā tu bhujaṅgaṃ tasya darśayet |
paryāṃ ca darśayedyātu īśvaraṃ tasya darśayet || 18-42 ||
122b g] nitambaṃ darśayed yā tu dviraṇḍaṃ tasya darśayet |
nābhiṃ ca darśayed yā tu krodhīśaṃ tasya darśayet || 18-43 ||
guhyaṃ ca darśayed yā tu khaḍgīśaṃ tasya darśayet |
sūkṣmaṃ tu darśayed yā tu anantaṃ tasya darśayet || 18-44 ||
ūruṃ tu darśayed yā tu umākāntaṃ tu darśayet |
dakṣajaṃghāṃ darśayed yā tu raudrīṃ tasyāpi darśayet || 18-45 ||
vāmajaṃghā darśayed yā tasya jyeṣṭhā tu darśayet |
jānuṃ ca darśayed yā tu sadyojātaṃ pradarśayet || 18-46 ||
pādau ca darśayed yā tu diṇḍimo hi tu darśayet |
evamādi mahābhāge bhedaiḥ śatasahasraśaḥ || 18-47 ||
tadaṃśa sādhakendrasya kathayanti na saṃśayaḥ |
anyonyajñāpanārthāya veditavyaṃ tu mantriṇā || 18-48 ||
kadācid divyasaṃyogāt kṣetrapālādidarśanāt |
ādeśaṃ tu prayacchantu kartavyaṃ niścitena tu || 18-49 ||
śobhanāśobhanaṃ vāpi divyavākyaṃ na laṃghayet |
etallakṣaṇamākhyātamanekaistu prakārakaiḥ || 18-50 ||
jñātavyaṃ cumbakenaiva sarvasiddhyarthakāraṇāt |
sa śivaḥ paramo devo bhūtale saṃvyavasthitaḥ || 18-51 ||
tasya pādarajo mūrddhni bandhayet satataṃ budhaḥ |
rakṣanti devatā nityaṃ putravat pālayanti ca || 18-52 ||
chidraṃ tasya na kurvanti kurvante bāhyanugraham |
kṣetrādi lakṣaṇaṃ jñehi tathaiva vallabho bhavet || 18-53 ||
iti bhairavasrotasi mahātantre vidyāpīṭhe saptakoṭipramāṇe śrītantrasadbhāve cchommakādhikāro nāmāṣṭādaśaḥ paṭalaḥ || 18 ||


devyuvāca

chommakāstu mayā jñātā akṣarārthāstu kaulikāḥ |
na śrutaṃ śrotumicchāmi mandabuddhyālpamedhayā || 19-1 ||
kṣetropakṣetrasandohe devatāstu kathaṃ sthitā |
kṣetrapālavibhāgaṃ ca mantrārcana balikriyā || 19-2 ||
etatsarvaṃ samācakṣu niḥsaṃdigdhaṃ yathā bhavet |

bhairava uvāca

ataḥ paraṃ pravakṣyāmi kṣetrabhedaṃ sudurlabham || 19-3 ||
upakṣetraṃ varārohe saṃdohaṃ tu yathāsthitam |
prayāgā varuṇā kollā aṭṭahāsā jayantikā || 19-4 ||
caritrekāmrakaścaiva koṭivarṣaṃ tu cāṣṭamam |
aindrīdiśi sāmārabhya yāvadīśānagocaram || 19-5 ||
jñātavyaṃ kṣetravinyāsaṃ mantriṇāṃ tu yathākramam |
upakṣetraṃ pravakṣyāmi śṛṇuṣvekamanādhunā || 19-6 ||
parastīraṃ pṛṣṭhapuryaṃ kodbhuṇḍīśo pāraraiva ca |
123a g] kṣīrī māyāpuraṃ caiva tathā cāmrātikeśvaram || 19-7 ||
aṣṭamaṃ tu varārohe rājaveśma prakīrtitam |
ete vai saṃsthitā caiva upakṣetrāstu bāhyataḥ || 19-8 || 
sandohāśca pravakṣyāmi śrūyatāṃ varavarṇini |
virambā cai rūḍī? caiva melāhadapuraṃ puram || 19-9 || 
kasmīrāmarukeśaṃ ca nagaraṃ pauṇḍravarddhanam |
pūrvādi digvibhāgaṃ tu īśāntāyāvadeva tu || 19-10 ||
viraṃjādyā sthitā devi saṃdohāstu yathākramaṃ |
jñātavyā sādhakendreṇa kramād dikṣvaṣṭake sthitā || 19-11 || 

88a] kṣetropakṣetrasandohā yathā ye saṃvyavasthitāḥ |
nāmaṃ ca digvibhāgaṃ ca kathitaṃ tava suvrate || 19-12 ||

devyuvāca

kṣetrāṇyeva upakṣetrā saṃdohāśca tathaiva ca |
nāmaṃ ca digvibhāgaṃ ca saṃkhyā caiva pṛthak pṛthak || 19-13 ||
etatsarvaṃ mayā jñātaṃ tvatprasādānmaheśvara |
kā kasmiṃ devatāsthāne tāsāṃ nāmāṃ ca kīdṛśam || 19-14 ||
kathaṃ tāsāṃ bhavenmantraṃ marcatantu kathaṃ bhavet |
kiṃ nāma kṣetrapālānāṃ teṣāṃ nāmaṃ ca kīdṛśam || 19-15 ||
etat sarvaṃ yathānyāyaṃ mamācakṣva maheśvara |

bhairava uvāca

sādhu sādhu mahābhāge samya gṛṣṭaṃ tvayādhunā || 19-16 ||
yanna kasyacidākhyātaṃ rahasyamatidurlabham | 
idānīṃ saṃpravakṣyāmi śṛṇuṣvekamanā priye || 19-17 ||
prayoge vāyuvegā ca bhīmavaktrāṃśasaṃbhavā |
dhvajahastā sthitā devi sarvaduṣṭanivāraṇī || 19-18 ||
tasminkṣetre sthitā devi audumvarasamāśritaḥ |
pavano nāma vikhyātaḥ kṣetrapālo mahāyaśe || 19-19 ||
varāṇā śāṃkarī jñeyā mahocchuṣmāṅgasaṃbhavāḥ |
gadāyudhā sthitā devī balamaiśvaryadāyakāḥ || 19-20 ||
tāḍavṛkṣaṃ samāśritya tasminkṣetre vyavasthitaḥ |
ūrdhvakeśeti vikhyātaḥ kṣetrapālo valotkaṭāḥ || 19-21 ||
kollagiryā mahālakṣmīḥ karālā yonisaṃbhavām |
kālarūpā sthitā devī daṇḍahastā subhīṣaṇā || 19-22 ||
tasmiṃ kṣetre sthitā devi parvatāgrasamāśritā | 
agniketi ca vikhyātāḥ kṣetrapālo mahādhipe || 19-23 ||
aṭṭahāse sthitā devī raktādevyāḥ samudbhavāḥ |
saumyamukhyeti vikhyātā vajrāyudhavibhūṣitāḥ || 19-24 ||
123b g] tasminkṣetre sthitā devī kadambadrumasamāśritāḥ |
mahābaleti vikhyātaḥ kṣetrapālo mahāvalaḥ || 19-25 ||
jayantyā daṃturā devī jvālāmukheti viśrutāḥ | 
khaḍgahastā sthitā devī sarvasatvavaśaṃkarī || 19-26 ||
tasminkṣetre sthitā devī nimbavṛkṣasamāśritāḥ |
mahābaleti vikhyātaḥ kṣetrapālo mahotkaṭaḥ || 19-27 ||
caritre kṛṣṇatuṇḍā tu karālā yonisaṃbhavāḥ | 
śaktihastā sthitā devī sarvasiddhipradāyakāḥ || 19-28 ||
tasminkṣetre sthitā devī karaṃjadrusamāśritaḥ |
pāśahastā sthitā tatra sarvapāpapraṇāśanī || 19-30 ||
tasminkṣetre sthitā devī aśvatthavṛkṣamāśritaḥ |
kṣetrapālo mahākāyaḥ kailāso nāma nāmataḥ || 19-31 ||
karṇamoṭī tu koṭyākhye mahāvalakulodbhavāḥ |
śūlahastā sthitā devī sarvayogeśvareśvarī || 19-32 ||
tasminkṣetre sthitā devī vaṭavṛkṣasamāśritaḥ |
kṣetrapālo mahākāyo hetuko nāma nāmataḥ || 19-33 ||
athātaḥ saṃpravakṣyāmi upakṣetra śṛṇu priye |
parastīre prasannā ca raktāyā kulasaṃbhavāḥ || 19-34 ||
upakṣetre sthitā devī vajrāyudhasamanvitā |
trikaṭā saṃsthitastasminkṣetrapālo balotkaṭaḥ || 19-35 ||
pṛṣṭhāpure vidyumukhī karālā kulasaṃbhavāḥ |
upakṣetre sthitā devī daṇḍaśaktisamanvitāḥ || 19-36 ||
kṣetrapālo mahādevī vajradaṇḍeti viśrutāḥ |
kohuṇḍikā śivā jñeyā karālāṅgasamudbhavāḥ || 19-37 ||
upakṣetre sthitā devī mudrāsaṃkulabhūṣitāḥ |

88b] ulkāmukheti vikhyātaḥ kṣetrapālo mahāyaśe || 19-38 ||
vahnimukhīti vijñeyā sopāre danturodbhavā |
upakṣetre sthitā devī khaṭvāṅgakaraśobhitā || 19-39 ||
māṃsāśina sthitastasminkṣetrapālo balotkaṭāḥ |
kṣīrikelomajaṃghāśca caṇḍākṣīkulasaṃbhavā || 19-40 ||
upakṣetre sthitā devī khaḍgahastā mahābalā |
kṣetrapālo mahāmerustasminkṣetre vyavasthitaḥ || 19-41 ||
bhīmavaktrodbhavā bhīmā māyākhyā kampanīti ca |
vajrāṃkuśadharā devī upakṣetrasamāśritāḥ || 19-42 ||
124a g] bhīmo nāma sthitastasmin kṣetrapālo balotkaṭaḥ |
mahocchuṣmakule jātā pūtanāmrātikeśvare || 19-43 ||
upakṣetre sthitā devī bhīmā halagadāyudhā |
kṣetrapālo mahākrodhastasminkṣetre vyavasthitaḥ || 19-44 ||
rājagṛhe cipiṭanāsā mahābalakulodbhavā |
upakṣetre sthitā devī vajrāṃkuśasamanvitāḥ || 19-45 ||
kṣetrapālo mahākarṇastasminkṣetre vyavasthitaḥ |
upakṣetre yathārūpaṃ kathitaṃ tava suvrate || 19-46 ||
sandohānāṃ pravakṣyāmi śṛṇu devi trilocane |
virajāyāmbikā devī mudrā paṭṭisadhāriṇī || 19-47 ||
prathamāyāṃ viniryātā sandohe tu samāśritāḥ |
analaḥ kṣetrapālaśca tasmiṃ sandohasaṃsthitāḥ || 19-48 ||
erumāgnimukhī jñeyā karālākulasaṃbhavāḥ |
vajraśaktidharī devī sandohe tu samudbhavāḥ || 19-49 ||
ghaṇṭāraveti vikhyātaḥ kṣetrapālo balotkaṭaḥ |
tasminkṣetre sthitā devī bhīma bhīṣaṇalocane || 19-50 ||
hatapure piṅgalayā karālākulasaṃbhavāḥ |
muśalahastodharā devī kṣetrapālāgnijihvakaḥ || 19-51 ||
danturā yonisaṃbhūtā elāpure kharānanā |
paraśuhastā mahatā sandohe saṃvyavasthitāḥ || 19-52 ||
gajakarṇeti vikhyātaḥ kṣetrapālo balotkaṭaḥ |
kasmaryāṃ caiva gokarṇī caṇḍākṣāyonisaṃbhavāḥ || 19-53 ||
mudrā śṛṃkhalasaṃyuktā nandī jayaśca kṣetrapaḥ |
kṣetrapālasthito devī nandī jayaśca viśrutaḥ || 19-54 ||
marukeśe cakramaṇī bhīmavaktrākulodbhavā |
mudrāṃkuśasamāyuktā karālakṣetrapānvitā || 19-55 ||
mahocchuṣmāṅgasaṃbhūtā nagare cetanā smṛtā |
halāyudhasamāyuktā sandohe caiva saṃbhavāḥ || 19-56 ||
romajaṃgheti vikhyātaḥ kṣetrapālo mahotkaṭā |
carmamuṇḍeti vikhyātā devatā puṇḍravarddhane || 19-57 ||
mahābalakulotpannā khaṭvāṅgakaraśobhitāḥ |
kumbhakarṇa mahādevi kṣetrapālo vyavasthitaḥ || 19-58 ||
ataḥ paraṃ pravakṣyāmi śṛṇuṣvekamanā priye |
kṣetre caiva upakṣetre sandohe ca tathaiva ca || 19-59 ||
yā sthitā devatā tatra tāsāṃ mantraṃ vadāmyaham |
oṃ hrī/ hū/ sphohehaḥ proktaṃ svāhā padavibhūṣitam || 19-60 ||
namaskārāntasaṃyuktaṃ mantraṃ devyāḥ prakīrtitam |
anenaiva tu mantreṇa kṣetrāṇāmarcanaṃ smṛtam || 19-61 ||
anenaiva baliṃ dadyādāliṃ cānena dāpayet |
upakṣetra saṃdohānisvasthāne caiva pūjayet || 19-62 ||
124b g] ataḥ paraṃ pravakṣyāmi kṣetrapālāṃ mahādhipe |
svānāmamāditaḥ kṛtvā bhairavaṃ rūpasaṃyutam || 19-63 ||
vighnāṃ nāśaya ityeti valiṃ gṛhṇeti saṃyutam |
pālākṣara * * * * * * * * * * * * ? || 19-64 ||
* * * * * * * * * * * * * * * * ? |

89a] baliṃ cāliṃ ca dātavyaṃ devi teṣāṃ pṛthak pṛthak || 19-65 ||
kṣetrabhedaṃ mayā sarvaṃ kathitaṃ tava suvrate |
athānyaṃ saṃpravakṣyāmi sāmānyaṃ sarvataḥ sthitam || 19-66 ||
mantraṃ vai kṣetrapālānāṃ lakṣaṇena vilakṣitam |
grāme vā nagare vātha pure vā catvarepi vā || 19-67 ||
śmaśāne caiva udyāne gṛhe vā patanethavā |
yatra tatrāsthito mantrī dhyānārcana samārabhet || 19-68 ||
baliṃ caiva viśeṣeṇa mantreṇānena dāpayet |
nīlajīmūtasaṃkāśaṃ ghoradaṃṣṭraṃ bhayāvaham || 19-69 ||
ūrddhakeśaṃ ca raktākṣaṃ kṣetrapālaṃ bṛhodaram |
ehi ehi kuru kuru suru suru muru muru bhañja bhañja hana hana vikṛtabhairavarūpa svabaliṃ gṛhṇa gṛhṇa | 
tārādimantramuddiṣṭaṃ namaskārāntayojitam || 19-70 ||
durlabhaṃ triṣu lokeṣu kṣetrapālasya tarpaṇam |
anena rahito mantrī kṣetraṃ paryaṭate yadi || 19-71 ||
tasya vighnaiḥ sahasrāṇi bhavantī hana saṃśayaḥ |
vighnaiścopahato mantrī na siddhiphalabhāgbhavet || 19-72 ||
tasmātsarvaprayatnena jñātavyaṃ sādhakena tu |

devyuvāca

sthanalakṣaṇamākhyāhi mantriṇāṃ siddhimicchatām || 19-73 ||
nirvighnaṃ bhavate yena sādhakaṃ tu mahātmanām |

bhairava uvāca

śṛṇu devi pravakṣyāmi sādhakānāṃ hitāya vai || 19-74 ||
sthānalakṣaṇapūrvaṃ tu kṣetrapālavidhistathā |
pūrveṇa salilaṃ yatra hradaṃ svasthāmbupūritam || 19-75 ||
tatsthāna māsrayetmantrī sādhakaḥ siddhimicchatā |
dakṣiṇena yathā toyaṃ hrado vāpi nadīthavā || 19-76 ||
yamādheyo varārohe kṣetrapālaḥ prakīrtitaḥ |
uttare saṃsthitaṃ toyaṃ vāpī vā hradameva vā || 19-77 ||
yakṣendrodhipatistatra nirdiṣṭaṃ kāmahetunā |
paścimena hradaṃ divyaṃ kusumotpalaśobhitam || 19-78 ||
varuṇodhipatistatra siddhistatra na dūrataḥ |
hradaṃ tu vimalaṃ devi sthitaṃ vā vāyumaṇḍale || 19-79 ||
vāyoradhipatistatra niyuktau rakṣakarmaṇi |
nai-ṛtyāṃ tu hrado yatra padmotpalasuśobhitam || 19-80 ||
rakṣendrādhipatistatra sanniyukto mamājñayā |
āgneyāṃ tu hradaṃ yatra kumudotpalaśobhitam || 19-81 ||
125a g] agnirājādhipastatra tatra siddhirna dūrataḥ |
aiśābhyāṃ kamale sthānaṃ hradaṃ puṇyavahūdakam || 19-82 ||
aiśānādhipatistatra niyukto rakṣakarmaṇi |
nadī pūrvavahā yatra āgneyāṃ diśi māgatā || 19-83 ||
cakrapāṇisthitaḥ kṣetre kṣetrapālastu saṃsmṛtaḥ |
āgneyāmāgataṃ toyaṃ dakṣiṇe nairṛte gatam || 19-84 ||
brahmaṇodhipatistatra niyuktaḥ śaṃkareṇa tu |
nairṛtyāmāgataṃ toyaṃ vāyavyāṃ paścime gatām || 19-85 ||
rakṣendrādhipatistatra sthito devi samājñayā |
vāyavyā māgataṃ toyaṃ īśānyāṃ gatamuttare || 19-86 ||
piśācādhipatistatra niyuktaḥ śūlapāṇinā |
aiśānyāṃ salilaṃ devi vāyavye tu yadā gatam || 19-87 ||
tasminyakṣādhipaḥ proktaḥ kṣetrasiddhiḥ surakṣakaḥ |
vāyavyāṃ salilaṃ devi nairṛtyāṃ paścime gatam || 19-88 ||
gandharvādhipatistatra kṣetrapāla iti smṛtaḥ |
nai-ṛtyāmāgataṃ toyaṃ āgneyāṃ dakṣiṇe gatam || 19-89 ||
kapālādhipatistatra kṣetrapālaḥ prakīrtitaḥ |
āgneyāmāgataṃ toyaṃ pūrveṇa kusumākulam || 19-90 ||
bhīmamadhipatistatra niyuktaḥ kṣetrakarmaṇi |

89b] pūrveṇaivāgataṃ toyaṃ paścimena yadā gatam || 19-91 ||
kabandhastu sthito devi tatsthānasthāṃ trinandanam | 
dakṣiṇena nadī ramyā punaruttaratāṃ gatāḥ || 19-92 ||
kumārakṣetrapālastu niyuktaḥ parameśvari |
uttareṇa nadī ramyā dakṣiṇenāpi bhāmini || 19-93 ||
surūpaḥ saṃsthitastatra kathitaṃ tava suvrate |
paścimenāgataṃ toyaṃ pūrveṇa tu manoharam || 19-94 ||
karālaḥ saṃsthito devi kṣetreśaḥ surasundari |
aiśānyāṃ ca viniṣkrāntaṃ nai-ṛtyāṃ tu yadā gatam || 19-95 ||
sugrīvodhipatistatra niyuktaḥ śūlapāṇinā |
āgneyāṃ salilaṃ devi vāyavyāṃ gamasaṃsthitam || 19-96 ||
subhadraḥ saṃsthitastatra kṣetrapālo mahābalaḥ |
nai-ṛtyāmāgataṃ toyamīśānyāyāṃ gataṃ yadā || 19-97 ||
vijayastu varārohe niyuktastu karālinā |
pūrveṇaivāgamaṃ toyaṃ dakṣiṇena yadā gatam || 19-98 ||
ambukaḥ saṃsthitastatra kṣetrapālo mahābalaḥ |
dakṣiṇena nadī ramyā paścimena pravāhiṇī || 19-99 ||
surarāṭ saṃsthitastasminmahāvighnakaro nṛṇām |
paścimena viniṣkrāntaṃ uttareṇa punāgatam || 19-100 ||
125b g] anantaḥ saṃsthitastatra mahātejodhipastathā |
uttareṇa varārohe pūrveṇa salilāgatam || 19-101 ||
ketumālā sthitastatra sādhakānāṃ bhayāvaham |
pūrveṇa vimalānandā uttareṇa punargatā || 19-102 ||
dhūmāgno?dhi patistatra mama rakṣo mahābalaḥ |
paścimābhimukhā naṃdyā uttareṇa vinirgatā || 19-103 ||
suramyaṃ vipulaṃ toyaṃ sugandhaṃ yatra saṃsthitam |
hā hā halasthito devī sādhakānāṃ sunandakaḥ || 19-104 ||
dakṣiṇenāgataṃ toyaṃ pūrveṇa pravahe sthitam |
bhairavo bhairavaṃ sthānaṃ niyuktastu kapālinā || 19-105 ||
saṅgamaṃ pūrvato yatra vāmadakṣiṇaśobhitam |
ghaṇṭāravasthito devi sādhakānāṃ hitāya vai || 19-106 ||
āgneyāṃ diśi devasya nadīnāṃ saṅgamaṃ śubham |
tryakṣarudrasthito devi sādhake siddhidāyakaḥ || 19-107 ||
dakṣiṇe digvibhāge tu saṅgamaṃ yatra śobhane |
bhīmanādasthito devi mantriṇāṃ ca varapradaḥ || 19-108 ||
nai-ṛtyāṃ tu yadā devi saṅgamaṃ vipulodakam |
sutejo valinastatra avaddhyastridaśairapi || 19-109 ||
paścimena sthitaṃ divyaṃ saṅgamaṃ vāma dakṣiṇe |
grahendrādhipatistatra namaskṛtvā parityajet || 19-110 ||
vāyavyāṃ vipulāṃ divyāṃ saṃgamamaparājitam |
saurākṣādhipatistatra susiddhaprakaro mahān || 19-111 ||
uttareṇa yadā tasya saṅgamaṃ śubhalakṣaṇam |
bhagāsthi tatra tiṣṭhetamantriṇāṃ siddhimicchatām || 19-112 ||
aiśānyāṃ saṃgamaṃ divyaṃ anekadrumasaṃkulam |
sādhakasya drutā siddhirdadāti bhīmapūjanāt || 19-113 ||
saṅgamaṃ yatra tatrasthaṃ sthitaṃ paryaṅkamaṇḍalām |
tatsthānaṃ tarjayenmantrī nīlīgrahavyavasthitam || 19-114 ||
parvataṃ śikharaṃ divyaṃ dakṣiṇena yadā bhavet |
sākṣādaṣṭabhujo devi dadaṃ saukhyaṃ tu sundari || 19-115 ||
nai-ṛtyāṃ śikharaṃ divyaṃ nātidūraṃ śilātalam |
bhāskaraḥ saṃsthitastatra adhipatyā na pālakaḥ || 19-116 ||
paścime saṃsthitaṃ divyaṃ anekadrumasaṃkulam |
yatvātamāśrayed devi avadhyastridaśairapi || 19-117 ||
vāyavye parvataṃ divyaṃ vartulaṃ śikharākulam |
kṣetrapālaḥ kamalākṣa sukhasiddhiphalapradaḥ || 19-118 ||
uttare parvataṃ divyaṃ nirjharai rūpaśobhitam |
kṣetrapālo hayagrīva sadyomṛtyukaraḥ smṛtaḥ || 19-119 ||

90a] aiśānyāṃ parvataṃ divyaṃ oṣadhībhirnirantaram |
maṇibhadrasthitasta ca kṣetrapālo mahābalaḥ || 19-120 ||
dadate sādhake siddhiṃ mokṣaṃ caiva na saṃśayaḥ |
pūrveṇa parvataṃ divyaṃ campakāśokamāvṛtam || 19-121 ||
bhṛkuṭīmukho mahāraudra kṣetrapālo vyavasthitaḥ |
126a g] āgneyāṃ parvataṃ divyaṃ sughanaistarubhirvṛtam || 19-122 ||
bahupuṣpasamākīrṇaṃ nirjharairupaśobhitam |
tatrāstegnimukho nāma kṣetrapālaḥ prakīrtitaḥ || 19-123 ||
atha mantraṃ pravakṣyāmi sāmānyaṃ sarvakṣetrayām |
aliṃ phalguṃ mahādevi baliṃ ye naiva dāpayet || 19-124 ||
oṃ namo bhairavāya vikṛtarūpāya namaścaṇḍāya hūṃ phaṭ svāhā |
oṃ amṛta bhairavāya svāhā |
oṃ sarvānnamamṛtaṃ kuru svāhā |
anena mantreṇa baliṃ dadyāt sarvakṣetrapālānāṃ parama hṛdayaṃ vidhimantraḥ |
oṃ namo lohaka gṛhṇa 2 vikṛtarūpāṃ hana 2 daha 2 paca 2 vidrāvaya 2 grasa 2 niha 2 naha 2 trailokyasatrāsana kara haraṇe ha prasaha 2 nṛtya 2 grāhaya 2 mohaya 2 prapīḍaya 2 hū/ 3 phaṭ svāhā |
sarvakṣetrapālānāṃ sannidhā na kara mantraḥ |
oṃ ruru devi koṭyo ya sugravo nokṛṣṇarudramaḥ || 19-125 ||
ujjainyām agnikaṃ caiva aṭṭahāse vṛkodaram |
jālandhare bhavetkṛṣṇa kulūtāyāṃ prabhākaraḥ || 19-126 ||
turuhuru * * karṇaṃ devyā dvāre tu bhairavam |
aṣṭau te yogapīṭhānāṃ kṣetrapālā prakīrtitāḥ || 19-127 ||
yastu dhārayate nityaṃ śṛṇu pāṭhāvi nityaśaḥ |
mucyate sarvapāpebhyo labhate bhairavaṃ padam || 19-128 ||
kathitāḥ seśvarāḥ kṣetre mantrārcanavidhiryutāḥ |
kṣetrāṇāmabhidhānaṃ ca vanabhedena kathyate || 19-129 ||
vaṭā vṛkṣā janāśokā aśvatthodumbarastathā |
kadambaṃ sinduvāraśca śrīvṛkṣapanasākulam || 19-130 ||
sarjārjunaṃ mahāramyaṃ rudrākṣamadhukastathā |
ambukaḥ kumbhavṛkṣaśca śrīparṇa bilvatā kulam || 19-131 ||
etaivṛkṣairvarairdevi samantāt sthāna maṇḍalam |
tat sthānaṃ vijayaṃ nāma drutaṃ siddhipradāyakam || 19-132 ||
pūrveṇa vaṭavṛkṣaṃ tu aśvatthaṃ dakṣiṇe sthitam |
taddevi vijayaṃ nāma jayaṃ caiva vinirdiśet || 19-133 ||
plakṣo dakṣiṇabhāge tu vaṭaḥ paścimataḥ sthitaḥ |
drutaṃ siddhikaraṃ mantrī vijayaṃ tatsuśobhitam || 19-134 ||
aśvatthaṃ dakṣiṇe bhāge plakṣamuttarataḥ sthitam |
sugrīvaṃ tu varārohe sādhakasya praśasyate || 19-135 ||
uttare vaṭavṛkṣaṃ tu pūrvatodumbarastathā |
subhadraṃ nāma taddevi jayasiddhikaraṃ smṛtam || 19-136 ||
pūrve tu dakṣiṇe caiva vaṭavṛkṣaṃ varānane |
126b g] vyādhiroga bhayaṃ tatra na ca siddhipradāyakam || 19-137 ||
yāmyāyāṃ vaṭavṛkṣaṃ tu bahupādapamaṇḍitam |
mārakaṃ tatsamuddiṣṭaṃ sādhakastadvivarjayet || 19-138 ||
nai-ṛtye tu diśābhāge aśvatthodumbarastathā |
tatsthānaṃ tu parityajya durbhagaḥ siddhi dūrataḥ || 19-139 ||
etānyapi parityajyā aiśānyāṃ yadi kāñcanam |
āgneyāṃ nāri nāmaṃ tu nai-ṛtye gauritistathā || 19-140 ||
vāyavyāṃ tu tathā proktaṃ nāmaṃ tatra japāntakam |
jātīvanaṃ yadā pūrve sughanaṃ kāñcanaiḥ saha || 19-141 ||
tatsthānaṃ bhadrakaṃ nāma yatnena sādhako bhavet |
kadalīvanasaṃchantaṃ kadambavana paścime || 19-142 ||
saugandhaṃ nāma vikhyātaṃ sādhakasyābhivṛddhaye |

90b] jātīvanaṃ sajambūbhiḥ sthitamuttarakāñcanaiḥ || 19-143 ||
rājadantaḥ savijñeyaḥ kadalīvanasaṃkulam |
tathā katakajamvvāmrairhemacampakaśobhitam || 19-144 ||
dakṣiṇe vinataṃ nāma drutaṃ siddhirna dūrataḥ |
aśokā panasapunnāgaiḥ kadambadrumasaṃkulam ||145 ||
paścimena varārohe tadbhīmaṃ nāma viśrutam |
kadalīvanasaṃcchannaṃ mambupunnāgasaṃkulam || 19-146 ||
siddhārthakaṃ samākhyātaṃ ṣaṇmāsā siddhidāyakam |
ketakībakulairyuktaṃ aiśānyāṃ diśi saṃsthitam? || 19-147 ||
kadambavanakharjūrairāgneyāṃ diśi cottatam |
vanaṃ tanmanṇḍalaṃ nāma kṣetraṃ taṃ vanaśobhitam || 19-148 ||
vane kadamba tālaṃ ca mayukhacampakānvitam |
samantād raviṇaṃ devi prasannaṃ nāma viśrutam || 19-149 ||
saralaṃ tālavahulaṃ jātīkhaṇḍaṃ sakāñcanam |
śobhitaṃ nai-ṛte bhāge gaganaṃ nāma viśrutam || 19-150 ||
jātīcampakapracuraṃ ambujambusamanvitam |
vṛkṣarājāsamāyuktaṃ aśokadrumasaṃkulam || 19-151 ||
surūpaṃ nāma vikhyātaṃ devadevena sundari |
vibhāgaṃ tu varārohe raktākṣamavaśobhitam || 19-152 ||
punnāgabakulairyuktaṃ siddhasāraṅgaśobhitam |
suvanaṃ nāma vikhyātaṃ ratiprītivivarddhanam || 19-153 ||
pūrveṇa dakṣiṇenaiva sughanaṃ puṣpaśobhitam |
latāgulmaparicchatraṃ surāmaṃ nāma kīrtitam || 19-154 ||
sarjārjunaṃ vanaṃ pūrve kadambaṃ caiva dakṣiṇe |
tatsthānaṃ nandanaṃ proktaṃ ketakī va kulaiḥ saha || 19-155 ||
niśāntakavanaṃ divyaṃ aśvatthaṃ kāñcanaiḥ saha |
vāruṇe vinitā devi bhagastiṃ nāma viśrutam || 19-156 ||
supānaṃ ghanasañcāraṃ nāraṅgavanasaṃkulam |
madhuvṛkṣavanaṃ devi samantāduttare sthitam || 19-157 ||
127a g] viṣkambhaṃ nāma vikhyātaṃ vanaṃ gulmalatākulam |
trigarta ca samākhyātaṃ sakahlāravanākulam || 19-158 ||
sādhakasya varārohe tasmiṃ siddhirna dūrataḥ |
vaṃsaṃ kadamcakaṃ cāmraṃ samantāt sthānamaṇḍale || 19-159 ||
āgneyī diśi dṛśyeta suramyaṃ nāmataṃ viduḥ |
ete vai kathitāḥ kṣetrā vanyādinadi? saṃgamaiḥ || 19-160 ||
śobhanāśobhanāścaiva vidhilakṣaṇalakṣitā | 
eṣāmekatamaṃ gṛhya athavā sarvaśobhanam || 19-161 ||
sarvalakṣaṇasaṃpūrṇaṃ sthāvarādyupaśobhitam |
tasminsiddhi varārohe acirātprāpnuvanti hi || 19-162 ||
nocetsiddhirbhavet tasya yantrādīṃ vātha sādhayet | 

iti bhairavasrotasi mahātantre vidyāpīṭhe saptakoṭipramāṇe śrītantrasadbhāve kṣetropakṣetrārcanaikonaviṃśati paṭalaḥ || 19 ||


devyuvāca

kṣetropakṣetrasandohamarcanaṃ ca balikriyā |
sthānalakṣaṇamantraśca kṣetrapālavidhistathā || 20-1 ||
jñātaṃ deva mayā sarvaṃ tvatpradātsureśvarā |
sāṃprataṃ yantramālāyā grathanaṃ kathayasva mām || 20-2 ||
vaśyādīni ca karmāṇi yathā tānyeva bhairava |
tathāhaṃ śrotumicchāmi kathayasva sureśvara || 20-3 || 

bhairava uvāca

śṛṇu devi pravakṣyāmi vaśyādīnāṃ ca sādhanam |
māraṇādīni karmāṇi uccāṭaṃ ca viśeṣataḥ || 20-4 ||
stambhanaṃ mohanaṃ devi āpyāyanavidhiḥ śubhaḥ |
andhī vadhira mūkāśca bhagavandhanameva ca || 20-5 ||
ākarṣaṇaṃ tu deveśi rakṣā caiva viśeṣataḥ |
kathayāmi na sandehastvatprītyā suranāyike || 20-6 ||
hṛdayaṃ bhairavaṃ devi ādikṣānte niyojayet |
sampaṭasthaṃ tato nāma dātavyaṃ tu varānane || 20-7 ||

91a] gairikābhaṃ mahādīptaṃ jjvalitaṃ kuliśarūpiṇam |
dhyāyamānaṃ japeddevaṃ sthāpayedvahninā adhaḥ || 20-8 ||
vaśamānayate kṣipraṃ mantrarājena mohitaḥ |
punarevaṃ tataḥ piṇḍaṃ vāyunā dīpitaṃ adhaḥ || 20-9 || 
ādikṣānte tato yojyā dhūmravarṇaṃ vicintayet |
nīyantaṃ vāyave bhāge daṃṣṭrārūḍhaṃ tu sādhakaḥ || 20-10 ||
dhvaje vā mṛgacarmevā kākapakṣaistu homanāt |
tuṣakaṇṭakasaṃmiśrairuccāṭaṃ kurute bhṛśaṃ || 20-11 ||
rakārākrāntagaṃ piṇḍaṃ nirvāhaṃ māyayā saha |
127b g] saṃpuṭasthaṃ tato nāmaṃ adhorddhvaṃ caiva dīpitam || 20-12 ||
dahyamānaṃ tato dhyāyejjvālāmālākulaṃ priye |
dāghamutpādayatyāśu sādhyasya suranāyike || 20-13 |
lakārākṣaramārūḍhaṃ piṇḍaṃ mātṛkayā saha |
vidadheta tato devi haridrāyāstu karpaṭe || 20-14 ||
śilāyāmatha gocarma indracāpasamaprabham |
stambhayennātra sandeha indramaṇḍalamadhyagaḥ || 20-15 ||
piṇḍakaṇḍe samārūḍhaṃ mātṛkākṣarayojitam |
saṃpuṭasthaṃ tato nāma madhorddhvaṃ kaṇḍepū kṛtam || 20-16 ||
bhūrjapatrelikheddevaṃ dūrvākāṇḍaiḥ śamīdalaiḥ |
trimadhvāktaistilairhomau vauṣaḍante niyojayet || 20-17 ||
sra?vantamamṛtaṃ dhyāyet plāvyamāna samaṃtataḥ | 
indrasaṃpuṭamadhyasthaṃ ravīndrapuṭamadhyagam || 20-18 ||
āpyāyane prayogo yaṃ amṛtārthe varānane |
makārākṣaramārūḍhaṃ piṇḍaṃ vai dahanātmakam || 20-19 ||
mātṛkākṣaragaṃ sādhyaṃ likhet tatpuṭamadhyagam |
kālarūpaṃ tato dhyāyenmārayejjagadeva tu || 20-20 ||
śmaśānasthaṃ tato homaṃ viṣaraktāsthikhaṇḍakaiḥ |
mārayeta na sandeho ripu brahmasamo priyaḥ || 20-21 ||
meṣaśṛṅgāsanasthaṃ tu piṇḍa mātṛkayā saha |
mūkatā vadhirāndhatvaṃ bhagabandhatvameva ca || 20-22 ||
karoti nātra sandehastu hī kṣīre tuṣānvitam |
homaḥ śmaśānavihito arddharātreṇa saṃśayaḥ || 20-23 ||
piṇḍaṃ nakārasaṃyuktaṃ vidyāyāṃ tu parā priye |
sainyamohaṃ karotyāśu jvalatkuliśatejasām || 20-24 ||
piṇḍaṃ saṃpuṭamadhyasthaṃ rakārākrāntayojitam |
ākarṣayenna sandeho aṃkuśākārabhāvanā || 20-25 ||
adhorddhvaṃ dīpitaṃ piṇḍaṃ likhedvarṇakulāraṇi |
tatra saṃpuṭamadhyasthaṃ devadattaṃ likhetpriye || 20-26 ||
dahyamānaṃ tato dhyāyed bhakṣamāṇāṃ śivādibhiḥ |
vṛttaṃ tu śākinī vṛndairhastapādanikhādanām || 20-27 ||
mārayeta na sandeho viṣāsthikhaṇḍahomanāt |
piṇḍaṃ thakāra? saṃyuktāṃ vākstambhe yojayet sadā || 20-28 ||
ṣaṇḍhī?karaṇa mūkatvaṃ bhagabandhanameva ca |
tāntrasthaṃ? tu karotyāśu yathā bhairavamatravīt || 20-29 ||
śākinī hṛdayenaiva kṣipramevaṃ tu māraṇam |
śākinī hṛdayaṃ likhyamātṛkākṣaragaṃ priye || 20-30 ||
128a g] saṃpuṭasthaṃ tato nāmaṃ hū/ phaḍantena bhāmini |
mārayet tad virabhyāsāt pṛthivīsamapi saṃharet || 20-31 ||
parāparagataṃ sādhyaṃ śākinīmarmasaṃyutam |
māraṇaṃ sarvasatvānāṃ kurute nātra saṃśayaḥ || 20-32 ||
kruddho jhaṇṭiti nistriṃśo anivartakaroṣadhṛt |
vidhūtathātmanaḥ keṣāṃ kopārtoraktalocanaḥ || 20-33 ||
tadā devi vidhiḥ śreṣṭhamācarenmantravit sadā |
anyathā tu viromaṃ syādbhavate vīranāyike || 20-34 ||
astavyastakṛtāṃ vidyā parāparamahābalāḥ |

91b] śākinīmarmasaṃyuktā hū/ phaṭkāraniyojitāḥ || 20-35 ||
phaṭ hū/ ca phaṭāstastho vilomāt sādhyamālikhet |
gocarme tu varārohe yāvat saṃjñāntikaṃ priye || 20-36 ||
pretavaktre nidhāpyeta āruhyorasi? sādhakaḥ |
nagno muktaśikho bhūtvā śmaśānāṅgāradhūlitaḥ || 20-37 ||
bhūmitaḥ śavamālaistu ākruddhoṃ raktalocanaḥ |
śaṃkhākṣamālayā jāpī mahāsavakriyārataḥ || 20-38 ||
trikoṇamarddhacandraṃ vā kārayecchava agrataḥ |
tuṇḍaṃ tu mekhalāhīnaṃ viṣamātrānvitaṃ priye || 20-39 ||
dhvāṃkṣasyaṃ kārayeddhīmā kharasthaṃ vātha kukkuram |
asṛkpūrṇakapālaṃ tu arghapātraṃ tu kalpayet || 20-40 ||
śmaśānasyendhanairdevi tadutthamanalaṃ priye |
saṃvukṣya ca tato homo viṣāktairasthikhaṇḍakaiḥ || 20-41 ||
tuṣaiḥ kaṇṭakasaṃmiśraivilomānmantramuccaret |
hu/ phaḍantaṃ tatoccārya mārayetad dvibhiryutam || 20-42 ||
śīghraśabdaṃ tatoccārya grahadvayamato tataḥ |
amukaṃ tu tato yojyā āhutī tu pradāpayet || 20-43 ||
akṣamāṇaṃ tato dhyāyecchākinīvṛndamadhyagam |
haviṣyāntaṃ tato devyaisthitvā kartṛkayā ume || 20-44 ||
khaṇḍakhaṇḍairanekaiśca vibhaktaṃ tu śarīrakam |
praharārddhena deveśi yāti sādhyo yamālayam || 20-45 ||
anivartakametaṃ tu na kuryānmantravit kvacit |
nṛpe caivaṃ prakāriṇyo devāgnigurunāśake || 20-46 ||
tasminneva vidhiḥ kṛtsnaḥ kartavya sādhakena tu |
ekaliṅgetha kartavyaṃ prayogamidamuttamam || 20-47 ||
viṣastu hīrādhikayā unmattakasalāṅgalī |
raktaṃ mānuṣa māhiṣyaṃ kharasyāśvavṛkasya ca || 20-48 ||
śaśakasyāpi deveśi likhelliṅgasya mūrddhani |
yathāpūrvaṃ tathaivātra kartavyamaviśaṃkitaḥ || 20-49 ||
śavamādāya bhūtākhye rātrau muktakacaḥ sudhīḥ |
128b g] tadutthai kārayeddevi rajju caiva śavodbhavaiḥ || 20-50 ||
kadalaiḥ kārayetpūjyaṃ nirmālyaistu anekaśaiḥ |
dhūpaṃ phalguṣasaṃmiśraṃ asthikhaṇḍaiḥ samanvitam || 20-51 ||
dāpayeddevadevasya tato mantraṃ samuccaret |
rajjunāveṣṭayelliṅga trivārā gatacetasaḥ || 20-52 ||
pādaṃ tu mastake datvā dvitīyaṃ tu śavopari |
hāhākāraṃ tataḥ kṛtvā mantramuccārayet priye || 20-53 ||
vilome pūrvavaddevi mathanaṃ tu samārabhet |
ghaṭikārddhaṃ yāvaddevatāvacchatrornikṛntanam || 20-54 ||
bhavantīha na sandehaḥ satyaṃ satyaṃ na saṃśayaḥ |
pratyūṣe vimale gatvā kṣīrasvānaṃ tu kārayet || 20-55 ||
anyaliṅgaṃ pratiṣṭhāpya abhiṣekaṃ tatonmanaḥ |
kārayeta varārohe yathāśāstra pracoditaḥ || 20-56 ||
grāmasya dvāramāśritya sthitvā caiva suyantritaḥ | 
tatrayā udakā hṛtya prakṣipanti ghaṭāghaṭam || 20-57 ||
mastake sādhakasyaiva taṃ snānaṃ yogamaṇḍale | 
sahasraghaṭamevātra yāvadbhavati śobhane || 20-58 || 
snātavyaṃ tāvadevaṃ tu yathā śāstre pracoditam | 
yāgaṃ kṛtvā tu pūrvoktaṃ abhiṣekaṃ tu kārayet || 20-59 ||
hutvā dūrvā trimadhvāktā puraṃśarkarayāyutam |
tataḥ śāntimavāpnoti nānyathā vīranāyike || 20-60 ||
kalaśaṃ raktasaṃpūrṇaṃ kṛṣṇacchāgasamudbhavam |
tatra devīṃ nyased vidyāṃ parāparasamujjvalām || 20-61 ||
hṛdayena yutā pūrvaṃ yathāvat saṃprapūjayet |
kṛṣṇāmbaradharo bhūtvā ārabhet kṛtyasādhanam || 20-62 ||
kanyakāścaturo devi āhṛtya śubhalakṣaṇāḥ |

92a] kṛṣṇanepathyayuktāstu caturdikṣu samaṃtataḥ || 20-63 ||
śvetāścābhimukho devi anyonyaṃ tu nirīkṣayaḥ |
mānuṣyanalakaṃ sārddhaṃ bālarajvāvṛtaṃ priye || 20-64 ||
mathayet tena kalaśaṃ vidyāṃ caivamanusmaret |
praharārddhaṃ yāvadevaṃ tāvatkṛtyā samutthati || 20-65 ||
grasantī bhuvanaraudrā kiṃ karomīti bhāṣate |
sādhakena tu vaktavyamamukaṃ bhakṣayatpathaḥ || 20-66 ||
tato dhāvati sā kṛtyā yatrādiṣṭaṃ tu sādhakaiḥ |
taṃ hatvā praviśettatra yatrotpannā mahābalāḥ || 20-67 ||
athānyaṃ saṃpravakṣyāmi sainyabhaṅgaṃ varānane |
yena daityāḥ purā yuddhe nirjitāstu divaukasaiḥ || 20-68 ||
tadahaṃ saṃpravakṣyāmi śṛṇupārvati tatvataḥ |
likhedvidyā yathāpūrvaṃ parāparamahāvalām || 20-69 ||
tatrānulomato devi vinyaset sainyanāyakām |
hastyaśca rathasāmantā praticārā mahābhaṭāḥ || 20-70 ||
yoddhā dhanuṣi vikrāntā gocarme vātha karpaṭe |
129a g] caturasre kṛte kṣetre vajralāñchanalāñchite || 20-71 ||
tatra madhye valaṃ likhya parātyuṃ vihvalīkṛtam |
vāhyāvaraṇasaṃsthā tu mātṛkāyākṣarānvitāḥ || 20-72 ||
parāparā mahāvidyā anulome svabalānvitāḥ |
parārūpadharā sarve svasainye ye vyavasthitāḥ || 20-73 ||
cakramudgarakhaḍgādyairāvṛtaṃ tat samantataḥ |
parārūpadharairanyairanekaistu mahāvalaiḥ || 20-74 ||
bāhyato nāgayūthādyairghaṇṭāghaṭitabhīṣaṇaiḥ |
punarevaṃ tato bāhye āśvāsannugrabhīṣaṇāḥ || 20-75 ||
nistriṃśokhātahastāstu khaḍgādyairāyudhodyatāḥ |
dhanuṣāyodhayuktāstu likhettat samatārditam || 20-76 ||
cāturaṅgabalenaiva vidyayā sahitena tu |
prakṣipetpretavaktre tu sainyasyābhimukhaṃ sthitam || 20-77 ||
nikhaneta tato vidvāṃ raṅgabhūmyā tu madhyataḥ |
tatropari pratiṣṭhāpya devīṃ viśvasya nāyikām || 20-78 ||
grasamānāṃ tataḥ sainyaṃ kartavyā yogayoginī |
dhvajacīrikavitānaiḥ patākā cīrikākulam || 20-79 ||
evaṃ kṛtvā tataḥ paścād balikarma samārabhet |
mahāpaśusamapādyairbhakṣyabhojyairanekaśaiḥ || 20-80 ||
tarpayeddevatāṃ sarvaṃ kṣetrapālādibhūtayoḥ |
digbaliṃ tu tato datvā tathā bhūtabalīni ca || 20-81 ||
saṃtarpyamevamevaṃ tu tato rājābhiṣecayet |
yāgaṃ kṛtvā tu pūrvoktaṃ kalaśaṃ vijayātmakam || 20-82 ||
saṃpūjya vidhivanmantrī yathā śāstre pracoditam |
pratyūṣe vimale devi snāpayedvīranāyikam || 20-83 ||
kadalairaṣṭabhirdevi pīṭhabandhaṃ tu kārayet |
tatopaviṣṭarājānāṃ snāpayettena cāmbhasā || 20-84 ||
parāparāsamo devi bhavate sādhakottamaḥ |
prokṣayentena kumbhena samantāttu svakaṃ balam || 20-85 ||
nirgatyātra tato bāhye devyāyāḥ paścime sthitaḥ |
bherīmṛdaṅgamaśvānāṃ dantināṃ yodhanāyakam || 20-86 ||
nyasedvidyāṃ mahādevi parāparasubhīṣaṇām |
tato rāvaṃ prakartavyaṃ bhīṣaṇaṃ cāti dāruṇam || 20-87 ||
gṛhṇa gṛhṇa hana dve dve vaktavyaṃ sarvata sthitaiḥ |
sainyasābhimukho dhāve tato bhavati vihvalam || 20-88 ||
vepamānaṃ bhayatrastaṃ muktavaktraṃ tu tiṣṭhati |
utkhanodyataśatruṃ ca hastātpatati sarvasu || 20-89 ||
vimukhā mohitāḥ sarve naśyante tyaktapauruṣāḥ |
129b g] gajāśvavimukhā yānti aśvāśca gatavikramāḥ || 20-90 ||
evaṃ devaiḥ purā yuddhe sainyabhaṅgaṃ kṛtaṃ priye |

92b] athānyaṃ saṃpravakṣyāmi patākā cocchrayo vidhiḥ || 20-91 ||
sainyabhaṅgaṃ bhavatyāśu tacchṛṇuṣva samāsataḥ |
kanyākartitasūtreṇa paṭaṃ kṛtvā ghanaṃ pṛthum || 20-92 ||
tatra madhye parāṃ likhya śavāsana vyavasthitām |
vikarālā mahākāyāmaṣṭahastāṃ subhīṣaṇām || 20-93 ||
adhastāt tu nyaset sādhyaṃ nirbhinnaṃ mūlakena tu |
grasamānāṃ mahāmāyāṃ parasainyaṃ samaṃ tataḥ || 20-94 ||
kapālasthaṃ likhetsarvaṃ parasainyaṃ parāparām |
evaṃ likhya tato devīṃ paścāt pūjāṃ tu kārayet || 20-95 ||
bhakṣabhojyānnapānaiśca tarpayet sarvato diśi |
kadalairaṣṭabhiḥ kuryāddevyāpīṭhaṃ suśobhanam || 20-96 ||
tatrasthāṃ pūjayeddevī gandhadhūpapavitrakaiḥ |
puṣpaiśca śobhanairmāṃsaiḥ surāsavasamanvitaiḥ || 20-97 ||
evaṃ pūjāṃ tataḥ kṛtvā valiṃ datvā subhāvitaḥ |
