ānayedaṣṭaveśyāstu gatavayāḥ subhāvitāḥ || 20-98 ||
tarpayenmadyamāṃsena yāvatprītyā bhaviṣyati |
nagnā muktaśikhāyāstu mantramevaṃ samuccaran || 20-99 ||
hana bhaṃjāpade dve dve gṛhṇa gṛhṇeti māraya |
kandalaṃ vāmahastasthaṃ ekaikāyā varānane || 20-100 ||
dhāvante grasato devyaḥ sainyasyābhimukhā sthitāḥ |
tāṃ dṛṣṭvā naśyate sainyaṃ patākādarśanena tu || 20-101 ||
gatalajjābhimānaṃ tu vimukhaṃ naśyate priye |
jitvā sainyaṃ varārohe śataguṇaṃ cāpi sādhakaḥ || 20-102 ||
akṣayaṃ svagṛhaṃ devi āgacchati na saṃśayaḥ |
athānyaṃ saṃpravakṣyāmi patākāyā samucchrayam || 20-103 ||
pūrvavatpadamāhṛtya bhaktiyuktastu sādhakaḥ |
caturasraṃ puraṃ likhya madhye mantraṃ samālikhet || 20-104 ||
pūrvādikramayogena bhramantaṃ cakravadyathā |
hanumantraṃ likhettatra daṇḍapāṇimahābalam || 20-105 ||
uṣṭraṃ samālikhedhastāt tat pṛṣṭhe sainyanāyakam |
uṣṭramadhye likhenmantraṃ pādeṣu ca tataḥ punaḥ || 20-106 ||
caturdikṣu nyasedanyaṃ catuṣpādagataṃ priye |
oṃ vajrakāya vajratuṇḍa kapilapiṅgalorddhakeśaraktamukha taḍijjihva mahāraudre daṃṣṭrākarālina dṛḍhapraharaṇa laṃkeśvara setubandhaka śailapravāha gaganacara ehyehi bhagavan mahābalaparākrama bhairavojñāpayati dīrghāṇāṃ gulena veṣṭaya veṣṭaya sarvata stambhaya stambhaya kledaya kledaya jambhaya jambhaya khakha re khakha re sarvaduṣṭān kha kha hū/ hū/ phaṭ svāhā |
130a g] oṃ himavantaṃ yāhi yāhi uṣṭradehe likhet |
oṃ yamadevī svāhā |
caturṣu pādeṣu deyam |
oṃ vātali vātali virale varāhamukhi sarvaduṣṭamukhānāṃ kāyaṃ vajrāntaṃ stambhaya stambhaya svāhā | 
vajramaṇḍale caturdiśā deyam | 
evaṃ likhya vidhānena paścāt pūjāṃ samārabhet || 20-107 || 
gandhadhūpānnapānādyairbalikarmāstu kārayet |
patākāmucchrayenmantrī sainyasyābhimukhasthitām || 20-108 ||
darśanād vikalībhūtaṃ naśyate tyaktapauruṣam |
athavā bahurūpaṃ tu parāparasamanvitam || 20-109 ||
śākinīhṛdayenaiva nirvāheṇa vidarbhayet |
māraṇaṃ sainyabhaṅgaṃ ca kurute nātra saṃśayaḥ || 20-110 ||
mahāmārīṃ likhed vātha śākinīṃ marmabheditām | 
rudhireṇa śaśakasya grāsaṃ mārīṃ praveśayet || 20-111 ||
gardabhasya tu raktena puramārī na saṃśayaḥ | 
yena yena tu raktena mahāmārīṃ likhet priye || 20-112 ||
tena tena tu rūpeṇa mahāmārī pravartate |
parāparā mahāmārī astavyastakṛtā padā || 20-113 ||

93a] śākinī marmasaṃyuktā saṃharejjagadevatu |
yamarājaṃ tato devi yojayedyamaśāsanam || 20-114 ||
yamāntakamidaṃ mantra kālāntakamiti smṛtam | 
yamarāja tathā devi vahurūpaṃ tathaiva ca || 20-115 ||
saṃyogena yadā yojya bhairave hṛdayātmatā |
māraṇaṃ śoṣaṇaṃ dhvaṃsaṃ vidveṣoccāṭanaṃ tathā || 20-116 ||
kurute sādhakendrastu yadi dhyāne suyantritaḥ |
mahāpāśupataṃ yojyāṃ śākinīmarmabheditām || 20-117 ||
mārayennātra sandeho ripu śakraṃsamo priyaḥ | 
mahāpāśupataṃ likhya yamarāḍastra? saṃyutam || 20-118 ||
mārayeta na sandeho ghaṭikārddhena saṃśayaḥ |
athānyaṃ paramaṃ devi mantrakośamanuttamam || 20-119 ||
yena vijñātamātreṇa kāmikīsiddhimāpnuyāt |
uccāṭe piṇḍakaṃ śreṣṭaṃ taṃ pravakṣyāmyavistarāt || 20-120 ||
oṃ hrā/ hrī/ hūhaḥ phaṭ | 
mātṛkākṣaragaṃ devi nirvāhobhayadīpitam |
vāyunā preritaṃ caiva uccāṭaṃ kurute bhṛśam || 20-121 ||
vaḍavāmukhaṃ mahātejaḥ kālāgnirivamutthitam |
śoṣaṇaṃ dhvaṃsanaṃ caiva māraṇoccāṭanaṃ tathā || 20-122 ||
kulocchādanamevaṃ tu yaccānyaṃtra visaṃsthitam |

tena plāvitadeśaṃ tu maṇḍalaṃ viṣayaṃ pivā |
evaṃ pratidinaṃ kuryād yāvat saṃvittikārakam || 20-182 ||
na cāgnivāpanaṃ yāti na ca kṣīyati maṇḍalam |
tadevaścāvitaṃ sarvaṃ tajjvālādīpitaṃ ca yat || 20-183 ||
ālekhyaṃ ca purakṣobhaṃ kathitaṃ tava śobhane |
134a g] athavā raktasūtreṇa tadakṣarasamanvitam || 20-184 ||
vedayet? tatpuraṃ mantrī samantato? vanirantaram |
evaṃ pratidinaṃ kuryādyāvat kṣobhaṃ prajāyate || 20-185 ||
anena kramayogena kārayet kṣobhamuttamam |
athavā cāvasāne tu tannāmaṃ samudīrayet || 20-186 ||
purakṣobhaḥ prajāyeta yāvadayutamāyutam |
anena kramayogena deśakṣobhaḥ prajāyate || 20-187 ||
athānyaṃ saṃpravakṣyāmi purakṣobhamanuttamam |
yena vijñātamātreṇa sādhakaḥ krīḍate kṣitau || 20-188 ||
aṣṭapatraṃ likhetpadmaṃ bāhye caiva tu ṣoḍaśam |
caturviṃśadale paścāt krameṇaiva tu sundari || 20-189 ||
savargaṃ yojya pūrvādi yavarga vidiśā nyaset |
ṣoḍaśāre akārādi caturviṃśat tu kādikāḥ || 20-190 ||
bhāntaṃ tu karṇikā yojyā nṛpaṃ garbhe niyojayet |
acatuṣkasamākrāntaṃ vargādi vīranāyike || 20-191 ||
digdalaṃ svadalāntasthāṃ? guhyaśaktiṃ samālikhet |
rocanā vāmajo rakte ajakṣīreṇa saṃyutam || 20-192 ||
devasyāyatane vāpi athavā ca catuṣpathe |
ātmadevagṛhe vāpi nidhāpya surasundari || 20-193 ||
pūjāṃ kṛtvā mahāvīraḥ pūrvacakramasaṃsthitam |
purakṣobha bhavatyāśu nātra kāryā vicāraṇā || 20-194 ||
purakṣobhaṃ mayā khyātaṃ guhyādguhyataraṃ param |
sādhakānāṃ hitārthāya rahasyaṃ prakaṭīkṛtam || 20-195 ||
kalākarṣamato vakṣye sarvākarṣaṇamuttamam |
kāmeśvaraṃ hṛdi sthāpya pañcavāṇadhanurddharam || 20-196 ||
śadvādyābhiḥ kalābhiśca parita parivāritam |
manasā pūjayitvā tu gandhadhūpapavitrakaiḥ || 20-197 ||
pūjayitvā tu vidhivat saṃhāraṃ mudrayā punaḥ |
kadambagolakākāraṃ cintayedraśmi saṃkulam || 20-198 ||
jñātvā tadudayaṃ mantrī tataḥ karmāṇi kārayet |
śaktīnāmudayaṃ tadvata kuryāt karmamanepsitam || 20-199 ||
amṛtāmṛtanāḍīnāṃ śaktīnāṃ priyadarśanām |
ete dvepi maheśāni saṃyojyotavicakṣaṇaḥ || 20-200 ||
karmakāle sa ca nyāsaḥ kuryād devātmavigrahe |
amṛtāmṛta vinyasya tataśca priyadarśanā || 20-201 ||
dhyātvānya dhyāpya hṛtpadmaṃ tatkāmaṃ bindurūpiṇaṃ |
ṣva?kāsaṃ vindunā sārddhamātmānaṃ ca niyojayet || 20-202 ||

96a] bindūrddhvaṃ kāmaśaktiṃ ca tatastvaukāra cintayet |
pūrvavannāḍimārgeṇa śaktiṃ tatra praveśayet || 20-203 ||
134b g] taṃ kāmaṃ vindumūrddhnāntaṃ vedhayecchaktitantunā |
vṛthākṛṣya yathānyāyāṃ yojayed ātmabinduke || 20-204 ||
rāgaṃ tasya kṛtau sādhye pūrvavaccintayet tadā |
kālākarṣa mayā proktaṃ kāmasyākarṣaṇaṃ param || 20-205 ||
evaṃ krameṇa sarveṣāṃ kathitaṃ te yaśasvini |
nātaḥ parataraṃ vaśyaṃ triṣu lokeṣu vidyate || 20-206 ||
asamaṃ tu idaṃ proktaṃ vaśīkaraṇamuttamam |
athānyaṃ saṃpravakṣyāmi kālānāṃ ca yaśasvini || 20-207 ||
śraddhādīnā ca nāmāni yathāvadanupūrvaśaḥ |
śraddhā tuṣṭikarī caiva kānticaiva manoramā || 20-208 ||
śaśinī ca samākhyātā cchāyā saṃpūrṇamaṇḍalā |
amṛtā mṛtāvahā caiva anaṅgā mekhalā tathā || 20-209 ||
mohā ca mohanā caiva śoṣaṇā madanāvahā |
madanā ca samākhyātā yoginyaḥṣoḍaśaḥ smṛtāḥ || 20-210 ||
evaṃ jñātvā tu mantrajña kṣobhayeta carācaram |
athānyaṃ saṃpravakṣyāmi vidyā trailokyakṣobhaṇī || 20-211 ||
catuṣpatre'kṣate padme karṇikākeśarānvite |
yonijā dvārapālinyaḥ kṣetrajā pīṭhavāsinī || 20-212 ||
īśapatrāt samārabhya catuṣpatre tu tān likhet |
siddhayogeśvarī madhye mahāmāyānuveṣṭitam || 20-213 ||
sarvayonigataṃ saumyaṃ bāhye cāṣṭadalaṃ likhet |
mātarastatra yojyeta ātmamantrakrameṇa tu || 20-214 ||
guhyaśaktigataṃ likhya puna rudragataṃ likhet |
māyayā veṣṭitaṃ kṛtvā diśāpālāṃkitaṃ priye || 20-215 ||
kuṇḍagolodbhavenaiva rocanācandanena ca |
kuṃkume candane rakte vaṭapatrīsamanvite || 20-216 ||
ekīkṛtya likhenmantrī bhūrje'naṃgatrayodaśī |
raktāmbaradharo bhūtvā raktagandhānulepanaḥ || 20-217 ||
madirādisamāyuktairyaṣṭvā? pūrvakrameṇa tu |
ityevaṃ kathitā devi vidyā trailokyakṣobhaṇī || 20-218 ||
anaṃgena tvayā pūrvaṃ dhāritā vīravandite |
purā dāruvane gaṃ tu ṛṣiṇyā kṣobhamicchatā || 20-219 ||
mayāpi dhāritā vidyā apratiśca sureśvari |
eṣā guhyā samākhyātā guhyaśaktisamudbhavā || 20-220 ||
athānyaṃ saṃpravakṣyāmi dhyānākṣobhaṇamuttamam |
agnimaṇḍalayugmaṃ syāt tanmadhye koṇagaṃ punaḥ || 20-221 ||
raukāraṃ dāpayed bhāsva dhyāyeccakrāruṇaṃ bhramam |
yonimudrāṃ parāṃ vadhvā yasya yonau gatāgatam || 20-222 ||
dravate niścitaṃ śātu bhairavasya vaco yathā |
rāmaṃ ravyādhyapayenayiṣṭvā paiśācakena tu || 20-223 ||
135a g] rasamadhvā samālabhya haste gṛhṇeta yāvalā |
sāmlā'thā'yātyasaṃdigdhā kāmāgnitanutāpitā || 20-224 ||
svakareṣṭadalaṃ padmaṃ subhagālaktakāmbunā |
svanāmaṃ tu likhenmadhye tuṃ vaḥ kāmini rañjaya || 20-225 ||
vāhveṃganāṃ likhet tadvadgṛhītā ślathayedvalā |
piṇḍasthaṃ piṇḍamadhyasthaṃ jvalatpāvakasannibham || 20-226 ||
śaktyāgre bhāvayed devi lalāṭasthaṃ samujjvalām |
kaṇṭhato hṛdayā nābhau kṣobhayat tantu bhāvayet || 20-227 ||
tāpayet tanna sandeho bhramantaṃ guhyamadhyataḥ |
caturthasvarabhinnaṃ tu retāruṇitavibhramam || 20-228 ||
tatkṣaṇāt kṣubhyte kanyā yadi caivadvivarṣikā |
punareva tu tatpiṇḍaṃ nābhisthaṃ bhāvayedyadā || 20-229 ||
bhramantaṃ śaktinādhārāṃ kṣubhyte dravamuñcati |
ūrddhamagnimadhastoyamagnisomātmakaṃ smaret || 20-230 ||
yasyā yonau varārohe sā śukraṃ kṣarate kṣaṇāt |

96b] aikāraṃ śaktiragre tu jvalatpāvakasannibham || 20-231 ||
bhramantaṃ cintayedyonau kṣaṇādretaṃ pramuñcati |
jaṃbhanaṃ staṃbhanaṃ mohaṃ śoṣaṇaṃ kṣobhaṇaṃ tathā || 20-232 ||
pañcāvasthā samākhyātā kāmavāṇāḥ prakīrtitāḥ |
jaṃbhanaṃ vaktradeśe tu staṃbhanaṃ hṛdaye tathā || 20-233 ||
mohanaṃ nābhisaṃsthaṃ tu śoṣaṇaṃ tanumadhyataḥ |
kṣobhaṇaṃ guhyamadhye tu jñātavyaṃ vīranāyike || 20-234 ||
etaisthānaistu saṃkṣobhya dravate dravamuñcati |
hutādyaḥ pavanendraśca ṣaṣṭhasvaravibhūṣitaḥ || 20-235 ||
sānusvārāgnicakrasthaṃ raktābhaṃ svaraveṣṭitam |
yasya yonau bhrama dhyāye dānayet tā na saṃśayaḥ || 20-236 ||
elāvakāramadhyasthaṃ guhya deśāruṇaṃ calam |
aṃkuśena tadākarṣecchakti kroṃkārasaṃyutam || 20-237 ||

devyuvāca

śobhanaṃ devadeveśa tvayā proktaṃ maheśvara |
kāmabāṇopayogaṃ ca tanmantrāṇi vadeśvara || 20-238 ||
vaśyamākarṣaṇādīni kurute yena sādhakaḥ |

bhairava uvāca

ataḥ paraṃ pravakṣyāmi mantraṃ rativivarddhanam || 20-239 ||
hitāya sādhakendrāṇāṃ kāmadevasya dhīmataḥ |
kāmaṃ sarvasvakaṃ nāma śivena samudāhṛtam || 20-240 ||
sarvavyāpīśvaro devaḥ sūkṣmo manmatharūpadhṛk |
kalābhiḥ ṣoḍaśābhistu jagad vyāpya vyavasthitaḥ || 20-241 ||
tasya bhāvā kriyāyogāṃ viṣayāṃścānupūrvataḥ |
bāṇāṃkuśāṃkurasthāne prayogāṃ sambravīmyaham || 20-242 ||
pratipanmūrti? vinyasya dvitīyā tu bhruvottare |
tṛtīyā vāmapārśve tu caturthī dakṣiṇe tathā || 20-243 ||
pañcamyāṃ stanamadhye tu * * trabhyāṃ ṣaṣṭhisaptamī |
135b g] aṣṭamī nābhideśe tu navamīti bhage sthitā || 20-244 ||
daśamīti lalāṭe vai urasyekādaśī tathā |
dvādaśī pṛṣṭhamadhye tu trayodaśyāṃ tu kakṣayoḥ || 20-245 ||
caturdaśyāṃ kaṭīmadhye pañcadaśyāṃ bhujopari |
mūrdhni netre yuge vaktre hṛdaye ca tathā bhage || 20-246 ||
pañcaite manmathasthānā sādhakaiḥ samadhiṣṭhitāḥ? |
nābhyāṃ madāṃkuro jñeyastasya mūrdhni niveśayet || 20-247 ||
kṛtvā manmathakāyaṃ tu sādhaye strīnarāṃkurottamaḥ |
vaśyamākarṣaṇaṃ guhyaṃ sārātsāraṃ samarasya tu || 20-248 ||
akāramaṃkurākāramindragopakasannibham |
pāśākāraṃ tu īkāramasitāñjanasaprabham || 20-249 ||
aṃkuśaṃ ca dadenmūrdhnipāśaṃ netre prayojayet |
vaktre dadendukārasyādai hṛdaye yathāvidhiḥ || 20-250 ||
aukāraṃ bhage kṣiptvā svamantreṇānugṛhya ca |
evaṃ tu kramaśaḥ kuryāt prayogāt kāmatantravit || 20-251 ||
tithijñaḥ kāmavāṇajñaḥ kāmatantraviśāradaḥ |
kāmavadvicarelloke sarvatraiva yathāsukham || 20-252 ||
prathamaṃ tu kalāsparśa tato bījairanukramāt |
bāṇāṃścaivāṅkuśaṃ kuryād varāgre cāṃkuśaṃ nyaset || 20-253 ||
evaṃ tu satataṃ kuryād yathānyāyaṃ yathāvidhiḥ |
viśaśaraistu jānī hi tadaṃgāṃgaistu nityaśaḥ || 20-254 ||
kṣubhyanti vaśamāyānti āgacchanti na saṃśayaḥ | 
trisandhyāmarddharātre tu tridināni vicintayet || 20-255 ||
madonmattapramattā vai kāmārto madanotkaṭā |
svapne paśye tato nityaṃ tadvarṇarūpadhāriṇam || 20-256 ||
puruṣasya bhavedevaṃ yadi nārī prayujyate |
ekāraṃ ca vakāraṃ ca lakāraṃ ca tṛtīyakam || 20-257 ||
dhyāyed madhye? trayo varṇāsvamaṇḍalavimadhyagām |

97a] tejorūpāruṇaṃ? sarvaṃ bhramantraṃ cakravadyathā || 20-258 ||
nadī prākāratugāni parvatādyalakāni ca |
laṃghayitvā muhūrtena āgaccheta na saṃśayaḥ || 20-259 ||
anaṃga śasvaraktā tu vaśamāyānti tatkṣaṇāt |
ākṛṣṇenāpi jighāṃsanti śuktena bhavate madam || 20-260 ||
raktena dravate nārī pītena tu visarjayet |
ekāraṃ yoni madhye tu vinyaset karṣayet kṣaṇāt || 20-261 ||
caturthaṃ svara saṃyuktaṃ mekāraṃ ca prayojayet |
trailokyamānayedkṣipraṃ? sadevāsuramānuṣam || 20-262 ||
sakāravaktramadhye tu jakāraṃ daśane tathā ? |
akāraṃ jihvadeśe tu dakāraṃ tālumadhyataḥ || 20-263 ||
yāmabhibhāṣayennārī tāstāṃ vaśamānayet |
136a g] anṃgīkaraṇaṃ vacmi? samantraṃ tacchṛṇuṣva me || 20-264 ||
oṃ asure sure susure svāhā |
amodhākhyaṃ mantraṃ dṛṣṭaṃ ā ī ū ai au pañca bāṇāḥ |
oṃ kāreyaṃ? kraṃkāraḥ sarvāṃgeṣu pradātavyaḥ |
oṃ namo anaṅgāya kāmarājiko kāmadruhaṃbhve druhai anaṅgarūpa pratipadyantu svāhā |
anaṃgīkaraṇametat |
oṃ anaṅgāya vidmahe kandarpāya dhīmahe tatro kāmaśca codayāt | kāmagāyatrī | 
oṃ amṛtarūpāya svāmukhamamṛtībhavatu anaṃgīkaraṇanāmaḥ |
athānyaṃ saṃpravakṣyāmi udayaṃ manmathasya tu |
rathacakrakrameṇaiva sthānānāṃ parivartanam || 20-265 ||
guhyādguhyataraṃ guhyaṃ rathaṃ vai manmathasya tu |
udayāstamanaṃ caiva saṃyogaṃ ca viyojanam || 20-266 ||
svake bījakrameṇaiva mṛgāṅgasya tu yojanam |
kathayāmi yathātathyaṃ śṛṇu saṃsāratāriṇi || 20-267 ||
aṅguṣṭhapṛṣṭhasandhīṣu jānunī liṅganābhigam |
hṛtstanau kaṇṭhanāsākṣi karṇabhrūśaṃ khamūrddhani || 20-268 ||
śuklapakṣe prayātyūrddhvaṃ kṛṣṇapakṣe hyadhomukhaḥ |
pramāṇāmīdṛśaṃ lakṣellalanānāṃ ca vāmagaḥ || 20-269 ||
mantramudrā kalātītaṃ dhyeyadhāraṇavarjitam |
viṣanāśaṃ ca kāmaṃ ca jānāt saṃvittiriṣyate || 20-270 ||
ācūṣaṇā viṣaṃ prāsenmardanāt kṣarate valāt |
tadanaṃgaṃ tu tatraiva śikhimaṇḍalamadhyagam || 20-271 ||
nāmādyakṣarasaṃyuktaṃ yojyāṃ caiva varānane |
kṣobhamāyāti tatraiva anyathā na kadācana || 20-272 ||
kṣayavṛddhiṃ tato jñātvā vṛddhiśca kṣayatāṃ gatāḥ |
yuktācāragatiṃ candre kṣobhayennātra saṃśayaḥ || 20-273 ||
eṣa devi bhagacakraṃ kāmadevasya tatvataḥ |
śaśikāmagatiṃ caiva eka eva dvidhā smṛtam || 20-274 ||
tadvidhā ekato jñātvā na punarjanmatāṃ vrajet |
abhyaseta krameṇaiva mṛtyujitsabhavettadā || 20-275 ||
etadvai rathacakraṃ tu kāmadevasya suvrate |
rathayogakramaṃ jñātvā yatra tatraiva sādhakaḥ || 20-276 ||
kṣobhayejjagataḥ sarvaṃ kiṃ punarmanujā gatāḥ |
gopanīyaṃ mahāguhyaṃ samayīputrake tathā || 20-277 ||
anyaśāstrakrameṇaiva ye bibuddhāstu vaiśikāḥ |
teṣāṃ caiva tvayā gopya bhaktihīnā narādhamām || 20-278 ||
nānyaṃ kasyacidākhyātaṃ tvayi muktā tu pārvati |
gopanīyaṃ varārohe mayā proktaṃ kramaṃ tathā || 20-279 ||
athānyaṃ saṃpravakṣyāmi ākarṣavaśyakṛtparam |
makāraṃ ca manovidyāṃ tadrathaṃ vai smarasya tu || 20-280 ||
136b g] manorathena mantreṇa samastaṃ parikalpayet |
oṃ namo manorathāya paripūrṇāya namaḥ || 20-281 ||
rathamantraṃ dhyātvā samyagrathaṃ divyaṃ tatra ruhya dhanurddharam |
dhyāyedvasantamantraṃ ca madanonmādanaṃ mahat |
smaret deva deveśi nandanākhyaṃ vanaṃ mahat || 20-282 ||
97b] ikṣudaṇḍadhanu smṛtya śaraiḥ pañcabhirāharet |
jambhanaṃ preṣayed guhye staṃbhanaṃ nābhimaṇḍale || 20-283 ||
mohanaṃ hṛdaye nyasya andhanaṃ netrayostathā |
tatastu pādayoḥ patitaṃ dṛṣṭvā sādhyaṃ tu vihvalam || 20-284 ||
śoṣaṇaṃ tu vimucyeta pṛṣṭhamadhye tu sādhakaḥ |
evaṃ viddhaḥ śaraiḥ sādhyaṃ pañcabhiścottamairbhṛśam || 20-285 ||
balāt mantradharo bhoktā piśācoragarākṣasāḥ |
yakṣāgaṇendurādityā vāsavaṃ vita raharim || 20-286 ||
sarvete karṣayenmantrī viddhāḥ kāmaśarairyadi |
athānyaṃ saṃpravakṣyāmi umānandeti kīrtitam || 20-287 ||
yasya smaraṇamātreṇa sānanda iva jāyate |
oṃ namo bhagavāya śāntāya ekavārāya asampannāya umāvadana kamalakiñjalka bhrama bhrama bhrāmaya bhrāmaya mahāsuravadhūnāṃ hṛdaya bandha bandha tānyākarṣayonmā dayapārvatyā stanajaṃghanorupugalāya madīna prasṛta asrusitā bhūtānandakara samanmatha bhaganayana darśasanṛtyaka dakṣayajñavidhvaṃsanakararūkṣa sunāpulinā śramakāma sahedā sitarasitadāsamāvahi timiranirnāśaṃkaravā manakāma japa vāma liṅgā vāma devavaṇitā dharāsavamadamuditavaraghanamathana karāhatabhagavatyā umādevyā divyaṃ varṣasahasraṃ bhagavanmatha apariśāntakaruṇa divasakarajaṃbūnadābhena meḍhreṇa tadanusmaramāgamanaṃ gaśarapīḍita kṛpaṇamanāthamaśaraṇamadurbhagamalasamasaṃjñāsurayuvatibhageṣurasaṃjñā kuru ratirasāsvādanubhukṣatā ca paribādhyamānaṃ candratārakakamale sarasaṃ pūrṇacandravadanānā kathamahamapi varajaṭhara kuryāpamardanasparśamanubhavati |
rasadhātvatprasādagadūrddhvaravivabhasmotkaṭāya jvālita huta vahavisphuliṅgāṭṭahāsa candrakṛtaśekhara trinayanakamalakiñjalkasadṛśaṃ tu svarāsanavarakusumaśironikaravarodyatāyudhaṃ jagatsaṃmohana karamohanadīpana tāpana śoṣaṇa māraṇa vimāra vatāraṇa tāraya gopana gopaya pālaya vandhanā nāśa nāśaya sarvaduṣṭavighna vināśaya kāntānyekavīrā drava drāvaya drāvaya divyabhaumāntarikṣagatānāṃ varayuvatīnāṃ hṛdayaṃ bandha bandhatānyākarṣayonmādaya dṛṣṭau mānarpanaṃ jvareṇābhibhūtājaghajagha varai sravadbhiḥ pra * tārṇave navomāmupadātiṣṭhantu vaddhāśca vaśyo bhavantu he anaṅga paramānandaparanirvākara hara hara kaṭātkaṭa trijaṭārddhaliṅga namostute svāhā |
137a g] mūlamantraḥ | 
oṃ namonagāya svāhā hṛdaya |
oṃ namo ratipriyāya svāhā śira |
oṃ namo kusumāyudhāya svāhā | śikhā |
oṃ sarvato ānandakara svāhā | kavaca |
oṃ ā/ ī/ ū/ ai/ au/ asaṃpannāya hū/ phaṭ namaḥ svāhā | astra |
oṃ o/ yanuḥ | oṃ jyeṣṭha namo guṇaḥ |
oṃ ā/ ī/ hra/ mukhe? śarāya namaḥ |
oṃ o/ namaḥ ākarṣa |
preṣaṇe tu śaraṃ dhyātvā mantraṃ visarjayet |
oṃ hana hana namaḥ svāsapāśa |
oṃ kayyakaṣya hū/ phaṭ namaḥ svāhā | aṃkuśa |
oṃ yajyanuśarāsanāya namaḥ svāhā | śaramantraḥ |
98a] oṃ saḥ saṃhāra namaḥ svahopasaṃhāraḥ |

devyuvāca
yade tatparamaṃ devaṃ pravaraṃ mantramuttamam || 20-288 ||
umānandeti vikhyātaṃ tailokyaprabhavaṃ prabhum |
aṣṭānāṃ devayonīnāṃ samāsādyaṃ prakīrtitam || 20-289 ||
mantrabhedena tatsarvamekatra samatāṃ gatam |
pṛthagbhedo prayogaṃ tu yathāvantad vadasva mām || 20-290 ||

śrībhairava uvāca

aṃkuśaṃ pāśamevaṃ syādāyudhaṃ dhanureva ca |
vāmahastaṃ nyaset pāśaṃ aṃkuśaṃ dakṣiṇe kare || 20-291 ||
sarvā paśyanti vai devi eṣo'naṃgo vyavasthitam |
devadaityendrabhūtāni vetālā rākṣasāni ca || 20-292 ||
nāgā yakṣā piśācāśca yathā gacchanti vaśyatām |
prayogavidhisaṃhāre preṣaṇe sthāpane na ca || 20-293 ||
kiṃ tvastu manujai kṛtyaṃ jarāmaraṇabhīrabhiḥ |
sarveṣāṃ sarvakāryeṣu uttameṣu prayojayet || 20-294 ||
labhyate tūttamaṃ sthānaṃ dīkṣā saṃyojyatāṃ vrajet |
tārā hṛdaya rudraṃ ca vikhyātaṃ tu vibhīṣaṇam || 20-295 ||
umā tilakamevasyānnaravīramathāpi vā |
bhaginyotha bhagonmādaḥ saptākarṣastu vismṛtāḥ || 20-296 ||
umānando'ṣṭamasteṣāṃ uttamaḥ parikīrtitam |
nāsādhyaṃ vidyate cāsya umānandasya tejasaḥ || 20-297 ||
137b g] sa tu sarvasya jagataḥ patibhūto bhaviṣyati |
dhyānenālokataṃ viddhaṃ tataḥ pāśena vandhayet || 20-298 ||
śareṇa tāḍayedbhūyo aṃkuśena nirodhayet |
ekaviṃśa japed vārāṃ mantrarājeti mūrddhani || 20-299 ||
asampannamado na syādastraṃ paramadāruṇam |
panyastrīṣu prayoktavyaṃ śoṣasantāpakārakam || 20-300 ||
siddhyanti sarvakāryāṇi api duṣkṛtakarmaṇaḥ |
trailokya yā striyaḥ kāścidabhimānena garvitāḥ || 20-301 ||
anena jāpyamānena prakṣubhyante sahasradhā |
rambhā vā urvaśī vāpi duhitā vā vṛhaspateḥ || 20-302 ||
śatajapte vaśīkuryāt kiṃ punaḥ prākṛtaḥ priyaḥ |
bhavet saubhāgyamatulaṃ darśanāt sparśanādapi || 20-303 ||
na tasya sādhanaṃ kiñcinna vrataṃ nāṭanocyate |
siddhe paṭhitamātrepi arcasyātra yathāharam || 20-304 ||
yattatpadaṃ vedavido vadanti nirvāṇamagnyaṃ śivamaprameyam |
saṃjñātasarvāṇi avāpnuvanti muñcanti te mānuṣajanmabhāvam |
idamamara vadhūnāṃ kṣobhaṇaṃ vai rajasya japati rativiraktaḥ kāmasantāntarātmā |
girivaramiva rūḍhāṃ sarvamantreśvareśaḥ vrajati śivamanāḍhyaṃ jāyate neha loke |
svajanaguru samīpe naiva jāpyaṃ kadācinmadanaśarasasahasraiḥ kṣobhaṇaṃ bandhuvarge |
nagaragamanamārge naiva jāpyaṃ kadācid giriśikharaguhāyāṃ saṅgamevājapet |
śūdraviṭ kṣetragāyatrā yātudhānāpiśācarān |
vetālo ragayakṣāṇye gandharvā siddhadānavāḥ || 20-305 ||
daityadevā vaśaṃ yānti lakṣajāpāntarāntarāt |
yojanānāṃ sahasreṇa sadyamākarṣayeddhruvam || 20-306 ||
atha pātālasiddhyarthī martyabhogaparāṅmukhaḥ |
satyaśaucarato dhīro dayādākṣiṇyasaṃyutaḥ || 20-307 ||
niḥsaṅgī cāpratigrāhī susahāyo'thavā priye |
asakhā yasya deveśe sakhāyaiṣa japādbhavet || 20-308 ||
niścayī satvavā dhīraḥ kanthākaupīnakānvitāḥ |

98b] sarvasaṅgaviraktātmā sādhayet sādhakottamaḥ || 20-309 ||
asurī nāginī yakṣī daityāṃganāthavā priye |
sādhayeta yadā tūrṇaṃ nārasiṃhaṃ tadā japet || 20-310 ||
pātāleṣu ca sarveṣu bhedakaḥ paramo mahān |
drāvayeta? tathā yantrāṃ sphāṭayeta mahāsurām || 20-311 ||
138a g] vajralepamahādvārāṃ yaṃtrāṇāṃ nā vidhā priye |
apadvārāstathā vikaṭāḥ sarve klidyanti nānyathā || 20-312 ||
smaraṇānāra siṃhasya nāśayanti diśo daśa |
japaṃ mantra varārohe udvegaṃ ca prajāyate || 20-313 ||
sakhāyā dvedhatāṃ yānti rātrau durdarśanā labhet |
bhikṣā na labhate mantrī janaiśva paribhūyate || 20-314 ||
īdṛśeṣupayāteṣu na jaheta japārcanam |
jahi tretra vilomasyādunmatto mriyate'thavā || 20-315 ||
tasmāttaṃ niścaye naiva manudvignadhī * * * |
mṛto'haṃ vāthavā siddha iti kṛtvā vrataṃ hṛdi || 20-316 ||
vicaradvāramāśritya lakṣākṣaravibhāgaśaḥ |
kṣubhyanti puravāsinyaḥ sādhakaṃ tu tato'naghe || 20-317 ||
prārthayanti praveśanti dadanti manasepsitam |
rasaṃ rasāyanaṃ divyaṃ auṣadhyo baladarpitāḥ || 20-318 ||
añjana pādalepaṃ ca pāduko'tha manaḥśilā |
guṭikā śastramālā vā yadanyaṃ siddhikāraṇam || 20-319 ||
sarṣapānabhimantritvā yantraṃ vaitāḍayet priye |
śatadhā bhidyate tūrṇaṃ nātra kuryādvicāraṇā || 20-320 ||
atha śatābhijaptena tena yaṃ tāḍyate priye |
bhasmanāsagraho devi bhavate vigatagrahaḥ || 20-321 ||
mudritaṃ drāvayet tena viṣārtaṃ nirviṣaṃ bhavet |
māraṇoccāṭanādīni tatkṣaṇāt kurute'naghe || 20-322 ||
ataḥ paraṃ pravakṣyāmi mantraṃ vai lakṣaṇānvitam |
nārasiṃhaṃ mahātejaṃ puṇyaṃ jagati viśrutam || 20-323 ||
oṃ namo bhagavate narasiṃhāya pradīptasūryakoṭisahasratejase |
vajranakhadaṃṣṭrāyudhodyatāya sphuṭavikaṭavikīrṇakeśaracchaṭāya kṣubhitamahārṇavāya śodundubhinirghoṣāya sarvayantronnarādhihṛdayāya ehyehyehi bhagavato nārasiṃhaṃ viṣṇo puruṣavareṇa brahmasatyena sphura sphura vijṛmbha vijṛmbha svāhā |
sarvapātālānāṃ kramaya ākramaya garja garja muñca muñca siṃhanāda muñca muñca vidrāvaya vidrāvaya darśaya darśaya āvisa āvisa grasa grasa sarvayantrarūpāṇi sarvayantrajātiṃśca hana hana chinda chinda nidhana nidhana saṃkṣipa saṃkṣipa dara dara dāraya dāraya sphoṭaya sphoṭaya sphāṭaya sphāṭaya jvala jvala jvālaya jvālaya anantajvālavajrasaṃhātamaya sarvatonantajvālāvajrāśanicakreṇa sarvapātālācchādaya sarvatonanta jvālā vajra *?ra jālena sarvapātālā nighātaya sarvatonantajvālāvajrarūpeṇa sarvapātālānāṃ parivāraya mathaya sarvapātālāsuralokavāsinyāṃ hṛdayānyākampaya kampaya dhuna dhuna śīghraṃ daha daha śoṣaya śoṣaya nikṛntaya nikṛntaya tāvadyāvanme vilakanyakāvaśamāgatā svāhā |
138b g] pātālikebhyaḥ phaṭ āsukebhyaḥ phaṭ yantrarūpebhyaḥ phaṭ sasahāyoyaṃ māṃ rakṣa rakṣa bhagavan sarvāpadbhyaḥ sarvabhūtabhayebhyaḥ sarvayantrabhayebhyaśca oṃ oṃ hū/ hū/ kṣā/ kṣā/ phaṭ svāhā |

99a] mūlamantraḥ |
oṃ nārasiṃhavikrīḍitasiṃhavikramarājāya sarvayantrāhanabhaṃjodghāṭaya hū/ phaṭ hṛdaya |
oṃ vedavedani vedāṃgo hū/ phaṭ śiraḥ |
oṃ daṃṣṭrā ghāṭaka hū/ phaṭ śikhā |
oṃ nārasiṃha rakṣa rakṣa mahārakṣa sarvabhayebhyaḥ svāhā hū/ phaṭ kavaca |
oṃ asurāntakara vidara vidara chinda chinda hū/ phaṭ namaḥ |
oṃ jvāla prajvāla svāhāgniprākārāstraḥ |
apratighaṃ tu pañcāṅgaṃ kathitaṃ tava supriye |
vidyākośavido vātha phalamiṣṭaṃ labhedatha || 20-324 ||

iti bhairavasrotasi mahātantre vidyāpīṭhe saptakoṭipramāṇe śrītantrasadbhāve yantrādhikāre viṃśatipaṭalaḥ || 20 ||


bhairava uvāca

athānyaṃ saṃpravakṣyāmi vidyākoṣamanuttamam |
lakṣaṇaṃ sādhanopetaṃ yamanuṣṭhacaret sukham || 21-1 ||
śikheti padamuccārya catuṣpathasamanvitam |
vāsinītyaparaṃ yojyā namontā praṇavādikā || 21-2 ||
vidyāyogini kāryasya padaṃ yacchati pūjitā |
yathāvasthā purā japtvā yāgaṃ kuryāt tato budhaḥ || 21-3 ||
tatastu vidhivatsamyag yogisaṃghasya suvrate |
gatvā catuṣpathaṃ grāmād dūrāntadigvyavasthitaḥ || 21-4 ||
tatra maṇḍalamālikhya śubhapadmavibhūṣitam |
ekahastaṃ dvihastaṃ vā caturaṣṭamathāpi vā || 21-5 ||
mantrāṃga ṣaṭkasaṃyuktaṃ nābhisthāṃ yoginī yajet |
nṛmāṃsaṃ homayedryaktaṃ matsyamāṃsamathāpi vā || 21-6 ||
śatamekaṃ sahasraṃ vā japaṃ paścāt samārabhet |
āgacchanti tato devyo yogine sarvakāmadāḥ || 21-7 ||
divyasiddhiṃ prayacchanti aṇimādiguṇānvitām |
napa * * ? thavā devyastadā siddhistu mānuṣī || 21-8 ||
jāyate sādhakendrasya vaśyākarṣaṇakārikā |
sakāreṇa puṭāntasthā vaśīkaraṇakarmaṇi || 21-9 ||
139a g] hrī/ puṭasthā tathā kṛṣṇau hū/ puṭasthā tu māharet |
hū/ phaṭ dveṣa vidhau yojyoccāṭe yaḥ phaḍānvitāḥ || 21-10 ||
phaphaḍantasamāyuktā prāṇināṃ prāṇanāśane |
prayojyā śāntike devi vauṣaḍāntā tu sundari || 21-11 ||
prāksiddhaiśca tathā dravyairhomāṃ siddhistu jāyate |
yaḥ sadā pūjayatīmāṃ catuṣpathanivāsinīm || 21-12 ||
sarvakāmasamṛddhau'sau mātṛsāyujyamāpnuyāt |
hū/ hiḥ śabdaṃ samālikhya śmaśānanilayeti ca || 21-13 ||
viccedānāntasaṃyuktā vidyeyamaparāśubhā |
śmaśāne padmamālikhya bhairavīṃ pūjayenniśim || 21-14 ||
vāgvidhānena mantrajñe aśeṣaphalabhāgbhavet |
śikhāyā devadevasya aparā pūjyamātaraḥ || 21-15 ||
nṛmāṃsāhutibhiḥ sarvā devyaḥ saṃparitarpayet |
mātṛmaṇḍalasāmānyaṃ padamabdādavāpnuyāt || 21-16 ||
śmaśānavāsinī nāmaṃ sarvayoginamaskṛtām |
yaḥ smarenmātṛmadhyasthā divyādivyaiḥ sa pūjyate || 21-17 ||
caṇḍeti padamuccārya krodheśvarasamanvitam |
hū/ phaṭkārasamāyuktā satkāreṇa samanvitāḥ || 21-18 ||
eṣā vidyā mahāraudrī nāmnā krodheśvarīti ca |
śmaśānanilayo bhūtvā bhasmoddhūlitavigrahaḥ || 21-19 ||
padamantrāṅgasannaddhastato maṇḍalamālikhet |
sāsthinā śitacūrṇena susamaṃ sitabhasmanā || 21-20 ||

99b] dalāṣṭakasamāyuktaṃ nābhikiñjalkamaṇḍitam |
hastamātraṃ likhetpadmaṃ tasyānte tu vicakṣaṇaḥ || 21-21 ||
tatra madhye sthitāṃ pūjya krodheśīṃ bhīmavikramām |
jvālāmālākulāṃ ghorāṃ sarpagotra? samaṇḍitām || 21-22 ||
evaṃ tu pañcabhirlakṣai siddhā bhavaṃti mātaraḥ |
tataḥ samācaret karma yadiṣṭaṃ sādhakasya tu || 21-23 ||
pādapāśu samādāya rājikālavaṇānvitā |
sādhyasya pratimāṃ kṛtvā nāmaṃvidyāvidarbhitam || 21-24 ||
hṛdaye nihitaṃ tasya tāpayet khadirānale |
yasyaivaṃ kriyate karma sa vaśyo bhavate dhrūvam || 21-25 ||
mahāmāṃsasamāyuktāstilā naravaśaṃkarāḥ |
striyā puṣpasamāyuktairgomāṃsaiśca tathāntyajāḥ || 21-26 ||
vaśamānayate mantrī śatenāṣṭottareṇa tu |
prāguktajātibhedena vaśyākarṣaṇamāraṇam || 21-27 ||
karoti mantravitkarma yadvāmana śirocyate |
cāmuṇḍeti padaṃ pūrvaṃ kiliyugmamataḥ param || 21-28 ||
hū/ phaṭkārasamāyuktā praṇavādinamontikā |
sārddhadviṣaṭkavarṇā ca jñātavyā mantravādinaḥ || 21-29 ||
139b g] asyāṃgānāṃ samuddhāraṃ kathayāmi samāsataḥ |
cāmuṇḍeti padaṃ pūrvaṃ maghoreti tathā param || 21-30 ||
amoghakiliyugmaṃ ca vicce hū/ phaṭ satārakam |
aṣṭādaśārddhavarṇaṃ ca hṛdayaṃ samudāhṛtam || 21-31 ||
aghoreti padaṃ pūrvaṃ cāmuṇḍeti puraḥ saraḥ |
hū/ phaḍanta bhavādyaṃ ca navākṣaramidaṃ śiraḥ || 21-32 ||
oṃkāreti samāyuktaṃ cāmuṇḍeti padaṃ param |
aghorarūpeti tathā kiliyugmamataḥ param || 21-33 ||
śikhāmantraṃ samākhyātamiti pañcadaśākṣaram |
cāmuṇḍeti padaṃ pūrvaṃ satkāreṇa samanvitam || 21-34 ||
kapālaṃ haste ca mahāvicce hū/ phaṭ samanvitam |
varmametatsamākhyātaṃ varṇapañcadaśānvitam? || 21-35 ||
oṃkāramuccaretpūrvaṃ cāmuṇḍeti tataḥ param |
raudrarūpeti hū/kāraṃ phaṭkāreṇa samanvitam || 21-36 ||
daśākṣarasamāyuktamastraṃ devyā mahaujasam |
cāmuṇḍeti kiliyugmaṃ ca vicce hū/ phaṭ samanvitam || 21-37 ||
tārakādyaṃ samākhyātaṃ hṛdayaṃ paramaṃ śubham |
anena gandhadhūpaṃ ca valipuṣpanivedanam || 21-38 ||
upacāraṃ prakartavyaṃ rajasūtrābhimantraṇam |
śirasā sthāpanaṃ kāryaṃ sānedhyatve śikhā smṛtā || 21-39 ||
hṛdayenārghapādaṃ ca kavacenāṃ gadāni tu |
mantreṇa? sarvataḥ śuddhi rakṣāṃ cāpi samārabhet || 21-40 ||
aṣṭāre caiva vaktre tu pūjayet parameśvarī |
svāṃgapañcakasaṃyuktāṃ pūjayenmatra? vigrahaḥ || 21-41 ||
athāsya vivṛtaṃ kalpaṃ? kathayāmi suśobhanam |
sarvakarmakaraṃ siddhaṃ sukhabodhyaṃ mahatphalam || 21-42 ||
ekānte vijane dhyātvā japet tat tu parāyaṇaḥ |
lakṣamekaṃ susantapto? mūlamantrasya sādhakaḥ || 21-43 ||
daśasāhasrikaṃ paścādaṃgānāṃ tu japasmṛtaḥ |
tataḥ siddhā mahāvidyā bhavatīti na saṃśayaḥ || 21-44 ||
svasthāne śaṅkarī devī sarvagrahavināśanī |
karoṭakaijvarāveśaṃ? smaritā sā kariṣyati || 21-45 ||
vātāsanimahāvṛṣṭividyunmaṇibhidāruṇam |
karoti divyamastambhamāyudhānā nivāraṇam ? || 21-46 ||
praviśya jvalamadhye tu yāvadaṣṭaśataṃ japet |
devaṃ varṣāpapatyāśu ghaṭikārddhe tu mantravit || 21-47 ||
mānuṣāsthimayaṃ kīlaṃ kārayitvā ṣaḍaṅgulam |

100a] śatābhimantritaṃ kṛtvā yasya veśma nidhāpayet ? || 21-48 ||
tamucchādayate kṣipraṃ saptāhānnātra saṃśayaḥ |
nikhātaṃ goṣṭhamadhye tu kṣīranāśaṃ karoti ca || 21-49 ||
140a g] aśvamadhye tu taṃ nyastamaśvamārī pravartake |
hastino hastimadhye tu nihitastu nikṛntayet || 21-50 ||
yūrikārṇā gṛhe nyastaṃ durbhagatvaṃ prajāyate |
kuṃbhakāragṛhe nyastaṃ bhāṇḍāni tu vināśayet || 21-51 ||
sauṇḍikasya gṛhe nyastaṃ madyanāśaṃ prasādhayet |
kṛṣṇāṣṭamīmupoṣitvā kṛṣṇā vātha caturdaśī || 21-52 ||
devyāyā mahatī pūjāṃ kṛtvā dakṣiṇata sthitaḥ |
śmaśānabhasma saṃgṛhya śatajaptaṃ tu kārayet || 21-53 ||
gṛhe yasya kṣipet taṃ tu jvaramutpādayed bhṛśam |
nā nyomuñcāpayet kaścit mukavā sādhaka puṃgavam ? || 21-54 ||
devyāyāścopahāraṃ ca datvā māṃsā'savānvitām |
kṛtvā pūjyāṃ suvistīrṇāṃ punarmocāpayet kṣaṇāt || 21-55 ||
citikāṣṭhai śmaśānāgniṃ prajvālya niśi madhyataḥ |
bhasmāsthirājikāguhyādgudhireṇasamanvitāḥ ? || 21-56 ||
aṣṭottaraśatenaiva saptāhānmārayed ripum |
rājā vā rājapatnī vā yadā mārayate tu saḥ || 21-57 ||
tadvāramṛttikāṃ gṛhya śmaśānāsthi ca bhasmani |
rājikāṃ ca tato home rudhireṇa samanvitām || 21-58 ||
śastraṃ nipātayecchatroḥ saptāṣṭakaśatena tu |
uṣitvā somasaṅgrāhe prajvālāgniṃ śmaśānajam || 21-59 ||
trimadhvāktāṃ tilāṃjuhyādavaśyaṃ grahaṇaṃ budhaḥ |
yasya nāmnā sa dūrepi sthito bhavati dāsavat || 21-60 ||
āgacchate samākṛṣṭo mriyate? viraheṇa tu |
śmaśānabhasmalavaṇaṃ rājikāpādamṛttikāḥ || 21-61 ||
tilacūrṇasamāyuktaiḥ pratimāṃ kārayed budhaḥ |
śmaśānāgni prajvālya trimadhvāktāṃ huned budhaḥ || 21-62 ||
tīkṣṇaśastreṇa cchitvā tu vāmāṅguṣṭhādi pūrvavat |
yasya nāmā savasastu bhavate nātra saṃśayaḥ || 21-63 ||
puṣpaṃ patraṃ phalaṃ toyaṃ tāmbūlaṃ snānalepanam |
dadyād yasyābhijaptaṃ tu sa vaśyod bhavate bhṛśam || 21-64 ||
śrotāñjana samādāyakapāle cābhimantritam |
tenākṣiṇyā japet prājñaḥ priyo bhavati sarvaśaḥ || 21-65 ||
śmaśānabhasma saṃgṛhya vidyāṣṭaśatamantritam |
tripuṇḍaṃ tena kurvīta sarvatra priyakārakam || 21-66 ||
kṣetre rājakule dyūte vyavahāre tathāhave |
sarvatra japamāpnoti caurāṃstambhayate pathe || 21-67 ||
bhasmanā tena sainyāni stambhayatyavicārataḥ |
prayānti bhagnadaryāstu bhagnadaṃṣṭrā yathoragāḥ || 21-68 ||
śmaśāne maṇḍalaṃ likhya sarvavarṇopaśobhitam |
140b g] tatra saṃpūjya deveśīṃ mahāpretakṛtāsanām || 21-69 ||
kapālakhaṭvāṅgadharaṃ sphuradvidyutsamaprabhām |
kapālamālābharaṇāṃ trinetrāṃ vikṛtānanām || 21-70 ||
prāguktena vidhānena pūjayitvā samāhitaḥ |
japedaṣṭasahasraṃ tu mahāmāṃsaṃ hunet tataḥ || 21-71 ||
yasya nāmaṃ samuccārya tamākarṣayate dhruvam |
udbandhapretamānīya snānaṃ caiva malaṃkṛtam || 21-72 ||
vaktre tasya hunet prājña paramānnaṃ ghṛtānvitam |

janapriyaśca bhavate vyomacārī mahābalaḥ || 21-75 ||
na hanyate gatistasya devadānavarākṣasaiḥ |

100b] udvaṃdhasya ca pañcāṅgaṃ gṛhya cūrṇaṃ tu kārayet || 21-76 ||

141a g] athānyāṃ saṃpravakṣyāmi vidyāṃ vai sādhanānvitām |

savanatrayaṃ vidhistho vidyāmāvartayeṣṭa śatamekam |
ānayati madanaviddhāṃ bhūpatiyantrīha saptāhāt || 21-324 ||
kaliyugmaśarvaripadaṃ hara hṛdayaṃ sādhanā mapādāghaḥ |
svāhāntā praṇavādyā pañcadaśārṇābhidhānapadarahitā || 21-325 ||
guḍaśālipiṣṭasahitāṃ ghṛtena caiva vimiśritāṃ pramadām |
pakavāṃ bhuktvā satataṃ saptāhā karṣayeṣṭaśatajaptvām || 21-326 ||
sevārcanavidhiryuktaḥ karṣayati varāṅganāṃ gūrāt |
147b g] etādvidhānamatulaṃ kṛtvā mīramatibhogaiḥ || 21-327 ||
iti sūcitaṃ vidhāṃ dvādaśa sāhasrikematavyāsam |
āryācchandasi racitaṃ jñātvā samyak phalāvāptim || 21-328 ||

iti bhairavasrotasi mahātantre vidyāpīṭhe saptakoṭipramāṇe śrītantrasadbhāve śrīvidyākośe viṃśatipaṭalaḥ || 21 ||


śrībhairava uvāca

athānyaṃ saṃpravakṣyāmi mantrāṇāṃ dīpanaṃ param |
hrā/ hrī/ hrū/ phaṭ-ṭa phṭṭe/ hū/ ca puṭīkṛtam || 22-1 ||
tatra madhyenasensādhya mantravidyāthavāyutam |
praharārddhena deveśi uccāṭaṃ kurute bhṛśam || 22-2 ||
hra/ strī/ strī/ ebhistu sahite mantraḥ sadyaḥ siddhipradāyakaḥ |
hau/ saṃpuṭayogena mantrāṇāṃ caiva dīpanam || 22-3 ||
hrī/ kro/ kāraprayogena ākarṣaṃ kurute dhruvam |
kliṃkārapuṭitaṃ mantrī strīkṣobhe tu niyojayet || 22-4 ||
kli/ kle/ kli/ puṭitaṃ nāma strīṇāṃ kṣobhaṇamuttamam |
kṣamityanena saṃyuktaścaturvargaphalodayaḥ || 22-5 ||
dharmārthakāmamokṣaṃ ca siddhyatyevaṃ na saṃśayaḥ |

105b] hrīmityanena saṃyukto sarve bhavati kāmadaḥ || 22-6 ||
sarveṣāṃ caiva satvānāṃ vallabho bhavate priye |
smvaṃ? sampuṭa yogona kāmikāsiddhimāpnuyāt || 22-7 ||
svāṃ sampuṭayogena āpyāyanakaraṃ mahat |
mā?yoge tu samuddiṣṭaṃ amṛtīkaraṇottamam || 22-8 ||
rakāratrayasaṃyuktaṃ vāyunā ca pradīpitam |
yojayenmālayā mantrī māraṇaṃ paramaṃ smṛtam || 22-9 ||
yakāratrayasaṃyuktā mālā uccāṭakārikā |
lakāratrayasaṃyuktā mā * staṃbhakarī smṛtā || 22-10 ||
sāṃpratamagnisaṃyogāt staṃbhanaṃ divyamālayā |
caturo maṇḍalāṃ likhya vajralāñchanalāñchitān || 22-11 ||
anyonyamadhyagā devi kārayecchubhalakṣaṇāṃ |
madhye samālikhed devi amṛtodaramadhyagām || 22-12 ||
devadattaṃ surairveṣṭā caturdikṣu vakārakam |
nyasenmaṇḍalamadhye tu vāhyaivaṃ tu śakārakam || 22-13 ||
tasyāpi bāhyato bhānurbhānośca bāhyato'nilaḥ |
kṣakāraṃ tasya bāhye tu anupāṭhyā samālikhet || 22-14 ||
vasabhayakṣāne naiva krameṇa krameṇa viniyojayet |
bhūrjapatre tu deveśi rocanākuṃkumena vā || 22-15 ||
likhya tvevaṃ prayatnena veṣṭayeta yathā vidhiḥ |
mṛnamayaṃ tu tato liṅgaṃ kārayeta suśobhanam || 22-16 ||
148a g] tatra madhye kṣipedbhūrjaṃ veṣṭitaṃ sūtritaṃ priye |
kṣīrapūrṇe tataḥ kumbhe prakṣiped vṛtta madhyataḥ || 22-17 ||
dīpaṃ ghṛtena prajvālya ghaṭasyaiva tatopari |
pūjayet kusumaiḥ śuklairgandhadhūpapavitrakaiḥ || 22-18 ||
uddhareddivyamevaṃ tu śītāṃ śumupajāyate |
sadoṣopi sumuhūrte iti śāstrasya niścayaḥ || 22-19 ||
vasabhalavakṣayavava hasvauṃ kṣmauṃ tathā likhet |
pūrvavat kṛtavinyāsaḥ pūrvavatmaṇḍalāni ca || 22-20 ||
anena vidhinā tṛrṇa pañcadivyāṃ samuddharet |
agnirddhṭaṃ tathā kośaṃ jala vai taptamāṃsakam || 22-21 ||
uddharennātra sandehaḥ prayogena anena tu |
abījāntagataṃ sādhyaṃ suraiḥ ṣoḍaśabhirvṛtam || 22-22 ||
varuṇālayamadhyasthaṃ nābhibījāntagaṃ punaḥ |
varuṇapuramadhyasthaṃ ? pañcadivya samuddharet || 22-23 ||
kṣāṃjabhakṣāṃ staṃbhe mohe mantreṇānena ? deveśi abhimantra pradāpayet | 
śālyodana dadhiyuktaṃ saptavārābhimantritam || 22-24 ||
piṇḍamekaṃ varārohe yasya caiva pradāpayet |
sadoṣopi samuttarte maitryamaitryavibhāgavit || 22-25 ||
harate hārate caiva nātra kāryā vicāraṇā |
yathā vahni mahādīpto varuṇena tu prāvitaḥ || 22-26 ||
yathā śītatvamāyāti śītamagnitvamāgataḥ |
yathā bhānuruciraughaṃ cchādyate ghanamālayā || 22-27 ||
tathā divyaṃ varārohe mantraśaktyāpyalīkṛte? |
arimitroktakaṃ jñātvā sarvakāryāṇi sādhayet || 22-28 ||
sugandha pāṭalāmūlāṃ jalūkāśaśitasya tu |
māṇḍūkavasayā yuktaṃ lepaṃ pāṇidvayasya tu || 22-29 ||
vahnistambhe praśasyeta tathānāṃ kathayāmi te |
sevālaṃ dārduraṃ stekaṃ lepāgni hananaṃ matam || 22-30 ||
vaṃjalālana sevālaṃ phaṭākhyā tu kumārikā |
raktavelivasālipto jvalanaṃ himaśītalam || 22-31 ||
athānyaṃ saṃpravakṣyāmi mantriṇāṃ krīḍanaṃ param |
nirdoṣo dahyate yena ? staṃbhito vānyamantriṇā || 22-32 ||
caturviṃśa likhedvījaṃ madhorddhaṃ vahnidīpitam |
pāvakasparasenaiva lekhyanyā havisaṃbhavā || 22-33 ||
prakṣipettadrasemantrī likhitaṃ mṛtakarpaṭe |

106a] dahate nātra sandeha staṃbhitaṃ vajrapāṇinā || 22-34 ||
vahnimaṇḍalamadhyasthamagnivāyusamanvitam |
sādhyanāmasamāyuktaṃ vahninā mūrddhni dīpitam || 22-35 ||
tadrasena likhenmantrī lekhanyā vahnisaṃbhavā |
pāvakasya rase kṣipya vakṣyādhastā nidhāpayet || 22-36 ||
148b g] vahniṃ prajvālayedūrddhvaṃ kāṣṭhairvahnisamudbhavaiḥ |
dahyate nātrasandeha staṃbhitaḥ śūlapāṇinā || 22-37 ||
evaṃ devi mayākhyātaṃ mantraiḥ krīḍā tu sādhakaiḥ |
mūlayonavā mantrī sarvakāmān vāpnuyāt || 22-38 ||

iti bhairavasrotasi mahātantre vidyāpīṭhe saptakoṭipramāṇe śrītantrasadbhāve mantradīpano dvāviṃśatipaṭalaḥ? || 22 ||



devyuvāca

śrutaṃ deva mayā sarva rahasyaṃ gūḍhagocaram |
mantrāṇāṃ ca vibhāgaṃ tu viśeṣādavadhāritam || 23-1 ||
na śrutaṃ śrotumicchāmi mūlayogaṃ vada prabho |
vaśyamākarṣaṇaṃ caiva vidveṣoccāṭanaṃ tathā || 23-2 ||
staṃbhanaṃ mohanaṃ caiva māraṇaṃ ca viśeṣataḥ |
nirviṣīkaraṇaṃ caiva āyudhānāṃ ca staṃbhanam || 23-3 ||
rasāyanāni cānyāni sadyomṛtyuharāṇi ca |
eva mādīni cānyāni yānyuktāni na te vada || 23-4 ||

bhairava uvāca

śṛṇu devi pravakṣyāmi yattvayā paripṛcchitam |
hitārthaṃ sādhakānāṃ tu viśeṣāt tad vadāmi te || 23-5 ||
ambilyāmbu prasūtānāmekatra kusumaṃ phalam |
dalamūlāṃgamekatra kuryāccūrṇaṃ samaṃ budhaḥ || 23-6 ||
śālicūrṇasamopetaṃ guḍājyapayasā saha |
bhakṣepakṣaṃ prayuñjīta tantu bhuktvā vaśo bhavet || 23-7 ||
bandā karmāmbalījāta striyā vā puruṣasya vā |
vaśamānayate danto na cānyaṃ sevate punaḥ || 23-8 ||
kṛṣṇamārjāradugdhena bhāvya kṛṣṇān tilāmbudhaḥ |
satkulyāstaistu saṃsiddhā piṣṭena tu samanvitāḥ || 23-9 ||
bhakṣe yasya pradīyante na jīvedviraheṇa saḥ |
sārasāṇḍaṃ samādāya gokṣīreṇa tu pīṣayet || 23-10 ||
pāyasaṃ taṇḍulaiḥ siddhaṃ khāne yasya pradīyate |
yāvadāyurbhaved vaśyo viyoge mṛtyumāpnuyāt || 23-11 ||
malaṃ madaṃ suraktaṃ ca māṃsaṃ gṛhya caṭodbhavam |
svaneccāmbu samāyuktaṃ yasyāḥ khānaṃ prayojayet || 23-12 ||
na cānyaṃ kāmayet sā tu vaśagā yāvadāyuṣam |
udvandvāyā kapālaṃ tu athavā puruṣasya tu || 23-13 ||
dhārāmṛtena saṃghṛṣya yāvantaṃ candanaṃ yathā |
yakṣīkṣīreṇa saṃbhāvya dharmaṇasya vasā tathā || 23-14 ||
149a g] markaṭaśiracūrṇena adhapuṣpasamena tu |
avaśyopi bhaved vaśyo nirvikalpo yadā bhavet || 23-15 ||
unmattakaṃ tu saṃgṛhya putrājjārī tu mohanā | 
saha devīśikhā caiva pīṣayitvā vidhānataḥ || 23-16 ||
yakṣī kṣīrasamaṃ kṛtvā vaśyārthe caiva siddhidam |
bhakṣayitvā tilaiḥ kāryaṃ tailaṃ śaṣkulikāpi vā || 23-17 ||
taṃ bhuktvā vaśamāyāti yāvajjīvaṃ na saṃśayaḥ |
mohanā sahadevā ca bhūdhātrī ca kulāñcchatā || 23-18 ||
rāmavelā sahaikatra ātmabījena pīṣayet |
bhakṣe pāne pradātavyaṃ vaśīkaraṇamuttamam || 23-19 ||
uttarvāruṇimūlañca puṣpārkeṇa tu gṛhṇayet |
ātmendriyasamāyuktaṃ vaśīkaraṇamuttamam || 23-20 ||
śravaṇākṣimalaṃ lālārudhirendriyasaṃyutam |
bhūkadambasamāyuktaṃ dātavyaṃ payasā niśi || 23-21 ||

106b] aprakāśe pradātavyaṃ mriyate viraheṇa vā |
ṣaṣṭhiṃ kanakabījāni ṣoḍaśaṃ maṇicandrikā || 23-22 ||
naragodantasaṃyuktāṃ pradadyāyāsya bhāmini |
eṣa kāpāliko yogo gacchantamanugacchati || 23-23 ||
śvetārkamūlamaṃjiṣṭhāccadakasya śirastathā |
gṛhodbhavasya kuṣṭhaṃ ca suraktendriyasaṃyutam || 23-24 ||
bhakṣe pāne pradātavyaṃ vaśīkaraṇamuttamam |
mohanā caiva kāntā ca mayūraśikhayā saha || 23-25 ||
ātmendriyasamāyuktaṃ vaśīkaraṇamuttamam |
lajjālukā ca gorambhā caṇḍālīkandakaṃ tathā || 23-27 ||
nāgendramadamiśrāṇi ātmaretasamāni tu |
eṣa yogavaraṃ divyaṃ yasya dīyati suvrate || 23-28 ||
madhureṇa samāyuktaṃ yāvadāyu vaśībhavet |
phalamaunmattakaṃ kuṣṭaṃ tagaraṃ pakṣaṣaṭpadam || 23-29 ||
dīpadānasamāyuktaṃ bhūtāyāṃ tu vaśaṃkaram |
gorocanā ca govandātamālaṃvyādhi sarpiṣī || 23-30 ||
kāyo gīrṇasamāyuktaṃ nirddhanenaiva dāpayet |
pārāvatapurīṣañca matsyapittasvaretayuk || 23-31 ||
etadbhakṣe padattaṃ ca vaśīkaraṇamuttamam |
dantakāṣṭhaṃ karañjotthaṃ prātaścotthāya bhakṣayet || 23-32 ||
saptarātraṃ tu gṛhṇīyājjihvākledaṃ tathaiva ca | 
bhāvitaṃ saindhavenedaṃ dīyate yasya kasyacit || 23-33 ||
bhuñjīta svāyuṣāṃ yāvanmṛtasyāpyanugacchati |
bhramaraṃ kākajihvā ca kuṣṭaṃ ca tagarānvitam || 23-34 ||
utpalena samāyuktaṃ cūrṇedaṃ maraṇāntikam |
sahā ca sahadevā ca putrānnārīkṛtāñjalī || 23-35 ||
149b g] retaraktasamāyuktaṃ naranārīvaśaṃkaram | 
sadyojātasya vaśyasya viṣṭiṃ gṛhṇa varānane || 23-36 || 
rudhirendriyasamāyuktaṃ pānametad vaśaṃkaram |
mohanā sahadevā ca bhūdhātrī ca kulāñchanām || 23-37 ||
pīṣayedātmaraktena surāyā pānamuttamam |
caṇḍalī caṇḍakaṃ gṛhya ślakṣṇā?tpūrṇaṃ tu kārayet || 23-38 ||
gajendramadasaṃyuktāṃ śīdhupāne vaśaṃkaram |
putrāṃjārī śiphāṃ gṛhya suraktendriyasaṃyutām || 23-39 ||

tulyabhāgayutāṃ kṛtvā dātavyaṃ nayanāmbunā |
pānaṃ prasannayā yuktaṃ naranārīvaśaṃkaram || 23-41 ||
putraṃjārī ca muśalī kolla markaṭikā tathā |
retaraktasupiṣṭāni kṛtvā pānaṃ pradāpayet || 23-42 ||
etadvāsakaraṃ proktaṃ madanonmadanaṃ tathā |
bhūkadambaṃ śiphāṃ gṛhya tathā raktā'parājitā || 23-43 ||
sahadevī mahānīlā pīṣayīta prayatnataḥ |
pānametadvaśaṃ kṣipraṃ ānayetpuṃ valāpi vā || 23-44 ||
cūtavandākakāṣṭhaṃ ca kolamarkaṭikā tathā |
gajendramadasaṃmiśraṃ pānaṃ sarvavaśaṃkaram || 23-45 ||
sūryavartiśiphācorddhvaṃ aśokakusumaṃ tathā |
kārayetsūkṣma * * * * * ? raktena tu bhāvitam || 23-46 ||
anena cūrṇayennārīmastake sādhakottamaḥ |
vaśagā tasya śātyanta abdhamātraṃ bhaviṣyati || 23-47 ||
aśokakākaviṣṭena retaraktayutena tu |
cūrṇitā vaśamāyāti bhūtārthaṃ duhitāpi vā || 23-48 ||
nīlāśokasrajaṃ gṛhya nīlamutpalameva ca |
priyaṃgunāgapuṣpaṃ ca svaraktendriyabhāvitam || 23-49 ||
cūrṇitā mastake nārī gacchantamanugacchati |

107a] śvā jihvā hṛdayaṃ caiva gṛhyalakṣasamudbhavam || 23-50 ||
bhumareṇukācaiva sindūrarajasaṃyutam |
cūrṇitā mastake nārī viddhā kāmaśarairiva || 23-51 ||
rohitalavaṃga? pittena narapittaṃ tu bhāvayet |

150a g] saṃgṛhya siddhalavaṇaṃ bhāgāṃ cakrāṃ tu kārayet |

putraṃnnārīsamāyuktaṃ lavaṇaṃ bhuvanāṃkuśa |

ekaviṃśatirātraṃ nikhanet taṃ catuṣpathe |

dine dine tato gatvā ekaikaṃ puṣpakaṃ kṣipet |
gṛhyet taṃ tu vidhānena valiṃ datvā vicakṣaṇaḥ || 23-62 || 
pūrvāṃ diśimāsthāya śivāmbhena tu pīṣayet |

rudracūlikasaṃyuktaṃ karavīrārkajaiḥ saha |
uragekṣaṇasaṃyuktaṃ samabhāgāni kārayet || 23-64 ||

yojitaṃ khānapānena yāvajjīvaṃ vaśaṃ nayet |

viparyastena mantreṇa japtvā cāṣṭottareṇa tu |

añjayitvā tu nayanau tato gacchenmahāhavam || 23-72 ||

107b] kapāle kajjalaṃ gṛhyet puṣpelaktakavartinā || 23-77 ||

mṛgāsṛgvartibhāvitvā varāhavasayāñjanam || 23-79 ||

trirātraṃ tu sthitaṃ gṛhya sthāpayet tat tu mantritam |

tenāṃjitākṣo yaṃ paśyed vihvalaḥ patate bhuvi |

157b g] etena piṣṭvā cakṣūṇāṃ sadya unmādakārikāḥ |
trirātroṣita puṣpeṇa śastrāhatasya śūle protasya vā puṃsaḥ śiromṛttikā pūryaguṃjāvāpyodanena secayenāṃ tāmasāvāsyāṃ pūrṇamāsyāṃ vā puṣpayogena guṃjāvalī grāhayitvā maṇḍalikāṃ kārayet teṣvana pānabhojanāni nyastāni vyayamānāni na kṣīyanto |

daśadoṣavinirmuktaṃ vaṅge bhavati nānyathā |
andhāsthi * * * cūrṇī kṛtvā jyotiṣmatī tailena * * * * * * * varṇa bhāgadvayaśaśāṅka? bhāgaikaṃ manaḥśilā bhāgatrayam |
ekī kṛtyonta vāruṇīrasena bhāvitam |
ekaviṃśati vārambhāvayet tena śuṃlvasya? kathaṃ niṣekaṃ uttara vāruṇīrasena dvisaptavārāṃ kṛtvā bhavati kālikā bhāvavarjitaḥ |
asyaśulvasyabhāgatraya ravibhāṣasya catvāri hemasya dhāmaikamekīkṛtya pacet tato jātarūpaṃ bhavati |
rasalāṅgalīkā kāntā ṛṣikāntiphaleti ca? |
eka dvi tri caturaṣṭa pañcai tatsaṃgraha kramāt || 23-321 ||
ghṛte dve? madhu taṃ caika ekatra sarvamardayet |

ṣaṇmāsa?dravayogena jīvaye?ccandratārakam |

iti bhairavasrotasi mahātantre vidyāpīṭhe saptakoṭipramāṇe śrītantrasadbhāve pramada? yogādhikāre trayoviṃśaḥ paṭalaḥ || 23 ||


devyuvāca

* *?ṣu devadeveśa kathitaṃ jñānamuttamam |
adyāpi saṃśayo nātha kālajñānapratidhruvā || 24-1 ||

bhairava uvāca

kalpādvidhā tu vijñeyaḥ saura madhyātmiko paraḥ |
vārādi tithinakṣatraṃ sayogakaraṇaiḥ priye || 24-2 ||
ī o ya ca? yadā syāyā? dakṣiṇe yantramuttamam |
grahaṇaṃ candrasūryābhyāṃ lagnādi ṛtubhiryathā || 24-3 ||
pakṣamāsaṃ ca velādyairvisūryāsyantarādibhiḥ |
nābhya dho meḍhrakaṃ corddhve sthito vai nābhimadhyataḥ || 24-4 ||
tasmād vinirgatāmadye? adhorddhvaṃ tiryagāḥ priye |
cakravatsaṃsthitāstatra pradhānā daśa nāḍayaḥ || 24-5 ||
dvāsaptatisahasrāṇi nābhenādmo? vinirgatāḥ |
punarviniḥ sṛtāścānyāstābhyo asyā punaḥ punaḥ || 24-6 ||
yāvantyo romakoṭyastu tāvantyo nāḍayaḥ smṛtāḥ |
yathāparṇaṃ palāśasya vyāptaṃ sarvatratantubhiḥ || 24-7 ||
śarīraṃ sarvajantūnāṃ tad vad vyāptaṃ tunāḍibhiḥ |
marutā pūritāḥ sarvā ātmāśaktibharāḥ sadā || 24-8 ||
pṛthaṅmūrtiprabhedena bhinnā? vāta? prabhedataḥ |
cāṭuvṛttiprabhedena saṃjñābhedo varānane || 24-9 ||
nāḍīnāṃ vāyavānāṃ tu bhedā jñeyā sahasraśaḥ |
pradhānāstā daśa proktā nāḍayaśca varānane || 24-10 ||
tāsāṃ madhye tu deveśe vāyavo ye vyavasthitaḥ |
nāḍīnāṃ vāyavānāṃ tu saṃjñāvṛttiḥ puroditāḥ || 24-11 ||
vāyavo nāḍayaścaiva cakravatsaṃsthitāḥ priye |
vāusañcarataḥ siddhiryāgaṃ caiva varānane || 24-12 ||
etāḥ prāṇavahā proktāḥ pradhānā daśa nāḍayaḥ |
daśayāntu? paraṃ devi nāḍī trayamudāhṛtam || 24-13 ||
vindunādātmake dve tu madhye śaktyātmikā smṛtāḥ |
hṛccakretāḥ samākhyātāḥ sādhakānāṃ hitāvahā || 24-14 ||
prāṇo vai carate teṣu ahorātravibhāgataḥ |

115b] tathā te kathayiṣyāmi pravibhaktyā yathā sphuṭam || 24-15 ||
prabhuśaktisamākṛṣṇaṃ marutprāṇātma saṃsthitam |
trayaite hyavibhāgena saṃcaranti samaṃtataḥ || 24-16 ||
adhorddhvaṃ prāpayed yasmāt sarvanāḍyaḥ pravāhayet |
vṛttisaṃjñāprabhedena varṇarūpāṇyanekadhā ? || 24-17 ||
dvāsaptatisahasrāṇi jāyante daśabhiḥ priye |
koṭidhā ca varārohe sa ekaḥ saṃvyavasthitāḥ || 24-18 ||
prāṇāprāṇa mayaḥ prāṇo visargāpūraṇaṃ prati |
nityamāpūrayatyeṣā tasmātprāṇaḥ prakīrtitaḥ ? || 24-19 ||
ahorātragatiḥ prāṇe adhunā kathayāmi te |
udayaḥ ṣoḍaśaprāṇe pūrva hi kathitaṃ mayā || 24-20 ||
bāhyenaiva tu kālena telavāḥ parikīrtitāḥ |
taiścaturbhistu deveśi prāṇoryāmo vidhīyate ? || 24-21 ||
taireva praharairdevi caturbhistu dinaṃ bhavet ? |
rātriścaturbhivijñeyāṃ ahorātraṃ tathā'ṣṭabhiḥ || 24-22 ||
śive dharmeṇa haṃsastu sūryo haṃsaprabhānvitāḥ ? |
ātmā vai haṃsa ityuktaṃ prāṇo haṃsaṃsamanvitaḥ || 24-23 ||

162a g] tasyodayātkaletkāyā grahāṇāmudayo bhavet ? |
rikhā vai rāśayaścaiva tārāstvaṃsāstathaiva ca || 24-24 ||
prāṇe caivodayantyete ahorātreṇa suvrate |
ahorātrodayasyaiva vibhāgaṃ kathayāmi te || 24-25 ||
hṛdayorddhve tu kaṇṭhādho yāvad vai pravahet priye |
aṅgulena vihīnaṃ ca praharaḥ prathama smṛtaḥ || 24-26 ||
dvitīyorddhve tu vijñeyo madhyāhna tālumadhyataḥ |
taṃ tu homaṃ japaṃ dhyānaṃ kṛtaṃ vai mokṣadaṃ bhavet || 24-27 ||
nāsāgrāt ryaṅgulorddhve tu yāvatprāptastu suvrate |
praharastu tṛtīyo vai bhavate varavarṇini || 24-28 ||
śaktyante tu caturthaṃ ca praharo'haḥ prakīrtitam |
caturthānte ca deveśi prāṇasūryo sa cāstame || 24-29 ||
tato'stamana sandhyā tu truṭyarddhaṃ tu bhavet priye |
tatkāle tu vilamvitvā punaścādhaḥ pravartate || 24-30 ||
sa ca candrodaye devi rajanī ca vidhīyate |
pūrvoktakramayogena yāmeṣu carate tathā || 24-31 ||
tāluke cārddha rātraṃ tu punareva vidhīyate |
hṛtpadme tu yadāprāptaḥ prabhātasamayaṃ tathā || 24-32 ||
tadarddhaṃ tu varārohe pūrvaṃ sandhyā bhavenna tu |
tasmāccaiva tu udayaṃ sūryasya bhavate punaḥ || 24-33 ||
pūrvavatkramayogena te caranti sadā śubhe |
vāsare tu caretsūryo dhārāyāṃ carate śaśī || 24-34 ||
candrasūryo dvayo hyeṣa mayā te parikīrtitaḥ |
bhaumādyāstu grahāpyevaṃ caranti pravibhāgaśaḥ || 24-35 ||
prāṇevaivodayaṃ tyete prahare prahare priye |
velā vāro bhaved yasya sañcaret praharadvayam || 24-36 ||
rāhu carati some tu ketuścarati bhāskare |
śaniśca kulike caiva grahāṇāmudaya smṛtaḥ || 24-37 ||
ye grahāsteṣva vai bhāgā lokapālāṣṭakaṃ ca tat |
mūrtayaścaiva te cāṣṭāvaṣṭau netragaṇeśvarāḥ || 24-38 ||
te ca pañcāśato rudrāstathā pañcāṣṭakeśvarāḥ |
anantādi śikhadhyāntametad vidyeśvarāṣṭakam || 24-39 ||
sakulādyāstathā tatvāste vai paratarā sthitāḥ |
pūrvoktā bhairavācāṣṭau te sarveṣu vyavasthitāḥ || 24-40 ||
grahādīni tvadhiṣṭhāya sarveṣūdayakārakāḥ |
rāśibhiḥ saha nakṣatraista hyudanti tvaharniśi || 24-41 ||
madhyāhne ca dvirātre ca abhitostūdayo bhavet |
abhīpsitaphalaṃ tatra sādhakānāṃ bhavediha || 24-42 ||
ahorātra vibhāgaṃ ca evaṃ te kathitaṃ mayā |
162b g] adhunā pakṣa māsā ca varṣāṇi kathayāmi te || 24-43 ||

116a] adhyātmenā'horātreṇa vāhyekāsā? vidhīyate |
māsenādhyātmikā naiva bāhye caikā kalā bhavet || 24-44 ||
te triṃśadahorātrāṇāṃ māsena varavarṇini |
māsairdvādaśabhiścaiva bāhye tu ghaṭikā bhavet || 24-45 ||
ahorātrāṃśataṃtīśa ṣaṣṭiścaiva tathādhikā |
varṣametat samākhyātaṃ bāhye vai ghaṭikātmikam || 24-46 ||
ghaṭikāṣaṣṭhistvahorātre bāhye tu pravadanti ca |
tairevāntaracāreṇa ṣaṣṭhisamvatsarā smṛtāḥ || 24-47 ||
prāṇasaṃkhyā punastvetā adhunā kathayāmi te |
sahasrāstvekaviṃśaṃ tu ṣaṭśatāni varānane || 24-48 ||
ahorātreṇa bāhyena ādhyātme tu surādhipe |
prāṇasaṃkhyā samākhyātā jñātavyā sādhakena tu || 24-49 ||
prāṇo haṃse yadā līnaḥ sādhakaḥ paratatvavit |
tasyedaṃ japamuddiṣṭaṃ siddhimuktiphalāvaham || 24-50 ||
adhaḥ pravahataḥ siddhi hṛtpadme yāvadāgataḥ |
muktiścaiva bhavedūrddhve paratatve ca suvrate || 24-51 ||
manāpyatyatra nikṣiptaṃ cakṣuranyatra patitam |
tathā pravartate prāṇo ayaṃtā deva sarvadā || 24-52 ||
tannoccārayitvā kaścit pratihantā na caiva hi |
svayamucchvasate haṃsaḥ prāṇināmurasi sthitaḥ || 24-53 ||
māsasaṃvatsarasaṃkhyā eṣā te samudāhṛtāḥ |
candrasūryoparāgaṃ ca kathayāmi tvataḥ śṛṇu || 24-54 ||
ahorātraṃ ca yat proktaṃ prāṇe'smin surasundari |
tadetat pakṣadvayena māsaṃ ca kathayāmi te || 24-55 ||
tudūrddhaṃ? tu tataścorddhve viśrāmaḥ parikīritaḥ |
madhye pañcadaśa proktā stiyastāḥ prakīrtitāḥ || 24-56 ||
padmodaye hṛtpadmā tu tadyarddhaṃ? tu dinaṃ bhavet |
dvitīye caiva trutyarddhe yadā carati śarvarī || 24-57 ||
rāśayo grahanakṣatrāścodayanti yathākramam |
asminnaivā tvahorātre pūrvavacca varānane || 24-58 ||
truṭiḥ pañcadaśairevaṃ pakṣaṃ tu savidhīyate |
tithicchede ṛṇaṃ jñeyaṃ vṛddhau caiva dhanaṃ bhavet || 24-59 ||
ṛṇaśca bhavate kāśo niśvāso dhanamucyate |
kṛṣṇapakṣorddhvacāreṇa saṃhāraḥ pakṣayorbhavet || 24-60 ||
krūrakarmāṇi vai taṃtu kurvantaḥ siddhimāpnuyāt |
śubhakarmāṇi kṛṣṇena na ca sidhyanti suvrate || 24-61 ||
śaktirvai viṃśataḥ prāṇan yā truṭistu vidhīyate |
163a g] amāvāsyāṃ tu sā jñeyā kṛṣṇapakṣe varānane || 24-62 ||
śaktimadhyorddhvabhāge tu truṭyarddhaṃ yat prakīrtitam |
pakṣasandhistu sā jñeyā amāvāsyā'rddhapratipadā || 24-63 ||
tithicchedena vai tatra sūryasya grahaṇaṃ bhavet |
raverbimvāntare devi candra vimvaṃ tadā bhavet || 24-64 ||
tadantare bhaved rāhuramṛtārtho varānane |
amṛtaṃ sravate candro rāhuśca grasane tu tam || 24-65 ||
tatpītvā tyajate viṃvaṃ tadā muktaḥ sa ucyate |
āditya grahaṇaṃ caiva loke ca tadudiśyate || 24-66 ||
rāhurādityacandraśca trayaścaiva yadā grahāḥ |
dṛśyanti samavāyena tanmahāgrahaṇaṃ bhavet || 24-67 ||
tatkālaṃ sarvalokānāṃ mahāpuṇyatamaṃ bhavet |
tatra snānaṃ tathā dhyānaṃ pūjāhomajapādikam || 24-68 ||
yat kṛtaṃ sādhakairdevi tadanantaphalaṃ bhavet |
sa caivārddhaṃ truṭiṃ tyaktvā śuklapakṣād vayo bhavet || 24-69 ||
śaktirgarbhādadhaḥ sṛṣṭistasmād vṛddhi prajāyate |

dhyānamātrābhiyuktasya siddhyante nātra saṃśayaḥ |
prāṇahaṃso yadā prāpto adhastāt prathamā tuṭiḥ || 24-71 ||

116b] pūrvārddhaṃ ca ahaḥ proktaṃ tuṭyarddhamaparaṃ niśi |

pakṣadvayaṃparityajya pūrvoktakaraṇena tu || 24-80 ||

163b g] parityajyā tvadhaḥ sarvaṃ dhyānaṃ saṃsthāpya yojayet || 24-81 ||
