dvādaśaite tvahorātrā dvādaśābde bhavanti vai || 24-119 ||
pañcabhirdvādaśāhanya dvādaśābde ṛturbhavet |
tadeva dviguṇitaṃ kṛtvā kālastu saṃvidhīyate || 24-120 ||
triguṇena bhavedayanaṃ vatsaraṃ ṣaḍguṇena tu |
dvādaśaiva tu saṅkrāntyo * yabdo parikīrtitāḥ || 24-121 ||
dvādaśābdodaye prāṇevatsarāste prakīrtitāḥ |
164b g] dvādaśābde tvahorātrāsteṣāṃ saṅkhyā nibodha me || 24-122 ||
sahasrāścaiva catvāri triṃśatyā viṃśayādhikāḥ |
dvādaśābdodayaṃ devi prāṇesmin kathitaṃ mayā || 24-123 ||
ṣaṣṭhicābdodayo traiva punaśca kathayāmi te |
ānandādyāstu te jñeyā ṣaṣṭyābdātha varānane || 24-124 ||
svānanda prabhṛtā devi mantramārādhaye trayaḥ |
tasmānandaṃ sadā devi mantreṇa saha jāyate || 24-125 ||
te caivādhorddhvage prāṇe ekasmin surasundari |
carante pratibhāgena tathā te kthayāmyaham || 24-126 ||
dvādaśābde tvahorātrā pañcadhā guṇayet tu te |
ṣaṣṭyāvdena tvahorātrāḥ pañcaiva parikīrtitāḥ || 24-127 ||
te ca vai saḍguṇitvā tu māsamekaṃ prakīrtitam |

117b] dvādaśaiśca guṇontaṃ? vai varṣamevaṃ vidhīyate || 24-128 ||
aṅgulāni sapañcāśat mānametat prakīrtitam |
ṣaḍaṅgulaiśca pañcābdāḥ ṣaṣṭyāvde udayānti te || 24-129 ||
hṛtpadmādyāvaśaktyūrddhvaṃ triṃśadavdā udanti hi |
śaktyadho yāvat hṛtpadmaṃ punāstriṃśodayo bhavet || 24-130 ||
ṣaṣṭyābde ye tvahorātrāḥ saṅkhyā teṣāṃ vadāmyaham |
viṃśatiśca sahasrāṇi sahasraṃ ṣaṭśatādhikam || 24-131 ||
ahorātrāṇāṃ saṅkhyeyā ṣaṣṭyābde tu varānane |
ṣaṣṭirabdodayaḥ khyātaḥ prāṇaikasmin mayā tava || 24-132 || 
candrasūryoparāge ca pakṣamāsāyane'bdake |
varṣairdvādaśabhiścaiva ṣaṣṭyabde tu varānane || 24-133 ||
snānadānena yajñena pūjāhomajapena ca |
dhyānayogatapenaiva bāhyakālena yat kṛtam || 24-134 ||
amunokte varārohe yatphalaṃ labhate mahat |
prāṇahaṃsagatiścaiva jñātvaikasmiṃstu tadbhavet || 24-135 ||
svasamvedyaṃ bhaveccāraṃ nāḍī prāṇajapāt sphuṭam |
athavā tu japād devi prakṛtīva upavṛṃhitaḥ || 24-136 ||
yogaṃ mantrī vijātāni? jñātvā sarvajñatāṃ vrajet |
punareva pravakṣyāmi nāḍītraya vibhāgaśaḥ || 24-137 ||
dakṣiṇottarasaṅkrāntau viṣuveṇa ca suvrate |
yathāsau carate haṃso jagatyasmiṃścarācare || 24-138 ||
antasthaḥ kālarūpeṇa kalābhiḥ kalate jagat |
nāḍītrayakṛtādhāro mārgatrayamavasthitaḥ || 24-139 ||
guṇatrayasamāviṣṭastriṣvavasthāsvavasthitaḥ |
kāraṇaiḥ ṣaḍbhirākrāntaḥ śaktitrayamadhiṣṭhitaḥ || 24-140 ||
icchā-jñāna-kriyāviddhaḥ somasūryāgnimadhyagaḥ |
165a g] dakṣiṇāsāpuṭe caiva nāḍī vai piṅgalā smṛtāḥ || 24-141 ||
iḍā caiva tu vāmena suṣumṇā madhya tasthitāḥ |
dakṣiṇe devamārgaṃ tu pitṛmārgaṃ tathottare || 24-142 ||
madhyama śivamārgaṃ tu tatra gatvā na jāyate |
dakṣiṇe satvajāgrasthaḥ svapnastho rajavāmagaḥ || 24-143 ||
madhye tamastu vijñeyaḥ suṣumṇāvastha vṛttidaḥ |
brahma īśastu dakṣastho vāme viṣṇuḥ sadāśivaḥ || 24-144 ||
madhye rudraśivaḥ proktaḥ sarvatītaḥ paraḥ śivaḥ |
jyeṣṭhā jñātā ca dakṣasthā kriyā vāmā tathottare || 24-145 ||
raudracecchā tu madhyastho parā śaktiṃ parāparā |
dakṣiṇasthastapetsūryo vāme somo virājate || 24-146 ||
pacetprakāśakaścaiva madhyasthaścaiva pāvakaḥ |
paṃcayet sarvapāpāni anādiguṇasambhavā || 24-147 ||
prakāśayet svasāmarthyāt pare tatvamanāmayam |
rāśayaśca grahāḥ sarve ṛkṣayogāda * * * ? || 24-148 ||
candrasūryapathā sarve caranti anupūrvaśaḥ |
somasūryopi te sarve bhuñjanti kramaśaḥ priye || 24-149 ||
somasūryātma kāste vai pathatrayavyavasthitāḥ |
vāryate tapate sūryaḥ? somovarṣīti cāmṛtam || 24-150 ||
somasūryātmakaṃ tasyājjagat sthāvarajaṅgamam |
śauraṃ dakṣiṇamārgaṃ tu uttarāyaṇasaṃjñitam || 24-151 ||
vāmaṃ somaṃ tu yatproktaṃ tatra vai dakṣiṇāyanam |
somasūryātmakaṃ viṣuvaṃ puṭadvayavinisṛtam || 24-152 ||
uttare pañca saṅkrāntyaḥ pañca vai dakṣiṇāyane |
dakṣiṇottara viṣuvesaṃkrānti dvayamadhyataḥ || 24-153 ||
śauraṃ ca dakṣiṇaṃ mārgaṃ abhicāre prasiddhidam |
amṛtāpyāyanaṃ puṣṭiḥ śāntidaṃ saumyaṃ cottaram || 24-154 ||
dakṣiṇācottaraṃ yāti uttarā dakṣiṇaṃ yadā |
dakṣiṇottarasaṃkrānti sā caivaṃ tu vidhīyate ||155 ||

118a] dakṣiṇāyāṃ tu nāḍyāyāṃ saṅkrāmaṃ tasya cottare |
yāvadūrddhaṃ tu tatrastha marddhenottarato vahet || 24-156 ||
tāvattaṃ viṣuvaṃ proktaṃ uttaraṃ cottarāyaṇe |
uttare dakṣiṇāyāṃ tu kramaṃ tasya varānane || 24-157 ||
yāmārddhaṃ vahate tatra arddhaṃ vai dakṣiṇe vahet |
tāvad dakṣiṇaṃ viṣuvaṃ dakṣiṇāyanajaṃ priye || 24-158 ||
tatra pūjā japaṃ homaṃ yajanaṃ? muktidaṃ bhavet |
dhyānayogena dīkṣāyāṃ tatstho vai mocayedgurum || 24-159 ||
vāhe caiva ahorātre ādhyātmena varānane |
caturviṃśati saṅkrāntyaḥ prāṇahaṃsastu saṅkramet || 24-160 ||
āhastu dvādaśa proktā rajanyāṃ dvādaśa smṛtāḥ |
165b g] pūrvāhne viṣuvaṃ hyekaṃ madhyāhne tu dvitīyakam || 24-161 ||
tṛtīyaṃ cāparāhṇe tuṃ arddharātre caturthakam |
catvāro viṣuvāḥ proktā ahorātreṇa suvrate || 24-162 ||
caturviṃśati saṅkrāntyaḥ samadhāto svabhāvataḥ |
navaśatāni vai haṃsa ekaikāyāṃ vahet sadā || 24-163 ||
etanmānaṃ samākhyātaṃ anyathā vahate yadā |
iṣṭaṃ caiva tathāni.ṣṭaṃ tadā saṃsūcayet tu saḥ || 24-164 ||
ātmārthaṃ vā parārthaṃ vā tasmād yogī nirūpayet |
pūrvodaye tu samprāpte bhāskarasya varānane || 24-165 ||
jīvitaṃ maraṇaṃ caiva tamārabhya vicārayet |
susaṃyatamano yogī dhīro yogāsane sthitaḥ || 24-166 ||
smaramāṇotmajaṃ prāṇasuṣumṇāntarbhavaṃ priye |
suprasannastadā tiṣṭhet prāṇaikagatamānasaḥ || 24-167 ||
pañcādipañcaviṃśāntaṃ varddhate pañcasaṅkhyayā |
paścādekaikasaṅkhyā tu varddhate tu dinād dinam || 24-168 ||
yāvanniruttaraṃ devi daśakaṃ triguṇīkṛtam |
guṇapādau śaśi arcanti? dvekamāsasaṅkhyayāḥ || 24-169 ||
pūrvādiśā sarātrīṇi dve śaśi jīvate ahāḥ |
prārabhed bhāskare kāle pūrvasaṃkhyā vijānataḥ || 24-170 ||
dinā pañca yadānāḍī vahate dakṣiṇāyane |
jīvitaṃ tu tadā jñeyaṃ samāni trīṇi suvrate || 24-171 || 
daśairvā tu dvayaṃ jñeyaṃ tripañcairvatsaraṃ smṛtam |
viṃśatiśca tathā māsā rasasaṅkhyā varānane || 24-172 ||
pañcaviṃśaddinairdeviguṇamānena jīvati |
yugma indu ime māsādinasaṃkhyā mataḥ param || 24-173 ||
tithināḍī iṣū caiva kālapādau tathā śaśiḥ |
evaṃ krameṇa deveśi prāṇasaṃkhyā vijānatā || 24-174 ||
antare ye dinā proktāḥ ṣoḍaśaite varānane |
bhāgakrameṇa te śodhyā dvāsaptatimanukramāt || 24-175 ||
ṣaṭtriṃśāṣṭadaśaṃ caiva navabhistu yathākramam |
jñātvā caiva varārohe apahṛtya śanaiḥ śanaiḥ || 24-176 ||
śeṣaṃ tu yad bhavet piṇḍaṃ jñātavyaṃ jīvitasya tu |
pramāṇaṃ dinasaṅkhyāyācakre kālātmake priye || 24-177 ||
ṣaḍ dināni yadā vyūhā ekoccāreṇa suvrate |
varṣatrayaṃ tu saṅguṇyaṃ triśataiṣaṣṭyadhikaiḥ priye || 24-178 ||
bhāgaśeṣapramāṇaṃ tu kathayāmi samāsataṃ? |
varṣadvayaṃ samuddiṣṭaṃ māsāśca rudrasaṅkhyayā || 24-179 ||
166a g] dināni daśa pañcaiva pramāṇaṃ kathitaṃ tava |
saptame'hani antyajya yadā vahati mārutaḥ || 24-180 ||
varṣadvayaṃ samākhyātaṃ sāmānyekā daśe priye |
jīvitaṃ tu tadākhyātaṃ aṣṭamaṃ tu ataḥ param || 24-181 ||
varṣadvayaṃ tathā māsā daśasaṅkhyā tu jīvitam |
na ca devi dinairvyūḍhā varṣadvayaṃ sa jīvati || 24-182 ||
māsāni tu tathā cāṣṭau pramāṇaṃ kālavedinām |
dinānāṃ daśabhirdevi varṣadvayaṃ sa jīvati || 24-183 ||

118b] dinānyekādaśaṃ caiva yadā nāḍī pravartate |
varṣamekaṃ samākhyātaṃ māsāni rudrasaṅkhyayā || 24-184 ||
dināni viṃśati proktā kathitaṃ parameśvari |
dvādaśāhapravāhena varṣamekaṃ prakīrtitam || 24-185 ||
māsāni rudrasaṅkhyoktā dināni daśa śobhane |
daśa trīṇi yadā devi vahate dakṣiṇāyane || 24-186 ||
varṣamekaṃ sa jīveta māsā caiva daśa priye |
dināni viṃśati khyātā kathitaṃ tu tvayā'naghe || 24-187 ||
dināṃ caiva yadācāro dvisaptaparisaṅkhyayā |
varṣāyuṣaḥ samuddiṣṭo māsāni navasaṅkhyayā || 24-188 ||
dināni ca daśaścaiva pramāṇaṃ jīvatasya tu |
tripañcadinacāreṇa varṣamekaṃ na saṃśayaḥ || 24-189 ||
dviraṣṭakasya cāreṇa dinānāṃ parameśvari |
jīvitaṃ rudramānena māsāni parisaṅkhyayā || 24-190 ||
pañcaviṃśaddinānisyuḥ pramāṇaṃ kathitaṃ mayā |
dināni daśa saptaiva yadā vahati mārutaḥ || 24-191 ||
tadā tasya pramāṇaṃ tu sāmānyekādaśa priye |
dināni viṃśatiścaiva kathitaṃ tu na saṃśayaḥ || 24-192 |
daśāṣṭadinacāreṇa māsānyekādaśapriye |
dināni daśasaṅkhyā tu kathitaṃ kālavedinām || 24-193 ||
viṃśadekonato cāre dinānāṃ parameśvari |
jīvitaṃ tatra coddiṣṭaṃ daśa māsā na saṃśayaḥ || 24-194 ||
dināni viṃśati proktā kālacakramupāsinām |
viṃśatistu yadā devi ṣaṇmāsā vigatāyuṣaḥ || 24-195 ||
ekaviṃśadinā devi dināni pravahed raviḥ |
pañca māsā samācaṣṭe dināni saptaviṃśati || 24-196 ||
ghaṭikā caiva triṃśoktāḥ pramāṇaṃ samudāhṛtam |
viṃśa dve ca padā vyūḍhā pañcamāsairgatāyuṣaḥ || 24-197 ||
dināni pañcaviṃśacca tava devi udāhṛtam |
viṃśatrikaṃ yadā devi pravaheta atandritaḥ || 24-198 ||
jīvitaṃ māsa pañcaite dināni viṃśati priye |
166b g] viṃśacatvārya hyekā * dinānāṃ pravahet priye || 24-199 ||
jīvitaṃ māsa pañcaite dinānyatra daśaiva tu |
pañcaviṃśa dinā vyūḍhā māsatrayaṃ sa jīvati || 24-200 ||
atorddhve pūrvamākhyātaṃ kālacakrasamāsataḥ |
antare ye dinā proktāḥ ṣoḍaśaiva varānane || 24-201 ||
bhāgakrameṇa te śodhya tatphalaṃ tu sphuṭīkṛtam |
mandabuddhiprabodhāya kālacakraṃ prakāśyate || 24-202 ||
athānyaṃ saṃpravakṣyāmi kālajñānaṃ varānane |
mandamatibodhāhetoḥ sukhabodhamataḥ śṛṇu || 24-203 ||
abdaikaṃ jīvite yogī ahorātreṇa suvrate |
abdadvayaṃ sa jīveta ahorātradvayena tu || 24-204 ||
trīṇyavdāni tribhiścaiva caturbhiścaturabdikam |
dinai pañcahi pañcābdā ṣaḍbhiḥṣaḍvarṣameva ca || 24-205 ||
saptabhiḥ sapta varṣāṇi aṣṭabhiraṣṭameva ca |
navabhirnava varṣāṇi daśabhirdaśameva ca || 24-206 ||
dinaikādaśabhirdevi abdaikādaśakaṃ priye |
dinairdvādaśabhiryogī jīvedabdāni dvādaśaḥ || 24-207 ||
saptapraharavāhena ṣaṇmāsaṃ yāvajīvati |
ṣaṭpraharapravāhena māsāni trīṇijīvati || 24-208 ||
pañcapraharavāhena dvyarddhamāsāyuṣo hi saḥ |
caturbhiḥ praharairdevi māsamekaṃ sajīvati || 24-209 ||
praharatrayavāhena māsārddhaṃ caiva jīvati |
praharadvayaṃ vahed yasya dinānyaṣṭau sa jīvati || 24-210 ||
catvāro praharāṃ jīvet praharaikaṃ vahed yadā |

119a] praharārddhaṃ vahed yasya sa jīvet praharadvayam || 24-211 ||
sadyo mṛtyurbhavet tasya yasya haṃsastripanthagaḥ |
yamārabhya nirūpetaṃ prāṇaṃ vai kālamīśvaram || 24-212 ||
māsapakṣadinaṃ vāraṃtadahnārabhya suvrate |
saṃlakṣyaivaṃ suyatnena tatkāle niścayo bhavet || 24-213 ||
ayuktasyāpi prāṇe tu mṛtyujñānaṃ nibodha me |
athānyaṃ paramaṃ guhyaṃ vakṣyāmi tava sundari || 24-214 ||
puruṣaṃ dvādaśānte tu dvādaśāṅgulamānataḥ |
śuddhasphaṭikasaṅkāśaṃ sarvāvayavasaṃyutam || 24-215 ||
nimīlitākṣo hṛṣṭātmā hṛnmantraṃ smaramānataḥ |
aśirastu yadā paśyet tadā mṛtyurna saṃśayaḥ || 24-216 ||
ṣaṇmāsena tu deveśi nātra kāryā vicāraṇā |
akṣihīnaṃ yadā paśyedakṣirogaṃ tadā bhavet || 24-217 ||
karṇena karṇarogaṃ tu nāsāhīne śirastathā |
167a g] hastahīne tathā hastaṃ pādahīneṃ tu pādayoḥ || 24-218 ||
yad yadaṅgaṇa paśyet tad tad vyādhiṃ vinirdiśet |
dhūmravarṇaṃ yadā paśyenmahāvyādhiṃ vinirdiśet || 24-219 ||
nṛtyamāno jvaraṃ vidyād rudante kalaho diśet |
hasamāne tathā lābhaṃ dinaiścaivaikaviṃśati || 24-220 ||
evaṃ dhyānaviśeṣeṇa phalaṃ vividharūpiṇam |
na jñāyate varārohe vīryaṃhīne tu sādhakaiḥ || 24-221 ||
adhānyaṃ saṃpravakṣyāmi puruṣaṃ kālarūpiṇam |
yena vijñāyate mantrī śubhāśubhaphalāphalam || 24-222 ||
gatvā tu nirjane sthāne śucirbhūtvā tu sādhakaḥ |
ekacittasthito yogī tatvaṃ sandhāya tatvataḥ || 24-223 ||
cakṣurniveśaṃ hṛdaye mudrāṃ badhvā tu jvālinī |
nimīlitākṣo medhāvī jalaṃ paśyet tataḥ pumān || 24-224 ||
dṛśyate puruṣaṃ tatraṃ kṛṣṇabhinnāñjanaprabham |
tasya mūrddhni sthitaṃ binduharitālasamaprabham || 24-225 ||
pittakṣobho bhavet tasya jvarasyaiva prajāyate |
dāghaścaiva tṛṣārtiśca vedanā mahatī bhavet || 24-226 ||
atha vaktrasthitaṃ binduṃ lākṣārasasamaprabham |
rajasrāvaṃ bhaved ghoraṃ śastrapātramathāpi vā || 24-227 ||
tadeva hṛdaye sthāne taḍidvalayasannibham |
binduṃ dṛṣṭvā vidhānena na śeṣubhayamādiśet || 24-228 ||
agnidāghaṃ purakṣobhaṃ arthahāniśca jāyate |
uditāstamane caiva īdṛgvarṇaṃ vibhāvyate || 24-229 ||
ādeśaṃ ca tataḥ kuryād vidhānaṃ saṃprayojayet |
śāntiṃ cātra prakurvīta kṣīradhārāṃ puredade || 24-230 ||
guhyadeśe yadā binduṃ nīlotpaladaladyutim |
vāhyataścāsavarṇābhaṃ bhayaṃ cātra pradarśayet || 24-231 ||
nṛpayuddhaṃ vijānīyād rāṣṭrabhaṅgaṃ vinirdiśet |
durbhikṣaṃ cātra dṛśyeta kṣudhā sīdanti jantavaḥ || 24-232 ||
yadā pāde pradṛśyeta binduṃ kapilapiṅgalam |
taskareṣu bhayaṃ vidyādarthahāniśca jāyate || 24-233 ||
anyadeśaṃ vrajellokaṃ parasparavirodhitam |
aśubhena ca gṛhyante adharmaścaiva jāyate || 24-234 || 
īdṛśe caiva lakṣe ca yadā pratyakṣamāgatāḥ | 
cakṣuragre sthito hyeṣa tatvasannaddhavigrahaḥ || 24-235 ||
ādeṣṭavyaṃ prayatnena lokānāṃ hitakāmyayā |
sarvārtha sādhayedetallakṣabhedaḥ prakīrtitaḥ || 24-236 ||
evaṃ kramavibhāgena ātmanasya parasya vā |
ādeśaṃ kuru kalpāṇi śubhāśubhamaninditam || 24-237 ||
adhunā saṃpravakṣyāmi mṛtyulakṣaṇaṃ me śṛṇu | 
167b g] sadyomṛtyurvijānīyādātmanasya parasya vā || 24-238 || 

119b] vinyasyā''mano hyevaṃ pūrvavacca pracoditam |
dhyāyet tatvamidaṃ divyaṃ udayārkasamaprabham || 24-239 ||
ātmanasya parasyaiva cchāyārūpaṃ nirūpayet |
jale vātha sthale vātha lakṣyaṃ paśyeta buddhimān || 24-240 || 
kṛṣṇasiṃhākṛtiṃ dṛṣṭvā vatsarānmriyate tu saḥ | 
agnivajjvalamānastu ṣaṇmāsāt mriyate dhruvam || 24-241 ||
kṛṣṇapītaṃ ca dṛśyeta sta?yā madhyagataṃ mṛgam |
māsatrayeṇa mriyate sādhakastatvacintakaḥ || 24-242 ||
dhūmravarṇaṃ yadā cānyaṃ chāyāmadhye tu dṛśyate |
māsadvayena mriyate tavākhyātaṃ hi suvrate || 24-243 ||
chā?yā madhye yadā devi mayūraṃ raktapītakam |
māsamātrā bhavet mṛtyu satyaṃ satyaṃ guṇānvitam? || 24-244 ||
chāyāmadhyagataṃ gauri yadā nīlaṃ kapotakam |
dṛṣṭvā caitanmahotpātaṃ pakṣānmaraṇamādiśet || 24-245 ||
yadā paśyati chāyāyāṃ nīlajīmūtasannibham |
daśāhānmriyate janturnātra kuryād vicāraṇāt || 24-246 ||
chāyāmadhyagataṃ devi kākaṃ kāñcana saprabham |
dṛṣṭvā caiva varārohe pañcāhānmaraṇaṃ bhavet || 24-247 ||
chāyāmadhye yadā kākaṃ haritālasamaprabham |
dṛṣṭvā caiva trirātreṇa mṛtyustasya vidhīyate || 24-248 ||
chāyāmadhyagataṃ bhadre mārjāraṃ kapipiṅgalam |
dṛṣṭvā caiva dvirātreṇa mṛtyuṃ tasya vidhīyate || 24-249 ||
kṛkalāsaṃyadā paśyet sindūrasadṛśaṃ priye |
ekāhāca bhavenmṛtyurnātra kāyāryā vicāraṇā || 24-250 ||
uditārkaṃ prabhāteṣu yotmacchāyāṃ na paśyati |
mṛtyu samvatsareṇaiva śanivāreṇa saptamī || 24-251 ||
hastanakṣatrasaṃyukte pakṣe puṇye tu śuklake |
arddharātorddhvagā mṛtyuḥ sannipāte vinaśyati || 24-252 ||
ghṛte vā darpaṇe taile yotmacchāyāṃ 
yāḍha ṛkṣe vudhavāre velāyāmanayā priye || 24-254 na paśyati |
māse caikādaśe so hi mriyate tu na saṃśayaḥ || 24-253 ||
śrāvaṇe śuklapakṣe ca trayodaśyāṃ tu uttarā |||
arddhayāme vyatikrānte rājāvutkramate priye |
akṣavṛkṣotiprāmāṇyāṃ śleṣmāntaka mahādrumam || 24-255 || 
yastu paśyati svapnānte bhūtapretāvṛtaṃ ghanam |
168a g] jīvate nava māsāni āṣāḍhe jīvitaṃ tyajet || 24-256 ||
daśamyāṃ śuklapakṣe tu anurādhā'ṅgārake dine |
dinārddhaṃ yāmakadve tu raktadoṣairvinaśyati || 24-257 ||
sūkṣmahrasvaṃ tu dvirekaṃ vāhuṃ paśyati cātmanaḥ |
viparītāni sarvāṇi aṣṭamāsāṃ sa jīvati || 24-258 ||
jyeṣṭhapaurṇimarātre tu śukravārārddhayāmake |
pradoṣe jāgramānaistumriyate saviṣūcikaḥ || 24-259 ||
agrataḥ pṛṣṭato vāpi khaṇḍapādaṃ yadā bhavet |
pāṃśukardamagomedhā saptamāsaṃ sa jīvati || 24-260 ||
śuklapakṣārddharātre tu tṛtīyā kārtikasya tu |
mūlaṛkṣārkavāre tu akṣaṇastu vinaśyati || 24-261 ||
kapota kākaḥ gṛdhro vā līyate yasyamūrddhani |
ṣaṇmājjāyate mṛtyuḥ kālavelā nigadyate || 24-262 ||
caitrapūrṇamicitrāsu somavāre dvitīyake |
śastrapātātkṣayaṃ yāti tadantaṃ tasya jīvitam || 24-263 ||
vikṛtātmā yadā paśyet karālaṃ kṛṣṇarūpiṇam |
śvānaiśca khādyamānaṃ tu ūrddhvapādamadhomukham || 24-264 ||
kharoṣṭramahiṣārūḍhaṃ pañcamāsān sa jīvati |

120a] dvādaśyāṃ ca yuje kṛṣṇe āśleṣāṅgārake dine || 24-265 ||
mriyate cā'hinā daṣṭo arddharātre hyupasthite |
anabhre nirmale śuddhe vidyut paśyati dakṣiṇe || 24-266 ||
niśāmindradhanurvā'tha caturmāsaṃ sa jīvati |
śuklamārgaśiro'ṣṭamyāṃ vārendro rudradevatā || 24-267 ||
rogārtapīḍitetīvra jalāgnipatanaṃ bhavet |
aśirāṃ paśyate cchāyāṃ vāhuhīno'thavātmanaḥ || 24-268 ||
pīḍitaṃ kāśaśvāsaiśca trimāsāṃ jīvitaṃ tyajet |
dvitīyāṃ māghakṛṣṇe tu hasta ṛkṣagurordine || 24-269 ||
aparāhṇe tu saṃprāpte yogī utkrāntinā vrajet |
aṣṭanāḍyāmanirvāhaṃ mukhe vāyuṃ ca recayet || 24-270 ||
hṛṣṭatuṣṭamanetyaktvā dvimāsāṃ jīvitaṃ tyajet |
bhūmirānekadhāre tu śuklapadrapadā'ṣṭamī || 24-271 ||
indra-ṛkṣe śanirvāre viṣūcyārto vinaśyati |
agandha ātmagātreṣu romaharṣaṃ ca dāruṇam || 24-272 || 
śṛṅkhalā pāśahastaṃ tu daṇḍamudgarapāṇinam |
anantaghorahāraudraṃ daṃṣṭrā karālabhīṣaṇam || 24-273 ||
tāḍyamānonmanantena svapnānte yadi paśyati |
kṛtāntarūpaṃ sākṣāt sa māsamekaṃ sañjīvati || 24-274 ||
śrāvaṇapūrṇāmadhyāhne dhaniṣṭhā vudhavārake |
aṭavyāṃ gartamadhyastho mriyate cauraśastrahan || 24-275 ||
168b g] yasyorukarapādau tu snātamātrasya śuṣyati | 
dhūmavartiḥ śire vāpi arddhamāsaṃ sa jīvati || 24-276 ||
puṇyasaṃkrāntidvādaśyāṃ śuklaśukradineṣu ca |
kuryādagniprapātaṃ tu kālacakraṃ nigadyate || 24-277 ||
yasya jihvā'tikṛṣṇā tu mukha gorocanaprabham |
raktamaṇḍalakau gaṇḍe dināni daśa jīvati || 24-278 ||
somavāra āṣāḍhe ca rogavyādhirupadrutaḥ |
yo naśyati cātmānaṃ paratārāsuvyaktimā || 24-279 ||
pañcarātrād bhavenmṛtyuḥ svātisomadineṣu ca |
sarṣayākārapulikāṃvra svedotīva jāyate || 24-280 ||
trirātrānmṛtyumākhyāti punarvasaṃ gārake dine |
netramekaṃ sva?vedyasya karṇanāḍī dvayaṃ samam || 24-281 ||
cārastu bhavate yasya jīvate sadinadvayam |
uttarāṣāḍhanakṣatre cāre gharmāṃśukasya ca || 24-282 ||
kṛṣṇāmbaradharā nārī kṛṣṇātmā yadi paśyati |
kaṇṭhalagnadvayordvābhyāṃ vrajato dakṣiṇāṃ diśi || 24-283 ||
svapnānte ced vaśaṃ dṛṣṭvā ahorātraṃ sa jīvati |
pretaskandhe samārūḍhaṃ valāntaṃ harṣitātmanam || 24-284 ||
śastrapātād vinaśyeta āmadhyāhnaṃ sa jīvati |
darpaṇodaramadhyasthaṃ kṛṣṇātmā yadi paśyati || 24-285 ||
bimbamadhye pratibimbaṃ kṛṣṇarekhāpraveṣṭitam |
tasya kaṇṭhagataḥ prāṇaḥ sukhasuptena buddhyati || 24-286 ||
akasmād devatāgre tu jātamānastu paśyati |
daṇḍapāśakarāgraṃ tu puruṣaṃ kṛṣṇahā balam || 24-287 ||
tatkṣaṇāt tasya taṃ mṛtyuḥ svapne ca na prabuddhyati |
yadā paśyati svapnānte puruṣaṃ kṛṣṇapiṅgalam || 24-288 ||
āyasena tu daṇḍena hanate pṛṣṭhamadhyataḥ |
tasyāḥ samanavelāyāṃ maraṇaṃ tu vinirdeśet || 24-289 ||
kharāḍhaṃ ca yadā svapne tailābhyaktaṃ śarīriṇam |
divasasyāṣṭame bhāge maraṇaṃ ca dhruvaṃ bhavet || 24-290 ||
atha uṣṭrasamārūḍhaṃ dakṣiṇāṃ brajate diśi |
kṛṣṇavarṇadharaiḥ krūrairlohadaṇḍadharāvṛtam || 24-291 ||
tāḍyamānodiśiṃ yāmyāṃ amadhyāhnād vinaśyati |
tailābhyaktaśarīrastu kardamena vimajjataḥ || 24-292 ||
dinasyaghaṭikā ṣaḍbhirmṛtyusteṣāṃ samādiśet |

120b] pakṣamāsaṃ yadā svapne khādyamāno vivudhyati || 24-293 ||
kṛṣṇavastradharā nārī tailābhyaṅgaṃ va gūhati |
gacched yamālayaṃ śīghraṃ yāvadastamane raviḥ || 24-294 ||
169a g] raktambaradharā nārī raktagandhānulepanā |
avagṛhyati yaḥ svapne sā rātrī tasya paścimaṃ || 24-295 ||
evaṃ svapnagataṃ khyātaṃ jñātavyaṃ kālavedibhiḥ |
utpātā kāṇodayaṃ vai mṛtyuyogaśca me śṛṇu || 24-296 || 
saṅkrāntipañcakaṃ caiva mukhena vahate yadā | 
tadutpāṭaṃ bhaved yogaṃ sthānāt sthānāntaraṃ brajet || 24-297 ||
cittanāśaṃ manodvegaṃ rogavṛddhiśca jāyate |
suhṛdgṛhavināśaśca tejāhāniśca jāyate || 24-298 ||
dakṣiṇe tu puṭaikasmin dakṣiṇāyanavarjite |
saṅkrāntyaṣṭapravāhena yoga kāryodayo hi saḥ || 24-299 ||
bhagandarāt tu? granthiśca netrarogaṃ ca kāmalā |
śūlavisphoṭikā duḥkhamuradoṣā bhavanti ca || 24-300 ||
vāmanāsāpuṭānte vai saṅkrāntyaśca trayodaśa |
jvaraḥ śironti śūlaśca aryāstaṃ manameva ca || 24-301 ||
mūtrakṛcchṛpramehāśca pāṇḍurogaśca varddhate |
iḍastha śleṣmalāṃ vyādhiḥ prakopayati suvrate || 24-302 ||
yasmiṃścāraṃ nirūpyena tatkāle divase pare |
vyādhibhistu sa pīḍyeta vāmāvāmetare tare || 24-303 ||
athānyaṃ sparśavijñānaṃ nāsādhastāt tathopari |
ūrddhvena sparśate corddhve rugdoṣāḥ prāk pracodinām || 24-304 ||
vācākrośā'bhibhavanaṃ dakṣiṇe spṛśate yadā |
madhye madhyapuṭasparśī parābhibhavatāṃ brajet || 24-305 ||
itaścetaśca vahudhā saṅkrāntyekāvahet padā |
pūjanaṃ bahusanmānaṃ lābhaṃ tasya tadā bhavet || 24-306 ||
mandacārī suṣumṇāyā prāṇahaṃso bhaved yadā |
bhū lābhaṃ dharmamaiśvaryaṃ bhavate ca priyāgamam || 24-307 || 
dvādaśaiva tu saṅkrāntyo viṣṇu vai ke vahed yadā |
tadā tu vatsaraikena maraṇaṃ ca samādiśet || 24-308 ||
hrāso saṅkrāntirekaikā māsekaikaṃ hraset tadā |
saṅkrāntyekā varārohe triṃśatprāṇakṣayo yadā || 24-309 ||
dine dine tu vai vāhye yāvat triṃśaddināni tu |
māsāntye ca bhavenmṛtyuḥ sadya evaṃ varānane || 24-310 ||
evaṃ vai mṛtyuliṅgāni ariṣṭā'nyāni yāni ca | 
yogā jñāyati yogīndro nādajāntargatāni ca || 24-311 || 
nirjityaitāni yogena evamuktakrameṇa tu | 
ayogī jñāyate yāni ayukto'pi hi suvrate || 24-312 ||
vahniliṅgāni tānyatra aṅgāriṣṭā * me śṛṇu |
śuṣkatālvoṣṭhakaṇṭhaśca akasmād dhūsaracchaviḥ || 24-313 ||
skandhau tu bhagamāyāti ṣaṇmāsānmṛtyumādiśet |
169b g] sunīlaṃ maṇḍalaṃ vyomni paśyate ca dine dine || 24-314 || 
sitaṃ haritakṛṣṇaṃ ca vatsarārddhaṃ mriyet tu saḥ |
viraśitaṃ paśyate bhānuṃ somaṃ vai lakṣmavarjitam || 24-315 ||
tārakāṃ jyotsnā kṛṣṇāṃ vai paśyet ṣaṇmāsamāyuṣī |
karṇarandhra kṛtāṅguṣṭhau ghoṣaṃ na śṛṇu te yadā || 24-316 ||
maraṇaṃ tasya vai devi ṣaṇmāsena samādiśet |
ghoṣamadhye paraṃ śabdaṃ cīraṃ vā ciñciṇīravam || 24-317 ||
māsamekaṃ sa jīveta na śṛṇoti yadā priye |
ātmanasva śirādvāyāṃ paśyaṃ * *? sa jīvati || 24-318 ||
* * varta ato? madhye gulphayormarmasatviṣu | 
so'vaśyaṃ vaśamāyāti yasyaite spandanaṃ na hi || 24-319 ||
* * * * * * * * * * * * * * * *? |
 * * * * ? naṃ sovaśyaṃ mriyate naraḥ || 24-320 ||

121a] tālarandhragataṃ dhūmaṃ mahāyānaṃ prakīrtitam |
jihvā arundhatī jñeyā nāsāgraṃ dhruvam ucyate || 24-321 ||
netrānte karajākrānte jyotistat sūryasomayoḥ |
na paśyedyastu muhyante? ṛtumekaṃ sa jīvati || 24-322 ||
gagayyetā nirīkṣyeta sopyevaṃ mriyate naraḥ |
sthūlo kasmāt prajāyeta akasmāt kṛśatā bhavet || 24-323 ||
atikruddho'tibhītaśca varṣamekaṃ sa jīvati |
ameśe? vidyutāṃ paśyedivolkendradhanurniśi? || 24-324 ||
diṣṭedaśe ca divyohomāsamekaṃ sa jīvati |
yatra? vai jātamātrasya hṛdayaṃ caiva śuṣyati || 24-325 ||
atyuṣṇaṃ ca bhavedgātraṃ ṛtu mekaṃ sa jīvati |
nābhistu kampate yasya satataṃ caiva śuṣyati? || 24-326 ||
vakrakaṇṭho ca suśroṇi ṣaṇmāsāt tu gatāyuṣuḥ |
yasya jihvā bhavet sthūlādantāḥ klidyanti bhāmini || 24-327 ||
mriyate so naro devi varṣānte ca na saṃśayaḥ |
cakṣuṣaḥ sravate nityaṃ sphuṭāśabdaṃ na ca śṛṇot || 24-328 ||
vāgjāḍyaṃ gandhanā ghrāti māsaikena gatāyuṣaḥ |
raktaṃpadmopamaṃ vaktraṃ jihvā'tyuṣṇā tu jāyate || 24-329 ||
gātre varṇānyanekāni hṛdayaṃ yasya rudet sadā |
tālukampo'tha nābhiśca arddhamāsaṃ sa jīvati || 24-330 ||
pratyakṣe kākanāśīraṃ nāghrā te dhūmadīpajam |
pūrvadṛṣṭaṃ na jānāti caturmāsaṃ sa jīvati || 24-331 ||
binduṃ yasya na paśyeta nityaṃ vaktrānugā marut |
hiṣkā tu vahate nityaṃ pakṣamekaṃ sa jīvati || 24-332 ||
abhinne tārakā jyotsnāṃ paśyanti suranāyike |
ṣaṇmāsām mriyateso vai nātra kāryā vicāraṇā || 24-333 ||
170a g] bhānumadhyagataṃ devi puruṣaṃ tejarūpiṇam |
yadā na paśyate taṃ vai svarūpasthaṃ svalakṣaṇam || 24-334 ||
tadā tasya pravakṣyāmi yādṛśaṃ bhavate priye |
kṛṣṇavarṇe bhaved vyādhi kalahaṃ babhruvarṇake || 24-335 ||
kāyaste dhvasanaṃ jñeyaṃ vicitraiśca kulakṣayam |
śirohīne bhavet mṛtyuhastahīne ca bandhanam || 24-336 ||
pādahīne tu jñātavyaṃ śṛṅkhalābandhanaṃ priye |
evaṃ nānāvidhai rūpaijñātavyaṃ mṛtyulakṣaṇam || 24-337 ||
hastaparvaṇi vakṣyāmi yathā bhavati tacchṛṇu |
śukle vā yadi vā kṛṣṇe kathayāmi samāsataḥ || 24-338 ||
vāmaṃ śuklamiti proktaṃ dakṣiṇaṃ kṛṣṇamucyate |
savyāsavyagatiṃ jñātvā tataścaiva tu lakṣayet || 24-339 ||
ālaktakaṃ tu saṅgṛhya hastābhyāṃ mārjanaṃ kuru |
puṣpa dhūpaṃ tato datvā vīkṣayeta śubhāśubham || 24-340 || 
pakṣadvayaṃ samāśritya hastayorubhayorapi |
aṅgulyātraya parva syāllakṣayeta vicakṣaṇa || 24-341 ||
hastadvayepi vīkṣyāstu paśyate tatra cihnikām |
anvayaḥ parvaṇo yāstu kṛṣṇarekhā yadā bhavet || 24-342 ||
vāme vā yadi vā madhye pratipadārabhya nirdiśet |
kanīyasāmadhaḥ parve parve parve tu lakṣayet || 24-343 ||
aṅguṣṭhaṃ yāvad deveśi tāvadevaṃ tu lakṣayet |
taddine maraṇaṃ tasya śuklakṛṣṇe'tha śobhane || 24-344 ||
kṛṣṇarekhāmadhaḥ parve kṛṣṇe ca'suklaparvaṇi |
maraṇaṃ tasya vijñeyaṃ māraṇo nirjane? priye || 24-345 ||
sa evorddhve yadā sarpe tasya mṛtyurjale sthitaḥ |
madhyaparvaṇyadhastāṃ tu yadi rekhā tu sarpate || 24-346 ||
mṛtyustasya samuddiṣṭa urasrāveṇa śobhane | 
sā evorddhaṃ yadā sarpemaraṇaṃ śastraghātane || 24-347 || 
tiryakcaiva yadā sarpet saṅgrāme maraṇaṃ bhavet |

121b] tṛtīyaṃ parvamāsādya adhastād yadi sarpate || 24-348 ||
tīrthe tu maraṇaṃ tasya jāyate nātra saṃśayaḥ |
visarpaṇaṃ tṛtīyāyāmūrddhvataścaiva jāyate || 24-349 ||
icchāyā maraṇaṃ tasya jāyate ca śivecchayā | 
parveṣveva tu pādābhyāṃ kṛṣṇo dvau tu yadā bhaveṃt || 24-350 || 
evamekaṃ tu tatrāpi maraṇaṃ sarpata smṛtam |
divā rātrau tu maraṇaṃ lakṣayet pādayorniśi || 24-351 ||
mṛtyuliṅgāni caitāni aṅgāriṣṭāni yāni ca |
svasvādhyātmikapūrvāṇi? kathitāni mayā tava || 24-352 ||
pūjayā japahomena dhyānadhāraṇa yā priye |
170b g] śāntirakṣāvidhānena vacyate nānyathā tu vai || 24-353 || 
jāraṇaṃ vacanaṃ proktaṃ duṣkaraṃ calamānasām | 
iti bhairavasrotasi mahātantre vidyāpīṭhe saptakoṭipramāṇe śrītantrasadbhāve kālādhikāro nāma caturviṃśatiḥ paṭalaḥ || 24 ||


devyuvāca

bhagavan parameśāna sarvajñaḥ kāraṇeśvaraḥ |
kathitaṃ tu tvayā sarvaṃ vāṅmayaṃ sacarācaram || 25-1 ||
nākhyātaṃ tu tvayā nāthakālavañcanalakṣaṇam |
mṛtyuñjayavidhānaṃ tu rudraśāntistathaiva ca || 25-2 ||
arcanaṃ havanaṃ caiva rakṣāvidhiviśeṣataḥ |
dhyānadhāraṇayogaṃ ca kathayasva mama prabho || 25-3 ||
aṇḍamṛtyusamīpastho vartate puruṣeśvaraḥ | 
yathā cāsaṃ ca mṛtyośca vaṃcanā bhavate prabho || 25-4 ||
tathāhaṃ śrotumicchāmi prasādād vaktramarhasti |

bhairava uvāca

śṛṇu devi pravakṣyāmi yat tvayā pṛcchito hyaham || 25-5 ||
anekaistu prakāraistu kālabandhanalakṣaṇam |
kathayāmi samāsena tvatprītyā suraṃnāyike || 25-6 ||
aṣṭapatraṃ catuṣkoṇaṃ caturdvāropaśobhitam |
padmagarbhe likhed vidvān rudraśāntau tu maṇḍalam || 25-7 ||
kathyāṅgaṃ prathamam tasyā padmalakṣaṇasaṃyutam |
uttaraṃ caiva vaktāraṃ? kīrtitaṃ padmalakṣaṇam || 25-8 ||
kathyāṅgā dvau purā kalpastatrākhyātamataḥ param |
anuṣṭupchandasā yuktāścatvāriṃśad dinaikataḥ || 25-9 ||
rudrādīni tu vaktrāṅgairmantrayantrapatrapadāni -tu |
proktāni triṃśadaṣṭau ca devatairiyamījyate || 25-10 ||
sarvapāpapraśamanī sarvaroganivāraṇī |
sarvasamyat karī devī maṅgalā vijayāvahā || 25-11 ||
samastaireva hi padaiḥ paṅktiyāgaḥ śivoditaḥ |
īśānīdiśimārabhya dikṣvasvāsvapi saṃsthitaḥ || 25-12 ||
yāgavyastapadaṃ vakṣye sarvasandohalakṣaṇam |
dve pade parame tatra karṇikāyāṃ niveśayet || 25-13 ||
rudraṃ ca vṛṣabhaṃ caiva aviśuddhamasambhavam yatiṃstu agneye trijaṭā nyaset |
tatoṅkāravaṣaṭkārau svadhākārasamanvitam || 25-15 ||
sahasrāṇi tu catvāri vinyaseddakṣiṇe dale |
171a g] karālavikṛtaṃ caiva nairṛte tu dale nyaset || 25-16 ||
anantaṃ caiva ārdraṃ ca śuṣkamāvogataṃ tathā |
vāruṇe vinyased dhīmāṃścatvāryaite pado'nugāḥ || 25-17 ||
catvāraḥ piṅgalā nyasyā vāyaveṃ tu dale budheḥ |
uttare pañcarūpāṇi padāni viniveśayet || 25-18 ||
sugandhadhūpakusumairvidhivacca prapūjayet |
agnau homaṃ ca kartavyo madhumiśreṇa sarpiṣā || 25-19 ||
sacaruḥ sapuroḍāśo hotavyastarpaṇāya tu |
śamīpatrāṇi dūrvā ca sakṣīratarupallavāḥ || 25-20 ||
cūtapatrapavitrāṇi uktāni juhuyāt sadā |

122a] apāmārgasya samidhā sapalāśyā udumbarāḥ || 25-21 ||
kṛṣṇā tilāyavāścaiva taṇḍulā cāpyakhaṇḍitāḥ |
siddhārthā caiva lājāśca dadhikṣīrasapāyasām || 25-22 ||
sarvametad yathāśaktyā juhuyāt satataṃ budhaḥ |
annādyāṃ vyañjanādyāśca bhakṣāṇi vividhāni tu || 25-23 ||
saptapañca trisaṅkhyā vā ekaikā vāhutī bhavet |
nityahome vidhistveṣa satiko vā vidhīyate || 25-24 ||
laukikānāṃ tu kāmānāṃ sāhasro divyasaṃbhavāt |
utpāteṣu ca sarveṣu mahotpātā yudhāhutiḥ || 25-25 ||
sarvarogagṛhītānāṃ grahaiścaivārditātmanām |
upasargaparītānāṃ vighnaiścopadrutātmanām || 25-26 ||
abhicārābhitasthānāṃ śāntimetāṃ prayojayet |
abhiṣekaśca kartavyaḥ saṃpātāhutamantritaiḥ || 25-27 ||
aṣṭabhiḥ kalaśairnityaṃ sarvottarapadena tu |
abhiṣiktāya dātavyaścarukaḥ parikalpitaḥ || 25-28 ||
tena prāśitamātreṇa sarvagamyo bhavennaraḥ |
rudraśāntiṃ pravakṣyāmi śivāṃ sarvārthasādhanīm || 25-29 ||
sādhakānāṃ hitārthāya devadevena bhāṣitam |
sarve vighnāḥ praṇaśyanti śrutvaivaṃ pāpanāśanīm || 25-30 ||
duḥsvapnā vyādhayaścaiva grahā yānti diśo daśa |
vahurūpāya śarvāya viśvarūpaharāya ca || 25-31 ||
jagaddhitāya devāya sarvapāpaharāya ca |
pārvatyā sahitāyesa sahanandigaṇāya ca || 25-32 ||
rudraśāntiṃ namakhyāmi? vetālānāṃ vināśanīm |
narāṇāmupasṛṣṭānāṃ devāyatanaveśmasu || 25-33 ||
yāsāṃ na garbhasambhūtiḥ kulahāniśca jāyate |
yatra jātā vinaśyanti bhavanti ca na puṃsakāḥ || 25-34 ||
mārī cotpadyate yatra satataṃ kalahaṃ bhavet |
171b g] garbhaḥ patatyakāle ca ruṇḍaṃ vā yatra jāyate || 25-35 ||
durbhikṣeṇa prapīṅyante rāṣṭrotpātaśca dāruṇaḥ |
gaṇo yatra viruddhyeta bhrātaraḥ śvaśuraḥ suhṛt || 25-36 ||
pitā mātā sva sā caiva kasmalopahato gṛhe |
paśyantiṃ ca kapiṃ svapne jīva kṣetreṇa rohati || 25-37 ||
gāvośca mahiṣo dopi?nāgānye karmakāriṇaḥ |
gṛhasthitā viruddhyante tatra śāntiṃ prayojayet || 25-38 ||
kūpo vā garjate yatra pṛṣṭhavaṃśaśca bhidyate |
taravo'nāhatāḥ santaḥ sravanti rudhiraṃ bahum || 25-39 ||
devatā svācca vṛkṣāśca nṛtyanti ca hasanti ca |
akāle puṣpitā vṛkṣāḥ pālitāścāpyanekaśaḥ || 25-40 ||
ulkāpātāśca jāyante bhūmikāpothadāruṇaḥ |
nimittairaśubhairebhiranyaiścāpi sudāruṇaiḥ || 25-41 ||
ekāgraḥ prayato bhūtvā tatra śāntiṃ prayojayet |
giriśaṃ janako nāma śailarājasya mūrddhani || 25-42 ||
nānājanapadākīrṇaṃ auṣadhībhiḥ samanvitaḥ |
tatra madhye sthitaṃ rudraṃ paraṃ bhūtahite ratam || 25-43 ||
yakṣarākṣasakūṣmāṇḍairvṛtaṃ bhūtairanekaśaiḥ |
tato devagaṇāḥ sarve ṛṣayaśca tapo'dhikāḥ || 25-44 ||
brāhmaṇamagrataḥ kṛtya śrāvayanti maheśvarāt |
ṛgyajuḥsāmabhistotraiḥ pavitraiścavarapradam || 25-45 ||
umayā sahitaṃ stutvā nīlakaṇṭhaṃ vṛṣadhvajam |
tato devagaṇāḥ sarve ṛṣayaśca tapodhanāḥ || 25-46 ||
vijñāpayanti deveśaṃ brahmāṇa sahitaṃ haram |
devadeva mahādeva mānavī duḥkhitā prajāḥ || 25-47 ||
tasya śāntiṃ prayaccha stvaṃ sarvagrahavimocanī |

122b] devānāṃ vacanaṃ śrutvā harastribhuvaneśvaraḥ || 25-48 ||
samādiśya gaṇāḥ sarve kṣipramājñā pitāmahā |
sarve te lokapālāśca rāṣṭrapālā sudurjayāḥ || 25-49 ||
piśācāḥ pūtanā yakṣāḥ kaumāraṃ graharākṣasāḥ |
kūṣmāṇḍā ca savetālāḥ pakṣapātā dhanurgrahāḥ || 25-50 ||
devāpatanapātālā bhūtāgrāmaniśācarāḥ tathā kālī ye cānye hiṃsakā grahāḥ || 25-51 ||
apasmārāstathot pādā netrarogagrahāstathā |
hṛdrogā mukharogāśca kukṣirogā jvarā kṣayāḥ || 25-52 ||
śirorogāḥ pāṇḍurogā nāyakāśca sudāruṇāḥ |
ete cānye ca bahavo vyādhayaḥ parikīrtitāḥ || 25-53 ||
te grahāstu gaṇaiḥ sarvairupanītā harāntikam |
172a g] vijñāpayanti deveśaṃ praṇipatya muhurmuhuḥ || 25-54 ||
tejāṃśodruma śaktyā'sya kiṃ punastvaṃ tamīkṣitum |
ājñāpaya mahādeva kiṃ kartavyaṃ tavājñayā || 25-55 ||
tamuvāca mahādeva krodhāt saṃraktalocanaḥ |
mānuṣā duḥkhitāḥ kena kasya doṣeṇa vā punaḥ || 25-56 ||
teṣāmeko varaḥ prāha bhūrlokeśvareśvaraḥ |
eṣā vṛttirihāsmākaṃ brahmadattopyayaṃ varaḥ || 25-57 ||
ye grastāstamasā loke mānuṣāḥ pāpakarmiṇaḥ |
anarthā hantukāmāśca balikāmāśca te grahāḥ || 25-58 ||
harṣitā duḥkhitāścaiva naṣṭadravyāśca mānavāḥ |
nānācittābhibhūtāśca cchidramīkṣya viśanti te || 25-60 ||
etat te kathitaṃ deva prasīda bhagavan mama |
ājñāpaya mahādeva kartavyaṃ kimataḥ param || 25-61 ||
evamuktastu bhagavāṃstryambakaḥ parameśvaraḥ |
rudraśāntimimāṃ prāha varado bhūtabhāvanaḥ || 25-62 ||
yastu vidyāmimāṃ śrutvā guhyakona nivartate |
saptādhā sā sphuṭo mūrddhni rudradaṇḍena hanyate || 25-63 ||
saptajātisahasrāṇi raurave pacyate grahāḥ |
punaśca sumahāghoro yeṣāṃ vidyāṃ vyatikramet || 25-64 ||
evamuktāstu bhagavān grahāṇāmagrataḥ sthitaḥ |
samaye sthāpayitvā tu prāha vidyāmimāṃ prabhuḥ || 25-65 ||
oṃ aprameyāpradhṛṣyāya namaḥ svasti parāyaṇe |
oṃkāro'tha vaṣaṭkāro brahma svāhā svadhā tathā || 25-66 ||
sāvitrī bhūtasāvitrī rudrasāvitrireva ca |
rudrāṇī brahmasāvitrī devī caiva sarasvatī || 25-67 ||
umā kṛṣṇāmitā caiva yā nityaṃ hṛdi saṃsthitām | 
surāsuramanuṣyeṣu sthāvareṣu careṣu ca || 25-68 || 
oṃ hiri hiri rudrāya namaḥ svāhā |
oṃ vṛṣavāhanāya namaḥ svāhā |
oṃ śvetapiṅgalāya namaḥ svāhā |
oṃ kṛṣṇapiṅgalāya namaḥ svāhā |
oṃ madhupiṅgalāya namaḥ svāhā |
oṃ viśvarūpāya namaḥ svāhā |
oṃ mahāvirūpāya namaḥ svāhā |
oṃ karālāhatāya namaḥ svāhā |
oṃ vikṛtāya namaḥ svāhā |
oṃ vikṛtarūpāya namaḥ svāhā |
oṃ anantāya namaḥ svāhā |
oṃ surūpāya namaḥ svāhā |
oṃ ekapiṅgalāya namaḥ svāhā |
oṃ ekajaṭāya namaḥ svāhā |
oṃ dvijaṭāya namaḥ svāhā |
oṃ trijaṭāya namaḥ svāhā |
oṃ pramathādhipataye svāhā |
oṃ * * * * * * * * * * * * * |
172a g] oṃ * * * * * * * * * * * * * |

123a] oṃ tatpuruṣāya namaḥ svāhā |
oṃ sahasraśīrṣāya namaḥ svāhā |
oṃ sahasravaktrodarāya namaḥ svāhā |
oṃ sahasrakaracaraṇāya namaḥ svāhā |
oṃ sahasraliṅgāya namaḥ svāhā |
oṃ bhūtapataye namaḥ svāhā |
oṃ mahābhūtapataye namaḥ svāhā | 
oṃ paśupataye namaḥ svāhā |
oṃ umāpataye namaḥ svāhā |
oṃ sarvabhūtādhipataye namaḥ svāhā |
oṃ oṃkārāya namaḥ svāhā |
oṃ vaṣaṭkārāya namaḥ svā |
oṃ svadhākārāya namaḥ svāhā |
oṃ ḍamarūpadhāriṇe namaḥ svāhā |
oṃ turu tururuṇḍi śi rudeśi devānāṃ deva viśākhaṃ hana hana daha daha paca paca matha matha curu curu muru muru suru suru rudraśāntimanusmara kṛṣṇe piṅgale kālapiśācādhipativiśveśvarāya namaḥ svāhā |
nityajāpena sarvapāpaiḥ pramucyate |
śāntisamidhā ghṛtaṃ kṣīraṃ dadhi madhu tilā yavā dūrvā śamīpatrāṇi pāyasaṃ sarvānnaṃ juhuyāt |
anyeṣūktāntikteṣubaliṃ ca kuryācchāntirbhavati |
rudraśāntirmayā proktā athānyaṃ śṛṇu suvrate || 25-69 ||
lakṣaṇaṃ sampravakṣyāmi amṛtīśavidhistathā | 
kriyāyāgavidhānaṃ ca kathyamānā calātmaje || 25-70 ||
vyāpakaṃ ca nyaset pūrvam amṛtaṃ rudrasambhavam |
kakārāṣṭamakaṃ bījaṃ kalayā ṣaṭkayā yutam || 25-71 ||
kalārddhopari saṃyuktaṃ tṛtīyaṃ parikīrtitam |
bhujaṅgakuṭilākārā ūrddhve tu śaśinaṃ prabhāḥ || 25-72 ||
tryahorddhvaṃ? mriyanti? tatpūrvaṃ tadūrddhvaṃ mūrddhnimeva ca |
eṣā hi amṛtā nāma amṛtīśaprabhā smṛtā || 25-73 ||
asyād yaṃ vai parītena yathāsamyagvyavasthitam |
tadānāmāmṛtīśaṃ tu mṛtyuhantā prakīrtitam || 25-74 ||
rasāyanāni sarvāṇi bhūṣitāni vināyakaiḥ |
amṛtatvaṃ prayacchanti amṛtīśaprabhāvataḥ || 25-75 ||
tasmād vyapohyayaṃ pūjyo japed etacca nityaśaḥ |
amṛtaṃ vāri purādheyaṃ prāṇaṃ caiva prakalpya tu || 25-76 ||
rudrāvaraṇaṃ samāyuktā yamārddhena vibhūṣitā |
racchiraśca śikhāvarma krameṇaiva prakīrtitāḥ || 25-77 ||
mātṛrodani vinyasya bālenduparitaṃ tataḥ |
173a g] amṛtīśāstra metaddhi sarva vighnanisūdanam || 25-78 ||
jīvanaṃ śikhimārūḍhaṃ nīlagrīveṇa bheditam |
prāṇaṃ nāsorddhvasaṃyuktaṃ asyendramādimākṣaram || 25-79 ||
havyavāhanamārūḍhaṃ jīvanānta prabhedayet |
rudrabāṇena vidhivat tataḥ paścād vibhūṣayet || 25-80 ||
hantorddhena ṣaṭ cakraṃ tatonyaṃ vinyaset punah |
dharitryantaṃ nyased bījaṃ tasyaivoparisaṃsthitam || 25-81 ||
rudrabāṇādma sambhinnaṃ yamorddhvena samanvitam |
vinyasya vinyaset paścānmātṛrodanamāditam || 25-82 ||
ātmā prāśaṃ nyasen mūrddhni piṅgakeśasya pārvati |
varmāstraṃ purato nyasya astrendraṃ bhavate tadā || 25-83 ||
praṇavādyo mahāstrendra eṣate kathito mayā |
rakṣārthe yojayedva akṣṇormadhye kare tathā || 25-84 ||
duṣṭā vai hiṃsakā ye tu tathā vighnāḥ sudāruṇāḥ |
satyaṃ nāśayate hyeṣa astrendraḥ kathito bhuvi || 25-85 ||
kādimāntā smṛtāstatvā surā dīrghāstu śaktayaḥ |
sānusvāravisargāntā mayā te samudāhṛtāḥ || 25-86 ||
hrasvā vaktrā samākhyātāḥ sānusvāreṇa saṃyutāḥ |
nālaṃ hakāramityuktaṃ kandāntaṃ parikalpayet || 25-87 ||
dharmādyā vyāpakenaiva kalpanīyā yathākramam |

123b] ete mantrāḥ samākhyātā amṛtīśavidhau sadā || 25-88 ||
astrendreṇa jalaṃ śodhya śaktikumbhaṃ prakalpayet |
amṛtenāmṛtī kṛtvā snātvā śeṣāmṛtena tu || 25-89 ||
caturasraṃ puraṃ keca likhetpūrva karādikam |
madhyebjāṣṭadalaṃ śuklaṃ caturvaṇopaśobhitam || 25-90 ||
tasmin yajeta deveśaṃ kalaśe kalpate sati |
varddhanī tu nyased dakṣe astrendreṇa vipaścite || 25-91 ||
pūrveva māsanaṃ nyasya puṃstatvaṃ karṇiko pari |
prabhuśaktiṃ tatorddhvaṃ tu amṛtīśaṃ tatopari || 25-92 ||
viśuddhasphaṭikaprakhyaṃ samantādamṛtasravam |
caturbhujaṃ śūlahastaṃ nāgayajñopavītinam || 25-93 ||
mahāhibaddhaparyaṅkaṃ karpūrakṣodadhūsaram |
jaṭāmakuṭavaddhorddhvaṃ ninetraṃ pūjayet sadā || 25-94 ||
paścādaṅgāṃgikaṃ kuryād vidhinā tu vicakṣaṇaḥ |
keśarāgreṣu tatvāni dalamūle tu śaktayaḥ || 25-95 ||
hrasvāḥ patreṣu dātavyā pūrvamādikrameṇa tu |
ā ī caiva kramāṅgāni astraṃ digdalasaṃsthitam || 25-96 ||
astrendra pūjayed vidvān dalāgre dakṣiṇe sadā |
pūjayeta svamantreṇa sarvāṇyeva yathākramam || 25-97 || 
173b g] arghapādyādikaṃ? caiva svamantreṇaiva dāpayet |
evaṃ pūjya vidhānena tato hyoṣadhamālabhet || 25-98 ||
prokṣayed vidhinā tacca auṣadhaṃ upayuñjayet |
caruṃ ca amṛtīśena vaktavyaṃ vidhivad budhaiḥ || 25-99 ||
tribhāgabhājitaṃ kṛtvā devāyāgran nidayet |
tatognau hutaśeṣaṃ tu śivaistu sahabhakṣayet || 25-100 ||
prokṣitairamṛtīśena ālabdaiśca? prapūjayet? |
auṣadhaṃ ca caruṃ caiva nānyathā tu varānane || 25-101 ||
svāhāntena tu devena amṛtīśena auṣadham |
sampātābhihutaṃ kuryād vidhidṛṣṭena karmaṇā || 25-102 ||
sahasrāhutihomena sarpiṣā payasāpi vā |
madhuratrikahomo *? kartavyo hi bhippagvaraiḥ || 25-103 ||
sṛṣṭikṛcchāntayuktena amṛtīśena dāpayet |
evaṃ homavidhiṃ kruyāt sakalīkṛtavigrahaḥ || 25-104 ||
ātmānamamṛtīśena samapūrṇaṃ vicintayet |
amṛtīśena taṃ kuryāt paścādaṅgikaṃ budhaḥ || 25-105 ||
tato mūrddhni sthitaṃ padmaṃ ūrddhvanālamadhomukham |
dhyāyeta śaśinā pūrṇāmamṛtīśaṃ tatopari || 25-106 ||
pūrvanyāsāṅgavinyastaṃ viśuddhasphaṭikaprabham |
sravantamamṛtaṃ divyaṃ pralayābhaṃ vicintayet || 25-107 ||
mantrayāgaṃ tathaiveha sravadamṛtabindavaḥ |
avicchinnamukhā śubhrā viśed brahmabile va tu || 25-108 ||
tenāpyāyita mātmānaṃ hṛdrataṃ padmasaṃsthitam |
tatra padmaṃ himābhaṃ tu ūrddhvavaktrāmṛtātmakam || 25-109 ||
muñcantamamṛtaṃ hyūrddhvaṃ plāvayaṃcātmamaṇḍalam |
ubhau vai tatvamāpannādamṛtaṃ vyāpakaṃ puram || 25-110 ||
cintayet tadamṛte naiva? ugrarogavināśanam? |
pādamūle tathā padmamūrddhvavaktrākṛtātmakam || 25-111 ||
dalairanyonyasaṃlagnaiḥ padmābhyāṃ tu parasparam |
cintayet sampuṭāviddhamātmānamasya madhyagam || 25-112 ||
gacchantiṣṭhan svapana jāgraṃvadaṃ yaścintayennaraḥ |
nā'sau badhyati kālena auṣadhamamṛtaṃ bhavet || 25-113 ||
evaṃ nyāsavidhiṃ kuryādauṣadhamupayañjayet |
acireṇaiva kālena sa mṛtyuṃ vijayiṣyati || 25-114 ||
sampuṭam amṛtākhyaṃ tu na deyaṃ yasya kasyacit |
bāhyenyasampuṭaṃ kṛtvā hṛtpadme yajanaṃ tataḥ || 25-115 ||
174a g] ūrddhādhaḥ sampuṭaṃ paścādamṛtīśasamantataḥ |
vidhivad vahnimādāya astrendraprokṣitaṃ purā || 25-116|| 
vibhugarbhe tu hṛdinā prokṣitaṃ varmaṇā kṣipet |

124a] prajvālyamamṛtāstreṇa hṛdināstaraṇaṃ tataḥ || 25-117 ||
brahmā hariharaṃ siddhiryat pūrvā ca krameṇa tu |
viṣṭharasthāstathā cedhyāḥ paridhiṃ ca prakalpya tu || 25-178 ||
paścādvai devatānyāsaṃ praṇavena prakalpayet |
ājyādīnāṃ ca saṃskārā yat kiñcid vidhicoditam || 25-179 ||
tat sarvaṃ praṇave naiva hṛdayenāthavā bhavet |
paścād yajeta deveśaṃ yathāpūrvaṃ tathaiva hi || 25-180 || 
ājyaṃ kṣīraṃ dadhiṃ vātha tilaṃ vāpyathavā madhum |
vrīhayovā'tha nīvārā yājñikānmetha pācitaḥ || 25-181 || 
anuvargatha homyaḥ pāyasādyotha saṃskṛtaḥ |
madhuratrikasaṃyuktāṃ homayeta bhṛśaṃ bhiṣak || 25-182 ||
dūrvāṅkuraira khaṇḍaiścaruhyaṃ kalpāyuṣo bhavet |
śatahomaṃ samastena vyaste tu parameśvari || 25-183 ||
pratilome sa bījaṃ tu nirbījaṃ tvanyathā bhavet |
akṣarāṇāṃ japellakṣaṃ vidhivacca vidhānavit || 25-184 ||
devadevasya cāṅgāni abhiṣiktastanurnaraḥ |
tataḥ sāmarthiratulā jāyate sādhakasya tu || 25-185 ||
niṣkalaṃ śivavinyāsamācāryāya pradāpayet |
sakalaṃ dhyānamālambya sādhakaṃ cābhiṣecayet || 25-186 ||
kalaśairvā dalairvāpi pālāśaiśca padmodbhavaiḥ |
jalena kusumairvāpi ācāryastatvacintakaḥ || 25-187 ||
kalairhomaṃ tataḥ kṛtvā amṛtīśaṃ tato yajet |
ātmaśuddhinimittaṃ hi astrendreṇa śataṃ hunet || 25-188 ||
digdhatvamamṛtāstreṇa mahāstreṇā'thavā tu tam |
śuddhayaśca tathānyeṣāṃ dvābhyāmeva na saṃśayaḥ || 25-189 ||
āvāhanaṃ bhavet svārṇaiḥ svākṣaiścaiva visarjanam |
devaṃ tu pūjayekena anenaiva visarjayet || 25-190 ||
hṛdinā sthāpayed devaṃ gandhapuṣpādikaṃ dadet |
sarveṣāṃ tarpaṇaṃ kṛtvā ante pūrṇāhutiṃ dadet || 25-191 ||
devadevena dīkṣānte evameva na saṃśayaḥ |
niṣkalaṃ tu śivaṃ dhyātvā sakalena tu sādhake || 25-192 ||
vidhinā amṛtaṃ japtvā dvayaṅgulāṃ sakalāṃ budhaḥ |
śaktyāyā amṛtāhvāyā hutvā mṛtyuñjayo bhavet || 25-193 ||
dūrvākāṇḍā svakhaṇḍā vā hunedāpyāyane vidhau |
174b g] āpyāyito'bhiṣiktaśca tato mṛtyuṃ hariṣyati || 25-194 || 
audumbarokhalaṃ kṛtvā tasmin vinyasya pūrvavat | 
devadevaṃ tu vidhivat kusumaistu śitaistataḥ || 25-195 ||
ādyaṃ tena purādheyamamṛtīśena yojitam |
sṛṣṭikṛtsahinaṃ vidvānabhiṣekavidho hitam || 25-196 ||

jarāvyādhivinirmuktaḥ kalpāyuṣyo bhaviṣyati |
129a] mantraśca kramate tasya siddhyatyevaṃ na saṃśayaḥ || 27-48 ||
anena vidhinā devi paśubījavivarjitaḥ |
puraveśaṃ samāyāti dṛḍhalakṣo yadā bhavet || 27-49 ||
prathamaṃ śodhya nāḍī tu prāṇāyā maṃtato'bhyaset |
ṣaḍviṃ?śanmātṛko jñeya uddhātaḥ parikīrtitaḥ || 27-50 ||
madhyamastu dvirudrātaiścaturbhiścaiva uttamaḥ |
prāṇajiśca śatoddhātaistava devi prakāśitaḥ || 27-51 ||
āhāraṃ tu jayet pakṣānnidrājayamataḥ param |
śāśanaṃ tu tatojitvā yad rocet taṃ samabhyaset || 27-52 ||
svastikaṃ caiva paryaṅkaṃ daṇḍāsanamathāpi vā |
padmāsanaṃ ca deveśi jayedekatamaṃ tataḥ || 27-53 ||
paścād vedhaṃ tu kartavyaṃ arkatulaiḥ śanaiḥ śanaiḥ |
evaṃ guḍe tatha nimbe vedhaṃ kṛtvā tu mantravit || 27-54 ||
svādam ākarṣayed yāvantāvadevaṃ samabhyaset |
karpūrāgarukhaṇḍādyaiḥ prakṛtiṃ kārayet sudhīḥ || 27-55 ||
sarūpā sastanā caiva sarvālaṅkārabhūṣitā |
vicitrairvarṇakairdevi citritavyā prayatnataḥ || 27-56 ||
ekānte vijane ramye girigahvaranirjhare |
bhūgṛhe vā sugupte ca yatra vā ramate manaḥ || 27-57 ||
sudhūpadhūpite caiva puṣpaprakarasaṅkule |
duṣṭasatvavinirmukte abhyased yogavit sadā || 27-58 ||
ekacittasthito dhīrapratimāsvagrataḥ sthitaḥ |
āsanaṃ yat purābhyastaṃ tatrasthaḥ susamāhitaḥ || 27-59 ||
vṛttaṃ vā kārayed devi utsedhvāccaturaṅgulam |
mṛduṃ sukhāsanaṃ divyaṃ āsanaṃ āsanottamam || 27-60 ||
182a g] atra sthito mahāvīra ekacitta suyantritaḥ |
nāsāgre nāsikāgraṃ tu dṛṣvā dṛṣṭi niyojayet || 27-61 ||
tatrāpita manobhūtvā śaktyārūḍhastu pudgalaḥ |
nirodhastatrakartavya pratimāgataceta saḥ || 27-62 ||
vedaghaṭṭanirodhaṃ ca kartavyaṃ suranāyike |
punarevāgamaṃ mantrī yāvat taddhṛdayaṃ punaḥ || 27-63 ||
bhāvayed vāramāyanta pañcabhūtāni caiva hi |
trayodaśavidhaṃ devi karaṇaṃ tatra bhāvayet || 27-64 ||
satvādayo guṇā ye tu tenastāstatra bhāvayet |
sūkṣmarūpāstu te devi tad bhāvagatacetasaḥ || 27-65 ||
evamabhyasamānasya calate pratimā tu yā |
bādhate ṣaṇmukhā tasya gacchate ca parāṅmukhā || 27-66 ||
evaṃ krameṇa deveśi siddhyate nātra saṃśayaḥ |
ṣaṇmāsā yogamuktastu yuktāhāro dṛḍhavrataḥ || 27-67 ||
svecchayā vicared yogī manasāṃ yatra rocate |
agnivyādhijarādyaiśca mṛtyubhirnaiva bādhyate || 27-68 ||
kṣaṇād dīrghakṣaṇād hrasvo mṛgapakṣisarīsṛpaḥ |
nicchidrairnirgacchated yogī sudṛḍhairgṛhamaṇḍapaiḥ || 27-69 ||
prāsādyairbhūgṛhairvāpi evamādyairanekaśaiḥ |
śṛṃkhalai rajjubhirvāpi vaddho muñcati tatkṣaṇāt || 27-70 ||
brahmādīṃ? sarvadevāṃśca paśyate divyacakṣuṣā |
aṇimādiguṇāvāptirjāyate hyavicārataḥ || 27-71 ||
krīḍate satataṃ yogī svecchayā yatra rocate |
caret kāmacaredbhūtvā yathāhatagati priye || 27-72 ||
tasyā te saṃlīyate sūkṣme śive parama kāraṇe |
yogasiddhimavāpnoti siddhyatyevaṃ na saṃśayaḥ || 27-73 ||
kathitaṃ sarahasyaṃ tu śāstrasadbhāvamuttamam |
ātmācāragatijñānāt sa siddhiphalabhāg bhavet || 27-74 ||

iti bhairavasrotasi mahātantre vidyāpīṭhe śrītantrasadbhāve saptakoṭipramāṇe raktākṛṣṭi? saptāviṃśatiḥ paṭalaḥ || 27 ||

śrībhairava uvāca

ātmācāragatirdevi nāḍī padmavinirṇayam |
129b] pañcaviṃśatitatvāni puruṣeṇa samanvitam || 28-1 ||
yathā sthitāni sarvāṇi yathā vakṣyāmi bhairavi |
kakārādi makārāntāḥ pañcapañcasu saṃsthitāḥ || 28-2 ||
nāḍīmukhāstute jñeyā anyonya vyāpya saṃsthitāḥ |
182b g] ātmā sañcarate nityaṃ sthānāt sthāna manukramāt || 28-3 ||
navapadmā smṛtā ye tu ha ja da ṭha dvayastathā |
ta tha da ma mahādevi navapadmāḥ prakīrtitāḥ || 28-4 ||
ya ra la vāstu ye proktāḥ krameṇa vinivedayet |
vāyavye cāgnisaṃsthe ca īśenaiṛtigocare || 28-5 ||
haṃsākhyaṃ kukṣayobhau tu śaṣau yau liṅgapāyugau |
evaṃ krameṇa deveśi navapadmāḥ prakīrtitāḥ || 28-6 ||
madhyamegraṃ smṛtaṃ padmaṃ aṣṭāraṃ tad vinirdiśet |
tatra nāḍyā sthitā bhadre tadādhārāstu vāyavāḥ || 28-7 ||
iḍādi śaṅkhinī cāntā nāḍyastasmin vyavasthitāḥ |
pūrvādi īśaparyantā adhaścordhve ca bhāmini || 28-8 ||
nāḍī padmāntarālasthā śūle vāyupratiṣṭhitam |
prāṇādyā vāyavaḥ proktā kathitāstu mayā'naghe || 28-9 ||
puryaṣṭakaṃ tu yat proktaṃ dalamadhye vyavasthitam |
karṇikasthaṃ svayaṃ devo maṇḍalatritayānvitaḥ || 28-10 ||
agni sūryaśca candraśca catuḥśaktistathaiva ca |
tanmadhye tu svatejena hutāśa kaṇikākṛtiḥ || 28-11 || 
kuṇḍalyudarasaṃsthaṃ tu tiṣṭhate tadvasāt priye |
prasuptabhujagākārā prasuptā'mṛtakuṇḍalī || 28-12 ||
etairdvandvairmahābaddho vivasaḥ sarvadevinām |
tiṣṭhate hṛdaye caiva ucchvāsaśvāsasaṃyutaḥ || 28-13 ||
yāvad ūrdhvagatistasya devadevasya sundari |
tāvad vyāpī smṛto hyūrdhve adhobhāge visargiṇaḥ || 28-14 ||
vyāpayeta avārastho devadevo jagatpatiḥ |
akāraṃ hṛdayaṃ caiva brahmā tatrā'dhidevatā || 28-15 ||
īkāraṃ kaṇṭhato devi viṣṇustatra jagatpatiḥ |
ukāraṃ tāluke jñeyaṃ rudrastatra pratiṣṭhitaḥ || 28-16 ||
bhruvorbindurmaheśaṃ ca vakre nādaṃ sadāśivam |
jñātavyaḥ so'dhikārasyo yathā te kathayāmyaham || 28-17 ||
antaryāgaṃ yathāpūrvaṃ uccāraṇaparaṃ yathā |
daśadhā yogamārgeṇa ātmā svacchandamabhyaset || 28-18 ||
mātrā vindumatītaṃ ca nādātmā jyotivigraham |
kalpanālakṣa se kalpaṃ dhyāyed vaitena sarvagam || 28-19 ||
apasavyena pūreta savyenaiva tu recayet | 
nāḍī saṃśodhayo metat mokṣamārgapathasya tu || 28-20 ||
183a g] recanā pūraṇād rodhā prāṇāyāmastraya smṛtaḥ |
sāmānyā bahiretāni punaścābhyantarāṇi tu || 28-21 ||
abhyantareṇa receta pūredabhyantareṇa tu |
niṣkaṃpaṃ kumbhakaṃ kṛtvā trayaścā'bhyantarāṇi tu || 28-22 ||
tābhyāṃ hṛdayasañcārānmanaścendriyagocarāt |
prāṇāyāmaścaturthaśca supraśāntastu viśrutaḥ || 28-23 ||
prāṇarodhe tu sampūrṇe nābhau nītvā samucchvaset |
śanairvimocayed vāyuṃ vāmanāsāpuṭena tu || 28-24 ||
yāyavīṃ dhārayeguṣṭhe āgneyīṃ nābhimadhyataḥ |
māhendrī kaṇṭhadeśe tu vāruṇī ghaṇṭikāśritāḥ || 28-25 ||
ākāśadhāraṇā mūrddhni sarvasiddhi karī smṛtī |
ekadvitricatuṣpañca udghātaiśca prasiddhyati || 28-26 ||
saṃruddhe caiva prāṇe ca mūrddhni gatvā nivartate |
udghātamiti tat proktaṃ jñātavyaṃ yogibhiḥ sadā || 28-27 ||
rāgadveṣau prahīyete prāṇāyāmaistu dhāritaiḥ |
dhāraṇaiḥ sarvapāpāni pratyāhāraikasaṃyamaḥ || 28-28 ||
hṛdgudānābhikaṇṭhe ca sarvasandhau tathaiva ca |
prāṇādyā saṃsthitā hyete rūpaṃ śabdaṃ ca me śṛṇu || 28-29 ||
130a] drutatāranibhaḥ prāṇa indragopakasannibhaḥ |
kṣīrābha sphaṭikābhaśca pañcānāṃ rūpalakṣaṇam || 28-30 ||
ghaṇṭānādeva madhuro gajanādo mahādhvaniḥ |
prāṇādīnāṃ ca pañcānāṃ śabdametadudāhṛtam || 28-31 ||
bāhyamadhyātmikaṃ cārayeṣāṃ ca śṛṇu sāmpratam |
prāṇastho gacchate hyūrdhve apānastho'dha gacchati || 28-32 ||
samānasthastadā devi sarvatra vyāpyasaṃsthitaḥ |
udvegabhayasantrāsaṃ karotyanilasaṃsthitam || 28-33 ||
udānastena samproktā māruto varavarṇini |
vyāno vyāyāmasaṃkarma karoti suranāyike || 28-34 ||
nāgodbhārāṃ pramuñcyeta kūrmakonmīlayo sthitim |
kṛkaraḥ kṣubhite caiva devadatte vijambhikā || 28-35 ||
dhanañjaya sthito ghoṣe mṛtasyāpi na muñcati |
adhyātmagatirityuktā daśānāṃ vāhyagā yathā || 28-36 ||
jalpitaṃ hasitaṃ gītaṃ nṛtyaṃ yuddhagatistathā |
kampaśilpaṃ ca karmāṇi prāṇasyaiva hi ceṣṭitam || 28-37 ||
183b g] praveśayennapānāni vimalaṃ śrāvayedadhaḥ |
andhatvaṃ śrotrarogasśca apānastu kariṣyati || 28-38 ||
aśitaṃ līḍhapittaṃ ca samānaḥ samatāṃ nayet |
kṣudhā hikkā tathā chikkā kāśodānasya ceṣṭhitam || 28-39 ||
romaharṣañca svedañca śūladāghagataṃjanam |
vyānametāni karmāṇi sparśatvaṃ caiva vindati || 28-40 ||
aṅguṣṭhe jānuhṛdaye locane mūrdhni saṃsthitaḥ |
nāgādyā bahurūpāśca karmateṣāṃ nibodha me || 28-41 ||
āhlādodvegajananaḥ śoṣaṇastrāsanastathā |
nidrātīvakaraścānyo yojako hi dhanañjayaḥ || 28-42 ||
śvāsaṃ saṅkocacchedaṃ ca sphiguru svāṃ hyameva ca |
nāgādīnāṃ tu pañcānāṃ mṛtyukāle viceṣṭitam || 28-43 ||
na caiva yānti cotkāntyā na tyajet tu dhanañjayaḥ |
aṃkucayati vai kūrmaṃ śoṣayecca kalevaram || 28-44 ||
prāṇameva jayet pūrvaṃ jite prāṇejitenmanaḥ ? |
jite manasi śāntātmā paraṃ tatvaṃ prakāśayet || 28-45 ||
prāṇāpānaṃ gudāṃ dhyāyet prāṇaḥ samano nābhigaḥ |
prāṇodānaṃ ca kaṇṭhe ca prāṇodānaṃ ca sarvagam || 28-46 ||
nāgādyā prāṇasaṃyuktāḥ svasthāneṣu nirandhayet |
niruddhasya ca yat kālaṃ pravakṣyāmi nibodha me || 28-47 ||
tāluṃ prabhṛti dhāryeta yāvat pañcadaśāntagam |
jito'nilo bhavatyeva saṃkrāntyuntikakarmaṇi || 28-48 ||
divyakānti śubho gandhaḥ prajñā cāsyavivarddhate |
divyadṛṣṭiśca śravaṇo divyavācā pravartate || 28-49 ||
vāyuvad vicarellokān siddhān devāṃśca paśyati |
manecchācintitāprāptiḥ pravarteta guṇāṣṭakam || 28-50 ||
sarvakāmasusampūrṇaṃ sarvadvandvavivarjitaḥ |
saṃsārabandhanirmuktaḥ śivatulyaśca jāyate || 28-51 ||
prāṇāpāne tu saṃyojya hrasvakoṭisamanvitam |
nābhyāṃ dhāryetayogīndra sveda kampaśca jāyate || 28-52 ||
punarevaṃ hṛdistho hi prāṇāpāna nirundhayet |
dīrghakoṭisamāyogāt tatkṣaṇāt patate mahīm || 28-53 ||
kaṇṭhasthaṃ hi tathaiveha prāṇamevaṃ nirundhayet |
plutakoṭisamāyogāt svapravṛttiḥ suto bhavet || 28-54 ||
bhrūmadhye binduyuktena prāṇarodhaṃ tu kārayet |
suṣuptajāyate tatra kṣaṇāccaivaṃ prabuddhyati || 28-55 ||
130b] mūrddhni dvāraṃ samāsṛtya niṣkalaṃ dhyānamārabhet |
184b g] evamabhyasatastasya pratyayastu tadā bhavet || 28-56 ||
pipīlikaṇṭhakāvedhānmūrddhni dvāraṃ vibhidyate |
bhitvā krameṇa sarvāṇi utpanāṃ taṃ hi yāvataḥ || 28-57 ||
pūrvoktalakṣaṇāṃ devi tyaktvā svacchandatāṃ vrajet |
jāyate tatsamatvaṃ hi dehe nānena sādhakaḥ || 28-58 ||
saṅkramet paradeheṣu kṣuttṛṣābhyāṃ * bādhyate |
atītānāgataṃ caiva trailokye yatpravartate || 28-59 ||
pratyakṣaṃ tad bhavet tasya sarvajñatva prajāyate |
evaṃ cāragatiṃ jñātvā śarīre sarvadehinām || 28-60 ||
gacchate cāṣṭabhiḥ patraiḥ punarvarṇāntaraṃ brajet |
kādibhānteṣu deveśi vikāraṃ ca prakurvanti || 28-61 ||
satvastho rajasaṃstho ca tamastho guṇavedakaḥ |
evaṃ paryaṭate devi sthānāt sthānāntaraṃ brajet || 28-62 ||
padmasūtranibhā nāḍyasteṣu saṃcarate'nilaḥ |
aṅguṣṭhe caiva aṅgulyastatra nāḍyā vyavasthitaḥ || 28-63 ||
tarjanyā madhyamā'nāmā tatra nāḍyā kasaṃjñikā |
aṅguṣṭhe ca khasaṃjñā ca cakāreṇa kanīyasī || 28-64 ||
dakṣahastasya suśroṇi trayo nāḍyāstu sūcitā |
vāmāṅguṣṭhe prakārasthāṃ hakāre madhyamā smṛtāḥ || 28-65 ||
ñakāre tu kanīyā tu vāmahastasya suvrate |
pādanāḍyaḥ pravakṣyāmi yathāvadanupūrvaśaḥ || 28-66 ||
ṇakāreṇa kanīyāṃtu nakārasthā tu madhyamāḥ |
pakāre'guṣṭhakā jñeyā nāḍī trayamudāhṛtā || 28-67 ||
dakṣapādasya suśroṇi sāmprataṃ vāmagā yathā |
kathayāmi samāsena vibhavena varānane || 28-68 ||
suṣumṇā guhyadeśasthā yāvad vrahmavilāntaram |
gatā sāṃ paramā nāḍī jñātavyā deśikena tu || 28-69 ||
tatrārūḍho masaṃjñastu adhaścordhve ca gacchati |
tāluke ṣoḍaśāraṃ tu adhovaktra vyavasthitaḥ || 28-70 ||
tatra prāpto nivarteta nāsādvāreṇa dhāvati |
dvādaśāntaṃ tataḥ prāptaḥ puraṃ caiva viśet punaḥ || 28-71 ||
bindusthāne tathā'ṣṭāraṃ jñātavyaṃ satataṃ priye |
śaktisthāne ṣaḍāraṃ tu sūkṣmamārge vyavasthitaḥ || 28-72 ||
brahmasthāne tu sampūjya devatācakramuttamam |
viṣṇusthā yoginī caiva dūtī rudrapadāśritāḥ || 28-73 ||
kiṅkārya īśvare devi mātarastu sadā śive |
184b g] śaktisthā caiva deveśi viśvādyāḥ kramaśo nyaset || 28-74 ||
prathamaṃ devatācakraṃ dvitīyaṃ yoginī smṛtam |
tṛtīyaṃ dūtikaṃ devi kiṃ kāryastu caturthakam || 28-75 ||
mātṛcakra smṛtaṃ devi pañcamaṃ parikīrtitam |
viśveśvaryāstu ṣaṣṭhaṃ syāt tava devi mayoditam || 28-76 ||
ṣaḍcakrāṃ bhāvayeddevi adhyātme parameśvari |
yakāre vāyavī sṛṣṭirakāre māraṇātmikāḥ || 28-77 ||
lakāre stambhasṛṣṭi syād vakāre varuṇātmikā |
hakāre prāṇasṛṣṭiḥ syāt sakāreṇa samanvitā || 28-78 ||
amṛtārthe samuddiṣṭā kārayet sādhakottamaḥ |
kakārādimakārāntāḥ pañcaviṃśamudāhṛtāḥ || 28-79 ||
etaistu sṛṣṭivedho'sti jñātvā kāmavibhāgaśaḥ |
mantrasṛṣṭi samuddiṣṭā statva sṛṣṭistathaiva ca || 28-80 ||
jīvasṛṣṭi samākhyātā varṇasṛṣṭistathā parā |
bhūtasṛṣṭistathā devi yogasṛṣṭistathaiva ca || 28-81 ||
131a] yadrūpaṃ bhāvayitvā tu vedhayet parameśvari |
tadātmakaṃ tu vijñānaṃ bhavate sādhakasya tu || 28-82 ||
sṛṣṭirūpaṃ bhavet sarvaṃ jñātavyaṃ sādhakena tu |
dvārāṇi pañca deveśi kathayāmi samāsataḥ || 28-83 ||
hastau pādau tathā guhye pañca vedhāḥ prakīrtitāḥ |
kīlikī siddhi saṃprāpya yogasiddhiḥ pravartate || 28-84 ||
evaṃ cāragatiṃ jñātvā sṛṣṭivedhaṃ tu kārayet |
yatra cakre tu vedho vai dīyate parameśvari || 28-85 ||
taccakraṃ tasya deveśi pratyakṣeṇa prapaśyati |
tadātmakaṃ tu vijñānaṃ bhavate sādhakasy tu || 28-86 ||
athavā sṛṣṭivedhaṃ tu kriyate parameśvari |
etanmārgarahitastu yovedhaṃ karttumicchati || 28-87 ||
hanate muṣṭinākāśaṃ pibate mṛgatṛṣṇikām |
nāsā karṇau ca cakṣubhyāṃ tasmin vedho na vidyate || 28-88 ||
apadvārāstu te proktā * vahnivyavasthitāḥ |
tena te niṣphalā vedhā vidveṣoccāṭamāraṇe || 28-89 ||
ityevaṃ sarahasyaṃ tu tantre'smin dvidaśātmake |
sāhasre sāramādāya sukhavodhamanākulam || 28-90 ||
kathitaṃ saprapañcedaṃ nānāvijñānasañkulam |
185a g] yena janmasahasraistu bhaktayācā'rādhito hyaham || 28-91 ||
saprāpnoti tvidaṃ tatra anyo dṛṣṭvāṃ tu muhyati |
etat tatra varaṃ devi nākhyātaṃ kasyacinmayā || 28-92 ||
tava devi samākhyātaṃ rahasyaṃ prakaṭīkṛtam |
ṛṣidevagaṇairvandyaṃ yoginībhiḥ prapūjitam || 28-93 ||
asmāt tantrāt paraṃ nāsti rudraśaktiradhiṣṭhitam |
ye yoginīkule jātāstadbhāvagatacetasaḥ || 28-94 ||
jñāyante te tvidaṃ śāstraṃ gūḍhārthaṃ jñānasāgaram |
likhitaṃ tiṣṭhate devi yatra taṃtraṃ mahāyaśe || 28-95 ||
te'pi? muktiṃ gamiṣyanti piṇḍapātā varānane |
yoginyaścaiva rakṣanti putravat pālayanti hi || 28-96 ||
na tasya vartate mārīnā śubhaṃ vidyate kvacit |
yastu pūjayate śāstraṃ amṛtaṃ sāravat priye || 28-97 ||
tena puṣṭaṃ śrutaṃ hyetaṃ kṛtaṃ samayarakṣaṇam |
svabuddhyā yodurātmāna pīṭhamārgavikalpakāḥ || 28-98 ||
na teṣāṃ punarāvṛttirgatāste rauravāntikam |
tasmāt paramparāyātaṃ mantrārthasiddhikaulikam || 28-99 ||
śrotavyaṃ gurusāmīpye yāvantaṃ nirmalaṃ bhavet |
gopanīyaṃ prayatnena na śrāvyaṃ tadabhāvitam || 28-100 ||
pramādācchrāvayed yastu avijñātvā tvanukramam |
gacchate narakaṃ ghoraṃ gurudrohī durātmanaḥ || 28-101 ||
nāhaṃ tasya paritrātā majjamāne bhavārṇave |
yoginīgaṇamadhyasthaḥ paśurevātra sādhakaḥ || 28-102 ||
samadrohī gurudrohī devadrohī varānane |
yāṃ gatiṃ gacchate devi tāṃ gatiṃ gacchate tu saḥ || 28-103 ||

buddhīndriyāṇito datvā śrāvayed dāpayedatha |
132a] anyathā bhavate doṣa iti śāstrasya niścayaḥ || 28-132 ||
etacchāstravaraṃ devi na deyaṃ yasya kasyacit |

iti bhairavasrotasi mahātantre vidyāpīṭhe saptakoṭipramāṇe śrītantrasadbhāve ātmācārādhikāro nāmā'ṣṭaviṃśaḥ paṭalaḥ || 28-28 ||

śrītantrasadbhāvapustakaṃ samāptaṃ śubham |
dviśate samvatsaretīte saptadaśasapūrataḥ |
phālgunasya tu śuklasya uttameṣu trayodaśī |
tārakā pūrvaphalgunyā śukragrahasuśobhane |
sumūrte pūjyasatkāre siddhihetuvarārthinā |
śrīnepālasya vijayarājye mahān śrīharṣadevarājasu |
lekhakaḥ śrīpaśupatisthānasaṃjñā śovarddhanāṅkitaḥ |
etat saṃyogayuktaṃ tu śrīmāntantra mahadbhutam |
apracāramahātantraṃ guṇāḍhyaṃ sāramuttamam |
rahasyaṃ gopanīyaṃ ca dakṣiṇavaktranirgatam |
atiguhyamidaṃ tantraṃ śrīmāntantrasadbhāvamuttamam |
cūḍāmaṇiviṣayaḥ śreṣṭhaḥ phaṇāṅkanāmam |
